Mahābhāṣyadīpikāyāṃ prathamam āhnikam

[1.1] panārthaṃ[prādhānyakhyāpanārthaṃ] vaidikānāṃ pṛthagupādānam / prādhānyasyāpi hi sāmānye 'pyantarbhūtasya pṛthagupadeśo dṛśyate, brāhmaṇā āyātā vasiṣṭho 'pyāyāta iti / atha vā laukikānām ityevamukte laukikaśabdaṃ sāmānyamapi [na?] viśeṣaṃ pratipadyeta / bhāṣāśabdānāmevedamanuśāsanaṃ na vaidikānām / yasmātte hi niyatasaṃveśāḥ pratiniyataprātiśākhyāḥ, dṛṣṭānuvidhitvātteṣviti / ata ubhayorupādānam -- laukikānāṃ vaidikānāṃ ceti / 

[1.2]	 nanu caivamātmakatve sati nānenānuśāsanaṃ vaidikānām iti ye santi prātiśākhyalakṣaṇe / puruṣāḥ pramādādaśaktervā apabhāṣante yata idamanuśāsanam / yathā taittirīyāḥ kṛtaṇatvamagniśabdamuccārayanti / kiṃ ca / ye ca śabdā anāmnātā apūrvasyābhivyaktau vyāpriyante, tadyathā saṃkhyāya kapālānyudvāsayatīti saṃkhyāśabdapratipāditaiḥ eko dvau traya udvāsayan śāstreṇānuśiṣṭairudvāsayati / evaṃ yeyamagnihotre mānuṣīvadidaṃ kurvityatrāpi viniyogaśabdāḥ śāstreṇānvākhyātāḥ prayoktavyā iti teṣu vyāpārāḥ / ye cānye labdhānupūrvīkāḥ teṣv api vyāpāro 'sti / katham iti / śāstrapūrve prayoge 'bhyudayaḥ / śāstrāt vijñātatattvāḥ prayujyamānā dharmaḥ / 

[1.3]	 laukikāstāvat / gauraśvaḥ puruṣo hastī brāhmaṇa [iti] / itiḥ prakārārtha evāṃprakārādau adhikṛtatvāccānvākhyānasya / loke cāniyatapaurvāparyāṇi vākyeṣv arthapradhānāni padānyevānvākhyāyanta iti laukikāḥ padabhūtā eva nirdiśyante / apauruṣeyavaidikānāṃ śamādayo na kevalāḥ prayujyanta iti vākyānyeva nirdiśyante / vākyasthānāmevānuśāsanaṃ na vākyānāmeva / etac ca sarvaṃ bhāṣyakārasya prayojanāni vaktuḥ prastāvanāmātram / prākprayojanānvākhyānamadhyetṛprotsāhanārtham iti / 

[2.1]	 atha gaur ityavayavaśabdo 'yaṃ bahuṣvartheṣu abhidhādiṣu eva / tacca sarvaṃ bhāṣyakāranirdiṣṭam / yathā saṃskāraśabdaḥ śāstrāntare evamanekārtha iti / śabdānuśāsanam iti śabdaśabdaḥ tasya yadi prasiddhārthe vṛttiḥ kalpyate anuśāsanavaiyarthyameva / laukikānāṃ kartṛsiddhatvāt vaidikānāṃ ca vede / atra yathaiva kaścit athāto niśvāsapraśvāsān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ evaṃ bruvannanarthakaṃ brūyāt evaṃ śabdānuśāsanam api / tasmādatra śabdaśabdaḥ prasiddhe 'rthe [na] saṃbhavati / evaṃ keṣāṃ śabdānām ity atrāpi / evaṃ gauraśvaḥ puruṣa iti / evaṃ śabdaśabdo yadi vārthe vṛttaḥ anenopāttaḥ tasya cāpyanuśāsane sati prayojanam / athāsya kaścidanyaḥ pāribhāṣikaḥ śabdo 'sti yasyānuśāsanaṃ kriyate sa ca na paribhāṣāṃ gacchati / gaur ity atra kaḥ śabdaḥ / atha vā dvau bhāgau śabdasya svarūpabhāgo 'rthabhāgaś ca / rūpabhāge 'nvākhyānavaiyarthyāt dvitīyo bhāga āśrīyate tadanvākhyānāt śabdeneti / yathā dharmaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti prastutya dravyādyupadeśastatpratipattereva / athāpi cātra gaur iti śabdena dravyādayaḥ sāhacaryāducyante / 

[2.2] tatra gaur ityetasmin saṃniviṣṭāstānupanyasyati / kiṃ yattat sāsnālāṅgūlakhurakakudaviṣāṇi artharūpam / dravyapadārthikasya hi gośabde kenacid dharmeṇa asaṃspṛṣṭasya nirupākhyasya dravyasya viṣāṇādayaḥ saṃsargiṇo 'rthabhedakāḥ agośabdavācyasya tu [na] / yathā guḍasya mādhuryamandhakāre dravyāntarebhyo bhedakaṃ guḍo 'yaṃ nānyadravyam iti / artharūpo gopiṇḍa ity arthaḥ / netyāha / dravyaṃ nāma tad iti / netyāha / nehaṃ gośabda ityevaṃ prasiddham / kiṃ tarhi / dharmāntareṇa prasiddham iti / dharmāntaraṃ ca darśayati / dravyaṃ nāma tad iti / yathā saṃkhyāyā ekaśeṣo dvābhyāṃ nirvartyate saṃkhyāyā arthāsaṃpratyayāt anyapadārthatvāc ca anekaśeṣa ityevam / kim iti dravyasya svāṃ prasiddhim utsṛjya prasiddhyantareṇa ca  vyapadeśaḥ syāt / dravyānuśāsanaṃ cedvivakṣitamabhaviṣyat evam evāvakṣyat  -- dravyānuśāsanam iti / yattarhi tadiṅgitaṃ ceṣṭitaṃ nimiṣītam iti / iṅgitaṃ nāmābhiprāyasya sūcakaḥ kaścideva śarīragato vikāraḥ / ceṣṭitaṃ kāyaparispandaḥ / nimiṣitamakṣigatāḥ kriyāḥ / kathamāsāṃ śabdatvaṃ manyeta / saṃbhavitvādāsāṃ kriyāṇām / saṃbhavanti hyetāḥ kriyāḥ gavārthe / atha vā jātereva gotvasya kriyātvam / yathā sattetyasyāḥ / kriyāśabdenābhidhānam asti / yathaiva gataṃ gotvamevaṃ iṅgitādyapyarthataḥ mahiṣādiṣu dṛṣṭam / vyutpattyāpi karmaṇyāśrīyamāṇe gamivat viśeṣaṇaṃ durākhyānamupādadīta gacchati garjati gadati vā gaur iti / evaṃ nimeṣādayo 'pi śabdatvena vyapadiśyante / atrāpi tathaiva dharmāntareṇa prasiddhimāha -- netyāha / kriyā nāma seti / yattarhi śuklo nīla iti / eṣām api saṃbhavitvāt viśeṣeṇa ceṅgitādivadavasthānāt / bhinnāni goviṣayāṇi śauklyādīnyaśvaviṣayebhyaḥ / ataśca bhinnāni yadaśvaḥ karkaḥ śoṇo hema ityucyate, gaustu śuklo rakto nīla iti / tathā varṇasādhano 'pyevaṃ śakyate kalpayituṃ gamyate 'neneti / śuklādibhiś ca gamyate 'nye 'yamasmādarthāntaram iti / netyāha / guṇo nāma saḥ / atrāpyanyayā buddhyā kathanamarthāntarād idi netyāha guṇo nāma sa iti na yuktaṃ dravyasya nirdeśāt / naiṣa doṣaḥ / ubhayavacanāt / śuklādayo dravyavat prāgupanyastāḥ / saṃprati guṇaparo nirdeśa iti / yattarhi bhinne vabhinnamityekatvaṃ kathayati / chinneṣvacchinnam iti nityatvaṃ jāterāha / sāmānyabhūtam iti / bhūtaśabda upamāvācī / tatra yeṣāmarthāntarabhūtā jātih, teṣāṃ viśiṣṭeṣu gopiṇḍeṣu anuvartamānaṃ gotvaṃ teṣāṃ sāmānyaṃ nāśvādīnāṃ sarveṣām iti tadapekṣa upamāsaṃbandhaḥ / yeṣāṃ tat dravyāṇi ca teṣām api yebhyo 'bhedo nopapadyate teṣāṃ parityāgādyasmāc ca śakyābhedapratītiḥ tasya copādānāt sāmānyaguṇatvam ityuktam / netyāha / ākṛtirnāma sā / ākṛtir iti [na] tat saṃsthānam / kiṃ tarhi ? jātir eva / yathākṛtyabhivyañjakā [?] vājapyāyana iti / ākriyate 'nayeti ākṛtiḥ / ākriyante iti bhidyante padārthāntarebhya ityākṛtiḥ [?] / ākriyete buddhiśabdāvasthā iti ākṛtiḥ / saṃprati dravyādiṣu nirasteṣu dvayam api sthitaṃ samudāyo vā kaścit pāribhāṣiko [vā] / tatra samudāyo 'pi dravyādivatsamudāya iti prasiddhaḥ / anyaś ca pāribhāṣiko nāsti / prasiddhasya copādāne vaiyarthyam ityataḥ pṛcchati -- kas tarhi śabda iti / yenoccāriteneti / atrānekaṃ darśanam / kecin manyante / yo vāyam uccāryate kramavān avaraḥ / kaścid anyaḥ akramaḥ śabdātmā buddhistho vigāhate / tasmādarthapratipattiḥ / kutaḥ / yathiavārthāntaranibandhano nārthāntaraṃ pratyāyayati evaṃ svarūpanibandhano notsahate pratyāyayitum / anye manyante / yathā varṇeṣu varṇaturīyā bhāgā varṇajātiṃ vyañjayanti evaṃ varṇā vākyāntareṣu ye kramajanmānaḥ ayugapatkālāste tāṃ padasthāṃ varṇajātimabhivyañjayanti / vṛkṣaśabdo vṛkṣatvam / jāterarthasya pratipattiḥ / etac ca arthasvarūpaṃ sphoṭo 'yam eva śabdātmā nityaḥ /  ye tu kramajanmānaḥ ayugapatkālā, vyaktayo dhvanyātmānaste iti / anye manyante / dviśaktiḥ śabda ātmaprakāśane 'rthaprakāśane ca samarthaḥ / yathā pradīpaḥ ātmānaṃ prakāśayan nidhyarthān prakāśayati / yastvādhyātmikaḥ indriyākhyaḥ prakāśaḥ sa ātmānamaprakāśayan bāhyamarthaṃ prakāśayatīti / ato grantho yenoccāriteneti ubhayathā varṇyate / yenoccāritena prakāśitena, atha vā yenoccāriteneti / atha vā pratītapadārthaka iti / śabdaḥ ityetasya pratītaḥ padārthaḥ dhvaniḥ / dhvanirasyābhidheyaḥ / dhvanāvayamarthaprakaraṇaśabdāntarāṇyantareṇa svarūpaṃ pratilabhate / etamevārthaṃ padagrahaṇena vyācaṣṭe / padasyāyamartho nārtho vākyagamya iti / śabdaḥ / kimarthaṃ punaridamucyate / nirṇayasya vyākaraṇāpākaraṇābhyāṃ prakaraṇapravṛttidarśanāt / tadyathā / kati bhavataḥ putrā ityukte saṃkhyā pūrvaṃ saṃkhyāmupādāya praśne nnisṛtā / tasyāḥ nirṇayo vyākaraṇena apākaraṇena vā / kaścidāha eko dvau bahava iti / eko 'pi me nāsti [iti] ayamapākarotyeva praśnam, naivāyam praṣṭavya iti / evāmihāpi gaurityatra kaḥ śabda iti naivāyaṃ praṣṭavyaḥ prasiddhyuparodhāt / ya eva prasiddhaḥ śabdastasyaiva śabdaśabdena grahaṇam / pratītapadārthakatāṃ ca kathayati / śabdaṃ kuru / mā śabdaṃ kārṣīḥ / śabdakāryayaṃ māṇavaka iti dhvaniṃ kurvannucyate / tatra dhvaniṃ kurvan itīdaṃ śabdakāryayam ityasya saṃbandhitvenopāttam / pūrvayostvanyat pratipattavyam / atha vā yo dhvaniṃ karoti sa preryate śabdaṃ kuru iti / evaṃ pravṛttakriyaśca pratiṣidhyate iti dhvanimārabhamāṇa eva pratiṣidhyate mā śabdaṃ kārṣīr iti / nanu ca dhavaniḥ śabdaguṇaḥ / evaṃ hy uktam -- 
sphoṭaḥ śabdo dhvanistasya vāyāmādupajāyate // iti //
ucyate / dravyādayo na bhavanti śabdāḥ, evaṃparāyāāṃ coddanāyāṃ dhvaniśabdayoranyatve prayojanābhāvāt ekatvena vyapadeśaḥ / itaraḥ na cet prasiddhyuparodho ya eva laukikaḥ śabdo 'sāvevāśrīyate / tasya cedamanuśāsanaṃ śāstram // 

[3] śāstre ca yā pravṛttiḥ adhyayanaṃ prati sā satyarthitve / arthitvaṃ ca satsu prayojaneṣu / na cedaṃ nityakarmāvaśyaṃ kartavyam / nitye karmaṇyapravṛttāvadharmaḥ / anityeṣu kāmacāraḥ / yadi ca payojanāni asya na bhaviṣyanti [na] tataḥ pravartiṣyata iti pṛcchati -- kāni punarasya śabdānuśāsanasya prayojanāni / rakṣohāgamaladhvasaṃdehāḥ prayojanam / 

[3.1] sato 'rthasyāpāyaparihāro rakṣā nāma / lopāgamavarṇavikārajño hi samyak vedānanupālayiṣyatīti / lopodāharaṇam / pīvopavasanādīnāṃ chandasi lopaḥ / pīvopavasanānāṃ payopavasanānām iti / āgamaḥ / nīcāyā duk chandasi nīcāduccā iti / tathā ktvo yak / saubhāgyamasyai dattvāyāthāstaṃ vi paretana / vikāraḥ / hṛgrahorbhaḥ chandasi / tat kanyāsu sukanye kim iti māṃ jīrṇimanarthyaṃ jarbhurāṇaṃ kṛtyārūpaṃ kāmayase / tathā udgrābhaṃ ca nigrābhaṃ ca brahma devā avīvṛdhan / madhorgṛhṇāti / kvacit tvavikāraḥ / āha / syādīnāṃ vyatyayastato bāhulakena kṛtsasādhutvaḥ / yathā triṣṭubhaujaḥ śubhitamugravīram / suhitam iti prāpnoti / nanu ceha lopāgamavarṇavikārāḥ kṛtsnāmnāyagranthasya tathābhūtasya pāṭhāt jñātā eva / ucyate / prayoktradhīnatvāt prayogasya / prayoktā ca pramādāt karṇadoṣāt vismaraṇādvānyathoccārayet saṃdihyeta vā / tajjñāścāsamnihitāḥ / santi cānyaśāstrajñāḥ / vedādanyaduccārayan mliṣṭamuccārayet / ato 'smin sthityantare sati naiva kaścidupaśliṣyet / ātmasaṃskārārthaṃ cādhyeyaṃ vyākaraṇam / ataśca samyaggrahaṇam / kṛtātmasamskāro hi samyagvedaparipālane samarthaḥ / 

[3.2.1] ūhaḥ khalvapi / prakṛtau samarthānāṃ vikāreṣu sāmarthyābhāvaḥ / prakṛtirupādānārthe prasakte śabdaliṅgavacanāntareṇa vikriyate / tadyathā / prakṛtāvagnaya  iti agniśabdaḥ samartho dṛṣṭaḥ / tatra vikāreṣu ūhaḥ / ekadeśāpādāne nivṛttau adhikṛtāyāṃ mukhyavṛttivyatikrameṇa vārthāntaropādāne prasaktiḥ / arthāntareṣvarthāntaravṛtterupādānaṃ kriyate / tatra yadi vikārastūṣṇīmupāṃśu kriyate śabdavatī prakṛtiḥ aśabdo vikāraḥ syāt / athāpyagniśabda evopādīyeta / mukhyā vṛttiḥ sūryārthaṃ na pratipādayet / athāpyasya guṇādarthāntare mūrye mukhyavṛttivyatikrameṇa prayoga āśrīyeta, prakṛtiviparītaḥ śabdapravṛttidharmaḥ āśritaḥ syāt / tasmād atra vākyāntarametadvibhaktikamupādīyate / 

[3.2.2] āśrayaliṅgavacanabhedena bhavatyuhaḥ / tadyathā / devīrāpaḥ śuddhā yūyam / deva ājya śuddhaṃ tvam / śuddhamityetat prathamāntaṃ avyatiriktaprātipadikārthaṃ yadāmantritāntam iti / vibhaktyantarāṇāmapyāyurāśāste iti āyurāśāsāte [iti] āyurāśāsate iti / yeṣāṃ tu prakṛtārthe vidhinā sāmarthyaṃ nāsti tadyathā vāyavaḥ sthopāyavaḥ stheti prakṛtau eva bahuvacane vatso vidhīyate tatrāsāmarthyāt vikṛtāvūho na bhavati / tathaikasminneva prakṛ´tipāśe pāśāniti bahuvacanāntaṃ śrūyate, aditiḥ pāśān pramumoktviti / tatrāpi vikṛtāvūho nāsti / vājasaneyināṃ tvekavacanāntaḥ paṭhyate, aditiḥ pāśamiti / teṣāmūhaḥ prāpnoti yadi tatrāstyaditiḥ / atha vā pāśeṣu noha iti anena tu naigamavibhāṣā / bahuvacane sati yatheṣṭaṃ prayogo bhavati / jūrasi dhṛtā manasā juṣṭā viṣṇave tasyāste satyasavasa iti evaṃ vede strīliṅgena paṭhitaḥ / tasmādā[tasmād yadā] sādyaskreṣu sāṇḍastrivatsaḥ somakrayaṇo bhavati tadā stryarthavṛttitāmanapekṣyaiva padānāṃ pumarthābhidhāyinamadṛṣṭaṃ vede puṃśabdamūhante / jūrasi dhṛto manasā juṣṭo viṣṇave tasya te satyasavasa iti / tathā rājakrayaṇīsaṃstave cidasi manāsi dhīrasi dakṣiṇāsi yajñiyāsi kṣatriyāsyaditirasyubhayataḥśīrṣṇī sā naḥ suprācī supratīcī bhava sā devi devamacchehīti strīliṅgena paṭhitaḥ / sādyaskreṣu puṃlliṅgenohyate / cidasi mano 'si dhīrasi dakṣiṇo 'si yajñiyo 'si kṣakriyo 'syubhayataḥśīrṣā sa naḥ suprāksupratyag bhava sa deva devamacchehīti / vājaameyinastathaivainamūhante / tathā somakrayaṇyānayanamantre strīliṅgāni padāni vasvyasi rudrāsi aditirasyādityāsi candrāsīti / eteṣāṃ sādyaskreṣūho bhavati / vasurasi rudro 'si adhitirasi ādityo 'si candro 'sīti / eteṣāṃ sādyaskreṣūho bhavati / vasurasi rudro 'si aditirasi ādityo 'si candro 'sīti / tathā paśuprakṛtau puṃlliṅgena mantraḥ paṭhataḥ / dāru cāsmin pratimuñcati / tatra yadā mūrdhanyasyā ālabhyate tadā strīpratyayamūhante / tā asyai prativedayeti / tathā paśāveva huto yāhi pathibhirdevayānair iti, ūhe hutā yāhi pathibhirdevayānair iti / 

[3.2.3] ūhyamānānāṃ cāmantratvānmantre ghasahvaretyevamādi na bhavatīti eke manyante / kathaṃ tarhyaghastāmiti / tathā paṭhitatvādadoṣaḥ / indrāgnibhyāṃ chāgenāghastāṃ taṃ medastaḥ pratipacatāgrabhīṣṭāmavīvṛdhetāṃ puroḍāśeneti / tasmādakṣannityūho māstvamānuṣatvādityeke manyante / mantrāntarāṇyevaitānyūhaviṣayāṇītyapare manyante / aghasadaghasatāmaghasannagrabhīṣurakṣannityūhaprakaraṇe paṭhyate / tathā āmnāya eva / gharmasya tapasā tapyadhvam iti tapyasvatapyethāmiti kvacidūho nirdiśyate / śākhāntareṣu ca bahava ūhitavyā bhāvyante / evamidamūhyamidamanūhyam iti nyāyādfavasthite liṅgavacanavibhaktīnāṃ samyagivaniyoge vyākaraṇasya vyāpāraḥ / 

[3.2.4] aṅgāni jñātināmānyupamā cendriyāṇi ca / 
etāni nohaṃ gacchanti adhrigau viṣamaṃ hi tat //
adhrigostvanyatra / praśasā bāhū śalā doṣaṇī kaśyapevāṃsācchidre śroṇī kavaṣorūsrekaparṇāṣṭhīvantā / tadāvyayamanekasmin paśau dvirabhyasyate / aṅgāni / yatpaśurmāyumakṛtoro vā padbhirāhata / agnirmā tasmādenaso viśvānmuñcatvaṃhasa iti / yat paśū māyumakṛṣātāmuro vā padbhirāhasātāṃ yatpaśavo māyumakṛṣatoro vā padbhirāhasateti / jñātināmāni / anvenaṃ mātā manyatāmanu pitānu bhrātā sagarbhyo 'nu askhā sayūthya iti / upamā / kaśyapevāṃsācchidre śroṇī kavaṣorū / śyenamasya vakṣaḥ kṛṇutāditi / indriyāṇi / sūryaṃ cakṣurgamayatādvātaṃ prāṇamanvavasṛjatādantarikṣamasuṃ diśaḥ śrotraṃ pṛthivīṃ śarīram / ekadhāsya tvacamāchyatāt purā nābhyā apiśaso vapāmutkhidatādantarevoṣmāṇaṃ vārayadhvāt iti / tatrohaprakaraṇa evaiṣāṃ mātā pitā bhrātā sanābhisaṃsargiśabdā ityevamādīnyanūhyānīti paṭhyate / tathā aṅgānyapi pāṇipādaṃ śirogrīvaṃ sapta prāṇā ityevamādīni parigaṇitāni kaiścit / atha aghriguḥ / daivyāḥ śamitāra uta manuṣyā ārabhadhvam / upanayata medhyāduraḥ / āśāsānā medhapatibhyāṃ medham / prāsmā agniṃ bharata / stṛṇīta barhiḥ / anvenaṃ mātā manyatām / anu pitānu bhrātā sagarbhyo 'nu sakhā sayūthyaḥ / udīcīnāṃ asya pado nidhattāt / sūryaṃ cakṣurgamayatāt / vātaṃ prāṇamanvavasṛjatāt / antarikṣamasum / diśaḥ śrotram / pṛthivī śarīram / ekadhāsya tvacamāchyatāt / purā nābhyā apiśaso vapāmutkhidatā / antarevoṣmāṇaṃ vārayadhvāt / śyenamasya vakṣaḥ kṛṇutāt / praśasā bāhū / śalā doṣaṇī / kaśyapevāṃsācchidre śroṇī / kavaṣorū srekaparṇā aṣṭhīvantā / ṣaḍviṃśatirasya vaṅkrayastā anuṣṭhyoccyāvayatāt / gātraṃ gātramasyānūnaṃ kṛṇutāt / ūvadhyagohaṃ pārthivaṃ khanatāt / asnā rakṣaḥ saṃsajatāt / vaniṣṭumasya mā rāviṣṭorūkaṃ manyamānāḥ / nedvastoke tanaye / ravitā ravacchamitāraḥ / adhrigo śamīdhvam suśabhi śamīdhvam / śamīdhvamadhrigo / 
adhriguśca vipāpaśca devānāṃ śamitārau / 
tā enaṃ pravidvāṃsau śrapayataṃ yathāsya śrapaṇaṃ tathā //
iti ūhante / uruprathā uru prathasvoru te yajñapatiḥ prathatāmiti / puroḍāśaprathanaparatvāt saptadaśasvapi yajamāneṣu yajñapatayaḥ prathantāmiti ūho na bhavati / tatretikartavyatohe gītyūhe ca nāsti vyākaraṇasya pravṛttiḥ / lokato lakṣaṇāntarāccsa prātiśākhyāditastau vyavatiṣṭhete / śabdaviṣaye tūhe yāvān vyākaraṇasya vyāpāraḥ tatra nidarśanamātramabhihitam / prakṛtivikārabhāvaś ca prasiddho yatra sa eva viṣaya ūhasya na tu yo dustyajo durmatibhirmatastadabhidhānamātreṇa [sa] bhavatyūhaḥ / tataścobhayeṣāṃ tvā devamanuṣyāṇāṃ priyaṃ karomīti / yadi prakṛtikṛtau kvacidāmnāto naivāyamūhasya viṣayaḥ / yajñaviṣaye hi kāścit prakṛtayaḥ kāścid vikṛtayaḥ kāścit prakṛtayo vikṛtayaśca kāścinna prakṛtayo na vikṛtayaḥ / sarvasomaprakṛtiragniṣṭomaḥ / darśapūrṇamāsayośca sarveṣṭiprakṛtitvam / 

[3.3] āgamaḥ khalvapi / pāramparyeṇāvicchinna upadeśa āgamaḥ / śrutilakṣaṇaḥ smṛtilakṣaṇaśca / kaḥ punarasāvāgamaḥ / brāhmaṇena niṣkāraṇo dharmaḥ ṣaḍaṅgo vedo 'dhyeyaḥ / niṣkāraṇa iti kāraṇamanuddiśya yathā nityāni karmāṇi kartavyāni ityakaraṇe teṣāṃ pratyavāyaḥ / yāni tvānityāni karmāṇi tadyathā kāmyāni putrakāmo yajeteti annādyakāmo yajeteti teṣāmakaraṇe nāsti pratyavāyaḥ / arthitve sati pravṛtteḥ / idaṃ tu vyākaraṇādhyayanaṃ nityaṃ karmāvaśyakartavyamupanayanādivat ityetat pradarśyate / 

[3.4] brāhmaṇena śabdā jñeyāḥ / adhyāpanaṃ brāhmaṇaya vṛttiḥ / apaśabdajñaśca viśliṣṭavāktve sati agṛhītavākyatvānnopagamyata iti śabdā jñātavyāḥ / teṣāṃ ca jñāne vyākaraṇādanyo laghurupāyo nāsti / upāyo nāma kāryanirvṛttiṃ prati yaḥ kāraṇānāṃ vinyāsaviśeṣaḥ / tasmād vyākaraṇaṃ laghurupāyaḥ śabdajñānaṃ prati / anya upāya eva na sambhavati / tasmādadhyeyaṃ vyākaraṇam / atha vā acirakālā saṃpratipattirlāghavam / tasyā vyākaraṇamupāyaḥ / 

[3.5]	 yājñikāḥ paṭhanti / sthūlapṛṣatīmāgnivāruṇīmanaḍvāhīmālabheteti / pṛṣatī kṣudrapṛṣatī sthūlapṛṣatī [tā] maitrāvaruṇya iti vede paṭhyate / tatra yaḥ pṛṣatīsaṃnidhāne sthūlaśabdaḥ pṛṣatāṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ na pratipadyate / pṛṣadviśeṣaṇe hi sati vyaktamanyapadārthatvam / svarabhedādarthabhedaṃ vaiyākaraṇāḥ pratipadyante / tasmiṃśca karmaṇi yasyāḥ sthūlāni pṛṣanti labhyante tatra vaiyākaraṇistvasaṃdehena pūrvapadaprakṛtisvareṇa bahuvrīhirevātra niścita iti //

[4] imāni ca bhūyaḥ śabdānuśāsanasya prayojanāni / bhūyaḥśabdaḥ punararthaḥ / imāni punaraparāṇīty arthaḥ //

[4.1]	 te 'surā helayo helaya iti kurvantaḥ parābabhūvuḥ / 

[4.1.1] ayam arthavādo mlecchanasya nivṛttyartha upādīyate / arthavādastu pravartako nivartako vā / tatrāyaṃ pravartako vādaḥ / sarvā vā imā diśaḥ paśuyājyabhijayati sarvāṃllokān sarvā evāsyemā diśo 'bhijitā bhavanti sarve lokāḥ / nivartako -- na dato gamayed yaddato gamayet sarpā enaṃ ghātukāḥ syuḥ sarpāneva śamayatyahiṃsāyai / yasmātta evaṃ kurvantaḥ parābhūtāstasmād brāhmaṇena na mlecchitavai na mlecchitavyam / kṛtyārthe tavai iti / kiṃ punarmlecchanam iti / ata āha -- nāpabhāṣitavai / apabhāṣaṇaṃ mlecchanam / kiṃ punaratrāsurairapabhāṣitam / tatra kecidāhuḥ / haiheprayoge haihayoriti pluto na kṛta iti / apare bruvate kṛtaḥ plutaḥ kiṃ tu svarasaṃdhiḥ kṛto na prakṛtibhāva iti / apara āha / he 'layo he 'laya iti dvirvacanaṃ kṛtam iti / apare lepurlakārādirayaṃ na bhavati diṣṭāprayuktatvātteṣāṃ tatra latvamapalāpanam iti / atha kimarthamevamupasaṃharati mlecchā mā bhūmeti na punarmāparābhūmeti / ucyate / uktameva arthavādo 'yam, amlecchanaṃ vidhīyamānam iti / 

[4.1.2] apare varṇayanti yasmāddhetoḥ teṣāṃ parābhavastasya tyāgādaparābhavo 'pyukta iti / kaḥ punarasurāṇāṃ parābhavaḥ / pratyavāyayogaḥ / yadyogādadyāpi nottiṣṭhanti / yairapi putrapautrairasurāṇāṃ na prayuktaste 'pi parābhūtāḥ / tasmādyathā asurā evaṃ vayamapi mā parābhūmetyadhyeyaṃ vyākaraṇam //

[4.2] duṣṭaḥ śabdaḥ svarato varṇato vā / ādyāditvāttasiḥ / svaravarṇābhyāṃ duṣṭaḥ / yaścābhyāṃ duṣṭaḥ sa ucyate mithyāprayukta iti / sa evaṃbhūto na tadarthamāha / sa tadarthamanabhidadhad vāgvajro yajamānaṃ hinasti / vāg vajro vāgvajraḥ / asāvenaso yogāt tena cainasā prayukto 'mutra pratyavāyāddhiṃsito bhavati / yajamāno hījyāyāṃ pradhānam / atha vā ṝtvijo hiṃsāyāmayam api hiṃsitaḥ / yathendraśatruḥ / yathāyamindraśatruryajamānaṃ hiṃsitavān evamanyo 'pi mithyāprayukto mā hiṃsīdityadhyeyaṃ vyākaraṇam / duṣṭaśabdaprayoga iti śrūyate / svāhendraśatrurvardhasveti mantraḥ kartavyaḥ / ādyudāttaḥ kṛtaḥ / tato 'syendra eva śātayitā saṃvṛtta iti / atra codyate / yadyapi samāsāntodāttatvamevamapi nāyamartho vyāvartata iti / indraḥ śatrurasyeti / yathā somasakho rājasakha iti / idaṃ cātra vaktavyam / indraśatro vardhasveti / kasmādāmantrite prathamā na bhavati / yattāvaduktamantodāttatve 'pi na tasyārthasya vyucchittiriti / naipa doṣaḥ / yo 'yaṃ śatruśabdo mantre prayukta eṣa rūḍhiśabdo 'pi sannarthaprakaraṇavaśād hyuktanirūḍhiṃ nimittamapekṣamāṇaḥ prayuktaḥ / tasmānnāyamevāśrīyata indrasyāmitra indro vāmitro 'syeti / kathaṃ tarhi / kriyāmeva nimittatvenopādāya prayuktaḥ śātayitā śamayitā vā śatruriti / tatrendrasya śātayitā bhaveti prayuyukṣita indraḥ śātayitāsyeti prayuktam / yacco
ktamāmantritavibhaktyā bhavitavyam iti / yo 'rthaḥ svena dharmeṇa prasiddho dharmāntarasaṃbandhaṃ prati abhimukhīkriyate tatrāmantritavibhaktiḥ / yathā devadattaṃ dharmāntarasaṃbandhaṃ pratyabhimukhīkaroti devadattādhīṣva bhuṅkṣveti / yasya cādhīyamāna evāsau dharmastasya kuta āmantritavibhaktiḥ / tadyathā rājā bhava yudhyasva puruṣo bhaveti rājatvamevādhīyamānaṃ puruṣatvaṃ ceti nāstyāmantritavibhaktiḥ / evamihāpīndraśatrutvamevādhīyamānamityadoṣaḥ / tatra pūrvasmin varṇadoṣaḥ pradarśito he 'layo he 'laya iti / saṃprati svaradoṣaḥ // 

[4.3]	 yadadhītamavijñātaṃ nigadenaiva śabdyata iti / anenaitadākhyāyate / ya evāsau vedastasyaiva vyākaraṇānugatasya yadi saṃśabdanamapyetadabhyudayāyeti / tatra niruktakāro 'vijñātārtham iti varṇayati / iha tu svaravarṇasaṃskārāvijñātamavijñātam iti parigṛhyate / yaccaivaṃbhūtaṃ tannigadenaiva śabdyate / ācāryopadeśenaiva kevalenetyarthaḥ / anagnāviva śuṣkaidhaḥ / yathā jvalanasamarthamapi sat kāṣṭhamagninānupagṛhītaṃ na jvalanasamarthaṃ niṣprayojanamevamadhītam api śāstraṃ vyākaraṇenānupagṛhītamasamarthaṃ phalaniṣpattiṃ prati / tasmādanarthakaṃ mādhigīṣmahīti niṣprayojanaṃ mādhigīṣmahītyarthaḥ / 

[4.4] yastu prayuṅkte iti // vyākaraṇāt pratyavāyenāyoga iṣṭena ca phalena saṃbandhaḥ ity etadākhyāyate / prayuṅkte prerayati / kuśalo vidvān / viśeṣe / sa eva śabdo 'rthaviśeṣe kasmiṃścitsādhuḥ kasmiṃścidasādhurityetadākhyāyate / yathā goṇīśabdaḥ sāsnādimatyasādhuḥ / tathāśvaśabdaḥ kesarādimati sādhurna niḥsva iti / asva iti nirdhane sādhurnaikaśaphādilakṣaṇe / yadi tu goṇīśabdo 'pi nimittāntarāt sāsnādimiti prayujyeta goṇīva goṇīti sādhureva syāt / aśve vā asvaśabdaṃ dhanābhāvadvārakaṃ prayuñjīta sa sādhureva / atha vā kuśalo 'viśeṣe iti / aviśeṣe 'pi śāstrasya tata āgata iti tena khanatīti sādhureva viṣeṣa eva prayujyate na sarvatra / vṛkṣamūlādāgataḥ aṅgulyā khanatīti / anyeṣāṃ granthaḥ / kuśalo viśeṣair iti / viśeṣaiḥ śāstrapratipāditairityarthaḥ / yathā tadadhīte tadvedetyanena / vyavahāro vṛttiḥ śiṣṭavyavahāro yājyaiva karmavidhiḥ / kaḥ? vāgyogavideva / anyasyāśrutatvādāha kaḥ ? vāgyogavideveti / evaṃ manyate / yasminnarthe āsevyamāne dharmaḥ / tasmādyo viparītaḥ tadāsevāyāṃ tadviparītena phalena bhavitavyam / yathā brāhmaṇasya sukhaṃ kṛtvā dharmaḥ duḥkhaṃ kṛtvā adharmaḥ / evaṃ vāgyogavidyasmācchabdāñjānāti tasmād dharmaḥ / śabdajñānaṃ cāpaśabdajñānānuṣaktamevetyāha yo hi śabdān jānāti / arthopapattimāśrityaivaṃ codyate na śabdavyāpāram / bhūyāṃso hyapaśabdāḥ alpīyāṃsaḥ śabdā iti / kathaṃ prakarṣapratyayaḥ? ucyate / paramatāpekṣayā parikalpyaṃ samānaguṇatvam / katham? evaṃ tvaṃ manyethāḥ / yady api vāgyogavidubhayaṃ jānāti tathāpi bahavaḥ śabdās teṣāṃ bahutvāt alpā apaśabdāḥ tadalpatvād alpaḥ pratyavāya iti / etac cāpi na / alpīyāṃsaḥ śabdā iti / apabhraṃśā ye [te] tv apy apratyāyakā bhavanti / eṣa eva pakṣo naite 'rthaṃ pratyāyayantīti / ajñānaṃ tasya śaraṇam / kāmam asau dharmeṇa na yujyeta pratyavāyena tu tasyāyogaḥ / tathā tiryagādayaḥ ajñānāt pravartamānā na pratyavāyena yujyante / idaṃ sarvam evam upanyastaṃ jñāne dharmaḥ / prayogas tu jñānasyaivānumānam iti / nātyantāyājñānaṃ śraṇaṃ bhavitum arhati / na hy ajñānādviṣayaṃ bhakṣitaṃ maraṇāya na prakalpate / tiryagādiṣv evājñānaṃ śaraṇam / na mlecchādīnāṃ brahmahatyādiṣu pravartamānānām iti / evaṃ tarhīti / etad ācaṣṭe / pratyāsatteḥ sāmarthyaṃ balavad iti avāgyogavid abhisaṃbadhyate / tatra vāgyogavit śabdāpaśabdajñaḥ śabdān prayokṣyate nāpaśabdān / prayogāccābhyudayaḥ pratyavāyo vā, na jñānād iti ayaṃ na śrutāvupanibaddho nāpi smṛtāv ity āha -- kva punar idaṃ paṭhitam iti / bhrājā nāma ślokāḥ / asyābhiprāyaḥ / yo vārthaḥ sa smṛtāv āmnātaḥ tataḥ pramāṇaṃ bhavitum arhatīti / kiṃ ca bhoḥ ślokā api vai pramāṇam / nāyam ekāntena ślokānāmapramāṇyam ācaṣṭe / bhavanti hi ślokāḥ smṛtyupanibaddhāḥ pramāṇam / evaṃ bhūtās tv apramāṇam / evaṃbhūtānāṃ prāmāṇye sati yaḥ kaścit pramāṇaṃ bhavitum arhati -- yadudumbaravarṇānām iti / ayaṃ hi ślokaḥ sautrāmaṇyā asāmarthyamācaṣṭe / tasmāt sautrāmaṇīṃ hitvā pānāgāraśālāyām eva madyaṃ pātavyam / pramattagīta eṣa tatrabhavataḥ asamāhitacetasa ity arthaḥ / kathaṃ jñāyate / śrutismṛtivipratiṣedhāt / ayaṃ tvapramattagītaḥ / asti hi smṛtiḥ ekaḥ śabdaḥ samyagjñātaḥ suprayuktaḥ śāstrānvitaḥ svarge loke kāmadhugbhavatīti / 

[4.5]	 avidvāṃsaḥ pratyabhivāde ity anenaitat kathayati / vyākaraṇam antareṇa śiṣṭebhyaḥ pṛthakkṛtāḥ strīpakṣe bhavanti na cābhivādayitāraṃ śaknuvantyanugrahītum / pratyabhivādena hy asau saṃskṛto dharmabhāgbhavati svayaṃ cābhyudayena yujyate / 

[4.6] prayājāḥ savibhaktikā iti / prayājamantrā ity arthaḥ / yāstāvat prayājaśabdeṣu pratipadaṃ vibhaktayo na tābhiḥ savibhaktikś ity ucyante, nityaṃ saṃnidhānāttāsām / tasmādāmnātādanyacchabdarūpaṃ prajājeṣūpādīyate / kevalāyāśca vibhakter aprayogāt prātipadikaṃ prakṛtir arthagṛhītā / tatrāpi tvamagne prayājānuyājānāṃ purastāt tvaṃ paścād iti darśanād arthāntaravṛtti śrutyantaraṃ nopādīyate / vibhaktīnām api sarvāsāṃ prayoge prāpte yā dvyakṣarā vā satyaścaturakṣarā vā bhavantīti vacanād agnināgnineti na prayujyate / tathā na śabdajāmi kuryāt / śabdajāmi hi tad bhavati yat pañcamyantam / tasmādagneragner ity anena rūpeṇa ṣaṣṭhyantaṃ prayujyate / punarādheye prayājānuyājānāṃ purastādvopariṣṭād [vā] vibhaktīḥ kuryāt / narāṃśaso agnimagnim iti vā ubhayathā dṛṣṭatvāt / samidhaḥ samidho 'gne 'gna iti saṃdhirdṛṣṭaḥ / il@o agnināgna iti vivṛttir dṛṣṭeti bahvṛcasūtrabhāṣye / āśvalāyanasūtre ye yajāmahe samidhaḥ samidho agnim agna ājyasya hiviṣo vetu / il@o 'gnināgna ājyasya hiviṣo vyantu / bahirragniragna ājyasya haviṣo vetu / āpastambasūtre agnāgne 'gnāv agne 'gnināgne 'gnim agna iti caturṣu prayājeṣv iti vibhaktaya uktāḥ / agnā iti yathāmnāt yathāmnāt uttamaḥ prayājaḥ / tasyopariṣṭāt [na] kriyate vibhaktiḥ / vikṛtāvapyavibhaktiḥ kriyate tadā / tadyathā / ye yajāmahe svāhāgniṃ svāhā somam / agnihotraṃ svāhā agnīṣomau svāhā devā ājyapā juṣaṇā agna ājyasya haviṣo vyantu iti / eteṣu prayājeṣu savibhaktikeṣu kartavyeṣu vaiyākaraṇo vibhaktyantān samyaguccārayet / tasmādevamarthamadhyeyaṃ vyākaraṇam //

[4.7]	 sa ārtvijīna iti / yaśca ṛtvijamarhati yaś ca ṛtvikkarmobhāvapyārtvijīnau vidvāneva yājayatīti / [na] avidvāṃso 'nadhikārāt / brāhmaṇasya vyākaraṇamantareṇa yajamānatvaṃ yājakatvaṃ vā na saṃbhavatīti protsāhyate //

[4.8.1] catvāri padajātāni yāni tāni asya śṛṅgāṇīva / karmapravacanīyā nipāteṣv antarbhūtā iti catvāry ucyante / anyeṣāṃ dve padajāte nāmākhyātaṃ ca / trayaḥ kālāḥ abhidhāyāḥ śabdasya prayujyate prayokṣyate prāyojīti / abhidhānasya vā bhavati bhaviṣyati abhūd iti / dvau śabdātmanau nityaḥ kāryaśceti / kaiścinnityo 'yam iti dṛṣṭaḥ kaiścidanitya iti / atha vā jātir vyaktiśceti / atha vā sphoṭo dhvaniśca / saptavibhaktayaḥ svādayaḥ / atha vā saha śeṣeṇa kārakāṇi sapta vibhaktayo na tu kārakaḥ śeṣe 'py aṣṭamaḥ saṃbhavati / 

[4.8.2] na iṅgayantīti / na duryanti / na nimiṣanti na ceṣṭanta iti / sāyujyādimabhedenopādānam / manīṣiṇo ye taiḥ saha sāmyaṃ gacchemeti / te 'pi hi śāstrādevemāṃ kāṣṭhāṃ prāptāḥ //

[4.9]	 paśyannapi na paśyati / śṛṇvannapi na śṛṇoti / vyākaraṇādhyāyino vāca īśate prabhutvaṃ yeṣāṃ vāci bhavatīti //

[4.10] saktumiva titaunā /  sādhyaṃ yad dravyaṃ duḥśodhyaṃ tasyopalakṣaṇaṃ saktavaḥ / evamapaśabdāḥ śaktyā vyākaraṇena śodhyante iti / atrā sakhāyāḥ / ṛci tunudhamakṣviti dīrghaḥ / atra sakhāyaḥ sakhyāni samānakhyātayaḥ samānaprajñānāḥ / 

[4.11] āhitāgnirapaśabdaṃ prayujyeti / etat pratipādayati / yathāgnimādadhadavaśyaṃ jāyāmānayati tāmantareṇāgnyādhānasyāpravṛtteḥ, evamanenāgnimādadhātvāvaśyaṃ vyākaraṇamadhyeyam iti nityakarmatām eva samarthayate //

[4.12] daśamyāṃ putrasya nāma vidadhyāt iti / etaduktaṃ bhavati / nāmakaraṇasaṃskārādārabhya vyākaraṇam upayujyata iti //

[4.13] sudevo asi varuṇa / bārhaspatye sūkte pragāthe varuṇopastutiriyam / varuṇasya ceyaṃ stutiḥ tvaṃ satyadeva ityeṣā yasmād vyākaraṇajñānādataḥ paṭhitavyam iti //

[5] kiṃ punar idam iti / nātra śiṣyācāryācāryadeśīyāḥ pratipattavyāḥ / granthakāra evemaṃ granthaṃ vibhajate na punaranyad api kiṃcit / sati hi prayojanākhyāne evaṃ pratipadyeta protsāhye 'ham anena prakāreṇa / naitāni santi prayojanāni / atha vā vyākhyāyate kim iti niṣprayojanaṃ śramakaramadhyeṣye iti naivādhīyīta / om ity uktvā / [na] oṃkāramuccāryetyevagantavyam / kiṃ tarhi ? evam ity abhyupagamya anapekṣitaprayojanā evetyarthaḥ / vṛttāntata iti / vṛttāntāḥ prapāṭhakā ucyante / tatrādyāditvāttasiḥ / vṛttāntaiḥ vṛttāntaiḥ prapāṭhakaiḥ paṭhanti / anyeṣāṃ vṛttāntaśa iti pāṭhaḥ / ta evaṃ paṭhanti / vṛttāntaṃ vṛttāntaṃ paṭhantīti vṛttāntaśaḥ paṭhantīti / purākalpa etadāsīt / etadācaṣṭe / yasyām avasthāyām asau vyākaraṇamadhyāpyate tasmām asau paripraśnāsamartha eva bhavati / kutaḥ prayojanāni pravakṣyati / tatra sthānakaraṇānupradānajñebhya iti / purākalpe sthānakaraṇādīn vyākaraṇād eva pratipadyate / tato vaidikāḥ śabdā upadiśyante / tatra vedādhyayane prayojanapraśnaḥ syāt / tasyāpi prayojanamākhyāyate, vedāṅgam api vyākaraṇamavaśyamadhyetavyam iti / tadadyatve na tathā /  adyatva iti nipāto 'smin kāla ity anena samānārthaḥ adyatve hi bālā evānadhigatavyākaraṇā vedamadhyāpyante / vedo avijñātasthānakaraṇānupradāna eva / vedam adhītya tvaritā vaktāro bhavanti / tvaritā iti / dhanopādāne vā turitā bhavanti bhogopabhoge vā / atha vā punaradhyāyāntare pāratantryakaraṇo niyogo mā bhūd iti tvaritā idam āhur vedādhyayanasamakālameveti //

[6.1]	 uktaḥ śabdaḥ / svabhāvo 'py asya vyākhyātaḥ dhavaniḥ śabda iti / rakṣādīny api prayojanānyuktāni / śabdānuśāsanam idānīṃ kartavyam ākhyātavyaṃ prastotavyam ity arthaḥ / tat kathaṃ kartavyam ityākhyātavyaṃ nirdarśayati / ya ācaṣṭe tena kathaṃ kartavyam iti / kiṃ śabdopadeśo 'paśabdopadeśa ubhayaśabdopadeśa iti / svalakṣaṇena paralakṣaṇābhāvena yadākhyāyate tadvispaṣṭataraṃ bhavatīty ubhayopadeśakaraṇasyopādānam / yady api vispaṣṭataraṃ bhavati atigauravaṃ tu śāstrasyeti svalakṣaṇenaivānuśāsanaṃ parigṛhṇāti / anyataropādānena kṛtī śabdāpaśabdayor ity arthaḥ / yadi hy ubhayopadeśo 'pi parigṛhītaḥ syāt tamameva kuryāt anyatamopadeśeneti / tadyathā / bhakṣyaniyamenābhakṣyapratiṣedho gamyate / bhakṣyābhakṣyaprakaraṇe idaṃ śrūyate pañca pañcanakhā bhakṣyā iti / prakaraṇād anyasya pañcanakhasya nivṛttiḥ / atha vārthitvād bubhukṣāprayuktaṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ sarvaviṣayam aśabdakaṃ pravṛttam / tatredaṃ vacanaṃ pañca pañcanakhā bhakṣyā iti / tadyadyanena pañca pañcanakhā bhakṣyā ity etat pratipādyeta yadahareva na bhakṣayati tadaharevāyaṃ vihitākaraṇāt pratyaveyāt / ye ca śalyakādibhyo 'nye pañcanakhāsteṣāṃ ca bhakṣaṇaṃ hyanimittaprayuktam avyāvṛttam eva syāt / tad eva bhakṣaṇaṃ paurastyam arthitve sati pravṛttaṃ tadeva pañcasu pañcanakheṣūpadiśyate / kṣutpratīghāta etair eva kartavyo nānyair iti / iyati śabdavyāpāre pañcanakhāntarāṇāṃ nivṛttiḥ / asau na śabdavatī / kiṃ tarhi ? sāmarthyalakṣaṇā / evaṃ ca sati vrata ityetasmin sūtre aśrāddhabhojītyatra yaducyate yadi yo 'śrāddhaṃ bhuṅkte 'sāvaśrāddhabhojī yadaharevāsāvaśrāddhaṃ na bhuṅkte tadāsya vratalopa it atrāpi śrāddhabhojanam arthāt prasaktam / tata idam ucyate śrāddhaṃ na bhoktavyam iti / paurastyasyaiva śrāddhabhojanasyārthāt prasaktasya nivṛttiḥ kriyate / bhuñjānaḥ śrāddhaṃ mā bhuṅkṣveti / paurastyasyaiva śrāddhabhojanasya vidhānasāmarthyāt vinivartamānaṃ śrāddhabhojanam iti kuto vratalopaḥ / evamarthapratyāyanaṃ prati śabdāpaśabdayoḥ pravṛttau satyāṃ śabdopadeśenāpaśabdanivṛttir iti vyaktam / abhakṣyapratiṣedhena vā bhakṣyaniyamaḥ / abhakṣyo grāmyakukkuṭaḥ ityarthamukhyaḥ pratiṣedha iti / na hy atra prāptyanumānabādhā / kiṃ tarhi ? bādhaiveyam / evam ihāpy apaśabdopadeśena gāvyādibhyaścānyaḥ śabdo gaur iti pratipadyate / kiṃ punar atra jyāyaḥ / ubhayopadeśāt guror laghutvāt praśasyatvam anubhūya mithaḥspardhāyāṃ prakarṣapratyayena saṃbadhyate / iṣṭānvākhyānaṃ khalv api bhavati / kathaṃ śabdopadeśa iṣṭānvākhyānam / ucyate / yathāmnāye śabdānām sa dharma evam eṣām api dharmārthatvād idam ucyata iṣṭānvākhyānam iti / atha vāpaśabdeṣūpadiśyamāneṣu ānumānikī pratipattir ayaṃ śabdas te ityānumānikyāṃ ca pratipattau parikalpasyānantyāt kiṃ gaur ity eṣa gāvyādibhyo 'nyaḥ śabdaḥ āhosvid anya ity etat durjñānam eva bhavatīti naivedam anvākhyānaṃ syād iti / 

[6.2] athaitasmin śabdopadeśe sati / asmin kāle pratipadapāṭham eva sukaraṃ manyante / na hy atra kiṃcit smartavyaṃ lakṣaṇam / kevalaṃ śabdā evoccārayitavyāḥ / prakṛtyādyupadeśe hi sati cirakālasaṃpratipattir iti / pratipadapāṭha iti / svarūpeṇa yaduccāraṇaṃ sa pratipadapāṭhaḥ / yenārthaḥ paricchidyate tat padam / anabhyupāya iti / upāyaśabdaparyāyavācyevāyam abhyupāyaśabdaḥ / evaṃ hi śrūyate / bṛhaspatir indrāyeti / arthavādo 'yaṃ pratipadapāṭhasya nivṛttaye upādīyate / īdṛśo vaktā īdṛśaḥ śrotā iyatā kālena nāntaṃ gataḥ / kiṃ punaradyatva iti / śabdānāṃ śabdapārāyaṇam iti / rūḍhiśabdo 'yaṃ kasyacit granthasya vācakaḥ / yathā śirorogaḥ kukṣiṃ bādhate iti / śirorogaśabdaḥ kukṣirogasya vyādhiviśeṣasya rūḍhiḥ / sa śiraḥ kṛtsnaṃ ca kukṣiṃ bādhata iti / evaṃ śabdapārāyaṇaṃ nāma granthaḥ / śabdānāṃ provāceti / caturbhiś ca prakārair vidyopayuktopayuktā bhavati / ardhopayuktāpi satyanupayuktā bhavati niṣphalatvāt / atha vā loke ekadeśopayogād apy upayuktam iti gamyate / tadyathopayuktaṃ ghṛtam iti / evam idaṃ naikadeśopayogād upayuktam iti / kiṃ tarhi ? samudāyopayogād upayuktam ity eva kathayati / āgamakālo grahaṇakālaḥ / svādhyāyakālaḥ kālo 'bhyāsasya / pravacanakālaḥ śiṣyebhyaḥ saṃpradātavyaḥ / vyavahārakālo yājñe karmaṇi viniyogaḥ śiṣṭamadhye vā yo viniścayaḥ / kiṃcit sāmānyaviśeṣabat lakṣaṇaṃ pravartyam / sāmānyaviśeṣau yasmiṃstat sāmānyaviśeṣavat / yathā karmaṇy aṇ iti / dhātor ity etat sāmānyam asmin lakṣaṇe 'sti / āto 'nupasarge ka ity asmiṃś cāta iti viśeṣo 'sti / anena cālpena yatnena mahato mahataḥ śabdaughān pratipadyate / kumbhakāraḥ kāṇḍalāva ityevamādi / evaṃ pārṣṇitro goda ityevamādīn / evaṃ sarvatra yojyam / sthānyādeśabhāve vidhipratiṣedhe ca / 

[7] kiṃ punar ākṛtiḥ padārtha āhosvit dravyam / ācāryāṇāṃ vipratipatteḥ saṃdehaḥ / vājapyāyanasyākṛtiḥ / vyāḍes tu dravyam / atra kecidvarṇayanti / yadyapīdamviśeṣeṇoktam evam api saṃbhaviṣu śabdeṣu idaṃ pṛcchyate yeṣūbhayaṃ saṃbhavati dravyam ākṛtiś ca / gaur aśva ity evamādiṣu na punar ḍittha ity evamādiṣu / apare punarāhuḥ / ākṛtiśabdena yāvat paraviśeṣaṇaṃ sattvavaducyate / ākriyate yena paricchidyate yeneti bhinnakriyā paricchidyate / anye āhuḥ / sarvatraivākṛtiḥ saṃbhavati / jātiḥ sābhidhīyata iti / tatra vājapyāyanasya śabda ākṛtim eva codayati na dravyādīn / tadyathā nīlaśabdo 'rthāntaranirapekṣo guṇasāmānyas tadrūpaṃ pratilabhate / tara pratilabdhasvarūpas tadadhiṣṭhāno yadā guṇavacano nīlasya sāhacaryāt nīlatvena vyāveśaṃ taddharmatām anubhavati ekayogakṣematām āpadyate vibhāgābhāvād ākṛtyabhibhūta eva san guṇapratyayamādadhāti / yathopāśrayavaśāt sphaṭikaṃ tadguṇam upalabhyate, evaṃ dravyam apy ayam abhidadhannīlaśabdaḥ svābhidheyasya nīlatvasya ya āśrayo guṇas tasyādhāro dravyam iti samavetasamavāyād vyavahito 'pi guṇena tadadhiṣṭhāna eva dravye pratyayam ādadhāti / tathā dravyāntareṇāpi rakte vastre / saṃyuktasamavetasamavāyād eva pāramparyeṇa yāva[d] dvayo raktayor vastrayor madhye śuklaṃ vastraṃ tadguṇam upalabhyate iti / evaṃ gaur ity apy ayaṃ śabdo gotve pratilabhya svarūpaṃ tadadhiṣṭhāna eva dravye pravartate / evaṃ pacatiśabde yā adhiśrayaṇādikriyās tāsāṃ yāni pratyarthaniyatāni sāmānyānyadhiśrayaṇādīni taiḥ sahaikārthasamavāyiyat sarvaviṣayaṃ sāmānyam abhivyajyate tat pacatiśabdavācyam / yathā bhramaṇatvam anekakarmaviṣayaṃ bhramaṇam ity ucyate / evaṃ ḍitthe 'pi yadutpattiprabhṛtyā vināśāt eva tad bhavaty ayaṃ ḍittho 'yaṃ ḍittha iti / bālyakaumārayauvanasthāvireṣv abhinnaḥ sa evāyam iti saṃpratyayaḥ sā ākṛtiḥ śabdavācyā / mugdhe 'pi ca saṃpratyayo bhavati ḍittho 'yaṃ likhyata iti / tasmāt sāmānyam atrāpy asti / yo vā saṃniveśaviśeṣaḥ saṃyogaviśeṣaṇaṃ ca hastyādiṣv iva sākṛtir eva / asya tu dravyābhidhānaṃ guṇakalpanayā jātitvam āpāditasya tacchabdenābhidhānāt / dravyapadārthikasya tu dravyam ucyate / jātyādayas tu bhedakā dravyasya sattvaṃ vaktum / upādhibhūtās te śabdasyeti / tatraikeṣāṃ mādhuryādayo 'tyantamanabhidhīyamānā guḍādibhiḥ śabdaiḥ tānarthān [sajante] iti / evaṃ gotvādayo 'pi / asyāpi jātyabhidhānaṃ gauṇam / dravyatāmāpāditā jātistacchabdenābhidhīyate / tatra vājapyāyanadarśanam / pāṇineḥ sarvatrākṛter bhāvāt sakṛd gatau vipratiṣedhabādhanaṃ śabdāntaraprāptiś ca nopapadyate / atha dravyam eva padārthaḥ, evam api sarvāsāṃ vyaktīnāṃ sarvābhiścodanābhir aṅgīkaraṇād utsargāpavādā na prakalpante / vyavasthitavibhāṣāś ca na prakalpante / ubhayam ity āha / vyaktirākṛtiś ca padārthaparair laukikaiḥ prayujyete / ye ca śāstre upadeśaśabdās teṣu kaścijjātim abhidadhāti kaścid dravyam / evaṃ ca sati na kaścid gauṇaḥ padārtho 'sya / yo jātiśabdas tasya jātir evārtho yo dravyaśabdas tasya dravyam evārtha iti / upadeśaśabdeṣv api lakṣyavaśād icchātaḥ padārthāśrayaṇe sarve śāstre prakriyābhedā darśanabhedāś copapadyante / ubhayathā ācāryeṇa iti / atra codyate / nanu ubhayapadārthikasya dravye padārthe bahuṣu bahuvacanaṃ siddham / ekenānekābhidhānaṃ jātau padārthe / satyam evaṃ matam / yadā tu kasyāścit kriyāyā upadeśe vā pratiṣedhe vā jātir eva prakaraṇe vā jāteḥ śabda upādīyate tadā tasyā ekatvād aprāptaṃ bahuṣu bahuvacanamārabhyate / evaṃ yadā dravyavivakṣā bhavati tasya ca prakaraṇaṃ tadā vākya iva sarupāṇāṃ plakṣanyagrodhadhavakhadirapalāśā ityanekaśabdatve prasakte vṛkṣavṛkṣavṛkṣa ityekaśabdatvāya yatnaḥ kriyate / aparaḥ prakāro 'tra vakṣyate / iha śuklavāsāḥ kriyatām ity atra śuklavāsastvam eva kriyamāṇaṃ dravyam [akriyamāṇam / śuklavāsasamānayety atra kriyamāṇaṃ dravyam] akriyamāṇas tu tadā saṃbandhaḥ / iha tu śuklavāsaḥ śuklavāsasamānayetyupalakṣaṇam api kriyāyām antarbhavati / parigṛhītopalakṣaṇo hi vyāpriyate tadvattvāt / iha tu yaḥ śuklavāsā dṛṣṭaḥ sa ānīyatām iti tadvataḥ kevalaṃ bhedake vāsasī na tu tayos tatra kriyāyām antarbhāvaḥ / evam ihāpi gaur ity anena śabdena gotvam evābhidhīyate na dravyam iti vājapyāyanadarśanam / tathā dravyam api abhidhīyate upalakṣaṇaṃ gotvādaya iti vyāḍimatam / ayaṃ tu bhagavān pāṇinir ubhayam ucyate śabdeneti pratipanna ity etat kathyate idam / idaṃ ca saṃbandham upanīyate ubhayathā hy ācāryeṇeti / iha kaḥ siṃho 'yaṃ śvetyukte yadā śauryam upādīyate tadā śvā siṃhatvena vipariṇamyate / dhātvarthavivakṣāyāṃ siṃhaḥ śvatvena pariṇamyate / evaṃ brīhiḥ ity ubhayāśrayaṇe yadā dravyatvamāpādyate jātis tadā siddhaṃ bahuvacanam / jātitāṃ cāpādite dravye siddham ekaśabdatvam / viparītārthakriyāyāṃ tu ekavacanam anekaśabdatvaṃ mā prāpaditi yogadvayaṃ paṭhati / evam upadeśaśabdeṣv api yojyam / ata eva taduktaṃ kiṃcit pradhānabhūtaṃ kiṃcid guṇabhūtam iti //

[8.1]	 kiṃ punar niryaḥ śabda āhosvit kārya iti / atrāpi vipratipattiḥ / vedavidāṃ nityo 'nyeṣām anityaḥ / atha vāyam arthaḥ / nityā eva te śabdā gaurīty evamādayaḥ / yeṣāṃ tu ākṛtir lakṣaṇam utpādyavastv evedam / samayo 'yaṃ vācikair vṛtaḥ / kecid evaṃ manyante / ya evaite prākṛtāḥ śabdāḥ ta evaite nityāḥ / prakṛtau bhavāḥ prākṛtāḥ / anye manyante / iyaṃ daivī vāk / sā tu puruṣāśakter ālasyād vā prakīrṇā / yathā svastīti śikṣamāṇo bālo anyathoccārayatīti / atha vā yathaiveha vastu dvedhāvasthitaṃ dravyākṛtibhedenaivaṃ śabdo 'pi / ya ete śabdāḥ kiṃ te śabdākṛtaya āhosvit śabdavyaktaya iti / tatra yadāyaṃ vṛkṣa [iti] vṛkṣādayaḥ śabdāḥ kramajanmānaḥ ayugapatkālāḥ vṛkṣaśabdatvād arthapratipattiḥ / sā ca nityā / abhyāsāc ca sākṛtiḥ kenacit puruṣeṇa katibhiścid eva varṇavyaktibhir avagṛhyate / yeṣāṃ rekhāmātreṇaiva citre pratipadyate puruṣo 'yaṃ likhyate hastī neti etasmin darśane nityaḥ śabdaḥ / tatra caitat uktam ubhayataḥ sphoṭamātraṃ pratinirdiśyate raśruter laśrutir iti / yasyāpi śabdavyaktiḥ tasyāpi nityaḥ śabdaḥ / sa tu nādābhivyaṅgyaḥ / padaniyato nādaḥ / yathā cakṣurādayo niyatāḥ abhivyañjakāḥ abhivyaṅgyeṣu rūpādiṣu rūpavṛddhihrāsānuvidhāyinaś ca / yad vādarśamaṇḍalādiṣu pratibimbāni dīrghāṇi parimaṇḍalāni mahānti anyāni ca dṛśyante / evaṃ śabdā api nādabhedena bhidyante / yathā salile taraṅgabhedena ekaś candro 'neka upalabhyate / pradīpabhedāc ca chāyā bhidyate / ādarśabhedāc ca pratibimbabhedaḥ / tasmān niyatanādābhibyaṅgyā / nādavṛddhihrāsānuvidhāyino vyaktiśabdā api nityāḥ / 
sa ca nādaḥ śrotrasyānugrahe vartate / tadanugṛhītaṃ śrotraṃ śabdopalabdhau samarthaṃ bhavati / yathākṣṇorañjanam ity eke / apare śabdasyaiva / yathā prokṣaṇaṃ pṛthivyā eva na ghrāṇasya / ubhayor ity apare / yathā vaśyendriyasya prathamataś cakṣū raśmīn ghaṭaṃ cānugṛhṇāti / evaṃ ca sati varṇāḥ padaṃ vākyaṃ ca sarvaṃ nityam / vedavākyeṣu tv eṣa viśeṣaḥ / na tatra viparyāsena śabdasyārthe prayogo 'sti / loke tv asti prayogaḥ / āhara pātraṃ pātramāhara / tvaṃ pacasi pacasi tvam / iṣe tvorje tvā ity evaṃbhūtaḥ prayogo 'sti / [na] tvāiṣe tvā ūrje iti / laukikavaidikayor evaṃ viśeṣaḥ / prayoganityatvaṃ vaidikānām anityatā laukikānām iti / 

nityatā cāpi dvividhā / vyavahārāśrayā paramārthāśrayā ca / paramārthāśrayā ca vaiśeṣikadarśane paramāṇavaḥ ākāśādīni ca / vyavahārāśrayā nāgarāriviṣāmustakvāthaḥ syād āmpācana iti na carakavacanādeṣām āmapācanatvam / kiṃ tarhi / svabhāvāt / evam ihāpi pāṇininānyena vā śabdāḥ [smṛtā] eva svato 'rthapratyāyakā iti vyavahāranityataivehopakāriṇī / saṃgraha etat prādhānyena parīkṣitaṃ nityo vā syāt kāryo veti / caturdaśa sahasrāṇi vastūni asmin saṃgrahagranthe / śāstrapūrvāt prayogād abhyudaya iti ubhayathāpi lakṣaṇaṃ pravartyam ity eva saṃgrahe nirṇayaḥ / kathaṃ punar idaṃ bhagavataḥ kāryo vā nityo vā / ayaṃ tv ācāryaḥ kiṃ darśanaṃ pramāṇīkṛtya pravṛttaḥ / kiṃ vedavidām utānyeṣām iti / etat pṛcchati / atha vā kathaṃ punar idam iti / yady api pāṇiner abhirucitā nityatānityatā va idaṃ tu śāstraṃ kathaṃ prastutam / kiṃ tāvad avagacchāmaḥ aham eva śabdārthasaṃbandhānāṃ sraṣṭā yathā brāhmaṇo 'bhimānataḥ prayotpādanaṃ prati / athāpīdaṃ darśanam / avasthitā eva amī śabdārthasaṃbandhāḥ / ahaṃ tu vṛttajñatvāt bahuśrutatvād vā [smartā] syāṃ teṣām iti //

[8.2]	 siddhe śabdārthasaṃbandhe / paramārthanityatā vyavahāranityatā vā śabdasya nityaśabdavṛttiḥ anityaśabdavṛttiḥ kalpaneti / karaṇasaṃnipātotpannaḥ pratiniyato varṇādiviṣayo dhvanirabhivyañjaka iti ākṛtau vyaktau vā saṃskāraḥ kriyate / ākṛtir vyaktir vārthasya pratyāyikā / ākṛtisaṃskāras tv ālambhanādivat vyaktidvārakaḥ / tatra nityaśabdavādina itaretarāśrayatvaṃ suparihāraṃ bhavati / pratideśatve śabdānāṃ niyamaḥ siddhaḥ / yathā hammatiḥ surāṣṭreṣu iti / tathā aviśeṣeṇopadiṣṭasya ṭittha ityāder arthaviśeṣa evāvasthānam / evaṃ hy ete prayuktāḥ / kevalaṃ pāṇiniḥ smarteti / artho 'pi / yeṣām ākṛtir abhidheyā sā nityā / dravye 'pi padārthe vyavahāranityatā / yadā yadā ghaṭādayas tadā tadaivaite evaṃsaṃniveśā eveti saṃbandho 'pi yogyatā yathā rūpopalabdhau yogyaṃ cakṣur na śrotram ity eva gṛhyate / atha vā arthaḥ śabdaprayukta iti śabdārthayoḥ kāryakāraṇalakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandhaḥ / tatrārthasya nityatvād anirdiśyamānā apy arthā vīpsākṛtvorthādayaḥ upasiktādayaś ca saptaparṇastridaśā iti dadhyodana iti cāṅgībhavanti / tathā coktam / svabhāvato 'rtheṣu vartamānānāṃ nimittatvenānvākhyānam / tad yathā / kūpe hastadakṣiṇa iti / saṃbandho 'pi nitya uktaṃ sarūpasūtre / tasmād vaktavyaṃ dvyartho bahvarthaś ceti / ucyeta na tu gamyeta / yo hi gām aśva iti brūyāt na jātucit saṃpratyayaḥ syāt iti / 

[8.3]	 atha siddhaśabdasya kaḥ padārthaḥ / nāvayavārthaḥ pṛcchyate / kas tarhi / samudāyārtho laukikaḥ / sāmarthyāt rūḍhiśabdaḥ / nityaparyāyavācī siddhaśabdo nityasyārthasya paryāyeṇa vācakaḥ / kadācid dhi nityaśabdas tam arhtam āha kadācit siddhaśabdaḥ / yadā svarūpam āśrīyate nitya ity asya svarūpasya nityaśabdasya yaḥ paryāyas tad vācī siddhaśabdaḥ nityaśabdasya ca paryāyaḥ siddhaśabdaḥ taṃ cāha siddhaśabda iti / tatra yathaiva vibhāvādātmani sarvagatasyātmeti vyapadeśaḥ pravartate prāptisāmānyān niṣkriye 'pi evam eva sādhyamānānāṃ yatsthā pariniṣpattiḥ tatsāmānyād asādhyeṣu siddhaśabdasya pravṛttiḥ / kathaṃ jñāyate / abhiprāyaḥ / paryāyaśabdaḥ  śabdāntaranirapekṣas tam arthaṃ bravīti / yathendraḥ śakraḥ puraṃdara iti / na ca śakrādayaḥ indrārthād arthāntareṣu vartante / ayaṃ tu siddhaśabdaḥ śabdāntaropasaṃhito 'nityārtham āha / kāryeṣu ca ghaṭādiṣu dṛṣṭaḥ / yat kūṭastheṣv avicāliṣu bhāveṣu vartate / kūṭasthaśabdaḥ / śabdo 'yaṃ na vyayīkriyate [yatra] tatra pravartate / tad yathā / kūṭasthaṃ rāśiṃ kuru nāsmād vyayaḥ kartavyaḥ iti / avicāliṣv iti deśāntarāprāptiṃ [a]vipariṇāmaṃ cāha / yasya hi mātrāpanīyate vipariṇamyate vā tan nityaṃ na bhavatīti asyābhiprāyaḥ / paryāyavācino 'pi sāmānyaviśeṣabhāve sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ dṛśyate / varāhaśabdaḥ parvate vartate sūkare ca vartate / prasiddhataras tu sūkare / parvatena tu sāmānādhikaraṇyam anubhavati / varāho himavān varāhaḥ pāriyātra iti / tatra yataiva varāhaśabdaḥ paryāyaḥ san śabdāntaropasaṃhitaḥ prayujyate viśeṣapratipattyartham evaṃ nityaviśeṣapratipattyarthaṃ pṛthivyādīnām upādānam iti na virudhyate paryāyaśabdatvam / śuklaśabdas tu paryāyo bhūtvā pravartate śuklavastram iti / siddhā dyauḥ siddhā pṛthivī siddham ākāśam iti / ārhatānāṃ mīmāṃsakānāṃ ca naivāsti vināśaḥ eṣām / sati vā vināśe prayoktṝṇāṃ śabdavyavahāram atikrāntā ete iti nityā ity ucyante / na hi prayoktṛbhedena utpadyamānā vinaśyanto vā dṛṣṭā iti / nanu ca bhoḥ kāryeṣv api dṛśyate / abhyupagacchāmo vayaṃ viśeṣapratipattyartham upādānaṃ saty api paryāyaśabda iti / tatra kiṃ vā tanniṣpattirūpam āśritya siddhaśabdo nityeṣu upasaṃhriyate / yadi niṣpattirūpam āśrīyate kāryeṣv api niṣpattirūpam astīti nāsti siddhaśabdāt nirṇayaḥ / prathamato manyase saty api niṣpattirūpe tad āśrīyate / kiṃ tarhi / rūḍhir eva / tad yathā gaṅgā hi rūpeṇāśritā tannāmikāṇam utpādayati gāṅga iti / devatādirūpeṇāśrīyamāṇā ḍhakaṃ gāṅgeya iti / evaṃ vāsudeva iti / yadāpatyatvenocyate vasudevasyāpatyam iti tadā kṣatriyākhyā / yadā tu vasanty asmin devā iti vāsudevaḥ tadā saṃjñaiṣā tatrabhagavata iti / duryodhana iti kṛcchreṇa yodhyata iti tadā kauravatve nākhyā [daur yodhanir iti] / kaurava iti dauryodhana iti / anukārī yathaiva nityeṣu rūḍha evam api kāryeṣv apīti / yadi nimittāpekṣo yadi rūḍhiśabdaḥ sarvathā nāsti nityaparyāyavācina iti nirṇayaḥ / saṃgrahe kāryapratidvandvibhāvāt / iha sāmānyasya svaviṣayasmṛtihetur anekaḥ sāhacaryādiḥ / yathā śilāśabdasya sāmānyaṃ sahacaritair bhidyate / lohadaṇḍamānaya śilāṃ cety ukte vyāyāmāpratipattiḥ śilāṅgatvāc ceti peṣāṅge / tathā rāmalakṣmaṇāv iti dāśarathau / rāmakeśavāv iti baladeve / virodhenāpi kiṃcit sāhacaryaṃ yathā rāmārjunāv iti jāmadagnye kārtavīrye ca pratipattiḥ / evaṃ saṃgrahe etat prastutaṃ kiṃ kāryaḥ śabdaḥ atha siddha iti / kāryaśabdasaṃnidhāne prayogāt siddhaśabdo nityasyārthavṛttiḥ / tathā na / kiṃ tarhi / kāryo na kāryaḥ siddha iti / kāryatvapratiṣedhāt siddhatvāpratiṣedhāc ca niścitaṃ saṃgrahe nityaparyāyavācinaḥ (°num) / ihāpi tad eva / kutaḥ / saṃgraho 'py asyaiva śāstrasyaikadeśaḥ tatraikatantratvād vyāḍeś ca prāmāṇyād ihāpi tathaiva siddhaśabda upāttaḥ / 

[8.4]	 atha vā santy ekapadāny apy avadhāraṇāni / anapekṣya granthāntaraṃ saṃpratyudhyate / iha kiṃcid dvipadam evāvadhāraṇaṃ bhavati yatraivakāraḥ śrūyate / apa eva bhakṣayati / vāyum eva bhakṣayatīti / avadhāryamāṇasyārthasya śabdāntareṇopāttasyānavadyotitam avadhāraṇam avadhotyata evaśabdena / yadā tv arthaprakaraṇādeḥ so 'rtho 'bhivyajyate tadaivaśabdo na śrūyate ity avadhāraṇam ekapadam ity ucyate / tatra na kaścid apo bhakṣayatīti [abbhakṣaḥ] / abbhakṣaśrutiḥ sāmarthyād anyanivṛttiparā / apa eva yo bhakṣayati sa ānīyatām iti / tathā yaḥ saṃpratyabbhakṣaḥ saṃpanna iti prāg api tasyāpsu pravṛtter avadhāryante āpo 'nyanivṛttyartham / yathā punar atra sāmarthyam iha siddhaśabde / kiṃ sāmarthyam / ucyate / siddhaśabdopādānāttāvannāyam ubhayārtho gamyate / etāvad eva vastu nityam anityaṃ vā / na kiṃcid anyan nityānityam asti / tatra yathaivābhirūpāya kanyā dātavyetyabhirūpasya prakarṣe pravṛttir nābhirūpamātre evaṃ kevalasiddhaśabdo ya evāvyabhicāriṇā siddhatvenānugatas tam evopādatte / dhruvāṇāṃ cāvyabhicāriṇāṃ siddhatvaṃ kāryāṇāṃ vyabhicāri ti kāryeṣu padāntaropasaṃhitaḥ pravartate / tatrāpi ca pravartamāno nityaviṣayam ekadeśam āśritya pravartate ity ata idam ucyate -- siddha eva yo na sādhya iti / etac ca niṣpattirūpāśritāyāṃ śabdapravṛttau yujyate na rūḍhyāśritāyām / evaṃ ca sati abbhakṣo vāyubhakṣa iti yujyate / yadā tu vratanāmadheyam abbhakṣo vāyubhakṣa iti ca rūḍhiḥ tadāpi jātivacanaḥ / tadātyantaṃ rūḍhau kriyāyāṃ pravṛtter avadhāraṇābhāvaḥ / 

[8.5] atha vā pūrvottarapadalopo 'tra draṣṭavyaḥ / kaḥ punar ayaṃ pūrvottarapadalopopāyaḥ / na hy anuccāritasya devadattaśabdasya pāṇinir anyo vā kaścit samartho lopaṃ kartum / tatraike manyante / ekapadopalabdhau samudāyasmṛtiḥ / tata evārthapratipattir iti / etad asamyak / na hi pratīyamānād arthapratyāyanaṃ na cānumānikasya saṃskāraḥ / nityatvaṃ ca vyākaraṇe uktam / yasmāt idaṃ darśanam / nāmakaraṇakāle anuniṣpādito nāntarīyakatvād vājinavat saha niṣpadyate / tad yathāmikṣārthinaḥ / kṣīramupādadānasya ekasādhanasādhyamāmikṣayaikakālaṃ vājinam api niṣpadyate / evaṃ satyabhāmāśabdena saha satyaśabdo bhāmāśabdaś ca niṣpādyate / tac ca pāṇinir lopadvāreṇaivaṃbhūtānāmamanuniṣpādināṃ sādhutvam ācaṣṭe / na tu jyeṣṭhā ity evamādīnāṃ jye ity ucyate / viśākhā ca khā iti / evam iha siddhaśabdo 'tyantasiddhaśabdānuniṣpādī yaḥ sa nirdiṣṭaḥ / yathā punas tatrārthaprakaraṇād iti / vipratipattis tu tasyāsaṃbhave / dattaṃ dāyamāhvayati artho vā sa tādṛśo yo devadattena kartavya iti taddaśabdena devadattaḥ pratipadyeta / evam ihāpi / katham / ucyate / ihāpi yadi niṣpattirūpāśrayaṇaṃ kriyeta niṣpadyamānatāyā yac ca niṣpannaṃ tasyedam anvākhyānam ity abhūyoviṣayatvaṃ syāl lakṣaṇasya / tasmād yaś ca niṣpanno yaś ca niṣpadyate yaś ca niṣpatsyamānaḥ sarva ete siddhāḥ / evaṃ ca ākāśe niṣpadyamānatā niṣpatsyamānatā niṣpannatā cāsti / atyantasiddhā ete ity etatsāmarthyād atyantasiddhānuniṣpādī ayam iti vyapadiśyate / 
[8.6]	atha vā vyākhyānato viśeṣapratipattir iti / etat pratipādayati na yasmāt saṃdehas tasmād alakṣaṇam / na hi vaktrādayaḥ pāṇyādayo vā saṃdihyamāne vastuni sthāṇoḥ puruṣasya ... lakṣaṇaṃ na bhavati / tasmād yathaiva vṛddhirādaijityevamādayo 'nuvṛttā idaṃ vyākhyānādavasīyate evam iha siddhaśabdaṃ nityaparyāyavācinaṃ tadvacanāt pratipadyate / 

[8.7]	 maṅgalārtham / nirupakruṣṭābhimatārthasiddhir maṅgala / tadarthaṃ ca yad upādīyate tad api tadarthatvāt maṅgalam ity ākhyāyate / tatra maṅgalārtham abhipretārthasiddhyartham / māṅgalika iti / maṅgalaṃ prayuṅkte / tathaivābhipretārthasiddhyāmaṅgatayā prayuṅkta aindramahika iti yathā / mahataḥ śāstraughasyeti dustaratvaṃ khyāpyate / prathante / prathamaṃ cānabhibhavaḥ / aparo na vicchedādyadhyayanasyeti idam āha / vīrapuruṣāṇy āyuṣmatpuruṣāṇi ceti / adhyetāraś ca siddhārthāḥ / kāryasiddhis teṣāṃ śāstrasyāpratibandhena parisamāptir vābhyudayo vā tatpūrvakaḥ / nityaśabdopādāne 'py eṣa eva vṛttānto yaḥ siddhaśabda ity etat kathayati -- ābhīkṣṇye iti / syād etat kriyāśabdaprayogādābhīkṣṇyaṃ yujyata iti / evam api nitye śabdārthasaṃbandha ity ucyamāne abhīkṣṇaṃ yaḥ śabdaḥ prayujyate āgopālādibhyas teṣām idam anvākhyānam anyeṣāṃ neti gamyate / kiṃ ca āścaryam anityaity atrābhīkṣṇaṃ yan na bhavati tad anityam ity ucyate ity asti kriyāpadamantareṇābhīkṣṇyavacanatvam iti //

[9.1]	 kaṃ punaḥ padārthaṃ matvaiṣa vigrahaḥ kriyate siddhe śabde arthe saṃbandhe ceti / śabde 'bhidheyo 'rths taṃ praty eva praśnaḥ / kiṃ śabdasyābhidheyaṃ matvaiṣa vigrahaḥ kriyate ity arthaḥ / ākṛtim ity āha / śabdo hy anapekṣitārthāntarasaṃbandho jātāv eva svarūpaṃ pratilabhate jātiguṇakriyāyadṛcchāśabdeṣu / dravyādīnāṃ tu tādrūpyāpādanenābhidhānam / yathā gośabdena bāhīkasysa / saṃjñāśabdena ca dravyasya / tatra kriyāṃ pratipadyamānaṃ dravyaṃ gāmānayeti pararūpeṇābhidhīyate / evaṃ śuklo gaur iti śuklatvenaikāryasamavetasamavāyena gotvam upalakṣyate / eko brāhmaṇa ity ekatvena brāhmaṇatvam iti sarvaḥ śabda ākṛtim āha / tasyāś ca tattvānyatvaprakhyayor yugapat kāryatvam ekasminn arthe na saṃbhavati / yathā sa eva yajñadatto yo 'nyo yajñadattād iti / iha tu madhurāyāṃ bālaṃ durbalaṃ kapilaṃ dṛṣṭvā pāṭaliputre nīlaṃ sthūlam upalabhya sa evāyam artho nīlatvādiguṇayukto yo mayā bālatvādiyukto dṛṣṭa iti pratyabhijñānād astitvam avasīyate / na cādṛṣṭe pratyabhijñānam asti / nūnaṃ dṛṣṭaḥ prāg artho yataḥ pratyabhijñāyata iti / sarveṣām eva vyatiriktā jātir ity evaṃvādināṃ nityatvaṃ pratyavipratipattir iti idam ucyate -- ākṛtir hi nityeti / sā tv anityair dravyādibhir vyajyata iti iyān vyāpāro dravyādīnām / 

[9.2]	 atha dravye padārthe vigrahaḥ kartavyaḥ / dravyam evābhidhīyate / gotvādayas tv anabhidhīyamānāḥ śabdasyopādhibhūtāḥ pravṛttinimittam / yathā svastikādayo devadattagṛhasyāvacakāḥ santa upalakṣaṇaṃ gṛhasya bhavanti / dravyasya ca vināśadarśanāt / atha vā tatraivaṃ vigrahaḥ kartavyaḥ / siddhe śabde arthasaṃbandhe ceti / arthena saṃbandhaḥ arthasaṃbandhaḥ / tasmin nitya ity arthasya na svārtham upādānaṃ vākye / kiṃ tarhi / saṃbadhopalakṣaṇatvena / yathā numgrahaṇam anusvāropalakṣaṇārtham / evaṃ hy ucyamāne siddhe śabde saṃbandhe ceti idam api gamyeta / śabdasya yaḥ svayākṛtyā saṃbandhas tasmin nitya iti / arthagrahaṇe tu sati abhidheyasya pratipattir iti / atha katham anitye 'rthe saṃbandho nityaḥ syāt / ucyate / samavāyavat / yathā samavāyo nityaviṣayo nityānityaviṣayaḥ anityaviṣayaś ca nityaḥ evam ayam api syāt / atha vā ghaṭaśabdasya yā sarvakālam arthenāśūnyatā sarvakālamāviṣṭam evārtharūpeṇa śabdarūpam ity evaṃviṣayāṃ yogyatām apekṣya śabdadharmeṇāha -- nityo hy arhtavatām arthair abhisaṃbandha iti / etad eva ca darśanamante vakṣyati / atha vā kiṃ na etena idaṃ nityam idam anityam iti / dravya eva padārtha eṣa vigraho nyāyyaḥ / dravyaṃ hi nityam / nityaḥ pṛthivīdhātuḥ / pṛthivīdhātau kiṃ satyam / vikalpaḥ / vikalpe kiṃ satyam / jñānam / jñāne kiṃ satyam / oṃ / atha tad brahma / tad etad uktaṃ bhavati / ataḥ paraṃ śabdārthavyavahāro nivartate / vyavahārātīto 'yam artha iti / tatra yāvanto nirdeśā anitya iti vā nitya iti vā sarva evaite 'vasthāpekṣāḥ tāś cānanyās tasmād iti / yathaiva ca svapno 'nyad vā jñānaṃ trailokyam iti vā gṛham iti vānākāraṃ parvatādibhedair avibhaktaṃ parvatādipradeśeṣv evam asāv arthaḥ / sa puruṣādiḥ sarvān śabdavyavahārānatikrānto nityaḥ parikalpyate / tasya ca nityatve upapanno vigrahaḥ / 

[9.3] ākṛtāv api padārthe eṣa vigraho nyāyyaḥ / apiśabdo dravye 'py evaṃbhūta upapanna iti pradarśanārthaḥ / na kvacit uparateti kṛtvātaḥ sarvatroparatā bhavati / yad ucyate ākṛtir anyotpadyate iti tad asat / kasyāś cit tirobhāvāt kasyāś cit cāvirbhāvāt / paurastye hi darśane sati bhāvābhāvau na stoṃ jāteḥ / kiṃ tarhi / āvirbhāvatirobhāvau / tau cānupalabhya smartur bhagavato 'sya pāṇiner ubhayaṃ padārtha ity eṣa siddhāntaḥ / tatrobhayasmin api padārthe arthasya nityatā na vyāvartata iti na śakyate 'syā bhāvābhāvau kalpayitum / evaṃ pradhānātmany avasthitam asaṃvedyaṃ nāstitvāt pracyavate / dravyāntarasthopalabhyate / dravyāntarasthābhivyajyate / tatra tattvānyatvaprakhyayor avikalayor ekasmin āśraye virodhādākṛter ekatvam avināśo 'sti ity avakalpante / athāpi yathopanyas taṃ darśanam / atrāpy ananyatvāt yākṛtir iti tad eva dravyam ucyate bhavatā / naivam / yadi dravyaṃ nityaṃ ākṛtir api nityā / atha vedam api darśanam / ekam api tāvad virūpaṃ sarvam / śaktīnāṃ samudāye kācit tu śaktiḥ kadācit tirobhavati kadācit āvirbhavatīti / tathā śabdaḥ pravartate / 

[9.4]	 atha vā nedam eva nityalakṣaṇaṃ dhruvaṃ kūṭastham avicālīti / anena granthena trividhām anityatām āha / trividhā hy anityatā / saṃsargānityatā vipariṇām anityatā vastuvināśānityatā ca / ato viparyayeṇa nityatā / saṃsargānityatā yathā sphaṭikasyopāśrayaviśeṣaiḥ saṃsargibhir dravyāntaraiḥ pracchāditaṃ svarūpaṃ nopalabhyate svataḥ sac ca tad api / tathā vipariṇāmānityatā yathā badarasya śyāmatātirobhāve raktatāprādurbhāvena / vastuvināśānityatā yaḥ sarveṇa sarvapradhvaṃso nirūpākhye / tadā naitat pratipattavyaṃ yadaivaite dharmā na santi / tan nityam iti / evaṃ hi parikalpyamāne sāṃkhyasyaiva ātmāvikāritvanityaḥ syāt / pradhānaṃ tu vikāradharmi ity anityatvaprasaṅgaḥ / tasmād idam api nityalakṣaṇaṃ yasmiṃs tattvaṃ na vihanyate tan nityam iti / sa evāyam iti tulyabuddhihetutvam / sa evāyam ātmā / vastunā avinaṣṭaṃ cātmavastu ghaṭatvādi / atha vā sa evāyaṃ sa tayāvasthayocyate / atha vā kiṃ na etena / nahīdaṃ śāstraṃ kasyacid ekasya sahāyabhūtaṃ sarvasādhāraṇam / yathaiva sāṃkhyādīnāṃ dravyād eva pratipattiḥ rūpādisamavāyo ghaṭo 'rthāntarabhūto veti yasya yo ghaṭas tasmin ghaṭaśabdaṃ prayuṅkte / evaṃ yasya yādṛśī nityatābhipretā tāṃ nityatām āśrityārthasyāyaṃ vigrahaḥ kriyate / tatra kṣaṇikavādinām avicchedena pravṛttir yā sā nityatā / itareṣāṃ pradhānavyavasthānadarśanena nityatā / atha vedaṃ pratipādayati tatra kiṃ na etenedaṃ nityam idam anityam iti / kim asmākaṃ vastugatena vicāreṇa / yad asmākaṃ nityaṃ taṃ padārthaṃ matvaiṣa vigrahaḥ kriyate / arthaś cāsmākaṃ yaḥ śabdenābhidhīyate / tatra vastu yadi nityaṃ yady anityaṃ śabdas tu na kadācid abhideyaś cānitya iti śabdo nārthāpravṛttapūrvaḥ / tatra nityakālaṃ svarūpeṇa vyāveśādyaś ca ghaṭaśabdo yaś ca śaśaviṣāṇaśabdo sarva ete sarvakālam arthair aśūnyā vyāviṣṭā evārtharūpeṇety evaṃbhūte padārthe yukta evaiṣa vigrahaḥ / siddhe śabde arthe saṃbandhe ceti //

[10.1] kathaṃ punaḥ prastūyate / upapattiṃ pṛcchati / lokataḥ / lokād dhi prasiddham idaṃ nityam idam anityam iti / yādṛśī lokapravṛttiḥ kāryeṣu bhāveṣu tādṛśī na śabdeṣu / ye laukikāḥ arthāḥ śikṣāpūrvakāḥ te na śikṣāmantareṇa nirvartayituṃ śakyante / tad yathā / ghaṭenārthī na ca śikṣitaḥ ghaṭakaraṇe tu kuśalaṃ kartāram upaiti kumbhakāraṃ kuru ghaṭaṃ kāryam anena kariṣyāmi iti evam / yadi śabdāḥ gaur ity evamādayaḥpāṇiniprabhṛtibhir eva śārakrīḍāvat vyavahārārtheṣūtpāditāḥ syuḥ tatrāśikṣitaḥ puruṣaḥ kuśalān prayojyatve vaiyākaraṇānupeyāt / na copaiti / tāvaty evārtham artham upādāya śabdān prayuṅkte / buddhau samīkṣyārthaṃ prayuṅkta eva tasmād etad avaseyam / avasthitā eva amī śabdārthasaṃbandhā iti / 

anye varṇayanti / saumasaurakrīḍāvad utpāditāḥ / yathā śabdān pṛcchati evam arthān pṛcchati / asya śabdasya ko 'arthaḥ / evaṃ saṃbandham api karoti / na hi śabdam avasthāpyārthaṃ ca tāv ubhāvupaśleṣayati / tasmād etad avaseyam / vyavasthitā evāmī śabdārthasaṃbandhāḥ / yata ekasminn eva kāle buddhyā nirūpyanta iti / 

anye varṇayanti / yat tad uktaṃ darśanasya parārthatvāt viprapravṛttatvād iti tad evānena bhāṣyeṇoktam iti / kāryāṇām eva viniyogād apy anyaddarśanam asti utpattiṃ prati / asya tu yaddarśanaṃ yopalabdhiḥ yā niṣpattiḥ sā parārtharūpā eva / na hi aparārthatāśūnyaḥ kālaḥ kvacid asti / tasmād etat pratipattavyam / avasthita evāsau prayoktṛkaraṇādisaṃnipātena abhivyajyata iti / asyāṃ kalpanāyāṃ dvayaṃ na saṃbadhyate / yadi tarhi lokaḥ śabdeṣu pramāṇam iti / lokato 'rthaprayukte śabdaprayoge śāstreṇa dharmaniyamaḥ / lokāt lokataḥ / arthena prerite arthaprayukte / yasmāl lokādayaṃ śikṣate sa lokaḥ sāparādhaḥ / sādhūnasādhūṃś ca śikṣayati / yaś cātmapratyāyane 'rthaḥ prerayitā saḥ / yathaiva gaur ity etaṃ prerayaty evaṃ gāvyādīn api / ataḥ śāstrapravṛttiḥ evam arthā katham asādhavo nivarterann iti / atha śabdaprayoga iti prayogahagraṇaṃ kim artham / etāvad vaktavyaṃ lokato 'rthaprayukta śabda iti / ucyate / arthapratyāyanaṃ bodhaḥ / tatra pratyāyyo 'rthaḥ / pratyāyanaṃ phalam / śabdaḥ karaṇam / śabdārthayoḥ karmakaraṇayoḥ asati kriyārthe saṃbandhābhāvāt kriyābhyupagantavyā / yathā ca dhānyānāṃ vrīhīṇām avahanane muśalodyamananipātanābhyāṃ vyavahitena saṃbandho bhavati tataś ca kaṇamokṣatuṣavipramokṣaḥ phalaṃ nirvartate / evam ihāpi śabdo dravyabhūtaḥ karaṇam / arthaḥ karma pratyāyyate / tad avabodhaḥ phalam / tat phalaṃ kriyāyāḥ / kā punaḥ sā kriyā / ucyate / praṇidhiḥ prayoktuḥ śabdān prerayatas tadviṣayaḥ / praṇidhiś cetaso vṛttir ayaṃ yogya etam arthaṃ prayāyayitum iti / sā kriyā / evaṃ hy uktam / ātmā buddhyā samarthyārthān mano yuṅkte vivakṣayeti / yathā saty eva cakṣuṣi sati ca rūpe na cakṣurapraṇihitamanadhiṣṭhitaṃ manasā viṣayālocane samarthaṃ bhavaty evaṃ śabdo vyavasthito 'rthe 'pi prayoktuḥ praṇidhānamantareṇa pratyāyane asamarthaḥ ity etat prayogagrahaṇenācaṣṭe / anyeṣāṃ darśanam / śabdasyāneko dharmaḥ / yatheṇ gatāv ity ayaṃ yadānvayavyatirekābhyām arthavān iti samadhigamyate tadārthavattvād dhātutvaṃ pratipadyate / ayam eva tu yadārthaparityāge ekāco dve iti varṇatvenāśrīyate tadā tasyārthapadārthakasyāyam ekaḥ ajiti vyatireke asyeti saṃbandham upanīyate / tathā brāhmaṇaśabdā abhyāsakāle 'narthakāḥ parapratipādane svarūpapadārthakāḥ evaṃ paṭhaivaṃ paṭheti / viniyogakāle 'rthavantaḥ / tatra yāsau viniyogakāle parigrhītārthavato 'rtheṣu prapattiḥ sa saṃbandhaḥ / yac copalakṣyate pāratantryaṃ sa śabdasyātmīyo dharmaḥ / nityaḥ śabdātmā / sa prayogaśabdenopasaṃgṛhyate / evaṃbhūtaḥ śabdo 'rthena preryate na sarva iti / 

anye varṇayanti / prayujyata iti prayogaḥ / śabdaś cāsau prayogaś ca śabdaprayogaḥ / na śabdamātre dharmaniyamaḥ / kiṃ tarhi / prayogārthe śabda iti / 

anye varṇayanti / kaścicchabdaḥ prayujyate kaścin na prayujyate / guhā trīṇi nihitā neṅgayanti / na ceṣṭante ity ete na prayujyante / turīyaṃ vāco manuṣyā vadantīti turīyo bhāgaḥ prayujyate / ye prayujyante teṣāṃ niyamaḥ / tasmāt ya utkṛṣṭo dharmaḥ tasmāt teṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ jñānam iti / dharmāya niyamo dharmaniyamaḥ / dharmaniyamo nāma rūḍhaḥ kaścit / tasya tu niyataṃ tādarthyam iti caturthyā vigrahe darśyate / ṣaṣṭhyā samāso hy ayaṃ yathāśvaghāso nāma kaścit iṣīkādayas teṣām eva rūḍhaḥ / niyatatvāt tādarthyasya caturthyā vigṛhyate aśvebhyo ghāsa iti / ṣaṣṭhīsamāsa evāyam / tathā coktam / aśvaghāsādayaḥ ṣaṣṭhīsamāsā bhavantīti / dharmārtho vā niyamo dharmaniyama iti śākapārthivāditvād uttarapadalopaḥ / dharmārthatvād ayam eva niyamo dharma ity āsannaṃ kāraṇaṃ dharmaṃ prati vyapadiśyate / dharmaprayojano vā iti mīmāṃsakadarśanam / avasthita eva dharmaḥ / sa tv agnihotrādibhir abhivyajyate / tatpreritas tu phalado bhavati / yathā svāmī bhṛtyaiḥ sevāyāṃ preryate phalaṃ pratyevam ayaṃ niyamo dharmasya phalanirvṛttiṃ prati prayojaka iti //

[10.2] yathā laukikavaidikeṣu / priyatāddhitā dākṣiṇātyāḥ / atha kiṃ priyataddhitatvād apaśabdo 'yam iti pratipattavyam / nety āha / atra hi ye te 'vayavā loke bhavā vede vā śakyās te taddhitamantareṇopasaṃgrahītuṃ samudāyaśabdena loka iti vā veda iti vā / ayaṃ tv ācāryaś cānavayavabhūtān pṛthakkṛtya lokavedayoś cādhāratvaṃ nirdiśati / tatra kim anyat priyataddhitatvāt / yathā kaścid vanas pataya iti bhedena bravīti kaścit samūharūpeṇa vānaspatyam iti / atha vā yuktaḥ evātra taddhitārthaḥ / nātrāvayavāvayavibhāvaḥ / kiṃ tarhi / vākya evānekadeśabhūto 'rtho 'bhipreto lokavedābhyām / kaḥ punar asau / kṛtāntaḥ siddhāntaḥ samayagranthasaṃdarbhaḥ / etad uktaṃ bhavati / yathaivānye granthasaṃdarbhā dharmaniyamārthā evam ayam api kasmāt gṛhyate dharmaniyamārtha iti / 

loke tāvad abhakṣyo grāmyakukkuṭa iti naiṣa sādharmyeṇa dṛṣṭāntaḥ / kiṃ tarhi / pakṣe / pakṣe na yo bhakṣya iti dharmaniyama iti / asmāt sāmānyād iha upāttamarthitvād bhakṣaṇaṃ prasaktam evam aitannopadeśāt / tatra niyamaḥ kriyate etena kṣut pratihantavyā nānyeneti / atra keṣāṃcid darśanam / yadi tadvacanam abhakṣyo grāmyaḥ kukkuṭa ity asyānupādānāt dharma iti / anye manyante / yathaiva viṣabhakṣaṇāt dhruvo mṛtyur iti viṣaṃ na bhakṣyata evam upapātakam iti svabhāvāt tasmāt tatparityāgāt dharma iti / evam arthāvaboddhaṃ prati sarvo 'rthaḥ śabdam apaśabdaṃ ca prayuṅkte / tatra śabdenāsau pratyāyyo netareṇeti niyamaḥ kriyate / 

nanu ca kṣuddvitīyasyākaraṇāt kṛtyaḥ kṣudhi karmaṇy utpannaḥ tatraivaṃ bhavitavyaṃ śakyā cāneneti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / sāmānyenopakramo viśeṣeṇābhidhānam / iha śakeḥ kṛtyaḥ sāmānyadravyaviṣaye karmārthe pravṛttaḥ / sa prāptaliṅga evājahalliṅgatāviśeṣeṇa bhidyate / tatra viśeṣe yaḥ karmabhāvaḥ sa upayuktaḥ kṛtyeneti prathamā / yathoktaṃ mukhanāsikā vacanam asya / nocyate mukhanāsikā vacanī yasyeti / tathā khedāt / khedo nāma yad indriyasyātmaviṣaye pravartamānasya nigrahaṃ pratyasāmarthyam / atha vā khedayatīti khedaḥ rāga evocyate / rāgāt khalu strīṣu pravṛttir iti atha vā dainyaṃ khedaḥ / evaṃ tāvat pauruṣeyābhimataṃ smṛtiśāstraṃ smṛtvopanibaddhaṃ vede tad api pradarśayati -- vede khalv api payovrato brāhmaṇa iti / atha kiṃ payovratatvaṃ kratvartham / nety āha / yadi kratvarthaṃ syāt yathānyeṣu avadānaprāśanādiṣv avaśyaṃ pravṛttir evam atrāpi syāt / na cāvaśyaṃ dīkṣitaḥ payo vratayati / arthitve hi sati / yathā kṛṣṇaviṣāṇayā kaṇḍūyatīti prakṛtam eva kaṇḍūyanam upajātāyāṃ kaṇḍvāṃ kṛṣṇaviṣāṇayā kriyate / yadi ca ktarvartham abhyupagamyate / yat prakṛtabhojanaṃ pratiṣiddham anyasmin loke sa cāsmin arthitve pravartate / yathājyamavekṣata iti kratvartham avekṣaṇam iti na dravyāntarāṇām avekṣaṇaṃ pratiṣidhyate / baulvaḥ khādiro yūpaḥ syād iti / paśor asvatantrīkaraṇārthaṃ yūpa upādīyate / tatra hy asvatantrīkaraṇaṃ yad dṛṣṭaṃ phalaṃ niṣpadyate yadi śiṣṭe kṛtacaṣāle 'ṣṭāśrau kāṣṭhe paśvanubandhaḥ kriyate / yathāgnau kapālāni ity atrāpi puroḍāśānāṃ pāko dṛṣṭaṃ phalam / na cānena mantreṇa kaṃcit pākaviśeṣam upalabhāmahe / tataś cādṛṣṭam atrāsti phalam iti etad anena pratipādayati / 

yady apy eṣa pakṣa syāt sādhuvad apaśabdā api vācakāḥ ity evam api dṛṣṭaṃ phalaṃ tulyam arthāvabodhaḥ / iha tu adṛṣṭaṃ phalam asty abhyudaya iti / ata āha -- evam ihāpi samānāyām arthāvagatau śabdena ca iti / abhyudayakāri ca bhavati iti ca bruvatā idam abhyupagataṃ bhavati avihitaparityāgād adharmo naśyata iti / anye manyante / tatparityāgāt adharmaḥ kevalaṃ na bhavati / ayam eva cābhyudayo yad atra enasā na yujyata iti / 

[11.1] asty aprayuktaḥ / etat pratigṛhītaṃ prayujyamānānāṃ vyākhyānam iti / ete coṣādayo na prayuktā koke vyākaraṇāya grantheṣu ca likhitā na vā / sā kataragranthe nītir upapadyate / pūrvapakṣadvāreṇaitat pratipādayitukāmo yad etad aprayuktatvaṃ śabdānāṃ naitad yasya kasyacit vacanād eva tiṣṭhate / ya eva bahuśrutāḥ śiṣṭāḥ saṃmatāḥ śabdavyavahāreṣūparatāḥ svatantrās teṣām eva vacanād iti / 

idaṃ vipratiṣiddham / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / ye'rthā arthakriyām antareṇa prasiddhās teṣv iyaṃ vācoyuktir upapadyate / yathā ghaṭo 'ntareṇa svām arthakriyām abādyāharaṇādikāṃ prasiddhaḥ siddhaḥ / śabdaḥ tv ayaṃ svām arthakriyām antareṇa na jātu prasiddhaḥ siddhaḥ / prayogād eva hy ayam astirūpaṃ pratipadyate / tatra yathaiva kaścid brūyād aprayogād iti tathājātīyakam idam / prayuñjāna eva khalv api bhavān iti anuparatakaraṇavyāpāra evāhāprayukta iti / aciranirvṛttaṃ prayogam ācaṣṭe / saṃpraty eva hy ayaṃ prayukta iti / yathā kaścid brūyād yāvaj jīvaṃ me maunamato na brabīmīti tahtaivedaṃ bhavatā / tatra yadi aprayogo 'numanyate tvayāprayukta iti prayogān manyeta / atha manyase ahaṃ prayuñje na tv anye iti anyenāprayogādasattvam eṣām iti / etad apy asat / śabdā hy ete 'nyair noccāryeran na caitāvataiva na syuḥ / bahavo hi śabdāḥ strībhir noccāryante / athaiṣā bhavato buddhiḥ kiṃ mamoccārayataḥ anāpto 'ham itīdam āha protsāhanārtham / mā avamaṃsthā ātmānam āpto bhavān iti / kaś cedānīm anyo bhavajjātīyakaḥ / ko 'nyo bhavato vidvattara iti / 

naitad vipratiṣiddham iti / etad āha / dvidhā sattā pratyakṣānumānikī ceti / tatra pratyakṣā dādharti dardhartīti / ānumānikī prakṛtipratyayopadeśena kartavyā / tatroṣety evamādīnāṃ prakṛtīnāṃ pratyayānāṃ copadeśāt / dhṛṅaḥ śluḥ śapaś caiṣāṃ niyataviṣayatvānnoktam / grantheṣu ca likhitatvād idam avastitaṃ santy eta iti / atha loke na prayujyante iti idaṃ brūmo 'rayukta iti / bruvāṇaś cāhaṃ kathaṃ brūyām aprayukta ity āha -- na brūmo 'smābhir aprayuktā iti / abhyantaro 'haṃ loke na tv ahaṃ loka iti etad uktaṃ bhavati / yathā lokaḥ śabdān prayuṅkte naivam aham etānūṣādīn prayuñje iti //

[11.2] nārthe śabdaprayogāt / arthā hi kālapuruṣādayaḥ santi / śabdasya cārthāntareṣu guṇabhāvāt pradhānasaṃnihitena śabdena bhavitavyam / atha vārthopalabdhiḥ śabdāstitvānumānam / na hi kaś cid artho 'śabdo 'sti / tatra yathaiva svargāpūrvadevatāśabdā upalabhyamānā atyantāparidṛṣṭānām arthānām astitvānumānam evaṃ kiṃ na gṛhyate 'rtha upalabhyamānaḥ śabdāstitvasyānumānam iti / 

[11.3] aprayogaḥ prayogānyatvāt / yo 'yaṃ śabdāstitve hetur upadiṣṭaḥ aśabdakasyārthasya rūpād iti / atra brūmo yady eṣām arthānāṃ vācakā na syuḥ pratipadyemahy eṣām astitvam / yatas tu santi tasmād ahetur ayam / nanu cocetyasya uṣitā iti samānārtho na bhavati puruṣasya parokṣatāyāś cābhidhānāt / naiṣa doṣaḥ / vākyaṃ tathābhūtaṃ prayoktavyaṃ yataḥ sarvārthābhivyaktir bhavati / 

[11.4] aprayukte dīrghasattravat / ādyena samayena pratipādayaty astitvam / yathā pūrvakālaṃ prayuktāni dīrghasatrāṇi idānīm aprayujyamānāny api dadhimadhvādayo dharma iti karmatādiviṣayaḥ sidhyata evam anyaiḥ prayuktānāṃ sarvakālam idānīm aprayujyamānānām apy anuvidhānaṃ yuktam / ye tu dadhimadhvādayo dharma iti teṣāṃ vyākaraṇe 'yam artho na saṃbhavati / na hi iha śabdoccāraṇād dharma iti / 

[11.5] sarve deśāntare / atha yājñikāściraṃ jīvanto 'dyatve 'py āhuḥ hariḥ arir iti / evam api brūmaḥ sarve ete deśāntare prayuktā iti / vākovākyam / uktipratyuktigranthaḥ kiṃsvidāvapanaṃ mahad ity evamādiḥ / itihāsaḥ / pūrvacaritasaṃkīrtanam / eteṣu kaścid eva śabdaḥ kasmiṃś cit prayujyate / yathā puruṣīśabdaḥ strīliṅgaḥ kvacid aprayukto maitrāvaruṇīyānāṃ kalpe prayujyate / tasyām anaḍvāhīṃ puruṣīṃ ca dhenuke dakṣiṇe dattvā kārapacavaṃ prati yamunām avabhṛtam abhyupeyur iti / śavatirgatikarmā sarvavibhaktyāntaḥ kambojeṣv eva bhāṣito vikāra enam āryā bhāṣante śava iti / jīvataḥ prakṛter mṛto vikāraḥ / mṛto nāśaṃ gata ity arthaḥ / atha vā gater vikāraḥ gatyuparamaḥ / uparatakriye bhāṣante śava iti / nirukte tv evaṃ paṭhyate -- vikāram asyāryeṣu bhāṣanta iti / tatrāyam arthaḥ / śavateracpratyayāntasya yo vikāra ekadeśas tam eva bhāṣante na śavatiṃ sarvapratyayāntāṃ prakṛtim iti / 

[12.1] kiṃ punaḥ śabdasya jñāne dharmaḥ āhosvit prayoga iti / etāvad ucyate śāstreṇa dharmaniyama iti / sa dharmaniyamaḥ kiṃ jñānād atha prayogād iti / abhijñ eva pṛcchati kātra smṛtir iti / atha vā smṛtijña eva pṛcchati / sā tu smṛtiḥ kvacit śrutyā virudhyate / yathāṣṭācatvāriṃśād varṣāṇi vedabrahmacaryaṃ caritavyam iti / iyaṃ śrutivirodhinī smṛtiḥ / śrutau hy uktaṃ kṛṣṇakeśaḥ pitā putraṃ yojayed iti / tatrānumānapakṣe smṛtiḥ nityapramāṇaṃ bhavati / kiṃ cādṛṣṭārthā smṛtir apramāṇam / yathā haṃsaḥ kaiścid bhakṣya ity abhyupagatam / kaiścin mātṛyonibaddhānām udvāhaḥ / tatra laulyāt rāgād vedaṃ pravartitam / tatra pūrvaṃ dharmaṃ matvā śiṣṭā ācaranti adyatve / smṛtir ekaḥ śabdaḥ samyak jñātaḥ śāstrānvitaḥ suprayuktaḥ svarge loke kāmadhug bhavatīti / kiṃ cedam / prayukta evāsau samyak jñāto bhavati / yadi samyak prayujyate jñānād eva tu dharmaḥ / athaivaṃ prayuktaḥ samyak jñāto bhavatīti / samyak prayogas tu samyagjñāte sati bhavatīti / tatra pṛcchāmaḥ / kā upapattiyuktā smṛtiḥ / kasyāḥ śruteḥ anuvādinīti / 

[12.2] jñāne dharma iti cet tadvad adharmaḥ / pṛthak kṛtvedam ucyate / yadi śabdajñāne dharma evaṃ sati apaśabdajñānād adharmaḥ / etasminn arthe etat saṃbhaved brāhmaṇavadhādadharmo jīvanāt tu dharma iti / na tv etac chakyate vaktuṃ brāhmaṇavadhādadharma iti śūdravadhād dharma iti / atra hīdaṃ yujyeta śabdajñānād yadi dharmaḥ śabdasyaivājñānād adharmaḥ prāpnotīti / ucyate / dṛṣṭārthayā smṛtyā tulyatvam asyāḥ smṛter āha / dṛṣṭārthāyāṃ hi smṛtau vaidyake yat śleṣmaṇaḥ prakopanaṃ tadviparītaṃ rūkṣaṃ vāyoḥ / evaṃ śabdajñānān na dharmo nāpy adharmaḥ apaśabdajñānāt tv adharma iti anye manyante / śabdajñānād dharmo na tv apaśabdajñānād adharma iti / yathottaratra vakṣyati śabdapramāṇakā vayam iti / kecin manyante / śabdāpaśabdajñānābhyāṃ dharmādharmau pravartete / adharmasya tu dharmeṇābhibhavaḥ / yathā kūpakhānakam upādāsyate / tatrāpaśabdajñānād adharma ity etat pradarśayati / vyākaraṇādhyayanād anartha āgataḥ / tasmān na kiṃcid etat na cāntareṇa vyākaraṇaṃ śabdāḥ śakyā jñātum iti / 

athaitat syāt / bhūyān dharmo 'lpīyānadharma iti / etac cāsat / bhūyas tvād apaśabdānāṃ phalenāpi bhūyasā bhavitavyam ity adharma eva bhūyān prāpnoti / 

[12.3] ācāre niyamaḥ / evaṃ tāvad uktaviruddheyaṃ smṛtiḥ / śrutiś caināṃ nānuvadati / ato 'py apramāṇam / ācāre prayoge ṛṣir mantraḥ te 'surā iti pradarśayaty etad ayaṃ tv ācāraḥ / tasmān na mlecchitavai nāpabhāṣitavai iti ca / iyaṃ smṛtir evam āha mlecchanam apabhāṣaṇaṃ na kartavyam iti / na punar evam āha mlecchanam apabhāṣaṇaṃ na jñātavyam iti / 

nanu cātraitāvad ucyate ayam apaśabdo na prayoktavya iti evaṃ tu naivāsti śabdasya prayogād dharma iti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / apabhāṣaṇe pratiṣiddhe kiṃ tūṣṇīṃbhūtenāsitavyam / tatrānugṛhīto 'pi vyāhāraḥ sa sādhakaḥ kartavyaḥ ity etad vijñāyate //

[12.4] prayoge sarvalokasya / śāstrasyopādānam anarthakam ity abhiprāyaḥ / antareṇāpi vyākaraṇaṃ prayogadarśanāt / 

atra codyate / yady apy antareṇa śāstram api prayogaḥ tathāpi na yatnānarthakyam / yathā saty api svabhāvārogatve keṣāṃcit vaidyopadeśaṃ pratyākhyāya pravṛttā iti api na vaidyakaṃ praṇīyante / kecid vyādhitās tena cikitsyanta eva / tathā vyākaraṇam api pravartayitavyamadhyetavyaṃ ca / yasmāc chabdān prayoktum icchan adhyāpyate tad arthaṃ ca praṇetavyam iti / 

ucyate / satyam evam / teṣāṃ tu tatraivoktam / 
dīptāgnayaḥ kharāhārāḥ karmaniṣṭhā mahodarāḥ / 
ye narāḥ prati tāṃś cintyaṃ nāvaśyaṃ gurulāghavam //
iti / iha tu na kiṃcid evaṃbhūtam asti / etāvad ucyate na cāntareṇa vyākaraṇaṃ śabdāḥ śakyā jñātum iti //

[12.5] śāstrapūrve prayoge 'bhyudayaḥ / śāstrahetuke jñāne sati yaḥ prayogaḥ tasmāt abhyudayaḥ / yaḥ śāstrapūrvakaṃ jñātvā śabdān prayuṅkte so 'bhyudayena yujyate / tasmād asau smṛtir ekaḥ śabdaḥ samyak jñāta iti / tattulyaṃ vedaśabdena / vedaśabdito yo 'rthas tena tulyam / vedaśabdena yo 'gniṣṭomena yajate ya u cainam evaṃ veda / sa vedaśabda etad artham abhidadhāti / yo'gniṣṭomaṃ vidvāṃs tu prayuṅkte so 'bhyudayena yujyate viduṣaḥ karmaṇyadhikārāt / apara āha / saṃprati vedavākyānyevodāharaṇāni / yathā tāni brahmacaryādibhir niyamair adhītāni dharmasādhanam evaṃ prayogasya dharmasādhanatve śāstrapūrvakaṃ jñānam iti viśiṣṭāt prayogād abhyudaya iti vyavasthitam / atha vā punar astu jñāna eva / neyaṃ smṛtir dṛṣṭārthayā tulyā / tatra pāramparyeṇāvicchinnam idaṃ smaryate jñānād dharma iti / tatrāgamaprāmāṇyād yad vihitaṃ pratiṣiddhaṃ vā tad eva kriyate varjyate vā / nanv idaṃ vacanam apaśabdajñānād adharma iti vā dharmo vā na bhavaty apaśabdajñānād iti / yac ca na vihitaṃ na ca pratiṣiddhaṃ na tatra karaṇe varjane vā guṇadoṣāv iti / atha vābhyupāya evāpaśabdajñānaṃ śabdajñānasya / yaś ca yasyābhyupāyaḥ tam antareṇa tasyāpravṛttir eva iti tad api tasyāṅgam eva bhavati / yathā kaścit manyeta kṣitigataṃ śauklyaṃ tasya darśanād dharma iti / asau kṛṣṇā / sa paśyan na tatra śaknoti śuklaṃ draṣṭum / ata upāya evāsau śukladarśanasya / yathāśvamedhādiṣv abhyupāya eva hiṃsā dharmasya / evam idam apaśabdajñānānuṣaktam eva śabdajñānam iti / nāsyādharmeṇa saṃbandhaḥ / evaṃ smṛtiḥ samarthitā bhavati / evam ānarthakyaṃ na bhavati smṛteḥ / 

atha vā kūpakhānakavad iti / iyam upapattiḥ / yady apy apaśabdajñānād adharmaḥ syāt tathāpy alpīyān / śabdajñānād eva tūtkṛṣṭo dharmo bhūyāṃś ceti pravartata evādhyayane vidyamāne 'pi doṣe / tat tu smṛtipraṇayanasāmarthyād evāvagamyate śabdajñānāt prakṛṣṭo dharma iti / tasyodāharaṇaṃ kūpakhānakaḥ / yathāsau bhūyasābhyudayena yogaṃ manyamāno doṣavaty api karmaṇi kūpakhanane pravartate śakyāmyenam api doṣam apanetum iti / anena ca śabdajñānasyopastutiḥ kriyate / yathoktaṃ nāvākśirāḥ śyenacitiṃ cinvīta pramāyuko 'sya yajamāno bhavatīti / neyam avākśiraso nindā / kiṃ tarhi / anena dvāreṇoccaśirasaḥ stutiḥ kriyate / viddhi ke mṛtāḥ striya iti / evaṃ ca kṛtvedaṃ vacanaṃ yājñavalkyasyaivaṃ vakṣyata imau palitau bāhū kvasvid brāhmaṇasya vaco babhūveti / yadi hy avākśirā yajamānaṃ ghātayedaham eva jñātvā nābhaviṣyam iti / tasmād idam api karma dṛṣṭam iti / anyeṣāṃ darśanam / nindaivaiṣāvākśirasa iti / 

yājñe karmaṇi niyamaḥ / etad uktaṃ bhavati / na sāmānyānuvādinī sā śrutiḥ / kiṃ tarhi / prakaraṇānuvādinī / prakaraṇārthaś cānyeṣām evaṃbhūto 'pi bhavati / agamyāgamanaṃ gosave / surāpānaṃ sautrāmaṇyām / sarvamedhe brāhmaṇavadhaḥ / yathā ca kūrmābhāve pratinidhir niyataviṣaya eva / yadi kūrmaṃ na vindet [karkaṃ kacchapam upadadhyāt ...] ... [avabhṛtheṣṭyante] piṅgalasya khalater viklidhasyātreyasya udake magnasya jumbakāya svāheti mūrdhanyāhutiṃ juh iti / tasmāt yājñe karmaṇi sa niyamo nānyatrety evam arthaṃ pratipādayati / evaṃ hi śrūyate iti / purākalpe / ye pratyakṣadharmāṇaḥ lokadharmā ye parokṣā lokasya te pratyakṣās teṣām / parāparajñāḥ / yuktāyuktajñāḥ / syuś ca tāvad āgamāt pratyakṣadharmāṇo na tu yuktāyuktajñāḥ / viditaveditavyāḥ / yajñārhā yājñāt karmaṇo 'nyatrāpabhāṣamāṇā api na parābhūtā iti / 

[13.1] atha vyākaraṇam ity asya śabdasya kaḥ padārtha iti / kim anenābhidhīyate kvārtharūḍha iti padārthagrahaṇenaitat pṛcchati / śabdo hi kaścit tulyarūpaḥ pravartamāno bhinnārtho bhinnanimittaḥ parasparama apekṣamāṇaḥ pravartate / yathāśvaśabdaḥ subantastiṅntaś ca / kaścit tu nimittasya tulyatvāt pravartate / yathā apśabdaḥ samudre bindau ca jātinimittaḥ / kaścij jātibhede 'pi rūḍhiviṣayaḥ kriyā tulyeti pravartate / yathā sarvaḥ aśnoterakṣaḥ padyateḥ pādaḥ mimītermāṣa iti / tatra kriyāsāmānyāt siddham iti / kaścit tadbhāvāpādanena vādhyāropeṇa vā yathā vāhīke gośabdo rajjudravye ca sarpaśabdaḥ / tatrāyaṃ vyākaraṇaśabdaḥ kiṃbhūta āśrīyate iti / anena dvāreṇa śabdapravṛttinimittamācikhyāsannupanyāsaṃ karoti / idaṃ punaḥ punar uktaṃna cāntareṇa vyākaraṇaṃ tannāvaiyākaraṇa iti tatrāsya padārtham ācaṣṭāṃ bhavān iti / sūtram / sūtrasamudāyo 'ṣṭādhyāyyām asyārthaḥ iti / ṣaṣṭhyartho 'nupapannaḥ / nanu ca sūtraśabdaprayogo 'py anupapannaḥ / ucyate / sāmānyopakrame 'bhidhāne sūtram / kiṃ sūtram iti vyākaraṇam iti viśeṣo yuktaḥ / vṛkṣaḥ śiṃśapeti yathā / śabdārthas tu bhedābhāvād aprayuktaḥ / yadā tu vyākaraṇaśabdaḥ samudāyavṛttiḥ sūtraśabdaḥ kasmiṃś cid avayave vṛtta upādīyate vṛddhirādaijityevamādau tadā ṣaṣṭhyartho 'py upapannaḥ / yadā tu dvāv api samudāyaśabdau tadā ṣaṣṭhyarthābhāva iti codyate / 

[13.2] śabdāpratipattiḥ / na hi vṛddhirādaijityetasmāc chabdān pratipadyāmahe / kiṃ tarhi / vyākhyānataḥ / udāharaṇād vayaṃ samānajātīyān śabdān pratipadyāmahe / nanu ca tad eva sūtraṃ vigṛhītaṃ vyākhyānaṃ bhavati / sūtrārthasyābhivyaktyupāyo vyākhyānam / yathā vaṭavṛkṣasthito daṃṣṭrītyasyārthasyābhivyaktiḥ kriyate vaṭe vṛkṣe sthito daṃṣṭrī / na kevalāni carcāpadāni / yathā vaṭe vṛkṣa iti carcāpadebhyaḥ pratipādyate 'rtho naivaṃ sūtrādvigṛhītāt / 

[13.3] śabde lyuḍarthaḥ / bhede sati ṣaṣṭhyarthapratipattir asti / śabdasya hy anvākhyāpakatvena sūtraṃ saṃbandhi bhavati / lyuḍarthas tu nopapadyate / saṃskriyamāṇo hi śabdo 'parārthatvāt pradhānam / na ca pradhānasya karaṇabhāvo yuktaḥ / yathā puroḍāśasyotpattisaṃskārapratipattiṣūtpādyatvāt saṃskāryatvāt pratipādyatvāt prādhānyam eva / guṇabhāvaḥ śabdapratipattau / 

[13.4] bhave ca / nanu cānvākhyāpakatvāt tasmin bhavāḥ syuḥ / evaṃ ca sati yāvān yogaḥ sarvaḥ vaiyākaraṇaḥ prāpnoti / tasmād avayavāvayavibhāvo 'trābhipretaḥ / 

[13.5] proktādayo 'pi nopapadyante / na hi śabdāḥ proktāḥ / purastāditam iti / bhāṣyasūtreṣu gurulāghavasyānāśritatvāt lakṣaṇaprapañcayoś ca mūlasūtreṣv āśrayaṇād ihāpi lakṣaṇaprapañcābhyāṃ pravṛttiḥ / tad yathā praskandanaṃ prapatanam iti / nanu ca bhīmādiṣv ete paṭhyante / ucyate / etad evāha kṛtyalyuṭo bahulam iti tasya bhīmādiḥ prapañca iti / 

atha vā śabdair api śabdā vyākriyante / śabdo hy uccārasya svarūpaṃ prati codayati viparītavyāvṛttatvam abhivyanakti / samānajātīyānāṃ śabdāntarāṇāṃ pratipādako bhavati / bhinnajātīyakasya sādhor api nivṛttiḥ sarvathā bhavati / tatraikadeśe bhāṣye kathyate nāśvo na gardabha iti / 

[13.6] evaṃ tarhi lakṣyalakṣaṇe vyākaraṇam / naitat pratipattavyaṃ yathānyo bilvaśarado vanaspatimāhānya eva phalam iti / nāpy apśaradavat ekaśabdatvam ekanimittatvāt / nāpy akṣavat kriyāsāmānyāt / nāpy ekaśeṣāśrayaṇam / evaṃ hi kalpyamāne sarvaḥ śabda eva bhāṣyam anu ghaṭate / yad vakṣyamāṇam anyaś cel lakṣyam āha anyo lakṣaṇam anyaḥ samudāyaṃ kim ucyate samudāyeṣu ca śabdaḥ pravṛtta iti / tasmāt yathaiva saṃghamaṇḍalasenāvanam ity ete śabdāḥ samudāyābhidhāyino nāvayavābhidhāyinaḥ evaṃ vyākaraṇaśabdo 'pi samudāyābhidhāyī nāvayavābhidhāyīti / evam apy ayaṃ doṣaḥ / yathaivaite vanādayonāvayave vartante evam ayam apy avayave na vartate / 

pūrve pañcālā iti / samudāyatvam āpāditaḥ samudāyaśabdenāpadiśyate / yathā gotvam āpādito bāhīko gośabdeneti / na hi śabdasyedaṃ saṃbhavati samudāyaṃ tyaktvāvayavam upasaṃkrāntum iti / ataḥ samudāyo yadāvayave kṛtsnam ātmānaṃ saṃniropayati tadā pravartate samudāyaśabdaḥ / evaṃ vyākaraṇaśabde 'pi samudāyavṛttir yadaikadeśe sūtre ātmānaṃ saṃniveśayati tadā tacchabdapravṛttiḥ kena kartavyā / 

tailaṃ bhuktaṃ ghṛtaṃ bhuktam iti / kasminn upaghātake 'nugrāhake vā ghṛte taile śabdaṃ parikalpya samudāyo 'vayavavṛttiḥ / evaṃ śuklādiṣv avayavasyaiva kasyacit śuklīkaraṇāt samudāya evāyaṃ paṭākhyo 'nyarūpaḥ kṛta ity ekadeśe samudāyaśabdo vartate / yathā na ca tāvat kārtsnyena ghaṭam upalabhate bhavati cāsya keṣucid evāvayaveṣu ghaṭabuddhiḥ evam eva sūtramātrāt samudāyaṃ pratipadyamāna eva vyākaraṇam iti tāvaty eva śabdaṃ prayuṅkte / yathā tādarthyād adhiśrayaṇamātraṃ dṛṣṭvā pacaty ayam iti prayuñjānaṃ prati vacanam ucyate nāyaṃ pacaty adhiśrayati kiṃ tu udakaṃ saṃpādayatīti /
