[13.7] vyapadeśivadbhāvena bhaviṣyati / vyapadeśivadbhāvaś ca kvacit kathaṃcit / iha ekāco dve prathamasya ity asty evānvayavyatirekasamadhigamyamānārthavattayā dhāturūpatāsti varṇarūpatā / tatraikena rūpeṇāśritasya rūpāntareṇa yukto vyapadeśaḥ / atha vā samudāyāvayavakalpanāyāṃ yo 'sau samudāyaḥ prayogasamavāyī sa śabdabhedāya eti itaḥ ayanam iti tasyām ekadeśa etir iti / yathā śilāputrako 'yam anekakriyāviṣayo dṛṣṭas tasyedaṃ śarīram iti / iha tu sthūlaśirā rāhur ity asaty api samudāyāvayavabhāve śabdavyāpāramātrād evāsati bhede vyapadeśaḥ / śabdo hi vastujñānasadbhāvāsadbhāvānapekṣaḥ kaṃcid evārthabhāgaṃ niścitya pravartate / tad yathā vidyamānām api parvatām aṅguleraṅguliśabdo [na] bravīty evaṃ vidyamānām api śiromātratāṃ rāho rāhuśabdo na bravīti / yāvān eva hi rāhuśabdavācyo 'rthas tāvān evocyate / tasya śirasā yukto vyapadeśaḥ / yathā saty apy ekatve vastuno 'vasthāyā abhidhānād bhavati prakarṣapratyayaḥ parud bhavān paṭutaraś caiṣama iti / evam ihāpy asty eva vyākaraṇasya granthasaṃdarbharūpatāsty evāvayavarūpatā / tatrāvasthābhedād upapanno vyapadeśaḥ / sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvād vā / iha sūtram iti sāmānyābhidhātṛ jñāyate / kasyeti viśeṣabhāve cānanyatvavādino 'pi upādīyate vyākaraṇam iti / yadā samudāyavṛttau vyākaraṇaśabdaḥ kvacid eva ca sūtre sūtraśabdas tadā mukhyavadabhedaḥ / tatra viśeṣa eva sāmānyam eva vā / 

tat nāsti vyapadeśivadbhāvaḥ / candanasya candanaṃ rūpādayo rūpādīnām iti / sāmānyaviśeṣabhāve tv ananyatvavādino 'pi bhavati vyapadeśaḥ candanasya gandha iti / avijānata etad evaṃ bhavati / etad uktaṃ bhavati / kaścid vyākhyānato 'pi na pratipadyate / kiṃ tarhi / akṣinikocādibhiḥ / tatra kiṃ śakyate vaktum / abhinayāt śabdapratipattir iti / tatraivaṃ pratipattavyam / pratipadaṃ nopāyā ete / sūtrāt tv abhivyaktiḥ / sūtrād eva tv abhivyaktārthāc chabdapravṛttir iti / yad etad udāharaṇādīnām upādānam etan nānutantrāṇāṃ bhāṣyasya vā brūyāt śabdāntaraṃ pratipadyeteti / kiṃ tarhi / anabhibyaktaṃ sūtre abhivyañjayed iti / etam eva cārthaṃ samarthayate / 

yo hy utsūtraṃ kathayen nādo gṛhyate / na tat gṛhyate / kimartha punarutsūtraṃ na gṛhyate / ucyate / smṛtiśāstram idam / tatra yadā kaścid evam eva brūyāt ayaṃ śabda iti so 'vaśyaṃ pṛcchyeta katham asmābhiḥ pratyetavyam iti / yadi smṛtisūtram āha saṃdhīyate 'thāpralāpas tasya gṛhyate / yathā pāṇinīyasūtram idaṃ nāsti nyāṅkoḥ pratiṣedha iti / tatraivaṃ vipratipattiḥ / naiyaṅkavam iti bhavitavyam iti / yas tv evaṃ prayuñjīta nyāṅkavam iti na vayam apaśabda iti pratipannāḥ / yadi tv asau smṛtyantaraṃ pradarśayati tasya hi pratiṣedhaḥ smṛtyantare / yatheha nyagrodhasya ca kevalasyeti muktasaṃśayaṃ pratipadyāmahe śabdo 'yam iti / tasmād idam eva pratipattavyaṃ sūtrād evodāharaṇādīni jñātārthāt śabdapravṛttir iti / 

[14.1] kimartho varṇānām upadeśaḥ / arthaprayukte śabdaprayoge dharmaniyamaḥ / na tatraite 'rthavantaḥ / tatraiṣām upadeśo 'narthakaḥ / samudāyārtho 'py upadeśo na saṃbhavati / dhātuprātipadikapratyayānām upadeśe tatsthānāṃ varṇānām apy upadeśa iti / vṛttisamavāyārtha upadeśaḥ / na hy eṣāṃ svarūpāvadhāraṇārtha upadeśaḥ / kiṃ tarhi / ānupūrvyārthaḥ / anenānupūrvyeṇa śāstre kiṃcit kāryaṃ kuryur iti / yad va śāstrāṅgopasaṃhārārtham etad ity evamartham ācaṣṭe / vṛttaye samavāyo vṛttisamavāya iti / ṣaṣṭhīsamāso 'yam / bhedas tādarthyena pradarśyate 'śvaghāsavat / vṛttyartho vā samavāya iti / vṛttyarthasamavāyasya vṛttiṃ prati nimittabhāvaḥ ākhyāyate / yathāsaṃkhyaṃ hi saṃniveśamātrād eva pravartate / pravṛttyarthaś cāsau samavāyaś ceti pravṛttisamavāya iti / yathā śākapārthiva iti / vṛttiprayojano vā iti / pārasparyeṇa nimittabhāvo nirdiśyate / pāramparyeṇa śāstrapravṛttiḥ samavāyasya prayojikā / yaiṣānubandhopalakṣitā śāstrapravṛttirakaḥ savarṇa ity asyāḥ pāramparyeṇa samavāyo nimittam iti / etac ca bhāṣye 'pi drakṣyata eva / kā punar vṛttiḥ / śāstrapravṛttiḥ / kuta evam / vṛttir iti kriyā / kriyā ca sādhanākāṅkṣiṇī / tatra prākaraṇikaṃ śabdānuśāsanaśāstraṃ sādhanatvena pratīyate / śāstrapravṛttau yaḥ samavāyas tadartho varṇānām upadeśaḥ / kim upadiśyate 'nenety upadeśaḥ / na / na hy etaiḥ kiṃcid upadiśyate / itaro bhāvasādhanatvam ācaṣṭe / uccāraṇam / kuta etat / diśir uccāraṇakriyaḥ / uccārya hi varṇānāhopadiṣṭā mayā varṇā iti / iha yasmin vyāpāre nivṛtte 'nantaro yaḥ śabdaḥ prayujyate tasya tādarthyaṃ vijñāyate / yathā bhuktavatsu praviṣṭeṣu brāhmaṇeṣu bhuktam brāhmaṇaiḥ praviṣṭā brāhmaṇā iti / gamyate etan nūnam ayaṃ bhujeḥ praviśeś cārtha iti / evam ihāpi varṇeṣūccāriteṣpanantaraṃ pṛcchati kimartha upadeśa iti / te pratipadyāmahe diśir uccāraṇakriya iti / 

[14.2] anubandhakaraṇārthaś ca / anubandhakaraṇaṃ śāstrasya pravṛttyaṅgam avaśyaṃ kartavyam / taccopadeśam antareṇa na bhavati / tatrānubandhā hala eva / ayaṃ tv ekaḥ ajanubandhaḥ laṇ iti lakāre akāraḥ / uraṇ rapara ity atra ca ṛkāralkārayo ralaparatvārthaḥ / pratyāhārārtham / pratyāhāro nāma vistīrṇasya yaḥ saṃkṣepaḥ / tatrādirantyena sahetyayaṃ pratyāhāro na saṃniveśam anubandhāṃś cāntareṇāstīti idam ucyate / samavāyaś cānubandhakaraṇaṃ ca pratyāhārārtham / pratyāhāro vṛttyarthaḥ / akaḥ savarṇe dīrgha eco 'yavāyāva iti / 

[14.3] iṣṭabuddhyarthaś ca varṇānām upadeśaḥ / upadeśe hi sati yathābhūtā uccāritās tathābhūtāḥ sādhavo viparītāḥ tv asādhava iti sādhutvapratipattyartham api kartavya upadeśa iti / udāttādīnām apy upadeśaḥ kartavyaḥ / ete hi varṇā ekaśrutyā paṭhitā acmātṛkā / tad atrodāttādibhinnānāṃ bhinnakālānāṃ ca sādhutvaṃ prāptamataḥ sarva upadeṣṭavyāḥ / nanu ca sarva evaite śāstreṇānvākhyātā udāttādayaḥ / ucyate / anupadiśyamānānāṃ kiṃbhūtā udāttādayaḥ pratipattavyā iti svarūpeṇānupadeśāt aśakyā teṣu pratipattir iti / 

[14.4] ākṛtyupadeśāt siddham / ākṛtyupadeśe nāntarīyako viśeṣaḥ / sa tv anāśritas tasmin kāle iti kālaguṇabhinneṣv ākṛtir astīti pratipatsyante ayam evāsau varṇo yo 'kṣarasamāmnāye upadiṣṭaḥ / yathā ekākṛtyupadeśe bhinneṣv api ca ya ākṛtiviśeṣeṣu sa evāyam iti pratipadyate / tatra yathaivākṛtisadbhāvāt kālaguṇabhinnānāṃ sādhutvam evaṃ guṇasvarabhinnānām apy ākṛtisadbhāvāt prasaktaṃ sādhutvam / atas tannivṛttyarthaṃ yatnaḥ kartavya ity āha / saṃvṛtādīnāṃ pratiṣedho vaktavyaḥ / saṃvṛto nāma yo bāhyaḥ prayatnaḥ / ābhyantaro hi prayatno 'kārasya niṣpādakaḥ / naivaṃ kalaḥ / dhmāto yaḥ śvāsabhūyiṣṭhaḥ / eṇīkṛto 'vyaktaḥ / etāḥ svaradoṣabhāvanāḥ / etāni svaradoṣagotrāṇi / avagato hi vaktṝṇāṃ pramādāśaktijaḥ svaradoṣa iti / gargādibidādipāṭhāt saṃvṛtādīnāṃ nivṛttirbhaviṣyati / evaṃ manyate / naiva kvacid varṇā uccāryante / kiṃ tarhi / śabdasamudāyā eva paṭhyante / varṇātmakaś ca samudāyaḥ / samudāyaś ca nivṛttakalādikaḥ paṭhyate / atra siddhā saṃvṛtādīnāṃ nivṛttir iti / asty anyad gargādibidādipāthe prayojanam / etad uktaṃ bhavati / evam artho gargādibidādipāthaḥ / katham / anena saṃniveśena sādhutvaṃ syād anyena mā bhūt / garga iti / kiṃ vā kartavyam / pāṭhasthitānāṃ kalādinivṛttiḥ kṛtā syāt / yāni tu na paṭhyante śabdarūpāṇi eteṣv anivṛttāḥ kalādaya iti / evaṃ tarhi aṣṭādaśadhā bhinnā śāstrānte pratyāpattir yā tāṃ nidarśanaprakāreṇa kariṣyāmīti / kimarthaṃ punar nivṛttakalādikām ity ucyeta / na nivṛttasaṃvṛtādikām iti / saṃvṛtatvāt / kāryārthaṃ kalādayo 'bhupagamyante / śīṅmāsiṃ ca kalaṃ dhmātaṃ vādhyeṣye / ṅkāram akāraṃ ca no 'dhyeṣye / evaṃ ca vakṣyami / kalād dhmātād ātmanepadaṃ bhavati / atra tv idam aśakyaṃ pratipattum iko yaṇacīti / anubandhe 'sati itsaṃjñāśāstraṃ ca nāstīdam ādir antyena saheteti / tatra yāvanti pratyāhāropalakṣitāni kāryāṇi teṣu sarve nirdeṣṭavyāḥ //

[14.5] evaṃ tarhy ubhayam anena kriyate / samavāyaś ca sādhutvena pratipādyate / yathākārādayas te caivaṃbhūtā eva nirdiṣṭāḥ / kalādikāḥ sarvatrāsādhava iti / kathaṃ punae ekena yatnenobhayaṃ labhyam / iha saṃdhyakṣareṣu samudāyapara upadeśa iti nāvayavau varṇakāryaṃ pratipadyete / yathā varṇānām upadeśo 'vayavavinirjñānārtha ity akṣarasamāmnāye 'vayavā evāśrīyante na samudāyaḥ / evaṃ gargādibidādipāthe 'vayavā eva vinirjijñāsitāḥ syus tadanurodhāt tu samudāyopādānam / atha vā samudāyā eva / yadi samudāyās tatrāvayavānām evaṃviṣayāṇāṃ kalādyabhāvaḥ syāt / anyatra nivarteta eva / athāvayavaparam / evaṃ sati samudāyasya sādhutvapratipattir aśakyā / tataś ca gargādayo na nivartitā iva / labhyam ity āha / katham / dvigatā api hetavo bhavanti / dvāv arthau gatāḥ dvigatāḥ / hetavaḥ kāryanirvṛttau samarthāḥ / iha loke eka eva hetur dvayor arthayoḥ prasādhako dṛṣṭaḥ / ekam udakam / tadviṣayā kriyā tyāgalakṣaṇā āmramūle kṛtā pitṛtarpaṇe druseke ca sama eko hetuḥ / anyatra tad evodakaṃ na dvigataṃ bhavati yadā devadattayajñadattau tarpayitavyau bhavataḥ / āmrā api viprakṛṣṭadeśāt sektavyā bhavanti / tatra yathaivodakadānaṃ dṛṣṭam adṛṣṭārthaṃ sādhayituṃ samartham ity evaṃ buddhyā prakramya pravartamānam ubhayaṃ niṣpādayaty evaṃ gargādibidādipāthaḥ samartha ubhayaṃ kartum avayavān samudāyaṃ ca kārye pravartamāna iti / atha vā prasaṅgalakṣaṇārthasiddhiḥ / yady arthāprayojako 'nyadvāreṇārthaṃ pratipadyate sa prasaṅga ity ucyate / yathā bhojanenārthī yaḥ prayojako 'dhiśrayaṇasya ca vighasāśivadaprayojakaḥ / yo hi niṣṭhite 'py anne punaḥ pācayet sa evaṃbhūtaḥ yadā tadarthaṃ bahvannaṃ prasādhitam / saṃvṛtādiṣu pratiṣedhaś codyate / tatra yady avayavāḥ samudāyopadeśaparaśabdena svarūpaṃ sarvatraiva viśiṣṭaṃ pratipādyeran ko doṣaḥ syāt / evam āmrāḥ pṛthak sekasya prayojakāḥ pitaro 'prayojakāḥ / tatra prayuktam udakadānaṃ prasaṅgenānubhavantīti //

[14.6] tathā dviṣṭhāny api vākyāni dṛśyante / etat kathayati / na kevalam arthadharma evāyam / kiṃ tarhi / śabdadharmo 'pi / iha kaścid eka eva śabdaḥ śaktipracitaḥ / tasya kevalaṃ śakyavacchedamātraṃ kriyate / yathaika eva purāśabda ārācchabdaś ca purāṇasahavacanaḥ saṃnikṛṣṭaviprakṛṣṭavacanaś ca / evaṃ śvetaśabdo 'nekaśaktipracitaḥ / tasya kevalaṃ śaktyavacchedamātraṃ kriyate / śveto gaur iti / śvā sa ita iti prayoktā prayuṅkte / pratipattāraḥ yathāśaktyavacchedenārthaṃ pratipadyante / arthāntareṇa dvāvetau śabdāv uccāritau / yathā pradīpas tantreṇa pravartamāno 'rthināṃ yathābhipretam arthaṃ nirvartayati / ekasyaiva vā vidyādikā didṛkṣoś ca tantreṇa nirvartayati / evaṃ śabdeṣu tādṛśīṃ kriyām āpāditakaraṇaparispandāṃ yathā tantreṇa dvād eva śabdāv uccāryete / upāyadvayaṃ ca loke śabdaprayogo vā yaugapadyaṃ vā / kadācit kramavataḥ śabdān prayuṅkte / sa katham / akṣo bhajyatām akṣo dīvyatām iti / kadācid upasaṃhṛtya kramaṃ yaugapadyenoccārayati / akṣā bhajyantāṃ dīvyantām iti / 
dvāvabhyupāyau śabdānāṃ prayoge samavasthitau / 
kramo vā yaugapadyaṃ vā yau loko nātivartate // iti /
tasmād ihāpi gargādibidādipāthas tantrābhyupāyaḥ samudāyāvayavāyoḥ pratipādaka iti / 

[14.7] atha vedaṃ tāvad ayaṃ praṣṭavya iti / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / sarveṣv evāmī samudāyasthā upadiṣṭāḥ / tatra siddhaiva kalādinivṛttir iti / dhātavo 'pi śuddhāḥ paṭhyante / culumpādayo 'pi bhāṣya evoccāryanta ity abhiprāyaḥ / yāni tarhy agrahaṇāni ḍitthādīni eteṣām apy avaśyaṃ svaravarṇānupūrvījñānārtham upadeśaḥ kartavyaḥ / atha vā gargādibidādīnāṃ ca svaravarṇānupūrvyarthe upadeśaḥ kartavyaḥ / evaṃ smaryate / iha yad eṣām uccāraṇaṃ tat pratyayavidhyartham iti / atha vā anenaivaṃ bruvatā etadāśritaṃ vyutpādyante ete sarva iti / ye na vyutpādyante teṣāṃ ko vṛttāntaḥ / ucyate / etamarthaṃ pratipādayati / ye 'py ete gargādayaḥ paṭhitāḥ ete 'pi na pāṇininotpāditāḥ / kiṃ tarhi evaṃbhūtā iti te smaryante / yathaiva ta evaṃbhūtā eva smaryante evam anye 'pi śabdāḥ sāmānyena pratipāditā uṇādayo bahulam ity anena / pratyayataḥ prakṛteś ca tadūhyam iti / śiṣṭaprayogam abhisamīkṣya prakṛtirūhitavyā pratyayaś ca / avaśyaṃ caitadaivaṃ pratipattavyam iti / tathā hi / saty apy udāttādyupadeśe kalādinivṛttau kṛtāyāṃ ḍittha ity ayaṃ sādhur na tu ḍuttha iti / saiṣā śiṣṭapratipattir avalambitavyā / tasmān na śiṣṭaprayogamantareṇaitat bhavati sarvatra kalādinivṛttir upapannā iti //


iti bhartṛhariṭīkāyāṃ prathamādhyāyasya prathamam āhnikam //


Mahābhāṣyadīpikāyāṃ dvitīyam āhnikam 

[1] akārasya vivṛtopadeśaḥ / spṛṣṭatādayaś catvāra ābhyantarāḥ prayatnāḥ savarṇasaṃjñāyāḥ pravṛttau ca nivṛttau ca vyāpriyante / tatrākāraḥ saṃvṛtaḥ ākāro vivṛta iti sāvarṇyasyābhāvāt ākāro 'sati grahaṇe kāryaṃ na pratipadyeta, ato vivṛtopadeśa āśrīyate / nanu ca niyataprayatnatvāt varṇānāṃ sthānaniyamavat, yathā niyatasthānatvād aśakya ikāras tv oṣṭhābhyām uccārayitum akāro vā dantena, evaṃ saṃvṛto 'kāraḥ kthamiva śakyo vivṛta uccārayitum? ucyate / kaścid eva prayatno na śakyate kartuṃ vaktrāpramādāt / ayaṃ tu saṃdaṣṭādivat śakyate 'pramādāt kartum iti / ataś ca śakyate, yat vivṛtopadeśaś codyate /

[1.1] tatrākāra iti kāraśabdaḥ pratyayo "varṇāt kāraḥ" (P 3.3.108, vt. 3) iti / varṇavācino varṇāt svārthe varṇābhidheyatāyāṃ (? varṇābhidhāyino 'yaṃ) pratyaya iti / vākyasthenākāreṇa sūtrasthaḥ 'aiuṇ' ity akāro vyapadiśyate / atha kakāra iti kathaṃ pratyayaḥ? ny hya [hy ayaṃ] varṇaḥ / kiṃ tarhi? varṇasamudāyaḥ / naiṣa doṣaḥ / evam artham evedam upanyastam, 'varṇābhidhāyino 'yaṃ pratyaya' iti / vyañjanam aśakyam anackam uccārayitum iti sāckād varṇād varṇābhidhāyinaḥ kārapratyayaḥ / pratipādyamānas tv anacka eva pratīyate / yathā jihvāmūlīyopadhmānīyāv upasthitau khakāraḥ phakāraś coccāryete, pratipannau tu śuddhāv eva / 

[1.2] nanu ca kārapratyayasya nityatvāt "asya cvau" (P 7.4.32) iti nirdeśānupapattiḥ / akārasya cvāv iti bhavitavyam / ucyate / vācyavācakabhede 'vivakṣite sa evāyam ity ekatvādhyavasāye varṇavācitvaṃ nāstīti pratyayābhāvaḥ / atha vāsmād eva nirdeśād anityaḥ pratyayaḥ iti pratipattavyam /

[1.3] akārasya vivṛtopadeśaḥ / vivṛtaśabdasya sāpekṣatvāt asāmarthye saty upadeśaśabdena samāsānupapattiḥ / ucyate / vivṛtasya guṇatvāt guṇasya guṇyapekṣatvam avyāvṛttam ity apekṣāvān pravartate / samudāyaviśeṣaṇaṃ vā sad vṛttasyāvayavam api saṃsprakṣyati / 
(p. 2) kiṃ ca / gamakatvaṃ tatroktam "iha gamatvād [gamakatvād] vṛttir bhaviṣyati" (MBh 2.19.11) / atha vā vivṛtenopadeśa iti "sādhanaṃ kṛtā . . . " (P 2.1.33, vt. 2) iti samāse kṛte sa vivṛtopadeśo 'kārasyeti doṣābhāvaḥ / 

[1.4] atha kimartham idam ucyate "akārasya vivṛtopadeśaḥ" iti, yāvatā sūtroccāraṇād evādhigatam anena vivṛto 'yam akāra iti / kaścid viśeṣaḥ śabdagato 'bhyāsād eva gamyate / yathodāttādir vedādhyāyinām eva prasiddhaḥ, tathā ṛṣabha [ṛṣabhaḥ], gāndhāra iti gāndharvāṇām eva / evam ayaṃ vivṛto durvedyo 'bhyāsād ['nabhyāsād] ity ataḥ kathayati, vivṛto 'yam upadiṣṭa iti / 

[1.5] anye manyante / ayam api pratyāhāre kṛtapratyāpattir eva paṭhitaḥ / ayaṃ tūccāryāśravaṇārtham akāraṃ pratyāyayati saṃvṛtaḥ pratīyate / yathā 'ṣṭhā gatinivṛttau' (DhP 1.175), "dhaḥ karmaṇi ṣṭran" (P 3.2.181) iti ca kṛtaṣṭutva uccāryamāṇo lakṣyaviṣayaṃ pratīyamānaṃ sthā iti nn iti [trann iti] caivaṃrūpaṃ pratipādayati / tasmiṃś ca prayogaviṣaye eva rūpe ṣṭutvasyāsiddhatvāt dhātvādivikāraḥ pravartate / tatra kṛtapratyāpattāv aśvārkādiṣu teṣāṃ kasyacid evānukaraṇe vyaktipakṣe / ākṛtipakṣe tu sāmānyam anukriyate / tatrākṣarasamāmnāyikasya pratyā [pratyāpattau] sarve kṛtapratyāpattaya iti naiva bhavatyādiśabde rūpasiddhau pratyāpattim upāditsanti /

[1.6] anye manyante / naivāyam anukaraṇo na ca kṛtapratyāpattiḥ kasyacit pratipādakaḥ / kiṃ tarhi? anarthaka evāyam akṛtapratyāpattir vivṛta uccārita iti /

[1.7] kiṃ prayojanam? ākāragrahaṇārthaḥ  nanv evaṃ nirdeṣṭavyam, 'ākāragrahaṇam' iti / ucyate / . . . 

[1.8] [ucyate /] kadācid dharmī dharmavyapadeśakaḥ / tadyathā---viṣāṇī, kakudmān, prāntevāladhiḥ, sāsnāvān iti / tadvānetra [tadvān eva] vyapadiśyamāno viśeṣaṃ pratipadyate, hrasva iti / evaṃ dīrgha iti / evaṃ ca tricaturmātra iti / tatra yasyām avasthāyāṃ karaṇasaṃnipātād dhvanir dhvanyantaram ārabhate tadoparata evāsyavyāpāra iti yuktam ucyate---bāhyaś ca punar āsyāt kāla iti / athāpy ādya eva yatnaḥ pratipadyate, yathā śarkaronmajjanena recakādikarmaṇi, bāhya eva ya ākśepaviśeṣas ta [tata] eva pravṛttir ityevamarthaṃ prayujyamāno nābhipradeśa eva vāyoḥ prakāraprayatnam ārabhyata iti kālasyā [kālasyāsyād] bāhyatvam asty eva / (p. 3)

[1.8.1] nanu cedaṃ virudhyate "tapas [taparas]  tatkālasya" (P 1.1.70) iti / evaṃ hi tatroktam "sphoṭas tāvān eva" (MBh 1.181.21) kevalaṃ vṛttibhedaḥ / tataś ca sarvāsu vṛttiṣu tatkālatvam iti ihāpi hrasvadīrghayoḥ kālasya tulyatvāt khaṭvābhir iti bhavitavyam aisbhāvena / ucyate / naiṣa doṣaḥ / bhyupagamāt [nityatvābhyupagamāt] / evam artham eva śabdānāṃ nityatvam abhyupagamyate / tasya nityasya punar ye 'bhivyañjakās te ca kecit prākṛtāḥ, kecid vaikṛtāḥ / yaḥ karaṇasaṃnipātād utpadyate, yaś ca tasmāt, tau prākṛtau / tābhyāṃ viśeṣopalabdheḥ / yas tu dhvato [dhvanito] dhvanir utpadyate, sa vaikṛtaḥ, tato viśeṣābhāvāt / kevalaṃ tu sa evam upalabdhikṛt iti / eṣa viśeṣaḥ---yadi [dīrghapluteṣu] śabdāntarābhāvād bhinnakālatvam, drutamadhyamavilambitāsv api bhinnakālatvam / atha dīrghapluteṣu punaḥ punaḥ karaṇābhighātaḥ taddrutādiṣv evaṃ sati kālasyāhyatvaṃ [kālasyāsyādbāhyatvaṃ] durupapādaṃ, karaṇābhighātasyānuparamād iti / 

[1.9] kiṃ punar idam iti / kim idam apūrvaṃ pāṇini [pāṇininā]
 vivṛtatvam akṛtam upasaṃkhyāyate, uta kṛtasyānvākhyānam iti / yad ayam 'a a' (P 8.4.68) iti . . . pratyāpattiṃ śāsti / yadīha saṃvṛta evākāraḥ pratijñātaḥ syāt, kiṃ tasya pratyāpattyā? tatra pratyāpattivacanād avasīyate vivṛto 'kāra iti, yaś ca pratyāhāre, yaś cānuvṛttinirdeśe 'asya cvau (P 7.4.32), 'akaḥ savarṇe dīrghaḥ' (P 6.1.101) ityevamādiṣu, yaś ca prayogaśabdeṣv aśvārkārthādiśabdeṣu / asti hy anyad etasya vacane prayojanam / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / 'a a' iti yo 'yaṃ saṃskāraśabdo 'yaṃ prayogaśabdeṣv evākārasyopapadyate, nānarthakānāṃ pratipādakaśabdānām / ayaṃ ca vaktṛpramādajo doṣo 'sty eva vitatvam [vivṛtatvam] / ātaś cāsti yat pāṇininā kṛtam iti / evam atikhaṭvādiṣu prayogaśabdeṣu vivṛtatvam uccārayet / tasmāt pratyāpattir ucyate / 

atha ca na vivṛta upadiṣṭaḥ syāt, tatra codyate---na cet pratyāhāre 'nuvṛttisaṃdeśeṣu (? 'nuvṛttinirdeśeṣu) cākāro vivṛtas tatra prayatnabhedāt nāpy ākāraḥ savarṇo nāpi tadādeśaḥ / asāvarṇyād agrahaṇe sati sthānyādeśayor actvābhāvaḥ / [na] sthānī [ac], nāpy ādeśa iti kasya pratyāpattiḥ? ucyate / liṅgād idam avasīyate bhavaty ākārasya akāra iti, yad ayam ittve ". . . ātaḥ sthāne yakapūrvāyāḥ" (P 7.3.46) ity āha / na "ke 'ṇaḥ" (P 7.4.13) hrasvatvam antareṇākāro 'sti; yasmāl liṅgāt vikalāṅgā paribhāṣā nopatiṣṭheta iti ca "ūkālo 'j  jhrasva . . . " (P 1.2.27)  (p. 4) iti ca yakapūrva ā / dy  [yady] evam, idaṃ kasmān na kalpyate, lit [liṅgāt] sāvarṇyam astīti? yadi ca sāvarṇyam, kiṃ vivṛtena pratyāpattyā vā? ucyate / liṅgam anumānapakṣaḥ / [na] sarvatrānumānāt pratipattir iti viśiṣṭaviṣayam anumānaṃ pratipadyāmahe / sāvarṇyaṃ nāsti liṅgāt / kiṃ tarhi? ādeśamātram iti / atha vā gostrīgrahaṇaṃ tatra kṛtam / tatra strīgrahaṇam anavakāśam iti pravartetādeśo, na tu sāvarṇyam iti / 

tatrāntarya [? atrāntaryata] iti / vaktuḥ pramādajo 'styaratam ['sty antaratama] ity abhiprāyaḥ / naiva loke na [ca] vede iti / etad uktaṃ bhavati / bhavatoktā doṣā vaktṛpramādajāḥ santi / [na] tāvatāṃ nivṛttyarthaṃ pratyāpattir vaktavyā / santi doṣāḥ kalādayaḥ / na ca tadviṣayāsti pratyāpattiḥ / te hi gargādibidādipāṭhād eva / tasmāt pāṇinir eva vivṛtatvaṃ pratijānīte ity asyaiva liṅgaṃ pratyāpattiḥ / kaḥ punar atra viśeṣa iti / vākyagataṃ vyāpāraṃ pṛcchati / vākye hi kalpyatvāt vākyaśeṣasya, kartavya iti vā kṛta iti vā nāsti kaścid viśeṣa iti / na khalu kaścid . . . āhopuruṣikāmātraṃ tu / ahaṃpuruṣa ity ucyate / [ahopuruṣaḥ], ātmasaṃbhāvitaḥ, parasmād utkṛṣṭam ātmānaṃ yo manyate, ahaṃkāravān / tasya bhāva iti manojñādidarśanād vuñ / āhopuruṣiketi ahaṃyutvam ucyate / bhavān āha / na samyag bhavatoktam, mayaiva tu yad uktaṃ tad eva yuktaṃ pratyāpattivacanād iti / 

[1.10] tasmād [? tasya] vivṛtopadeśāt (vt. 2) / ākṣarasamāmnāyikaḥ anarthako vivṛtaḥ kṛtapratyāpattiḥ, yaḥ śāstra eva 'aiuṇ' ity asya pratipādakaḥ / yathā 'vartamāne laṭ' (P 3.2.123) iti noccāryamāṇasyetsaṃjñā, kiṃ tarhi? varṇavikriyāviṣayasya buddhisthasya / sa tv anena pratipādyate / atha cānukaraṇo 'yaṃ samudāyasthānāṃ dravyavac copadeśe kasyad [kasyacid] evānukaraṇaṃ syād itīdam ucyate---dhātvādisthasyāpi vivṛtopadeśaḥ kartavyaḥ / itarathā hi prayatnabhedāt ākṣarasamāmnāyikenāgrahaṇāt asaty actve 'sthānitvāt śāmyatīti dīrgho na syāt / prātipadikasyāntodātto bhavatīty etan na syāt, dṛṣad iti / pratyaye cāyaka ity āyādeśo na syāt / avagata iti svaro na syāt / yad ayam 'akaḥ sarvarṇe dīrghaḥ' (P 6.1.101) . . . iti / ayam anubandhena cihnena jñāyate, sa evāyam ākṣarasamāmnāyika iti / yadi dhātvāsthaś [dhātvādisthaś] ca na gṛhyeta, etāvat brūyāt 'ikaḥ savarṇe dīrghaḥ' iti / na hi r [dvayor] ākṣarasamāmnāyikayor (p. 5) iti / yady api eko [eko 'pi] nāsti, tathāpi dviviṣayatvād dīrghasyedam uktam / [tadartham /] ākārārtham etat syāt, sa hi vivṛtatvāt savarṇo bhavatīti / ka eṣa yatna [yatnaś] codyate vivṛtopadeśo nāma / etad evāha---ko 'trādaro yāvatā dhātavaḥ paṭhyanta eva / na ca te paṭhyamāṇāḥ saṃvāreṇa vivāreṇa vā ṅimcit gauravaṃ lāghavaṃ vā janayantīti / yāny etāni [prātipadikāny] agrahaṇāni / ya ete raṅkādayo loke prayuktā eteṣu saṃvṛto 'kāraḥ prayujyate / śāstre caiṣām uccāraṇaṃ nāsti / na coccāraṇam antareṇa śakyaṃ vivṛtatvaṃ pratijñātum / sati copadeśe guru śāstraṃ bhavati / tasmād idaṃ paṭhitavyam, dhātvādisthaś ca vivṛta iti / atha dhātugrahaṇaṃ kimartham, yāvatā paṭhyanta eva dhātavaḥ? ucyate / prātipadikārthaṃ ced idam ārabhyate, taddhātvartham api bhaviṣyati / tataś coccāraṇe saṃvṛtā evoccārayiṣyante, kārye tu vivṛtā iti jñāsyante / evaṃ ca sati karaṇavigamo 'py ākhyātuṃ na bhavatīti / 

[1.11] dīrghaplutavacane ca saṃvṛtanivṛttyarthaḥ (vt. 3) / kecid varṇayanti---ākṣarasamāmnāyikasyaiva vivṛtopadeśe dvitīyaṃ prayojanam anvākhyāyate / tasya saṃvṛtatvād dīrghaplutāvāntaratamyāt saṃvṛtau prāpnutaḥ / kecid dhātvādisthasya vivṛtopadeśe prayojanam ācakṣate / atra cedaṃ codyate---saṃvṛtatvād dīrghaplutasyānactvāt sthānitvānupapattir iti / naiva loke na ca vede iti / śiṣṭaprayogaṃ hetum āha / na hi śiṣṭāḥ dīrghaplutau [saṃvṛtau] prayuñjate / sthānī prakalpayed etau iti / nirvartakam idaṃ lakṣaṇam iti evaṃ kecit manyante / ihabhavantas tv āhur yathaiva śiṣṭā antasthāḥ sānunāsikāḥ prayuñjate, iha tu sthānentaratamaparibhāṣāśrayaṇāt bhavati pratipattiḥ, anujñāto 'smin viṣaye 'nunāsika iti / evaṃ yady apy atra dīrghaplutau saṃvṛtau na syātām, tadāntaratamyāt syād anujñā, iti / viṣamapanyāsaḥ [viṣama upanyāsaḥ] / [yuktaṃ yat] satas tatra prakḷptir bhavati / anedam [anenedam] āha---aśakyatvād iti / iha kiṃcit śakyam, yathā hastābhyāṃ śakyeta gantum, na tu śirasā / evaṃ cākāro dīrghaḥ pluto vāśakya eva saṃvṛta uccāratuṃ [uccārayituṃ] sarveṇa lokena / niyatāś [aniyatāś] ca varṇadoṣāḥ / evaṃ hy uktāḥ---svaradoṣabhāvanāḥ / ato 'nye vyañjanadoṣāḥ (MBh 1.13.26--27) iti / antasthās tu śakyanta uccārayituṃ sānunāsikās tatrāpi repho 'śakya iti / dvau hetū vyapadiṣṭau, śiṣṭāgo [śiṣṭāprayogo] 'śakyatvaṃ ca / evam api kuta etat / athocyate, saṃvṛtaś ced akāra īkārokārayo vṛtatvāt [īkārokārayor vivṛtatvāt] tulyaprayatnatvaṃ kutaḥ? evaṃ hy uktaṃ 'vivṛtaṃ karaṇaṃ svaroṣmaṇām' iti / tatra varṇayanti---'yvṛtaḥ saṃvṛtā anyatrārbhavasāmnaḥ' (p. 6) iti / ec [etac] cāsati / pārṣadakṛtir eṣā / asyām eva śākhāyām ārbhavaṃ muktvā sāmāntare na vivṛtatvam, anyeṣāṃ tu vivṛtā eva / tasmād ayam arthaḥ / evam api kuta etad iti / vivṛte 'pi saty akārasya na sidhyati / akāro hivivṛtaḥ, ākāro hi vivṛtataraḥ, ikārokārau tu vivṛtatarāv api / tatra tulyaprayatnābhyām eva bhavitavyam / yady evaṃ grahaṇam api na prāpnoti / vacanād grahaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati / atha vā, vivṛtopadeśa etad uktaṃ bhavati, vitataropadeśa [vivṛtataropadeśa] iti / tat kathaṃ 'saṃdhyakṣareṣu yad atrāvarṇaṃ vivṛtataraṃ tad anyasmāt' (MBh 1.25.14) iti? etad api vivṛtatamam iti pratipattavyam / 

[2] tatrānuvṛttinirdeśe savarṇāgrahaṇam anaṇtvāt (vt. 4) / 'aiuṇ' ity aparaḥ / 'akaḥ savarṇe . . . ' (P 6.1.101) ity anyaḥ / śuddha eva 'asya cvau' (P 7.4.32), 'yasyeti ca' (P 6.4.148) iti / tatrānubandhena pratipadyā [pratipadyāmahe], sa vāyam [evāyam] iti / śuddheṣu tu nāsty aṇtvam / tatra yathaivākāraḥ pratyāyya eva nityaṃ na tu pratyāyakaḥ, evam ayam apy akāraḥ pratyāyya eva / tenāyam eva kāryaṃ pratipadyeta, śuklībhavati / mālībhavatīti na syāt / evaṃ cauḍhiḥ, bālākir iti /

[2.1] ekatvāt [akārasya] siddham (vt. 5) / anuvṛttād atvāt / 

[2.2] anubandhasaṃkaras tu (vt. 6) / vastuto 'kāsyābhedāc ['kārasyābhedāc] ca / evaṃ 'karmaṇy aṇ' (P 3.2.1) ity asya / evaṃ ca 'āto 'nupasarge kaḥ' (P 3.2.3) iti / yathaiva ca muṇḍena kṛte caurye kuntalī saṃpanna iti na ca [na] gṛhyate cora iti / tatra goda iti paratvāt kaḥ syāt, uttarārthaṃ kitkaraṇam / atha vānavakāśatvāt paryāyaḥ syāt /
