[2.5] viṣayeṇa tu nānāliṅgakaraṇāt (vt. 9) / vākyakāraḥ sāmānyaṃ karaṇaṃ nirdiśati, bhāṣyakāro bhedam adhikaraṇabhāvaṃ ca vīpsāsaptamībhyāṃ kathayati / ekam evāyaṃ sarvaguṇam uccārayet / karmaṇy upapade kaṇpra [kaṭpratyayo; kaṇṭpratyayo] bhavatīti brūyāt / (p. 7) yāvato vā śaknoty uccārayituṃ tāvata uccārayet / anyāṃs tu luptanirdiṣṭān pratijānīta iti / āyamya hi dvayor evetsaṃjñāt [evetsaṃjñā syāt] / itsaṃjñaiva śāstrāpekṣayā āyatīty [āyacchatīty] ekakartṛkasiddhatvāt [ekakartṛkatvasya siddhatvāt] / tatrā [tatrāpi] dvayor itsaṃjñā, antarālapatitānāṃ tu naiva syāt / viṣayeṇa [tu] punar liṅgakaraṇāt siddham / nanu ca niyamārtham etat syāt---tatkṛtam eva bhavati, na ñnitkṛtam antodāttādīti [? ādyudāttādīti] / vidheye 'sati niyamo vijñāyata iti idam eva jñāsyate, anyatra tatkṛtaṃ na bhavatīti / itarathā hy evaṃ vaktavyaṃ 'śivādibhyaḥ a' iti / lokata etat siddham / naivam ucyate, ekam evāyaṃ sarvaguṇam uccārati [uccārayed iti] / kathaṃ tarhi? śabdavat yatra liṅgaṃ kārya āśrīyate, talloke talliṅgasyaiva bhavati / caurye tu muṇḍādayo 'śabdavantaḥ / tatra yathaiva muṇḍādayo (?) na kartavyam iti tatra śikhī jaṭī vā kāryaṃ pratipadyate, evam ihāpi kāryārtham anubandhāsaṅgās tena tasmin tasmin viṣaye tattat kālaṃ [? tat tat kāryaṃ] lapsyante iti / 'a sāṃpratike' (P 4.3.9) ity atra kimarthaṃ ṇitkṛtaṃ na bhavati? na hy asmin kiṃcid asti liṅgam ity aniyamaḥ prāpnoti / ucyate / 'madhyānmo 'sāṃpratike' (P 4.3.8--9) iti paryudāsena siddhe niranbandhāśrayaṇam evamartham, anubandhaviyoga eva liṅgaṃ kathaṃ syād iti / tatra yathaiva kaścid ucyate, 'iha nagno bhava' iti, evaṃ 'niranubandhako bhava' iti niyujyate / 

[2.6] āvṛttisaṃkhyānāt (vt. 10) / iha lo [loke] yatra mukhyā saṃkhyā . . . śatvaṃ [saptadaśatvaṃ] saṃpadyate tatra cāvṛttāv aniyataviṣayāyām ucyate 'triḥ prathamām anvāha trir uttamām anvāha' (Tai.S. 2.5.7) iti / prathamottame āvṛttisaṃkhye iti / bhaved āvṛtti [āvṛttitaḥ] kāryaṃ yatrānekatvam āśrīyate / yatra tv ekatvam eva tatra svāśrayasyānivṛttatvāt prāpnoty eva svaraḥ, kiriṇā giriṇeti / etad api siddham / [saṃkhyā] saṃkhyāntareṇātyantaṃ nivartyate, gauṇena mukhyena vā / yathā śāstre naidheya iti sati sthānittve [sthānivattve], tryacko'yam iti dvyacka [dvyackatve] bādhite, ḍhako 'prāptir itīdam udāhriyate 'pūrvavidhāv iti kimartham' (MBh 1.142.23) iti / tadyathā / ṛṣisahasram ekāṃ kapilām ekaikaśa iti / ṛṣisahasram iti sāmānādhikaraṇya ekaika [ekaikaśaḥ] iti sākṣāt saṃbandhaḥ / ṣaṣṭhīsamāse vā sāmarthyalakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandho bhujivat iti / te hi parasparaṃ vittena svīkṛtya sahasrakṛtvo daduḥ / etac ca dānaṃ svīkṛtasya vastunaḥ parityāgaḥ / tatraikasyā api dāt [dānāt] 'sahasradakṣiṇā ānīyantām' ity e 'py [ete 'py] ānīyante, (p. 8) 'ekadakṣiṇā ānīyantām' ity ete nānīyante / yathaivātra saṃkhyā nivartikaiva pūrvasaṃkhyāyā evam ihāpi iti doṣābhāvaḥ /

[3] ānyabhāvyaṃ tu kālaśabdavyavāyāt (vt. 11) / sādharmyavaidharmyābhyām ayaṃ hetuḥ / prasiddhanānātvaiḥ sādharmyam, prasiddhaikatvaiś ca vaidharmyam / iha śabdavyavāyo---yeṣāṃ siddhaṃ nānātvam---teṣāṃ dṛṣṭaḥ / tadyathā---dṛtir iti ṛkārekārayos takāreṇa / ihāpi daṇḍa iti dakāre yo 'kāraḥ ḍaśabde ca, tayor  'eḍ'śabdena [eḍśabdena] vyavāyo dṛṣṭaḥ / ekasya cātmano ['yaṃ] dharmo nāsti / yadāyam akāraḥ śuddha uccāryate a iti, tadā nāsty asya śabdena vyavāyaḥ / tathā paurvāparyaṃ kālavyavetam asaṃhitāyāṃ prasiddhanānātveṣv eva dṛṣṭam a i u iti / na tv ekasyātmano 'kārasya ddhasyoccāraṇaviṣaye [śuddhasyoccāraṇaviṣaye] kālabhedo vidyate / iha tu daṇḍa agram iti asaṃhitāyām asti paurvāparyaṃ kālavyavetam / tasmāt nānā akāraḥ / nanu śabdavyavāye 'py asti kālavyavāyaḥ, tatra kālavyavāya ity eva siddham / ucyate / kālavyavāya eva dvedhā pradarśitaḥ---śabdaśūnyaḥ, śabdavāṃś ca /

[3.1] yugapaddeśapṛthaktvadarśanāt (vt. 12) / deśapṛthaktvenākārasya darśanaṃ yugapad asti / ayam ekatvavirodhī dharmaḥ / na hy eko devadatto yugapad deśapṛthaktvenopalabhyate / akāras tūpalabhyate / tasmād aneko 'kā ['kāra] iti / anyatve hetudvayam upanyastam / ekasya parihāram āha---yad ucyate bhavatā 'kālaśabdavyavāyāt' iti, atra brūmaḥ---yadi punar ime varṇāḥ śakunivat syuḥ iti / etad uktaṃ bhavati---yo 'yaṃ vyavāyaḥ parikalpyate bhavatā kālaśabdābhyām, nāyaṃ vastuno vyavāyaḥ / kiṃ tarhi? vyañjakasya nimittasya pravṛttinivṛttibhyām upalabdhyanupalabdhī ete / yad abhivyañjakanimittatvam akāsya [akārasya] tad varṇāntarābhivyañjake nimitte pravṛtte apravartamānaṃ nopalambhayati akāram, punaḥpravartamānam upalambhayati / tasmād upalabdhyanupalabdhyoḥ pravṛttinivṛttibhyām idaṃ bhavati, na tu vastuno bhedāt / yathā śakuniḥ pūrvaḥ paraś ca vastuno bhinnaḥ śakunyantareṇa vyavahita iti? etat tu śakyate vaktum, pūrvam anupalabdhaḥ saṃpraty upalabdhaḥ iti / evaṃ ca kṛtvedam āha---evam ayam akāro d ity atra dṛṣṭo da ity atropalabdhaḥ ṇḍa ity atra dṛśyate palabhyate [upalabhyate] / naivaṃ śakyam / anityatvam evaṃ syāt / asty ayaṃ tarkāśritaḥ samādhiḥ / śāstrasiddhāntas tu na bhavati / śāstre hy arthavataḥ śabdasya nityatvaṃ pratijñātam, padasya vākyasya vā / sa ca samudāyo 'bhyupagataḥ śāstre / samudāyaś ca varṇātmaka eva / (p. 9) tatraikatvapratijñāne karkaḥ, sa varṇasamudāyo nāsti / kakāro dvirupalabdho 'kāraś ca, rephaś ca sakṛd iti śakyaṃ vaktuṃ kar ityevaṃlakṣaṇaḥ samudāya iti / evaṃ kāruka ity ayam api kāru ityevaṃlakṣaṇaḥ / asya ca prayogābhāvād arthavattaiva nāsti / tasmād abhyupagantavyam etat, avasthitā evāmī samudāyā artheṣu, bhinnair eva varṇair anugatā iti / yadi tu punar amī varṇā ādityavat syuḥ / yugapaddeśapṛthaktvadarśanād ity asya vyabhicāraḥ / ekasyāpi ātyasya [ādityasya] deśapṛthaktveṣūpalabdhiḥ dṛṣṭā / tatrākāro devadatte narātmani punar āditye neti viṣamahetur iti (?) / viṣama upanyāsaḥ / etad ācaṣṭe---evaṃ mayoktam, na devadattam eko draṣṭā yugapat deśeṣūpalabhate [deśapṛthaktveṣūpalabhate] / ādityam api ca nopalabhate / yo hy upalabdhā sākete, nāsāv eva saurāṣṭrike / yo vṛkṣasyopariṣṭād āditya . . . upalabhate / akāraṃ rupalabhate [punar upalabhate] / yasyāpi ca kṣaṇaviṣayam upalabdhiyaugapadyaṃ nāsti, tasyāpy ādityasyopabdhyā [ādityasyopalabdhyā] visadṛśy upalabdhir akārasya, muhūrtaviṣayasyopalabdhiyaugapadyasya saṃbhavāt / na cākāśam ekam / tasmāt dekadeśatvam akārasyānupapannam / akāram api nopalabhate / yady akārasya bhedāḥ syur upapadyate pṛthaktvam / tathaika eva deśaḥ śabdasyāpy ākāśaḥ / tasmād ahetur ayaṃ 'yugapaddeśapṛthaktvadarśanāt' iti / śrotropalabdhir buddhir grāhyaḥ [buddhinirgrāhyaḥ] prayogeṇābhijvalitaḥ / kimartham etad upādīyate? ucyate / vyabhicāriṇāṃ [avyabhicāriṇāṃ] dharmāṇāṃ pradarśanārtham / śrotropalabdhitvam avyabhicaritam iti / na jātucit cakṣuṣā dṛśyate / tathā buddhinirgrāhyatvāt nānyena nirgṛhyate / dhvaninā vyajyamāno na pradīpādibhir abhivyajyate / evam ākāśam avyabhicāri / tasmān [nānyabhāvyam akārasya] / yā tv ekadeśasya sataḥ pṛthagdeśasyopalabdhiḥ, sā nimittakāraṇavaśāt / yathā dūrādivyapadeśaḥ, dūraḥ śabdaḥ saṃnikṛṣṭaśabdaḥ [saṃnikṛṣṭaḥ śabdaḥ] iti / karaṇavyāpārajanitā yā buddhayatā [buddhayas] tā ekasya cānekatvam api darśayanti, yathābhogavati sthāne śabda uccārite tatsadṛśaṃ śabdāntaram ihopalabhyate / buddhayo hy anavasthitākārāḥ pravartanta eveti / idaṃ tvayoktam 'na hy eko devadatto yugat [yugapat] srughne ca bhavati [mathurāyāṃ ca]' / atra brūmaḥ---pṛthivyā ekatvāt tad iha yo devadattaḥ kasmān na srughnaḥ kasmiṃścin mathureti (?), evam ākāśasyāpy ekadeśatve sati deśabahuvyavahāraḥ [deśabahutvavyavahāraḥ] pravartiṣyate / ākāśadeśā api bahavaḥ / nanu ca pṛthivī svair avayavair ārabdheti tatrāvayavā deśatvena yuktāḥ / anārabdhaṃ ca punar ākāśam / satyam, evam etat / te tv avayavā atadātmānaḥ santaḥ samavāyitvād vyapadiśyante / evaṃ kiṃ na gṛhyate vṛkṣādibhiḥ saṃyogād ihākāśam iti vyapadiśyate / etāvāṃs tu viśeṣaḥ---samavāyino deśā vastusaṃpramohahetunā samavāyenaikātmyam iva nītāḥ / ātaś ca nītā yad anityatvam api kecit pratipannāḥ / (p. 10) avayavāvayavino deśavyavahāre tu nāsti kaścit prativiśeṣaḥ / tasmād deśabahutvāt sthito hetuḥ, 'yugapaddeśapṛthaktvadarśanād ānyabhāvyam akārasya' iti /

[4] ākṛtigrahaṇāt tu siddham (vt. 13) / pratyāhāre anuvṛttinirdeśe ca jātir eva codyate, na vyaktiḥ / vyaktyupādānaṃ tu yathā nālikeredvīpanivāsinaḥ (? nālikeradvīpanivāsinā) idam upadiśyate---ayaṃ gaur eva tvayā na padā spraṣṭavya iti / sa taṃ bālaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ kṛśaṃ copadiṣṭo vṛddhaṃ śabalaṃ sthūlam api na spṛśati / tasyās tv ākṛter na vyavadhānaṃ vyavacchedo [vā] / vivṛtatvam, ye cānye dharmāḥ, sarve te vyaktidvārakāḥ / tatra pratyāhāre vivṛtākṛtir upadiṣṭā, tasyā eva ca śāstrānte pratyāpattir iti sarvaṃ siddham / yatra tv ākāragrahaṇe jātinirdeśād akāragrahaṇaṃ prāpnoti / tat tu na ca prāpnoti, prayatnabhedāt / yathā 'pūrvavayā brāhmaṇaḥ pratyuttheyaḥ' / plutasya tu prāpnoti / tatrāpi parihāraḥ 'plutaś ca viṣaye smṛtaḥ' (P 6.1.87, ślokavt. 2) iti /

[4.1] tadvac ca taparakaraṇam (vt. 14) / 'tathā ca taparakaraṇam' ity etad uktaṃ bhavati, 'tadvac ceti' / grahaṇenātiprasaktam [savarṇagrahaṇenātiprasaktam iti] / pratyākhyāte 'pi hi yoge taparakaraṇaṃ saṃhyata [saṃdihyata] eva---kiṃ vyaktau padārthe prāpakam athākṛtau nivartakam iti / ato yathaivākṛtigrahaṇe pratyākhyānam, tathaiva tapareṣv apy ākṛter grahaṇam iti /

[4.2] halgrahaṇeṣu ca (vt. 14) / tatra grahaṇaśāstrasyāvyāpāraḥ [grahaṇakaśāstrasyāvyāpāraḥ] / vyaktinirdeśakas takāro nirdiṣṭaḥ pratyāhāre tatratyānukaraṇam / ekasyāsau syāt, pūrvasya parasya ca (? vā) / tatra 'jhalo jhali' (P 8.2.26) iti pūrvaparāśrayo lopo na syāt / kva tarhi syāt? yatra bhinnarūpau jhalau 'abuddha' 'abuddhāḥ' iti / ākṛtinirdeśe tu takārākṛter nirdeśāt siddho lopaḥ / 

[4.3] rūpasāmānyād vā (vt. 16) / katham ayaṃ parihāraḥ? tatra kecid varṇayanti---yady apy ākṛtir naiva syād, evam api na doṣaḥ / yathā kārṣāpaṇādiṣv asatyāṃ jātau / bhavatāvat madhurā kārṣāpaṇam asti (?) / atha cārthavastv eva tattathābhūtaṃ yat tattvāvyavāye (? tattvāvyavāyo) bhavati / evaṃ ca (? na) tāvat śabdākṛtir iti / ihabhavantas tv āhuḥ --- ākṛter eva rūpasāmānyāt siddham ityevam ayam upātta iti / tatraitat syāt, vaktuṃ (? uktaṃ) tadākṛtigrahaṇāt tu siddha (? siddhaṃ) iti / tac ca na / pūrvam evam uktam ---ākṛtiḥ śabdenābhidhīyata iti, tatrākṛteś codyamānatvāt siddham / idānīṃ tu vyaktīnāṃ (p. 11) codyamānatvāt yady api vyaktaya upadiśyante, nākṛtir upadiśyate, evam api siddham / katham? idaṃ tāvad ayaṃ praṣṭavyaḥ --- yad etad anyatvam, kim etad uccāraṇāt śabdasya, akārād ayam ikāro 'nyaḥ iti; atha viśeṣāt kasmāccit padārthaḥ pratipadyate / evaṃ cet kimartham ākārasya bhavān viśeṣād anyatvaṃ pratipadyate, na punaḥ sāmānyāt sa evāyam iti? syān matam --- loke bhedenaiva vyavahāro nābhedeneti / etac ca na / abhedenāpi loke vyavahāra iti --- tān eva śāṭakān ācchādayāma iti / tasmād ākṛtir anabhidhīyamānāpi asti na tasya [tattvasya] pratyāpattau (p. 11) pratipattau nimittam iti etad ācaṣṭe / 

ṛ ḷ k //2//

[1] kiṃ viśeṣeṇa ḷkāropadeśaś codyate? uktaṃ hi prayojanaṃ sarvavarṇopadeśe 'viśeṣeṇa / atas tad evāsya prayojanaṃ bhavitum arthati / atha vā viśeṣeṇa vaidharmyeṇeti / anye hi varṇā anyena prakāreṇa codyante / akāro vivṛtatvena, rephahakārau pūrvaparopadeśena, tasthā [antasthā; aṃtasthā] aṇsūpadeśena / ayaṃ tu viśeṣeṇocyate iti / alpīyāṃś ca prayogaviṣaya iti / etad ācaṣṭe --- varṇānām udeśaḥ [upadeśaḥ] śāstrapravṛttyarthaḥ / śāstraṃ ca prayogārtham / sa ca prayogo nāsti ḷkārasyeti / alpīyān ity avarṇaviṣayaḥ (? ṛvarṇaviṣayaḥ) prakarṣaḥ / kārasyālpaḥ [ṛkārasyālpaḥ] prayogaviṣayaḥ / tasmād alpataro 'syeti / syād etad --- yac cāpi prayogaviṣayaḥ so 'pi kḷsthasyāt [kḷpisthastyāt] (? kḷpisthasyaiva) / kṛpeś ca latvam asiddham / tatra yāny ackāryāṇi udāttādīni, tāni ṛkārāvayām [ṛkārāvasthāyām] eva labdhāni / nārthaḥ ḷkāropadena [ḷkāropadeśena] / 

[1.1] yadṛcchāśaktijānukaraṇaplutyārthaḥ [yadṛcchāśaktijānukaraṇaplutyādyarthaḥ] (vt. 1) / yad uktam, prayogārthaṃ śāstram, na ca prayoge ḷkāro viniveśitasvarūpo vidyate iti / yadṛcchādiṣv asti ḷkāraprayogaḥ / yadṛcchā nāma yā asati pravṛttinimitte arthagataṃ (? arthagate) pravartakaṃ [nivartakaṃ] vā niyamahetum antareṇa pravartate nivartate vā sā yadṛcchayā / ḍitthaśabdo hi svarūpamātranibandhano yatra yatra prayujyate, 'idaṃ tāvad asya nāmadheyaṃ karomīti', tatra tatra [pravartate / anyataś ca] nivartate / svarūpamātranibandhanānāṃ ca śabdānāṃ tasya svarūpasya rūpāntareṇa pratyāyitum [pratyāyayitum] aśakyatvān nāsti nivartakaṃ śabdāntaram / tatra ḷtakaśabdo nāmadheyatvena niyujyamāno 'napekṣitabāhyanimitto 'sati nivartake (p. 12) śabdāntare svarūpanibandhanaḥ sādhuḥ / sa kimiti na prayujyate? tasmāt ḷkāravataḥ śabdasya prayogād ackāryaprasiddhyarthaṃ kartavya upadeśaḥ, yadi tan nāsti 'ṛkāraḷkārayoḥ savarṇavidhiḥ' iti (P 1.1.9, vt. 5) / sati hi tasmin ḷkāra eva ackāryaṃ pratipadyate / catuṣṭayī śabdānāṃ pravṛttiḥ iti / anena etat kathayati / kecit śabdā niyatārthāḥ / paryāyāś caiṣāṃ śabdānāṃ niyatāḥ / anutpādyāḥ puruṣeṇa, kartur asmaraṇātra [smaraṇād atra] / asti tu sa śabdagrāmo, yo nāmadheyatvena niyujyamānaḥ kasmiṃścid arthe 'niyataḥ pauruṣeṇa [pauruṣeyeṇa] vyāpāreṇa niyujyate /

[1.2] tatra jātiśabdo yadā jātau pravartate, tadāpekṣitanimittavattvāc [tadānapekṣitanimittavattvāc] chakyate vaktuṃ sa yadṛcchāśabda iti / anyānyā (? asty atrānyā) pauruṣeyī pravṛttiḥ / yathā jāter astitvamatena yāvatā veti (?) / na tatra śakyate puruṣeṇa prayāsaḥ sraṣṭum iti / eṣa viśeṣaḥ / yadā tu dravye nimittāpekṣayā pravartate, tadāpi (? tadāsau) jātiśabdaḥ / yadā tv abhidheyaṃ dravyam asya, dravyaśabdaḥ / evaṃ guṇaśabdo 'pi / śuklādir guṇaśabdaḥ sāmānye [guṇasāmānye] yadṛcchāśabdaḥ, [dravye] guṇanimittāpekṣayā jātiśabdaḥ, guṇābhidhāyitvāt guṇaśabdaḥ / evaṃ kriyāśabdaḥ kriyāsāmānye yadṛcchāśabdaḥ, kriyāyāṃ jātiśabdaḥ, kriyāśabdo vā / yāvati ca śabde kriyānuṣaṅgo 'sti, sarvasya kriyāśabdatvaṃ kṛteṣu [kṛdanteṣu] tiṅanteṣu [ca] /
anye manyante / jātipratyāsād eva te 'rthā ['rthās] tācchabdyaṃ pratilabhante iti / 
apare manyante --- yadṛcchāśabdā evāmī, jātyādayas tu kevalaṃ bhedikā iti / 
atas tisṛṣu śabdavṛttiṣu [śabdapravṛttiṣu] niyataśabdatvaṃ niyatārthatvaṃ ca / caturthyāṃ tu śabdapravṛttau asti pauruṣeyatvam iti / 

[1.3] aśaktijānukaraṇārtha iti / athāmukaḥ kaḥ kalpate? yadṛcchāśabdas tāvan na bhavati, niyataprayogatvāt / tasmāj jātiśabdo 'yam anukriyamāṇe yā jātis tayā saṃbandha ḷtakam āha / yathā ghaṭa iti ghaṭatvaviśiṣṭam arthaṃ bruvan jātiśabda ity ucyate / sa ca ḷtaśabdo [ḷtakaśabdo] nānyenānukaraṇena śakyate pratyāyayitum ity anivartitaṃ śabdāntareṇa ḷkāravad anukaraṇam iti / 

[1.4] plutyādyarthaḥ / plutisvaritadvirvacaneṣu latvasya siddhatvāt kḷpistha ḷkāra upadeśaṃ prayojayati / (p. 13)

[2.1] nyāyyabhāvāt kalpanaṃ saṃjñādiṣu (vt. 2) / nyāyyo 'sti ṛtakaśabdaḥ / kathaṃ punar asya nyāyyatvam? śiṣṭaprayogāt / yathaiva jātyādiṣu śabdevicchinnapāraṃparyopadeśena [śabdeṣv avicchinnapāraṃparyopadeśena] etāvanta evāmī śabdāḥ iti, evam idam api avicchinnam / yadṛcchāśabdā apy evaṃrūpā eveti / tatra yathaiva jātyādiṣu na svayam utpādyante śabdāḥ, evaṃ yadṛcchāśabdeṣv api / na hi śakyaṃ kākavāsitaṃ haṃsavāsitaṃ vāsya nāmadheyaṃ karomīti tathāvidhaṃ nāma kartuṃ, śiṣṭenācaritatvāt [śiṣṭenābācaritatvāt] / saṃjñādiṣv iti / ādiśabdaḥ prakāre / ke punaḥ saṃjñāprakārāḥ? yā apy etā goṣṭhīviṣayā mlecchā utpādyante, etā api na svayaṃ bdān [śabdān] utpādya kartavyā iti / atha vā saṃjñādiṣv iti yā etā jātiguṇakriyānimittāḥ saṃjñāḥ sarvāsām evāsāṃ yadṛcchāśabdā evādibhūtāḥ / na hy anapekṣitanimittāntarā artheṣu viniveśaṃ kurvanti / ataḥ saṃjñānām ādibhūteṣu yadṛcchāśabdeṣu nyāyyo yaḥ sa kalpayitavyaḥ / apara āheti / anenaitat pratipādayati / asminn arthe sanibandhanāpi smṛtir asti / tatra śāstrasamayād api pratipadyāmahe --- evaṃbhūtā śiṣṭānāṃ pravṛttir iti / ed [etad] uktaṃ bhavati / yatra prasiddhir asti tasyopādānam, na tv astitvamātrād eva pratipattiḥ yuktā / na santy eva śabdā ḷtakādayaḥ ity atas te [tena] prayoktavyāḥ / śiṣṭāś ca prasiddhiṃ śāstraṃ cāpekṣante / bhavāṃs tūbhayaṃ nirasyāstitvamātranibandhana eveti / ātaś caivaṃ, yadavyaktānukaraṇaṃ vyaktenānukurvanti śiṣṭāḥ, paṭapaṭāyatīti; na tathābhūtena śabdeneti / ayaṃ tarhi yadṛcchāśabdo 'parihārya iti / yad ucyate --- 'śāstrānvito 'sti ṛtakaśabdaḥ' iti / ayaṃ hy arter niṣṭhāyāṃ kvuni cānvākhyātaḥ / ṛtīyater vā iti ṛtaka iti / na caivaṃ ṛphiḍaḥ ṛphiḍḍaś cānvākhyātaḥ / tasmād ayam apy asti, ṛphiḍaḥ ṛphiḍḍaś ceti / asyedam uttaram --- eṣo 'pi hi (ṛphiḍaḥ) ṛphiḍḍa iti / apravṛttiś [artipravṛttiś] caiva hi loke lakṣyate / ṛtakādiṣv artir eva dhātupāṭha upadiśyate, na ḷkāraḥ / śakyete ca phiḍaphiḍḍau pratyayau kalpayitum, 'pratyayataḥ prakṛteś ca tad ūhyam' (P 3.3.1, ślokavt. 2) iti / atha vā artipravṛttir iti / arteś caiva vikṛtāpabhraṣṭā pravṛttir lakṣyate / loke cañcalo gandharva ḷphiḍḍa ity ucyate / ity evaṃ hu [bahu] varṇayanti / evaṃ caitat / atha vā, atrāpy ayaṃ paryanuyogaḥ --- na śiṣṭā ṛtakam eva prayuñjate / kiṃ tarhi? ḷtakam api prayuñjate / ḷphiḍḍaśaṃ [ḷphiḍḍasadṛśaṃ] pabhraṃśanāmadheyaṃ [apabhraṃśanāmadheyaṃ] kartavyam ity etaddarśanam / dṛṣṭārthā hy eṣā smṛtiḥ / haṃsatadbhakṣaṇādivat / dṛśyate hy asyā anurodhaḥ, eṣā kṛtāsmaraṇā bhakṣyatā iti / śiṣṭās tu mlecchanāmadheyavad apabhraṃśaṃ nābhimanyanta iti / evaṃ pratyākhyāya yadṛcchāśabdārthatām aśaktijānukaraṇārthatām api pratyācaṣṭe --- (p. 14)

[2.2] anukaraṇaṃ śiṣṭātiṣiṣu [śiṣṭāpratiṣiddheṣu] / yat vihitam, yadvā na vihitaṃ na pratiṣiddham, tad evānukriyate śiṣṭaiḥ / yat tu pratiṣiddham, na tadanyena kṛtam ity anukriyate / apaśabdas tu duṣṭas tasyānukraṇaṃ na kartavyaṃ yo ha vānukriyate yo nu (?) yady apy artho vaidikas tathāpi śabdānāṃ laukikatvāt loka ity ucyate / idaṃ tu viśvajitaḥ satrāṇyāsata iti vede ca ṭhyate [paṭhyate] iti /

viṣama upanyāsaḥ / etad uktaṃ bhavati / nānukaraṇād dharmo 'dharmo vā / kiṃ tarhi? tyāgād anuṣṭhānād vā / śakyaṃ caitat vaktum anukaraṇam evaitat na bhavatīti / yatra hy anukṛtamātraṃ (? anukṛtamātre) kāci [kācit] kriyā sadṛśā dṛśyate, [na] tadanukaraṇaṃ bhavatīty etad darśayati --- yas tu khalv idam asau iti / snātānulipta iti / idaṃ svāsthyopalakṣaṇam / asvasthena hi citte kāryam [cittenākāryam] ārabhyate / mālyaguṇakaṇṭha iti / prākāśyopalakṣaṇam / surāṃ hi piban brāhmaṇaḥ pracchannaṃ pibed, ayaṃ tu mālyaguṇakaṇṭha iti / yady api madyapānāśaṅkābhāvaḥ, evam apy atra kadacchedaḥ [kadalīcchedaḥ] / paraṃ ca kenacit sadṛśām anukaraṇatvena [na] cātra doṣo bhavati / kurvanti hi śiṣṭā evaṃbhūtam anukaraṇam / yadi brāhmaṇyā ḷtakaśabdam arthapadārthakam uccāritam arthapadārthakatvenānukurvan prayuñjīta, syāt pratyavāyaḥ / yatas tu śabdapadārthakaṃ prayuṅkte, tasmāt payaḥpānavad idam api na doṣāyeti / 

apaśabda ity api tasyāpaśabdaḥ syāt / asya hi yāvanto 'sādhavo gāvyādayaḥ sarva e [eva] vācyāḥ / ato 'rthabhedān nāsty apaśabdatvam / yathā goṇīśabdasyāvapane sādhutvam, sāsnādimaty asādhutvam / syād etat --- apaśabda ity ayam anvākhyātatvāt [ananvākhyātatvāt] / sādhur ity ayam anvākhyāto 'rthavat prātipadikam iti (P 1.2.45) /

ayaṃ khalv api bhūyaḥ . . . / ko 'sya viśeṣaḥ? ucyate / etad apy atra nāsty evāsādhor ayam anukaraṇa iti / yadi kḷpisthati [kḷpisthasyeti] / etad uktaṃ bhavati / ādāv evātra prativihitam iti / yaḥ prayoge vidyate so 'yaṃ, na ca prayoga idam asti 'pūrvatrāsiddham' (P 8.2.1) ity āha --- bhavet tadarthena nārthaḥ syād, ayaṃ tv anyaḥ kḷpisthapadārthaka iti /

prakṛtivad anukaraṇaṃ bhavatīti / saty api vacane kvacid iyaṃ paribhāṣā na pravartate / yadā tv ayavasya [avayavasya] rūpatām āśritya, nārtharūpatām anukaroti tadādhāturūpeṇāśritatvāt prakṛtāv evāsau dharmo naṣṭa iti tatkṛtaṃ kāryaṃ na bhavati / yathā viparābhyāṃ jeḥ (P 1.3.19) iti iyaṅādeśo [na] bhavati / yadārtharūpatām āśrayate, tadā (p. 15) dhātvnukaraṇa iti prakṛtau dṛṣṭam iyaṅādeśaṃ pratipadyate, 'parivyavebhyaḥ kriyaḥ' (P 1.3.18) iti / dviḥ pacantv ity āheti / ayaṃ ca tiṅ na bhavati, sādhanābhidhāyitvād dhi tiṅaḥ / evam 'agnī jvālitau' ity uktam / tam anukaroti ""agnī" ity ayam āha' iti / tatra dvayor arthayor vā ekābhivyañjakasya (? ekasyābhivyañjakasya) vā dvivacanaṃ [ekavacanaṃ vā], nānyasyeti / 

atra tv idam asti vaktavyam --- gamḷ sṛpḷ mucḷ ity eteṣv avasthito 'nukriyate, sādhu ḷkāram adhīte iti, tadā nāsty etat prakṛtivad anukaraṇam / atha manyase, kḷpiś [kḷpisthaś] cāyaṃ dhātvādiṣv anuṣakta iti ṛtake 'pi kḷpistha eveti tulyam / tatraivaṃ bruvate tad uktaṃ bhavati / aṭprabhṛtiṣv ekaranukāro vā saṃvaktavya iti / ṣṭad iti ḷti vācanaṃ [? lḷvāvacanam / rṛvāvacanam] / anenāpītsaṃjñā bhaviṣyati (?) /

yadi prakṛtivad anukaraṇaṃ bhavati, apaśabda evāsau bhavati . . . 'brāhmaṇyḷtaka ity āha' iti / anukaraṇasya prakṛtir arthapadārthikāsāv apaśabdaḥ / prakṛteś ca dharmān anukaraṇaśabdo labhata iti apaśabdatvam āyātam / apaśabdaś ca na prayoktavya iti śiṣṭavyavahāraḥ / tataḥ prakṛtivad anukaraṇaṃ bhavatīti prāmāṇikā yā prakṛtir upadiśyate, sā prakṛtitvenāśrīyate / yathā 'sthānivad ādeśaḥ . . . ' (P 1.1.56) iti śāstre yaḥ sthānī sa āśrīyate / na ca vyākaraṇe apaśabda ity evaṃ kaścit  ḷ prakṛtau upadiśyate, sādhūnām anvākhyānāt / athānumānād apaśabda ity evam anvākhyāyate, na ca sāmarthyagṛhītā prakṛtir bhavitum arhatīti / atha vā 'prakṛtivad' iti śāstrapūrvakaṃ kāryam atidiśyate / na cāpaśabde kiṃcit śāstrapravartakaṃ (? śāstrapūrvakaṃ) kāryam asti / na hi śakyaṃ vyākaraṇenāpaśabdatvam anvākhyātum / yad api ca kāryayogi śabdarūpam, tasminn api na śakyam apaśabdatvam atideṣṭum / tasmāt prakṛtyabhāvāt, tattadviṣayasya ca dharmasya śāstreṇānāśritatvāt, anukaraṇe cātideṣṭum aśakyatvād idam ucyate apaśabdānukaraṇo 'pi nāpaśabda iti / evam avasthito 'śaktijānukaraṇārtha ḷkāropadeśaḥ /

apare pratyāta [pratyākhyāta] eveti varṇayanti / 'apaśabda evāsau bhavati' yasmāt 'apaśabdo hy asya prakṛtiḥ' / na cāpaśabdaḥ / prayojanaṃ bhavitum arhati [arhatīti] vākyaśeṣaḥ / kuta etad iti itara āha / tasmān na hy apaśabdā upadiśyante iti / na cānupadiṣṭā prakṛtir asti / yaṇādvanam iti (?) vākyaśeṣaḥ / (p. 16)

[2.3] ekadeśavikṛtasyānanyatvāt plutyādayaḥ (vt. 4) / nātra grahītavyam ādeśagrahaṇād iti / evaṃ hi vijñāyamāne 'lvidhir ayam iti plutir na syāt / tasmāl lokapravṛttir evāśrīyate / nanu ca loke jātyanuṣaṅgāt śuni saṃpratyayaḥ / ihāpy asti jātyanuṣaṅgaḥ / yadā ca ṛkāraḷkārayoḥ savarṇavidhiḥ, ākṛtigrahaṇāc ca grahaṇakaśāstraṃ pratyākhyāyate, tadā saty api śrutibhede ekākṛtitvam eva, yathā hrasvadīrghayor iti / rājñakīyam [rājñaḥ ka ca / rājakīyam] ity allopo 'na iti / evaṃ manyate / kasyacid dharmasya bhāvānuṣaṅgāt smṛtyanuṣaṅga iti saṃprati vacanam ārabhate --- ṣaṣṭhīnirdiṣṭasyeti / yasya vikārakāle [vikāravidhānakāle] ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśas tasyānanyatvam / atra ca kakāravidhānakāle rājñaḥ ṣaṣṭhī, nāna, iti rājaśabdaḥ syāt na anśabdaḥ / kḷptaśikha ity atra pluto na prāpnoti / na hy atra ṛkāraḥ ṣaṣṭhyā nirdiṣṭaḥ / kas tarhi? rephaḥ / tena plute nāsty ananyatvam / ṛkāro 'py atra ṣaṣṭhyā nirdiṣṭaḥ / sautro nirdeśaḥ, 'supāṃ suluk' (P 7.1.39) iti / ur iti ca saṃbandhamātram āśrīyate / sa kadācit sākṣāt avayavaṃ rephaṃ viśinaṣṭi kḷptādiṣu / saṃbaddhasaṃbandhāc ca kalpakādiṣu [kalpakārādiṣu] / nakārāntagrahaṇam iti / rājakīye nāntatvaṃ nāsti / na hi śvā pucche chinne pucchavān iti vyapadiśyate / iha tarhi kḷptaśikhaḥ, 'anṛtaḥ' iti pratiṣedhaḥ prāpnoti / ṛkāro yathaikadeśavikṛta iti / "aravata" iti vakṣyāmi / nanu ca matupātra [na] bhavitavyam / ucyate / viśiṣṭārthapratipattyarthaṃ matup upādīyate / aravata (? arasya) ity ucyamāne vartakety atrāpi pratiṣedhaḥ syāt / asyāpi repho 'sti samīpe / tasmāt avayavatvena tadvattā pradṛśyate, yathā viṣāṇīti / atra tv idam ucyate --- bahuvrīhir apy anantarādiṣu pratiṣiddhas tasmāt sarvabījī sarvakeśīty atra prakṣeptavyaḥ / sa eṣa sūtrabhedena / etad uktaṃ bhavati --- anyārtho (yam anya upadeṣṭavya) iti, saiṣā [mahato] vaṃśastambād iti / rūpasya sāmyāt tayā kriyayā sāmyam asyeti / 

e o ṅ //3//  ai au c //4//

[1] upadiśyamānatā taparātaparaviṣayā saṃdhyakṣarāṇāṃ bhāṣyavākyenocyate / tasyāś ca asattvabhūtatvād idamā saṃbaddhatvānupapattiḥ / naiṣa doṣaḥ / yo hi śabdopādānaḥ sa tathābhūtena śabdenocyamānaḥ saṃbanddhum aśakyo, yathā pacaty apatyam iti (?) / yadā vasturūpaṃ buddhāv upasthāpayati, tadā sarvo 'rtho vidyata ātmarūpeṇeti kimiti na saṃbhantsyate pacatis tasyāpatyam iti? sa eva vākyārtho 'sattvabhūto 'yam artha ity asattvaśabdenocyamānaḥ (p. 17) sattvabhūta ucyate / tathā cedamā pratirdiśyate [pratinirdiśyate] / evam ihāpi vākyārtho 'napāśritaśabdopādānaḥ ātmarūpe 'vasthita idamā saṃbadhyate / 

[2] kaś cātra viśeṣaḥ? kāryagataṃ viśeṣaṃ pṛcchati / kiṃ kriyamāṇeṣu tapareṣu labhyate, akriyamāṇeṣu ceti / 

[2.1] saṃdhyakṣareṣu taparopadeśaś cet taparoccāraṇam (vt. 1) / keyaṃ vācoyuktiḥ 'taparopadeśaś cet taparoccāraṇam' iti? yāvatā 'diśir uccāraṇakriyaḥ' [MBh 1.13.7] ity uktam / tatraitad uktaṃ bhavati --- taparoccāraṇaṃ cet taparoccāraṇam iti / ucyate / etat pratipādayati --- (1) yathānyeṣām upadeśaś ca tāvad asti, atha lakṣaṇena nivṛttis te na śrūyante / yathā yādī cuñcupcaṇapau / evaṃ takārasyopadeśe sati nivartakalakṣaṇaṃ nāsti iti idam ucyate --- taparoccāraṇaṃ kartavyam / (2) atha vā guṇair api pariprāpaṇam upadeśaḥ / saṃdhyakṣarāṇi cet taparāṇi, taparāṇi na ca tā [tāni] prāpyante 'taparāṇy uccāritānīti / (3) atha vā, taparopadeśa iti taparaphalaṃ prati nirdeśaḥ / taparaphalaṃ ced iṣṭaṃ manyase, saṃdhyakṣareṣu taparoccāraṇaṃ kartavyam iti / 

[2.1.1] saṃdhyakṣarāṇīti pūrvācāryasaṃjñaiṣā / teṣā [teṣāṃ] cānvartha [cānvarthatvaṃ] saṃdhīyamānāvayavatvāt, saṃdhīyamānau pūrvaparāvayavau eṣām iti /

[2.1.2] atra ca granthe vipratipattiḥ / kaścit sarvāṇy eva taparāṇi kartavyāni iti pratipannaḥ / kaścit ekāraikārāv eva taparāv iti, okāraukārau tu 'tād api paras taparaḥ' (MBh 1.23.20) iti taparatvaṃ pratipadyete iti /

yadīha sarvāṇy eva taparāṇi syuḥ, 'ot ṅ' 'aut c' iti itsaṃjñaiva na syāt / [na] hy upadeśe 'ntyas takāra iti / kiṃ punas takārasyetsaṃjñayā prayojanam? yāvatā taparasyetsaṃjñā na syāt / iyaṃ vikalpa 'titi pratyayagrahaṇam' (P 6.1.185, vt.) / iha mā bhūt 'diva aut' (P 7.1.84) iti yad uccāryate, tasyetsaṃjñā / tatra titā yaḥ pratyāyyate, sa tat kāryaṃ pratipadyate --- saṃjñī kāryaṃ pratipadyate / asti, tatra titkaraṇasyānanyārthatvāt / i [iha] tu taparatvam anyārthaṃ tatkālapratipādana upayuktam / saṃjñini tadvyapadeśasya nimittaṃ kathamiva syāt? anekārthatvād anubandhasyādoṣaḥ / dṛṣṭo hy eko 'nubandho 'nekārthaḥ, yathā ṭhagādiṣu kakāro vṛddhyarthaḥ svarārthaś ca / sarvatraiva cet saṃjñā vaktavyā / (p. 18)

itareṣāṃ hi taparaṃ saṃdhyakṣaraṃ tatkālān pratipādayat svasvarūpaṃ pratipādayati / sa tv atideva yaḥ titaḥ kāryaṃ pratipādyate / kutaḥ? tatpratipādyānānuccāraṇā [tatpratipādyānām anuccāraṇāt] / nāsty etaduccāraṇopāya iti / evam api saṃdhyakṣarāṇāṃ takārasyejñā [takārasyetsaṃjñā] vaktavyā / itarathā hi taparaṃ saṃdhyakṣaraṃ tatkālān pratipādayati [pratipādayatīti] satakārakaṃ saṃjñārūpam / pratipādite ca svarūpe itsaṃjñā nāsty upadeśābhāvāt / 

atheha kasmān na bhavati svarūpagrahaṇam iti? eke varṇayanti --- kā punar atra saṃjñā? ādir ity āha / tasya tūpalakṣaṇam antyaḥ / yadyem [yady evam], ādir eva saṃjñārthenārthavān, anubandhas tu kālavāsitavad iti vibhaktyabhāvaḥ, 'ikaḥ' 'aci' iti / tasmāt samudāyaḥ saṃjñā / yady api samudāyas tathāṇā (?) ataparā eva tathāpi itsaṃjñayāntyo nivartyate / iha tv anantyatvāt asatyām itsaṃjñāyāṃ satakārakaṃ svarūpaṃ prāpnoti / pratyāyyas tu tatpramāṇaḥ atapara eva / tathā [tad yathā] śuklavāsasā tulyaparimāṇam ānayeti anyopalakṣaṇapāre [anyopalakṣaṇavyāpāre] śuklavāsaso nāntarbhavanam, svavyāpāre tu tadguṇavijñānam asti / evaṃ taparatvam anyeṣūpalakṣaṇam, svavyāpāre tu tapara iti takāro 'py antarbhavati / atha vetsaṃjñāyāṃ pratividhīyate / samudāyāvabhedād [samudāyāvayavabhedād] adoṣaḥ / ṅakāro hi e o ity asyānubandhaḥ, takāraś ca okārasya / evaṃ cakāra ai au ity asya, takāra aukārasyaiva / yathā suṭaṣṭakāraḥ samudāyasya, [ṅakāraḥ] auṅ iti tadekadeśasyeti /

[2.2] plutyādiṣv ajvidhiḥ (vt. 2) / abhyugamya [abhyupagamya] plutaṃ dvirvacanādayo doṣā ucyante, yathābhyupagamya hrasvayalopau dhātuvidhitukpratiṣedha ucyate /

[2.3] saṃprati plutam eva codayati [--- plutasaṃjñā ceti (vt. 3)] / trimātro hi kālabhedād apratyāyitaḥ / tamapāraṇādyamapratipadyamāno (? tadagrahaṇād actvam apratipadyamāno) 'śakyaḥ pluta iti nirdeṣṭum / tatra yaḥ pluto 'sti kathaṃcid āsannaḥ, sa prāpnoti ikāra ukāraś ca / evaṃ sati gotrātādiṣu siddhaṃ ca dvirvacanam, aniṣṭas tu trimātraḥ śrūyeta /

[3] atapara eca igghrasvādeśe (vt. 4) / taparatve punaḥ saty ardhasya ekārasya bhinnakālatvād asaty actve hrasvasaṃjñāyā abhāvaḥ / nanu ca yasyāpi taparāṇi / etad uktaṃ bhavati / yogārambhaṃ prati sāmyam; eṣa tu viśeṣaḥ --- 'ecaḥ' ity u [uktam], saṃprati 'aica igghrasvādeśe' iti vaktavyam iti / aicos tu viśliṣṭāvarṇatvāt saṃśliṣṭāvarṇau ardhaekāra --- ardhaokārau na bhavataḥ iti / pratyākhyāyate tad [etad], 'aicoś cottarabhūyastvāt' (P 1.1.48, vt. 4) (p. 19) iti / ardhamātrā avsya [avarṇasya], adhyardhamātrā ivarṇovarṇayor ity eṣa prativibhāgaḥ / tatra brāhmagrāmaḥ [brāhmaṇagrāmaḥ] sālavanam iti yathā bhūyastvād vyapadeśaḥ, evam ihāpi iti / idam api pratyākhyāyate --- 'siddham eṅaḥ sasthānatvāt (P 1.1.48, vt. 3) iti / katham idaṃ tatra pratyākhyātam? na sta eva ardhaekāraardhaokārau iti / etau cen na staḥ, tālavyasya tālavyaḥ sasthānaḥ, oṣṭhyasya oṣṭhya iti / evaṃ śikṣākārāḥ pratipannāḥ oṣṭhyatālavyāv iti / atra ca [ardhaekāra ---]ardhaokārādarśanena śikṣābheda ity etat pratidya [pratipadyate] / kathaṃ tālavyatve saty ekārasya [aikārasya ca] śrutibhedaḥ, oṣṭhyatve ca okārasya aukārasya ca, teṣāṃ hi sthānaprayatnākāḥ [sthānaprayatnādikāḥ] sarve abhinnā iti? darśanabhedād adoṣaḥ / eke manyante / yo 'sau varṇasya niṣpādako vāyuḥ, sa ekārasya nirvṛttau svasamīpe yaḥ kaṇṭhas tam upaśleṣayati kevalam, sthānaṃ tālv eva / evam oṣṭhāv eva syānam [sthānam] okārasya, kevalaṃ tu vāyuḥ kaṇṭham abhihantīti / itare tad api sthānam abhimanyante / śrutibhedo śrotrāgramadhyamūlabhedād (? jihvāgramadhyamūlabhedād) bhavati yathā cayaśānām / nanu caiṅaḥ sasthānatarāv iti / evaṃ vidyate / vidyamānatvam etayor anena sasthānatvam uktam iti cet tāv eva sasthānatarau / na tau staḥ / lāghavāt pratyāhāre vidyamānau tāv eva paṭhitau syātām / nanu guṇavṛddhisaṃjārtha upadeśaḥ syāt / dīrghagrahaṇaṃ saṃjñāyāṃ kariṣyate / tathā ca sati 'sahivahorodavarṇasya' (P 6.3.112) iti taparakaraṇaṃ varṇagrahaṇaṃ ca kartavyaṃ bhavati / 

nanu ca bhośchandogānāṃ sātyamugrirāṇāyanīyāḥ / kimarthaṃ chandogagrahaṇam? sātyamugryādīnām aprakāśatvāt / sujāte aśvasūnṛte / aṃpādamavyaparasyaiva [antaḥpādamavyaparasyaiva] ekāram ādeśaṃ vidadhāti / pārṣadakṛtir eṣā tatrabhavatām / prātiśākhyaṃ te kurvanti / nanu caikacaraṇaviṣayam upalakṣaṇaṃ kriyate, yathā 'devasumnayor yajuṣi kāṭhake' (P 7.4.38) iti / satyam, evam etat / pārṣadakṛtir iti etad uktaṃ bhavati / pārṣadam uccāraṇam eṣām, svasthāne ca pārṣadavyavahāra eṣa, nātreti [nānyatreti] / evaṃprakārāś ca teṣāṃ bahavo vyavahārāḥ kitakiṭākāraḥ stobhādayaḥ / evartham [evamartham] idam uktaṃ chandogānām iti /

[3.1] āntaryas [āntaryatas] trimātracaturmātrāṇām / anyasyābhāvāt idam apy atra nāsti, 'plutaś ca viṣaye smṛtaḥ' (P 6.1.87, vt. 2) iti / iha tāvat paśum iti, '[ami] ekaḥ' itīyatā siddham / etad api vā na kartavyam / siddho hi dīrghaḥ 'prathamayoḥ pūrvasavarṇaḥ' (P 6.1.102) iti / satyam / evam etat / (p. 20) kiṃ tv ārabhyamāṇe 'ami' ity etasmin, 'vā chandasi' (P 6.1.106) ity etat katham ārambhād anuvartetyevamartham 'amyekaḥ' ity etat syāt / tasmāt pūrvagrahaṇaṃ pūrvarūpārthaṃ kriyate / [nanu ca pūrvakaṃ pūrvagrahaṇam anuvartate? satyam /] tathāpi dvitvam (? dīrghatvam) apy anuvartate / nanu (? tatra) caivamarthā pūrvānuvṛttiḥ, pūrvo dīrgho yathā syāt / paraṃ tūbhayāntaratamo mā bhūt / idam apy atrānuvartayitavyaṃ dvitīyaṃ pūrvagrahaṇaṃ 'prathamayoḥ pūrvasavarṇaḥ' iti / atha vācāryapravṛttir jñāpayati / nanu ca niyamārtham etat syāt, iha mā bhūt uta iti / idaṃ tarhi jñāpakaṃ 'sau ca' (P 6.4.13), 'saṃprasāraṇasya' (P 6.3.139) iti / etad api hrasvanivṛttyarthaṃ syāt, 'iko hrasvo 'ṅyo . . . ' (P 6.3.61) iti / tasyaivedaṃ liṅgam, saṃprasāraṇapūrvatve dvau pūrvaśabdāv anuvartete iti / 'ato guṇe paraḥ' [itīyatā] siddham / tatra ekāra okāraś ca dīrghāv eva, kiṃ rūpagrahaṇena? tadakārārtham eva vijñāyate / iha tāvat khaṭvarśya iti / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / 'ād guṇaḥ' (P 6.1.87) iti sarveṣu sthānam upasthiteṣu ekāraokārau khaṭvendraḥ khaṭvoḍhā iti avasthitau / akāraś ca śrūyate guṇasaṃjñaḥ, ity ato vigṛhītaḥ śrūyeta / yathoktam, 'jhakārabhakārāv api padāntau na staḥ' (MBh 1.36.3) iti /

tapare guṇavṛddhī / yady api saṃjñino bhinnās tathāpi saṃjñārūpeṇocyamānās talliṅgasaṃkhyā ucyante, yathā 'taraptamapau ghaḥ' (P 1.1.22) iti / atha vā, taparasahite śāstre tapare / yadvā, tapara iti saptamīyam / tare [tapare] viṣaye saṃjñini tapare guṇavṛddhisaṃjñe iti / 

kasya punas taparatvam ucyate? saṃjñinām / kasmāt? saṃjñāyāḥ parārthatvāt / tatropāyaṃ (? tatropāyaḥ) saṃjñāyās taparatvam iti saṃjñāyām apy āsaktaṃ liṅgaṃ saṃjñinām eva vijñāyate / yady evam, saṃjñināṃ kālāvadhāraṇaṃ nāsti, anuryamāṇatvāt [anuccāryamāṇatvāt] / tat kiṃkālam [kiṃkālaparam] idaṃ taparatvam ity ajñāte sati taparakaraṇasāmarthyāt savarṇānāṃ na syāt / idaṃ tu nāsti, dvimātrāṇāṃ, na punas trimātrāṇām iti / atha manyase saṃjñaiva tapareti, evam api ekāras tapara aikāraś ca savarṇaṃ tatkālaṃ pratipādayet, na tu okāraokārau ceti grahaṇam eva vā na syāt / api vā sarvakālasya pratipattiḥ syāt / saṃjñāyās tāvat taparatve na doṣaḥ / yatra hi ec ca aic ca samudāyo na bhavati, tatrāntyavarṇasya vikalāṅgā paribhāṣā nopatiṣṭhate / tatra 'taparas tatkālasya' (P 1.1.70) iti idam etāvati vyāpriyate ---yasya grahaṇam asti tasya tatkālasyeti / tac ca grahaṇaṃ taparatvāt / tatas tarhi 'ādir antyena (p. 21) sahetā' (p. 21), 'tanmadhyasya ca' (P 1.1.71, vt. 2) iti aṇekārokārayor grahaṇe yathā aṇ ity aḍ iti vā ādibhūtenākāreṇa pratyāyitā śrūmāṇā [aśrūyamāṇā] api ikārādayo grāhakāḥ [evam okāraaukārāv api] / yadāpi saṃjñinas taparāḥ, tadāpi 'ādir antyena sahetā' iti yaḥ saṃniviṣṭaḥ sa gṛhīta uttarakālaṃ tatkālān hṇāti [gṛhṇāti] / dvimātrayoś ca saṃnihita iti yuktā tatkālapratipattiś ca / 

tād api ras [paras] tapara iti / anayor upādānam ekaśeṣāt, pravṛttimittasyānupādāne [pravṛttinimittasyānupādāne] svarūpamātrāśrayaṇāt / 'sahivarodavarṇasya [sahivahorodavarṇasya]' (P 6.3.112) iti liṅgāt dvau yogau tantreṇoccāritau vipratipatter yathā vibhaktiviśeṣanirdeśāt 'ig yaṇaḥ saṃprasāraṇam' (P 1.1.45) iti / ekasyaiva vā śabdasya dvyarthābhidhānayaugapadyād iti / kiṃ dakāre prayojanam iti / [atha kiṃ takāre?] ṛkārāntānāṃ sarveṣām udāttatvād anunāsikatvāc [ananunāsikatvāc] ca pravartakaṃ nopapadyate taparakaraṇam, nāpi nivartakam anaṇtvād iti dakāreṇa tulyas takāra iti /

[4] ya ete [varṇeṣu] varṇaikadeśā varṇasamānākṛtayaḥ / saṃdhyakṣareṣv akārekārokārāḥ avayavā varṇāntaraikākṛtayas tathā ākārādiṣu mātrikā evāvayavāḥ / evam ṛkāre rephaḥ, ḷkāre lakāraḥ / kim ete agādicodanāsv antarbhūtāḥ? tadavayavasya grahaṇāgrahaṇayor hetum āha --- iha [hi] samudāyā apy upadiśyante, avayavā api / abhyantaraś ca samudāye avayavas tadyathā, vṛkṣaḥ pracalan sahāvayavaiḥ pracalatīti / pūrvo hetur grahaṇe, aparahetur aprahaṇe 'py atra nirdiṣṭo bhāṣye / hetus tu grahaṇe grahaṇam, agrahaṇe 'grahaṇam / 'akaḥ savarṇe dīrghaḥ' (P 6.1.101) ityevamādiṣv avayavā gṛhyante / kutaḥ? ihāgrahaṇāt (? iha grahaṇāt) / iha pratyāhāre / samudāyā apy upadiśyante eoṅ aiauc iti / avayavā api aiuṇ iti / yatra cāvayavapara upadeśas tatra vidyamāno 'pi samudāyo neṣyate upadiṣṭa iti / yathā aiuṇ i [iti] na samudāyaḥ / aṅ vā, aḍ vā / kiṃ tarhi? avayavā eva, yathā hayavaraḍ iti / akārasyātropadeśāta . . . uccārayitum aśakyo nāyam akārasyopadeśa iti valādiṣv anackā eva halo 'bhidhīyante / evam eṅ iti aij iti copadeśe 'ādir antyena sahetā' (P 1.1.71) iti samudāya evopadiśyate, nākārādayo 'vayavāḥ, teśām anyatropadeśāt / inupadiṣṭā [ihānupadiṣṭā] api tu 'eco 'yavāyāvaḥ' (P 6.1.78) iti samudāyāvayavādeśaṃ [samudāyāvayavā ādeśaṃ] pratipadyante 'numānād avayavāḥ na hi te śabdavācyāḥ / kiṃ tarhi? samudāyāḥ / yathā vṛkṣaśabdo na (p. 22) phalādivacanaḥ / tatraim [tatraivam] upasthitam --- yatra samudāyakāryaṃ ca saṃnipatati avayavakāryaṃ ca, tatra kena bhavitavyam? avayavakāryeṇa / kutaḥ? tasminn avayavasya coditatvāt / samudāyakārye hy avayavas tenaiva pāratantryam āpāditaḥ samudāyānurodhāt nivartate / agna āyāhīti / yathā 'ghṛtam asmai dīyatām' iti bhājanaṃ hastaprāptam api dadātau nāntarbhavati / tad dattam api na dattam, pratigṛhītam api na pratigṛhītam / evam antarbhūto 'py avayavo nāntarbhūta iti 'agna indra' ity ānumānikaṃ samudāyakāryaṃ na pratipadyate, pratyakṣacoditam eva pratipadyate akaḥ savarṇe dīrghatvam / sarvatra varṇā arkāśvādiṣu samudāyeṣv avasthitā akcodanāyāṃ gṛhyamāṇāḥ kāryabhāja iti yathaiva padādisamuyasthāḥ [padādisamudāyasthāḥ] kāryaṃ pratipadyante, evaṃ varṇasamudāyasthā api ity ataḥ pratyāhāragrahaṇāt kāryadeśeṣu grahaṇam iti /

saṃpraty agrahaṇe hetur ucyate --- abhyantaraś ca samudāye 'vayava iti / yan manyase pratyāhāropadeśe samudāyā evopadiṣṭā iti, etat katham? saty api samudāyopadeśe etac cintyam / kiṃ tāvad avayavo 'nupāttaḥ, na hi śakyate 'nyathā samudāyarūpaṃ niṣpādayitum iti samudāyasthagataivāsya upadeṣṭuḥ pravṛttir iti? athābhyantarīkṛtyāvayavān iti? vayaṃ brūmaḥ abhyantarīkṛtya ti [iti] / tatra yathaivādeśam antarbhāvāt pratipadyate, evam ectvam api; kāryeṣu tu mukhya eva vyapadeśaḥ iti / yathā praṣṭhyāṃ kriyāsaṅgo gauṇaḥ śabdalābhas tu mukhyaḥ, evam idam api kāryam avayavānāṃ yaduta saṃjñālābha iti, etām api kriyāṃ pratipadyante 'vayavāḥ / etat pratipādayati --- samudāyasaṃjñālā [samudāyasaṃjñālābhāt] pāratantryaṃ pratipadyamāno 'vayavaḥ pratyākhyāya pravartate / yathā sadāyo [samudāyo] 'py asti varṇāntaram evedaṃ jātyantarayuktam / yathā narasiṃhajātiḥ sadṛśaḥ kaścit puruṣajāteḥ samudāyāntaram eva / tatra hi siṃham [siṃhamāṃsam] ānīyatāṃ puruṣaṃāṃsaṃ veti [na] tanmāṃsānayanaṃ bhavati / tasmāt samudāyeṣv asanto 'vayavāḥ katham iva nirdiśyeran, kathaṃ vā pratipadyeran iti /

	aparaḥ prakāro gṛhyate / samudāyasamudāyinor bhedam āśrityocyate --- anye 'vayavāḥ, samudāyo 'nyaḥ / ubhayaṃ copadiśyate / tatra yaḥ saṃdhyakṣareṣv avayavo 'sti, sa katham iva kāryaṃ na pratipatsyate ti [iti] / abhyantaraś ca samudāye 'vayavaḥ iti / ekatvaṃ samudāyasamudāyinoḥ / nānyaḥ samudāyo 'sti, tathaivāvayavau / tathā saṃniviṣṭau ced vyapadeśaṃ labhete, yathā etad eva pracalanaṃ yadavayavānām iti /

atha vā, iha samudā [samudāyā] apy upadiśyante, avayavā apīti ekaviṣayatvam ucyate / eoṅ iti, aidauj iti asminn upadeśe ubhayam apy upadiśyate / (p. 23) tātparyeṇeti śeṣaḥ / yathā gargādibidādipāṭhaḥ samudāyasādhutvapratipattyartho 'vayavānāṃ ca kalādinivṛttyarthaḥ iṣṭaḥ / abhyantaraś ca samudāye 'vayavaḥ iti / samudāyārtha evopadeśo nāvayavārthaḥ / yathā pūrvapakṣe gargādibidādipāṭḥāt /

[4.1] samānākṣaravidhipratiṣedhaḥ (vt. 6) / agne indra iti / samudāyāvayavāśrayau vidhī prāpnutaḥ / ayādeśasyāvakāśaḥ 'agna ā yāhi' iti / dīrghatvasya dadhīndra iti / iha agne indra iti vipratiṣedhā [vipratiṣedhāt] dīrghatvaṃ prāpnoti / atha vā, ayādeśa ānumānikaḥ saṃpratīyo dīrghatvaṃ sākṣād iti sākṣāc chiṣṭo balīyaḥ iti / yaṇādeśe katham? tatrāpy avayavaḥ sākṣāc chiṣṭa iti / satyam, evam etat / anavakāśatvāt sa bādhyate 'yenāprāpte . . . ' iti, 'madhye 'pavādāḥ pūrvān . . . ' iti vā /

[4.1.1] yad ayaṃ dīrghāc che tukaṃ śāsti / dīrghāt paro yaśchas tasmiṃs tug bhavati / kasya? 'tasminn iti nirdiṣṭe pūrvasya' (P 1.1.66) iti / nanu caikāraukārārthaḥ syāt / tatra hy uttarā bhūyasī mātrā / tatrāpi śakyam ardhamātrām apanīya pūrvasya kartum iti / yogavibhāgo liṅgam, tuk na bhavatīti / 

[4.1.2] taparakaraṇasāmarthyān na bhaviṣyati / atra hi pravṛttiḥ kṛtārthā vṛkṣair iti / nivṛttir akṛtā [akṛtārthā] / nivṛtteś ca liṅgaṃ taparakaraṇam iti sākṣāc chrutir bādhyate dīrghān na bhavatīti anumānaśrutyā / 

[4.1.3] yad ayam āto 'nupasarga iti / atrāpy anubandhasyāsaṅgo 'navakāśa iti ākārasya nivṛttir ucyate / atra codyate / yadi kitkaraṇaṃ na kriyate, iha doṣaḥ 'mīnātiminotidīṅāṃ lyapi ca' (P 6.1.50) / yady ayam 'āto 'nupasarge kaḥ' (P 3.2.3) iti akāra ucyeta, ejviṣayo 'yam iti mīnātyādīnām ākāre kṛte, akāre ca pratyaye ādantānāṃ yugāgamakarasyāṇo 'prāptidoṣaḥ syāt / naiṣa doṣaḥ / dīṅas tāvad akarmakatvāt karmopapadatvaṃ nāsti / sopasargaḥ sakarmakaḥ syāt / tatrāpy anupasarga ity ucyate / mīnātiminotyor api 'hvāvāmaś ca' (P 3.2.2) iti ca 'āta' ity etat sāmānyagrahaṇam / evaṃ ca kṛtvocyate --- uttarārtham etat syāt iti / evaṃ tarhi 'gāpoṣṭak' iti / tatrāpi paryanuyujyate --- chandogāyeti lopaḥ syāt 'āto dhātoḥ' (P 6.4.140) iti / na ca pratipadoktam āśrīyate, papuṣa iti na syāt / (p. 24) saṃnipātalakṣaṇe yakāre tu kāra [ākāra] eva na syāt / ucyate / ātvenālopa iti lopa eva / tad evaṃ sati sarvathā lopena bhavitavyam iti / 

[4.2] ekavarṇavac ca (vt. 8) / saṃkhyā nivartikā sarvatreti vākśabdo dvyaj iti vyapadiśyate / tatraikavarṇavad ity atideśo vā / etu prākṛtaṃ dvyactvam iti / 'sāv ekācaḥ' (P 6.1.168) iti kva syāt? srucā tvaceti / atha vā, gonaugrahaṇam / sāmānyaviṣayam anumānaṃ sarvaviṣayaṃ kalpyate / 

[4.3] nāvyapavṛktasyāvayave tadvidhiḥ (vt. 9) / avidyamānaṃ vyapavṛktam asmin samupāye [samudāye] so 'vyapavṛktaḥ / tasyāvyapavṛktasyāvayave tadvidhiḥ na kriyate / atha vā, avyapavṛktasya yo 'vayavasyāpṛgbhūtasyāvayave ['vayavas tasyāpṛthagbhūtasyāvayave] pṛgbhūte [pṛthagbhūte] yo vidhir dṛṣṭaḥ sa vidhir na kriyate / saptadaśa sāmidhenyo bhavantīti / ṛkṣu pravṛttaḥ śabdaḥ sāhacaryāt kāṣṭheṣu vartate / na ca sadaśāratnimātraṃ [saptadaśāratnimātraṃ] kāṣṭhaṃ saptadaśavyapadeśaṃ labhate / bhedādiṣṭhānā hi saptadaśasaṃkhyā / saikasaṃkhyāviśiṣṭe na pravartate / tatra yathaivāvayavā apṛthagbhūtā avyapadeśyā evaṃ varṇaikadeśā api, iti / pracaṃ [pratyṛcaṃ] caiva hi tat [karma] codyate / tatronātirikte dohe karmavaiguṇyam / athaitat syāt, sarvam evābhyādhāya ṛca uccārayatv iti --- evam apy asaṃvaś [asaṃbhavaś] cāgnau vedyāṃ ca / ardhacaturthahastā hi vediḥ / yathā tarhi / evam artham upanyastam --- nātra ṛksaṃbandhāt samicchabdo 'lpapramāṇatvāc ca syāt saṃbhava iti / atrāpi pratipraṇavam iti / pūrvavad eva / yathā tarhi ghṛtaṃ tailam iti / etad uktaṃ bhavati --- na saṃbhavamātrāt pratipattir yuktā / saty api ghṛtatailasaṃbhave, ghṛtarūpasya gośabdenābhidhānāt [gośabdenānabhidhānāt] / tailarūpasya [ca] sarṣapaśabdenābhidhā [sarṣapaśabdenānabhidhānāt tayor] vikrayo bhavati / evaṃ hi saṃbhavamātre govikrayī māṃsavikrayy api syāt / eṣa tu nyāyo virudhyate, māṣā na bhoktavyā iti / anena hi māṣā hi vyatimiśrā api na jyante [bhujyante] / surāpeyeti miśrā api na pīyate /

yatra tarhi vyapavargo dṛśyate / kvacid ekasaṃkhyāviśiṣṭe 'pi vyapavargo dṛśyate ti [iti] / kva? apavarakāḍiṣu / ucyate hy ayaṃ pañcakhaṭvo 'yaṃ saprakha [saptakhaṭva] iti / evaṃ saṃdhyakṣareṣu prayatnasya virāmopādānābhyāṃ lakṣyaḥ pṛthagbhāva iti /

saṃdhyakṣareṣu vivṛtatvāt (vt. 10) / vivṛtataram iti / vivṛtatamam iti grahītavyam iti / tatra prayatnabhedāt sāvarṇyam eva nāsti iti kuto grahaṇam iti / (p. 25)

[5] atha vā punar na gṛhyante / idam uktam agrahaṇaṃ 'nāvyapavṛktasyeti', etad ākārādīnām / yatra ca vyapavargaḥ saṃdhyakṣareṣu, tatrāpy asāvarṇyam / ata ṛkāraḷkārāv evāvasthitau / tatra vākyakarāsyedaṃ darśanam --- naivāvayavau staḥ / samudāya eva sa jātyantaryukto narasiṃhavad iti / anena darśanenedaṃ prakramate ---

[5.1]++nuḍvidhilāḍeśavināmeṣu (vt. 11) iti / athāvayavāḥ santi tasmin samudāye, tatra samudāyavyāpāre 'vayavasya kāryayogitvaṃ na syād ayādiṣu / yatra tv avayava eva vyāpriyate, na samudāyam, tatra vidyamāno 'vayavaḥ kāryayogī na (?) syāt / nuḍādiṣu samudāyasyācoditatvāt avayavāśrayā nuḍādayaḥ pravartiṣyante / nārtha ṛkāragrahaṇena / yady evaṃ tuky api hrasvasyāśritvāt [hrasvasyāśritatvāt] tukā bhavitavyam. ālūya pralūyeti / tatra ca prativihitam --- avyapavargāj, jñāpakād vā 'dīrghāc che tukaṃ śāsti' iti, yogavibhāgād vā / aśnoter evāvarṇopadha [evāvarṇopadhasya] / evam anumānaṃ kalpate, na tv evam --- aśnoter eva kathaṃ syād, aśnāter mā bhūd iti /

avibhaktiko nirdeśaḥ / asmin nirdeśe sati ur iti nirdeśaḥ / tasyānavakāśatvād avayavarephasya latvaṃ bhaviṣyati / sphoṭamātraṃ pratinirdiśyate / adhvanikaḥ sphoṭa ity uktaṃ bhavati / nanu ca dhvanim antareṇa sphoṭasyopalabdhir eva nāsti / evaṃ tarhi ya evāsau ādyo dhvanir ārūpamātrasya pratipādakas tāvān evāśrīyate / yas tv asau viśeṣasya pratipādakaḥ --- ayaṃ samudāyastho 'yaṃ svatantra iti --- nāsāv āśrīyate / vidyamāne 'pi tatra viśeṣe ārūpamātram, yathā gor viśeṣeṇopalabdhir ārūpamātreṇa vopalabdhiḥ / tasmād ārūpamātragrahaṇam ubhayoḥ / atha vā, kāryavat buddhiṃ kṛtvā idam ucyate / tatra kāryapakṣe sphoṭa eva saṃyogāt, vibhāgāt, saṃyogavibhāgābhyāṃ vā niṣpadyate / yat tv anuraṇanaṃ tac chabdata eva / tena ya evāsau sphoṭasya niṣpādakaḥ karasya [karaṇasya] vyāpāras tāvata evāśrayaṇam / atha vā, aphoṭamātram iti ākṛtinirdeśo 'yam ity uktaṃ bhavati / tatrākṛtinirdeśe nāntareṇa dhvanim ākṛtiṃ pratipadyate iti nāntarīyakaṃ dravyaṃ pratipattavyam iti dravyanirdeśe śrutasya kāryāyogitvam iti ākṛtyāśrayaṇasyedaṃ prayojanam antarbhūtānantarbhūtayo rephayoḥ pratipattyartham /

yat tad rephasya pa [paraṃ] bhakteḥ / repheṇa vā [vāsyāḥ] sāmānādhikaraṇyam, bhaktiḥ rephas, tasyā yat param iti / atha vā, akārabhaktir ikārabhaktir vā, tasyā yat param iti / aḍvyavāya ity evaṃ bhaviṣyati / evaṃ manyate --- yathākāro 'ṇtvāt dīrghaplutau gṛhṇāti, evaṃ bhaktam api grahīṣyati iti / ākṛtigrahaṇe vā sarvatrākṛter bhāvāt (p. 26) asty atra idaṃ darśanam iti / anena darśanena grahītuṃ śakyās ta eva ye saṃniviṣṭā iti / etad uktaṃ bhavati / yeṣām anyatrāṭtvam astīti / [ye] vyapavṛktā api varṇā bhavantīti / etad uktam ity anena yojanīyam / idaṃ tu varṇaturīyam anyatrādṛṣṭaṃ kvacit, sāvarṇyam api nāsti / na hy atra samudāyaprayatnād anyo 'vayavaprayatno, nāpi sthānam / na ceyān varṇabhāgaḥ śaknoti jātim upavyañjayitum iti / etair evākṣarasamāmnāyikair iti / pratyāyyā api ākṣarasamāmnāyikā ākārādayaḥ prtipattavyāḥ / bhaktis tu na pratyāyikā na pratyāyyā / tadartho yogavibhāgaḥ / 

[5.2] plutāvaica uditau (vt. 12) / asti hy atra vyapavargaḥ / saty api vyapavarge yadāgrahaṇam, tadā idaṃ vaktavyam 'aica uditau plutau' iti / sati tu grahaṇe aikāro gurus tasya yo 'ntyo 'c tadādi ṭisaṃjñam iti 'guroranṛta . . . ' (P 8.2.86) ity eva siddham / ākārādīnāṃ cātrābhāvo 'vyapavargāt / kriyata [etan] nyāsa eva iti / etat pradarśayati --- yady api gṛhyante, tathāpi idaṃ na sidhyati / evaṃ hy uktam 'saṃdhyakṣareṣu vivṛtatvāt' iti / tatra saty api samabhāge, bhinnaprayatnatvād asati grahaṇe actvam eva nāsti, kutaḥ plutaḥ? athāpy uttarabhūyastvam, eva py [evam apy] adhyardhamātrikasya nāsty actvam / na cārdhamātrāyās tasyā asti vyapavargaḥ / tasmāt yathānyāsāśrayaṇam eva yuktam iti /

[5.3] tulyarūpe saṃyoge dvivyañjanavidhiḥ (vt. 13) / tulyarūpāvayavatvāt tulyarūpaḥ saṃyogaḥ / na hy ekatvāt saṃyogasya tulyarūpatvam asti / abhiprāyaḥ --- yathaivākārādivyapavarga [yathaivākārādiṣv avyapavarga] iti nāsti avayavagato vyavahāra evam ihāpi tulyaḥ avyapavarga iti / ākṛtinirdeśenālpatvamahattvakṛtasya viśeṣasyānupādānāt samudāyo hal iti dvivyañjanāśrayā saṃyogasaṃjñā na syāt / evaṃ sati gurusaṃjñāyām asatyāṃ 'guroranṛta ---' (P 8.2.86) iti plutābhāvaḥ / mātrākālo 'tra gamyate, na ca mātrikaṃ vyañjanam asti / ihākārādayas tāvat pāṭhād avagamyante / ākārādayo 'pi santīti sāmānyato dṛṣṭānumānād --- 'asti hy agākṛtidīrghā, yathā saṃdhyakṣarāṇi' / etāni hi dvimātrāṇy eva paṭhitāni / pluto 'dhiko nirdiṣṭaḥ, 'ūkālo 'jjhrasvadīrghaplutaḥ' (P 1.2.27) iti / tasya grahaṇam asti 'aṇudit savarṇasya . . . ' (P 1.1.69) iti / tatropadeśāt vidhānāc ca manyāmahe, śiṣṭair abhyupagatā eta upadiṣṭā iti / vyañjanānāṃ tu nopadeśo, na grahaṇam, na kvacit vidhānam asti / ataḥ śiṣṭair anupadiṣṭāḥ kathaṃ śakyā (p. 27) nirdeṣṭum iti / etasya cedaṃ bījam --- yathākārādiṣu ekaprayatnapreritakaraṇadhvanir dhvanyantaram ārabhamāṇo 'nuparata eva karaṇavyāpāre kālabhedaṃ darśayati, naiva vyañjaneṣu / vyañjanam uccārya punar yatnāntareṇa kaṇaṃ [karaṇaṃ] vyāpriyate vyañjanāntara ity atrāvipratipattiḥ śiṣṭānām / ayaṃ viśeṣo yad ihāsakṛd uccārayati / tatra yathaivāsakṛd akāram uccārayan punaḥ punaḥ karaṇaṃ vyāpārayati, evaṃ vyañjaneṣv iti / 

iha tu katham --- sayṁyantā satsaraḥ [savṁvatsaraḥ] talṁlokaṃ yalṁlokam iti / pūrvatra grahaṇaṃ nāstīti abhāvaṃ pratipannaḥ / antasthās tūpadiṣṭā aṇsu / tā grahīṣyante samudāyaṃ tatrākāravad imāḥ pratipāditāḥ śāstreṇeti / atrāpi mātrākālo gamyate / idam apy atrāsti --- eko 'trānunāsikaḥ, aparo niranunāsikaś ca / atra sānunāsiko niranunāsikaś ca bhaviṣyatīti durupapādam etat / tasmād upasaṃhṛtya karaṇaṃ yatnāntareṇa punar dvitīyasyoccāraṇam iti nāsti mātrākālaṃ vyañjanam / asac cet kathaṃ gṛhyeta? vidyamāno hi śakyaḥ savarṇena grahītum / na hi mātrākālo 'kāraḥ śaktaḥ mātram [ardhamātram] akāraṃ grahītum / kutaḥ? abhāvād iti / 

ha ya va ra ṭ //5//
[I]
[1] ayaṃ hakāro dvir upadiśyate, pūrvaś caiva paraś ca / pūrvaparatvaṃ na varṇāntarāpekṣam / varṇāntarāpekṣāyāṃ hy akāraṃ muktvā sarve eva pūrve pare ca / 

[1.1] aḍgrahaṇeṣu grahaṇam [hagrahaṇam] (vt. 1) iti / kiṃcid atrāḍgrahaṇaṃ niṣprayojanam uccāryate, 'śaścho 'ṭi' (P 8.4.63) iti / aḍgrahaṇaṃ cāpi prayojanavad upanyastaṃ vākyakāreṇa / atra hi 'mahāṁ hi saḥ' iti hakārasyānaṭtvāt 'āto 'ṭi . . . ' (P 8.3.3) iti rutvaṃ na syāt /

[1.2] aśgrahaṇam api tu prayojayati 'bhobhago . . .' (P 8.3.17) iti / atra hi 'bho hasati' ity atra anaśtvād 'aśi' iti yatvaṃ na syāt / arthapradarśanamātram idam / na [tena] vaktavyam --- utve hakāragrahaṇam iti / 

[1.3] yadi [ca] kiṃcid anyatrāpy upadeśa iti / etat pradarśyate --- na hy atra varṇānāṃ rūpapratipattyarthaḥ pāṭhaḥ / kiṃ tarhi? śāstrapravṛttyarthaḥ / tac ca śāstraṃ saty anupadeśe hakārasya na vyavatiṣṭhate iti / (p. 28)

[II]
[1] ayaṃ rephaḥ / ubhayatra prayojanaṃ doṣāś ca santīti praśnaḥ / 

[1.1] 'svarnayati' iti . . . anunāsiko ṇakāraḥ prāpnoti / na hy asmin yoge savarṇagrahaṇam astīti / madrahrada iti / atra dvirvacane rephasya kṛte asiddhe dvitve [na] 'ro ri' (P 8.3.14) lopa iti dvau rephau śrūyeyātām / kuṇḍaṃ rathena iti / atra cānunāsikenaiva parasavarṇena bhavitavyam ity asya viśeṣasyānupādānāt parasavarṇo 'nunāsiko ['nanunāsiko] rephaḥ prāpnoti / parasavarṇasya asiddhatvāt 'ro ri' lopo nāsti / 

[2] pūrvopadeśe kittvapratiṣedhaḥ (vt. 5) / pūrvopadeśe sati vakāro 'pi ral iti / vyalopavacanaṃ ca (vt. 5) / valyanantarbhāvāt rephasya / vyalopavacanaṃ vakārārthaṃ pratyākhyātaṃ na yakārārtham iti /

[2.1] rephoṣmaṇāṃ savarṇā na santi / nanu coktam, ekatra sāvarṇyasyācoditatvāt, anyatrānunāsikasya [ca], bhavitavyaṃ rephaṇakārābhyām iti / satyam, evam etat / ayaṃ tu vaktā dvirvacane parihāraṃ bruvan tayor api pratividhatte / nemau rahau kāryiṇau [dvirvacanasya] / kiṃ tarhi? nimittam imau rahau dvirvacanasya iti / asmin hi vākye 'aco rahābhyām . . . ' (P 8.4.46) ity atra rephahakārau nimittatvenāśritau notsahete asmāt vākyāt kāryitvaṃ pratipattum / kutaḥ? asmin vākye 'yara' iti nirdeśe rephahakārayor anumīyamānatvāt / 'rahābhyām' iti nimittatvena sākṣāt coditatvāt / tadyathā / brāhmaṇā bhojyantāṃ māṭharakauṇḍinyau pariveviṣṭām iti / asmin prakaraṇe vākye bhujikriyā tayor anumīyamānā sākṣāt coditayā pariveṣaṇakriyayā bādhyate ity etasmāt vākyāt na tau bhujiṃ prati kartṛtvam anubhavata iti / 

yady evaṃ dadhyudake doṣaḥ / 'iko yaṇ aci' (P 6.1.77) ity asmin nirdeśe aj iti nimittabhāvaṃ prati anumānā [anumīyamānā] ikaḥ kāryayogitvaṃ prati sākṣān nirdiṣṭā iti bādhitavyo nimittabhāvaḥ kāryayogitvena / atha matam etat, ekasya nimittabhāvāt kāryayogitvaṃ nāsti, iha tu bhinnāv eva 'ikaḥ' 'aci' iti, ihāpi madrahrade hakāro nimittam anya eva, tasmāt paro repho dvirvacanaṃ kim evaṃ na pratipadyate?

darśanabhedādoṣaḥ [darśanabhedād adoṣaḥ] / ākṛtiḥ padārthaḥ, sakṛllakṣaṇaṃ pravṛttam, samudāye lakṣaṇaṃ pravartate, jñānāṅgaṃ śāstram ity etasmin darśane idam ucyate 'rephoṣmaṇāṃ savarṇā na santi', (p. 29) 'nemau rahau kāryiṇau' iti / katham iha 'yaro 'nunāsike 'nunāsiko vā' (P 8.4.45) iti cet, śāstram anapekṣita [anapekṣitaprayogam] / prayoge rephoṣmaṇāṃ anunāsikādeśo nāsti / śāstratve (? śāstraṃ ca) punar na pravartate, yataḥ pratipattiḥ syāt / yathoktam --- 'jhakārabhakārau padāntau na staḥ' (MBh 1.36.1--2) iti jhuṭbhuṭau na bhaviṣyata iti / evam 'anusvārasya yayi parasavarṇaḥ' (P 8.4.58) iti anusvārasamudāyaṃ parasavarṇasamudāyaṃ [ca] niścitya śāstraṃ pravartate / tatra 'sthāne 'ntaratamaḥ' (P 1.1.50) iti samudāyenānunāsikanimittam āntaratamyam / bhavati hi samudāyaḥ keṣucid avayaveṣu tadvatsu tadvān / yathā 'gomad idaṃ nagaram', 'daṇḍino gacchanti' iti / evaṃ parasavarṇenāntara [parasavarṇenāntaratamena] bhavitavyam iti pratipattikāle / rephas tathābhūto nāstīti aparādhyati / sakṛllakṣaṇavyāpāra iti nāsti repho 'nunāsikaḥ / evam 'aco rahābhyām . . . ' ity etad api śāstram anapekṣitaprayogam / kim asti prayoge rephahakārayor ekasmin sāṃnidhyam? nāsti / etāvati pravartate, rahābhyām uttarasya yaro dve bhavata iti / tatra yathaiva yaro (? hakāro) nivartitā (? nivartitaḥ), evaṃ repho 'pīti / etāvati jñāte punaḥpravṛttyabhāvāt kayā pravṛttyā hakārāt paro repho dvirvacanaṃ pratipadyate?

dadhyudakādiṣu punaḥ vyaktiḥ padārthaḥ, prayogāṅgaṃ śāstram, asakṛllakṣaṇaṃ pravartate, pratilakṣyaṃ yāni bhinnāni lakṣaṇāni [tāni] ālasyāt tu noccāritāni iti anena darśanena pratipattir iti / ataś ca 'na veti vibhāṣā' (P 1.1.44) iti pakṣe 'bhyupagamaḥ pakṣe pratyākhyānam / yugapal lakṣye lakṣaṇaṃ pravartata ity ayam āvedvānyaḥ? / madrahradādisiddhis tenaiva jñāpitā bhavati / pratilakṣyaṃ vyāpāre śāstrasya prākṛtī bhavati siddhir dadhyudakādiṣu / yadvadyogas tasmād aśiṣyo 'yam? / 

[III]
[1] ime ayogavāhāḥ na kvacid upadiśyante / pratyāhāre 'nyavan na paṭhyante / śrūyante ca prayoge, śāstre ca vidhānakāle 'kharavasānayor visarjanīyaḥ' (P 8.3.15), 'kupvoḥ :ka:pau' (P 8.3.37) iti / teṣāṃ kāryārtha upadeśaḥ kartavyaḥ / vidhānād eteṣāṃ sādhutvaṃ syāt, yat tu kāryaṃ pratyāhārapratibaddhaṃ tan na syāt iti / ke punar ayogavāhāḥ? pūrvācāryasaṃjñeyam / mahatī camūrnūnam iyam anvarthā iti pṛcchati --- kathaṃ punar iti / [yad] ayuktā vahanti, anupadiṣṭāś ca śrūyante / iha kecit varṇā yuktā vaha [vahanti], lakṣaṇena vākṛtyā [vā] kāryaṃ pratipadyante, yathākārādayaḥ / (p. 30) e [ete] hi 'aṇudit savarṇasya . . . ' (P 1.1.69) ity anena vā yuktā vahanti, akārākṛtisamanvayāt [vā] / kecit punar upadiṣṭāḥ śrūyante, yathākārādayaḥ / evam ime na lakṣaṇena yuktā, nāpy ākṛtyā, nāpy upadiṣṭā iti / athāyogavāhaśabde ko vyāpāro 'nupadiṣṭatvasya? eṣo 'bhiprāyaḥ --- dvividho yogaḥ, lakṣaṇayoga upayogo [upadeśayogo] vā / dvividho 'py eṣāṃ nāsti iti / anyeṣāṃ grantho --- yadayuktā vahanti, anupadiṣṭā [anupadiṣṭāś ca] śrūyanta iti / te 'yuktā vahanti ity asyaivārtham anupadiṣṭāḥ śrūyanta ity anena pradarśayanti / upadeśaś ca śāstre lakṣaṇena pāṭhena vā / sa caiṣāṃ nāstīti /

[2] [śarṣu jaśbhāvaṣatve (vt. 7) /] śartvadaśāyāṃ jhaltvānurodhena jaśtvam ucyate / jaśtvaṃ cātrāpy upadeśena siddham / upadhmānīyādeḥ / yadi pūrvatrāsiddham advirvacane, athāpi pūrvaṃ dvirvacanam, ubhayathāpi aniṣṭaṃ rūpam / nipādanā [nipādanād eva] siddham iti / ko vṛttānto dvirvacane? atrāyam abhiprāyo --- nipātanaṃ hi liṅgam / naitat pratipatsyāmahe, anenāsya bhatvaṃ bhavatīti / kena punaḥ? 'hṛgrahorbhaśchandasi hasya' (P 8.2.32, vt.) iti / vākyaśeṣo 'numāsyate udjeś ca dakārasyeti / na tv idam eva nipātanaṃ prāpakasty [prāpakam asty] asyeti / akutvaviṣaye [tat] nipātanam iti / viśiṣṭaviṣayam anumānaṃ labhyate / gamer eṣa dvyusargāḍ [dvyupasargāḍ] ḍa iti / saṃprati yady api nipātanam eva pratipādakam, tathāpi dakāpadeśasāmarthyān [dakāropadeśasāmarthyān] nipātanaṃ pratīkṣiṣyate dvirvacanam iti /

[3] avaśyaṃ numo grahaṇaṃ kartavyam / etasmin viṣaye numo 'nusvāreṇa bhavitavyam / tatraivam artham eva numo grahaṇam --- yatraivānusvāras [yatraivāsyānusvāras] tatra yathā syād iti / śarvyavāye tu saty anusvāramātre prāpnoti / --- puṃsv iti / tasmā [tasmāt] numo grahaṇam avasthitam / 

[4] atha vāviśeṣeṇopadeśaḥ kartavyaḥ / anitad anenocyate, aḍbhyaḥ śarbhyo 'nyatrāpi kartavya iti / kiṃ tarhi? prayojanagato viśeṣa udāhriyate / ṇatvaṃ prayojanam, jaśbhāvaṣatve prayojana [prayojanam], iti etan na vaktavyam / aṭsu ced upadiśyante śarṣu ca, yāny aviśeṣaprayojanāni tāni sarvāṇi siddhāni bhavantīti / śarṣūpadeśād eva haltvam, tataḥ saṃyogasaṃjñāsti [śvaḥ] stoteti / aṭsu cāltvam / 

[5] nirdiśyamānasyādeśā bhavantīti / yathā vaiyāghrapadya iti / (p. 31)

[6] dvirvacanam --- uraḥkeṇeti (? uraḥka iti) / śrutibhedam adhyavayocyate [adhyavasāyocyate] /

[7] vyūḍhoraskeneti / aṭi viśeṣe sāmānyam altvam asti / visarjanīyaś ca syād upadeśād al iti / ṇatvam alviyas [alviṣayas] tatra nāsti sthānivadbhāva iti / 

[IV]
[1] kiṃ punar ime varṇā arthavantaḥ / sati nimitte prayojane ca praśnaḥ / tatreha kiṃ nimittaṃ vā prayojanam? ucyate / iha kiṃcit pratyakṣata upalabhyate / kiṃcid anupalabhyamānād [anupalabhyamānam anumānād] astīti vyavasthāpyate / kiṃcit pratyakṣābhimatam anumānān nāsti iti pratīyate / vṛkṣādiṣu ca samudāyeṣu varṇānāṃ pratyakṣato na kaścit artha upalabhyate / tatrānumānād astitvam avasthāpyate, yathānupalabhyamānāyā ādityagater deśāntaraprāptyā / alātacakravat ca pratyakṣābhimasyāpi [pratyakṣābhimatasyāpi] tv em [evam] anumānād eva kriyate, 'kriyasyāpi tadrūpasya darśanāt / tadyathā / rathacakram uparatakriyam api cakrabuddhinimittam, naivam alātacakram / tasmād bhrāntir eṣā, yad eṣā vyāpino rūpasyopalabdhiḥ kila / carūpasya [cakrarūpasya] sahātyantam aviyogāt sparśasya / sa cakre hi yathaiva rūpopalabdhiprabandhaḥ, evaṃ sparśopalabdhiprabandho 'pi / naivam alātacakre / viśiṣṭadeśasthaiva hi sparśopalabdhiḥ / arthavattā ca vyākaraṇe sarvatraivānumānikī / anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ dhātvādīnām / varṇānām api śakyate 'rthaṃ kalpayitum saṃghātārthavattvād iti / 

prayojanaṃ cārthavacchabde prātipadikasaṃjñā, tadāśrayā ca svādyutpattir iti / arthavattā varṇānāṃ yadi tv ānumānikī, samudāyārthād asaṃbaddhenaivārthenārthavattā surāṅgavat / atha vā samudāyasaṃbandhibhir evārthair yathā bhavati tathā samudāyasaṃbandhino 'rthā vayavānāṃ [avayavānāṃ] pravibhajyante --- kriyāṃ dhātur āha, sādhanaṃ pratyaya iti / evaṃ samudāyo dravyaṃ bravīti, jātyādīṃs tv avayavān varṇāḥ / atha vā, yathā padāni sāpekṣāṇi tam evārtham āhuḥ, evaṃ tam evārthaṃ varṇā api parasparāpekṣayā brūyur iti / 

[2.1] ekavarṇānām arthadarśanāt (vt. 9) / anvayavyatirekābhyām hy artho dṛśyate / kim atra darśanaṃ hetuḥ? yadi darśanam, yeṣv eva dṛśyate teṣām evārthavattā prāpnoti, nānyeṣām / tasmād ekavarṇatvaṃ [ekatvaṃ varṇatvaṃ] ca hetuḥ / iha dhātvādīnām ekatvavarṇatvābhyāṃ sahacaritārthavattā dṛṣṭā / amī ca ekatvavarṇatvābhyām anugatāḥ / tasmād eṣām apy arthavattānugamena (p. 32) bhavitavyam iti / sa ayaṃ tāvaty etadharmo (? etadvarṇadharmo) 'yaṃ tv asyābhiprāyaḥ / iha yo 'rthātmā kenacit dharmeṇāyogyaḥ so 'tyantam ayogyaḥ / tadyathā / paramāṇur upalabdhiṃ praty ayogyaḥ sarvatraivāyogyaḥ syāt / tasmā [tasmāt] tipādyamānena [pratipādyamānena] dharmeṇa yo [yogyo] varṇa iti / sa tu kadācid arthavattopalabhyate kevalasyaivāsāpekṣasya / 

[2.2] varṇavyaye [varṇavyatyaye] cārthāntaragamanāt (vt. 10) / yathā rājavyatyaya iti ekasya cāpagame anyasya copaga [copagame] bhavati, evam iha kasyacit varṇasyāpagame [anyasya copagame] varṇavyatyayaḥ parigṛhītaḥ / ihārthavatām upagamāpagamābhyām arthāntaragamanaṃ dṛṣṭam / yathā pratilomo 'nuloma iti lomaśabde vyavasthite anupratyor arthavator upagamāpagamābhyām arthāntaragatir bhavati prātikūlyasyānukūlyasya ca, evam ihāpi ūpaśabde 'vasthite kakārasakārayor vyatyaye 'rthāntaragatir bhavati / tasmād asty etayor arthavattā / 

keṣāṃcit granthaḥ --- yaḥ kūpe kūpārthaḥ sa kārasya [kakārasya] iti / ta evaṃ varṇayanti --- yady api sāpekṣasya, tathāpi asau bhavatīti / anyeṣāṃ granthaḥ --- sakārasya iti / ta evam āhuḥ --- saha kakāreṇārthavattā iti / saha pratiśabdenānunā ca yatheti / 

[2.3] varṇānupalabdhau cānarthagateḥ (vt. 11) / ihārthavato 'nupalabdhau so 'rtho na gamyate / yathā pibati vipibati iti / vir atra kbhāvakarmā [pṛthagbhāvakarmā], vipibati ao 'yaṃ pṛthakkarotīti / viśabdānupalabdhau pibatīti pṛthagbhāvo na gamyate / evaṃ pratiṣṭhate tiṣṭhatīti / iha ca ṛkṣa iti vakārānupalabdhau mūlādisamudāyo na gamyate / atraitad uktaṃ bhavati --- sarve varṇāḥ sarvābhidhānaśaktiyukāḥ / teṣu tu tasyāṃ tasyām ānupūrvyāṃ kācit śaktis tirobhati, kācid āvirbhavati iti / 

'anarthagateḥ' iti cāsamarthasamāsaḥ, arthasyāgateti [arthasyāgater iti] / asya pūrvasmād eṣa viśeṣaḥ --- pūrvatrāpāyopagamāv iha tv apāyamātram eveti / kiṃ tarhi ucyate ''nagateḥ ['narthagateḥ]' iti / evaṃ hi bhavān āha so 'rtho na gamyata iti / evaṃ ca bruvatā idam uktam --- arthāntaraṃ na (? tu) gamyata iti / tatra kim ucyate arthasyāgateḥ iti? na sādhīya iti / laukikī vācyoyuktiḥ / tadyathā / kaścit aṃcid āha --- kim anena gatena? nāyaṃ kariṣyatīti / kaścit pratyāha --- ayam eva gacchatu, sutarāṃ kāryaṃ kariṣyatīti / tasyārthas tadarthaḥ / tasya śabdasyārthaḥ prakrāntasyāgater [prakrāntasya, tasyāgater] iti / atha manyase, tasyeti kiṃ samudāyo nirdiśyate atha varṇa iti, tasyaiva vastunaḥ sādhyatvāt ayuktam iti tata āha (p. 33) --- so 'rthas tadarthaḥ / yo 'rthaḥ prakrāntaḥ, so 'rthatvenāśritaḥ / sa eva vakārāpāye na gamyate iti / tadyathā . . . uṣṭramukha iti / atra noṣṭra eva mukhaṃ saṃbhavatīti ivaśabdo nivartate / sa ca samudāyaśabdo nānyatra avayave vṛtta iti mukhaśabdo nivartate / sa ca samudāyaśabdo nānyatra avayave, mukha evety em [evam] api viśeṣeṇocyate / evam ihāpi prakṛtārthe tasyānabhidhānam āśrīyata iti sāmarthyāt gamyate idam --- tatraiva tacchabdārtham upādāya pravṛtta iti / 

[2.4] saṃghātārthavattvāc ca (vt. 12) / darśanam adarśanaṃ ca hetuḥ / yathā tile 'sti kācit tailamātrā, evaṃ varṇeṣv api asti kācit arthasaṃbodhanamātrā / anekavarṇoccāraṇe tu paripakvā buddhir arthaṃ pratipadyata iti / 

[3] yadi tarhi ime varṇā arthavantaḥ, arthavatkṛtāni prāpnuvanti / etad uktaṃ bhavati --- prayoge 'rthavattāyāṃ satyāṃ na doṣo 'sti / śāstre tu ṅiṃcid vinaśyatīti / syād eṣā buddhiḥ --- na saṃjñā doṣāyeti / ata āha kārthatvāt [kāryārthatvāt] saṃjñāyāḥ svādyutpattiḥ syāt / śravaṇaṃ tu na svādayati [svādeś codayati], vibhakter lukā bhavitavyam / 

nanu cārthavatsamudāyānāṃ niyamārthatve na vākyasyārthavata [prātipadikasaṃjñā] iti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / ekakālaiva prātidikasaṃjñā [prātipadikasaṃjñā] varṇānāṃ samudāyasya ceti nāsti subantasamudāyatvam / ekatvam eṣām arthaḥ / atha vā, aviśeṇotpadyante [aviśeṣeṇotpadyante], utpannānāṃ niyama iti asti vibhaktiyogaḥ / nanu ca samudāyād utpadyate vibhaktir nāvayavebhyaḥ, samudāyadvirvacanavat / satyam, evam etat / avayavāḥ pṛthadatvenārthinaḥ [pṛthak padatvenārthinaḥ] / tatra yadi manyase, samudāye dvirukte avayavā api dviruktā iti, evaṃ samudāyasya padatve 'vayavānām api padatvam ity asty eva padakāryaṃ tarhi / 

[4.1] saṃghātasyaikārthyāt subabhāvo varṇāt (vt. 13) / kecid varṇayanti / saṃghātasyaikatvād yad evam apy artho varṇānāṃ, jātyādayo naikatvaprayuktāḥ / kāryakāle (? kāryakālatve) saṃjñāparibhāṣayo [saṃjñāparibhāṣayoḥ] prātipadikāt 'dvyekayor dvivacanaikavacane' (P 1.4.22) iti katham ekatvarahitānām avyayānāṃ vibhaktir iti / avyayād āpsupo lugvacanaṃ liṅgaṃ subutpatter iti / 

[4.2] apare varṇayanti / varṇāḥ (? varṇānāṃ) saty api arthavattve vastumātrasaṃnipāta etāvat gamyate --- prakrāntaṃ kiṃcid vaktuṃ vastv iti / tasya tu vastunaḥ saṃkhyādhigamaḥ samudāyād eva / tasmāt samudāyasyaikatvam artha iti / (p. 34)

[4.3] anye varṇayanti / ekam etad ekatvaṃ varṇānāṃ samudāyasya ca / tatra samudāyād utpadyamānā vibhaktir yadi [samarthā] na syāt sarvasādhāraṇam ekam [ekatvam] abhivyaṅktum utpadyate vibhaktiḥ / yatas tu samarthā . . .  / yathāneko draṣṭā naikenaiva pradīpena kāryaṃ kurvan pradīpāntaram upādatte / 

[4.4] ihabhavantas tv āhuḥ --- saṃghātasyaikatvam arthaḥ / sahaiva varṇair yathā naikācāṃ samudāya ekāc, tenaivāvayavā api / śaknoti cāvayavān anumānāt spraṣṭuṃ samudāyād utpadyamānā vibhaktiḥ / na ca padasaṃjñāvayavānām / kutaḥ? padasaṃjñāvidhānasya śabdattvāt [śabdavattvāt] / śabdavadvidhānaṃ padasaṃjñāyā yasmāt suptiṅvidhiḥ yathaivāṅgasaṃjñā yā śabdavati [śabdavatīti] kākadantaparīkṣaṇam idam --- 'kiṃ varṇā arthavanto 'narthakā' iti / yady arthavanto yady anarthakā na kaścid doṣaḥ / 

[5.1] anarthakās tu prativarṇam arthānupalabdheḥ (vt. 14) / anumānād apy eṣām arthavattā nopalabhyate / na hi kṣa iti kaścid artho 'nvayī lakṣyate, yathā [bhavati] bhavato bhavantīti sattānvayinī / tasmā [tasmāt] daśadāḍimaślokavat pratyakṣānumānābhyām anarthakā varṇā iti / prativarṇam iti / vākye śrutasya prater lakṣaṇādiṣu karmapravacanīyatvāt pṛcchati --- kim idaṃ prativarṇam iti / itaro dvirvacanena vīpsāṃ darśayati --- varṇaṃ [varṇaṃ] prati iti / vīpsā ca samāse nivartate uktatvāt, saptaparṇo 'ṣṭāpadam iti yathā / 

[5.2] varṇavyatyayāpāyopajanavikāreṣu cārthadarśanāt (vt. 15) iti / nāyam ānarthakyaprasādhako hetuḥ / kiṃ tarhi? pareṇoktasya vyabhicārahetur ayam / vyatyayaś cehāvasthitayoś caikasmin samudāye āder antyatvam, antyasya cāditvam iti / kālavyatyāsena prayogaḥ / bhavatyādidhātvādibhyo 'nyeṣāṃ varṇānāṃ arthavattāyām idam anumānam uktaṃ bhavati / iha sati varṇavyatyaye 'rthavyatyayo na dṛśyate / yadi ca varṇāḥ pratyekam anyonyenārthavantaḥ syuḥ, yathaivādyantāv antādī ity ādyantaprayogabhinnaḥ saṃpratyayaḥ, evam ihāpi syāt / atha manyase --- eka evārthaḥ sarvavarṇeṣu pratyekaṃ parisamāpter iti, evam api vyatyayasya vaicitryam --- kvacid arthāntaragatiḥ kūpādiṣu, kvacit tasyaivārthasya gatis tarkvādiṣu, kvacid ānarthakyam eva kākaḥ kakā iti / apāyo yathaiva vakārasyārthāntaragatiś caivam ihāpi pāyo [apāyo] ghnantīti akārasya, [na] tadārthāntaragatir asti / tasmād anumānaṃ [na] bhavaty arthavattāyāḥ / lavitā lavitum iti / arthavato hi padasya upajane 'rthasyāpy upajano dṛṣṭaḥ, datto yajñadatta iti / śabdaś cānumānikyārthavattayā pravartamānaḥ śaktyādhānaṃ vā . . . , yathaupagava iti / (p. 35) na caivam āgamas tasmād varṇasya kuto 'rthavattā? vikāraḥ sūpādibhiḥ samāno 'trāpi kaścid varṇo  'paiti, kaścid upajāyate, na cārthāntaragatiḥ / 

[6] ubhayam idaṃ varṇeṣūktam / etat pṛcchati --- kiṃ dhātvādīnād [dhātvādīnām arthavattvād] idaṃ pratipattavyam --- sarve 'rthavantaḥ, anupalabdhis tu kasmāccin nimittād iti? āhosvit kvacid ānarthakyāt sarvatrānarthakyam iti, dhātvādiṣu bhrāntir iyam alātacakravad iti? ubhayam ity āha / etat pratipādayati --- arthasyopalabdhyanupalabdhī bhinnaviṣaye draṣṭavye / yasya vyāvahārikī arthavattā śakyānumānāt, tatra kalpayitavyā / dṛṣṭaś ca samāne 'rthe sarvasmin kasyacit tadarthayogaḥ, kasyacin neti idam āha --- samānam īhamānānām iti / saṃghātāntarāṇy evaitāni / tattvakathanam etat / yatra hy ānumāniky arthavattā tatrānvayinaḥ śabdasya saṃbhave bhūyiṣṭho 'rthāntaro yo (? 'rthānugamo) dṛṣṭaḥ / kaścid eva tu viśeṣaḥ, pratyayavyabhicāreke (? pratyayavyabhicārakṛtaḥ); paktiḥ pacanaṃ pāka iti liṅgaviśeṣamātraṃ pratyayakṛtam / evam iha ūpaśabdānugame na kaścid arthānugamo dṛśyate / tasmān naitat pratipattavyam --- ūpaśabde vyavasthite varṇavyatyaya [varṇavyatyayaḥ] / idaṃ khalv api bhavatā iti / idaṃ pratipādyate --- samudāyāntaratve nābhyupagamyamāne ānarthakyam eva sutarām āpadyate, bahūnāṃ śabdānām ānarthakyābhyupagamāt / eṣa hi nyāyo yadūpaśabdo vyavasthitas tam arthaṃ brūyāt / na ced bravīti, ānarthakyam abhyupagataṃ bhavatīti / saṃghātārthavatvāt [saṃghātārthavattvāt] iti / asya parihāraṃ vakṣyati --- 'dṛṣṭā hy atarthenāpi [atadarthenāpi] guṇena guṇino 'rthavattā suraṅgavad rathāṅgavat' iti /

[V]
pratyāhānubandhānāṃ [pratyāhāre 'nubandhanāṃ] katham ajgrahaṇeṣu na (ślokavt.) / ete 'pi hy atra saṃniviṣṭāḥ paṭhitāḥ, saṃnihitāś ca / kāryārthaś cāsakṛd upadiṣṭaḥ hakāraḥ / evam amī api kāryārthaṃ bahukṛtva upadiśyeran iti / 
	
ācārāt (ślokavt.) / upacāranimittā pravṛttiḥ / evam ācāryāḥ pravṛttāḥ yad eṣv ackāryāṇi na kṛtavantaḥ / 'tṛṣimṛṣikṛṣeḥ . . . ' (P 1.2.25) iti / tatra yathaiva nipātanaṃ bādhakaṃ sāmānyaprāptasya ṇatvasya '. . . sarvanāmāni' (P 1.1.27) iti, evaṃ saty api grahaṇe bādhakaṃ nipātanaṃ yaṇādeśasya halṣv iti / iyam ekā kalpanā / atha vā, ācārād im [idam] avasīyate, aprajakānām [aprayojakānām] eṣām ihasthānām upadeśa iti / akārādaya evātra prajakāḥ [prayojakāḥ] 'ādir antyena sahetā' (P 1.1.71) iti, na ete / (p. 36)

[2.2] apradhānatvāt (ślokavt.) iti / anena śeṣaśeṣibhāva ākhyāyate / śeṣaḥ śeṣiṇaṃ prati viśeṣaṇatvenopātto na śakyas tulyavat prasaṃkhyātuṃ kāryayogitvaṃ pratīti viśeṣaṇabhāvenopāttānām anubandhānāṃ nāsti kāryayogitvam / tatra yathaiva prakṛtyādīnāṃ pratyayasaṃjñā na bhavatīti hetudvayam uktam --- ekenāprayojakatvaṃ khyāpyate 'nimittasya nimittakāryārthatvāt . . . ' (P 3.1.1, vt. 2) iti, dvitīyena śeṣaśeṣibhāvāpattiḥ 'pradhāne kāryasaṃpratyayāt . . . ' (P 3.1.1, vt. 3) iti, evam ihāpy anubandhānām aprayojakatvāt śeṣatvāc ca grahaṇam [agrahaṇam] iti / kuta etat / ayam abhiprāyaḥ --- yathaiva gargādibidādipāṭhaḥ ubhayakārī evam ayam api pāṭho 'nubandhānāṃ svārthaḥ parārthaś ca syād iti / etat nivartayati ācāryācārād eva / yathaiva lokaḥ pravartate --- tulyajātīyeṣu brāhmaṇeṣu brāhmaṇair eva saha bhokṣyate, kṣatriyeṣu kṣatriyaiḥ, evam ihāpi eṣaiva śailī lakṣyate / śīle bhavā śailī, samādhānapūrvikā pravṛttiḥ /

[2.3] lopaś ca balavattaraḥ (ślokavt.) / yady upapattir na gṛhyate, itaḥ pratipadyasvāgrahaṇam / aj iti pratyāhāre anubandha eva gṛhyate 'ādir antyena . . . (P 1.1.71) . . . asata iditi saṃjñā kṛtā, asan svayaṃ kāryeṣu vyāpriyate iti jñātam / evaṃ hy uktam --- 'bhāvo hi kāryārtho 'nanyārtho lopaḥ' (P 1.3.9, vt.) iti / tad asataḥ katham iva grahaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati? iti /

[2.4] ūkālo 'jiti vā yogaḥ (ślokavt.) / 'ādir antyena' iti prasiddhe saty actve yogavibhāgaḥ kriyate, kālena paricchinnasyāctvapratipattyartham / yathā pūrvaparaprayoge sati gatyupasargasaṃjñe prasakte niyamyete / saṃjñāniyamanapakṣe 'te prāg dhātoḥ' (P 1.4.80) iti prāk prayujyamānā [prayujyamānānāṃ] gatyupasargasaṃjñā prāpnoti / tatrokālatvam ardhamātrikāṇāṃ nāstīti bhāvaḥ / evam api kukkuṭaḥ atrāpi prāpnoti / kathaṃ punar asya paṭhitasya [apaṭhitasya] pratyāhāre 'ntarbhāvaḥ? ākṛtigrahaṇād ity abhiprāyaḥ / ākṛtir upadiṣṭā, kadācid ardhamātrādhīnā kadācin mātrikādhīnā / kadācid ekena halābhivyajyate, kadācit dvābhyām / asmiṃś ca pratyākhyātaṃ grahaṇakaśāstram 'aṇudit savarṇasya ca' (P 1.1.69) iti /

[2.4.1] ayam āha --- hrasvādīnāṃ vacanāt prāg yāvat tāvad eva yogo 'stu (ślokavt.) / 'hrasvadīrghaplutaḥ' (P 1.2.27) ity asmāt prāg (p. 37) yāvat śabdarūpaṃ tāvad yogo 'stu / kiyat? yatad [yad etad] 'ūkālo 'c . . . ' (P 1.2.27) iti / etad atra varṇyate --- 'vyaktinirdeśo 'yam / idaṃ cāsti 'aṇudi [aṇudit]' ti [iti] / pratyāhāre ca ūkālatā āśrīyate, ūkālo yaḥ so 'j iti pratyāhāre saṃniviṣṭa iti / nidarśanamātraṃ cedam aj iti / satya [saty actve] pratyāhāreṣv evāj iti vā aṇ iti vā saṃniviṣṭā eta iti pratijñātavyam / tasmāṇādīnām [tasmāt ṇādīnām] asaty aj iti (? aṇ iti) vyapadeśe grāhakatvaṃ nāstīti / satyaprathaṃ kuryād iṣṭakāryaṃ bhaviṣyati (?) /

[VI]
atha kimartham antasthānām iti / idam atra pṛcchyamānam, kimartham 'aṇudit savarṇasya . . . ' (P 1.1.69) iti ṇakāreṇa pratyāhāraḥ kriyate yatrāntasthā apy antarbhāvyante, na punaś cakāreṇa pratyāhāraḥ kriyata iti / itarathā hi yady atra na paṭhyeran, iṇgrahaṇāny anantasthākāni syuḥ / tataś ca ṣatvaṃ na syāt, gīrṣu, halṣu iti / 

nanu ca [bhedo] bhavati / śrutikṛtaṃ bhedam adhyavasāya bravīti / tad etad atyantaṃ saṃdigdhaṃ [vartate] āryāṇām [ācāryāṇām] iti / jñāpakād dhy anumimate vibhāṣānuvṛttim / teṣām anaikāntikatvāt anumāsya [anumānasya] saṃdigdham eva bhavatīti / 

laṇ //6//

[1] atrāyaṃ ṇakāro dvir anubadhyate / atra prakaraṇe ṣaṭ prakārā upakṣiptāḥ --- āsattiḥ, vyāptiḥ, sāmarthyaṃ, prasiddhiḥ, liṅgaṃ, ;āghavam iti / tter [āsatter] nyāyāt kramavyatikrame ca prayojanābhāvāt pūrveṇa syāt / bhūyasāṃ grahaṇam iti vyāpter nyāyāt pareṇa bhavitavyam / tatra lakṣyavaśāt kim āsattir āhosvit vyāptir iti saṃdihyate / tatra dvayor grahaṇayoḥ [aṇgrahaṇayoḥ] sāmarthyenāsattir avasthitā, vyāptir bādhitā / 

aṇo 'pragṛhyasya iti / bhāṣyakāreṇaiva paṭhitam aṣṭame vṛkṣav karotīti / tatra na jñāyate, kenābhiprāyeṇāha --- na padāntāḥ pare 'ṇaḥ saṃbhavanti / 

gṛdheryaṅlugantasya laṅi sipi rūpam ajardhāḥ iti, evaṃ spardher api apāspā iti / 
	
dhātugrahaṇaṃ karoti / śakyate 'tra vaktum, dhātugrahaṇāt liṅgāt mātrā kevalam upajāyate, na sthānyādeśabhāva iti / evaṃ tu cikīrṣatītyādidoṣo virudhyate / ihāpi tarhi na prāpnoti, cikīrṣati jihīrṣatīti / atra codyate --- dhātugrahaṇāt raparatve (p. 38) nivartie nanu siddham ittvaṃ cikīrṣatīti / ayam abhiprāyaḥ --- dhātugrahaṇād adhātor eva raparatvaṃ [nivartitam], dhātos tu raparatvaṃ syād eva / tataś cānantyatvād aprāptir iti / atha vā, dhātugrahaṇam evamarthaṃ syāt --- sarvatra raparatve kṛte anantyatvāt ittve 'prāpte dhātor yatratatrasthasya ṛta ittvaṃ bhavatīti / mā bhūd evam  upadhāyā eva bhaviṣyati / antareṇa dhātugrahaṇam ihāpi [tarhi] prāpnoti, mātṝṇām iti, raparatve kṛte upadhābhāvāt / tasmād avaśyaṃ kartavyaṃ dhātugrahaṇam / yaś copadiṣṭa uraṇraparasūtre 'udāttādiṣu raparatvam' (P 1.1.51, vt. 5) iti, sa tu doṣo 'sty eva pareṇa grahaṇe sati kṛtir ityevamādiṣu / evaṃ sāmarthyāt vyāptau bādhitāyām idaṃ prasiddham --- pūrveṇa grahaṇam iti / ataḥ prasiddheḥ pṛcchati --- asmiṃs tarhi [aṇgrahaṇe] saṃdehaḥ kim atra prasiddhir atha vyāptir iti / prasiddhir liṅgena bādhyate / savarṇe 'ṇgrahaṇam iti / ataḥ hy urṛt / acīkṛtat --- ṛkārasya sthāne mātrikaḥ kathaṃ syād iti taparakaraṇaṃ yuktam / nanu ca bhāvyamānena grahaṇaṃ neti / satyam, evam etat / asti tu grahaṇaprasaṅgo yo yatnena nivartyate / tasmāl liṅgeneha prasiddhir bādhyate /

iṇgrahaṇeṣu tarhi saṃdehaḥ / . . . [tac ca guru bhavati /] atra lāghavāt vyāptir bhavati iti na cāsattiḥ / nanu cāckālaṃ vyañjanakālo 'nividhatte / tatra ya evāckālaḥ sa eva pūrvabhavānāṃ vyañjanānām / ekāyadhātam (? ekīyamatam) etat / anyeṣāṃ bhidyate kālaḥ / atha vā, kleśenoccāraṇaṃ gauravam, sukhenoccāraṇaṃ lāghavam iti / bhavaty eṣā paribhāṣā / yady api śrotā na pratipadyeta tam artha (? tadarthaṃ) lāghavenāpi [lāghavenāthāpi] na tasya viśiṣṭārthābhidhāne 'śaktir upalabhyate / tasmād ācāryo vyavaharaty anena, vyākhyānāt viśeṣaḥ pratipadyata iti /

[1] kimartham imau / sthānadvavyavyāpārād gauravaṃ karaṇanāśaś cety ataḥ pṛcchati / evam api pañcāgamāḥ / ṅaṇanajhabhāḥ / traya āgaminaḥ / ṅaṇanāḥ / tena ekaikasya pañca pañca syuḥ / santu tāvad yeṣām āgamānām āgaminaḥ iti / iha uddeśānuddeśau śāstrapratibaddhau / tatra prayoganirapekṣād eva śāstrasaṃkhyāgamāḥ [śāstrāl labdhayathāsaṃkhyāgamāḥ] (? śāstrāl labdhayathāsaṃkhyā āgamāḥ) pratipannāḥ, yathāsvaṃ pravibhaktāḥ prayogakāle pravartante / tatra jhabhāvāgaminau na staḥ iti na pravartete / codanāyāś cavāt [codanāyāś cābhāvāt] punar ṅaṇaneṣu na pravartete /  
(p. 39) 
[etadvyākhyānenaiva vyākhyātaprāyatvāt tyaktāni nava sūtrāṇi veditavyāni /]

[2] atha kim idam akṣaram iti / keṣāṃcid varṇo 'kṣaram, kecit [keṣāṃcit] padaṃ vākyaṃ ca / idaṃ coktam --- 'yo vā imāṃ svaraśo 'kṣaraśaḥ' iti /

akṣaraṃ na kṣaraṃ vidyāt (ślokavt.) / kriyāśabdo yathā pācaka iti / na kṣarati, na kṣīyata ity ato 'kṣaram / 'na kṣarati' ity etad aprasiddham / asminn arthe prasiddhārthaṃ vyācaṣṭe 'na kṣīyate' iti / yathā 'kṣaṇaḥ kṣaṇoteḥ / prakṣṇutaḥ kālo bhavati' / kṣaṇautiḥ kṣayatikarmeti /

aśnoter vā saro 'kṣaram (ślokavt.) / aśnite 'rthaṃ yasmāt, arthaṃ vyāpnoti tena vā, arthena vyāptaṃ yasmāt, tasmād akṣaram / tac ca padaṃ vākyam / 

varṇaṃ vāhuḥ pūrvasūtre (ślokavt.) saṃprati nāmadheyam idam iti kathyate / evaṃ hy anyena paṭhyate, 'varṇā akṣarāṇi' iti / 

kimartham upadiśyate (ślokavt.) / na hi varṇānāṃ sādhutvaṃ pratipādya [pratipādyam] / kasya tarhi? samudāyasya, tasyārthe prayuktatvāt / 
	
varṇajñānaṃ vāgviṣayaḥ (ślokavt.) / varṇā yena jñāyante tat varṇajñānam / kiṃ punas tat? śāstram / tat vāco viṣayaḥ / śāstre hi vāk viṣīyate avabadhyate / 

yatra ca brahma vartate (ślokavt.) / yasmiṃś ca brahma vedo vartate / kva ca vartate? pade / tasya varṇasya śāstraṃ viṣayaḥ / etat kathayati --- laukikānāṃ ca śabdānām iti / 

tadartham (ślokavt.) / tad ity anena varṇajñānam abhisaṃbadhyate / śāstrapravṛttyarthaṃ varṇā upadiśyante / yad evoktaṃ vākyakāreṇa 'vṛttisamavāyārtha upadeśaḥ' (Paspaśā, vt. 15) iti, tad eva ślokavārttikakāro 'py āha /

iṣṭabuddhyartham (ślokavt.) / nivṛttakalādikān iti / 

laghvarthaṃ copadiśyate (ślokavt.) iti / anena cānubandhāsaṅgaṃ pradarśayati 'anubandhakaraṇārthaś ca' (Paspaśā, vt. 16) iti /

so 'yam akṣarasamāmnāyo vāksamāmnāyaḥ / etāvatīyaṃ vāk samāmnātā, etāvān vāgvyavahāraḥ / puṣpitaḥ phalitaś ca / dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalābhyām abhyudayaniḥśreyasābhyām / (p. 40) candratārakavat pratimaṇḍitaḥ / etad uktaṃ bhavati --- yathaivedam avyucchinnaṃ candratārakādi, evam asya vāgvyavahārasya na kaścit kartā asti; evam evedaṃ pāraṃparyeṇa smaryamāṇaṃ smaryamāṇam / brahmarāśiḥ iti / yathaiva saṃhṛtakramo brahmarāśir iti śaḥ (?) pratipuruṣaṃ vyavasthitaḥ, evam ayaṃ pratyāraḥ [pratyāhāraḥ] śakyaṃ vaktum / vijñānabrahmavad upasaṃbhṛto brahmaṇā / sarvavedapuṇyaphalāvāptiś cāsya jñāne bhavati / sarvavedādhyayane kṛte tasya puṇyasya yat phalaṃ tad avāpnoti / mātāpitarau cāsya iti / mṛtayor api adhyayanaṃ na tyaktavyam, tayor iṣṭaphalāvāptyartham / jīvator api vā; yadā mariṣyatas tadā svargaṃ gamiṣyata iti adhyayanaṃ prati aparaḥ protsāhyata iti /

iti śrīmahopādhyāyabhartṛhariviracitāyāṃ śrīmahābhāṣyadīpikāyāṃ prathamasyādhyāyasya dvitīyam āhnikam // pratyāhārāḥ samāptāḥ //

Mahābhāṣyadīpikāyāṃ tṛtīyam āhnikam

[1] etadvyākhyānenaiva vyākhyātaprāyatvāt tyaktāni nava sūtrāṇi veditavyāni / 

[2] kvacit . . . svarūpaṃ tu sarvatra saṃnihitam iti nāśrīyate / tathā hi / idam api saṃjñayā vidhānaṃ‘diva aut’(Pā. 7.1.84) iti / ayam aukāraḥ svarūpeṇa vidhīyate iti / 

[3] prakṛtaṃ guṇavṛddhigrahaṇam anuvartate / ika ity etāvatā siddham, ikaḥ prakṛte guṇavṛddhī bhavata iti / evaṃ siddhe sūtre guṇavṛddhigrahaṇam āśritaśabdavyāpārārtham / ayaṃ tu dvābhyāṃ guṇavṛddhigrahaṇābhyām etam arthaṃ pratipādayati / ekenāpi sidhyati / prakṛtaṃ guṇavṛddhigrahaṇam anuvartate / sarvatraiva vyākaraṇe grahaṇakavākyam uccarati / tat tu na kāryasya pratipādakam / tannirvartakaṃ tu vākyāntaraṃ lakṣyasamavāyy upaplavate, yathā‘kartari’(Pā. 3.4.67),‘paśau’(Pā. 3.2.25), kartror‘hastisūcakayoḥ’(Pā. 3.2.13 Vā.) iti / evam anumīyamānāt guṇavṛddhigrahaṇāt śrūyamāṇāc ca vākyāntaram eva pratipādyāmahe―iko guṇavṛddhī guṇavṛddhī iti / guṇavṛddhisahacarite ye guṇavṛddhī te [i]kaḥ sthāne bhavata iti / 

[4] yadi tad anuvartate ... adeṅāṃ vṛddhisaṃjñā[pi] prāpnoti, pratiyogam adhikārasyopasthānāt / nanu ca vṛddhiśabdaḥ svaritaḥ, ihādaicpadārthaka upatiṣṭhate ca, ata ādaicām eva guṇasaṃjñā prasaktā syāt / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / pradeśānāṃ saṃjñā pravartamānā saṃjñāntareṇa kṛtasaṃbandhāt śabdaparaiva, ity anumīyamāno 'pi [vṛddhiśabdaḥ] tathābhūto 'numīyate ity idam uktam adeṅām iti (? -m api) vṛddhisaṃjñā syād iti / 

[5] saṃbandham anuvartiṣya[te] / keyam saṃbandānuvṛttiḥ? ucyate / yathaiva anudeśāḥ pratilabdhayathāsaṃkhyā[ḥ] prayogakāle abhāvān nivartante, yathā‘ig yaṇaḥ’(Pā. 1.1.45) iti, evaṃ svaritāsaṅgād anuvṛttir anumitā, uttarakālam ādaico 'py atra saṃnihitā iti pratipadyamāna evam arthaiva ādaijanuvṛttir iti saṃbandhābhāvaṃ pratipadyate, liṅgāsaṅgāc cottaratra saṃbandham iti / 

[6] atha vā maṇḍūkagatayo 'dhikarārā iti / saṃprati muktvādaico vṛddhiśabda eva kevalo 'nuvartate / prapārthino maṇḍūkā akṣīṇavṛttayas tathābhūtāṃ kriyāṃ ārabhante, yathāntarāle pratibaddhagurutvāḥ, atha caikam iṣṭadeśam saṃnipatanti, evaṃ śabde 'py arthakṛtā; yasminn arthatvaṃ samīhate tatraivānumimīte ity etan manḍūkagatyā sāmyam /  

[7] atha vaikayogaḥ kariṣyata iti / pṛthakkriyāyogād ekayogatvam / bhavatir eka uccāraṇe adhyāhriyate /‘vṛddhir ādaij adeṅ guṇa’iti / yady api pratipattikāle bavatir bhidyate, eka eva pratipattikāla iti nāsti sa kālo yasmin vṛddhisaṃjñāśūnyā ādaicaḥ syuḥ, adeṅo vā guṇasaṃjñāśūnyā iti  / 

[8] atha vānyavacanāc cakārākaraṇād i[ti] / kim atrotsargāpavādābhyāṃ sābhyāṃ ko vā viśeṣaḥ? viśeśas tāvad―yathā sāmānyaviśeṣabhāva utsargāpavādayoḥ‘karmaṇi . . .’(Pā. 3.2.1),‘āto anupasarge kaḥ’(Pā. 3.2.3) iti /‘dhātor’(Pā. 3.1.91) iti codanāyām asty antarbhāva ākārāntasyeti sāmānyam ākārānteṣu / dhātvantareṣu [tv a]syāntarbhāva ākārād viśeṣaḥ / evam iha [na]‘vṛddhir ādaij’iti atra adeṅām antarbhāvaḥ, nāpi‘adeṅ guṇaḥ’ity ādaicāṃ bhavati / atha kiṃ sāmānyaṃ? saṃdigdhāsaṃdigdhatve / yathaivotsargacodanāyāṃ [saṃdihyamānāyām a]saṃdihyamāno viśeṣaḥ, evaṃ vṛddhir ādaij ity anuvṛttiḥ / saṃdihyamānā kim uttarārthā nāntarīyakatvād ihāpy arthitā, utaitadarthāpīti / adeṅ guṇa iti tu [a]kṛtārtham asaṃdegdhavyaṃ cety etat sāmānyam /
