atra codyate―yathaiva pratyayasaṃjñā saṃdihyamānānyāsām apy anuvṛttau saṃbhavantyāṃ sannādiṣu kṛtārthā asaṃdihyamānā[bhiḥ] kṛtkṛtyataddhitatadrājasaṃjñābhir akṛtārthābhiḥ sammucīyate, evam iha kasmāt sammucayo na bhavati? iti / atrocyate / pratyayasaṃjñāyāḥ saṃjñipratipattyarthatve pratipattir na tu pradeśe kenacid vyāpāreṇopakariṣyata iti / bhinnakālatvād avirodhāc ca viśeṣasaṃjābhiḥ sammucīyate / iha tu‘vṛddhir ādaij’iti nirdeśād idaṃ samadhigamyate so 'yam abhisaṃbandhenāntarīkṛtya saṃjñinaḥ saṃjñāvyapadeśo 'yam iti / svaritaliṅgāsaṅgāc cedaṃ vyaktaṃ,‘'iko guṇavṛddhī’ity atropasthitavṛddhiśabdena sāhacaryāt pradeśe kaścid upakāraḥ kariṣyata iti / tatra‘adeṅ guṇaḥ’ity atropasthitaṃ na kaṃ[cid] vyāpāram anuvidhīyate / kiṃ saṃjñāvyāpāram uta saṃjñivyāpāram iti saṃjñātvam evāsya saṃdihyamānam asaṃdigdho liṅgāsaṅgaḥ /‘adeṅ guṇaḥ’iti guṇaśabdasya saṃjñātvam asaṃdihyamānam / tatra saṃdihyamānasaṃjñāvyāpāreṇāsaṃdihyamānasaṃjñāvyāpārasya samakakṣatā na yukteti suṣṭhūcyate prakṛtsyāpavāda iti / 

[9] atha vā vakṣyaty etad iti / anenaitat pratipādayati―nyāyaprāptā maṇḍūkagatir vyākaraṇenāpi pratipāditeti / tatrānuvṛttasyāpi nirarthitve [asaṃbandhaṃ] vibhāṣāśrutir upasūcayati ity anena [yatrārthitvaṃ tatraiva] saṃnipatitavyam / antarāle tu grāmāntaraṃ gacchata iva mūlopasarpaṇam iti / 

[10]	atha vobhayaṃ nivṛttaṃ tad apekṣiṣyāmahe / nanu cātrānena bhavitavyam, anaṃ[ta]rasya vidhir vā bhavati pratiṣedho veti / yatra hy ubhau svaryete tatrobhāv anumīyete, yatrānyataras tatrānyatamasyānumānam iti / satyam, evam etat / apekṣye tu dvidhā matir bhavati apekṣituḥ, bhedenābhedena ca / abhedena tāvat―devadattaś ca yajñadattaś ca viṣṇumitraś cety abhedenaitad iti / yadā bhedenāpekṣate tadānantareṇa bhavitavyam / yadā tu samudāyam apekṣate tadā samudāya evānantara iti lakṣyavaśād ayaṃ kadācit kiṃcit apekṣata iti / 

[11a] kiṃ punar ayam alontyaśeṣa āhosvid alontyāpavādaḥ? śeṣo 'vayava ekadeśa iti paryāyāḥ / apavādo nivartakaḥ / svayaṃ cobhayam upapādayati, ekavākyatvā[t ta]ccheṣatvaṃ nānāvākyatvāt tadapavādatvam / (1) tat tv ekavākyatvaṃ nānāvākyatvaṃ ca katham iti naivaitad uktam / kathaṃ caitayor ekavākyatvaṃ syāt, kālabhedena śrutayor anekayogavyavahitayoḥ? (2) sati caikavākyabhāvanimitte nānāvākyasyābhāvāt sati ca nānāvākyabhāvanimitte ekavākyasyābhāvāt saṃdehānupapattiḥ / na caikasminn eva dvayor nānāvākyaikavākyabhāvayoḥ samavasthānam asti / (3) kim arthāv etāv evopanyastau bādhāṅgāṅgibhāvau, na punar vikalpasamuccayāv api? (4) tathā nānāvākyatvam iha bādhānimittam uktam, anyatra tu samāveśanimittam,“tatra khyunnādipratiṣedho nānāvākyatvāt”(Pā. 3.4.67 Vā. 2) iti / etat sarvaṃ vaktavyam / 

[11ā] (1) yat tāvad ucyate, vyavahitayoḥ kathaṃ saṃbandha iti / nānantaryaṃ saṃbandhahetuḥ / evaṃ hy ucyate―“arthato hy asama[rthā]nām ānantaryam akāraṇam”/ yathā,“anaḍvāham udahāri yā tvaṃ vahasi śirasā bhāgini kumbhaṃ abhidhāvantam adrākṣīḥ”iti / eṣām ānantaryeṇāpi samniviṣṭānām upakāro nāsti tasyāḥ kriyāyāḥ / kutaḥ? asaṃbhavāt / nāsti anaḍuhaḥ śirasā vahanaṃ kumbhasya ca saraṇam iti / etannibandhanam upalabdhur anyayānupūrvyā buddhiviṣayaṃ vākyāntaram upaplavate / yathaivopāṃśu guṇayaṃs tannibandhanam arthaṃ pratipadyate / kecin manyante―etadupāyam aśabdakam evārthaṃ pratipadyate iti / evaṃ vyākaraṇe 'py arthalakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandho nā[nanta]ryakṛto, yathā‘baḍuṣu baḍuvacanam’(Pā. 1.4.21),‘ṅyāpprātipādikāḍ’(Pā. 4.1.1) iti / etat tu tiṅarthaṃ bhinnadeśam upadiṣṭam / evam ihāpi‘rājñaḥ ka ca’(Pā. 4.2.140) ityevamādyarthaḥ pṛthagupadeśaḥ syāt / 

(2) atha cārthalakṣaṇaḥ kasmiṃścid viṣaye saṃbandhaḥ syād iti, yasmiṃś ca viṣaye svanimittaprayukayor etayoḥ saṃnipātas tasminn ubhayam api saṃbhavati, aṅgāṅgibhāvo bādhā cety ataḥ saṃdehaḥ / yatra tv eka eva saṃbhavati tatraikasyaiva pravṛttir yathā kartṛpaśucodanāyām‘kartari . . .’(Pā. 3.4.67) iti sādhanam āśrayam apekṣate śaktimattvāt / dravyanimittānuvidhāyitvāt paratantraṃ sa[d] dvitīyāpekṣam eva,‘paśau’(Pā. 3.2.25) iti dravyabhūtasya śabdenopādānāt / dhātoś ña pratyayaḥ kriyāvacanād utpadyamānaḥ sādhane bhavitum arhatīti na śakyate kalpayituṃ, paśau śuddhe pratyayaḥ karmādiguṇayukte veti / kutaḥ? karmādīnām acoditatvāt / tasmād etayor atyantam upakāritvenāpekṣāyām etadviṣayam ekavākyatvam anumīyate, paśau kartari bhavatīti / evam aṇiñor ekasminn arthe saṃnipatitayor ekaikasminn etasyārthasyābhidhānāt viśeṣavihitaḥ sāmānyaviṣayaṃ bādhata iti bādhakatvenaivāpekṣā / iha tv aṅgasya guṇavṛddhī bhavata iti ṣaṣṭhī guṇavṛddhī ca / ṣaṣṭhīm apekṣya‘alo 'ntyasya’saṃnipatati / guṇavṛddhī apekṣya‘ikaḥ’iti saṃnipatati / evam etadekaviṣayasaṃnipāte (?), yady aṅgasyetīyaṃ śrutir anye 'li pravartate, avayavasamudāyān na hi kiṃcit [a]nyad asya rūpam asti / idam eva hi tasya rūpam aṅgasya / yathā‘ruha[ḥ] paḥ’(Pā. 4.3.43)‘rājñaḥ ka ca’(Pā. 4.2.140) iti / etad uktaṃ bhavati―ruho ikārasya, rājño nakārasyeti / tatrāṅgasyeti yadā samudāyaśabdo 'ntye 'ly upasaṃhriyate, idam avatavasya viśeṣaṇam‘ikaḥ’iti / evam ayaṃ taccheṣo bhavati viśeṣaṇatvenāntyasyālaḥ / aṅgasyeti samudāyaśabdo 'ntye ali upasaṃhriyamāṇa ikā prāptatadantavidhir upasaṃhāreṇa yujyate, igantasyāṅgasyālo 'ntyasyeti / evam api igantasyāṅgasya guṇa iti viśeṣyatvenālontyaśeṣatām āpadyate / atha tv aṅgasyetīyaṃ guṇavṛddhividhau naiva sthānaṣaṣṭhī, kiṃ tarhi,‘ikaḥ’ity eṣā sthānaṣaṣṭhī, etadviśeṣaṇam‘aṅgasya’iti, yathā‘ūd upadhāyā gohaḥ’(Pā. 6.4.89) iti, evaṃ sati‘alontyasya’ity etasya guṇavṛddhiviṣaye atyantam apravṛttir iti bādhakatvam asyohyate / iyam eva ca bādhā yā aprāptir iti / tatra taccheṣakalpanāyām ikaḥ pradhānaviprakarṣaḥ, aṅgasyaiva guṇo vidhinoktam iti(? -kta iti) śabdapratipattir arthapratipattiś ca bādhyate / apavādapakṣe tv iko guṇo 'ṅgasya tu pradhānavipra[ka]rṣa iti / 

(3) samuccayaḥ kasmān nopanyasta iti / asaṃbhavāt / ihābhinnārthānām ekakriyāviṣayaḥ samuccayo dṛśyate, yathā‘devadattaṃ sūpena lavaṇena dhṛtana bhojaya’iti / sūpādayo 'bhinnārthā upasecanena rasotpādanena snehanena bhujau samuccīyante / evam ihāṅgam ik ca guṇe samuccetum aśakye / aṅgaṃ hi prāptuvantaṃ yā yadā ca pratipadyate guṇam, [tadā] tad itarayā viśeṣaṇaṣaṣṭhyā bhavitavyam / ity evam api samuccayo na kāryarūpaḥ / vikalpaḥ ekakāryānāṃ viro[dha]vatām anyatamena kṛtatvāt pravartate, yathā‘devadattaṃ bhojaya prāsāde gṛhe kāṃsyapātryāṃ rājatyām’iti gṛhe prasāde ca bhojyaḥ / evam ihāṅgasya vā bhavati, iko veti na yugapad vartate / ekasyāḥ ṣaṣṭhyāḥ kāryayogitvād iti / 

(4a) atha nānāvākyatve ko virodhe parihāraḥ? ucyate / iha‘dadhi brāhmaṇebhyo dīyatāṃ takraṃ kauṇḍinyāya’iti kecid ekam eva vākyam iti pratipannā ekasyākhyātasya śrutatvāt / eko hi dadātir bhinnakarmako bhinnasaṃpradānakaś ceti / yathaiva bhujir a[ne]kakaraṇa iti saty api bhujer bahukṛtvaḥ prayoge ekavākyataiva bhavatīti,‘devadattaṃ māsena bhojaya dadhnā bhojaya śākena bhojaya’iti / iha bhavantas tv āhuḥ dve ete vākye iti / ekaṃ vākyaṃ‘dadhi brāhmaṇebhyo dīyatām’, dvitīyaṃ‘takraṃ kauṇḍinyāya’iti / kriyāpadaṃ tu gatārthatvān na prayujyate / tatraiko dadātir dadhikarmako 'paras takrakarmaka iti dve eva dadātikriye / kimarthaṃ tu loke samudāyo na bhavati? etadaśakyaṃ vaktum / / kutaḥ? adṛṣṭatvāt / yair api śāstrāṇi na samadhigatāni, tair apy uktaṃ‘dadhi asmai na deyam’iti / athāvaśyam upapattir vaktavyā, iyam upapattiḥ / yeṣāṃ tāvat dve ete vākye teṣāṃ kriyāṃ kriyāntaraṃ bādhate / dadhikarmikā takrakarmikayā bādhyate / brāhmaṇaśrutau kauṇḍinyo 'pi yady apy antarbhūtaḥ, tathāpi kauṇḍinyarūpeṇa nocyate / kiṃ tarhi? sāmānyarūpeṇa / phalam api dātur brāhmaṇāya dattam iti bhavati / yat tu kauṇḍunyāyeti takradānaṃ [tat] tu viśeṣatayā śrūyate / dadhidānaṃ ca vināpi kauṇḍinyena saṃbhavati, na tu takradānam / tasmāt yatra ca tena rūpeṇa coditaḥ saṃdigdho, yatra ca viśeṣarūpeṇa codito 'saṃdigdhaḥ, te kriye tatra samuccayaṃ [na] pratipadyate / kathaṃ hy anumānaṃ pratyakṣatakradānena sa[maka]kṣīkartuṃ śakyam? tasmāt pratyakṣānumānayor na samuccayo nāpi vikalpaḥ / 

apareṣāṃ darśanam / vākyaśeṣasyātrānumānam / tasya cedaṃ liṅgaṃ takraṃ kauṇḍinyāyeti / etad atrādāv eva prakramyate kauṇḍinyaṃ tyaktaṃ‘dadhi brāhmaṇebhyo dīyatām’iti / 

apare manyante / bhūyāṃso hi brāhmaṇaśabdaḥ / tatra kecid uddhṛtakauṇḍinyāḥ, kecid uddhṛtamāṭharāḥ, kecid antarbhūtamāṭharāḥ, kecid antarbhūtakauṇḍiyāḥ / tatrāyaṃ brāhmaṇaśabdo 'ntareṇa vākyaśeṣānumānam utkālitakauṇḍinyeṣv anyeṣu pravṛttaś ce[ty eta]d ayaṃ pratipattā takraṃ kauṇḍinyāyety asmāt pratipadyate / kuta etad iti cet, ayam anyān pratipādayann evaṃ pra[ti]pādayati, ato 'sya bhavati, yathaivāham anyān pratipādayā[my e]vam aham anena pra[ti]pādya iti / asmin darśane brāhmaṇarūpeṇāpratipādito dadhidāne kauṇḍinyaḥ / yeṣāṃ tat tv ekaṃ vākyaṃ teṣāṃ sādhanāntarasya nivartakaṃ tarkaṃ dadhi nivartayati / kutaḥ? dadhi saṃdihyamānaṃ, takram sākṣānnirdiṣṭaṃ‘takram’iti / 

(4ā) khyunnādiṣu ca samāveśaṃ prakaraṇabhedāt pratipannaḥ / yathā prakaraṇabhedāt apatye bādhaka na te 'dhikāravihi[tā]nām apavādāḥ, evam eva ca kto vartamānakālavihi[ta]sya laṭo, [na] bhūtakālavihitalakṣaṇasya / tathādhikaraṇikāpatyalakṣaṇasya pācchaveyāmati (?) / evaṃ bhinnaprakaṇatvāt kartari kṛd iti pratipāditaḥ prakaraṇāntare khyun karaṇe bhavatīti kṛttvāt kartari syāt, [khyun]rūpāt karaṇa iti samāveśanaṃ pratipannaḥ / tatra parihāra ucyate / yathaivaikasminn arthe saṃnipatitayor aṇiñor viśeṣavatā sāmānya[va]to bādhanam, evam ihāpi khyunn ity ekasmin śabde dvāv arthau saṃnipatitau karaṇaṃ kartā ca / tatra khyunrūpam āśritya karaṇārthaṃ pratipadyate, kṛttvam āśritya kartrartham iti viśiṣṭaśrutir arthaḥ sāmānyaśrutim arthaṃ bādhate / evam laḍ ity etasmin śabde viśiṣṭopapadāśritaḥ kālaḥ kālāntaraṃ bādhate / evaṃ kte 'pi viśiṣṭaṃ dhāturūpaṃ āśritam i[ti] niṣṭheti yā bhū[ta]tā tāṃ vartamānakālo bādhate / evaṃ yaṅi bāvagarhā yā adhikārasamabhiḥāratā[m a]sya / tathā ḍhaki vṛṣarūpaviśeṣavatī vaḍavāśrutir apatyārthaṃ bādhate / kiṃ cāprayujyamānāḥ‘ca’śabdās tam e[va] viṣayam apaharantīti / rūḍher api vāparam abhyupagamyate / tathā 'saty api animiṣatve animiṣaśabdo matsyavṛttir astīti / ye tv eka[vākya]vādinas ta evaṃ pratipannāḥ―yathaivedam ekaṃ vākyaṃ‘dadhi brāhmaṇebhyas takraṃ kauṇḍinyāya dīyatām’iti, evam idam apy ekaṃ vākyaṃ‘kartari kṛt karaṇe khyun’iti / tatraitad uktaṃ bhavati―khyuno 'nyaḥ kartari bhavatīti / iha kathaṃ‘supo . . . luk’(Pā. 2.4.71),‘aluk uttarapade’(Pā. 6.3.1) iti / atrāpīdaṃ vākyam uttarapadād anyatra supo lug iti / atha vā, nānākālaṃ vākyam iti yathādeśaś codito tathā [pratyayo bhavati yathā] nānākāle dadhitakre [pra]tipadyate kauṇḍinyaḥ / arthād ekakālam iti parihāra uktaḥ / 

sarvatra cāprāpty anumānaṃ bādhā, śrutau smṛtau ca / iyam eva tu bādhā‘abhakṣyo grāmyakukkuṭaḥ’iti / na hy atra śabdavad bhakṣaṇam / kiṃ tarhi? arthitvaprayuktam / tat prasaktaṃ nivartyate / vyākaraṇaṃ ca smṛtiḥ śabdani[baṃ]dhaneti sarvatrāptāptir eva viśeṣaśabdasāṃnidhye 'numīyate / 

[12]	midādiṣv iggrahaṇaṃ kartavyam iti / anigantatvā[t mi]dādiṣv iggrahaṇaṃ kartavyam teṣām upasaṃhārāt (?) [aṃ]gam i[g]grahaṇaṃ na saṃnipatati / tasmāt pradeśeṣu iggrahaṇaṃ kartavyam iti / sarvādeśaprasaṅgaś cānigantasya / midādiṣv iggrahaṇaṃ kartavyam ity ayam eko doṣaḥ / ayaṃ cāparo―yātā vāteti sarvādeśo guṇaḥ prāpnoti / evaṃ manyate―aṅgasyeka iti nimittabḥāvād upasaṃhāraḥ / sa sati nimitte pravartate / tatra yathaiva sthānaṣaṣṭhyāṃ nirjñātāyām upasaṃhāraḥ, evam igantatve saty upasaṃhāreṇa bhavitavyam / yatra tūpasaṃ[hā]ranimittaṃ nāsti, tatrānupasaṃhṛtā vidyate ṣaṣṭhī / idam apy asya darśanam―‘aṅgasya’ity etadbhedena sarveṣv aṅgeṣu ṣaṣṭhīṃ pratipādayati / pratipāditayā ṣaṣṭhyā iko 'lontyasyeti iganteṣūpasaṃhriyate, negvatsu, nāpy aniganteṣu / yātādiṣu punar asti ṣaṣṭhī / ato bhavitavyaṃ guṇena sarvādeśenetīdam āha―alontyaṣaṣṭhī caiva hy antyam ikam upasaṃkrāntā / ākgasyeti ca sthānaṣaṣṭhī / tad yad idānīm anigantam aṅgam iti / itaro aṅgasyeti idam eva rūpam alo 'ntyaṣaṣṭhyāḥ, sā cāntye ikīti yeyaṃ ṣaṣṭhī aṅgasyetīyam antya iky evāvasthitety ata āha―naiṣa doṣaḥ/ yathaivālontyaṣaṣṭhī antyam ikaṃ saṃkrāntā / yad uktaṃ bhavatā, bhinnāni aṅgāni saṃskaroti iti, tan na / evaṃ ca sati kuto 'niganteṣu yātādiṣu ṣaṣṭhī, tadabhāvāc ca kutaḥ sarvādeśa iti / hy arthe cāyaṃ caḥ paṭhitaḥ / hiśabdaś ca hetāv ity ayam api hetvartho draṣṭavyaḥ / yasmāt sarvādeśaḥ prāpnoti, tasmān midādiṣu iggrahaṇaṃ kartavyam / mider guṇa‘ika’iti vacanāṇ nāntyasya, upasaṃhāranimittasyābhāvāt / antyasyeti vacanād iko na / alo 'ntyasya hy upasaṃhāre 'sati mider igantyo nāsti / ucyate ca guṇaḥ / sa vacanāt bhaviṣyati / kim,‘aṅgasya’ity anena? nety āha―mider iti / tatra yady api ṣaṣṭhī aṅgasye[ti] vātādiṣu nāsti, iha tv asti mider iti yā cānupasaṃgṛhīteti sarvādeśaḥ prāpnoi / 

[13]	iṅmātrasyeti cet / mātraśabdaḥ kṛtsnavacanah / arvasyekaḥ, antyānantyaviṣayasya, guṇāvṛddhibhyāṃ bhavitavyam / aṅgasya viśeṣaṇatvāt, ikaś ca viśeṣyatvāt, sthānaṣaṣṭhī na bhavati aṅgasyeti / sthāne vijñātā copasaṃhriyata iti nimittābhāvād aprāptir upasaṃhārasya guṇavṛddhiviṣaya iti sarvāvasthasya iko guṇavṛddhī prāpnuta ity anantasya pratiṣedho vaktavyaḥ / [he] agnicid iti / naitāny udāharaṇāni saṃbhavanti / katham? etābhir hrasvādicodanābhir aṅgaṃ viśeṣyam iti hrasvāntasyāṅgasya guṇa iti idaṃ nyāyyaṃ he buddhe / budhāḥ / śeṣāṇy api duhitari śuddhaye śu(? pi) cavya ity etāni udāharaṇāni upādeyāni, nāgnicidādīni / naiṣa doṣaḥ / aṅgasyeti ṣaṣṭhī tadapavādakalpanāpakṣe na kāryayoginīty etad avasthitam / tatra guṇavidhānam apradhānaṃ kathaṃ hrasvādicodanābhir viśeṣyeti ig eva kāryayogitvāt viśeṣyate / sa ca hra[sva]syārthato na saṃbhavatīti evam abhisaṃbandho bhavati―aṅgasya ya igdhrasvas tasya guṇo bhavatīti / atha vā, vidhīyamānatvāt guṇah pradhānam / tādarthyād ik cāṅgaṃ [ca] guṇau / tatra hrasvādiśrutiḥ pradhāne na saṃbhavatīti tadaṅge vijñāyamānā ya[dā]sannam aṅgam i[g i]ti tasya viśeṣaṇaṃ yuktam, na punar viprakṛṣṭasya guṇasyāṅgasyety alo [? -to] pi nyāyāt etāny eva saṃbhavanti / udāharaṇamātraṃ cedam / ihāpi anantyasya pratiṣedho vaktavyaḥ,‘aco ñṇiti’(Pā. 7.2.115) iti / atrāpy aṅgasyāco yatratatrasthasyeti pratipattavyam / daridrāyakādiṣv anantyasyāco vṛddhiḥ prāpnoti / nanu ca or guṇādiṣu nirdiṣṭasthā[ni]katvāt avyāpāro 'syāḥ paribhāṣāyāḥ / satyam; evam etat / idaṃ tv evaṃ kṛtvā ucyate / bhavatā anantyaprāptir eva na vyavasitā /‘aṅgasya’itīdaṃ sāmānyaṃ viśeṣasyeko viśeṣāṇām iti evaṃ cet ihāpi hrasvādīnāṃ viśeṣāṇām‘aṅgasya’iti sāmānyaṃ viśeṣaṇam bhavitum arhati / tataś cānantyasya prasaṅgo nāsti (? asti) / evaṃ ca sati igantalakṣaṇo na bhavaty ayam iti vakṣyamāṇam apy upapadyate,‘taddhitakāmyor iglakṣaṇāt’(Pā. 1.1.5 Vā. 4) iti / apare varṇayanti / atra iko vyāpāra [ā]śrīyate / aṅgasyeko guṇo bhavati / kiṃ sarvasya? na hi / kiṃ tarhi? or iti / kimarthaṃ punar idam upādānam? sarvāvasthasya pratipattyartham / asmiṃs tu vakṣyamāṇaṃ nopapadyate, iglakṣaṇatvād asya / 

[14]	pugantalaghūpadhagrahaṇam anantyaniyamārtham / siddho hi guṇo 'nantyasya / tatra punaḥśrutir niyamārthā bhaviṣyati / etad anena pradarśyate―sārrvadhātukārdhadhātukayoḥ (Pā. 7.3.84) ity atra‘aṅgasya’itīdaṃ kāryayogi / tad ikā viśeṣyate (? itīdaṃ kāryayogitvād ikaṃ viśeṣayati) iti / nanu ca vidheyasya bhāvān niyamānupapattiḥ / kiṃ ca vidheyam? hrepayatīti / anantyasya siddho guṇaḥ / antyasya ca pakā[ra]sya vidheyaḥ syāt, bhetteti dakārasya / ucyate / puki antaḥ, laghvī upadhetyeva sūtrārtham āśrityāyaṃ niyama iti varṇyate / nanu caivam api dāpayatyartham etat syāt / astv ākārasya guṇo, vṛddhiṃ kariṣyāmaḥ / cakrakeṣu ceṣṭato vyavasthā / ayaṃ paro (? -raḥ) sūtraikadeśo niyamāya bhaviṣyati, laghvī upadheti / prakṛtasyaiva niyamaḥ / . . . sārvadhātukārdhadhātukayoḥ pugantasyaiva nānyasyeti / sārvadhātukārdhadhātukayor aṅgasyeti laghvī [upadhā] kāryayoginīti na syād iha īhiteti / hrasvādiguṇas tv aniyata iti / atrāṅgasyeti viśeṣaṇaṣaṣṭhyau ceti / athāpy evaṃ niyamaḥ syāt―sārvadhātukārdhadhātukayor eva pugantalaghūpadhasyeti / nimittī niyato 'thātra hrasvādiṣu guṇaṃ na pratipadyeta, nimittasya tanniyamāt / īhitetyevamādiṣu guṇaḥ prāpnoty eva / athāpy ubhayato niyamaḥ syāt / kathaṃ punar ubhayato labhyo yāvatā punaḥśrutir niyamaḥ / tatraikasmin niyamite punaḥśrutir aparā nāsti, yathā dvitīyo 'rtho niyamyate / ucyate / satyam / evam artham evedam ucyate athāpīti / yady apy evaṃ vyākhyāyetobhayato niyamārtham etat sūtram iti, evam api jusi guṇo 'niyataḥ / ubhayatra parigrahāt / laghūpadhaṃ caitat, jusaś ca sārvadhātuka[tva]m iti / 

[15]	evaṃ tarhi naivāyaṃ taccheṣo nāpi tadapavādaḥ / yan manyase―ekaviṣayasamnipātād ete paribhāṣe apekṣete, virodhena vā aṅgāṅgibhāvena veti, tan na / parasparānapekṣayā kāryaṃ prati pravṛttā ekā‘rājñaḥ ka ca’(Pā. 4.2.140) ityādiṣu, aparā hi guṇavṛddhyor ikaḥ sthānitvapratipādanena / yadi kathaṃcit ikah (?) ekatra saṃnipāto bhavati, naitāvataiva tayor apekṣā śakyā kalpayitum / yathā dīrghatvaitvayoḥ parasparam anapekṣamāṇayoḥ dṛṣṭ wkaviṣaye saṃnipāto vṛkṣebhya iti, tathānayoḥ paribhāṣayoḥ / tatrālontyaparibhāṣā samudāyaṣaṣṭhyā ekadeśasthāpane vyāpṛtā, iyam api [guṇavṛddhividhau ikaḥ sthānitvapratipādane / niyamādiko] guṇavṛddhī bhavato vipratiṣedheneti / śeṣatve hy ekavākyatvād ātmanā saha kīdṛśo vipratiṣedhaḥ? virodhe ca saty utsargāpavādayor apy ayukto vipratiṣedha iti / niyamasyāvakāśo‘rājñaḥ ka ca’(Pā. 4.2.140) iti / yatra‘iko guṇavṛddhī’[ity etatparibhāṣāvyāpāro nāsti /‘iko guṇavṛddhī’ity asyāvakāśaḥ cayanaṃ cāyako lavanaṃ lāvaka iti /‘alo 'ntyasya’] ity etatparibhāṣāvyāpāranimitto na kaścid viśeṣa upalabhyate yatra, tatra tasyāḥ pravṛttir anarthikā / yadi ceka ity etayaivāntyasya guṇo pratipādite niyamo 'pi tasyaiva vidadhyāt, [na] kiṃcit kṛtaṃ syāt ity ata [au]dāsīnyaṃ niyamasya pratipadyāmahe iti / ihobhayaṃ prāpnoti, [me]dyati, mārṣṭhīti / niyamāt vyañjanasya syā[t, a]nayā ca iko 'nantyasya syād iti / naiṣa yukto vipratiṣedha iti / ādau tāvat sūtrasaṃniveśa eva tair na jñāyate ity etad ācaṣṭe pūrvo 'yaṃ yogaḥ paro niyama iti / evam apīṣṭavācinā paraśabdena vyavahāro 'sti, na vyavasthi[ta]tvavācinā, pūrvaprāpta[pra]tiṣedhād anārabhamāṇa ity ata (? ity etad) āha―iṣṭavācī paraśabda iti / evam a[py a]yukto [vipratiṣedhaḥ /] dvikāryayogo hi vipratiṣedhaḥ / dvābhyāṃ kāryabhyāṃ yogo yatraikasya sthāninaḥ sa dvikāryayogaḥ, yo 'tra tatstho vipratiṣedhā[t]=dvikāryayogaviṣayatvāt tādrūpyaṃ (? tācchabdyaṃ) labhate / atha vā yogaḥ saṃbandhaḥ / dvikāryeṇa yogo yaḥ sa vipratiṣedha iti saṃbandhaviśeṣa eva vipratiṣedhaḥ / yathā virodhaśabdasya saṃbandhaviśeṣo ['rthaḥ], evaṃ vipratiṣedhaśabdasyāpi / nāvaśyaṃ dvikāryayoga eva vipratiṣedhaḥ / kiṃ tarhi? asaṃbhabo 'pi / yo 'yaṃ viprati[ṣe]dho bhavatā parigṛhyate dvikāryayogitvena, [tatra] yo 'saṃbhavah sa vipratiṣedhaḥ / yasmin asaṃbhave na ca kāryam dvikāryayogo 'sti / dvikāryayogo 'pi yasmin na saṃbhavati, tasminn api vipratiṣedha iti tatrāpy asaṃbhava eva / asaṃbhavaṃ ca dvikāryayoga eva darśayati―iha tāvan vṛkṣebhya ity ekaḥ sthānī dvāv ādeśau, na cāsti saṃbhava iti / nanu caikasya sthāne dvāvādeśau bhavato yathā ekapakṣa ekasya pacśabdasya dvau pacśabdau, evaṃ vṛkṣebhya ity a[trā]kāra ekāraś ca kasmān na bhavataḥ / ucyate / na brūmah sarvatra na saṃbhavatīti / kiṃ tarhi? evaṃbhūto na saṃbhavatīti, yo 'yaṃ prastutaḥ / tatra hi dīrghatvaṃ yañy anantaraṃ sādhutvenākhyātam etvam api jhali / na ca saṃbhavo 'sti yad ubhayoḥ svena svena nimittenānantaryaṃ syād iti / evaṃ kṛtvedam ucyate na cā[sti] saṃbhavo yad ekasya sthānino dvāv ādeśau syātām iti / ihedānīṃ medyatīti dvau sthāninau eka ādeśaḥ; na cāsti saṃbhavaḥ iti / atrāpi dṛṣṭaḥ saṃbhavaḥ /‘ekaḥ pūrvaparayoḥ’(Pā. 6.1.84),‘bhrasjo ropadheyo ram anyatarasyām’(Pā. 6.4.47) iti ca / ucyate / iha saṃbhavo nāsti / katham? mider iti sthānaṣaṣṭhyāṃ satyām upasaṃhāreṇa bhavitavyam / upasaṃhāre ceko 'pratipattir anik pratipadyate / aṅgasyeti viśeṣaṇaṣaṣṭhyām upasaṃhārāsaṃbhavaḥ / atrāpi niyamaḥ prāpnotī[ti] / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / guṇo bhavatīty etat pravartate cayanādiṣu, niyamas tv audāsīnyaṃ karoti iti kiṃ kāraṇam? syād etat―na kaścid asti viśeṣa iti / ayam eva viśeṣaḥ, alontyavidhir bādhitavya iti / yāvac cedaṃ nārabhyate‘ika’iti, tāvat kiṃ manyase kim iha syād iti? niyamaḥ / evaṃ sa[rva]tra prāpte niyama idam ārabhyate ity apavādatva[m a]sya / tasmād asty etayor virodhitvenāpekṣeti kroṣṭrīyāṇāṃ na na samyag idaṃ darśanam, asaṃbaddham anayā paribhāṣayeti / athāpi kathaṃcit / adhyāruhyedaṃ vacanam / yady api kathaṃ cit niyama audāsīnyaṃ kuryāt cayanādiṣu, evam api doṣa eva vi[pra]tiṣedhe satīti / 

[16]	evaṃ tarhi vṛddhir bhavati guṇo bha[va]ti iti / etat kathaṃ nipa[ta]ti? nedaṃ sarvaṃ pravartate, guṇavṛddhisaṃśabdaneṣu ikaḥ sthāne guṇavṛddhī bhavata iti / kiṃ tarhi? guṇavṛddhī yatra guṇavṛddhiśabdābhyāṃ vidhīyete tatra ika ity eṣa ṣaṣṭhyartho 'pi saṃnihito bhavati / atha vā ika [i]ty eṣā śrutiḥ saṃnihitā bhavatīti / kriyāpratilambhas tu pradeśād eva bhaviṣyatīti yāvanti sūtrāṇi guṇavṛddhiśrutyupasaṃgṛhītāni tāvanty anenopāyena ika iti ṣaṣṭhīmanti kriyante / ayam eva nyāyo yat pārārthyāt paribhāṣā pradhānadeśā satī tataḥ kriyāṃ pratipadyate iti / anārabhya kiṃcit kāryam‘ika’[i]ty etat pradhānabhedāt bhinnam utsṛjyate [i]ty anārabhyavādo yaṃ parigṛ[hṇī]maḥ / sar[va]tra pradeśeṣu rūpamātre janite‘ikaḥ’iti, pradhānavākyam evaṃbhūtaṃ saṃpannaṃ‘mider iko guṇo bhavatī’ti / asamāśritavyāpāraviśeṣo nāṅgatvenālontyaparibhāṣām apekṣate nāpi virodhitveneti pauruṣeyo vyāpāra āśrīyate / puruṣaḥ svecchayā pravartamāno midādiṣu iko 'ntyasyāsaṃbhavāt midādīn viśeṣaṇatvena pratipadyate, ika viśeṣyatvena, yatheha‘ūd upadhāyā gohaḥ’(Pā. 6.4.89) iti gohim upadhāviśeṣaṇatvena / jusādiṣu tu [iko 'ntyasya saṃbhavād aṅgaṃ viśeṣyatvena pratipadyate, ikaś ca viśeṣaṇatvena /] bhūyasām anugrahād evaṃ kasmān na kalpitaṃ jusādiṣv aṅgasya ya ig iti? ucyate / kāmacāraḥ sa iti / evaṃ cet kāmacāraḥ puruṣasyekāṅgaṃ viśiṣyate iti nāsty eva (? cāsty eva) bhūyasām anugrahaḥ / upapattiṛ apy atraivaṃ varṇyate / iha yady anivāritarūpeṣv aṅgagrahaṇeṣu ika [iti] viśeṣyaṃ syāt, aṅgasya iti ca viśeṣaṇam, evaṃ sati śabdenāṅgasya guṇaṃ prati yat sā[kṣā]darthitvaṃ tat hīyate, viśeṣa[ṇa]vatā upayuktatvāt / arthena ca nāsti yogaḥ, ikah sthāne guṇasya nirvṛtteḥ / atha tv aṅgasya ity etat ikā viśeṣyate, śabdopādānaḥ pradhānasaṃyogo na hāpito bhavati / ikas tu yady api śabdena pradhānaviprayogo bhavet viśeṣāṇatvāt, evam api iko guṇena nirvartitavyam ity arthena pradhānasaṃnikarṣaḥ / tasmād yasyāṃ kalpanāyāṃ igaṃ[ga]yo[ḥ] pradhānena saṃsparśaḥ, sā kalpanā āśrayitavyā / iyaṃ tu kvacit vyabhicāriṇī śāstre dṛśyata iti, tasmāc chāstra[pra]vṛttir eva grahītavyā / 

[17]	atha vā sarvatraivātra sthānī nirdiśyate / taccheṣaṃ āśritya sthānino nirdiṣṭatvād i[g]grahaṇaṃ pradeśeṣu na kartavyam ity etad ācaṣṭe / kṣiprakṣudrayor api / pūrvagrahaṇād iko bhaviṣyatīti sūtrārtham āśritya yad ucyate, [tatra] yadi tāvat yaṇa ādi[ḥ] yaṇādiḥ, yaṇādeḥ paraṃ lupyate, pūrvasya guṇo bhavati=yaṇādeḥ pūrvasyeti, siddhaṃ kṣepiṣṭhaḥ, kṣodiṣṭhaḥ / atra hi yaṇādiḥ pakāro dakāraś ceti tayor guṇaḥ [sidhyati / yaviṣṭha ity atra tu yakārasya guṇaḥ] prāpnoti / atha yaṇ ādir asya so 'yaṃ yaṇādiḥ, yaṇādiparaṃ śābdarūpaṃ lupyate pūrvasya ca guṇaḥ; etasmin [pakṣe] yuvaśabde [va]n lupyate iti siddham yaviṣṭha iti / kṣepiṣṭhe tu pakārasya guṇaḥ prāpnoti / evaṃ tarhi sthūlādiṣu [o]r guṇa eva kariṣyate / prāg bhād asiddhatvam iti [cānityam] / idaṃ tu guṇavidhānaṃ kṣiprakṣudrārtham eva / tatra yaṇādeḥ pūrvasya guṇo bhaviṣyati / nanu co[kā]rāntānāṃ sthūlādīnām or guṇe kṛte anena cāniṣṭhaṃ kriyate / tasmād yogavibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ /‘sthūladūrayuvahrasvakṣiprakṣudrāṇāṃ yaṇādiparaṃ’lupyate / tataḥ‘pūrvasya guṇah’iti / kṣiprakṣudrāv eva svaritatvād anuvartete / sthūlādayo nirvṛttāḥ / teṣāṃ [o]r guṇā eva bhavatīti / 

yady evam aniglakṣaṇo 'yaṃ bhavati, nirdiṣṭasthānikatvāt / tatra bhinnaḥ bhinnavān ity atra prāpnoti / ucyate / yathaivātreglakṣaṇatve sati vyavadhānād aprāptāv idam ucyate,“yad etasmin yoge kṅidgrahaṇaṃ tad anavakāśam”iti pratiṣedho bhavati iti, evam ihāpy asati iksthānikatve iti / [o]r guṇe tarhi,‘ata upadhāyā’(Pā. 7.2.116) ity atra ca yad anavakāśaṃ kṅidgrahaṇam iti pratiṣedhaḥ syāt, tasmāt laghvī upadhā ig iti iglakṣaṇatvaṃ kāryam / nanu cātra nivartyābhāvād ikparibhāṣāyā [u]pasthānānarthakyam / akāraś cen nāsti viśeṣaḥ / ānumānikī na pugantalaghūpadhavat pūrvaṃ bhaviṣyatīti / 

[18]	kiṃ viśeṣeṇa vṛḍdhigrahaṇaṃ codyata iti / vākyakārasya‘vṛddhihrahaṇam uttarārtham’iti vaca[nā]d idam anumīyate, vṛddhigrahanam coditam iti / ato 'sau pṛcchyate‘kiṃ viśeṣeṇa’iti / guṇavṛddhau na kvacit sthānī nirdiśyate iti / vavahito nañ atra prayuktaḥ / guṇavṛddau sthānī kvacin na nirdiśyate / tadartham i[g]grahaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ kartavyam alontyanivṛttyartham / vṛddhividhau tu sarvatra sthānino nirdeśāt vṛddhyarthenaitena nārthaḥ‘saṃjñayā vidhāne niyama’iti / 

vṛddhigrahaṇam uttarārtham / pūrvaṃ hi pratiṣedhasūtraṃ pratyākhyātam, param apy asarvaviṣayam iti idam evopanyasyati, kṅi[tī]ti pratiṣedhaṃ vakṣyati sa vṛddher api yathā syāt iti / yady aco vṛddhir ucyate, nyamārṭ, aṭo 'pi vṛddhiḥ prāpnoti / ādi[ṣṭi]d ity aṭo mṛjigrahaṇena grahaṇāt / jatam aṃga bhavati (?) / kathaṃ punar atrāṭo vṛddhi[ḥ]? yāvatā paratvāt vṛddhyā bhavitavyaṃ, kāryakṛtatvāt punar na bhaviṣyati / syān matam, śabdāntarasya kṛtetīdaṃ nopapatyate . . . / a[na]ntyavikāre 'ntyasadeśasya iti / na hy atra vṛddhau kṛtāyām ubhayor asti prāptiḥ / kiṃ tarhi? anantyasyaivācaḥ / athāpi kalpyate, lāvasthāyām aṭ iti / vṛddhir api lāvasthāyāṃ kena pratiśiddhā? tasmād abhyupetyāṭaḥ pūrvatvam idam ucyate / 

ihānye vaiyākaraṇāḥ / atra codyate, kim avaśyam anyasmin vyākaraṇe ye 'nvākhyātās ta ihāpi anvākhyeyāḥ? nety āha / kiṃ tarhi? iyam api smṛtir, yatraiva smṛtyor virodhas tatraiva vikalpo, na ced anyatarā dṛṣṭārthā bhavati / iha tu nāsti virodhaḥ / ekatrāviśeṣeṇa vṛddhiḥ smaryate, anyatra viśiṣṭaviṣaye vibhāṣety asyāḥ smṛteḥ kṛtavṛddhiḥ prayojya(? -kṣya)te, tasyā api akṛtavṛddhir iti / nya[ṅkor neti] smṛtyantare pratiṣedhaḥ ārabhyate, nyāṅkavam iti / ihāpy avyutpattipakṣe nyāṅkavam iti śiṣṭaparṣadā uktatvāt ayaṃ vyākhyānaṃ(? -tā) saṃsādhnoti / [ni]yatakālā hi smṛtaya iṣṭās tathā śrutayaś ca / yathā gavām ālambho madyapānam ca / śukreṇāsevitam iti kriyamāṇaṃ pratyavāyāyeti / [gavālambhe] dṛṣṭā ca smṛtir niyatakālā / adyatva etat pāpāya / śabdāḥ smaryante abhyudayāya / anye tu doṣāya / idaṃ ca śabdarūpapam adyatve 'pi prayoktavyam ata etat sādhyam iti / tatra [mṛjer] vṛddhir aca ity etāvad evāstu / [tataḥ]‘aci kṅiti vā bhavatī’ti asya ca smartā bhāṣyakāraḥ / ayaṃ cādiśiṣṭa iti / 

acikīrṣīt . . . / lopo 'sya bādhako bhaviṣyatīti / akṛtāyām‘ato lopaḥ’(Pā. 6.4.48) pūrvavipratiṣedhāt, kṛtāyām api āto lopena bhavitavyam iti / uda[vo]ḍhām ity atra ḍhalopasyāsiddhatvāt / pūrvaṃ vṛddhau kṛtāyāṃ ḍhalopādiṣu kṛteṣu tv asyautve kṛte kāryakṛtatvān na bhaviṣyati / sici vṛddher apy eṣa pratiṣedhaḥ / katham? lakṣaṇaṃ hi nāma dhvanati bhramati muhūrtam api nāvatiṣṭhate / tatra dhvanatīty avyaktasāmānyaṃ parigṛhya prayujyate / yathā loke avyaktaṃ yo bravīti, sa ucyate‘kiṃ dhvanasi, kaṭakaṭāyasi?’iti / evam iha‘neṭi’(Pā. 7.2.4) ity avyaktaṃ pravartate / kim asya pratiṣedho yaḥ (? -dho 'nyasya) pratiṣedha iti eṣā vyaktir nāsti / atha vā yathā dhvaniḥ sarvaśabdaviṣayas tanmātropalabdhau tv etāvad bhavati―dhvanaty ayaṃ śabdo 'nena kaścid uccāratīti / evam ihāpi sarvaviṣayaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ,‘halantasya’‘sici’iti vṛddhir na bhavatīti sarvaviṣayasāmānyāt dhvanatīty ucyate / syād etat―dhvanad api tat [e]kasmin kṛtārthaṃ pratiṣedhye na pravartetānyatrety ata āha―bhramatīti / sāmānyacodanā viśeṣārthaiva / yathaikasmin viṣaye pravarteta, tathā[nya]sminn api viśeṣāntare / atha matam etat―yāvat tadviśeṣāntare pratiṣedhye pravartate, tāvad anyatra vidhiḥ pravṛttaḥ, pravṛttaś cāśakyo bhrāmyatāpi lakṣaṇena pratiseddhum ity ata āha―muhūrtam api nāvatiṣṭhata iti / vāg bahīrūpasāmānyā / tad evedam āśusaṃcāritvāt muhūrtam apy anavasthitam ity ucyate / na tv etad asti śabde / kiṃ tarhi? pratiṣedhāntarāṇi tāni bhinnāny eva pratiśedhyalakṣaṇāni / tasmān nāsti lakṣaṇaśūnyaḥ kaścit pratiṣedhya itīdam ucyate―muhūrtam api nāvatiṣṭhata iti / anye varṇayanti / dhvanīti punaḥpravṛttisāmānyād ucyate / yathā ghaṇṭāyāḥ punaḥ punaḥ śabda utpādyamāne dhvanati ghaṇṭeti vyapadiśyate / evam etāni lakṣaṇāni‘sici vṛddhiḥ’,‘halantasyācaḥ’‘neṭi’iti [pra]vartamānāni dhvanantīty ucyante / syād etat―‘sakṛt pravṛttir’iti, etan nāsti / bhramati=ghaṭīyantravad āvartate / āvartamānam api hy eva (? vaṃ) muhūrtam api nāvatiṣṭhate / tatraivaṃ sati na vidhir etasmin viṣaye na pratiṣedha ity apravṛttyaiva lakṣaṇasya bhavitavyam iti / athāvyavasthā cakrakeṣu, tathāpi iṣṭato vyavastheti pratiṣedhena vyavasthā bhaviṣyati, iti / asti punaḥ kvacid anyatrāpi / sāmānyam upādīyate ity etat pṛcchyate apravṛtti(? -tta)tvāt lakṣaṇasyeti / astīty āha / sujāte aśvasūnṛte /‘nāntaḥpādam’ity evaṃ pāṭham āśrityedam upanyasyati, na ‘prakṛtyāntaḥpādam’(Pā. 6.1.115) iti / atrāpi avyaktaṃ sāmānyaviṣayaṃ vā lakṣaṇam upādāya eṅaḥ atīti dhvanati idaṃ‘nāntaḥpādam’(Pā. 6.1.115) iti / atha vā lakṣaṇānām ayādīnām anavasthānād apravṛttir eveti / atha vṛkāv ity atra katham? atra‘nād ici’(Pā. 6.1.104) iti pratiṣedhena vṛddhir api pratiṣedhyā‘vṛddhir eci’(Pā. 6.1.88) iti / atha manyase bhinnaprakaraṇāv etāv iti, [i]hāpi yujyate aśvasūnṛte iti bhinnaṃ prakaraṇam utsargāpavādayoḥ / athārthadvāreṇaikaprakaraṇatvam, ihāpi tathaiva / tasmād atra vyaktasya viśeṣasyāśrayaṇam / prathamayoḥ pūrvasavarṇadīrghatvasyaiva pratiṣedhaḥ, na‘vṛddhir eci’iti / 

antaraṅgatvād uvaṅādeśaḥ / . . . sicparasmaipade āśritya vṛddhir iti luptanirdiṣṭo vakāra iti / kecid atra varṇayanti―nirdiṣṭaś cāsau luptaś ceti / etat tu nopapadyate, nirdiṣṭasya lopenāsaṃbaddhatvāt / nirdiṣṭo hi katam iva lupyate? tasmād yathaiva‘yasya . . .’(Pā. 6.4.148) ity asmād rūpāt pratipattim ikārasya āśrayāma evaṃ luptaṃ pratipattikāle vakāraṃ pratipatsyāmaha iti / avimavyor neti vakṣyāmi / syād etat, guru bhavati, [i]ty ata āha―ṇiśvibhyāṃ tau nimātavyau iti / ācāryapravṛttir jñāpayati iti / kiṃ vṛddhigrahaṇaṃ kariṣyāmīti neṣyate sicy antaraṅgatvam? nety āha / anenāyīt acecāyīt acirāyīt ajirāyīt ityevamādiṣu pravṛttyartham idaṃ neṣyate / cirijiryor hi, yaṅlugantānāṃ ca, guṇāyādeśayoḥ kṛtayor yāntatvāt pratiṣedhaḥ syāt / 

[19]	ṣaṣṭhyāḥ sthāneyogatvād ignivṛttiḥ / ika iti sthānaṣaṣṭhī / tatra guṇāvṛddhibhyāṃ sarva ig nivartitavya iti / atha vaitad upagantavyam, neyaṃ sthānaṣaṣṭhīti / anyatarārthaṃ punarvacanam / nanu cānigantārthaṃ syāt / ane[na] iko nirvarti[te]ṣu guṇeṣu aṅgādhikāre vacanam anigarthaṃ syāt / kasmāt? vidhi[ni]yamayor vidhir balavān ity ato 'nigarthaṃ syāt / tatrāyam abhiprāyaḥ /‘iko guṇāvṛddhī’ity atra guṇavṛddhigrahaṇam anuvartate / tatra lakṣa[ṇa]tvena pravṛttam etat punaḥ parāṅgatva[m anu]bhavati / guṇavṛddhī iti ca yatra brūyāt, [ikah] ity etat tatropasthitaṃ draṣṭavyam iti / saṃprasāraṇe ca / yaṇa iti ṣaṣṭhyāḥ sthāneyogatvāt sarvayaṇāṃ nivṛttiḥ prāpnoti / . . . yātā vāteti / prasāraṇaṃ pūrvam / katham? (? [saṃ]prasāraṇasaṃjñā katham?) yogavibhāgaḥ kriyate /‘ig yaṇaḥ’/ yaṇah sthāne ik bhavati / tataḥ‘saṃprasāraṇam’/ saṃprasāraṇasaṃjñaś ca bhavati ig, yaṇaḥ sthāne 'tra nirvṛtta iti / viṣayārthaṃ (puna)rvacanam / nanu ca etad apy ayaṇarthaṃ vijñāyamānam evaṃ parikalpyeta―saṃprasāraṇaśabda ādeśo vacyādīnām iti / tatrāyam abhiprāyaḥ―ig yaṇa ity atrāpy upatiṣṭhata iti / ur aṇ rapare ca / kartṛ hartṛ / atrāntara[ta]masyā[bhāvād a]ṇaḥ paryāyeṇa prāpnuvanti / etadartham eva ca prātipadikam upanyastam / dhātuniyamo 'nyatra syāt―“ṛta id dhātoḥ”(Pā. 7.1.100),‘ud oṣṭhyapūrvasya’a(Pā. 7.1.102) iti / siddhaṃ tu (ṣa)ṣṭhyadhikāre vacanād /  ṣaṣthyadhikāre sati svanimittaprayuktāḥ sthānaṣaṣṭhīm apekṣamāṇā‘aṅgasya’, ‘vacyādīnām’,‘ṛta id dhātoḥ’iti, svakāryaṃ niṣpādayanti, na ṣaṣṭhīm antareṇeti / imāv api yogau ṣaṣṭhyadhikāram anuvartiṣyete / svaryete etau yogau / tatra pāratantryāt nāsty etābhyāṃ kāryapratipattir iti / atha vā ṣaṣthyadhikāre imau yogau apekṣiṣyāmahe /‘ṣaṣṭhī sthāneyogā’(Pā. 1.1.49) iti yoga vibhāgaḥ kriyate /‘ṣaṣṭhī’iti / yad etad anukrāntam etad yatra ṣaṣṭhī tatra saṃnihitam iti / sāmarthyāt etāv eva saṃbadhyate / atha vedaṃ tāvad ayaṃ pra[ṣṭavyaḥ / ava]śyaṃ tvayānyatarārthaṃ punarvacanam iti samarthayamānena kartavyaḥ saṃbandhaḥ―yatra ca guṇa iti vā vṛddhir iti vā brūyāt, tatra‘ika’ity etad upatiṣṭhata iti / evaṃ yātādayaḥ śakyā nivartayitum / tatrāpekṣāyāṃ satyāṃ kimarthaṃ yogabhedenāpekṣā kriyate, na punar ekam evābhisaṃbaddhaṃ vakyam? tatraivam apekṣiṣyāmahe―guṇavṛddhī iti ye guṇāvṛddhī, te iko bhavata iti etad anena bhāṣyeṇa kathayati―idaṃ tatrāpekṣyate / . . . yathaiva tarhi idaṃ tatrāpekṣyate, evam ihāpi tad ape[kṣi]ṣyāmaha iti / 

iti śrīmahābhāṣyaṭīkāyāṃ bhartṛhariviracitāyāṃ tṛtīyam āhnikam // 
 



mahābhāṣyadīpikāyāṃ caturtham āhnikam /

na dhātulopa ārdhadhātuke // A 1.1.4 //

[1] aktaparimāṇasya śabdasya dhātusaṃjñā / tasya ca lope naivāsti prasaṅgo guṇavṛddhyoḥ. kiṃ pratiṣedheneti / ato vṛttikāro vyācaṣṭe---`dhātor lopo dhātupopaḥ / dhātvekadeśalopanimitta ārdhadhātuke' iti /

apara evam āha / dhātoś caikadeśasya ca lopo dhātulopaḥ / dhātulopanimitta iti / yo 'pi kṛtsnasya dhātor lopo 'sāv api dhātulopaḥ, yo 'py ekadeśasya lopo 'sāv api dhātulopa iti śabdavṛttaṃ kathayati / iha tv ekadeśalopaḥ saṃbhavati ity ayaṃ parigṛhate / 

anena prakāreṇaitad ākhyāyate / avayavānāṃ yā tu sati sārtsye dhātusaṃjñā tathā vyavahriyate / na tu kṛtsnaviṣayayā, samudāyalope pratiṣedhyayor abhāvāt iti dhātugrahaṇasya darśanam / 

dhātuśabdo dhātāv eva samudāye, lopo 'py  ekadeśābhāva eva / yas tv asāv ekadeśābhāvaḥ, samudāyasya saṃbandhatvena sa diśyate, avayavakarmaṇaḥ samudāye vyapadeśāt / paṭo dagdhaḥ, alaṃkṛto devadattaḥ, ki (? kli)nno devadatta iti yathā / 

ayam eva cārtho vṛttikāraiḥ pradarśitaḥ / dhātvekadeśalopo dhātulopa iti / 

[2] dhātugrahaṇaṃ kim artham? nanu guṇabhāvāt praśnānupapattiḥ / evaṃ ca kecid vṛttikārā `dhātulopa iti kimartham?' iti paṭhanti / samudāyam abhidadhatām api teṣām avayava eva codyate / anyathā bālavākyaṃ syāt, yatraiva vidhis tatraiva pratiṣedhaś ceti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / sāmarthyāt prāptiś cā(? prāptaṃ cā)pekṣyaṃ nirdiśati / 

lūñ lavitā / nanu cātra vṛttau pratyudāharaṇaṃ nopapadyate, dhātulopanimitta ārdhadhātuke prati[ṣedha]vacanāt / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / sati dhātugrahaṇe bahuvrīhiḥ saṃbhavati / bahuvrīhau saty ubhayaviśeṣaṇaṃ bhavati / 

[2.1] kathaṃ punar anubandhalopo bhavati, na dhātulopaḥ? ucyate / iha dhātusaṃjñā itsaṃjñā ca yaugapadyena pravartete / yaś cetsaṃjñaḥ sa nāstīti śāstre 'bhyupagamaḥ / evaṃ ca sati yasyām evāvasthāyāṃ dhātusaṃjñā tasyām eva cetsaṃjñā / yadā ca dhātusaṃjñā saṃnipatitā tadā naivāsty anubandha iti asato nāsti dhātusaṃjñā / atha pūrvaṃ dhātusaṃjñā evam api bhāvārthānāṃ dhātusaṃjñā / arthaś ca prayogaśabdasyāpodṛtyānvayavyatirekābhyām ekadeśasya ]parikalpyate / na cānubandhasyānvayo vyatireko veti / yāvata evāvadher anvayavyatirekau tāvata(ti) eva kriyāvacanatvaṃ parikalpya dhātusaṃjñā pravartate iti naiṣa doṣaḥ / sasakārasyāpi dṛṣṭā kriyāvacanatā tiṣṭhāsatīty evamādiṣu / 

[2.2] vṛṣabho roravīti / saty ārdhadhātukagrahaṇam / bhāṣyakāro 'py etadarthaṃ caitāni pratyudāharaṇāni upanyasyati, krameṇa sūtrapratyākhyānaṃ kariṣyāmi, pakṣe ca dhātugrahaṇam anarthakam iti vakṣyāmīti /

[2.3] catvāraḥ pakṣāḥ saṃbhavanto 'tro(?-vanti dvāvatro)panyastau---(?) lopaviśeṣaṇam ārdhadhātukagrahaṇam, (2) guṇavṛddhiviśeṣaṇam, (3) antareṇa bahuvrīhim ubhayaviśeṣaṇam āvṛtteḥ, yathopadeśagrahaṇam ‘ekāca upadeśe 'nudāttāt’ (A 7.2.10) iti, (4) bahuvrīhir iti /

[3] kiṃ punar idam ārdhadhātukagrahaṇam iti / kim ārdhadhātukādhāro lopo guṇavṛddhyabhāvasyopalakṣaṇatvenopādū¥īyate, atha ārthadhātukādhārayoḥ guṇavṛddhyor atannnimitto 'pi lopa upalakṣaṇatvenopādīyata iti / kiṃ punar atra nyāyyam ? ubhayam ity āha / katham ? dhātukam ubhayoḥ saṃbandhi saṃbhavatīti pṛcchati / 

upeddhaḥ, preddha i[ty a]trāpi prāpnoti / nanv antaraṅgatvāt pūrvam evād guṇaḥ prāpnoti / ‘pūrvaṃ dhātur upasargeṇa yujyate’ iti / tasmād idam udāharaṇaṃ kaṣṭeddha iti / yadi ca [u]pasargatvaṃ tvanyaktavyemupagatare (?) . . . [-sargāt pūrvaṃ pratyayotpattiḥ, katham upe]ddhaḥ, preddhaḥ ? naiṣa doṣaḥ / vakṣyaty evāyam antaraṅgabahiraṅgabhāvam / yadi pūrvam upasargayogo 'bhyupagamyate, guṇo 'ntaraṅgo bahiraṅgaḥ, pratiṣedhaḥ / atha pūrvaṃ pratyayotpattiḥ, evam api guṇo bahiraṅgo 'ntaraṅgaḥ pratiṣedha iti / ayaṃ tu noktaḥ parihāraḥ aniglakṣaṇatvāt ity uttaratra vakṣyāmīti / 

nanu ca ‘nājānantarye bahiṣṭvaprakḷptiḥ’ iti na sādhvasādhuparijñānaṃ ced bhavati iti / etat tu nopapadyate / śāstrāt sādhvadādhupravṛtteḥ / anye varṇaniṣpattir ity āhuḥ / hiraṇyadyūr iti yaṇo nirvṛttiḥ / evaṃ tu sati pratiṣedho 'pi niṣpādyata ity ayam arthaḥ / tasmād ayam arthaḥ, acāmānantaryasya vidhirūpeṇa yasmin śāstre āśrayaṇam asti tatrāpravṛttiḥ / iha tu pratiṣedhe na tayor āśrayaṇam ity adoṣaḥ / 

knopayatīty atrāpi prāpnoti / nanu cātra puko bahiraṅgatvāt yalopo na prāpnoti / atha nipātanāl lopaḥ ‘cele knope’ (A 3.4.33) iti, nipātanāt guṇo bhaviṣyati / 

yady evaṃ nārtho dhātugrahaṇena / antareṇa bahuvrīhim ubhayaviśeṣaṇam ārdhadhātukagrahaṇaṃ saṃbhavaty eṣa / lope ārdhadhātuke ye guṇavṛddhī prāpnutaḥ te na bhavata iti / na cānubandhalope ārdhadhātukanimittaḥ / 

[4] parigaṇanaṃ kartavyam  ‘na yaṅy akkyavalope ārdhadhātuke’ ity ayaṃ yogaḥ paṭhitavyaḥ / evaṃ paṭhyamāne knopayatvādayaḥ siddhā bhavanti / valopa ity atra kaścit viśeṣa āśrayitavyaḥ / tena jīveḥ pratyayo na sriveḥ / 

[4.1] numlopasrivyanubandhalope 'pratiṣedhārtham / num ity anuṣaṅgasya grahaṇam / pūrvācāryasaṃjñā [jñāta]vyā / anyathā numaḥ sthānāpnne [lope] iti iṣṭagrahaṇaṃ na grahyeta / atra parigaṇanād eteṣu nāsti pratiṣedhaḥ / asati hi parigaṇane dhātulopa iti pratiṣedhaḥ / 

[4.2] ikprakaraṇāt numlope vṛddhiḥ / yatra numi [ika iti] etad upatiṣṭhate sthānyantaranivṛttyarthaṃ tatrāyaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ / atra ika ity asyāvyāpāraḥ / 

[4.3] pratyayāśrayatvād anyatra siddham / ubhayaviśeṣaṇam ārdhadhātukam ity abhiprāyaḥ / 

jīradānur ity atra na prāpnoti / na hy ayaṃ pratyayāśrayo lopaḥ / kiṃ tarhi ? varṇāśrayaḥ / valopa iti vacanāt / 

[4.4] raki jyaḥ prasāraṇam / tatra kṅitīti siddhaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ / avagrahaś ca siddhaḥ / svaro 'pi bahuvrīhitvāt na duṣyati / 

bahiḥ prakṛtyantaram / ātaś ca prakṛtyantaram avigānena viśiṣya yad upadiśanti khilapāṭhe / na hy ayam āhir iva pañcapadaviṣaya iti /

bhdedyate, chedyate ity atrāpi prāpnoti / dvayam apy etad ārdhadhātuke, ṇilopasyārdhadhātukanimittatvāt, ṇau cārdhadhātuke guṇavṛddhyoḥ prāptir iti / idaṃ tu nopapadyate / prāg eva sārvadhātukavikaraṇābhyāṃ ṇāv eva guṇasya kṛtatvāt /

naivaṃ vijñāyate iti / bahuvrīhir ayam ity etat kathayati / bahuvrīhau saty ekanimittatvābhyupagamo lopasya guṇavṛddhyoś ca / iha bhedyate ity a[nya]smin ārdhadhātuke lopo anyasmin guṇavṛddhī iti nāsti pratiṣedhaḥ /

[5] yatra halacorādeśaḥ / yaṅo 'ci ca (A 2.4.74) iti samudāyaḥ pratyayasaṃjñaḥ / tasya ca lukā adarśanaṃ saṃbhavati iti / 

atrāpy akāralope kṛte / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / yakāramātraṃ pratyayasaṃjñam evākārālope / yathā apacad iti ekāntāpagame 'pi avaśiṣṭe bhavaty eva pratyayasaṃjñā / na ca `yaṅo 'ci ca' (A 2.4.74) iti sahakāreṇa samudāyo vivakṣyate / yathā gaur iti ākṛtyapadeśe varṇādayo na vivakṣyanta iti tadguṇaś cātadguṇaś ca bhavaty eva gaur evam ihāpi sati [asati] cākāre bhavaty eva yaṅ iti / 

luki kṛte na prāpnoti / vakṣyamāṇo 'bhiprāyo nityo lug iti / 

idam iha saṃpradhāryam / avaśiṣṭasya lug ity abhiprāyaḥ / lopasyāvakāśaḥ ---cikīrṣitā / luko 'vakāśo---yakāramātram / samudāya ubhayaṃ prāœnoti / paratvāt yaṅo[ 'kārasya] lopaḥ iti / 

nityo luk / śāstrapravṛttinirtyeti luko nityatvam / lukā tu sarvasmin nivartite asaṃbhavo lopasyety anityatvam / 

anavakāśas tarhi luk / katham anavakāśatvaṃ manyate / nāprāpte lope luka ārambhāt / yadi hi luk nocyeta, ato lopaḥ syāt / atha vā pratyayasya lug ucyate / na ca yakāramātraṃ pratyayaḥ / kutaḥ ? prayoga abhāvāt / upadeśe ca yakāramātrasyānupadeśāt / ato naivāsya prayogāt pratyayatvādhyavasāyo, nopadeśāt / kiṃca ‘yaṅo 'ci ca’ (A 2.4.74) iti samudāyo nirdiṣṭaḥ / tasmād anavakāśo lug iti / 

sāvakāśo luk / ko 'vakāśaḥ ? avaśiṣṭaḥ / evaṃ manyate / bhinnaviṣayau lopalukau / asti ca yakāramātrasya pratyayatvasamadhigamo lolūyitādiṣu ya eva ca so 'yam ity ekatvādhyavasāyāt / na ca ‘yaṅo 'ci ca’ iti nirdeśarūpaṃ vivakṣitam / tasmād avaśiṣṭam avakāśa iti / 

athāpi kathaṃcit iti / pūrvoktayā kalpanayā ucyate / 

evam apy allope yogavibhāgaḥ kariṣyate / yogavibhāgaś ca luko bādhanārtha eveti / 

samānāśrayo luk lopena bādhyate / tulyakālatvāt / ‘bahulaṃ chandasi’ (A 2.4.73) iti sa nimittam anapekṣyāntaraṅgatvāt pravartata eva / nanu cāto lopo 'py ārdhadhātukasāmānye vidhīyate / ārdhadhātuko 'trāśrīyate / aṅgādhikāraś ca / athā vā, yady api sāmānyena tathāpi buddhisāmānyam āśrīyate luki tu buddhyāpi nāśrayaṇam iti / 

[6] nikucite 'py uktam / [kim?] saṃhipātalakṣaṇo vidhir iti / kittvasaṃniyogena ca lopaḥ prakṛtaḥ, sa nimittam ‘udupadhāt’ (A 1.2.21) iti asyākittvasya na bhavati / tenātra ‘kṅiti ca’ (A 1.1.5) iti pratiṣedho bhaviṣyati, iti / 

[7] yadi sthānivadbhāvena yogaḥ pratyākhāyate, jaṅgama ity upadhālopaḥ prāpnoti / tathā yāya iti ‘āto lopaḥ . . . ’ (A 6.4.64), dedya iti yuḍāgamaḥ ‘dīṅo yuḍaci kṅiti’ (A 6.4.63) iti / nanu ca jaṅgama iti  ‘. . . anaṅi’ (A 6.4.98) iti niṣedho bhaviṣyati / kathaṃ punar ayam aṅ yāvatā yaṅ atropadiśyante ? athāpi yakārāpagame syād, evam api anarthako 'yam / athega kathaṃ punaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ agamad iti ? anuvāditvād arthavattvāt / 

kiṃca sthānini kāryaṃ dṛṣṭam ādeśe 'tidiśyate / na cākāre kāryāṇi dṛśyante / tatra kathaṃ lupte pravarteran ? evaṃ ca sati yāyāvara iti naivāto lopaḥ prāptaḥ / kiṃ sthānivat pratiṣedhena?

tatra pāṇiner ayam abhiprāyaḥ / yathaiva lakāre kāryam adṛṣṭam ādeśe prāpnoti iti codyate saṃbhavamātreṇa, [evam] ihāpi / yathaiva cākārāpāye bhavati pratyayo yakāramātram, evam ihāpi saṃbhavati saty asthāne 'kārasya yakārāpāye pratyayatvam iti yāyāvare sthānivatpratiṣedha ārabhyate / vārttikakārasya tu kathaṃ jaṅgamādayaḥ? asyāpi lug iti / tena yāyāyaḥ (? yāyaḥ), dedīyaḥ iti [na] bhavitavyam / loluvādiṣu tv abhyupagaṃsyate saṃbhavamātreṇa pratyayatvam, tatkṛtaś ca sthānivadbhāvād uvaṅādeśa iti / 

kṅiti ca || A 1.1.5 ||

guṇavṛddhī pratiṣedhaś ca dvāv apīhānumīyamānau, pratiṣedhas tu vidhīyamāna[tvā]t pradhānam / na cāsmāt śabdarūpād asti vyaktiḥ---pradhānam anarthyanena viśeṣaṇena kṅitīti / asti ced arthitvam, pradhānam atilaṅghya na yuktaṃ guṇavṛddhiviśeṣaṇam / tena kṅitīti saptamīnirdiṣṭe 'anantarasya pratiṣedhena bhavitavyam / syād etad, ubhayānu[gra]ha guṇavṛddhiviśeṣaṇam, yathā ‘striyām’ (A 4.1.3) ity etad guṇabhūtasya viśeṣaṇaṃ pātipadikasya / yuktaṃ hi tatrobhayānu[gra]hād, iha tu tathā neti yuktaṃ pratiṣedhaviśeṣaṇam / nanu ca guṇavṛddhiviśeṣaṇe vyavahitānantareṣu pravartamāno mahāviṣayaḥ [pratiṣedhaḥ] kṛto bhavatīti / etac ca na / śunaskarṇastomayajñavad etat syāt / yathā pradhānasya [ma]raṇe nārthi(?-ṅgā)nām iṣṭaṃ pravartayanti, evaṃ viśiṣṭavi[ṣa]yāvasthitayaiva prakṛtyā pradhānam arthi syāt, ity ato 'pi ayukto 'yaṃ pakṣaḥ / nanu ca pratiṣedhaviśeṣaṇe 'pi guṇavṛddhiviśeṣaṇam eva / sarvatraiva hi kriyā vidhīyamānā---guṇavṛddhī bhavato na bhavata iti vā / yady apy evaṃ tathāpi sattāsādṛśyam āpannā gṛhate, na nivṛttir iti sattāyāṃ guṇavṛddhī viśeṣaṇaṃ, na nivṛttāv iti / evam api pratiṣedhe nānantaryaṃ saṃbhavati iti tadviśeṣaṇam ayuktam / guṇavṛddhiviśeṣaṇe 'py evam eva / na hi kṅitā guṇavṛddhyor ānantaryam asti / tasmāt guṇavṛddhiviśiṣṭa[ḥ] pratiṣedhaḥ sthāninaḥ punar ārupaprasava ikaḥ saṃskāra iti / ikā ca saṃbhavaty ānantaryam iti idam ucyate---

[1.1] tannimittagrahaṇam iti / nāyaṃ pāṭhaḥ / kiṃ tarhi ? arthapradarśanam etat / tatra vyākhyātṛbhiḥ sa śabda upādeyo yasminn ayam arthaḥ parigṛhyate / evaṃ hi paṭhitavyam---‘kṅinnimitte ca guṇavṛddhī na bhavataḥ’ iti / bhāṣyakāro nimittagrahaṇam apūrvaṃ kartavyam iti pratipannaḥ / anye tu nimittagrahaṇaṃ vyākhyeyam iti varṇayanti / guṇavṛddhiviśeṣaṇaṃ kṅidgrahaṇam / guṇavṛddhiviśeṣaṇe cānupasthānam evāsyāḥ paribhāṣāyāḥ ‘tasminn iti nirdiṣṭe pūrvasya’ (A 1.1.66) iti / paribhāṣā vidhyaṅgabhūtatvāt yatra vidhīyate tatrpatiṣṭhateḥ, yathā ‘iko guṇavṛddhī’ iti (A 1.1.3) yatra guṇavṛddhī vidhīyete tatropatiṣṭhate / yatra tu siddhā vṛddhir anyārtham upādīyate, ‘vṛddhir yasyācāmādis tad vṛddham’ (A 1.1.73) iti, na tatropatiṣṭhate ‘ikaḥ’ iti / yady upatiṣṭhate śāliyādiṣu vṛddhasaṃjñā na syāt / jñāpakaṃ ca ‘udīcāmātaḥ sthāne . . .’ (A 7.3.46) iti sthānagrahaṇam, ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśe asthāneyoganimittaḥ pratiṣedho mā bhūd iti (?) / evam ihāpi guṇavṛddhī na vidhīyete / vihitayor anyatra bhūtayoḥ kṅitīti hy etadupādīyate ity upasthānād anantaraparibhāṣāyā vyavahite 'nantare ca siddha[ḥ] pratiṣedhaḥ / kṅiti ye guṇavṛddhī vihite te na bhavataḥ / kṅiti pratiṣedhe nāsti nimittaparigrahaḥ / etat tu nopapadyate / pratiṣedhasya prādhānyāt / arthe 'ntarbhūtasyaivāyam anugraha iti ko 'nurodhaḥ pratiṣedhānugrahe, na punar guṇavṛddhiśāstrānugrahe ? ‘tasminn iti nirdiṣṭe pūrvasya’ ity etat sthānaṃ paribhāṣāyā iti nimittagrahaṇaṃ kartavyam / liṅgāc ca kasmāc cit guṇavṛddhiviśeṣaṇaṃ kalpayitavyam / anena pradhānaṃ dharmavaicitryāt kadācid guṇeṣv āsajati dharmān---sa cāsaṅgaḥ svakāryapratipattyartha eva---kadācid ātmanaiva tān dharmān pratipadya kāryeṣu pravartate / yaś ca dṛṣṭānta[sta]-smin maraṇaṃ yajamānasya sākṣāt / śrutam / iha punarn nāstīti anekaviṣayatvaṃ kalpyate sākṣād guṇavṛddhiviśeṣaṇe /

[2] upadhāryaṃ roravīty artham [ca] / upadhāyā vyavahitārtham, itaratra tu yaṅnimittapratiṣedho mā bhūd iti / yatraiva kṅity anantarp guṇabhāvy asti iti / evamartham idam ucyate---guṇabhāvī[k]pratiṣedhe ānantaryābhāvāt (?) / yatheha ‘iko yaṇ aci’ (A 6.1.77) ikānantaryam acaḥ saṃbhavati yaṇā ca, evam iha na pratiṣedhena nāpi guṇavṛddhibhyām iti ikaivānantaryāt / guṇapravṛttim ācaṣṭe, vṛddhau kasmān nodāhṛtam ? ihaiva syāt mṛṣṭa iti / ayam abhiprāyaḥ---nirdiśyate tatra sthānī vṛddheḥ ‘[mṛje]r vṛddhir acaḥ’ iti /

[2.1] nanu ca yasya guṇa ucyate / kathaṃ punar asaṃśabditam aprakṛtaṃ ca pugantalaghūpadhaṃ śakyam āśrayītum ? yadi ca guṇabhūtasya pugantalaghūpadhasya kṅitā viśeṣaṇam, ko matsaro guṇavṛddhiviśeṣaṇe iti ? yat tāvad ucyate---aprakṛtam asaṃśabditaṃ ceti / atra brūmaḥ---nāvaśyaṃ śabdaprakaraṇābhyām eva saṃnidhānam, sāmarthyād api hi bhavati saṃnidhānam / iha ca kṅiti guṇavṛddhī na bhavataḥ / kasyety ākāṅkṣā / atha vā kṅitīti pratyayagrahaṇe sati aṅgam api saṃnihitam iti sāmarthyaprāpitasaṃnidhānaṃ pugantalaghūpadham / yac cocyate guṇavṛddhiviśeṣaṇe ko matsaro iti / guṇavṛddhiviśeṣaṇe 'pi sati vyavahite naiva prāpnoti / katham ? yathaiva ‘iko yaṇ aci’ iti bhūte 'pi yaṇy ānantarye vyāpriyate ‘ajanantaraparasya iko yaṇ bhavati’ iti ato yaṇ pravartate, e[va]m iha bhūtayor api guṇavṛddhyoḥ kṅitīti ānantaryaṃ syād eva / tena yatra guṇavṛddhibhāvī ik a[nanaṃ]taras tatra [na] syād eva pratiṣedha iti sa eva doṣo yaḥ pratiṣrdhaviśeṣaṇe / tasmāt pugantalaghūpadhasyaiva bhūtasuānantaryam āśrayitavyaṃ yatrānantaraparibhāṣā vyāpriyamāṇānugṛhyata ity arthaḥ / saṃpannaḥ---kṅity antare pugantalaghūpadhasya guṇo na bhavatīti / aṅgasyeti ca noktam / nāṇgamātraṃ guṇabhāk / kiṃ tarhi ? viśiṣṭam aṅgam---sārvadhātukārdhadhātukayoḥ / pugantalaghūpadhasyeti naivaṃ vijñāyate / nātra samudāyaḥ pratinirdiśyate / kiṃ tarhi ? avayavaḥ / sa ca vyavahita ity aprāptiḥ pratiṣedhasya / ladhvyā upa[dhā]yāḥ kṅitīti na bhavatīti / 

[2.2] śacaṅante doṣaḥ / tannimittagrahaṇe sati atannimitto guṇaḥ prāpnoti, yathā śe riyatīti / nanu ca kriyamāṇe tannimittagrahaṇe 'doṣa eva / bahiraṅgo guṇaḥ, antaraṅgaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ, asiddhaṃ bahiraṅgam antaraṅge iti / vakṣyati caitat parihārāntaraṃ roravītīty atra / atredam evam upāttinimittaṃ varṇyate---yan manyase tannimittapakṣo 'duṣṭa iti, atrāpy asti doṣa iti /

[2.3] hataḥ, hathaḥ / pūrvapakṣo 'trābhimataḥ / evaṃ manyate, yathaiva ‘adiprabhṛtibhyaḥ śapaḥ’ (A 2.4.72) iti, ‘āyādaya ārdhadhātuke vā’ (A 3.1.31) iti vākyabhedena vihitāḥ santo nirvartyante, evam ayam apy akāro dhātupāṭha upadiṣṭaḥ kṅitsu nivartata iti / prasaktasyā[na]bhinirvṛttasyeti / kimarthaṃ dvayam ucyate, na punar anyatarad ucyeta? evamarthaṃ vaktavyam---yadi tāvat prasakto dviprakāro 'bhinirvṛtto 'nabhinirvāttaś ca / yady evam, anabhinirvṛttasyety evāstu / asāv api dviprakāraḥ, prasaktaś cāprasaktaś ca / tadmād yayavadhārmāmayātra(?) bhavitavyam iti prasaktaḥ / tady(?thā)nabhinirvṛttasyātmapratiṣedhaḥ pravartata iti ubhayam ucyate /

asyāpy ayam abhiprāyaḥ---iha kaścid guṇaḥ sann eva hato hatha ity evamādiṣu / kaścid vidheyaḥ kartādiṣu vinā pāṭhaṃ tṛci ity evamādiṣu / [ig] upadiṣṭas tasya guṇo vidhīyate tṛjādiṣu / sa ca punarnirvartyate kṅiti pratyaye / tatrāyaṃ pratiṣedho vidhīyamānasya bhāvād eva tam eva nivartayati, na bhūtam / iha tu naivānyaḥ śap [a]sti nāpy āyādaya iti santo 'pi nivartyāḥ / ataś cāha---prasaktasyānabhinirvṛttasyeti / 

upoyata iti / ubhayapadāśrayo guṇo bahiraṅgaḥ / evam api prasakto 'nabhinirvṛttaḥ śakyate pratiṣeddhuṃ nivṛtta iti (?) / [au]yata iti  / āṭo bahiraṅganimittatvāt vṛddhir api [bahi]raṅgā / lauyamā[ni]r iti / bāhyanimittam apekṣya vṛddhiḥ / anenija(?) iti / pareṇa rūpeṇa vyavahitatvāt / pūrvapakṣaḥ katham ? yathā pugantalaghūpadhasyeti / yasya guṇo vidhīyate tat kṅitparatvena viśeṣyata iti / uttarapakṣaḥ---abhyāsa[sya]guṇaḥ, sa ca vyavahita iti / 

[2.4] dhātuṃ kṅitparatvena viśeṣayiṣyāmaḥ / svaritakaraṇāt prakaraṇaprāpto dhātuḥ / tatrārtho vānumīyate, śabdo vā / artho 'numīyate 'saṃnihitārtho, yo 'tha śabdaṃ prayuṅkte / śabdo vānumīyamāno yenārtho 'numī(? rtho 'vasī)yate / iti saṃśabditāc ca anyad eva vākyāntaram upakramate pratipādakam iti / ‘kṅiti dhātor antarasya guṇo na bhavati’ iti idaṃ vākyam upajāyate /

yadi dhātur viśeṣyate vikaraṇasya na prāpnoti / ayam abhiprāyaḥ---naivamarthaṃ dhātugrahaṇam anuvartyate, vikaraṇasya mā bhūt pratiṣedha iti / asti tadanuvṛtteḥ prayojanam, anuvṛttisāmarthyāt śakyam apradhānasya viśeṣa[ṇa]m abhyupagantum iti / vihitaviśeṣa[ṇaṃ] dhātugrahaṇam / sopaskāratvāt sūtrasya vākyaśeṣo 'dhyāhriyate / dhātor yaḥ saṃbandhī tasmin na bhavati / kaś ca dhātoḥ saṃbandhī ? yas tasmād vihita iti / itaro dhātor vihitasya na bhavatīti imaṃ vākyaśeṣaṃ samarthy āha---dhātor eva tarhi na prāpnoti iti / i[ta]raḥ kṅitīty etasya tulyaviṣayavākyaśeṣatvaṃ samarthayate---naivaṃ vijñāyate iti /

athavā, kāryakālaṃ saṃjñāparibhāṣam iti / katham iyaṃ paribhāṣā ? ucyate / ‘guṇavṛddhī’ ity etad anuvartate / ato yatra guṇavṛddhī ‘guṇavṛddhī’ ityevaṃ vidhīyete tatrānenopasthātavyam iti / iha ca ‘pugantalaghūpadhasya’ (A 7.3.86) iti guṇo vidhīyate ity upasthātavyam anena / tatraikavākyabhāvāpannasya pugantalaghūpadhasya kṅiti neti vacanāt ‘yena nāvyavadhānaṃ tena vyavahite 'pi vacanaprāmāṇyāt’ iti pratiṣedho bhaviṣyati /

atha vā, yad etasmin yoge iti / yadyapi paribhāṣā na syād, evam api guṇavṛddhī apekṣitapratiṣedhe [pratiṣedhye] na pravartete / itarathā hi pravṛtte aśakye pratiṣeddhum iti / idam api kṅitīti pratiṣedharūpaṃ pratiṣedhyāpekṣam evānantaryaṃ pratipadyate / ato yāvantaḥ pratiṣedhyāḥ yathābhūtās tāvanti pratiṣedhasūtrāṇīti pugantalaghūpadhaguṇe pratiṣedhye 'navakāśaḥ pratiṣedha iti vyavahite 'pi pravartate /

anye varṇayanti---kāryakālaṃ saṃjñāparibhāṣaṃ pradarśitam / idānīṃ yahtoddeśe parihāra ucyate iti / pradhānāni bhinnāni guṇadeśam upasaṃpadyamānāni guṇabhedaṃ prayuñjate / tatra kṅitīty asya pratipradhānam imān yogān pratipadyāmahe /

atha vācāryapravṛttir jñāpayati iti / [i]daṃ liṅgam anantaraparibhāṣā nopatiṣṭhate guṇabhūtayor vṛddhiguṇayor viśeṣaṇam / yasya vā guṇa ucyate, sa kṅitparatvena viśeṣyata iti / 

[3] na lumatā tasmin iti / pāṭhāntaraṃ gṛhītvocyate / athāpi yathānyāsam, evam api lumatetītthaṃbhūtalakṣaṇe tṛtīyā / lumatā ca [ya]syāṅgatvaṃ lakṣitaṃ tasya kāryaṃ na bhavatīti / athāṅgasyeti ca svaryate evam api . . . kāryakālaṃ saṃjñāparibhāṣam iti taddeśam evedam / iha tu lāghavārtho 'syopadeśa iti / 

atha vā, pūrvasmin yoge yadārdhadhātukagrahaṇaṃ tadanavakāśam / nanu cobhayaviśeṣaṇārtham ārdhadhātukagrahaṇaṃ sāvakāśam ucyate / satyam / yadi tv asatyārdhadhātukagrahane bahuvrīhir asti yathā ‘ekāco dve . . .’ (A 6.1.1) ity atra, tat etad upapannam / yāvatī dhātulope pratiṣedhaprāptis tāvatī sārvadhātuke na bhavatīti / ye 'pi sāvakāśa[tva]m ārdhadhātukagrahaṇasya varṇayanti---iha mā bhūt johortu iti, [ta] evaṃ paryanuyojyāḥ---naitat hurchārūpam, juhoter eṣa ‘devā aduhra’vat ruḍāgama iti /

[4] iha kasmān na bhavati---laigavāyanaḥ, kāmayate / tad apavādapakṣe or guṇo 'pīgla[kṣa]ṇa iti / taddhitakāmyor ikla[kṣa]ṇāt / tad apavādapakṣaḥ parityaktaḥ / ṣaṣṭhīprādurbhavanaṃ taccheṣatvaṃ sthitam / tasmād iglakṣaṇo na bhavati / ‘ikaḥ’ iti vartate, ‘guṇavṛddhī’ ca / tena ‘guṇavṛddhī’ ity evaṃ ye guṇavṛddhī ikas te na bhavata iti / 

[5] lakārasya ṅittvād ādeśeṣu sthānivadbhāvaḥ (vā. 4) / sthānivadbhāvāt kāryam anapekṣitaśāstram ādeśaḥ pratipadyate, sthānivad guṇavṛddhī na bhavata ity etāvan māntram / atha vā, śāstram anapekṣitanimittam / kṅitīty etacchāstraṃ sthānivad ādeśe 'pi pravartate, kiṃ nimittam asti nāstīty etannāpekṣate / yadi vā vyapadeśo 'napekṣitārthasaṃnidhānaḥ, kim asya ṅakāro '[sti] nāstīti / 

sarvaprakāraś ca sthānivadbhāva iha nopapadyate, alvidhitvāt / yathā pradīvyeti valādutvābhāvaḥ /

yady evaṃ lyapi guṇaḥ prāpnoti / tasmād idam atra varṇyate---sthānyāśraye alyāśrīyamāṇe pratiṣedhaḥ / anubandhas tu sthānini naivāsti, yo hītsaṃjñaḥ sa iha nāstīti pratijñānāt / val tu saṃnihitaḥ sthānini, tena pratiṣedho bhavati /

evam api guṇeneha bhavitavyam / evaṃ hy uktaṃ tṛtīye 'dhyāye ‘pinna ṅit ṅic ca pinna bhavati’ iti / tatrācinavam iti ṅittvāt pittvaṃ na bhavati, pittvāc ca ṅittvaṃ na bhavati [i]ty ucchinnayoḥ pittvaṅittvaoyḥ sārvadhātukāśrayo guṇaḥ siddha eva / 

bhāṣyakārasyeyaṃ kalpanā na vākyakārasyeti idaṃ cedyate /

[5.1] yāsuṭo ṅidvacanāt siddham / liṅo ṅittvāt tadādeśā ṅita eva, teṣāṃ ca bhakto yāsuḍ iti siddhaṃ ṅittvam / paśyati tu [ācāryo] nāsti ṅittvaṃ sthānivadbhāvāt iti, ato yāsuṭo ṅittvam ārabhate / yady etat jñāpyate, kathaṃ ‘nityaṃ ṅitaḥ’ ‘itaś ca’ (A 3.4.99, 100) iti / atra codyate---na hi tatraivaṃ varṇyate, ṅita ādeśasya lopa iti / evaṃ hi vijñāyamāne pacāvaḥ, pacanti ity atra lopaḥ syāt / kathaṃ tarhi ? ṅito lakārasya yāni parasmaipadāni teṣām iti / tatra kathaṃ kṛtvocyate, ‘yady etat jñāpyate, kathaṃ ‘‘nityaṃ ṅitaḥ’’., ‘‘itaś ca’’ iti’ ? ayam abhiprāyaḥ---yāsuṭo ṅittvāt pravartamānaṃ pratiṣedhasya ṅittvaṃ supratiṣiddham ? yadi sthānina eva ṅittvavyapadeśa ucchidyate / nūnaṃ lakāra eva ṅid iti / vyapadeśaṃ na labhate, tena yāsuṭo ṅittvam ucyate iti / kimarthaṃ ṅitkaraṇaṃ caitat ? viśeṣakāryapratipattyartham---‘liṅaḥ sūyuṭ’ (A 3.4.102), ‘luṅ[laṅ]lṛṅkṣvaḍudāttaḥ’ (A 6.4.71) iti / 

ṅiti yat kāryaṃ tan na bhavati / ṅito yat kāryaṃ tad bhavati / sūtrārtho yathaiva ṅito lakārasya ādeśa iti tatra sthānivadbhāvo [na] bhavati, na tu vyapadeśa eva vyucchidyate / kuta etad iti cet---yāsuṭ eva ṅidvacanāt / anumānaṃ hi yan na śakyate kalpayituṃ tan na kalpayitavyam / na ca śakyate kalpayitum, yathājātī[ya]kaṃ kāryaṃ ṅiti, yāsuṭi tathājātīyakaṃ kāryaṃ na / nanu pravṛtta ādeśe ṅiti ikaḥ ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśo 'sti / yas tu ṅid iti vyapadeśas tatra nāsti sthānivadbhāva iti / evaṃ saty ubhayaṃ [anu]gṛhītaṃ bhavati---yāsuṭaś ṅittvam, idaṃ ca ‘nityaṃ ṅitaḥ’, ‘itaś ca’ iti / itarathā hi kvedaṃ syāt ? sarvo ṅittvavyapadeśa ucchidyeta liṅgtāt / kiṃ ca kāryaṃ yāsuṭi ? kṅitīti pratiṣedhaḥ saṃprasāraṇaṃ ca / tannnimittagrahaṇaṃ ca pari[tyaktuṃ] śakyam / ato yāsuṭo ṅittvaṃ tasyaivānumānaṃ sthānivadbhāvāt ṅittvaṃ nāstīti /

yas tu vyapadeśo lakārasya ṅit iti tasya nivṛttau liṅgam ity etad ācaṣṭe---aparyāptaś caiva hi yāsuṭ samudāyasya ṅittve / aparyāptaḥ asamarthaḥ / samudāyasya ṅittve kartavye asamarthaś cen nāsti yāsuṭo ṅittvena samudāye kṅitīti pratiṣedho guṇasya / asti ca sthānivadbhāvena ṅittvaṃ samudāyasyety anarthakaṃ yāsuṭo ṅittvam iti / 

nanu cāvayave hi kṛtaṃ liṅgaṃ samudāyasya viśeṣakaṃ bhavatīti paryāpta eva yāsuṭ samudāyasya ṅittve / ucyate / āgamā asamarthā iti śastre darśanam asti / ‘ṭyuṭyulau tuṭ ca’ (A 4.3.23) iti tuṭa eva ṭitkaraṇāt siddhe pratyayasya ṭittve kimarthaṃ pratyayau ṭitāv ucyete ? doṣaḥ khalv api syāt---agnicit brāhmaṇī iti / tuka ugittvāt samudāya ugiti ṅīp syāt / naiṣa doṣaḥ / ‘ugityañcatigrahaṇāt siddham adhātoḥ’ (A 4.1.6, vā. 3) ity eva na saṃbhaviṣyati ‘u[khā]srad brāhmaṇīti’ / iha tarhi ‘paṭhitā vidyā’ iti iṭaḥ ṭittvāt ṅī[p] prāpnoti / nyāyaś cāyaṃ yad anavakāśam avyaveti liṅgāt samudāyasya viśeṣakam āgamānāṃ tad eva vidhau caritārthaṃ tāvan mātraṃ ca / śakyete saṃprasāraṇaguṇapratiṣedhau kartum itīdam ucyate---aparyāptaś caiva [hi] yāsuṭ samudāyasya ṅittve / 

[5.2] idam iti apare varṇayanti / ‘nityaṃ ṅitaḥ’ ity ayam evārtho ṅitām ādeśānām idaṃ kāryam iti / pacāvaḥ, pacanti iti kasmān na bhavatīti ? idaṃ tatroktaṃ sārvadhātukam apit ṅi[dva]d iti / ṅitīva ṅidvad iti saptamyarthe vatir bhavati [i]ti pūrvasya yo 'tideśas tatredaṃ yāsuṭo ṅidvacanam / ekatra sthānivadbhāvāt asti vyapadeśo ṅita iti ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśaḥ / yas tu ṅitīti vyapadeśaḥ tatra nāsti sthānivadbhāvaḥ, ity evaṃ saty ubhayam anugṛhītaṃ bhavati, yāsuṭaś ca ṅittvam idaṃ ca ‘nityaṃ ṅitaḥ’ iti / itarathā hi kvedaṃ syāt (akṅita iti) ? yadi sarvo ṅidvyapadeśo liṅgād ucchidyeta ity etad anumīyate /

[5.3] anyeṣāṃ granthaḥ / ‘a(?su)paryāptaścaiva hi yāsuṭ samudāyasya ṅittve’ / a(?su)paryāptaś caivaṃ hi yāsuṭ bhaktatvāt / atha vā yāsyṭsamydāyasya ṅittve suparyāptaḥ. loṅ iti vākyaśeṣaḥ / ṅitaṃ cainaṃ karoti / ka[sya? -thaṁ?] liṅgam ? evaṃ tarhy ayam e[tad e]vābhyupagacchati---yāsuṭ parasmaipadeṣūdātto ṅic ca liṅ bhavatīti / 

apare varṇayanti / udātto ṅic cety anena idam atidiśyate---ṅitīva cāsmin kāryāṇi bhavantīti / yadi ca sthānivadbhāvāt ṅitīti vyapadeśaḥ kāryaṃ vā labhyetān arthakaṃ ṅitkaraṇam syād iti / 

dīrghīvevīṭām // 1.1.6 //

[1] dīrghīvevyo(ḥ cha)ndoviṣayatvāt // vā. // etayos tāvad bhāṣāyāṃ nāsti prayogaḥ / 

dṛṣṭānuvidhitvāc ca // vā. // nanu ca bhāṣāyām api dṛṣṭam evānuvidhīyate, ‘lokato 'rthaprayukte śabdaprayoge’ iti / tatrāyam artho varṇyate---dṛṣṭānuvidhi[ś ca] chandasi bhavati / a[tha] na cedam udāharaṇaṃ chandasy asti, ādīdhya[nam], ādīdhyaka iti / niyataśabdasaṃbandhanāni hi cchandāṃsi / atha vā dṛṣṭānuvidhitvād iti / yathaiva guṇo na dṛṣṭa ity anuvidhānaṃ kriyate, evaṃ guṇo 'pi dṛṣyate, tadartham anuvidhānaṃ kartavyam / atha manyase, kathaṃ jñāyate guṇo dṛśyata iti ? ataḥ pratipādayati adīdhet, adīdhayur iti (vā.) / na (? sa) ca guṇo dṛśyate ity ato guṇadarśanād iti / atha vā guṇadarśanād iti evaṃprakārāṇy anyāny api cchandāṃsi dṛṣṭāni, teṣāṃ cānuvidhānaṃ kṛtam, tanmadhya evāyam api prakṣeptavya iti / kiṃ punas tad uktam ? bahulavacanam / adīdhet, adīdhayur iti ca guṇo 'pi dṛśyate / ayaṃ ca yogārāmbhe sati [vacanā]dṛte na sidhyati / adīdhed iti laṅo vyatyayena parasmaipadaṃ bhavati / evam adīdhayur iti ca guṇo 'pi dṛśyate / 

‘‘prajāpatir vai yat kiṃcana manasādīdhet / tadādhītayajurbhir evāpnot, tadādhītayajuṣām ādhītayajuṣṭvam’’ (Maitrāyaṇī-Saṃhitā, 3.6.4) /

etannirukte dhyāyater varṇyate / ayaṃ hi tatra vyākhyānahranthaḥ---‘‘prajāpatir vai yat kiṃcana manasādhyāyat tad etair āptavān’’ iti / tathā---hotrāya vṛtaḥ kṛpayan nadīdhet (ṚV 10.98.7) iti / ayam api dhyā[ya]ter eva / ‘‘hotrāya vṛtaḥ kṛpāyamāṇo 'nvadhyāyat’’ (Ni. 2.12) iti / evaṃ ca sati dīdhīṅaḥ ko 'bhisaṃbandho yad ucyate guṇadarśanād iti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / dīdhīṅa evai[tad] rūpam, arthas tu dhātvantareṇa tadarthenākhyāyate / yathā kṣaṇoteḥ kṣṇautinārtho varṇyate---‘kṣaṇaḥ kṣaṇoteḥ, prakṣṇutaḥ kālo bhavati’ (ni, 2.25) / kṣṇautiḥ kṣayakarmā iti / 

[2] bhaved idaṃ yuktam udāharaṇam yogapratyākhyāne / idaṃ tu saty api yoge naiva duṣyati---adīdhayur iti / ayaṃ jusi guṇaḥ pratiṣedhaviṣayam avagāhata iti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / tulyajātīyaṃ bādhate / kaś ca tulyajātīyaḥ ? pratyayāśrayaḥ / tena hi tasya tulyakālatā / ayaṃ tv anāśritya pratyayaṃ prakṛtimātrāśrayaḥ pratiṣedho 'ntaraṅgatvāt pravṛttaḥ katham avarakālena jusi guṇena bādhyata iti ? syāt mataṃ---pratiṣedho na pratyayam antareṇeti / yady apy evaṁ tathāpi śabdavad upādānaṃ pratyayasya nāsti / 

atha vā, yena nāprāpta iti / yena nāptāpte=ye[na] nāprasakte, yena vā hetunā taṃ vidhir bādhate / kutaḥ ? taṃ pratyanavakāśatvāt / yena tu prāpte cāprāpte ca taṃ prati sāvakāśatvaṃ bhavati, yo viṣayo nāvaṣṭabdhaḥ sa viṣayas tasyeti / etat tu mithaḥ śāstrapravṛttyapekṣāyāṃ saṃbhavati / yadā tu yugapat anekaśāstrapratisaṃdhā[naṃ] [bha]vati tadā [anekaśāstrapravṛttiḥ] yugapadapekṣa[? -kṣya]te / tadānavakāśatvāt sarvam eva bādahte / yadāpi kāryamātraparigrahas tadāpi sarvo viṣayaḥ kāryeṇāvaṣṭabdha iti bādhakatvam eva bhavati /

[3] kathaṃ dīdhyad iti / ekadeśa udāharaṇatvenopanyas taḥ / kvacit tu nipūrvasya prayogaḥ---‘aindraḥ prāṇo aṅge aṅge ni dhīdhyat’ (Maitrāyaṇī Saṃhitā 1.2.17) iti / asati yoge guṇaḥ prāpnoti letavyam iti (? dīdhayad iti) / dīdhyad iti [ca] śyan vyatya[ye]na (vā. 2) / śyanonittvād ādyudāttatvam, ṅittvād guṇapratiṣedho, ‘yīvarṇayoḥ’ (A 7.4.53) iti lope, nighātaś ca ‘tiṅṅatiṅaḥ’ (A 8.1.28) iti / evaṃ śe 'pi ṅittvāt guṇe pratiṣiddhe, nighāte, yaṇādeśe ca tulyam / evaṃ ca kecit paṭhanti---dīdhyad iti [ca] ‘śa’ vyatyayeneti / yeṣāṃ śab vyatyayeneti pāṭhaḥ, teṣām api guṇaḥ prāpnoti / 

atheha kathaṃ, ‘manasā dīdhyajjuhoti’ (Mai-Saṃ 3.6.4) iti ? śatrantam etat / atra yadi śyan kriyate numā bhavitavyam, ‘ugidacām . . .’ (A 7.1.70) iti, yas tasya (? abhyastasya) vyavadhānāt / svaras tu nittvāt siddhaḥ / atha śaḥ kriyate nāsti doṣaḥ, ya[smā]c chatur ‘adupadeśāt’ (A 6.1.186) iti nighāte, ekādeśe ca kṛte, numaḥ pratiṣedho ‘nābhyastāc chatuḥ’ (A 7.1.78) iti / 

athāpi ‘śabd vyatyayena’ iti, evam api vyatyaya ity evaṃ varṇyate---śapo vyatyayena=śapo vyatigamanena ‘adiprabhṛtibhyaḥ śapaḥ’ (A 2.4.72) iti, sidhyati / tasya hi vyatigame 'sti śatrānantaryam iti / 

[4] iḍ iti vartamāne neḍ vaśi kṛti (A 7.2.8) iti, punar iḍgrahaṇam evamartham---[iḍ] iḍ eva, rūpāntaraṃ na pratipadyate / avikṛtarūpo 'vatiṣṭhata iti / 

āṅgaṃ yat kāryaṃ tanniyamyate / aṅgasyety etad anuvartate / tena ‘aṅgasya’ iti yad ucyate tatra na iḍrūpaṃ nivartata iti / kathaṃ grahītā ? va[ca]nād bhavati / kathaṃ pipaṭhīṃṣi brāhmaṇakulāni ? aṅga[bhya] iti niyamāt ‘sāntamahataḥ’ (A 6.4.10) iti dīrghatvaṃ na prāpnoti / nanu ca numaivātra [na] bhavitavyam, ‘napuṃsakasya jhalacaḥ’ (A 7.1.72) iti / (numi kṛte) tatra ato lope (A 6.4.48) kṛte jhalantatā nāsti, sthānivadbhāvāt / evaṃ tarhi ajanta ity evaṃ bhaviṣyati / idam api midaco 'ntyāt parasyaiva num iti apūrvavidhiḥ / (evam api) ikāro 'ntyo na bhavati sthānivadbhāvāt iti nāsti ‘midaco 'ntyāt paraḥ’ (A 1.1.47) iti / tasmāt ‘pipaṭhiṣi brahmaṇakulāni’ iti bhavitavyam / 

atha vāsiddhaṃ dīrghatvam, tasyāsiddhatvāt niyamo na bhaviṣyati / asmin śāstre dīrghatvasyābhāvāt avikṛtarūpa evāyaṃ, prayoge eva vikṛtarūpa iti / tatra tv ayaṃ doṣo dṛśyate---alāvīt, apāvīt iti na prāpnoti / syān matam---iḍaniṭor ayam ādeśa iti / tac ca na / iha dvayoḥ ṣaṣṭhīnirdiṣṭaor yaḥ sthāne bhavati, labhate 'sāv anyataravyapadeśam / tasmāt idam eva jyāyo dṛśyate, āṅgaṃ yatkāryaṃ tanni[ya]myata iti /

halo 'nantarāḥ saṃyogaḥ // 1.1.7 //

[1] katham idaṃ vijñāyate ? kutaḥ praśnaḥ ? śabdāśrayas tāvad anupapannaḥ / anantaraśabdasya śrutatvāt / anekārthatvāt antaraśabdasya praśnaḥ / ayaṃ hi ya[pa]dyāty avivarapradeśabahir yogopasaṃvyāneṣu dṛṣṭaḥ iti / eṣāṃ saṃbhavāt vivarārtha eveti nāyaṃ praśnaḥ /

anantarā iti katham idaṃ vijñāyate ? avidyamānam antaraṃ yeṣām / antaram, vivaram, chidram, madhyam iti paryāyāḥ / kiṃbhūtaṃ punaḥ śabdasya vivaram ? varṇaśūnyo yo 'ntarālakālaḥ / āhosvid avidyamānā antarā eṣām iti / atra dvitīyā prāpnoti, tasyā uttaratra parihāraṃ vakṣyāmaḥ / keṣāṃcid granthaḥ---avidyamānam antaraṃ yeṣām i[ti] / asmin dvitīyasyā apravṛttir eva /

kiṃ punar idaṃ vigrahapadaṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ paṭhitavyam ? nety āha / kiṃ tarhi ? etad asau pṛcchati---dvau imau śabdau staḥ ‘halo 'nantarā’ iti / eko 'vastubhūtenādhikaraṇena viśeṣite anyapadārthe vartate / anyo 'pi [a]vyāvṛttenādhikaraṇenā[nā]dheyākāṅkṣeṇa / atha vā eka evāyaṃ śabdo 'nekaśaktiḥ / lakṣyānurodhāt kaḥ śabdaḥ āśritaḥ kiṃśaktir veti / 

avagrahe saṃyogasaṃjñā na prāpnoti / apsv ity ap 'su iti / vidyate hy atrāntaram / ardhamātrākālo hy avagraha iṣyate / ardhamātrākālaṃ sthitvā varṇāntaram uccāryate / kaḥ punar doṣaḥ syāt ? ‘guror nṛto 'nantyasua’ (A 8.3.86) iti pluto na syāt / nanu cādhikaraṇavācino nāsti saṃbandho guror anṛtādibhiḥ / evaṃ tarhi apsavya ity atra na syāt / 

yathā na doṣas tathāstu / śiṣṭaprayogānuvidhāyi idaṃ śāstram / tatra yathā śiṣṭhapravṛttir na virudhyate, tathāśrīyata iti / naiva doṣo na prayojanam / doṣanivṛttyarthaṃ vā saṃyogasaṃjñāśrīyeta, prayojanapravṛttyarthaṃ vā / na cātrāpravartamānāyāṃ doṣo nāpi pravartamānāyāṃ prayojanam iti naivātra saṃyogasaṃjñā pravartate / nanu coktam---avagrahe pluto na syāt / apsavya iti / naitasminn avagraham icchanti / vyākaraṇe 'py ekapade saṃhitāvidheyasyāvagrahasya lakṣaṇam eva nāsti / kevalaṃ hi saṃhitā cāsaṃhitā ca vyākaraṇe / 

anye varṇayanti---yathā paramavācā iti vākchabdasya ‘tadādi subantaṃ ca’ (MaBhā 1.167.1--2) ity anuvṛttyā bhasaṃjñā, evam apśabdasya ‘yaci’ iti / tan na / vyavadhānāt / vyavahito 'trāpśabdo vākśabdas tv anantara iti /

idaṃ[?ha] tarhi [na] prāpnoti, apsuyoniḥ, apsumatir iti / ‘apo yoniyanmatiṣu’ (A 6.3.1, vā. 7) iti aluki kṛte / atrāpi nāsti ava[gra]haḥ / evaṃ hi paṭhyate---‘samāsapadānām anto 'vagṛhyate’ iti / samāsapadaṃ ca saha supā, nāpśabda iti / 

[2] saṃyogasaṃjñā[yāḥ] ca śāstre (ca) pratyekaṃ saṃbandho 'nyatra sahavacanāt / ānantaryaṃ tūpalakṣaṇārtham, ānantaryeṇopalakṣitāḥ pratyekaṃ saṃjñāṃ pratipadyeran / yathānantaryeṇāvasthitebhyo brāhmaṇebhyaḥ pādyamācamanīyaṃ dīyatām ity ānantaryeṇopalṣitāḥ pratyekaṃ pratipādyante, yat tv asaṃbhavi tat sādhāraṇyena pratipadyante / ‘kanyā dīyatāmebhyaḥ’ iti jñāyate dāsī iyam ebhyo dīyatām iti / 

yathānyatrāpi / yadi samāsasaṃjñāyāṃ sahagrahaṇaṃ na kriyate, tato 'yam arthaḥ syāt---‘ṣaṣṭhī’ (A 2.2.8) ‘samāsaḥ’ (A 2.1.3) iti ṣaṣṭhyantasya yadā subantena saha prayogas tadā samāsasaṃjñā pravartata iti sarvasya na syāt / dvayos tu prayoge pratyekaṃ syāt / ṛkpādaḥ, rājāśva iti pratyekaṃ samāsāntaḥ prāpnoti, svaraś ca / evam ubhegrahaṇe sahārthe akriyamāṇe dve abhyastasaṃjñe pratyekaṃ syātām / evam ihānantaryaṃ naikasya dharmo yathāntādī / kiṃ tarhi ? dvayor ayaṃ dharmo, dvayoś cānantaryeṇopalakṣitayoḥ antareṇa saha vacanaṃ saṃyogasaṃjñā pratyekaṃ prāpnoti / 

kiṃ ca syāt ? ayam abhiprāyaḥ---ekasyāpi tu saṃyogasaṃjñāyāṃ pūrvasya ‘saṃyoge guru’ (A 1.4.11) iti siddhā gurusaṃjñeti / niryāyād ity etvaṃ prasajyeta, rephayakārayor ānantaryāt pratyekaṃ saṃyogasaṃjñeti yakāreṇa saṃyogādyaṅgaṃ bhavati iti / kiṃ nivartitam ? yāyādvāyād iti / atha samudāye saṃyogasaṃjñāyāṃ satyāṃ dvirvacane ‘aco rahābhyām . . .’ (A 8.4.46) iti kṛte prāpnoti / asiddhatvān na bhaviṣyati saṃyogasaṃjñeti yathoddeśe parihāraḥ / 

iha ca dṛṣatkarotīti / saṃyogaḥ ante yasya iti lopaḥ prasajyeta iti / vyapadeśivadbhāvena vā /

iha ca śaktā vasteti / saṃyogādilopaḥ prasajyeta / kakārataāra[yoḥ sakāratakāra]yoś cānantarye sati sakārakakārau saṃyogau cādī ca saṃyogādī bhavataḥ / athāpi saṃyogayor ādī iti, bhavataḥ sakayos ta[? sakau ta]kārayor ādī / 

saṃyogāvādī yasya so 'yaṃ saṃyogādir iti / nanu copasarjanaśabdasya saṃkhyāviśeṣopādānaṃ vṛttau nāsti ity uktam / satyam evaitat / kiṃ tu pañcake prātipadikārthe yāsau prātipadikopādhibhūtā vibhaktivyaṅgyā saṃkhyā, sā vṛttau nāśrīyate / śrūyamāṇāyām api ca vibhaktau na vivakṣyate, dāsyāḥputra iti / kva cit tu gamyata eva saṃkhyāviśeṣo, māsajātaḥ, śaurpikaḥ iti / parimāṇeṣūpasarjaneṣv aktaparimāṇasyaiva ete vācakā iti / kva cit ādeśarūpeṇaiva vyajyante, tāvakīno māmakīna iti / evam ihādiśabdopādānādāśninaḥ saṃkhyāviśeṣavyāpāraḥ / katham ? etāvat vaktavyam ‘saṃyogād āta’ iti / aṅgasya yaḥ saṃyogas tasmāt paro ya ākāraḥ tasyaitvaṃ bhavatīti / so 'yam ādiśabdaḥ evamartham upādīyate, asmin vākya upasarjanapade āśritaḥ saṃkhyāviśeṣaḥ iti / yadi ca samartho bhavati upasarjane tat ādiśabdo 'rthaprakaraṇasthānīyaḥ śaknoti pratipādatitum iti / yathā ‘sārasvatau bhavataḥ’ iti kṛtaikaśeṣād ayaṃ taddhita iti liṅgād avasīyate---‘mithunaṃ ha vā etad yad uta sarasvatī ca sarasvāṃś ca’ iti / 

atha vā aṅgasya (A 6.4.1) ity ucyate ‘āta’ iti ca, na ca liṅo yakāramātram asty aṅgaṃ saṃyogāt param ity evaṃ vijñāyate---ihāntaryaṃ nopalakṣaṇa[ta]yopanyastam / kiṃ tarhi ? parigṛhītānāntaryau saṃyogatvenāśritāv iti / niryāyād iti pūrvaḥ saṃyogo 'vayavo na bhavatīti nāsty etvam / 

atha vā, etat pratipadyāmahe---ādigrahaṇāt saṃyoga ity eṣa śabda ānantaryasyehopalakṣaṇam / yathā vadvidhinasbhāvādiṣūpasargaśabdaḥ prādyupalakṣaṇam, na tu teṣāṃ saṃjñbhāvaḥ prādīnāṃ vadvidhinasbhāvāduṣu, evam ihāpi saṃyogāder iti saṃyoga upalakṣaṇam ānantaryasyeti evam etad vijñāyate---aṅgasya saṃbandhinau anantarau yatrādī tatrākārasyaitvam iti saṃkhyāviśeṣagrahaṇam ādiśabdāt pratīyate---ānantaryā(t) tatkaraṇam upalakṣaṇam eva vā / 

saṃyogāvantāvasyeti / atrāpi tathaiva ‘saṃyogasya padasya’ iti tadantavidhau sati siddho lopo 'ntasya / tatrāntaśabdopādānādāśritaḥ saṃkhyāviśeṣa iti gamyate, ānantaryaṃ vābhyantarīkṛtam, upalakṣaṇaṃ vā saṃyogaśabdaḥ padasya saṃbandhināvānantarāv iti / 

naivaṃ vijñāyate, saṃyogāvādī saṃyogādī saṃyogādyor iti / kakārasakārabhedāt sa dvivacanena pradarśyate / atha kimarthaṃ prathamayā vinirdiśyate, na ṣaṣṭhyā---saṃyogasyādī saṃyogādī iti ? evaṃ manyate---ādiśabdo 'vayavavacanaḥ / yathā padāṅgādis tadavayava iti gamyate, evaṃ śaktetitakārasya saṃyogasya nāvayavaḥ kakāraḥ / ataḥ prathamopādīyate, saṃyogāvādī iti / saṃyogatvenāyamādirayaṃ pratham iti / 

[saṃyogayor ādī /] avayavādiśabdaḥ samīpavacano bhaviṣyati, saṃyogasyādiḥ samīpa iti / yady evaṃ, saṃyogayor ādī ete iti vijñāyamāne iha na prāpnoti---kāṣṭhataḍ iti / na hy atra tṛtīya ādibhūto 'sti / iha tv asti---taṣṭavyam iti, ṣakāra[ṭa]kārayor ādiḥ tṛtīyaḥ kakāra iti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / jhalī[ti] takārasya nimittatvenāśritatvāt saṃyogayor ādir ity anantarbhāvaḥ / ihāpi kāṣṭhataḍiti anta ity etannimittam / tasmāt sakārakakārayoḥ saṃyogayoḥ jhali padānte cāde(? -dyo)r lopa ity eva sūtrārthaḥ / yady evaṃ, saṃyogasyādir ity atrāpi na doṣaḥ / śakteti takāraḥ saṃyogasyādir ity ādiviśeṣaṇa upakṣīṇo nimittatvena na pravartate, na cānyo jhalastīti na bhaviṣyati lopaḥ / tatredam evānenopāyenoktaṃ bhavati, ‘skoḥ saṃyogādyoḥ’ (A 8.2.29) ity atra nopasarjanapadaṃ prathamāntam / kiṃ tarhi ? ṣaṣṭhyantam iti / niryāto nirvāta iti / atrāpi dhātugrahaṇāt ādigrahaṇāc ca tathaiva kalpayitavyam / 

[2.1] kathaṃ kṛtvā ekaikasyeti / ayam abhiprāyaḥ---nyāyād a[vi]śiṣṭam etat / ānantaryaṃ ca saṃyogasaṃjñāyāṃ śrutaṃ kim iti nābhyantarīkriyate ? yathā ‘ānaya tau [śuklavāsasau’ iti] śukle vāsasī antarbhavataḥ / na ca nāsti saṃbhavo yatrābhyantarīkriyate, lakṣyasya ca loka evaṃbhūtasya dṛṣṭatvāt / yathā śatadaṇḍana evaṃbhūtam eva śatarūpaṃ yad abhīpsitaṃ tad eva pravartate, na gargāḥ [pratyekaṃ] śataṃ daṇḍyatnte / evam ānantaryam apy evaṃrūpam eva yadābhyantarīkriyate saṃyogasaṃjñāyām iti / 

[3] atha yatra bahūnām ānantaryam, kiṃ tatra dvayor dvayoḥ [saṃyogasaṃjñā bhavati], āhosvit aviśeṣeṇa ? ko 'trāviśeṣo 'bhipretaḥ ? prakaraṇāviśeṣo, yat prakṛtaṃ dvayor ity eṣa viśeṣo nāśrīyate saṃyogasaṃjñāyām / eṣa cen nāsti, bahūnām iti / uttaratra śabdāviśeṣṃ vakṣyati---‘atha vā punar astu aviśeṣeṇa, dvayor bahūnāṃ ceti’ / bahūnām ānantarye ca kathaṃ bahūnām eva manyate, na dvayoḥ ? ucyate / yathaiva dvirvacane nimittavatsv avayaveṣu samudāye ca samudāya eva dvirvacanaṃ pratipadyate, nāvayavāḥ / atha caikāj vyapadeśaḥ sarveṣāṃ kathaṃ (? yathā) bhavati, pakārasyākārasya, [acchabdasya,] pacchabdasya ca, pacchabda eva [tu] dvir ucyate / evam ihāpi bahavaś cānantarāḥ ṣaṣṭarāḥ uṣṭra iti, ṣaṭāv apy anantarau ṭarāv api / saty api tv ānantarye nimitte ca bahubhir eva pratipannā, na dvābhyām iti / yatra tu dvāv evānantaray na tayor nivartyate saṃyogasaṃjñā / dvayos tu katham ity uttaratra pratipādayiṣyati / 

[3.1] samydāye saṃyogādilopo masjeḥ / numi kṛte trayāṇām ānantaryam / nasajāḥ saṃyogasaṃjñā, na nasau nāpi majāv iti / sasjeḥ sakāralopo na prāpnoti, asaṃyogāditvāt / 

[3.2] iha ca nirgleyāt nirmleyāt . . . ity etvaṃ na prāpnoti / yo 'tra saṃyogo nā[sā]vaṅgasya kaścid api, yaś cā[ṅga]sya nāsau saṃyoga iti / kva tarhi syāt ? gleyāt mleyād iti / 

[3.3] haloḥ saṃyoga iti cet dvirvacanaṃ na sidhyatīti / indidrīyiṣati ity atra hi dvau saṃyogau, ekasya nakāra ādir aparasyādir dakāra ity ubhayoḥ pratiṣedhaḥ prāpnoti ‘na ndrāḥ . . .’ (A 6.1.3) iti /

[3.3.1] na vājvidheḥ / evaṃ hy uktam ‘dvitīyasyeti na vaktavyam, ājāder iti pañcamī’ (A 6.1.2, vā. 1) iti / ihāpi ajāder iti vartate / tena ya evāsau varṇo 'nantaraḥ saṃyogādis tasya pratiṣedho, na dakārasyeti / 

[4] āhosvit anyatrasmin pakṣe bhūyaḥ sūtraṃ kartavyam iti / ekas tv atra nyāyya eva pakṣo bahūnāṃ saṃyogasaṃjñeti / itarasmiṃs tu pakṣe yatnaḥ kartavyaḥ yadi tathā lakṣyaṃ [na] sidhyati / gatam ity āha / ayam abhiprāyaḥ---saty api bahūnāṃ saṃbhave yadi tathābhūto 'rtha ihopātto yo 'ty antam asaṃbhavī bahūnāṃ, dvayor dvayor bhavitavyam iti / 

tadaivaṃ vigrahaḥ kariṣya[te], avidyamānam antaram eṣām iti / anantaraśabdo 'vivaravācī / bahavaś cānantarye 'vivarāḥ / acchidrau dvāv apy avivarau / tayos tu dvirvacana[vat] saṃyogasaṃjñā na pravartate / na tv evaṃ [vi?]grahītavyam, eṣām iti bahuvacanopādānāt dravyāśrayam etad bahuvacanam / itarathā hi dvayor na syāt / yadā [dvayor] dvayos tadaivaṃ vigrahaḥ kariṣyate---avidyamānā antarā eṣām iti / adhikaraṇārthavṛtto 'ntarāśabda upādīyate / yaś cāsau madhye bhavati nāsau bahūnām / kiṃ tarhi ? dvayoḥ / yadā ca na bhavati tadā dvayor eva, iti yayor eva sāntaratvam asāntaratvaṃ vāsti, tayor eva saṃjñayā bhavitavyam iti / etam atham ācaṣṭe---dvayoś caivāntarā kaścid vidyate / yayor eva vidyate antarā tayor eva tadabhāve saṃjñayā pravartitavyam ity āha---na veti / 

[4.1] kathaṃ punar atra ṣaṣṭhī, yāvatā ‘antarāntareṇa yukte’ (A 2.3.4) iti dvitīyā prāpnoti ? ucyate / yadāntariṇau bhedenopādīyete ‘tvāṃ ca māṃ ca’, ‘gārhapatyaṃ cāhavanīyaṃ ca’ iti, tadāntarirūpeṇa pravartamāno dvitīyāṃ pratipadyate / yadā tat sajya bhedaṃ saṃbandhirūpeṇa pravṛttiḥ, tadāntarirūpeṇānabhidhānāt na jñāyate dvayor antarety ukte kim anyena saha dvayor āhosvit tayor eva---iti ṣaṣṭhī kṛtā, ba dvitīyā / yady evam, iha katham ‘tadvācinaḥ śabdasya prayogam antareṇa’ (MaBhā 1.242.3) iti  ? tathā ‘antareṇa varārohām’ iti ? ucyate / ayam antareṇaḍabdo vināśabdārthavacano madhyavacanaś ca / ubhayārthenāpi yoge dvitīyā vidhīyate ‘antarāntareṇa yukte’ (A 2.3.4) iti / 

[4.2] evam api vahūnām eva prāpnoti / yān hi bhavān atra ṣaṣṭhyā pratinirdiśati / kecid eṣam iti bahuvacanopādānāt bahūnām eva bhavitavyam iti varṇayanti / tad ayuktam, ānantaryopalakṣaṇapare vākye saṃkhyāyā avivakṣitatvāt / yatpratyā(? yāvatpratyā)yyānantaryam ity etāvat vi[va]kṣitam / itarathā hi asvādiṣu na syāt / tasmād etenaitat pratipādayati---avidyamānā antarā yeṣām iti, yo 'sāv antarā iti sa dvayoś ca bahūnāṃ [ca] / na ced bahūnāṃ, kimarthaṃ teṣām anantarāṇāṃ saṃyogasaṃjñā na pravartate ? trayāṇāṃ hy ādyantau vyavahitau, na tu [madh]yaḥ / tatra yadi bahūnām atulyajātīyo 'ntarā syāt, na pravartate bahūnām / 

antyāt pūrvo masjer iti / tac cāvaśyaṃ vaktavyam, anuṣaṅgalopārtham, magna iti / 

[4.3] atha vā, aviśeṣeṇa saṃyogasaṃjñā vijñāsyate / dvayor api bahūnām iti / kathaṃ kṛtvedam ucyate, yāvato 'ktaṃ samudāyasyai[va] bhavitavyaṃ saṃyogasaṃjñayā nāvayavānām iti, yathā samudāyasyaikāco dvirvacanaṃ nāvayavānām iti ? eṣo 'bhiprāyaḥ---yathā samudāye dvir ucyamāne avayavā api dvir uccāritā bhavanti / ayaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ, ekaḥ pratyakṣato 'paro 'numānataḥ / kāryapratipattis tu [tu]lyā / evam iha dvirvacanavat yadi samudāyadvārakaṃ kāryam avayavānām api, atha saṃyogakāryaṃ na pratipadyante sahabhūtānām avayavā iti dṛṣṭānto dvirvacanaṃ na bhavati / tatra samudāyadvārakaṃ kāryam apratipadyamānā arthino 'vayavāḥ saṃyogasaṃjñāyāḥ sati nimitte kimiti na pratipadyanta iti / yathā ca dvirvacane virodhe na śakyam avayavasya kriyamāṇe dvirvacane avayavāntarasya samudāyasya ca kartum aco 'pahṛtatvāt, evaṃ saṃyogasaṃjñāyāṃ nāsti virodhaḥ / asti hi saṃbhavo yat samudāyasya ca syād avayavānāṃ ceti / 

[4.4] yad apy ucyata iti / ayaṃ grantho 'smād anantaraṃ yuktarūpo dṛśyate ‘vakṣyaty etat antyāt pūrvo masjer iti’ (A 1.1.47, vā. 2) / 

kiṃ punar ayaṃ bahūnām eva saṃyogasaṃjñā ity etasya parihāraḥ ? athāviśeṣeṇa saṃyogasaṃjñeti ? ekas tāvat parihāraḥ, bahūnām evābhyupagamyāyaṃ parihāra iti / tat katham iva samudāue 'kāryabhāji[ni ?] avayavā na labhanate ? samudāyaś caijiti vā nirdeśyate sya(?) atha vā svarūpeṇaiva eoṅ aiauj iti / iha tv anavadhāritā halo 'nantarāḥ saṃyogasaṃjñābhājo nirdiśyante / tatrāṅgasya saṃyogādeḥ (iti) āśrīyamāṇe trivyañjanaviṣayamānantaryamanāśrīyamāṇaṃ katham iva dvayor ānantaryanimittāyāḥ saṃyogasaṃjñāyāḥ pratibandhe vartiṣyata iti / anye varṇayanti, sarvam idaṃ bhāṣyaṃ parapakṣoccāraṇamātre nirdiṣṭam / yad uktaṃ bhavatā---aṅgena saṃyogo viśeṣyate, yaś cātra saṃyogo nāsāv aṅgasya, [yo 'ṅgasya] sa saṃyogo na bhavatīti / etad asat / kutaḥ ? yasmād aviśeṣeṇa saṃyogasaṃjñā dvayoś ca bahūnāṃ ceti / apare varṇayanti---anena granthena etad upapādayati, yad eva bhavaā aprāptinimittam uktam aṅgasya saṃyogaviśeṣaṇatvād iti, tad eva me prāptinimittam / vidyate hy atrāṅgasya saṃyogāditvam, saṃyogasaṃjñāyā aviśeṣeṇa pravṛtter iti / 

[5] svarānantarhitā halaḥ sāṃyoga[saṃjñā bhavantīti] paṭhitavyam / itarthā hi ajbhir vyavahitānām api prāpnoti saṃyogasaṃjñā / pacati, panasam iti / hala iti vacanāt sāckānāṃ nāsti prasaṅgaḥ / halām eva tu prāpnoti / tatra pacatīti rūpodāharaṇam / idaṃ tu kāryodāharaṇaṃ, panasam iti / kecin manyante prapāṭho 'yam iti / apare, vākyam i[ti] / etasmin halāṃ saṃyogasaṃjñāyāṃ satyāṃ yāvat saṃyogasaṃjñākāryaṃ tāva[t] . . . /

[5.1] [anan]ta rāv imau grāmāv iti / saty api vyavadhāne nadyā parvatena vā, anantaravyapadeśaṃ labhete / evam ihāpi halo vyavahitā api labhante anantaratvam iti kartavyaṃ svarānantarhitavacanam / anantaravacanam idānīṃ kimarthaṃ syāt ? eṣo 'bhiprāyaḥ---halāvacantarāv ity ucyamāne bahūnāṃ naiva syāt / bahūnāṃ cāvya[va]dhānāt pravartata eva saṃyogasaṃjñā / dvayor api vyavahitayor api bhavitavyam iti bravīṣi / tatra 

[5.1.1] kimartham anantaravacanam iti ? ekapratiṣedhārtham / anantaravacanena viṣayaḥ paricchidyate / yatrānantaryam ity etad asti sa viṣayo vijṣeyaḥ saṃyogasaṃjñāyā ity eko hal paryudasto bhavati / tato 'rthavacanantaravacanam / 

iheṣeti / ām prasajyeta / [guru]madvacanam idānīṃ [kimarthaṃ syāt ?] yaḥ śuddha ijā[diḥ] iṇ gatāv iti tannivṛttyartham / 

[5.2] na vātajjātīyavyavāyāt (vā. 8) / yo hi bhinnajātīyaḥ vyavadhāyako 'sau bhavati / lokato 'sya dṛṣṭāntam upādatte---evaṃ hi kaṃcit kaścit pṛcchatīti / . . . vṛṣalakulam [iti] atra codyate---nanu ca brāhmaṇakulam api tulyajātīyakaṃ tayor vyavadhāyakam eva ? naiṣa doṣaḥ / naitāvad eva, paraṃ tulyajātīyaṃ paraṃ vyavadhāyakaṃ bhavati / yas tu bhavataḥ pakṣaḥ, atulyajātīyakaṃ ca vyavadhāyakaṃ neti, etat nivartyate / ato lokavad ihāpi svaravyavahitānāṃ nāsti saṃyogasaṃjñā / halvyavadhāne 'pi nāsti uṣṭra iti ṣakārasya rephasya ca, ṭakāreṇa vyavadhānāt / trayāṇāṃ [a]vyavadhānāt, tathā ṣaṭayo rephaṭakārayoś ca pravartata eva ca saṃjñeti / 

kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇam iti / iha yas tathā [hy ubhayathā]nantaraśabdasya dṛṣṭā pratipattiḥ / tatropapattiṃ pṛcchati kātropapattir yad anantaraśabdaḥ kvacid vyavahite pravartate, kvacinneti ? itara ekāntenaiva vyavadhānamatajjātīyenābhyupagamyāha---sarvatrātajjātīyaṃ vyavadhā[ya]kam iti / grāmaśabdo[ 'yaṃ] bahvarthaḥ / etad ācaṣṭe---anantaraśabdo 'trāvikalpārthaḥ / grāmaśabdasya kaścid arthaviśeṣaḥ parigṛhyate / 

[5.3] tatra bahvarthatāyāṃ vipratipattiḥ / kecin manyante---śabdā eva pratibhaktarūpā anekavākyaviṣayāḥ / sārūpyāt tattvādhyavasāyaḥ / apare manyante---eka eva śabdo 'nekaśaktiḥ / tasya tv arthavaśāt kvacid eva śaktiḥ kasmiṃścit e[va] vākya āśrīyata iti / yathā tu bhāṣyaṃ, tathā śabdā eva pratibhaktā iti lakṣyante / evaṃ hi nigamanam---tadyaḥ sāraṇyake sasīmaka iti / śaktipratibhāgābhyupagame hi evaṃ vaktavyam---‘tad yadā’ iti / 

ato grāmaśabdas tasyās tasyāḥ kriyāyā upapādanāt vākte vākte taṃ tamartham upādāya pravartamānas tadartho 'pakṣepaka eva / tatra yo 'yaṃ grāmaśabdaḥ ‘anantarāv imau grāmau' iti, ayaṃ nityam eva sāraṇyake samīmake sasthaṇḍile vṛtta iti nadyā apy antarbhāvāt anantarāv evaitau / na vyavahitāv iti naivaṃ vyavahite anantaraśabdo 'vartate / 

[5.4] atha yadā grāmo 'stīti prayujyate, tadā kiṃ yugapad ete 'rthāḥ pratyāyyante itaḥ pṛthak puruṣāḥ, pṛthak śālāsamudāyaḥ, pṛthak ca samīmakaṃ sasthaṇḍilakam iti ? naivaṃbhūtam asti pratyāyanam / sāraṇyake hy antarbhāvitāḥ puruṣāḍayaḥ punarnirdeṣṭavyā iti nāsti saṃbhavati / tasmāt sarva eva śabdāḥ kaṃsmiś cid viśeṣe prayujyante, sa tu [kadācit] pratipattrā [na] vigṛhyate / etāvat tu pratipadyate, kaścid evārtho 'nena prakrānta iti / ataś ca na pratipadyate, yatra pṛcchati ko 'tra bhavato 'bhipreta iti /
