[5.5] keṣāṃci[t] tv atredaṃ darśanam---vāṭaparikṣepe 'sya mukhyā vṛttiḥ / puruṣādiṣu tu gauṇī, tātsthyāt sāmīpyād vā iti / yathā nandigrāmo mallagrāma iti saty api tannivāsināṃ purṣāṇām itaretaragamane na nandigrāmo mallagrāma iti saṃpadyate, mallagrāmo vā [na]ndigrāma iti / teṣāṃ tu karmanimittam idaṃ darśanam / evaṃ hi śrautāni smārtāni ca karmāṇi avasthitā[ni] / ‘‘grāme nādhyeyam’’, ‘‘grāmaṃ ca na praviśet’’ (gaudhasū. 3.33) / tathā karmāntaram ‘‘na bhuktvā na grāme śukriyaṃ ceti’’ (mānava gṛ. sū. 1.4.13--14) / vāṭaparikṣepe nādhīyate, tam eva ca na praviśanti, sīmni tu paṭhanti, praviśanti ca / 

[5.3] apara evaṃ manyante---sāraṇyake samīmake sasthaṇḍile mukhyā vṛttiḥ, samudāyaśabdas tu [gauṇyā] śrutvā(? vṛttyā)vayaveṣu puruṣeṣu vartate / yathā ‘pūrve pañcālā’ iti /

asmin darśane bhāṣyaṃ na [ni]gamitaṃ bhavati---grāmaśabdo 'yaṃ bahvartha iti / ato 'syaitat darśanam---yathā aśva iti nāmapadam ākhyātapadaṃ cātyantam itaretaram anupakāri svārthe pravartate, jātimatyarthe samādhanāyāṃ ca kriyāyām, evaṃ grāmaśabdo 'pīti / 

mukhanāsikāvacano 'nunāsikaḥ // 1.1.8 //

[1] kim idaṃ mukhanāsikāvacana iti ? kutaḥ praśnaḥ ? evaṃ manyate---yathāyaṃ śabdaḥ saṃśabdyate na tathābhūto 'rtha ucyate / evaṃbhūto hyartho 'bhipretaḥ---mukhena nāsikayā yaḥ ucyate so 'nunāsikaḥ iti / śabdas tv evam avasthito, mukhanāsikam avacanam asyeti / tatra na śabdo 'rthasya sadṛśaḥ, nārthaḥ śabdasyeti / 

[1.1] nipātanād dīrghatvaṃ bhaviṣyati / asyāyam abhiprāyaḥ---na loke 'nyathābhūtaḥ śabdaḥ śāstre kṛtadīrgha uccāritaḥ / kiṃ tu loke 'py ayam eva smaryate / smaryamāṇasya ca nipātanasādhutvam anvākhyāyate, yathā ‘‘sarvanāmāni’’ (A 1.1.27) iti / itarathā hi kimiti gurunirdeśaṃ kuryāt ? mukhanāsikavacana iti na bhavitavyam /

[1.3] īṣadvacanam āvacanam / sa hi varṇo na mukha eva nāpi nāsikāyām eva vātmavyaktiṃ labhate / kiṃ tu vāyunānyatarasmin anuparataśaktinānyataratra karaṇe 'bhihanyamāne sa varṇa ātmavyaktiṃ pratipadyata iti / īṣanmukhasya vyāpāra īṣat nāsikāyāḥ / atrāpi sāmānyaśabdasyāvyāvṛttatvāt idam anivṛttam eva śabdarūpaṃ mukhanāsikavacana iti / yo hi īṣad ucyate, ucyate hy asau / yathā śuklataro na bhavati na śuklaḥ, bhavaty eva śukla iti / yathā īṣat dūraṃ---dūram eva /

[1.4] mukhadvditīyā vā nāsikā vacanam asya / śākapārthivāditvāt samāsaḥ / mukhadvitīyeti mukhasahāyety arthaḥ / yathā ‘asidvitīyo 'nusasāra’ (MBh 1.426.8) iti / tatra sāhacaryāt nāsikā mukham iti vyapadiśyate, mukhanāsikā vacanam asyeti / atrāpi na daṇḍavārito dvandvaḥ ity avyāpṛto mukhanāsikavacana iti śabdaḥ / 

[1.5] mukhopasaṃhitā vā nāsiketi / [kecit] varṇayanti---pūrvatrottarapadalopo nānv ākhyātaḥ / iha tv anvākhyātaḥ, ‘siddhaṃ [tu] samānādhikaraṇādhikāre ktas tṛtīyāpūrvapada uttarapadalopaś ca’ (A 2.1.34--35, vā. 4) iti / etac cāsat, lopasya tatra pratyākhyātatvāt / tasmād anenaitat pratipādayati---nāsikāmukhopasaṃhitam asya sthānāntaram anyad eva / na mukhaṃ nāpi nāsikā / kiṃ tarhi ? tayor antarālam / yathā gaṅgāyamunayor veṇikā iti / asminn api prāsādavāsinyāyo durlabhaḥ, sthānāntaratvād iti / nanu caitasyāṃ kalpanāyāṃ ‘ṭita’ (A 4.1.25) iti ṅīpā bhavitavyam, ‘mukhanāsikāvacanīkaḥ anunāsikaḥ’ iti ? naiṣa doṣaḥ / vacanaṃ pradhānaṃ mukhanāsikayā viśeṣyate / na ca pradhānaṃ gaṇaliṅgaṃ pratipadyate ity evaṃ nāsikāvacana iti / yathā viṃśatir gāva iti guṇipradhāne [nirdeśe / guṇapradhāne] 'pi nirdeśe na---gavāṃ viṃśatiḥ / 

[2] [yamānusvārāṇām eveti / asati] caitasmin nyāye doṣa eva bhavati / antaratamasyābhāvāt ‘āṅo 'nunāsikaḥ’ (A 6.1.126), ‘aṇo 'pragṛhyasyānunāsikaḥ’ (A 8.4.57), ‘yaro 'nunāsika’ (A 8.4.45) iti eta eva yamānusvārāḥ prāpnivanti / ye tv anye ‘[viḍvanor a]nunāsikasyāt’ (A 6.4.41) iti evamādayaḥ, teṣv asaṃbhava eva yamānusvārāṇām / na hy ete dhātvante bhavanti iti / yeṣāṃ tu ‘yamānusvārāṇām api’ iti granthas teṣāṃ tu prāsādavāsinyāyo manasi vartate ity evaṃ varṇyate / evaṃ ca saty antara[ta]mā eva bhaviṣyati, na yamānusvārā iti / vakṣyati ‘naiva doṣo na prayojanam’ iti / kacaṭatapānām eva prasajyeta / evaṃ ca sati pakvam iti ‘anudāttopadeśa-’ (A 6.4.37) iti lopaḥ prasajyeta / odanapak, ‘anunāsikasya kvijhaloḥ’ (A 6.4.15) iti dīrghatvam / 

prāsādavāsinyāyenan / ayaṃ prāsādavāsinyāyaḥ kriyānimitteṣu vyapadeśeṣu pravartate, na rūḍau / yathā kaścit, na ca prāsādavāsitvaṃ bhūmivāsitvaṃ vā rūḍhiminittam, kriyānimittam eva iti ubhābhyāṃ vyavadiśyate, evaṃ vyākaraṇe 'pi na nāsikāvacana ity etat kasmiṃścit paribhāṣitaṃ prasiddhaṃ veti / kriyānimittatvād eva vyapadeśam ubhayavacano 'pi varṇaḥ pratipadyate / 

naiva doṣo na prayojanam / ānantara[ta]myān mukhavacanasya ubhayavacano bhaviṣyatīti / 

[3] itaretarāśraya uktam / kva ? vṛddhisaṃjñāyām / iha tu punar ucyate, asya [ni]darśanārtham / anunāsikasaṃjñā saṃhitāvasānasaṃjñāvat lokavidi[ta]tvāt naivārabdhavyā / athābudhabodhanārtham ārabhyate, yo doṣas tasmin prativihitam eveti / 

tulyāsyaprayatnaṃ [savarṇam] // 1.1.9 //

[1] tulyasthānaprayatnānāṃ pradeśeṣu kāryasaṃpratyayārthaṃ svarūpagrahaṇena śāstraṃ [guru] saṃpadyata iti saṃjñāpraṇayanam / atra na yathāśrutapadānu[gra]hapravṛtteḥ prāk dvaṃdvo yo 'dṛṭārthe svarūpagrahaṇe prāptaḥ, atha vā yasmin vṛttyarthe vākyam upanyastaṃ tāṃ vṛttim uccārayati---

[1.1] tulayā saṃmitaṃ tulyam, āsyaṃ ca prayatnaś ca āsyaprayatnam iti / kecit tulyam iti guṇakalpanaṃ kurvanti, tulyam iva tulyam iti / tat tu na yuktam / sāmānyena prayatnaḥ (?) / tulyam iti ukte savratrāviśeṣeṇa sadṛśe saṃpratyayo dṛśyate / yatra ca gauṇatvaṃ na tatrāntareṇārthaprakaraṇaśabdāntarābhisaṃbandhādi bhavati saṃpratyayaḥ / tasmāt yatpratyayānto rūḍhiśabdo 'yaṃ sadṛśasamānasadṛkparyāyaḥ / vyutpattikarmaṇi kevalaṃ tulopādīyate kasyacit śabdasyānugamāt / na tv atra tulā nāpi saṃmitatvam / yathā kuśalḥ, pravīṇaḥ, pratilomaḥ, anuloma iti / 

[1.2] kiṃ punar āsyam ? kim āsyaṃ manyase, samuditam atha pradeśān iti vākyopanyāsopakṣepa evam / 

laukikam āsyam / loke viditam / yathaiva paśur apatyaṃ devatā tathaivāsyam apīti / kiṃ punas talloke viditam iti idam ācaṣṭe---[o]ṣṭhāt prabhṛti prāk kā[ka]lakāt / [kākalakaṃ] grīvāyām utkṛṣṭaḥ pradeśa ucyate / 

kathaṃ punar āsyam ? evamarthaṃ pṛcchati / naitāvatā niścetuṃ śakyate / [o]ṣṭhāt prabhṛti prāk kālā[? kākalakā]d ity evam ucyamāne na jñāyate kim oṣṭhāt prabḥṛti prāk kākalakādye pradeśās te gṛhyante, atha samuditam āsyam iti / kecin nodayanti / phalābhāvāt nirjhāta āsyaśabde, arthapravṛttikāraṇapraśnatraiyarthyam / asti phalam / kiṃ nimittam upādāta pravṛttaḥ, athānimitto ḍitthavat svarūpam ? yadi nimittopakārī, sthānaviśeṣe 'pi vartiṣyata iti nāsti doṣa, iti pravṛttikāraṇaṃ draḍhayantaḥ praśnakāraṇaṃ darśayanti / 

itaro vyutpattim ācaṣṭe---asyanty anena [varṇān i]ty āsyam / kṛtyaḥ karaṇasādhanaḥ, snānīyaṃ cūrṇam iti yathā / yena varṇān asyanti / asanam atrābhivyaktiḥ niṣpattir vābhipretā / varṇān iti / anenāsanaviśeṣa eva ākhyāyate rūḍhiviṣayaḥ / 

annam etad āsyaṃ[da]ta iti āsyam iti vā / anenaitad ācaṣṭe---syader vārthe asyatir vartate / atha vā syader evaitad āṅpūrvasya ḍapratyaye rūpam iti / annaṃ cātra syandiviśeṣaṇārtham upādīyate / ‘etad’ iti cānena [ā]syam evābhisaṃbadhyate kartṛbhūtaṃ karmabhūtaṃ ca / annam etat prāpya dravībhavatīti / atha vā etad annaṃ dravīkaroti / 

[1.3] atha kaḥ prayatnaḥ ? kiṃ prayatanam, atha prārambha iti / prapūrvāt yatater bhāvasādhano naśabdaḥ / bhāvasaptamāni kārakāṇi iti bhavati bhāvaḥ sādhanāḥ / atha vā kartrādīni yathā sādhayanti evaṃ bhāvo[ 'pi] kārakāṇi sādhayati, viśiṣṭākhyāpravṛttinimittatvāt / evam api kathaṃ bhāvena naśabdaḥ sādhayte ? na sādhanaśabdo nirvartakārthaḥ / kiṃ tarhi ? abhidheyavācī / atha vā bhāvaḥ kaiścit sādhanatvenābhyupagamyate / abhyantaro bhāvo 'pi (? hi) prakṛto (? -ty artho) niradhiṣṭhāno na śakyo vyapadeṣṭum iti pratyayārtho bhāvaḥ siddharūpatayā sādhanatvenopādīyate / yathā vānaspatyam iti sa eva samudāyo bhedā[bhedā]bhyām āśritaḥ prakṛtyarthatvena pratyayārthatvena ca bhavati vanaspatīnāṃ samūha iti / sa ca bhāvaḥ kvacit kenacit dharmaṇa rūḍhaḥ saṃbandhitvenopādīyate naśabdavācyatvena / 


[1.4]  kim āsyopā[dāna] iti / nāsikāyām api varṇā niṣpādyante / tadvyudāsārtham āsyagrahaṇaṃ syāt / ucyate---āsya eva te niṣpadyante, ['nunāsamātraṃ] nāsiketi ekaṃ darśanam / anye manyante---na ceyaṃ nāsikā / kiṃ tarhi ?  antarāsye 'sti carmapaṭakaṃ,   paṇavacarmavad vitatam iti / tatsaṃbaddhā ca rekhā nāsikā / sā vāyunābhihanyamānā śrutiviśeṣe nimittaṃ bhavatīti / akārasya bāhyaṃ sthānam / taccodayiṣyaty eva / visarjanīyaś corasya ekeṣām / na cāsti tasya savarṇatve saty asati vā prayojanam / vakṣya[ty e]tad ayaṃ---[prayatna]viśeṣaṇam āsyopādānam iti / etad  ācaṣṭe---nāsyāyaṃ parīkṣākālaḥ / anyad eva tāvad vicārayiṣyāmaḥ / etad uttarakālaṃ parīkṣiṣyāmaha iti / 

[2] savarṇasaṃjñāyāṃ bhinnadeśeṣvatiprasaṅgaḥ / na hi dvaṃdve śakyata āsyena prayatno viśeṣayitum iti bhinnasthānānāṃ jabagaḍadaśām antarvartī spṛṣṭatādiḥ prayatnas tulya eṣāṃ, spṛṣṭaṃ karaṇaṃ sparśānām iti savarṇasaṃjñā prāpnoti / tataś ca parasparasāvarṇye sati tarptā, ūrgjaḥ, ūrgjyaḥ iti ‘jharp jhari savarṇe’ [A 8.4.65] iti lopaḥ prāœnoti / 

[2.1] siddhaṃ tv āsye tulyadeśaprayatnaṃ savarṇam / saṃjñā na bhaviṣyati / evam api kim āsyopādāne prayojanam ? yāvatāsyāntarvartina eva pradeśā iti / [prayatnaviśeṣaṇam iti /] itaro ya āsyāntarvartī bāhyaś ca na bhavati tadviśeṣaṇārtham āsyopādānam iti / vargyo vargyeṇa savarṇasaṃjña iṣyate / te ca parasparaṃ bhinnaprayatnāḥ / tatra mithas teṣāṃ savarnasaṃjñā na syāt / tasmād āsyagrahaṇena catvāra evāntarvartinaḥ savarṇasaṃjñāyāḥ pravṛttiṃ nivṛttiṃ ca prati āśrīyante / bāhyās tu vyudasyante / tāṃś ca bāhyān pratinirdiśati---saṃvāravivārāv iti / yathā caite bāhyās tathā śikṣāyāṃ vistareṇopapāditam / 

[2.2] eva[m apy avarnasya] savarṇasaṃjñā na prāpnoti / bāhyaṃ hy āsyāt sthānam avarnasya / kākalakasyādhastāt upajatru yaḥ pradeśaḥ tat sthānam avarṇasya / tac ca bāhyamāsyāt / nanu cāsyagrahanaṃ prayatnaviśeṣaṇam ity uktam / avarnasya cāntarvartī prayatnaḥ evaṃ manyate---yasya bāhyaṃ sthānaṃ prayatno 'pi tasya bāhya iti / atha vā, sthānam apy asti bāhyam ity ato deśaviśeṣaṇam api saṃbhavaty āsyagrahanam iti na doṣaḥ / 

sarvamukhasthānam avarṇam eka icchanti / sarvam eva mukhaṃ vyāpriyate 'varṇaniṣpattau, tena [na] bāhyasthānatā saṃbavaty asyāṃ śikṣāyām / bhinnasthānatvāt nāsti avarṇahakārayoḥ savarṇasaṃjñeti evamarthaṃ ‘nāj jhalau’ (A 1.1.10) iti na paṭhitavyam / 

evam api vyapadeśo na [pra]kalpate āsye [ye]ṣāṃ tulyo deśa iti / sarvamukhasthānatve sati ko 'nyo deśas tasmin ya ādheyatvena vādhikaraṇatvena vā vyapadiśyate ? vyapadeśivadbhāvena vyapadeśo bhavati / yac ca varṇāntaraviṣayam āsyaṃ, yac cotpādanakriyā[viṣayaṃ], yac cāvarṇaviṣayaṃ sarvaṃ tad āśritya tasminn idaṃ tulyam avarnaviṣayam āsyam iti samudāyāvayavakalpanayā bhedaḥ kriyate / atha vā, śabda eva bhāgopanipātī kaścit sāmānyarūpeṇa bravīti tad āsyaṃ, kaścid viśeṣarūpeṇety evaṃ vā bhedo bhaviṣyati / 

[2.3] na hi laukikam āsyam / nanu ca taddhitāntam api laukikam eva / etad ācaṣṭe---ya[? ṇya]tpratyayāntam āsyaṃ, yasya śrityā samadhigamaḥ kriyate, tannāśrīyate iti / sthānaṃ karaṇaṃ ca / sthānaṃ tālvādi / karaṇaṃ keṣāṃcit spṛṣṭatādi, keṣāṃcit jihvāyā agropāgramadhyamūlāni / yady evam, kimarthaṃ prayatnagrahaṇam ? etāvad astu---‘tulyāsyaṃ savarṇam’ iti / etāvad evāsye bhavaṃ---sthānaṃ prayatnaś ceti / pratyekaṃ vyāpāravyudāsārtham ubhayor upādānaṃ, yathaiva sthānaṃ sthānāntaranirapekṣaṃ syāt (?) / evaṃ ca sati sa eva doṣo---jabagaḍadaśāṃ tulyaprayatnānāṃ sthānabhinnānāṁ savarṇasaṃjñā prāpnoti / evam api prayatnaḥ aviśeṣito bhavati / āsyenety arthaḥ / tathā ca bāhyaprayatnā na hāpitā bhavanti / 

[2.4] prayatnaś ca viśeṣitaḥ . . . prārambho yatnasya prayatnaḥ / etad bhāṣyakārasya darśanam---spṛṣṭatādayaḥ pūrvam, uttarakālaṃ mūrdhni pratihatasya vāyor vivārādaya iti / 

praśliṣṭāvarṇāv etau / yathaiva codakumbhe kṣīrabinduḥ saṃśliṣṭho na jñāyate, evam eva avarṇam / anye varṇayanti---praśliṣṭāvarṇāv etāv ity etad uktaṃ bhavati saṃvṛtāvarṇāv iti / 

avarṇasya tarhi aicoś ceti / ayam abhiprāyo---viśliṣṭāvarṇāv etāv iti / tatra nāvyaṃ naubhūtaṃ nauyatīti lopa ītvaṃ ca sthātām / 

etayor eva tarhi mithaḥ / aikāraukārayoḥ / kiṃ punaḥ syāt ? ‘eta ai’ (A 3.4.93) ity atraukāro 'pi prāpnoti / yadi tan nāsti ‘bhāvyamānena savarṇānāṃ grahaṇaṃ na’ iti / 

abhedakā udāttādaya iti / pratipāditaṃ liṅgāt, na śāstre ete bhedena vyāpriyante iti / 

[2.5] yatsamānaṃ tadāśrayiṣyāmaḥ / prathamadvitīyayos tṛtīyacaturthayoś ca yady api orayatnabhedas tathāpi yas teṣāṃ samāno yatnas tadāśritā savarṇasaṃjñā pravartiṣyate / 

kiṃ sati bhede ? ayam abhiprāyaḥ---yadi vivārādīnāṃ bhede spṛṣṭatādisāmye pravartate, evaṃ [spṛ]ṣṭādibhede vivārādīnāṃ sāmye 'pi prāpnoti /yadi hi yatra sarvaṃ samānam iti / etaāvad brūyāt ‘akaḥ aki dīrghaḥ’ iti, ‘jharo jhari’ iti / kriyamāṇe 'pi savarṇagrahaṇe sarvaṃ samānaṃ na bhavatīti naiva lopaḥ syāt śiṇḍha, piṇḍhi, ity ata āha---sati bhede savarṇasaṃjñayā bhavitavyam iti / śakārachakārayoḥ ṣakāraṭhakārayoḥ sakārathakārayoś ca / sarvaṃ samānamaghoṣatvaṃ mahāprāṇatvaṃ ca / karaṇaṃ tu bhinnam / eke spṛṣṭakaraṇā apare vivṛtakaraṇāḥ / satyāṃ ca savarṇasaṃjñāyāṃ niśūśchadma, niṣṣṭhuram, nissthānam iti ‘jharo jhari savarṇe’ (A 8.4.65) iti lopaḥ prāpnoti / 

[2.6] evaṃ tarhi tripado 'yaṃ bahuvrīhiḥ, tulya āsye prayatna eṣām iti / abhidhānāc cāsamānādhikaraṇānām api bahuvrīhir abhyupagataḥ / nanu caivam āsyaṃ tulyatvenāviśeṣitaṃ bhavati, vivibhaktitvāt apradhānatvāc ca / naiṣa doṣaḥ / yadādheyaṃ prati yad adhikaraṇam upādīyate tadādheyatulyatayaiva tulyam iti vijñāyate / yathā devadattayajñadattayor madhurāyāṃ tulyaṃ bhojanam ity ukte gamyate etat, madhurāpi tayos tulyeti / evam ihāpi yayor varṇayor āsye tulyaḥ prayatno bhavati, āsyam api tayos tulyam iti / āsyaśabdasya taddhitā(ntasyā)śrayaṇasāmarthyāt vā / 

atha vā, pūrvas tatpuruṣas tato bahuvrīhiḥ / kena lakṣaṇena tatpuruṣaḥ ? yadi nipātanāt, samāsaḥ syāt, na tulyata(? na tu ta)tputuṣaḥ / tasmāt ‘sampatmī’ (A 2.1.40) iti yogavibhāgāt tatpuruṣaḥ / nipātanād apūrvanipātaḥ / atha vārthapradarśanam etat---tulya āsya iti / sāhacaryād āsye bhavaḥ pratilabdhāsyavyapadeśaḥ [prayatnaḥ]; prathamānta eva samasyate---tulya āsya iti, ‘kṛtyatulyākhyā ājātyā’ (A 2.1.68) iti / 

[3] tasya / tasyeti [tu] vaktavyam / arthapradarśanam etat, na tu pāṭhaḥ / pāṭhas tu tathābhūtaḥ kartavyo yathāyam artho labhyate iti / tatra vipaṣṭam eva vaktavyam yat ‘tulyāsyaprayatnaṃ yady asya tat tasya savarṇam’ iti / atha vaitad vaktavyam, ‘tulyāsyaprayatnaṃ tat tasya savarṇam’ iti / sāmarthyād gamyate ‘yasya yad’ iti / itarathā hi tulyāsyaprayatnatvāt savarṇam ity asmin nāmadheye labdhe pradeśeṣu ‘akaḥ savarne dīrghaḥ’ (A 6.1.101) itīdaṃ nāsti---yo yasya tulyāsyaprayatnas tasya tasmin, iti savarṇamātre dīrghaḥ pravartate / sūtrārambhaḥ kimarthaḥ iti cet, rephoṣmaṇāṃ nivṛttyarthaḥ / ‘rephoṣmaṇāṃ savarṇā na santi’ (MBh 1.28.12) iti nāsty eṣāṃ tulya[sthāna]prayatnatā / tatra rephe ūṣmasu ca savarṇasaṃjñākāryaṃ mā bhūd iti /

[3.1] tasyāvacanam iti / asamāso 'yam / yaḥ śabdo bhavato 'ccāritas tasyāvacanam iti / atha vānukaraṇaśandas ‘tasya’ iti / etasyāvacanam iti / kutaḥ ? vacanaprāmāṇyāt (vā. 3) / nanu coktam, rephoṣmaṇāṃ nivṛttyartham iti / tac cāsa[t] / te 'pi hi paraspareṇa tulyāsyaprayatnāḥ, repho rephāntareṇa, ūṣmā coṣmāntareṇa / atha vā, yady etāvat prayojanaṃ syāt savarnapradeśeṣv eva rephoṣmaṇāṃ nivṛttiṃ kuryāt / 

[3.2] saṃbandhiśabdair vā tulyam (vā. 4) / mātari vartitavyaṃ pitari śuśrū[ṣi]tavyam iti / mātṛśabdaḥ pitṛśabdaś ca loke na vandhyānivṛttyarthaṃ napuṃsakani[vṛ]ttyarthaṃ vā prayujyate / kiṃ tahir ? ayam evāsya svabhāvo yad apekṣitasvaprajanana eva pravartate / yā yaysa mātrā yo yasya piteti / evaṃ tulyaśabdo loke evaṃbhūta eva / tulyāya kanyā dātavyeti na caṇḍālena tulyāya brāhmaṇaḥ kanyāṃ dadāti / kiṃ tahir ? ātmanā / savarṇe dīrgho bhavatīti, tulyāsyaprayatne aci ako dīrgho bhavatīty etaduktaṃ bhavati / evaṃ ca sati tulyaśabdasyaivaṃ svabhāvatvāt antareṇa tasyeti vacanam ayam evārtho labhyata iti / 

[4] ṛkāralṛkārayoḥ savarṇavidhiḥ (vā. 4) savarṇāśrayasya kāryasya vidhiḥ / atha vā, vidheyā, tatra saṃjñāvidhir laghīyān iti / tasmāt saṃjñā vidheyety āha / athātra katham asaṃbandhiśabdatve etayoḥ savarṇasaṃjñety etat gamyate / na punar etayor anyaiḥ saheti ? tatra mitha iti vā vaktavyam / atha vā nāyaṃ pāṭhaḥ kartavyaḥ---ṛkāraḷkārayoḥ savarṇavidhir iti / kiṃ tarhi ? pradarśanam iti / sa tu pāṭhaḥ kartavyo yathaiva tayor eva mithaḥ savarṇasaṃjñā bhavati / 

hotṛṛkāraḥ hotṝkāraḥ iti savarṇadīrghatvaṃ yathā syāt / hotuḥ ḷkāraḥ śobhana iti saṃbandhaṣaṣṭhī / tataś ca samāso 'yam iti / naitad asti prayojanam iti / vakṣyaty etat savarṇadīrghatve iti / evaṃ manyate---dīrgha iti tatra vartate / [te]na ṛ iti, ḷ iti ca vibhāṣite ḷśabde dīrghatvaṃ bhaviṣyati / ḷti ḷvarṇasya dīrghā na santi, ubhayānugrāhī ca dīrgho nāstīti ṛkāra eva saṃbhaviṣyati iti / 

tat savarṇe yathā syāt / yadi savarṇasaṃjñā[m a]vidhāya tad ucyate, akaḥ ḷ iti bhavatīti, aṅmātrasya syāt / dadhyḷkāraḥ madhvḷkāraḥ iti / tasmād vidhātavyā savarṇasaṃjñā / yad etat ṛ . . . ṛtīti, etad ṛta iti vakṣyāmi / ṛtaḥ savarṇe [r]ṛvā bhavatīti ṛty eva bhavitavyam / tat ḷtīti / idam asavarṇārtham ārabhyate / te[na] ṛkāre trīṇi rūpāṇi---ekam asaṃhitāviṣayaṃ, dve saṃhitāviṣaye / evaṃ ḷkāre 'pi / 

tan na vaktavyaṃ bhavati / evaṃ manyate---dvāv apy etau dīrghau, (r)ṛkāra (l)ḷkāraś ca, tau bhaviṣyataḥ / ṛkāre ca savarṇaḥ, ḷkāre ca savarṇasaṃjñāyāṃ satyāṃ ṛkāraś ca (l)ḷkāraś ceti / 

avaśyaṃ tad vaktavyam / ūkālo 'jjhasvadīrghaplutaḥ (A 1.2.27), na cāyam aj iti / katham anactvam ? kecin manyante---dvau rephāvardhāv a[tha] cāṅmātreti / te īṣatspṛṣṭakaraṇatvāt prayatnabhedaṃ manyante / apare varṇayanti / dvau rephau turīyāv a[tha] cāṅmātreti / kecin manyante---sakalaś ca rephaḥ ṛkāraś cājbhaktiś cārdhamātreti / kiṃ tu saṃvṛtataro 'yam / ṛkāraś ca vivṛtaḥ / prayatnabhedāt nāsty asya ṛkāre[ṇa] grahaṇam iti / evaṃ tarhi 

actvaṃ vakṣyāmi / saty actve dīrghasaṃjñā labhyate / yasya tu pakṣo 'rdhatṛtīyamātro 'yam iti tasyāctve sati dīrghasaṃjñāyā abhāvaḥ, bhinnakālatvāt / tasmād dcimātra evāyam abhyupagantavyaḥ / tac cāvaśyaṃ vaktavyam tvayāpi ṛvāvacanaṃ bruvatā / katham ? vacanād idaṃ tāvat syāt / asya tu pluto 'śakyaḥ kartum / anayor hi pluta ucyate (? iṣyate) / yaś cāsau kriyate 'sāv apy anactvāt pluto na bhavatīti na sthānī nāpy ādeśa iti sthitam etat---yaś ca savrṇasaṃjñām ārabhate yaś ca savarṇadīrghatve ḷvāvacanaṃ karoti, ubhābhyām actvaṃ vakavyam iti / 

kiṃ punar atra jyāyaḥ ? kiṃ savarṇasaṃjñāvidhānam uta ḷvāvacanam iti ? atra codyate---yadi lakṣaṇayor lakṣye samāno vyāpāra[s] tat etad upapadyate---‘kiṃ punar atra jyāyaḥ ?’ iti / ayam iha tu bhinna eva vyāpāraḥ / katham ? yadi savarṇasaṃjñā vidhīyate, na tad ucyate ṛti ṛvāvacanam iti, hotṛ ṛkāra iti sthite dvayor dīrghayor upasthitayor yadi tāvat dvāv api ṛkārau sthāninau dvābhyāṃ rephābhyāṃ tadvantāv iti iti dvayo rephayorr dvirepheṇādeśena bhavitavyam iti---sarvakālaṃ dvirephako dīrghaḥ prāpnoti / athāyaṃ pakṣaḥ---saṃvṛtataram api(? -m iti), evam api vivṛtayoḥ ṛkārayoḥ sthāne kathaṃ saṃvṛtataro bhaviṣyati ? tena sarvakālaṃ vivṛtena bhavitavyam / tathā ḷkāre parabhūte ralavān nāsti dīrgha iti paryāyeṇa kadācit [ṛ iti] dīrghaḥ syāt, kadācit [l]ḷ iti doṣaḥ (? dīrghaḥ) / atha manyase rūpatrayaṃ sādhyam, tac caivaṃ sidhyatīti / tac ca na / bhinnaviṣayau ṛkāraḷkārau parigṛhya traiśabdyaṃ sidhyet, na tv anyat tasmāt / yas tu (l)ḷ vidhatte, tasya katham ? asau (l)ḷ vā vi[da]dhat pakṣe dīrghatvaṃ vidadhāty eva, (l)ḷ ity api /

nanu ca tasyāpi sa[va]rṇasaṃjñāṃ vidadhataḥ ṛ ṛtīti ḷkāraḥ syāt [saṃ]nihitaḥ, [ḷ]tīti ṛkāraḥ / tatra ekaikasmiṃs trairūpyaṃ prāpnoti / syād etat / pravibhāgopādānam anarthakam ṛti ḷti ceti / anyatarasmin pravibhāgopādānasāmarthyāt trairūpyaṃ mā bhūd iti / anyatrasmi[n] tat prāpnoti prāpnoti eva / 

ḷkārasya laparatvaṃ vakṣyāmi / vyākhyāsyāmīty arthaḥ / katham ? pratyāhāre ‘raṭ / laṇ /’ iti lakāre yo 'kāraḥ asāv anunāsikaḥ pratijñāsyate / atas tenānetarad iti (? -stenāntyenetā sahito ?) repha ādis tanmadhya[stha]sya saṃjñā bhaviṣyati iti, ‘uraṇ raparaḥ’ (A 1.1.51) iti rephas tanmadhya[stha]ṃ lakāraṃ pratyāyayati / evam apy ubhayoḥ ralau kasmān na bhavataḥ ? āntaratamyāt / yathā jaśbhāvināṃ jaśaḥ carbhāvināṃ caraḥ evaṃ rephavato rephavān ādeśaḥ, lakāravato lakāravāṃś ca iti / 

[prakḷpyamānam iti] / yathaivaṃ kḷpyamānam iti ṇatvaṃ bhavati evaṃ ‘prakḷpyamānaṃ paśya’ iti ‘kṛtyacaḥ’ (A 8.4.29) iti ṇatvaṃ prāpnoti / 

tribhiś ca madhyamair iti / ko 'tra viśeṣaḥ ? pūrvatra śarvyavāya iti sāmarthyāt ṣakāro 'numīyate, tasya ṇatve nimittatvāt / iha laśasaiśca iti vyavadhānakarāv eva śasāv upādīyete / nanu lakāravyavāya ity apy ucyamāne na prāpnoti / prakḷpyamānam iti lakāro 'yaṃ na bhavati, tat āha---varṇaikadeśāś ca varṇagrahaṇena gṛhyante iti / samudāyavyāpāre avayavo 'pi vyāpriyate, na tasya vyāpāra ucchidyata iti / yady evaṃ nārtho [raṣābhyāṃ] ṇatve ṛkāragrahaṇena / rephād eva ṇatvaṃ bhaviṣyati / evaṃ ca sati nāsti ṛkāragrahaṇam ity gṛhyamāṇaṃ kathaṃ ḷkāraṃ grahīṣyati iti sthitā savarṇasaṃjñā / 

evaṃ saty akṣarasa[mā]mnāye 'narthaka ḷkāropadeśa iti / ṛd iti tarhi yad ucyate tat ḷd iti atrāpi prāpnoti /* . . . na bhaviṣyati . . . / aṅarthaṃ tu ḷd ittvaṃ syāt / tasmāt ḷkāraḥ pratyāhāre paṭhitavyaḥ / paṭhamāne tasmin pṛthaganuvandhakaraṇasāmarthyād bhavati parihāraḥ / 

nāj jhalau  // 1.1.10 //

[1] ikāraśakārayo[ḥ] avarṇahakārayoś ca sthānakaraṇe tulye iti pratiṣedha ārabhyate sarvaṇasaṃjñāyāḥ, ‘ajjhalau mithaḥ savarṇasaṃjñau [na] bhavataḥ’ iti evam avasthita idam ucyate---

ajjhaloḥ pratiṣe(dhe śakārapratiṣe)dho 'jjhaltvāt (vā. 1) / ajjhalāv actvāt [haltv]c cāśakyau pratiṣedhdum iti prākraṇiko dharmaḥ pratiṣedhyatvena vijñāyate / kaś cāsau dharmaḥ ? savarṇasaṃjñā / atha vā pratiṣedhena nivṛttiḥ vyapadiśyate / ajjhaloḥ savarṇasaṃjñāto nivṛttau / śakārapratiṣedha iti ca dvedhā varṇyate / śakārasya pratiṣedhaḥ prāpnoti / tadartho yatnaḥ kartavyaḥ iti / atha vā yo 'yam ajjhalo[ḥ] pratiṣedhaḥ, atra śakāraprati[ṣedhaḥ] / yady apy ekavacanāntasya samāsas tathāpi sāmarthyāt saviśeṣaḥ pratīyate, bhedādhiṣṭhānatvāt savarṇasaṃjñāyāḥ / 

accaiva hi śakāro hal ca / pāṭhāt nirjhātam evāsya haltvam, actvam evāsya durpapādim ity actvam eva pṛcchati---kathaṃ tāvad actvam iti / itaro 'ctvam upapādayati---

ikāraḥ savarṇagrahaṇena śakāram api gṛhṇāti / savarṇagṛhītyā savarṇagrahaṇena, yadvā śāstreṇa tatra yathaiva ikāro 'ckāryāṇi pratipadyate, evaṃ śakāro 'pīti / evaṃ tāvad actvam / 

[1.1] kathaṃ punaḥ ikāraḥ śakāraṃ gṛhṇāti ? yāvatā nājjhalāv ity ayam asti pratiṣedhaḥ / 

[1.1.1] ucyate---asmin pratiṣedhasūtre yo 'yam avayavo 'j iti sa kiṃ gṛhītasavarṇakānām atha neti vicārayāmaḥ / tatredaṃ yuktaṃ, gṛhītasavarnakānām iti, yathā ‘akaḥ savarne dīrghaḥ’ (A 6.1.101) ity asmin grahaṇe / iha tu nāsti grahaṇam ‘aṇudit savarṇasya cāpratyayaḥ’ (A 1.1.69) iti / kiṃ kāraṇam ? asmin grahaṇe apariniṣpannatvāt saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhasya / grhaṇāt grahaṇam / grahaṇābhāve (? sāvarṇyāt grahaṇam / sāvarṇyābhāve grahaṇābhāvaḥ,) grahaṇakaśāstrasyānabhinirvṛttatvāt, grahaṇāntarasya cābhāvāt, svātmani ca kriyāvirodhāt / tatra hy aṇgrahaṇe (? hy ajgra-) graha[ṇa]kaśāstram anabhinirvṛttaṃ, kriyamāṇatvāt saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhasya grahaṇāntaraṃ ca nāsti / svātmani ca kriyāvirudhyate / yathā ghaṭo nātmanaṃ śaknoti spraṣṭum / evaṃ ca kṛtvā ‘hal antyam’ (A 1.3.3) ity atroktam---‘lakārasyānubandhatvājñāpitatvāt halgrahaṇāprasiddhiḥ (A 1.3.3, vā. 3)’ iti / asmin hi hal iti vyapadeśe svātmani kriyā virudhyata iti na śaknoti lakārasyetsaṃjñāṃ pratipādayitum / tasmāt pṛthak lakārasyetsaṃjñā vaktavyeti / tatra coktam---‘siddhaṃ tu lakāranirdeśāt’ (A 1.3.3, vā. 4) ‘ekaśeṣanirdeśād vā’ (A 1.3.3, vā 4.) iti / 

[1.1.2] yathaiva cāṇgrahaṇe (? cājūgra-) grahaṇaṃ nāsti, evaṃ pratiṣedhagrahaṇe pratiṣedhābhāvaḥ, pratiṣedhaśāstrasyānabhinirvṛttatvāt, pratiṣedhaśāstrasyaiva [śāstrāntarasyā]bhāvāt / svātmani ca kriyāvirodhāt / nājjhalāv iti pratiṣedho 'nabhinirvṛtta evāsminn aj iti pratyāhāre / śāstrāntaraṃ ca nāsti pratiṣedhakam / svātmani ca kriyāvirudhyate, ghaṭasparśavat / tatra yathaiva mā śabdaṃ kārṣīr iti nānenāsyaiva śabdasya pratiṣedhaḥ śakyaḥ kartum / kiṃ tarhi ? śabdāntarāṇām / evam iha pratiṣedhasūtre aśakyaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ kartum iti / yathaiva ca [? na] ikāraśakārayoḥ savarṇasaṃjñā pratiṣidhyate, evaṃ śakārayor apīti / yathaiva ‘kumārī śete’ iti nāsti savarṇasaṃjñā[pratiṣedha] [ī]kārasya śakāre parata iti / 

[1.2] nanu ca ‘prakalpya vāpavāda[viṣaya]m utsargaḥ pravartate’ iti sa[va]rṇasaṃjñā pratiṣedhaviṣayam utsṛjyābhiniviṣṭeti kuto grahaṇam ? satyam, evam etat, yady atrāpavādaḥ syāt / sa ca nāsti ity uktam, aniṣpannatvāt pratiṣedhasyeti / na ced atrāpavādaviṣayo 'stīti, pravartitavyaṃ savarṇasaṃjñayā, tatpūrvakeṇa ca tadgrahaṇeneti iha pratyāhāre śakārasyāctvena grahaṇād anyatra nāsti savarṇatvena grahaṇam iti idam āha---tatra savarṇalope doṣaḥ iti / viṣayavantaṃ doṣam ācaṣṭe, savarṇalopaviṣaye savarṇalopāprāptir doṣaḥ /

paraśśtāni kāryāṇīti / śatād atikrāntāni śatād adhikāni kartavyāni iti / ‘pañcamī’ (A 2.1.37) iti yogavibhāgāt samāsaḥ / ‘sādhanaṃ kṛtā’ (A 2.1.33, vā 2.) iti vā / rājadantāditvāt (A 2.2.31) paranipātaḥ / paraśabdena samāsaḥ / parārtho vā parasśabdaḥ, pāraskarāditvād (A 6.1.157) vā suṭ draṣṭavyaḥ / tatra sakārasya ścutve kṛte, dvirvacane, sāvarṇyasya pratiṣiddhatvāt triśakāraḥ syād iti doṣaḥ / 

[1.3] a[tra] codayanti / śakāraś ced aj iti ikāreṇa gṛhītaḥ, sa yathaivāckāryaṃ pratiṣedhaṃ labhate, evam idam api kasmāt [na] pratipadyate ‘śaro 'ci’ (A 8.4.49) na dve bhavata iti, ‘anaci ca’ (A 8.4.47) dve bhavata iti ? na cātra tena dvirvacanam api na bhaviṣyati ? ta evaṃ parihāram āhuḥ---‘anaci ca’ iti paryudāso 'yam, aco 'nyasminn iti / bhavati cāyam aco 'nya iti / 

etad asat / na hy etat saṃjñāsu pratipādayituṃ śakyam / iha śakāro hal cāc ca / tat yadi evam ucyeta---aco 'nyasmin bhavitavyam iti, śakāro varjyeta na varjyeta iti, niḥsaṃśayaṃ na varjyetaikārthacodanāyām api yasminn arthe pratipadyate / ‘aprāsādaāsina ānīyantām’ iti kim u[bhaya]vāsino bhūmivāsitvād bhavitavyam ānayanenāhosvit prāsādavāsitvād aśakyo 'sau prāsādavāsino 'nya iti vyapadeśṭum iti na bha[vi]tavyam ānayaneneti ? evaṃ śabalaḥ chāgaḥ, kim akṛṣṇam ālabheta śu(? -tāśu)śuklam ālabhetety ālabdhavyo nālabdhavyaḥ iti / nālabdhavyaḥ / 

evaṃ tu triśakāratvam iha nyāyaprāœtam eva / katham ? uktaṃ hi ‘pratiṣedhasūtre pratiṣedho nāsti anabhinirvṛttatvāt pratiṣedhasyāsya, [a]nyatra tu pratyāhāre 'sty eva pratiṣedhaḥ’ iti kuto 'sya dvirvacanavidhāvactvam ? tasmād asya sa yatnaḥ kartavyo yena ikāra iha pratyāhāre śakāraṃ na gṛhṇāti / grahaṇe hi sati vyaktam anyatra grahaṇam asti śakārasya / etac ca prasajyapratiṣedho 'yam iti kṛtvopanyastam / evaṃ ca kṛtvā vākyaṃ paṭhitam---ajjhaloḥ pratipedha iti / prasajyapratiṣedhaṃ pratiṣedha iti āha / yadi paryudāsaḥ syāt ‘tulyāsyaprayatnaṃ savarṇaṃ, nāj jhalau’ iti naiva kiṃcil lakṣaṇaṃ vidhāyakam ajjhalor asti nāpi pratiṣedhakam iti, ajjhalau mitho muktvā acāṃ halāṃ ca mithaḥ savarṇasaṃjñeti doṣābhāvaḥ / 

[2] siddham anactvāt (vā. 3) / āsyāntarvartinaḥ prayatnasya bhedāt sāvarṇyaṃ nāsti / asati tasmin kiṃ pratiṣedheneti sūtrapratyākhyānena parihāra uktaḥ / yathā tu pāṇinīyaiḥ paṭhitam, ubhayatra vā īṣat iti anuvartate, ubhayatra vā nivṛttam iti / tathā parihāram āha---

[2.1] vākyāparisamāpter vā (vā. 4) / nāsti grahaṇam iti / idaṃ vākyam aparisamāptārthatvāt ‘aṇudit savarṇasua ca’ (A 1.1.69) ity aparisamāptam ity ucyate / kathaṃ punar asyāparisamāptatvam ? aṅgayor anirjhānāt / vākyasya ime aṅge, aṇiti ca savarṇasyeti ca / te yadā nirjhāte bhavataḥ ‘ayamaṇ ayaṃ savarṇaḥ’ iti, tadā grahaṇam / tatra upadeśa, itsaṃjñā, pratyāhāra ity eteṣv asya grahaṇaṃ prati nāsti vyāpāraḥ / yatraiva tāvat aṇ iti rūpam uccaritam, ‘aṇuditsavarṇasya’ iti, tatraiva tāvat [na] grahaṇam / kim aṅga punar yatraitad upāyāni rūpāṇi iti ? upadeśakāle 'savarṇā hy ete / 

pratyāhārottarakālā na savarṇasaṃjñeti / nā[tra] pāṭhakramo 'bhipretaḥ / kiṃ tarhi ? pravṛttikramaḥ, ‘aṇuditsavarṇasya ca’ (A 1.1.69) ity asmin yaḥ kramo 'ṇ iti ca savarṇasyeti ca / tatra yathaivopadeśādiṣv anirvṛttaṃ rūpam iti nāsti grahaṇam evaṃ savarṇe 'py anirjhāte na grahaṇena pravartitavyam / yadā sotsargā sāpavādā savarṇasaṃjñā nirjhātā bhavati, tadā cānyatrāpy actvavyapadeśeṣu savarṇatvena grāhakaṃ bhaviṣyati iti naivātra grahaṇaṃ, na pratiṣedhaḥ / atra ca nekāro gṛhīto nāpi śakāra iti īkāraśakārayoḥ [na] pratiṣiddhā savarṇasaṃjñā / īkāraśakārayos tulyatvāt sāvarṇyam iti ‘kumārī śete’ iti sāvarṇyakāryam ekādeśaḥ prāpnoti / ajadhikārān nivartyate /

[2.2] yathaiva tarhi [ikāraḥ] śakāraḥ na gṛhṇāti, evam īkāram api na gṛhṇīyāt / evaṃ manyate / yatra yatra savarṇasaṃśabdanaṃ tatra tatra cakrakaṃ vartata iti / varṇānām upadeśādiṣv eva nāsti grahaṇam / te hi etad arthā upādīyante / yathā kapālāvasthāyāṃ ghaṭa eva nāsti, kutas tasya kāryaviśe[ṣa]ḥ ? ye tu pradeśāḥ ‘akaḥ savarṇe dīrghaḥ’ ( 6.1.101) iti, na te etadarthāḥ, idam eva tadartham iti kimiti grahaṇaṃ na bhaviṣyati ? ato 'nenopāyenaitat kathayati, iha viṣaye grahaṇam ihāgrahaṇam iti / 

[2.3] apara āha / sarvam etat samānam / etāvāṃs tu viśeṣaḥ / siddher anactvaṃ hetuḥ anactvasya vākyāparisamāptiḥ / atra tu sādhyasya bhinnatvāt hetuvikalpo na yujyate ity āha---

asmin pakṣe vety etadasamarthitaṃ bhavati / etac ca samarthitam / vākyaśeṣa upādāsyate pratijñāvikalpārtham ity abhiprāyaḥ / vākyāparisamāpter anactvd vā siddham, yadi vā grahaṇam ikārena śakārasyeti / nanu ca bhedo bhavati / abhyupagamya śrutikṛtaṃ bhedam idam ucyate / 

vibhāṣā dvirvacanam / ‘yaro 'nunāsike 'nunāsiko vā’ (A 8.4.45) iti vartate / sa eṣa kathaṃ bhedo na syāt / asty eva bhedaḥ ity arthaḥ / itara āha---yadi nityo lopaḥ syāt / syād bhedaḥ / 

kiṃ tu vibhāṣā [sa] lopaḥ / ‘jhayo ho 'nyatarasyām’ (A 8.4.62) iti vartate / evaṃ cet yathābheda āvayos tathā śāstram avasthitam / apareṣāṃ vyākhyānam---

sa eṣa kathaṃ bhedo na iti etāvati sthite pakṣe itara āha---

syāt yadi nityo lopaḥ [syāt] / syād eva bhedo yadi nityo lopaḥ syād iti / 

iti bhartṛhariṭīkāyāṃ caturtham āhnikam //


















mahābhāṣyadīpikāyāṃ pañcamam āhnikam / 

īdūdeddvivacanaṃ pragṛhyam // 1.1.11 //

[1] īdādiṣu yady api viśeṣavatyo vyaktayaḥ, śrūyante, svarūpavi[dhau] ca yady apy uccāryamāna eva śabdo[ 'rtha]pratyāyakaḥ āśritadviśaktitvāt pratyāyyatvena gṛhyate, ---abhinnaśruti[ta]yā śabdāntaram anukaraṇenānukartuṃ śakyaṃ---tathāpi nāntarīyakatvāt upāttān sato viśeṣān parityajya sāmānyam āśrīyate / viśeṣaparaiva vā pravṛttiḥ syāt / ubhayaṃ saṃbhavati---tatredaṃ taparakaraṇam ākṛtivivakṣyāṃ vā bhedaparityāge sāmānyāśrayaṇāt atiprasaṅgo(? -ge) bhinnakālebhyo vyaktyantarebhyaḥ ākṛte[r a]vacchedakaṃ syāt, vyaktivivakṣāyāṃ vā bhinnakālavad itareṣv āśriteṣu viśeṣeṣu, guṇāntarayukteṣu vyaktyantareṣu prāptyartham api cedam upapadyate / ākṛtau tāvat yatnādhikyād eva hrasvānāṃ siddhā nivṛttiḥ / plutānāṃ ceṣyate / evaṃ hy aniṣṭārthaṃ taparakaraṇam āpadyate / vyaktāv apy anaṅādīnāṃ svaśabdenopāttavacanād uccāraṇenāśritānām apy abhedakatvaṃ sādhitam udāttādīnām ity ata āha---kimartham idādīnāṃ taparāṇāṃ pragṛhyasaṃjñocyate / vyaktivivakṣāyāṃ(? -kṣām) āśritya bhedakā evodāttādaya ity āha---taparas tatkālasya (A 1.1.70) iti tatkālānāṃ savarṇānāṃ grahaṇaṃ yathā syāt / kathaṃ punarbhedakatvam udāttādīnāṃ yāvatān aṅādīnām udāttavacanam abhedakatve jñāpakam uktam cūrṇikāreṇa ? taduktaṃ vārttikakāreṇa ‘‘ākārasya taparakaraṇaṃ savarṇārthaṃ bhedakatvāt svarasya’’ iti (A 1.1.1. vā. 13) / ‘‘aṇsavarṇasyeti svarānunāsikyakālabhedāt’’ iti (A 1.1.69 vā. 1) uktavato bhedakatvam evābhipretam / sūtrakāraysa ca savarṇe 'ṇgrahaṇāt svasvarāsaṅgāc ca bhedakatvam apy astīty anumīyate / anaṅādīnāṃ tūdāttānām ākṛtivivakṣāyāṃ syād iti aliṅgatvam api śakyaṃ gamayitum / keṣām a[yam a]bhiprāyaḥ / nahi kriyamāṇe taparatve kālāntarayukta[sa]varṇāntarapratipattir asti tatkālasyeti vacanād iti / udāttānudāttasvaritānām / anunāsikagrahaṇam apy anyeṣāṃ vidyate / na tv īdādayo 'nunāsikāḥ saṃbhavanti pragṛhyasaṃjñāviṣayasyānunāsikyasya pratiṣedhavacanād iti prasaṅgoccāritam etat / 

[2] plutānāṃ tu pragṛhyasaṃjñā na prāpnoti / nāyaṃ taparakaraṇād doṣaḥ / kutas tarhi ? vyaktipadārthābhyupagamāt / asiddhaḥ plutaḥ / tasyāsiddhatvāt tatkālā eva bhavanti / nanu ca plutasyāsiddhatvāt trimātratā na syāt / tatkālatā tu pūrvam eva nivartitatvāt asatī kathaṃ punarbhavati ? naiṣa doṣaḥ / śāstrāsiddhatvavijñānāt / anivṛttau ca tatkālatā vijñāyate / śāstranimittā śabdeṣu pravṛttinivṛttibuddhiḥ pratipādakasya śāstrasyābhāve prākṛtenaiva pratipādyarūpeṇāvatiṣṭhate / prāk cādeśavacanād asiddhaśāstre 'pravṛtte sthānī cānivṛttaḥ ādeśaś cānupajāta iti pravṛttam api śāstraṃ yadā vacanād asiddhatām āpadyate tadā tadvyāpārasādhye lakṣye viparyayeṇa buddhiḥ pravartamānā punar api sthāninaṃ ca sattvenāśrayati, ādeśaṃ cāsattvena / tathā ca vakṣyati---māt pragṛhyasaṃjñāyāṃ tasyāsiddhatvād ayāvekādeśaprasaṅgadoṣo nopapadyate / 

nanu cānirvṛtte kārye yadā svārthābhinirvṛttyartho śāstrapravṛttis tadā śāstraṃ śāstrātmani pratipādakatve vyavasthitam asiddhaṃ vijñāyeta / śāstrapravṛttyuttarakālaṃ tu pratipanne śāstrārthe caritārthatvād eva svabhāvato nivṛttaṃ śāstraṃ kathaṃ śakyaṃ vacanenāsiddhaṃ vijñātum ? kāryaṃ tadānīṃ vidyata iti kāryāsiddhatvam āśrayituṃ yuktam / tathā ca sthānyabhāvāt tatkālatvaṃ na vidyata iti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / svam arthaṃ pratipādya nivṛtte 'pi śāstre yadā tat siddhatvenāśrīyate, tadā śāstrapratipāditam ity evam avaśyaṃ tadāśrayitavyam / antareṇa hi śāstrānugamamayvādīnām api sādhutvam aviśiṣṭam iti na teṣām āśrayaḥ / evaṃ ced yathaiva śāstrapravṛttyavasthāyām asiddhatve śāstrasya vijñāyamāne nimittābhāvāt śāstrakārye buddhiḥ nivartate, evaṃ yadā yadā śāstrānugamyatvena kāryam apekṣyate tadā tadā śāstram apy apekṣyata iti yuktaṃ tasyāsiddhatvam āśrayitam / ataḥ plute vihite pragṛhyasaṃjñāyāṃ vidhīyamānāyāṃ plutasya pratipādakaṃ śāstraṃ yadāpekṣyate tadā tasyāsiddhatvāt avihita iva plute sthānirūpam avyāvṛttam iti tat [kā]latvāt siddhāpragṛhyasaṃjñā // 

[3] siddhaḥ plutaḥ . . . yad ayaṃ . . . plutasya prakṛtibhāvaṃ śāsti / na cāsati prakṛtibhāve, asiddhatve plutasya, [plutasya] svarasaṃdhiḥ prāpnoti / tatrāvaśyaṃ prakṛtibhāvo vaktavyaḥ / na brūmaḥ prakṛtibhāvārtho yatnaḥ kartavya iti / asiddhatvāt tu punaḥ ‘‘[plutaḥ] prakṛtye’’ty evam āśrayo nopapadyate, ādri(? -śrī) yate ca / tasmāt nūnaṃ siddhaḥ pluta iti saṃnidhānam anumīyate / 

nanu ca yad viṣayaṃ siddhatvam antareṇa plutasya kāryavidhānam anupapannaṃ tadviṣayam evāvaśyaṃ sāṃnidhyaṃ śakyam anumātum / tena prakṛtibhāva eva plutaḥ siddhaḥ syāt / na svarsaṃdhimātre / tathā ca vakṣyati---‘‘āśrayāt siddhatvaṃ ca yathā roruttve’’ (A 1.1.12 vā. 4) iti / satyam evam, evam etāvad iha trayaḥ kalpāḥ saṃbhavanti, pra[kṛ]tibhāvaviṣayaṃ vā plutasya siddhatvānumānaṃ, svarasandhiprakaraṇaviṣayaṃ vā, plutābhāvina eva sthāninaḥ prakṛtibhāvo vijñāyeteti / anumānapakṣe 'pi vākye 'numīyamāne ekayogasiddhatvaviṣa[yaṃ] vā vākyam anumīyate, prakaraṇasiddhatvaviṣayaṃ vā / viśeṣadṛṣṭaṃ caiva hy anumānam asti sāmānyatodṛṣṭaṃ [ca] / ubhayathā plutasaṃbandhasya mukhyād abhidheyād apracyutiḥ / eṣa viśeṣaḥ---ekayogaviṣayatve nāntarīyaka evārtho 'numito bhavati / prākaraṇike tu liṅge yady apy āntarīyakasya sāmānyavadanumānaṃ tathāpi vyāptir āśritā bhavati / plutabhvī iti tu vijñāyamāne yady apy amukhye 'rthe plutaśabdasya vṛttiḥ tathāpi na kvacid aśrutam anumātavyaṃ bhavati / tad iha sāmānyatodṛṣṭaṃ liṅgam āśritya prakaraṇaviṣayaṃ vākyam anumīyate ‘‘siddhaḥ plutaḥ svarasandhiṣu’’ iti / laghutvam api cāsyāṃ kalpanāyām ity etad darśayati ‘‘aplutād apluta’’ ity etan na vaktavyaṃ bhavati / siddhe hi plute atatkālatvād uttve plutasya nimittatvaṃ na bhaviṣyati iti yathoddeśaṃ saṃjñāparibhāṣam ity āha---kim ato yat siddhaḥ plutaḥ svarasaṃdhiṣu ? nāyaṃ svarasaṃdhiḥ, prakaraṇāntare hīyaṃ pragṛhyasaṃjñā vidhīyate iti / 

[4] saṃjñāvidhau ca siddhaḥ / katham ? kāryakālaṃ saṃjñāparibhāṣam iti / kāryaprayuktā saṃjñāpravṛttir iti / kārye guṇabhāvam antareṇa saṃjñāyāḥ svārthalābho na vidyate / bhavatipacatīty evamādiṣu yady akārādīnāṃ ajādisaṃjñā na syuḥ na kiṃcid uṣyeta / na cāntareṇa prayojanaṃ śāstraṃ pravartitum arhati iti nātrājādisaṃjñā bhavanti / kāryatantrāṇām api tu saṃjñānāṃ bhinnapradeśeṣu bahuṣu pradhāneṣv aṅgabhāvapratipattyarthaṃ kenacid asaṃyuktāḥ prakaraṇotkarṣena sarvārthāḥ saṃjñā vidhīyante / asyās tu pradeśaviṣaya evātmalābhaḥ / tenānyatrāpi paṭhyamānā pragṛhyasaṃjñā svarasaṃdhiprakaranasaṃniveśinī vijñāyate / kvacid viśeṣābhāvāt pṛcchati---kiṃ punaḥ plutasya pragṛhyasaṃjñāvacane prayojanam ? yatra viśeṣas tam adhikṛtyāha---pragṛhyāśrayaḥ prakṛtibhāvo yathā syāt / pūrvābhipretam eva kvacid aviśeṣaṃ darśayati---mā bhūd evam pragṛhyaḥ prakṛtyeti / plutaḥ prakṛtyety evaṃ bhaviṣyati / na prakṛtibhāve kaścid viśeṣaḥ / yatra plutāśrayaḥ prakṛtibhāvaḥ niṣidhyate tatrāvaśyaṃ pragṛhyāśrayaḥ prakṛtibhāvaḥ eṣitavya [i]ty āha---naivaṃ śakyam / upasthite hi doṣaḥ syāt / agnī3ti / ‘‘aplutavad upasthite’’ (A 6.1.129) iti plutāśraye prakṛtibhāve pratiṣiddhe yadi pragṛhyasaṃjñā na syāt pragṛhyāśrayo 'pi prakṛtibhāvo nāsti iti svarasaṃdhiḥ prasajyeta / satyāṃ tu pragṛhyasaṃjñāyāṃ pratiṣiddhe plutāśraye pragṛhyāśrayaḥ prakṛtibhāvo bhavati / tatraitat syāt naivātreṣyate prakṛtibhāva iti tadartham āha---paṭhiṣyati hy ācāryaḥ / ‘‘vadvacanaṃ plutakryapratiṣedhārtham’’ ‘‘plutapratiṣedhe hi pragṛhyaplutapratiṣedhaprasaṅgo 'nyena vihitatvāt’’ (A 6.1.129 vā.1, 2) iti / yadi pluta eva pratiṣidhyeta yo 'pi pragṛhyaḥ plutaḥ tasyāpi trimātratāyāṃ nivṛttāyāṃ dvimātrasya śravaṇaṃ syāt / plutāśraye tu prakṛtibhāve pratiṣiddhe ya evāpragṛhyaḥ plutas tasyaiva svarasaṃdhir bhavati devadattā3 iti devadatteti / yas tu pragṛhyas tasya plutakārye pratiṣiddhe 'pi pragṛhyāśraye prakṛtibhāve kṛte trimātraśrutir upapannā bhavati / tasmād asti prayojanaṃ plutasya pragṛhyasaṃjñāyām / sā ca vyaktipadārthābhyupagamān na prāpnoti / 

[5] yadi punar dīrghāṇāṃ [a]taparāṇāṃ pragṛhyasaṃjñocyeta / kiṃ punar ayaṃ taparatvā[d] doṣa āgato yata ucyate---yadi punar dīrghāṇām iti ? vyaktipadārthābhyupagamāt tv ayaṃ doṣaḥ / tatra kecid āhuḥ---etad evāyam āha / parityajya vyaktiṃ yady ākṛtau padārthe dīrgha ucyeta, yatnādhiktyāt hrasveṣv aprasaṅgaḥ / syāt plutasya ca syāt pragṛhyatvaṃ, tathā pratuya (? ca hras)vanivartakaṃ (? -naṃ) syāt ity ataparāṇām ity āha / itaras tu vyaktāv eva padārthe pareṇedam uktam ity āha---evam apy ekāra [eva] ekaḥ savarṇā[n] gṛhṇīyāt / na tvīkārokārau gṛhṇīyātām / anaṇtvāt iti / atha vā vyaktāv eva padārtha kasyacit tāvad asti savarṇagrahaṇaṃ sāmarthyād ity āha ‘yadi punar’ iti / itara evam api kaścid doṣo [na] nivartata ity āha evam apīti / apara āha---dīrghāṇām api ‘‘aṇo 'pragṛhyasya . . .’’ (A 8.4.57) ity evamādiṣu ackāryāṇi dṛṣṭāni / tasmāt tat (? aṇ) savarṇasya ity atrāpi tathaiva bhavitum arhati ity uktaṃ ‘yadi punar dīrghāṇām ataparāṇām' iti / anyatrāpy a[ṇ]savrṇasyeti anena dīrghāḥ kāryāṇi labhante / grahaṇaśāstre yad aṇgrahanaṃ tatra nānyat grahaṇaśāstram asti / tasyaiva cāṇgrahaṇasya svātmani vṛttir ayuktā / antareṇa cāṇgrahaṇaṃ yat pariśiṣṭaṃ tad aparisamāptārthatvāt grahaṇaṃ pratyasamartham ity āha---‘evam apy ekāra eka’ iti / yadi punar hrasvānām . . . iti / taparatve agnī vāyū ity atra na syāt / ataparatvāt punaḥ vyaktāv api padārthe ‘aṇ savarṇān gṛhṇāti’ iti dīrghāṇāṃ plutānāṃ ca siddhā pragṛhyasaṃjñā / hrasvasyāpi tu prāpnoti / kathaṃ punar atra bahuvacanam ? kecid virūpaikaśeṣam āhuḥ ‘prathamayor’ iti (A 6.2.102; 7.1.28) yathā / udāharaṇabhedāt bhedam anye bruvanti / 

[6] evaṃ tarhi kiṃ na etena [yatnena yat] siddhaḥ plutaḥ [svarasaṃdhiṣu] iti ? kathaṃ yat [tat] jñāpakam uktam ? jñāpakam anyathā samarthyate---plutaṃ prāpnoti plutabhāvī, pluto bhāvī asyeti vā / prāpnoti plutabhāvī, pluto asyeti vā / plutabhāvena (? -bhāvinaḥ) / sthānina eva prakṛtibhāvaḥ / asyāṃ kalpanāyām [aśrutaṃ vākyaṃ nānumeyaṃ bhavati / asiddhapari]bhāṣāyāś cābdhayā [pra]kṛtibhāvaśāstrakalpanayā ca dvayam anugṛhītaṃ bhavati / guṇaśabdakalpanāyāś caitad eva liṅgaṃ, yad ayam ‘‘aplutād apluta’’ iti pratiṣedhaṃ śāsti / nanu ca sthāninaḥ plutām api tva(? plutabhāvitva)m āśraya[s] tāvat u(? a)pekṣitaḥ / tasya cāsiddhatvād asato 'pekṣā na yujyate / tatra samāhuḥ---iha kvacit śāstranimittakeṣu śabdeṣu buddhyā vyavahriyate, kvacit laukikaṃ darśanam āśrīyate / tathāhītaretarāśrayadoṣas tatra tatra parihriyate / ‘‘siddhaṃ tu nityaśabdatvāt’’ (A 1.1.1. vā. 9) / na hi vṛddhisaṃjñayādaico bhāvyante, satām ādaicāṃ sādhaka(? sādhu)tvamātram anvākhyāyata iti asaty eva hi pratipādake lakṣaṇe teṣāṃ sādhya(? sādhu)tvam ādrīyate / evaṃ kṛtvā ‘‘lakṣaṇapratipadoktayor’’ ity etat syāt / kvacit anāśrayaṇaṃ bhavati / śāstranirapekṣāyāṃ śabdapravṛttau na kiṃcit lakṣaṇoktaṃ na pratipadoktam asti / nanu ca loke sthānyādeśa[bhāvā]bhāvāt plutabhāvitvam anupapannam / naiṣa doṣaḥ / loke 'pi hi viṣayāviṣayabhāvaḥ paricchidyate, svaviṣaye ayaṃ bhavatīti / tathā coktam---‘‘ijādiyajādi[pravṛtti]ś caiva hi loke lakṣyata’’ ity evamādi / anantaram eva ca vakṣyati---‘‘mārthādīdādyarthānām’’ iti / etasyāṃ tu kalpanāyāṃ bhāvigativijñānam anarthakaṃ sāṃpratikatvam api hi laukikadarśane śakyam āśrayitum / ko vā viśeṣaḥ, yad upalakṣaṇārtham āśritasya plutasya sattvaṃ gṛhyeta na kāryiṇaḥ ? tatra kecid āhuḥ---satyaṃ, vartamānatvam api saṃbhavati bhāvitvam api bhāvitvaṃ tv ihāśritaṃ iti tan na yuktam / sāṃpratikābhāve hi sarvatra bhāvitvam ādrīyate / api cānya eva pakṣaḥ saṃbhavati---‘‘yathoddeśaṃ saṃjñāparibhāṣam’’ āśrayitavyam iti //

[7] evam api yat siddhe pragṛhyasaṃjñākāryaṃ tat [plutasya] na sidhyati, kāryakāle hi saṃjñāparibhāṣe / anunāsikaparyudāsadeśāyāṃ pragṛhyasaṃjñāyāṃ siddhaḥ plutaḥ / tatra trimātrā(? -tra)tvāt pragṛhyasaṃjñāyām asatyāṃ agnī3 ity atrānunāsikaparyudāso na prāpnoti / evaṃ tarhi kiṃ na etena [kārya]kālaṃ [saṃjñāparibhāṣam] iti / yathoddeśam eva bhaviṣyati / kathaṃ punar i[da]m abhyupagamamātreṇa śakyaṃ labdhum ? tatra kecid āhuḥ---ihāntareṇa guṇaṃ [pradhānāni] saṃbandhaṃ pratipadyante / upakārabhedāc ca pradhānānāṃ dvayī pravṛttir dṛṣṭā / kadācit rājā harmyā[? -styā]dideśa[m u]paśliṣyati, kadācit harmyā[? -styā]dayo rājadeśam upanīyante / tatra yadā svakālā[va]sthite saṃjñāparibhāṣe guṇakālaṃ kāryāṇy upanipatanti, tadā pradhānam api guṇadeśavarti svakāryapratipattyarthaṃ bhavati yathā [yathā] nimittam udakadeśagamanam / tatra yadā guṇadeśavarti pradhānaṃ tadāyathoddeśatvam / yadā tu bhinneṣu aṅgabhāvapratipattyrartham abhipre[tasya] kenacit pradhā[ne]nāsaṃyuktasya satyām api nānāvākyavidheyatāyāṃ laghvarthaṃ bhedaparityāge sāmānyāśrayaṇalabdhenābhedakalpe ca (-tvena) tattadupadeśe prakaraṇotkarṣeṇa kriyamāṇe pradhānaviyuktasya [saṃjñāparibhāṣasya] svakālānurodhena [na] kiṃcit kāryam asti, yataḥ kasyāṃcid avasthāyām asvātantryeṇāśrayaṇamarhedata autpattikenārthavyāpārakrameṇa pradhānagāmitvaṃ, [tadā] pradhānavākyenāvibhāgāpattau kāryakālatvam //

[8] anye tv anyathaitat dvaitaṃ samarthayante / anekapradhānaviṣayeṣu hi guneṣu pratipradhānaṃ ca bhe[de]na pravṛttir dṛṣṭā sraganulepanādiṣu, abhedena ca . . . pratipādi[te]ṣu [guṇeṣu] tu . . . / tatra pratipradhānaṃ bhedakalpanāyāṃ svakālād apracyutānām eva oradhānānām anugrahe saṃbhavati kāryakālatvam / abhedapravṛttau tv asaṃbhavo yad ekena guṇenānekaṃ pradhā[naṃ] svasmin kāle vyavashtitam api sambandhaṃ yāyād iti / guṇe svātmany avasthite viśiṣṭenātmasaṃskāreṇādhīne [sati] pradhānāni yugapat guṇakālam anupatanti [iti] yathodeśarūpa[tās tu] / dvayam api nopapadyate / evaṃ hi vakṣyati---‘‘yadi sapādāyāṃ saptādhyāyyāṃ pādo[no] 'dhyāyo 'siddho ya iha pañcamīnirdeśāḥ saptamīnirdeśāś ca’’ teṣām asiddhatvāt ‘tasminn iti nirdiṣṭe pūrvasya’ ‘tasmād ity uttarasya’’’ ity eṣa niyamo na prāpnoti / ‘‘naiṣa doṣaḥ / yady apīdaṃ tatrāsiddhaṃ tat tv iha siddham / katham / kāryakālaṃ hi saṃjñāparibhāṣam’’ iti / tatra yathaiva kāryakālānāṃ guṇānām ekavākyabhāve pūrvaparavyapadeśahānena (? -nā)pṛthakkāryatvāc cedam asmin siddham ity etat [na] vidyate, evaṃ guṇadeśatve pradhānānām ekavākyabhāve pūrvaparavyapadeśavidhir [na] vijñāyate / 

[9] evam ubhayasmin doṣaṃ parikalpya doṣāntaraprayojanāpa(? -pradarśanāpe)kṣāyāṃ praśnaḥ / [a] īdādayo dvivacanaṃ pragṛhyā iti cedanty asya vidhiḥ (vā. 1) / īdantādeḥ pacete iti / yo 'tredādir na tad dvivacanam / naiva kaścid ekāro dvivacanam astīti manyamāna āha---vacanād bhaviṣyati / dvivacanāvayave dvivavacanaśabdo vartate ity abhiprāyaḥ / yathā ‘liṅsicau’ ity atrātmanepadāvayave ātmanepadam iti vyapadiśyate ‘liṅaḥ sīyuḍ’ (A 3.4.102) iti / asti vacane prayojanam / [kim] / khaṭve iti / yady apy etat na mukhyaṃ dvivacanaṃ, tathāpy atrādivadbhāvenāsti dvivacanavyapadeśa iti / trapuṇī jatunī ityādau tu pūrvabhaktatvāt numaḥ mukhyam eva dvivacanam iti nopanyastam / [ā] īdādyantam iti ced ekasya vidhiḥ / (vā. 2) khaṭve iti / īdūtoḥ kevalayor eva saṃbhava iti na tāvad āśrīyate / kathaṃ punar ananyārtham ārabhyamāṇā ādyantavatparibhāṣā nāśritā ? ucyate / nāvaśyaṃ granthagauravam eva gauravaṃ bhavati / kiṃ tarhi ? pratipatti[gauravaṃ, prakriyā]gauravam api / tad etasmin pakṣe 'yam eva doṣa upādīyate prakriyāgauravam abhyupagantavyaṃ bhavatīti / itaras tu yady anyaḥ prakāro 'sti nirdoṣa evaṃ prakriyāgauravaṃ śakyam anabhyupagantuṃ, na tv atrāsti anyaḥ prakāra iti tam āśrayati ‘na vādyantavattvāt’ iti (vā. 3)

[i] etad api tu nāśrayiṣyāmīty āha---athavaivaṃ vakṣyāmi / īdādyantaṃ yad dvivacanāntam iti / nanu ca ‘saṃjñāvidhau pratyayagrahaṇe tadantavidhir nāsti’ iti / satyam, saṃjñāvidhau pratyayagrahaṇe tadantavidhiḥ pratiṣidhyate / na tv iha dvavacanaṃ saṃjñitvenāśrīyate, kiṃ tarhi ? īdādyantam, tadviśeṣaṇaṃ dvivacanaṃm, īdādyantaṃ pragṛhyasaṃjñaṃ bhavati tac ced dvivacanāntam iti / kathaṃ punaḥ śrutyā prakaraṇena vā kāryiṇaḥ saṃnidhānam antareṇedādibhis tadantavidhiḥ ? [na kva] cid asti / pūrvas tarhi pakṣo nopapadyata īdādayo dvivacanam iti / kecid āhuḥ---kāryakālaṃ hi saṃjñāparibhāṣaṃ yatho[dde]śaṃ ca / tatra kā[ryakā]le padādhikāre pragṛhyasaṃjñāvyāpārāt padaṃ kāryitvena saṃnihitam / tatra yathā ‘ṣyaṅaḥ saṃprasāraṇm’ (A 6.1.13) iti sāmarthyalabhyasaṃniyogaṃ pūrvapadaṃ kāryitvenāśrīyate, evaṃ padasya kāryiṇaḥ īdādibhiḥ tadantavidhir iti / tathodeśatāyāṃ tu pragṛhyakāryeṣu anapekṣiteṣu pradhānaṃ kāryi[pa]daṃ kāryitve[na] nāśrīyate / evaṃ padasya kāryiṇo 'bhāvāt yathāśrutaparikalpanā / atha vā kāryakāla eva satīdādīnāṃ prādhānyāt tadantavidhyabhāvaḥ, na tv idam upapadyate / ‘yathoddeśaṃ hi saṃjñābhāṣam’ abhyupagatam / evaṃ tarhi pūrvaṃ dvivacanena tadantavidhiḥ ‘pratyayagrahaṇe yasmāt sa vihita’ iti / na hy atrāvaśyaṃ kāryiṇaḥ saṃ[ni]dhānam apekṣyate / tato dvivacanāntasya saṃjñinaḥ īdādibhis tadantavidhiḥ / na hīdādyantaṃ dvivacanāntam iti ayam uccārakramas tantram / dvivacanāntam īdādyantam etat bhavati / evaṃ tu sati pratyayarūpeṇa saṃjñina āśrayāt tadantavidhir apy avasito darśanamātrāt, evam api / dṛṣṭo hi prātipadikasaṃjñāyāṃ tadantavidhiḥ ‘‘kṛttaddhitasamāsāś ca (A 1.2.46)’’ iti / caturtha eva pakṣe tadantavidhyanupapattir uktā / sā ihāpi draṣṭavyā / doṣāntareṇa vā nvṛtte(? -tter) vihitatvāt ayaṃ doṣo nābhihitaḥ / īdādyantaṃ . . . cet luki pratiṣedhaḥ / kathaṃ punar dvivacanāntasya samāsaḥ, yāvatā ‘‘saṃkhyāviśeṣo vyaktābhidhānam upasarjanaviśeṣaṇaṃ cayoga’’ ity ekārthībhāvakṛtā viśeṣā varṇitāḥ ? tathā ca ‘‘dvivacanabahuvacanāntānām asamāsa’’ ity uktam / tatra kecid āhuḥ---lukeyam anvākhyātā vibhaktir iṣyate / na ca sataḥ śabdasya lukānvākhyānamam asti / tatra vākye dṛṣṭasya dvivacanasya vṛttiviṣaye saṃkhyāviśeṣaparityāgāt, yaivāsau svābhāvikī nivṛttiḥ sā lukvyāpāreṇānvākhyāyate / tathā cāha---‘‘avyayādāpo lugvacanā[n a]rthakyaṃ liṅgābhāvāt’’ (A 2.4.82 vā. 1) / kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ bhavānāpo lugvacanaṃ na mṛṣyati, vibhakter mṛṣyati ? asty atra viśeṣaḥ / pratyayalakṣaṇam ācāryaḥ prārthayamāno vibhakter lukam ārabhata’’ ity uktam / uhayaṃ hi svabhāvato nivartate / yasya tu nivṛttatvākhyāne prayojanam asti tasya nivṛttir ākhyāyate iti / tat tu na yuktaṃ prasaṅgābhāvāt / prasaktādarśanaṃ hi lopasaṃjñam / yat tu samāsārthavākyaṃ loke 'vyaktam adṛṣṭaprayogaṃ samāsasya pratipādakaṃ tatraikārthībhāvaviṣaye svārthamātropayogiṣūpasarjaneṣu svabhedenānarthiṣu vyapadeśārtham abhedenaikatvasaṃkhyāyām āśritāyāṃ bhedasaṃkhyāviṣayāṇāṃ vacanānām aprasaṅgo metava(? bhavet) yat ekārthībhāvaviṣayāṇy upasarjanapadāni anāśritabhedasaṃkhyāni svapadārthamātropakārīṇi vijñāyante / śabdāntaropasaṃhitānāṃ tūpasarjanānāṃ saty api pradhānārthāskande saṃkhyāviśeṣavyaktāv anivṛttāyām eva sāmarthyāt [svi]śaurpikādiṣu mūlyaparimāṇāvadhāraṇārthe hi dviśabdaprayogaḥ saṃkhyāviśeṣasyānāśraye na syāt / na ca prayoganimittāpākaraṇaṃ yuktam / evaṃ māsajāte prayogaviśeṣasaṃpratyayaḥ / goṣucare 'navayavadravyasaṃyogārthaṃ vibhaktyupādānam / pratyākhyānapakṣe ‘jātyākhyāyām-’ (A 1.2.58) ity ekavacanam / vyavasthitavibhāṣā hi goviṣṭhira ekavacanam eva dṛśyate / varṣāsuje rūḍhiśabdasaṃpratyayaḥ / dviputra ubhayabhārya iti pūrvapadopādānasāmarthyāt / tathā hi [pra]tya[ya] eva tasya svārthaḥ / tathā vṛddhinimittādiṣu nimittaśabdopādānasāmarthyaṃ yathāsaṃbhavaṃ draṣtavyam / tathā śabdāntaropasaṃdhānād avyaktaviśeṣe 'py upasarjane saṃkhyāviśeṣopādānasāmarthyaṃ dṛṣṭam / tad yathā / gārgyakulādiṣu pratyayasya bhāvābhāvābhyāṃ bhedavyaktiḥ / godau varaṇā ity atideśabhājivikāradarśanāt / tyauṣmākastāvako yuṣmatputraḥ ity ādeśarūpāt / tatra kumāryagāre 'pi prakṛtibhāva eva dvivacanakāryaṃ saṃkhyāviśeṣopādānavyaktini[mi]ttaṃ syāt / etasyāṃ kalpanāyāṃ rājapuruṣādiṣu viśeṣavācinaḥ śabdasyābhāvāṭ viśeṣo na [ga]myate / dāsyāḥ putrādiṣu caikavacanasaṃnidhānād ekatvaṃ gamyate ity āpannam / tatraikārthībhāvakṛtaś cāyaṃ saṃkhyāviśeṣa ity etan nopapadyate / yac coktaṃ ‘‘nanu ca naitenaivaṃ hi [bhavi]tavyam’’ / na hi śabdanimittakena nāmārthena bhavitavyam, arthanimittakena śabdena bhavitavyam / tad evaṃ dṛśyatām artharūpam evaitad evaṃjātīyakaṃ yenātra viśeṣo na gamyata iti / avaśyaṃ caitad evaṃ vijñeyam / yo hi manyate yo 'sau viśeṣavācī śabdas tadasāṃnidhyād atra viśeṣo na gamyata iti’’ iha tasya viśeṣo gamyeta---apsucaro [gaṣucaro] varṣāsuja iti / tat vipratiṣidhyate / tasmān na śabdaviśeṣakṛte saṃkhyāyāḥ parityāgopādāne, ekārthībhāvakṛta evāyaṃ viśeṣaḥ / tad etat samarthavārttike eva nirṇeṣyate / saptamyāmarthagrahaṇaṃ (tu) jñāpakaṃ . . . / saptamīty eva śrūyamāṇāyām, lukā luptāyāṃ tu pratyayalakṣaṇena siddhe 'rthagrahaṇaṃ pragṛhyaprakaraṇe pratyayalakṣaṇanivṛttyartham / etat tarhi jñāpakārtham arthagrahaṇaṃ kartavyam / ādyantavat paribhāṣāṃ cānyārthāṃ satīṃ mā śriśriyam ity ayaṃ pakṣa āśritaḥ / tatrākartavya[pra]kriyābhyupagamo na yukta iti / 

[i] evaṃ tarhi īdādibhir dvivacanaṃ viśeṣayiṣyāmaḥ / tṛtīye pakṣe īdādīnāṃ dvivacanasya ca parasparam a[na]bhisaṃbandhe samudāyaṃ prati saṃnipātaḥ / iha tu prāk samudāyasaṃbandhādīnādibhir dvivacanaṃ viśeṣyate / tato labdhe tadantavidhā[v] īdādyantatayā viśabde[? viśiṣṭe]na dvicananena samudāyaḥ saṃbadhyate / ‘pratyayagrahaṇa’ iti ca dvitīyas tadantavidhir bhavati / yady evam, īdādīnāṃ samudāyena sākṣāt saṃbandhābhāvāt tadantam īdādyantam iti īdādibhiḥ saṃbandhapradarśanaṃ samudāyasya nopapadyate / naiṣa doṣaḥ / ākṣiptatadantavidhitvādīdādiśabdasyedādyanta[tva]m eva pratinirdiśati / tatra yāvad uktaṃ syād īdādīti tāvad īdādyantam iti / evam apy aśukle vastre śukle samapadyetāṃ śuklyāstāṃ vastre ity atra prāpnoti / kumāryagāre hi yad dvivacanam ‘os’ iti na tadīdādyantam iti, iha tu śuklaśabdāt subantāt cviḥ supluk, tataḥ cvilopaḥ, ‘asya cvau’ (A 7.4.32) iti ītvaṃ, taddhitād avyayād ekavacanaṃ, tasya ‘avyayāt . . .’ (A 2.4.82) iti luk, tatra vibhaktyādeśa īkāras tenedādyantadvivacanānta eṣa samudāyaḥ pratyayalakṣaṇena bhavati / yas tv ayam īkāraḥ śrūyate yaṇādeśaprāptipradarśanārtham ayam udāhriyate / tatra yeṣāṃ tāvat granthaḥ ‘tatra hi īdādi ca dvivacanaṃ, tadantaṃ ca bhavati pratyayalakṣaṇena’ iti, teṣām īdādyantam īdādiśabdenābhidhīyate iti draṣṭavyam / īdādyantaṃ dvivacanāntam ity arthaḥ / yeṣāṃ tu granthaḥ ‘atra hi īdādyantaṃ śrūyate, dvivacanāntaṃ ca bhavati pratyayalakṣaṇena’ iti, teṣāṃ yady api īdādyantasya saṃprati śrutir na vidyate, tathāpi vākyaśeṣasamarthanād adoṣaḥ / īdādyantaṃ ca śrūyate sūtre ‘īdādi dvivacanam’ iti, īdādibhir dvivacanasya sūtre tadantavidhir nāśrita ity arthaḥ / yaś cārthaḥ sūtre sa evaṃ parigṛhītaḥ, tasyānena granthenodāharaṇe samavāyaṃ darśayati---‘dvivacanāntaṃ ca bhavati pratyayalakṣaṇena’ / anye tu dvayoḥ pakṣayor imaṃ doṣanimittopasaṃhāram āhuḥ ‘‘etad dhi īdādyantaṃ ca śrūyate’’ / kurmāryagāram ity atra eva tṛtīye pakṣe doṣaḥ ‘dvivacanntaṃ ca bhavati pratyayalakṣaṇena’ / caturthe pakṣe iha ‘śuklyās tām’ iti viśiṣṭaṃ doṣaṃ pratijñāya tadupapattya[na]bhidhānād akṛte śībhāve īkārāntatāyām anupajātāyāṃ [pū]rvo lug bhaviṣyatīty āha---idam iha saṃpradhāryam iti / . . . kṛte 'pi śībhāve luk prāpnoti akṛte 'pi / na tu luki kṛte śībhāvasya prāptir asti sthāninno 'bhāvāt / sthānivadbhāvo [hy e]vaṃprakāreṣu nāsti / yady api bhūyiṣṭhāni lopa eva sthānivadbhāvodāharaṇāni, evam api yatra tannimittam anyasya kāryaṃ tatra sthānivadbhāvo, na tu lopasyaiva kāryam / ‘‘antaraṅgam api vidhiṃ bahiraṅgo luk bādhate’’ iti tv antaraṅgatvaṃ śībhāvasya nopanyastam / athāpi kathaṃcit nityo luk syāt / yadi lakṣaṇam anapekṣitaviṣayaṃ(? -viśeṣaṃ) bādhyate [i]ty eṣa pakṣaḥ, kāryabādhāpekṣāyāṃ vā yadi bhedān parityajya [ā]kṛtaviṣaye śāstrapravṛttiḥ, evaṃ saty api sthānyādeśabhede supjātir aviśiṣṭeti tasyaiva lugvidhānāt nityatvam / evam api doṣaḥ / vakṣyaty etad iti / nanu coktaṃ kṛttaddhitāntaṃ prātipadikasaṃjñāyām āśrayaṇīyam iti / satyam / evam etat / pakṣas tu kriyate---arthavadgrahaṇānuvṛtter idam eva prayojanaṃ laukikasyārthasya pratyāsannatā yathāśrīyeteti / api ca, īdādyantaṃ dvivacanam ity eṣa pakṣaḥ ādyantavadbhāvaṃ mā śiśriyam iti prakriyāgauravabhayāt parityaktaḥ / atrāpi ca agnī iti vāyū iti evam artham asya ādyantavadbhāva eṣitavyaḥ / aparihāryaṃ caivaṃ loke / sa eva madhyamaḥ pakṣaḥ / tṛtīyapakṣakalpaś caturtha iti dvitīyo madhyamaḥ, caturṇām api dvitīyo madhyama eva / athāsmin pakṣe ‘śuklyāstām’ iti kathaṃ pragṛhyasaṃjñā na bhavati ? kāryiṇo 'bhāvāt / dvivacanaṃ cātra kāryi, na cāsat kāryi vacanenāpy āśrayituṃ [śakyam] / evam ete catvāraḥ pakṣāḥ---(a) īdādayo [dvivacanam] (ā) īdādyantaṃ dvivacanaṃ (i) īdādyantaḥ samudāyo dvivacanāntaḥ (ī) īdādyantaṃ dvivacanāntam iti / 

adaso māt // 1.1.12 //

[1] māt pragṛhyasaṃjñāyām iti / viṣayopalakṣaṇaṃ nyāsanirdeśo vā / yady anto nyāsaḥ kriyeta, yatra saṃbandhīyadaso nāśrī[ye]ran, yatra ca neṣyate, taṃ viṣayaṃ paryudasya pragṛhyasaṃjñocyeta, na syād ayaṃ doṣaḥ / etasmiṃs tu nyāse nimittasya kāryiṇaś cāpy asiddhatvāt tam āśritasya kryasyāpy asiddhatvam / tasyeti kṛtsnam eva nimittanimittisamudāyam abhedenāśrayati / tasya īttvasya ūttvasya [ca] / cakārāt matvasyeti gamyate / trayāṇām asiddhatvāt ayāv ekādeśaprasaṅgaḥ / yuktaḥ tāvat īdādīnām asidhatvād yat pragṛhyasaṃjñā na syāt / sthānināṃ tu pūrvam eva nivartitatvād ayādīnāṃ kutaḥ prasaṅgaḥ ? api ca, yathaiva pragṛhyasaṃjñāyām īdādīnām asiddhatvam, evaṃ siddhayaṇādiṣv api yaṇādeśādipratibandhamātram eva ca pragṛhyasaṃjñayā prārthyate iti mā bhūt pragṛhyasaṃjñā / naitad asti / śāstrāsiddhatvam āśrīyate / śāstranimittā ca śabdeṣu buddhipariṇatiḥ / buddhipratipādakaśāstrasyāsattve prākṛtenaiva rūpeṇa sthāny avatiṣṭhate / tatra sthānino nivṛtter apratipannatvādayādayaḥ prāpnuvanti / amī āsata ity ayādeśo, amū āsāte ity āvādeśaḥ, amī atra iti ‘eṅaḥ padāntāt-’ (A 6.1.101) ity ekādeśaḥ / nanu ca dvipadāśrayatvāt bahiraṅgā ayādayaḥ īdādiṣv ekapadāśrayeṣu asiddhās tatra pūrva eva nāsti yato 'siddhatvaṃ vijñāyate pūrvatrāsiddhaparibhāṣayā / 

śe // 1.1.13 //

[1] śruter aviśiṣṭatvād viśeṣaparigrahābhāvāc ca ‘kāśe’ ity atrāpi prāpnoti / tatra pratiṣedho vaktavyaḥ / tad yathā / dāgrahaṇād atiprasaṅge 'dāp iti pratiṣedha ucyate / pratyāsatter, avyabhicārād, asādhāraṇatvāc cāntareṇāpi rūpagrahaṇaṃ svagrahaṇād evārthanivṛttau rūpapratipattau siddhāyāṃ rūpagrahaṇamātra bahiraṅgasyāpy arthasya rūpavadaṅgīkaraṇārtham / tena yathaiva tulyārthānāṃ rūpāntarasaṃyuktānām asaṃpratyayaḥ, tadvat tulye 'pi tadrūpe 'narthakānām iti / tatrānarthakasya śaśabdasyārthavatā saṃyuktasyaikavacanena sahaikādeśe vaiguṇyād arthavadanarthakayor arthāntaraṃ na kiñcidupajātam ity anarthako 'yaṃ śeśabadaḥ / 

[2] evam api hariśe babhruśe iti / tatra hi ṅimatvarthīyayoḥ pratyayayoḥ pratyayārthenārthavattvād arthavatoḥ saṃnipāte samudāyānām avayavadharmānugamād avayavārthenārthavattvāḍ ayaṃ samudāyo 'rthavān iti / jahatsvārthāyāṃ tu vṛttau prakṛter[r a]rthaparityāgāt pratyayārtha eva vibhaktyarthena yujyate / [te]na prakṛtir anarthikā / kevalaprayoganivṛttyarthe tu paravacane sati sādhutvāyaiva pratyayasya prakṛtir avatiṣṭhate ity arthavaśāt prāpnoti / lakṣaṇoktatvād adoṣaḥ / 

[3] atha vā iti / saty apy avayavayor artha[va]ttve samudāyasya śeśabdasyānyo 'rtho na vidyata ity ānarthkyam / tat katham iha saṃkhyākarmādayo hariśaśabdavācyasyārthasya vibhaktyābhidhīyante, hariśaśabdena vābhihitāḥ pañca kathaṃ vibhaktyā vyajyate, tenā(? yena) viśiṣṭhasya pratyayārthasya saṃkhyākarmādibhir yogaḥ ? tatra jahatsvārthāyām ajahatsvārthāyāṃ vā vṛttau ‘‘prkṛtipratyayau pratyayārthaṃ saha brūtaḥ’’ iti saprakṛtikaḥ saṃghātaḥ saṃkhyākarmādyādhāram arthaṃ bravīti / ataḥ kevalena śeśabdena yo 'rtha ucyate tasya vibhaktyarthena saṃbandhābhāvāt śe ity etasya samudāyasyānarthakyam / tatra yathaiva sann api kevalaḥ prakṛtyartho vibhaktyarthena nābhisaṃbadhyate tathā kevalaḥ pratyayārtho 'pi iti / 

nipāta ekājānāṅ // 1.1.14 //

[1] nipāta ity anenā[ci] viśeṣyamāṇe tadantavidhyaprasaṅgād doṣaprasaṅgo nottiṣṭhati / viparīte tu viśeṣyatve uttiṣṭhati / [nipāta iti kimartham ?] samudāyasyārthe prayogāt pratyayarūpāv adhiparigrahe ca niyamābhāvāt nipātasyaivārthavattā saṃbhavati / pratyayasya rū[pa]dvayam / arthaprayukte varṇavadānarthkyaṃ pratyayānām / kadācic ca kaścid eva oratyayarūpasyāvadhir abhyupagamyate / tad yathā / viṃśatiśabde śatijvā tiśabdo vā, aikāgārikaśabde iko vāgāriko vā / tathā keṣāṃcit vikaraṇāḥ prāk kartari utpadyante / vikaraṇāntāt svārthe sārvadhātykasyaityati(? -pratyayo) bhavati / atroṣyate / satyam / laukikasyārthasyopādāne nityatvāt śabdānāṃ tulyarūpapūrvabhāgānāṃ padānāṃ, vākyaikadeśapadasarūpāvayavānāṃ ca samudāyānāṃ, varṇavadānarthakyam avayavānām / teṣāṃ tu śāstre tulyarūpatvt tattvenāśritānām anvayavyatirekasamadhigamyenārthenārthavttā kāryārthaṃ parigṛhyate / yadi tarhy arthavttā pratyayānām āśrīyate, vyākaraṇe yad uktaṃ ‘‘kṛttaddhitāntaṃ caiva hy arthavatm, na kevalā kṛttaddhitāḥ’’ iti, tasya vyāghāto bhavati / naiṣa doṣaḥ / pratya[ya]syārthena nityayogād ‘arthavat’ ity anuvṛtteḥ punaḥ śrutir anarthikā mā bhūt iti kaścidarthaḥ sann api parityajyate vyabhicārābhāvāt / śāstrīya eva cārthaḥ prātipadujasaṃjñāyāṃ nimittatvenāśrīyate / itarathā hi kṛttaddhitāntasyāpy avibhaktikasya pratyayena tulyam ānarthakyam / tasmāt pratyayārthenārthavattvāt cakārātra ity atra ṇalaḥ pragṛhyasaṃjñā prasajyeta / tathā ‘atater ḍaḥ aḥ’ / a āgaccheti / 

[2] yadi tatpuruṣaḥ samānādhikaraṇaḥ nipātasyācā tadantavidhir vijñāyeta, kevalo 'py apy ādyantavadbhāvāt pratīyeta / evaṃ ca [ajanteṣu] nipāteṣu pragṛhyasaṃjñākāryasaṃbhavāt acgrahaṇam anarthakaṃ prasajyeta / ato 'cgrahaṇāt kaścid eva viśeṣa āśrayiṣyate, kaścit parityakṣyate / tatrānyataraviśeṣaparityāge yadi tadanta upādīyeta, kevalaḥ parityajyeta, āṅaḥ praghyasaṃjñāyā asaṃbhavāt paryudāso 'narthakaḥ syāt / ato liṅgaviśeṣād acśrutiḥ kevalam evācam avaru[ṇa]ddhi, tadantaṃ ca nirasyati / syād evaṃ yady akriyamāṇā(? -ṇe) 'jgrahaṇe halantāḥ pragṛhyasaṃjñāyām abhisaṃbadhyeran / asti cet saṃjñā(? -jña)yā halantānāṃ saṃbandhaḥ, halantanivṛttyarthaṃ syād ajgrahaṇam / na khalu saṃjñāsaṃbandhamātrād eva doṣo jāyeta / kāryeṣu cāsatsv aparārthā saṃjñā, mā bhūd anarthikā śāstrapravṛttir iti, sāmānyābhidhāne 'pi tathābhūtaṃ viṣayaṃ nāvagāhate / tatra kāryārtham abhyupagacchataḥ pragṛhyasaṃjñā, na kiñcit prayojanam asti / doṣavyudāsārthaṃ vā viśeṣam upādadānasya na kaścit doṣo 'sti yannivṛttyarthaṃ viśeṣa upādīyeta / saṃhitādhikāre hi prakṛtibhāva ārabhyate / tatra ca halāṃ na kiñcit kāryam ucyate yannivṛttyarthaḥ prakṛtibhāvaḥ syāt / nanu cādho 'gamannamo 'kārṣīd iti rorutve prakṛtibhāvaḥ syāt / iha satām ādeśinām ādeśanivṛttyatthaḥ prakṛtibhāvo, na cātra rur vidyate ‘pūrvatrāsiddham’ (A 8.2.1) ity asiddhatvāt / yadi na vidyate, uttvam api na prāpnoti / āśrayād uttve siddhatvam / iha tu vikāriṇaḥ prakṛtibhāva iti sa vikārī nāsti yasya prakṛtibhāvaḥ kriyeta / yaś cāsti sakāraḥ, na tasya kiṇcit kāryam ucyate / nanu ca samudāyasya prakṛtibhāvād avayavasyādeśo nivarteta / saty api samudāyasya prakṛtibhāve sa nāsti yasyādeśa iti kasyādeśo nivarteta / yaś cāsti sakāro na tasyottvam ucyate / evaṃ roḥ prakṛtibhāvāpekṣāyām avidyamānatvāt uttve cāśrayāsiddhatvāt sakārasya ca kāryāvacanāt uttvam atra na śakyaṃ nivartayitum / tasyāt halanteṣu na kaścid [naiva] doṣo na prayojanam / 

[3] evam api ajgrahaṇād anyataranivṛttau kriyamāṇāyām ādyantavadbhāvasyāntāśrayavidhyadhīnatvāt nimittanimittabhāvāt tadantavidhinā tv ekatra kāryāṇām asiddhatvāt dvayoḥ paribhāṣayoḥ sāvakāśayoḥ iti / niravakāśasya bādhanāsaṃbhavāt / yena vidhis tadantsyeti paribhāṣāyā avakāśaḥ cayaḥ jayaḥ / ādyantaparibhāṣāyā [avakāśaḥ] ‘‘dhātoḥ’’, ‘‘antaḥ’’, ‘‘midaco 'ntyāt’’, ‘‘aco 'ntyādi ṭi’’ (A 6.1.162; 159; 1.1.47; 1.1.64) yatra sākṣād antagrahaṇam asti / samavasthitayoḥ iti / iha prāptayoḥ, svaviṣaye vā samavasthānāt samavasthitayoḥ, yena vidhis tadantasyeti saṃjñātve 'py asya sāmānyaśabdenābhodhānam / yadyathā / ‘adhikāro nāma triprakāra’ ity adhikāraśabdo na idaṃ vicārayāmaḥ ko 'tra buddhikramaḥ kasyāś copādānaṃ yuktam iti / ādyantaparibhāṣāyās tāvat tadantavidhinimittatvād asaṃnidhāne nimittasyopādānaṃ na saṃbhavati / tadmād ‘ac’ ity eṣā saṃjñā tadantān tāvat saṃjñinaḥ pratyāyayati / acgrahaṇasāmarthyāt anyataranivṛttau tadantavidher upasaṃprāptasyāyuktaḥ parityāgaḥ / satyam, kramāt tadantavidhir evopādīyeta / kramāt tu liṅgaṃ balavat iti liṅgavirodhinas tadantavidher āṅparyudāsena nivṛttau kṛtāyāmādyantaparibhāṣayaiva vyavasthānaṃ bhavati / bhaved vyavasthānaṃ yadi nimittam antareṇa nimittavatāṃ pravṛttir labhyeta / tadantavidhau tv akriyamāṇe tannimittasyādyantavato 'bhāvāt tato vyavasthānam / evaṃ tarhy anenobhayor api paribhāṣayor vyāvṛttasāmānyayor gṛhyamāṇā evācaḥ pragṛhyasaṃjñāṃ lapsyante / tāvāt hi vidheyam / tac caivaṃ sati siddham / bhavet siddham, ayaṃ tu grantho nopapadyate iyam iha paribhāṣā bhaviṣyati ādyantavad ekasmin iti / parārthatvāt paribhāṣāṇāṃ tatkalanivṛttau satyāṃ gṛhyamāṇānāṃ pragṛhyasaṃjñayā saṃbandhe, yad asyāḥ paribhāṣāyāḥ phalaṃ tad astīti phalasya bhāvāt paribhāṣāyāḥ bhāva upapadyate / tad yathā / ‘ekaṃ cet ṅit kitau’ iti / atha vājgrahaṇād anyataraparityāge liṅgāc ca tadantavidhinimittatvāc cādyantavatparibhāṣāyā apravṛttau tadantavidhir upasthānamātreṇaivādyantaparibhāṣām anugṛhya kṛtārtho nivartate / tadyathā / liṅgopādānāt svārthā tadantavidheḥ pravṛttir na saṃbhavati, tasmiṃś cāsati liṅgānugrāhiṇyādyantavatparibhāṣā nopatiṣṭhate, tasmāt parārthaṃ tadantavidher atropasthānam / sa tāvatā kṛtārthe liṅgavirodhāt svārthaṃ na nirvartayati / atha vā halāṃ kāryam asti nāstīti saṃjñāpradeśeṣv apicāte teṣv eve(? -ṣv eva vicāryate / tathai)vāntaraṅgatvād ubhayoḥ paribhāṣayor upasthāne sati ajgrahaṇād anyataranivṛttau liṅgād ādyantaparibhāṣayā vyavasthānaṃ bhaviṣyati / 

[4] evaṃ tarhy ekagrahaṇam acsamudāyanivṛttyrathaṃ kriyate, anena caikagrahaṇena parāṅgabhūteṣu upāttasaṃkhyāviśeṣeṣu varṇagrhaṇeṣu anyatrāvivakṣitasaṃkhyātvaṃ jñāpyate / ihāpi ca pragṛhyasaṃjñāyā vidhīyamānatvāt saṃjāṃprati guṇabhūtam ajgrhaṇam / guṇabhūteṣu ca saṃkhyāvivakṣitā bhavati / tad yathā / ‘‘paśunā yajeta’’ iti  /nanu ca saṃjñāyā upasarjanatvāt rūḍhasaṃbandhiṣu saṃjñiṣv eva prādhānyam / tatra yuktaivāvivakṣā saṃkhyāyāḥ / satyam, prdeśe saṃjñināṃ prādhānyaṃ, vidhīyamānatvāt saṃjñāyāḥ prādhānyaṃ bhavati / ‘‘śuklaṃ paṭaṃ kuru’’, ‘‘śvetaṃ vāyavyam ālabheta’’ iti / tatrāvivakṣitatve saṃkhyāyā a i u apehīti nipātasamudāyasyācsamudāyasyaikā pragṛhyasaṃjñā prasajyeta / kathaṃ punar nipātasamudāyo nipātagrahaṇena gṛhyate ? ajanurodhād acsamudāyasya caikanipātatvābhāvāt nipātasamjdāyasya gatiḥ syāt / astu, jātinirdeśāt pratyekam api bhaviṣyati / yady evaṃ labhyeta kṛtaṃ syāt / acsamudāye tu saṃjñayābhisaṃbadhyamāne tayaivāvayavānām api kṛtārthatvāt nāsty avayaveṣu punaḥpravṛttiḥ / tadyathā samudāyaikāco dvirvacane kriyamāṇe kṛtārthatvāt samudāyasya avayavānāṃ na bhavati punardvirvacanaṃ, tathāviśeṣena saṃyogasaṃjñāyāṃ punardvayor na bhavati / yadi tarhi samudāyāśrayeṇa kāryeṇāvayavāḥ kṛtārthās teṣāṃ pragṛhyatvāt pratyavayavaṃ prakṛtibhāvo bhaviṣyati / nāsty avayaveṣu punaḥ prakṛtibhāvaḥ / samudāye prakṛtibhāvasya kṛtārthatvāt / tenāntyasya yaṇādeśo na syāt / pūrvayor ādguṇā[yā]deśau syātām / kathaṃ tarhi prātipadikasaṃjñāyāṃ samudāyasyārthavattve varṇeṣv arthavatsu ana[rtha]keṣu cārthavadgrahaṇe prātipadikasaṃjñāyām akriyamāṇe, vanaṃ dhanam iti prativarṇaṃ vibhaktyutpattau nalopaprasaṅgaś codyate ? arthavattāyāṃ tāvat bhinnārthatvāt yathāyathaṃ varṇānām arthāḥ prātipadikasaṃjñānimittatvāya kalpante / ānarthakye 'pi kvacit samudāyasyāparigṛhītatvāt teṣv idaṃ samudāyakāryaṃ vijñāyate / tatrāśritasaṃkhyatvāt nirdeśyaguṇabhūtasyājgrahaṇasya viśeṣaṇam ekagrahaṇaṃ kriyamāṇam ‘anyatra varṇagrahaṇeṣu guṇabhūteṣu viśiṣṭaaṃkhyeṣv anāśritasaṃkhyatvaṃ jāter bhavati’ iti jñāpakam / kim etasya jñāpane prayojanam ? ‘‘halantāc ca’’ (A 1.2.10) iti halgrahaṇe guṇabhūtasya halaḥ sanaḥ kittvātideśe na bhavati saṃkhyāviśeṣasya vivakṣeti / nanu coktaṃ ‘‘na sāmānyena paurvārpayam astīti’’ / nedaṃ vacanam / ārdhadhātuke hi bhinnārthānāṃ yugapadekasyāḥ prakṛter aṅgatvam astīty asaṃbhavāt, ekārthānāṃ ca pratyayānām arthasya caritārthatvāt, paradeśasya vyāptatvāt anekasya pratyayasya paratvasaṃbandho nāsti ity ataḥ sāmānyena paurvāparyaṃ nāstīty ucyate / yatra tulyarūpaḥ pratyayo bhavati tatra jāteḥ paratvam / tadyathā / ‘ekā prakṛtiḥ bahavaś ca yañaḥ’ iti / dambher halgrahaṇasya jātivivakṣitatvād ika uttaratvaṃ na virudhyate //

[5] iha kasmān na bhavati . . . ā evaṃ kila tat / anubandhanivṛttau śrutiviśeṣābhāvāt / yadi kiṃcit anubandhakāle viśiṣṭam artharūpaṃ saṃbandhitvena nirjhātaṃ, nivṛtte 'py anubandhe tenārthenānubandhasahacāriṇā sānuba[ndha]katvam anumīyate / tatreṣadarthe sānubandhakasya dṛṣṭo yoga āṅīṣadarthe / upasargasya ca sānubandhasya pāṭhāt kriyāyogo 'pi sānubandhasya pratīyate / ‘‘āṅmaryādābhividhyoḥ’’ (A 2.1.13) iti ca sānubandhaka eva karmapravacanīyasaṃjñāyāṃ pratinirdeśyate / ato 'trānubandhanivṛttau satyām apy anubandhasya tat sahacāribhir evārthair anumīyate / vākyasmaraṇayor aṅit / vākyaviṣaye ‘ā evaṃ nu manyase’ iti / kaścid eva vākyārthaḥ pūrvavidhiviṣayaḥ pratiṣedhaviṣayo vā vyavasthitaḥ / tasyānyathākaraṇe idaṃ prayujyate---ā evaṃ nu manyase / naivaṃ pūrvam amaṃsthāḥ, saṃprati tv evaṃ manyasa iti pūrvavākyaviṣayo 'yam ākāraḥ prayujyate / anye tv āhuḥ vākyaprakaraṇo vākyāvayavo 'tra vivakṣita iti / atrānarthakasyaivāṅo 'nunāsikādeśavacanāt śuddhasyānarthakyaṃ nipapadyate / ‘ā evaṃ kila tad’ iti smartavyārthaviṣayaḥ pūrvam ākāraḥ prayujyate / tataḥ smṛter uttarakālam ‘ā evaṃ kila tad’ iti nirvacnaṃ kriyate / 

ot // 1.1.15 //

[1] [nipātasamāhāro 'yam] anekārthatāyām satyāṃ niyatam anena nipātasamāhāreṇa bhavitavyam / athārthabhedo nāsti, vyaktam ekanipātatvam / tatra katham etat viruddham ubhayam ekatra saṃbhavati iti ? ucyate / i[m] kam sīm ity aevamādīnām ānarthakyād ekatve samāhāratve vā nityam arthasyāprādurbhāvāt arthabhedābhedanivṛtto 'vadhiparicchedo na saṃbhavati iti śakyaṃ manyate tāvat pratijñātum / ‘o ṣu yātaṃ maruta’ iti ‘ā u su yātam . . . ’ / tatra ‘nipāta ekāc’ iti pragṛhyasaṃjñā prāpnoti / ‘anāṅ’ iti pratiṣedhaḥ / tatra paratvāt pratiṣedhānāṃ balīyastvāt ‘anāṅ’ iti pratiṣedhena bhavitavyam / nanu ca ‘āṅo 'nyaḥ’ vidhir āśrīyate / bhavati cātrokāra ‘āṅo 'nyaḥ’ / antādivadbhāvāt vyapadeśe nityaṃ saṃnihite ‘āṅo 'yam anyaḥ’ ity aśakyo vyapadeṣṭum / kathaṃ tarhi ‘lūyate kedāreṇa’ iti kartur eva karmatvād akarmakavyapadeśaṃ dhātur labhate ? iha karmātideśe svāśrayam akarmatvaṃ kartur na nivartate / na ceha karmāśrayaṃ kiṃcid ucyate / svābhāvikaṃ cāsya kartur akarmatvam / tasmāt tatsaṃbaddhasya dhātvarthasyānāśrīyamāṇe karmāśraye yukto vyapadeśaḥ / tadyathā / ‘brāhmaṇavad asmin kṣatriye vartitavyam’ iti svābhāvikasya kṣatriyatvasyānivṛttatvād ‘abrāhmaṇāya dehi’ iti bhavati svāśrayikriyāviśeṣair abhisaṃbandhaḥ / yathā ca ‘brāhmaṇako grāma ānīyatām’ iti saṃnihite 'pi tasmin kṣatriye brāhmaṇāśrayasyānayanasya vidhīyamnatvāt tdvato grāmasyānayanaṃ bhavati, evam iha ādivadbhāvāt vyapadeśasya saāṃnidhyād asakṛtparyudāso vijñātuṃ śakyaḥ //

[2] evam apy agaur gauḥ samapadyata go 'bhavad ity atra prāpnoti / evaṃ tarhi ‘svaṃ rūpaṃ śabdasya’ (A 1.1.68) iti svagrahaṇāt siddhe rūpasyaiva saṃpratyaye, rūpagrahaṇaṃ bahiraṅgasyāpy arthasya vyākaraṇe rūpavatsaṃpratyayārtham / tatrānarthakanivṛttāv arthaparigrahe sati gauṇamukhyayor mukhye saṃpratyayāt / guṇād āgataḥ gauṇaḥ, mukham iva mukhyaḥ / tatra nedaṃ vacanam / laukiko 'yaṃ nyāyaḥ parigṛhyate / ihāyaṃ gośabdo jātidravyaliṅgasaṃkhyākarmādiṣu vṛtto bāhyaiḥ praiṣakutsanādibhiḥ saṃbadhyate, padāntarābhidheyānāṃ cārthānāṃ viśeṣyabhāvaṃ viśeṣaṇabhāvaṃ vā pratipadyate / tatrāntaraṅga evāpadapratibaddhe 7rthe 'saṃnihite 'yuktā padāntaraviṣayasya bahiraṅgasyārthasya vyapekṣā / gaur anubandhya ity atra vāhīkādir arthaḥ padāntarābhidheyo 'sati padāntaraprayoge na tāvat prādurbhūta ity asāṃnidhyād aśakyam asyāpekṣaṇaṃ kartum iti / nityānityayoḥ khalv apy arthayoḥ saṃnidhāvyabhicāritvāt nityasyārthasya yuktaḥ saṃpratyayaḥ / vyabhicārī hi gośabdasya vāhīkādibhir abhisaṃbandhaḥ, yasmāt kasyacit bāhīkādiṣv avivakṣitam api gotvaṃ bhavati / tathā ye 'pi rūḍhasanbandhāḥ kriyāguṇāḥ mauḍhyāśaucādiviparītāḥ te kadācit vāhīke vivakṣitā bhavanti taikṣṇyapāṭavādayaḥ / tatra virodhiguṇavivakṣāyāṃ gaur ity asya śabdasyātyantam apravṛttiḥ / sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇayoś cāsādhāraṇo yo gośabdasya padārthas tasya gośabdena pratyāyanaṃ nyāyyam / vāhīke tv anekasya gunasya samavāyād yasya yasyārthasya rūḍhasaṃbandhāt kriyāguṇās tatropalabhyante, tadvācinaś ca śabdasya tatrāsti pravṛttir iti yasya yadguṇo vāhīko vivakṣitaḥ, sa tadguṇavṛttiṃ tatra prayuṅkte / tathā ca ‘gauraś co hastī’ iti ‘śakuniḥ’ ity evamādīnāṃ pravṛtter na śakyam etat vaktuṃ ‘gaur evāyam’ iti / tasmāt gaur ity ukte 'ntaraṅgo 'vyabhicārī tatraiva ca pratiniyato yo 'rthas tasya saṃpratyayo bhavati / tasmāt na vāhīko 'nubadhyate / kathaṃ tarhi vāhīke vṛddhyāttve bhavataḥ gaus tiṣṭhati, gāmānayeti ? arthāśraya etad evaṃ bhavati / gauṇamukhyayor arthayor āśraye prātipadikasya sāmānyavṛtter ayaṃ viveko na śakyate nivārayituṃ kim atra gauṇo 'rtha uta mukhya iti / yady apy atra gauṇasyārthasya bījam asti tattvanāvirbhūtanimittam asya nivāraṇe / kasmāt ? savibhaktiko hi śabdaḥ śabdāntareṇārthavivekāvadhāraṇahetunā yadā saṃbaddho bhavati, tadā śabdāntaropajanitavibhāgasya gauṇasyārthasya paricchedako bhavati / asati ca śābdāntaraprayoge mukhya evārthaḥ pratīyate / tasmāt prātipadikārthasya saty apy anekārthabījasaṃbhave vyaktambhedābhidhāne sāmarthyābhāvāt prātipadikāśrayāṇi kāryāṇi śrutimātram āśritya pravartate / atha vā pūrvaṃ tāvat antaraṅgeṇāsādhāraṇena cāvyabhicāriṇā mukhyenārthena prātipadikam abhisaṃbadhyate / mukhyārthavṛttād eva ca vibhaktir utpadyate / aprādurbhūtatvāc cārthāntarasya vibhaktau mukhyārthavṛtter eva śabdasya kāryāṇi kriyante / savibhaktikas tu mukhyārthavṛttiḥ śabdo nirvṛttaḥ svakārye, vāhīkādeś ca śabdāntarasya bhāve mukhyārthavṛttir evārthāntaraṃ pariṇīyate / tatrārthāntarābhisaṃbandhe virodhino 'rthā nivartante / kriyāguṇānāṃ tu rūḍhisambaddhānām avirodhitvād avasthānaṃ bhavati / tasmāt vṛddhyātvavidhānakāle gauṇasyārthasya bahiraṅgasyānupajātatvāt gauṇamukhyayor ity anupasaṃprāptanimitta eṣa nyāyo naitaṃ viṣayam avagāhate / tasmāt pratidvandvino anupasaṃprāptanimittasyārthasyāsaṃnipātāt yady evaṃ jñāpako (?) virodhinimittābhāvād artho mukhyavyapadeśaṃ [na] labhate / saṃbandhiśabdatvāt hi sati gauṇe mukhyavyapadeśaṃ labhate / ato mukhyavyapadeśābhāvāt śabdāśrayaṃ kāryaṃ śabdamātre vidhīyate / virodhyarthābhāvāc ca gauṇamukhyavyapekṣāyām asatyāṃ śabdāśrayatvaṃ vṛddhyāttvayor iti // 

uñaḥ ūṃ // 1.1.17--18 //

[1] ‘uña [ūṃ]’ ity ekayoga ādeśavikalpe śākalyaśruter upayuktārthatvāt yatrādeśo nāsti tasmin viṣaye ‘nipāta ekājanāṅ’ iti pragṛhyasaṃjñā vijñāyate / yogavibhāge tu ādeśasyāpy anapekṣitatvāt śākalyaśrutyā saṃjñāvikalpo vijñāyate / uñaḥ saṃjñāvikalpe cāpi vijñāyamāne yady anunāsika ādeśo vāvacanenāsaṃyukto vidhīyetāty antaṃ sthānino nivṛttiḥ pratīyeta / tad yathā ‘aster bhūḥ’ (A 2.4.52) iti / yogavibhāgāt tu pragṛhyasaṃjñāvikalpe nirjhāte . . . pūrvayoge rūpadvayaṃ bhavati, ‘u iti’ ca ‘viti’ ca / uttarasmiṃś ca yoge nityam ādeśe prāpte vāvacanena saṃyukte ādeśa ucyamāne vibhāṣāśrutisaṃyogāt dve rūpe bhavataḥ ūṃ iti u iti ca, yogadvaye trīṇi rūpāṇi siddhāni bhavanti / 

[2] yeṣāṃ tu granthaḥ ‘kim artho yogavibhāgaḥ śākalyavibhāṣā mā bhūt’ iti teṣāṃ vāvacanam antareṇa niraunāsikasyāpākṛtatvāt pragṛhyasaṃjñā nāstīti kṛtaprakṛtibhāvasya śravaṇaṃ na syāt ‘u iti’ iti / ādeśāpekṣāyāṃ ca satyāṃ saṃjñiṣu rūḍhasaṃbandheṣu guṇabhūtāpi saṃjñā vaiguṇyāt svarūpamātrapadārthikaiva na saṃjñinā saṃbandham upagacchatīti //

īdūtau ca saptamyarthe // 1.1.19 //

[1] iha ‘saṁjñāvidhau pratyayagrahaṇe tadantavidhipratiṣedhāt’ saptamīśabdenātra pratyayamātram uctayate / tatra yadā tāvad īkārokārayoḥ prādhānyaṃ, saptamyā viśeṣaṇabhāvas tadaivaṃ vijñāyate īkārokārau yā saptamī tasyāḥ pragṛhyasaṃjñā bhavati / yadā saptamyāḥ prādhānyam īkārokārayor viśeṣaṇabhāvaḥ tadā īkārokārāntāyāḥ saptamyā abhāvāt tadantavidher atrāpravṛttiḥ / tatrārthagrahaṇam antareṇa yatraiva saptamī śrūyate tatra pragṛhyasaṃjñā syāt sarasī iti / luptāyāṃ tu saptamyāṃ saṃjñino 'bhāvād aviṣayā saṃjñā naiva pravartate / satyāṃ ca pragṛhyasaṃdhānakalpanāyāṃ saptamīsahacarite 'rthe yadvartate tasya sāmarthyābhāvād api saṃpratyayaḥ syāt / arthagrahaṇāt tu ‘somo gaurī adhi śritaḥ’ (ṚV 9.12.3) iti nivṛttapratyayasyāpi saptamyarthavṛtteḥ pragṛhyasaṃjñā siddhā bhavati /

[2] nātra saptamī lupyate / pūrvasavarṇaṃ dīrghatvaṃ ca bādhitvā āḍbhāvaś ca āmbhāvaś ca prasajyete / evaṃ tarhi sarvatraiva lukaḥ pravṛttiḥ  / tatra pradhānāsaṃbhave saptamyarthasaṃcāraṇāt īkārokārāntasya śabdarūpasya grahaṇaṃ vijñāsyāmaḥ / grahaṇaṃ vijñāsyāmaḥ / nedaṃ vacanāl labhyam / pradhānakalpanāviṣaye vacanāt guṇataḥ kalpayitum alabhyam / katham ? iha halantādyāsaptamyutpannā sā ‘ityāḍiyājīkārāṇām upasaṃkhyānam’ iti īkāre vihite ‘sarasī’ iti īkāro yā saptamīti mukhyā kalpanā saṃbhavati / tatsaṃbhave ca nivṛttavibhaktikeṣu na pravartate pragṛhyasaṃjñā somo gaurī adhi śrita iti / 

[3] tatrāpi sarasī yadi / nārtho 'rthagrahaneṇa / sarasīśabdād utpannāyāḥ saptamyā luki kṛte mukhyakalpanāviṣayābhāvāt īkārāntaṃ pratyayalakṣaṇena saptamyantaṃ, tatra pratyayasyātyantam asaṃbhavāt nedaṃ pratyayagrahaṇam / na cet pratyayagrahaṇam, saptamyarthavṛtter īkārokārābhyāṃ tadantavidhir bhaviṣyati / pratyayalakṣaṇena vā saptamyāṃ parato yāvīkārokārau saptamīsahacaritau tāv iti / jñāpakaṃ syāt tadantatve / īdūder dvivacanaṃ pragṛhyam ity atra tṛtīyacaturthayoḥ pakṣayoḥ ‘saṃjñāvidhau pratyayagrahaṇa’ ity anāśrīyamāṇe vāśrīyamāṇe vā osantatvagrahaṇe kriyamāṇe tasmiṃs tadantatve sati jñāpakam etad bhavati pratyayalakṣaṇābhāvasya / ‘syāt tadantatve’ ity apekṣyopadarśanam etat / yadi tarhi pratyayalakṣaṇaṃ pragṛhyasaṃjñāyāṃ jñāpakān na bhavati ko 'sya sūtrasyāvakāśaḥ ? atha pragṛhyasaṃjñāyāṃ pratyayalakṣaṇaṃ bhavati kim asyārambhe prayojanam ? tasmād ayam ārabdhavyo yogo 'nyatra pratyayalakṣaṇanivṛttijñāpanena kṛtārthaḥ / svaviṣaye vidhāsyata eva pragṛhyasaṃjñā iti saty eva vā pragṛhyasaṃjñāyāṃ pratyayalakṣaṇe / 

[4] mā vā purvapadasya bhūt / . . .vāpyaśvo nadyāmātir . . . iti / ‘saṃjñāyām’ (A 2.1.44) iti samāse saptamyarthavṛtter īkārāntasya pragṛhyasaṃjñā prāpnoti, arthagrahṇāt na bhavati / atha kriyamāṇe 'py arthagrahaṇe kasmād evātra na bhavati ? dvividhāyām api vṛttau---[a] jahatsvārthāyāṃ tāvat kartṛkarmaikārthasamavāyinīnāṃ kriyāṇāṃ dravyāntarasamavetānāṃ śaktir upakāraḥ pratyāśrīyate, tasyānivṛttatvāt / [ā] ajahassvārthāyām api vṛttau upasarjanībhūtasvārtheṣu viśeṣaṇeṣu [satsu], saṃbaddhatvād abhisaṃbandhī yamānasyārthasya tirobhūte saṃbandhe [sati], yady upasarjanaṃ pradhānārthe na vartea, pradhānopasarjanayor ity antam upakārābhāvāt guṇopādānam anarthakaṃ syāt / tasmād upasarjanībhūtasvārthāni viśeṣaṇāni pradhānārthopādānāni vyarthāni saṃpadyante / tatra mṛtpiṇḍe pāṃśūdakavat anivāryamāṇebhedasya saṃmūrchitasvārthātmana ekārthībhāvāt pūrvam upalabdhavivekatvāt samudāyatvenānumīyamānasyābhidhānaṃ bhavati / tatra ‘saptamyarthe’ ity arthagrahaṇena samudāyārtho nābhidhīyate / 

[i] atha vā pañcake prātipadikārthe saṃdigdhānām anāvirbhūtānāṃ pañcānām arthānām dyotikāsv abhihitārthānuvādinīṣu, trike vā prātipadikārthe saṃkhyākarmādyabhidhāyinīṣu vibhaktiṣu āśritasaṃkhyāviśeṣasyārthasamūhasyābhidhānāt saptamyarthagrahaṇena saṃkhyāviśeṣayukto 'rtharāśir abhidhīyate / samāse tu saṃkhyāviśeṣanivṛttāvakṛtasvatvāt tasyārthasya nyūnārthavṛtteḥ [sataḥ] saptamyarthasya na bhavati saṃpratyayaḥ / tasmād arthagrahaṇaṃ saṃvyavahāropāttasya parikalpitasya śāstrīyasyārthasya pratipattyartham / yadi tarhi nivṛttasaṃkhyāviśeṣaḥ saptamyartho 'tra na gṛhyeta, luky api kṛte saṃkhyāviśeṣasyāsaṃpratyayāt ‘somo gaurī adhi śrita’ iti pragṛhyasaṃjñā na prāpnoti / tasmān nedaṃ śakyaṃ vaktuṃ viśiṣṭaliṅgasaṃkhyābhidhānārtham arthagrahaṇam iti / evaṃ ‘---vāpy aśvo nadyātir . . . ’ ity atra samydāyārtho 'bhidhīyate / tatrādhikaraṇasaṃjñānimittaguṇāśrayasya dravyasya nyagbhūtaśakter upāttādheyaviśeṣatvāt dravyaviśeṣasamavāyinā viśiṣṭākhya(?)prādurbhāvanimittena guṇenāgṛhītasyādheyasyāpi bhāve (?) cārthāntareṇa kalpaḥ saptamyartha ādheyaviśeṣātmānam anugato 'numīyate / avyatikīrṇe (?) cārthāntareṇa mukhye saptamyarthe vidyamāne (?) naivaṃbhūtasya saptamyarthasya parikalpanā yuktā / tatra yathaiva ‘jātyākhyāyām’ ity ākhyāgrahaṇāt dravyanirmuktāyā jāter upādānaṃ vijñāyate, evam ihāpy arthagrahaṇād anupāttādheyaḥ svātmanyevāvasthitaḥ saptamyartho gṛhyate iti / 

[ī] atha vā ‘saptamyarthe’ ity arthagrahaṇāt laukiko 'rthaḥ parigṛhyate / kṛta etat ? ye hi samudāyārthāḥ śāstre tv anvayavyatirekābhyām anumeyāḥ sādhutvaṃ prati nimittatvenopādīyamānāḥ prakṛtipratyayapūrvapadottarapadārthā na [te] samudāyāḥ / loke tu varṇavad eṣām avayavānām ānarthkyam / pariniṣpannas tu samudāyaḥ svārthād apracyuto 'saṃbhinnam ātmānaṃ pratyāyayati / 

[u] atha vā samudāyārtha iti samudāyārthapratipattihetuḥ samudāyaḥ prātipadikārthatām upapanno (?) nivṛttavtatirekabhāvaḥ sattvabhūto vyapadiśyata iti / tatra saptamyarthagrahaṇāt yathābhūtasya grahaṇam / iha tu na tathābhūto bhavatīti na doṣo bhavatīti / 

dādhā dhvadāp // 1.1.20 //

[1] iheyam aṅgīkṛtarūpāṇāṃ dādhāṃ ghusaṃjñā kriyate / tac ca punar eṣāṃ rūpam avayava[dva]yātmakaṃ dakāravatāṃ dha[kāra]vatām (?) / [ta]ccaiṣām uccāraṇāt na śrayatha(? tadāśrayam) pāghrādjmāsthāmnāmātvabhūtatve 'pi sati, dakāradhakārayor abhāvāt yathaiva ca dīṅdṛṅoḥ dhīṅdhṛṅoś ca [da]kāra[dha]kārayor api bhāve śuddharūptvābhāvāt dādhārūpavirūpatve ghusaṃjñā na bhavati, evaṃ deṅdheṭor api śityātvābhāvāt dādhārūpavirūpatve sati ghusaṃjñā na syād ity ataḥ paṭhati ghusaṃjñāyāṃ prakṛtigrahaṇaṃ śidartham iti / pra(? vi)kṛtārtham ity etat tu na paṭhati / evaṃ manyate---lakṣaṇapratipadoktaparibhāṣāyāḥ śe (? do)ṣa[va]ttvāt / eṣa hy asyāḥ śe(? do)ṣo ‘gāmād āgrahaṇeṣv aviśeṣa’ iti / bhāradvājīyāḥ punaḥ anupadeśa āttvabhūtayor apy etayor ato 'nyair aupadeśikātvasamavetair dādhārūpair ghusaṃjñāyā apahṛtatvāt prāptiṃ manyamānāḥ paṭhanti---‘śidvikṛtārtham’ iti / teṣām ayam abhiprāyaḥ---prakṛtibhūtayor adādhārūpatvāt vikārabhūtayor anaupadeśikatvāt paribhāṣā śe(? do)ṣaś caiṣāṃ nāsti iti / asyaiva cārthavattvadyotanārtha upakṣepaḥ / prakṛtigrahaṇe tu sati vikārāṇāṃ ‘sthānivadādeśa . . .’ (A 1.1.56) iti vacanāt ghusaṃjñā bhaviṣyati / itara āha---kiṃ prayojanam iti / asyāyam abhiprāyaḥ---praṇidadātiyacchatidayatidadhātidhayatīnām anukaraṇaśabdaiḥ anukriyamāṇakriyāpadārthekenārthavattvād arthavad iti pratiṣedhaśabda (?) prātipadikasaṃjñaiḥ . . . prāg eva vibhaktyutpatter anaimittika ātvve, śiti ca pratiṣedhe vihitaikaśeṣair upadeśād ihāyaṃ prakṛtigrahaṇaṃ kartavyatayā codayati---kathaṃ prakṛtīnāṃ ghusaṃjñā syāt ? prakṛtiṣu ca labdhaghusaṃjñāsu prakṛtyanvayād vikārāṇāṃ sthānivadbhāvaśāstrād vā vikārāṇām api yathā syād iti / vikārāṇāṃ tu prakṛtīnāṃ na syāt vikāradharmeṇa prakṛtīnām anavayād iti / tatra prakṛtigrahaṇam antareṇāpi tāsām eva svarūpagrahaṇāt ghusaṃjñāsiddhau prakṛticodanānupapattiḥ / ayaṃ tu dātety evamādiṣu ārdhadhātukapratyayaparāṇāṃ pratilabdhāttvānāṃ svarūpaṃ dādhā ity anenānukaraṇaśabdena anvākhyātaṃ matvā kṛtsu kāritāṃ (?) matvā śabdasyāvasāyasāmarthyaṃ ghusaṃjñāśāstrasya saṃpādayiṣyan prakṛtigrahaṇaṃ kartavyatayā codayati / na ca prakṛtigrahaṇam iti prakṛtiśabdasyaiva grahaṇaṃ kartavyam iti vijñāyate / kiṃ tarhi ?
