āttvabhūtānāṃ yāḥ prakṛtayas tāsām eva svarūpato grahaṇaṃ tadvikārāpakarṣapūrvakaṃ kartavyam iti / cūrṇikāras tu vikārāṇāṃ grahaṇān apakarṣeṇa prakṛtigrahaṇaṃ coditaṃ manyamāno vikārān apakarṣeṇa prakṛtīnāṃ svarūpato grahaṇam aśakyaṃ prakṛtivikāramatyabhāvaprasaṅgād iti prakṛtiśabdasvarūpam eva kartavyatayā coditam iti parikalpya vivṛṇoti / itara ekaśeṣopadeśāśraye prakṛtiprakṛticodanānarthakyaṃ pravakṣyann āha---kiṃ prayojanam iti / itaro vikāranirdeśe prakṛtigrahaṇam alabhya[m apa]karṣanirdeśe śabdyate (?) ity āha---āttvabhūtānām iti / āttvaṃ bhūtā āttvabhūtāḥ / nanu ca bhavater akarmakatvāt āttvaṃ bhūtā ity etan nopapadyate / na cāsya bhavateḥ prāptivacanatvāt sakarmakopapattiḥ / asti punar ayaṃ bhavatiḥ kvacit prāptivacanaḥ ? astīty āha / tadyathā / ‘‘etena vai bharadvājaḥ pratardanaṃ daivodāsiṃ samanahyat / sa rāṣṭram abhavat / yaṃ kāmayeta rāṣṭriyam ayaṃ rāṣṭraṃ syād iti tam etena saṃnahyed rāṣṭraṃ ha bhavati’’ (Maitrāyaṇīsaṃhitā 3.3.7) / tathā ‘mahadbhūtaś candramāḥ’ mahattvaṃ prāpta ity ucyate / āttvena vā bhūtāḥ āttvabhūtāḥ, āttvena prāptā ity arthaḥ / nanu [ca] bhūyiṣṭhāni ghu[saṃjñā]kāryāṇi ārdhadhātuke ucyante / tatra caita āttvabhūtāḥ [dṛśyante] / kāryatuṣam eṣām anārdhadhātuke 'lpatvāt, na ca kāryatuṣeṇa parikalpanaṃ prakṛtigrahaṇasya yuktam / na hi saptadaśasu ādeśeṣu sthāneyogaṃ prayojayantyāḥ ṣaṣṭhyā arthāntaraparikalpanam iḍarthaṃ yuktam / yad api ca tat kāryatuṣaṃ tad api ṇatvavidhau mārthasya prakṛtigrahaṇasyāvaśyakartavyasya punastād apakarṣāt sidhyati / ato 'narthakaṃ prakṛtigrahaṇam iti dyotayati / yathā vā arthāntaraparikalpane sati bhūyiṣṭhānām uparodho naivaṃ prakṛtigrahaṇe sati, ato 'yam asamādhir ity āha---śidarthaṃ . . . śityāttvaṃ pratiṣidhyate / ghuprakṛtyor deṅdheṭoḥ dādhārūpābhāvāt asati ghutve ghukāryaṃ praṇidayate praṇidhayatīti ner ṇatvaṃ na prāpnoti /

[2] bhāradvājīyāḥ paṭhanti / vikṛtārtham iti teṣām adhikam / praṇidātā praṇidhātā / deṅdheṭer ete rūpe / pratipadoktam ākārāntatvam atra nāstīty āha---lakṣaṇapratipadoktayor iti / kātyāyanas tu ‘gāmādāgrahaṇeṣv aviśeṣa’ ity anena samarthyatvān naitad uktavān / 

[3] atha kriyamāṇe 'pi iti / evaṃ manyate---dādhāsaṃnidhāne prakṛtigrahaṇaṃ tu vikṛtibhūtānāṃ mā bhūt, dādhāṃ vānevaṃbhūtānāṃ mā bhūt iti ca kalpyamānā ghusaṃjñā sarvaviṣayā satī viśiṣṭaviṣayā kriyamāṇoparuddhā bhavati, tasmān naivaṃ śakyaṃ vijñātum / kathaṃ tarhi dvaitam ? ucyate / 

[a] sāmānādhikaraṇyakalpanāyāṃ dādhāśrater aparārthatvād anugrahaḥ / pārārthyāt tu prakṛtigrahaṇasyoparodhaḥ / 

[ā] ṣaṣṭhyarthe tu dādhāśruteḥ parārthatvād uparodhaḥ, prakṛtiśruter anugraha iti tulyabalatvād vicāraḥ / nanu ca dādhāṃ prakṛtayaḥ ity asmin nirdeśe bahuvacanena na bhavitavyam / dādhām ity etenaiva prakṛtibahutvasya gatatvāt / yathā ‘sūryaṃ cakṣur gamayatāt’ (Maitrāyaṇī 4.13.4) ‘uro vā padbhir āhata’ (Taittirūyasaṃhitā 3.1.4.3) iti / na vādeśabhede 'pi prakṛtibhedāt, jātitvād eva tv atra bahuvacananirdeśaḥ pratyādeśaṃ prakṛtibhedapratipattyartham / 

[4] naivaṃ vijñāyata iti / atrāyam abhiprāyaḥ / codanaivaṃ sati bahukarī bhavati, itarathā tv antararoparodha iti / nanu caivam api sati prakṛtimātrasya syāt / na, prakṛteḥ sākāṅkṣatvād anyasya ca vikārasyānirdeśāt dādhām eva yā prakṛtir iti vijñāyate / 

[5] tato vakṣyāmi dādhā dhu adābartha iti / nanu dādhām arthavattvād evānarthakānām abhāvāt viśeṣaṇānarthakyam / anuvṛttisāmarthyāt prathamānitdiṣṭam arthagrahaṇaṃ vijñāsyate / arthaś ca ghusaṃjño bhavatīty arthamātrasya ca ghusaṃjñāprasaṅgo, dādhām eva nirdeśāt teṣāṃ yo 'rthas tasya sambandho bhaviṣyati / arthe cāsaṃbhavāt tatpratyāyakānāṃ saṃbandho vijñāsyate / prakṛtivikārayoś cāsāv artha iti sarvasya bhaviṣyati / athavā saptamīnirdiṣṭam evānuvartate 'rthe iti, arthe ca yo vartate sa ca ghusaṃjña iti / na sarvasya cārthe vartamānasya saṃjñāprasaṅgaḥ / dādhā anantaranirdiṣṭāḥ saṃbadhyante / itaraḥ siddham iṣṭam, aniṣṭam api tu prāpnoti ity āha---naivaṃ śakyam iti / 

[6] śidarthena tāvan nārthaḥ . . . avaśyaṃ tatra ity āha / bhavatu, kṛtvāpi iha prakṛtigrahaṇaṃ ṇatve punaḥ kartavyaṃ, tad eva cobhayakāri bhaviṣyati / mā ity evaṃbhūte vidhīyamānaṃ meśabde ṇatvaṃ na prāpnoti iti tatra prakṛtigrahaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ tat purastāt apakrakṣyate dhuprakṛtau iti / tad eva ca mā ity etena saṃbhantsyata iti / itaro ṇatvaśāstagate prakṛtigrahaṇe doṣam āha---praniminoti pranimīnāti ity atrāpi prāpnoti iti / ete api māprakṛtī bhavato ‘‘mīnātiminotidīṅāṃ tyapi ca’’ (A 6.1.50) ity āttvasya vidhānāt / itaraḥ svamukhenaiva pratipādayiṣyann āha---athākriyamāṇe 'pi prakṛtigrahaṇe iti / yam evāyam atra ṇatvābhāve samādhiṃ vakṣyati pranimātā pranimātum iti sa eva me prakṛtigrahaṇe 'pi sati bhavati iti samādhim āha---ākārāntasya ṅito grahaṇaṃ vijṣāsyate / itaraḥ svamukhenaiva pratipādyāha---yathaiva tarhi akriyamāṇe iti / 

[7] samānaśabdānāṃ pratiṣedho vaktavyaḥ / evaṃ manyate---dṛṅdhṛṅoḥ vṛddhau kriyamāṇāyām ākāre sati dādhāśabdau nirvartyete / rephaś cāyaṃ . . . ṛtaḥ sthāne yo 'ṇ raparatayā vidhīyamāna āgamābhūtaḥ ‘āgamānām api [āgami]grahaṇena grahaṇāt’ arthavattvam aṅgasya na vihantīty arthavadgrahaṇa eva samānaśabdapratiṣedha idam ucyate / abhaktaparāditvayo repheṇārthavattvasyāviśeṣāt / athavā rūpagrahaṇānumitasyārthavadgrahaṇasya śāstre bahuprakāratvakhyāpanārthaṃ pranidārayati pranidhārayati [iti] rūpa[dva]yo[pakṣapaḥ] / 

[a] samānaśabdāpratiṣedhaḥ / uktam etat ‘svaṃ rūpaṃ śabdasya’ (A 1.1.68) iti ‘svaṃrūpa’grahaṇād dvyātmakaḥ śāstre śabdo vyāpriyate rūpātmako 'rthātmakaś ceti / tasmād dādhām arthavatām eva ghusaṃjñā / dāridhāryoś ca dādhāśabdau anarthakau / dā[r]dhā[r]śabdayor arthavattvāt / nanu cāgamās tadgrahaṇena gṛhyante / syād etad evaṃ yady ayaṃ repho dāśabdasyāgamaḥ syāt / kasya tarhi ? ṛkārasyāṇ raparo bhavati / abhaktaparādipakṣau tu naiva sta iti na tatra pratividhīyate /

[ā] anupasargād vā iti ayam idānīm anarthakasyāpi sati dāśabdasya ghusaṃjñām āśritya parihāram āha---astv asya ghusaṃjñā, tatprakṛtasya tu kāryasya bhāvāt doṣaḥ / na hi saṃjñāmātraṃ doṣāya / ghumādiṣu hi ‘naḥ’ ‘upasargāt’ (A 8.4.16, 14) iti ṇatvaṃ vidhīyate / kriyāyoge copasargasaṃjñā (A 1.4.59) / dāśabdaś ceha na kriyāvacanaḥ / yaṃ ca prati gatyupasargasaṃjñā (A 1.4.59--60) sa ghusaṃjño na bhavati / yasya ca saṃjñā tasya kriyāvācitvābhāvāt taṃ prati upasargasaṃjñā nāsti iti ṇatvasyābhāvaḥ / itaro yady evānarthakyād anupasargād vā nityanivṛttiṃ manyase, iṣṭe 'pi te viṣaye ghusaṃjñā na prāpnoti ity āha ihāpi tarhi na prāpnoti atrāpi hi dāpśabdasyārthavattvaṃ tam eva ca prati kriyāyogaḥ, na dāśabdasyārthavattvaṃ, na ca taṃ prati kriyāyoga iti ekaparihāreṇobhayor nivṛttiṃ karoti / sa tasya vyatirekaṃ karoti iti na tasyopacaye 'pacaye vā sati vyapadeśavyāvṛttir bhavati / tad yathā loke labdhadevadattavyapadeśahānaṃ na bhavati tathā tṛcsanoḥ pratilabdhārdhadhātukavyapadeśayoḥ pratyayavaśādikārādir avayavāntaram upajāyate, akāre vā sano vigate nārdhadhātukavyapadeśanivṛttir bhavati / yadi hi syāt lavitā pipaṭhiṣitā . . . (sati guṇe 'dābārdhadhātukanimitto na syāt), evam ihāpi dādhoḥ pratilabdhadhuvyapadeśayoḥ pratyayavaśāt pakāre 'vayavāntare lokaśāstrayor ivāvyatirekakara upajāte vigate vā praṇidayate iti ghusaṃjñāvyapadeśahānaṃ na bhavati / tatraivaṃ sati dādhoḥ pratilabdhadhusaṃjñayor avyatirekakare avayavāntare pakāropajane 'nivṛttaghuvyapadeśayoḥ parataḥ ‘upasargasthāt’ idam ucyate arthavta iti / anarthakasyāpi tu labdhavyapadeśasyāvayavāntara upajāyamāne 'vyatirekakare vyapadeśahānaṃ na bhavati / tathāhi ārdhadhātukanimitto ‘guṇo bhavati vṛddhir bhavatīti rephaśirasāvakārākārau bhavataḥ’ iti / tathā dāpayatimukhenedaṃ prastutam iti ‘arthavato hy āgamaḥ’ ity ucyate / labdhavyapadeśasyopacaye [ 'pacaye vā]pi svavyapadeśahānaṃ na bhavati / tathā hy ādyantamadhyānām api śabdānāṃ svavyapadeśa[hānaṃ na bhavati] /

taraptamapau ghaḥ // 1.1.22 //

[1] ihānuvandhatvāt pitsmṛtiviṣayāv eva taraptamapau / taraptamapau / taraptamapāv iti anukaraṇam / taraptamapāv iti anukaraṇānukaraṇo 'yam / darśandvayaṃ cānubandheṣu / anubandhaś cānukaraṇa eva kriyate / kāryaṃ kevalam anukriyamāṇaṃ pratipadyate / anye manyante / taddharmā evāyam anukaraṇaiḥ kriyate / sarvathā bhūapūrvagatir anubandha iti ihāpi nadītara iti smartuṃ śakyate / tasmāt ghasaṃjñā prāpnoti / atra dvau parihārau saṃbhavataḥ / lakṣaṇapratipadoktayor iti ca sāhacaryaṃ ca ‘asya gor dvitīyenārthaḥ’ iti / tamapo 'tyantādṛṣṭāpacārasya prakarṣārthīyas tarapśabdo yukta iti / tatra sāhacaryam anāśritam / lakṣaṇapratipadoktatvaṃ tu na saṃbhavati / na hi lakṣaṇaṃ ‘tarap’ ity asya pratipādakam / kasya tarhi ? ‘tara’śabdasya / pratipadoktaś cāyaṃ śabdaḥ / tasmān nedaṃ śāstre rūpaṃ na prayoge rūpam / nirdeśāntaram evedam iti / 

parihārāntaram upādatte / ghasaṃjñāyāṃ nadītare 'pratiṣedhaḥ / na pratiṣedhanaṃ kartavyam / pratiṣedhaś ca pratiṣedhanam iti kriyārūpeṇāśrito nañā saṃbadhyate / bhāṣyakāras tu dravyabhūyam āśritya vivṛṇoti---anarthakaḥ pratiṣedho 'pratiṣedhaḥ iti / pratiṣedho 'py ayaṃ kāryasyākaraṇāt na pratiṣedha iti vyapadiṣyate, pratiṣedham antareṇa siddhatvāt / tarabgrahaṇaṃ hy aupadeśikam / upadeśe prayuktam, upadeśe vā bhavam [aupadeśīkam] / śāstre yas tarapśabdaḥ sa gṛhyate avayavārthe iti etad vyācaṣṭe / śabdaḥ sahacāriṇo 'nubandhanārthasya saṃbandhita iti / sa cānubandho yatra samānaśabdatvaṃ tatrārtham anyad vā dhruvaṃ nimittam upalakṣayati / yatra tu nāsti samānaśabdatvaṃ tatra śabdam eva pratipādayati / yatrāsyaitad rūpaṃ bhavati anubandhasahitam iti, artharūpavaraṇaṃ ca śrutisaṃbandhe bhavati iti prayogo 'bhisamīkṣyate / tatra yo 'vyabhicārī nityaḥ pradhānārthe prayuktaḥ sa āśrayitavya ity āha---rūpanirgrahaś ca [śabdasya] nāntareṇa laukikaṃ prayogam iti / eṣa nyāyyaḥ / saty anubandhe laukike prayoge pravarteran / tatra na kāryā laukikāḥ prayogā iti laukikānubandhavyavahārasya nivṛttatvāt nāyaṃ tarapśabdaḥ / kas tarhi ? tarabśabda iti laukikāt prayogān nivṛttā anityaṃ guṇabhūtaṃ dvitīyaṃ prayogam upāsmahe / kāś cāsau ? upadeśo nāma / upadeśe 'pśabdo na tarapśabda iti / na laukike prayoge etadrūpaṃ, na śāśtre iti kālpanike rūpe prāptir na vidyate iti nāsti ghasaṃjñā / 

[2] atha vāstvasya iti / kāryaṃ doṣāya na saṃjñeti / samānādhikaraṇeṣu ghāḍiṣu / ayaṃ tāvat nadyās tara iti vyadhikaraṇaḥ / prakarṣārthīyau ca svārthikau ca kārakaprakarṣe vartamānau samānādhikaraṇau / yadā tarhi saiva nadī sa [eva] taraḥ / nanu ca tīrṇir iti bhavitavyam / naiṣa doṣaḥ / yadā tīryate iti taraḥ, tara iti sāmānyamātraṃ prādhānyenāśrīyate [yadā] punas tīryate nadīty uttarakālaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ, tadā tadā nāsti pradhānasya liṅgasya tyāgaḥ, yathā mukhadvitīyā nāsikā vacanam asyrti, na tu vacanī iti / athavā rūḍhiḥ, taro nadyā viśeṣyate / samudāyeṣu hi śabdāḥ pravṛttā iti / strīliṅgeṣu ghādiṣu (A 6.3.43) ‘striyām’ (A 6.3.3) iti ca vartate / samudāyeṣu hi liṅgyogāt svārthiko 'pi strīliṅga iti vyapadiśyate / brāhmaṇī rūpam iveti / brāhmaṇīveyam ākṛtiḥ brāhmaṇīrūpam iti / kecid udāharanti---sāmnas taraḥ sāmatara iti / nādūghasya (A 8.2.17) iti nuṭ prāpnoti / chāndasatvān na bhaviṣyati iti pratividhīyate / 

bahuguṇavatuḍati saṃkhyā (A 1.1.23)

saṃkhyāsaṃjñāyāṃ saṃkhyāgrahaṇam / adhikaraṇasaptamī, saṃkhyāsaṃjñāsūtre saṃkhyāgrahaṇam iti / athavā saṃkhyāsaṃjñāyāṃ vidhīyamānāyāṃ vidhārasyamānāyāṃ vihitāyāṃ vā saṃkhyāgrahaṇam iti / saṃkhyāgrahaṇaṃ cāsmin yoge, ‘bahuguṇavatuḍatisaṃkhyāḥ saṃkhyā’ ity evaṃ paṭhitavyam iti / etat pradarśyate---saṃkhyā ca saṃkhyāsaṃjñā bhavatīti vaktavyam / athavā evaṃ pāṭhaḥ kartavyaḥ---‘bahuguṇavatuḍati’ tataḥ ‘saṃkhyā ca’ iti / kiṃ prayojana,¥m ? abhiprāyo 'yam / yathā ‘paśur apatyaṃ devatā’, ‘prācāṃ, udīcāṃ’ iti laukikā evopādīyante śāstravyavahāre---na hy eṣāṃ śāstrāntare 'sti paribhāṣaṇam iti---evam ete 'py ekādadyaḥ śāstrāntareṇāparibhāṣitā laukikā eva / laukikaiś caivaṃ vyavahriyate / athavā kiṃ prayojanam iti kim adhikārthapratipattyartham / ihedaṃ saṃkhyāgarahaṇam eṣām eva bahvādīnāṃ saṃcakṣāṇānāṃ saṃjñāpratipattyarthaṃ, vaipulyārthasya bahuśabdasya mā bhūt bahurodanaḥ, bahuḥ sūpa iti / pūgavacanasya saṃghavacanasya ca guṇaśabdasya yathā na syāt---

‘‘lebhire vāri sāraṅgo gaṇaś cātra pipāsitāḥ’’ itīdam evaṃ varṇyate / [yadā] saṃkhyā ca bahvādayaḥ [ca] tadā saṃjñā saṃkhyā iti adhyavasāyārtham iti pradarśayati / ekādikāyāḥ saṃkhyāyāḥ saṃkhyāpradeśeṣu iti / saṃjñāyāḥ kāryārthatvāt pradeśam evopādatte / na hi saṃjñināṃ saṃjñāpratilambhārtham upādānām / kiṃ tarhi ? kāryapratipattyartham iti / paśur apatyam iti yathā saṃpratyaya evam ihāpi bhaviṣyati ity āha---nanu caikādikā [saṃkhyā] loke saṃkhyā [iti] pratītā iti / itarathā hy asaṃpratyayo 'kṛtrimatvād yathā loke / kutaḥ ? kṛtrimākṛtimayoḥ kṛtrime kāryasaṃpratyayaḥ / yathaiva lokāt pratipanno bhavān iti / ko 'yaṃ nyāyo yenaivaṃ loke saṃpratyayaḥ iti ? ucyate / iha gopālakaśabdo 'rthaviśeṣanimittam upādāya pravartamānaḥ pratividhānaṃ copādadīta yathā ‘pañcabhiḥ halaiḥ kṛṣati’ iti / kartṛtvaṃ vā ‘pālayaty etāḥ [gāḥ]’ iti jātiṃ vā / athavā rūḍhir nimittāntareṇopādīyamānā nimittāntaram apasārayati yathā kriyā jātyādīn apasārayati iti / tulyajātīyaviṣaya eva niyamo bhavati, evam ihāpīti / bahvādaya evātra prakṛtā iti bhavaḥ // . . . 






















mahābhāṣyadīpikāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhamāhnikam /

sarvādīni [sarvanāmāni] // 1.1.27 //

1.1 sarvādīni iti ko 'yaṃ samāsaḥ ? praśnānupapattiḥ, saṃdehasyābhāvāt / yatra śrutau samānāyāṃ viśeṣādhigamanimittaṃ nāsti, tatra saṃdeho bhavati / yathā sthūlapṛṣatīm iti svarā[n a]vadhāraṇāt prakṛtas tatpuruṣo ['thavā] bahuvrīhir iti na jñāyate / iha tu naivam / yadi tāvat sarvaś cāsāv ādiś ceti kalpyate, ādiśabdasyānarthakyam / prādhānyāc cādiśabdasya liṅgavacanatyāgo na yuktaḥ / viśvādīnāṃ ca guṇe saṃniveśo 'narthakaḥ syāt / 

1.2 athaiva kalpyate, ‘ādīyate 'smādityādiḥ’, sarve 'smādādīyanta iti / rūḍhiśabdatvenādiśabdasya [evaṃ] kalpanānupapattiḥ / 

1.3 atha sarvasyādir ity evam api [na], nānyo 'sti kaścit, yo 'syādiḥ syād iti bahuvrīhir evāyam iti saṃdeho nopapadyate / tathā ‘bahuvrīhir ity āhe'ty upadiṣṭe, idam api na praṣṭavyaṃ ‘ko 'sya vigraha iha ?’ iti / 

1.4 naiṣa doṣaḥ / iha ekaśeṣaprakriyāyāṃ gārgyāv iti vivakṣitā kadācit jīvatpitṛkatvaṃ viśeṣaṃ vivakṣati, kadācit gargāpatyatvasāmānyam iti / vivakṣitradhīnā ceyaṃ pratipattiḥ / yathā tadādyācikhyāsāyām iti, tadā[dyā]khyāyām iti vaktavye ācikhyāsāgrahaṇaṃ, vivakṣitā yadā āditvena vivakṣati tatra kathaṃ syāt ? ity evamartham / 

1.5 iha ca kṛtaikaśeṣo 'yaṃ bahuvrīhir atha prāthamaikārthavṛttir ity etat pṛcchati / sarvaś cāsāv ādiś ca sarvādiḥ / sarvaśabda ādir vaiṣāṃ tāni imāni sarvādīni iti / ‘napuṃsakam anapuṃsakena’ (A 1.2.68) iti vā ‘svarabhinnānāṃ yasyottaraḥ svaravidhiḥ’ (A 1.2.64, vā 24) iti vā / tulyarūpapratipattyarthaṃ ca sarvaś cāsāv ādiś cety upādīyate / evaṃ śakyate ekaśeṣaḥ prakalpayitum iti / antareṇa caikaśeṣatantraṃ śakyāśrayaṇaṃ sarvādiśabdavācyaṃ sarvanāmeti / 

1.6 itaro vakṣyamāṇaṃ nyāyaṃ buddhau kṛtvā bahuvrīhir ayaṃ mukhyā[rtha]vṛttir na punar abhyantarīkṛtapadārthāntara iti pratipādayati---bahuvrīhir ity āha iti / ko 'sya vigrahaḥ ? vigrahāt pratyāsannāḥ (? -nnāt) śakyo 'yam arthaḥ pratipattuṃ ‘kevalo vā bahuvrīhiḥ, kṛtaikaśeṣo vā’ / yathā sthūlapṛṣatīm iti vigrahāt viśeṣārtham iti samāso 'vagamyate iti pṛcchati ‘ko 'sya vigrahaḥ ?’ iti / 

1.7 atha ‘tānīmāni’ iti kimartham ? ucyate / anena vinānyapadārthatāyā [a]bhāvāt / na hi citrā gāvo 'syety etāv iti bahuvrīhiḥ, gavām upalakṣyamāṇatvāt / yatra ca citragavyas tadvato viśeṣaṇaṃ tatra bahuvrīhir iti virūpatā guṇapradhānabhāve bhavati / gomān dā[? mā]sajātaḥ vitragur iti pratyavamṛśyate sarvanāmnā ca anyapadārthaḥ / 

1.8 yady aevaṃ sarvaśabdasya / yadi mukhyārthavṛttir ayaṃ bahuvrīhiḥ / anyapadārthatvād bahuvrīheḥ / anyapadārtho hy arthaḥ prayojako bahuvrīheḥ / anyapadārthasya prādhā[nyā]t bahuvrīheḥ / yathā rājapuruṣamānayeti jahatsvārthāyāṃ vṛttau aśabdako rājārtho lakṣaṇaṃ puruṣaśeṣabhāvam āpannaḥ śeṣitvaṃ na pratipadyata iti kriyā tādarthyena na pratipadyate / evaṃ citrā gāvo 'pi lakṣaṇopaśrutāḥ śeṣitvena nety āha / kiṃ tarhi ? aśabdakāḥ śeṣatām āpannāḥ kriyāyāṃ saṃnidhāne vijñāyante /

1.9 atha vā saha guṇair vartate yaḥ so 'yaṃ tadguṇaḥ / tadguṇasya saṃnidhā[? -vijñā]nam iti saṃnihitaviśeṣaṇo 'nyapadārthaḥ kriyāyāṃ saṃvijñāyata ity arthaḥ / kutaḥ punar etad evam iti ? / evaṃ hy uktam---‘matvarthe vā pūrvasya vidhānāt’ (A 2.2.25, vā 5) iti / matvarthīyāś ca kecit svasvāmibhāvamātraviṣayā yathā gomān iti / kaścit saṃsargaviṣayo yathā daṇḍīti / kaśict ayutasaṃsargaviṣayo yathā viṣāṇīti / na hi maṇḍo gaur viṣāṇe grīvāyām āsakte ‘viṣāṇī’ iti bhavati / tathā yajñopavītīti, na ca yajñopavītībhavati, bhavati na ca nivītīti / evaṃ śuklavāsā lohitakoṣṇīṣāś cety etat bahuvrīhipadaṃ viśiṣṭesaṃsarge vartate yathā viṣāṇīti / ayaṃ citragur iti svasvāmibhāvamātraviṣaya eveti / śabdasvabhāva evāyaṃ pravibhāgenārtheṣu pravartata iti grahītavyam / 

1.10 na ca vyākaraṇe idaṃ darśanaṃ---yena vinā yo na vartate / yathā yathārthatattvā . . . / . . . vinā na bhujir asti / evaṃ vāsobhyām iti / tasmād vāsasī api bhujyaṅgam iti śabdārthaḥ / vyākaraṇe tadbhāvatadbhāvitvenārthavyavasthā / 

1.11 idam api nāśriyate, na cehāny atra śiṣṭasya guṇopaguṇasaṃvijñānam iti / yaḥ śuklavāsā dṛṣṭas tam ānayeti, naivātra vāsasī lakṣaṇam iti / yadā codyate ye devadattādayo dṛṣṭāḥ, tān bhojayeti, tadānyatra śiṣṭo 'pi devadatto bhujiṃ pratipadyate / tasmāc chabdasvarūpam evānumantavyam / 

1.12 nanu kriyāsaṃvijñāne 'pi sarvaśabdasya saṃjñayā [na] bhavitavyam / tadviṣṭānāṃ viśvādīnāṃ saṃnihitasarvaśabdānāṃ [saṃjñayā] bhavitavyam / yathā nāgayajñopavītī bhojyatām iti saṃnihitanāgayajñopavītī eva bhuṅkte, na pṛthaṅnāgaḥ / evam eva skandheputraṃ bhpjayeti sa putro bhuṅkte / 

1.13 naiṣa doṣaḥ / yathaiva sahaputraṃ bhpjayeti sahaśabdapravṛttāv apradhānam api putraṃ bhujiḥ pratipadyate / na hy abhuñjāne saputre bhuktaṃ bhavatīti bhujiviśeṣa evāyam, na tu brūmaḥ śabdāt pratipattir iti / yathā nu kaścid dvābhyāṃ bhuñjīta, evam ayaṃ suputro bhuṅkte iti kriyāṃ pratipadyamānaḥ putro viśeṣaṇaṃ, na ca śabdasyāyaṃ dharma iti / 

1.14 ihāpi sarvādīnāṃ viśvādīnāṃ saṃjñā pratyakṣā / anumānābhyāṃdi(? -nādinā) ca sarvaśabdo 'pi pratipadyate / evaṃ sarvādīnāṃ kṛtā bhavati / anekaprakārā hi śabdapravṛttiḥ / kvacid upalakṣaṇabhāva eva, na saṃnidhānaṃ, yathā citragur iti / kvacit kriyāyāṃ saṃnihitam, na tu kriyāyogiḥ, śuklavāsasaṃ bhojaya, nāgayajñopavītī bhojyatām iti / kvacit kriyāṃ pratipadyamāno viśeṣaṇam iti / 

1.15 atha vā ādiśabdaḥ samīpavacana ihopāttaḥ, sa ca samudāyasyaiva sāmīpyena viśeṣaṇaṃ na viśvādīnām avayavānām iti saumudāyasya saṃjñāyā[m a]nabhisaṃbandhād avayavānāṃ kriyate / te cānumīyamānāḥ / tatra yathaiva viśvādayo 'numīyamānāḥ saṃbadhyante, evaṃ sarvaśabdo 'pi kimiti na pratipadyate iti / 

1.16 yadā tv ādiśabdaḥ prakāravacanas tadāpi siddham / yathā kalyāṇpañcamā rātraya iti kalyāṇī pañcamīti nāsmāt prādhānyaṃ pratipadyate / kiṃ tarhi ? āsām iti / yathā devadattaśālāyā brāhmaṇā ānīyantām iti brāhmaṇyād devadatto 'py ānīyate / śālāvedād vā [? -c ca] / evaṃ sarvaśabdo 'pi eṣām iti anyapadārthe tu bhavati / 

1.17 vyavasthāyāṃ tv ādiśabdaḥ samīpavacanaṃ cāśritya coditaṃ parihṛtaṃ ca, tayoś ca tv anyatvam eva / tadguṇasaṃvijñānasya (? -naṃ ca) samudāyaś ca samāsārtha iti bhāṣye 'py uktam eva pradarśitam / 

[2.1] iha sarvanāmāni iti / nanu ca saṃjñānāṃ pūrvapadottarapadavyavahārābhāvaḥ / athā[pi] syāt, evam api hi na śāstrīyāṇām / kiṃ tarhi ? laukikīnām ity evam api nopapadyate / 

2.2 kiṃ ca, saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhasya ca kriyamāṇatvāt sarvatraiva vaktavyam / kathaṃ ṇatvaśāstraṃ pravartata iti / yāvatoktaṃ ‘siddhe śabdārthasaṃbandhe’ (MBh paspaśā vā. 1) iti / kiṃ ca, kiṃ kṛtaṇatvā saṃjñā, āhosvid akṛtaṇatvā uttarakālaṃ ṇatvaṃ pratipadyata iry etac ca vaktavyam / yadi tāvat kṛtaṇatvā, kena ṇatvaṃ prāk saṃjñābhāvāt ? athākṛtaṇatvasya ṇatvam evam api śabdasya vikārāpatter anitya[tva]prasaṅgaḥ / 

2.3 svarūpe ca viniyoktuḥ kāmacārāt, kadācid ayam ekapadavyavahāraḥ / katham iti ? paramārthadarśane ca sarvatraivāyaṃ pravibhāgo nāsti / 

2.4 śāstravyavahāre tu kvacit śabdārthānugamena pravibhāgo, yathā rājapuruṣaśabde vākyaviṣayaḥ śabdārthānugamo 'sti / kvacid arthāntaravṛttābhyām anvākhyānam arthāntaravṛttasya śabdasamudāyasya, yathā gaurakharo, lohitaśāliḥ, kṛṣṇasarpa iti / atyantaṃ samudāye gautvādayo na santi kharatvādayaś ca / jātyantaravṛttābhyāṃ svarūpamātre vṛttasya samudāyasyānvākhyāne anivṛttaḥ pūrvapadottarapadavyavahāraḥ / 

2.5 na ca śāstrasaṃjñā laukikībhyaḥ saṃjñābhyo bhidyante / yaś cāsau dharmaḥ. sa evāsām iti tadvad atrāpi ṇatvena bhavitavyam / 

2.6 na ca saṃjñāsaṃbandhe nityatvaṃ virudhyate / kutaḥ ? śaktiniyamasya pauruṣeyatvāt / ekaśakridarśane 'nekaśaktidarśane ca śaktyavacchedaparigrahe puruṣasya vyāpārāt / 

2.7 anyeṣāṃ darśanam / ya evaite pratyakṣāḥ śabdārthasaṃbandhāḥ gauraśvo 'gnir udakam iti ete nityāḥ / yas tv anumānāt saṃpratyāyayati asāv anityaḥ, yo 'yaṃ samayapūrvaka iti / yathaiva laukikān niviṣṭā anumānāt karaṇasaṃnipātino 'rthasyānupratipādayanti, śrutis tu sākṣātpratipādayati / 

2.8 anyas tv āha---tatrāpy arthe saṃjñāśabdagaṇo 'pi nitya eva, śāstraṃ kṛtaṇatvasya ṇatvaṃ pratipadyate / saṃjñāyāṃ ṇatvaṃ bhavati, kṛtaṇatvaḥ sādhur bhavatīti / na ca svarūpāntaraṃ sṛjate / akṛtaṇatvā saṃjñā [na] kartavyā / nivartakaṃ hi ṇa[tvaśāstram] evaṃ ca kṛtvā uktaṃ ‘nyāyyabhāvāt kalpanaṃ saṃjñādiṣu’ iti /

2.9 sarvanāmasaṃjñāyāṃ nipātanāṇ ṇatvābhāvaḥ / nanu caivam eva kṛtvā coditaṃ ‘ṇatvaṃ prāpnoti’ iti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / etatpratipādayati / nāyaṃ kṛtaṇatvo loke / śāstre akṛtaṇatva ācāryeṇa prayukta iti / evaṃ hi saty[ apy a]sādhutvam asya syāt / kiṃ tu loke 'py ayaṃ yasya yasya saṃjñā tatra tatrākṛtaṇatva eva sann ācāryeṇa atra smaryate iti evaṃ caitat / 

2.10 atha vā pūrvapakṣa idam ipanyastaṃ vidhivākyenopaskāravatā pratipādite ṇatve ‘pūrvapadāt saṃjñāyām agaḥ’ (A 8.4.3) iti / katham iyaṃ śrutir anyārthā pravartamānā saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhe vyāpṛtā, bādhikā bhaviṣyati iti / 

2.11 itaras tv anumānapakṣasya[? -ś ca] smṛtir iti / tasmāt ṇatvam anumīyate, asmād apy aṇatvam, iti svabhāvasiddhaṃ ṇatvam, aṇatvaṃ ca kevalaṃ smaryate iti / 

2.12 kim etan nipātanaṃ nāma ? pratiṣedho ṇatvasya vaktavya iti pratiṣedhaṃ nipātanena sādhayati bhavān / yadi ca pratiṣedhena tulyaṃ nipātanam ata etad upapadyate / kathaṃ ca tulyaṃ nipātanam ? yadi vidhiṃ nivartayati / avikalpitā nivṛttiḥ / tata eva tat tulyaṃ pratiṣedhena bhavatīti / 

2.13 itaro ya eva pratiṣedhasya dharmaḥ sa eva nipātanasya / pratiṣedho hi svabhāvato 'pravṛttasyānumānam / na tu kīlapratikīyanyāyena / evaṃ nipātanam apīti idam upanyasyati atha kaḥ pratiṣedho nāma ? iti / 

2.14 aviśeṣeṇa kiṃcid uktvā / ‘ṛnnebhyo ṅīp’ (A 4.1.5) iti / viśeṣeṇa ‘na ṣaṭsvasrādibhyaḥ’ (A 4.1.10) iti / tatra vyaktam ācāryasyābhiprāyo gamyate, idaṃ na bhavatīti / ācāryeṇa na sa śabdo vivakṣito nopāttaity ayam abhiprāya ācāryasya gamyate / 

2.15 kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ, vidhipratiṣedhacodanayoḥ vidhivākyasya na mradvīyate, pratiṣedhavākyasyaiva mradvīyate / brāhmaṇā bhojyantāṃ, kauṇḍinyo na bhojayitavya iti ? ucyate / 

2.16 [a] viparyayaḥ samuccayo vā na śakyate pratipattum / vikalpo 'pi na bhavati / nirvṛttir atyantaṃ bādhitā syāt / virodhāc ca samuccayo na śakyaḥ kalpayituṃ pratipattuṃ [vā] / vikalpo 'pi na bhavati, nivṛttyabhyupagamāt / nivṛttir eva hy ādau katham ? prasaktaṃ hi nivartayatīti cet . . . sarveṇa sarvaṃ nivṛttyā bhavitavyam / kṛtārthasyā(ś cā)nyatra bhojanavidhir iti / 

2.16 [ā] atha vā bhinnā eva prativākyaṃ śabdāḥ / tulyaśrutitvāt tulyapratipattuḥ pratipādanopāyāḥ / viśeṣaśabdād aviśṣṭa eva brāhmaṇaśabda uktaḥ kauṇḍinya iti manyate / 

2.16 [i] anyeṣāṃ darśanam / [na] śakalakaṇṭakā jighṛkṣitā, nāntarīyakaṃ tūpādānam / evam ihāpi na kauṇḍinyo vivakṣito, nāntarīyakatvāt tūpādatte iti tyajyate vidhau kauṇḍinyaḥ / 

2.16 [ī] aparaṃ darśanam / pratyakṣānumānayoḥ pratyakṣaṃ balavat iti rūpeṇa saṃdihyamāno brāhmaṇaśrutau, pratiṣedhe cāsaṃdigdhaḥ / yayoś ca kriyayoḥ [vidhiḥ] pratyakṣnumānābhyāṃ coditas tayor vikalpena pravṛttir ayuktā / 

nanu ca brāhmaṇyāt kauṇḍinye vidhiḥ pravartate, na kauṇḍinyatvāt, brāhmaṇaśrutau pratyakṣam iṣṭam eveti / naitad asti / viśeṣād anabhivyaktabhedaḥ sāmānyam ity ato na pravartitavyaṃ vikalpena / arthāntarabhūte ca sāmānye, kauṇḍinyo brāhmaṇyena saṃbadhyate yasmād atas tadanurodhāt pratipadyamāno 'numānād eva bhujinā saṃbadhyate / nivṛttau tu sākṣāc coditatvāt ity ato 'pi vikalpo na yuktaḥ / 

2.16 [u] anyeṣāṃ darśanam / asaṃbhave niyama evāyam / na hi saṃbhavo 'saṃbhāvyo 'sti / brāhmaṇyāt bhojitaḥ kauṇḍinyo na bhojitaḥ syāt ity asaṃbhavād arthenai[? -rthād e]va nivṛttir bhavatīti / 

2.16 [ū] apare manyante / bhujir evāyaṃ pravṛttinivṛttirūpo vidhīyate yathā vyavasthitavibhāṣā ceti / 

2.16 [e] anyeṣāṃ darśanam / prakaraṇe vākyam etat, kasmiṃś ceit pradhānavākye upāyabhūtam / na ca upāyavākye yo 'rthaḥ samadhigataḥ, tasya pradhānavākye prakḷptir yuktā, śābdāntaratvāt yathā gaurakharaśabdāvanarthakāv iti / 

2.17 ayaṃ tu siddhānto brāhmaṇān bhojayeti / [a]tha saṃbhojyamāneṣu pratyavāyo na bhavati, kṛto hy anyūno jāty arthaḥ / 

2.18 iha tu brāhmaṇo na hantavya iti kāṃścit saṃcakṣya kaścid ucya[? -ddhanya]te, tatra pratyavāyo 'vaśyaṃbhāvī / hantir hi nirvartitaḥ / iha tv ahananam upadiṣṭam / tatra viparītakriyācaraṇaṃ doṣāya / evaṃ brāhmaṇā bhojayitavyā iti keṣucit bhojiteṣu bhojitā eva bhavanti, na tu kasmiṃś cit kauṇḍinyo bhojite 'nyaḥ kauṇḍinyo bhojayitavyo, bhujeḥ pratiṣedhe ced iti / evaṃ ca kṛtvoktaṃ ‘nākaraṇāni vidhīn bādhante, pratiṣedhas tu kriyāṃ nivartayati’ iti / 

2.19 yac ca kalpyate brāhmaṇaśabdenopāttaḥ kauṇḍinyo nivartate, kiṃ tv ekasya śabdasyānurodhena pravartate puruṣaḥ, itarasya neti / yathaivaikenolte kurv iti, anyena ca mā kārṣīr iti, anyatarat pramāṇīkaroti / na tv anyatareṇāsāvartho noktaḥ, evam ihāpīti / sa tu kim ity ubhayathā na karotīty etad vaktavyam / etac ca vistareṇa pratipāditam / loke 'py evam eva gacchety uktvā punar bravīti mā gama iti / sa pratipadyate kiṃcid artham uddiśyāyaṃ pravartata iti / 

2.20 atha vā sāparādhā vaktāro loke / darbhapavitrapāṇis tu samīkṣyavāk [vakteti] kṛtisādhuś ca mā[tā]pitṛkalpo 'visaṃvādayitā śrutau smṛtau cety aprāptir evānumīyate / 

2.21 yat tu prasaktaṃ pratiṣidhyate ‘abhakṣyo grāmyakukkuṭa’ iti / atra nāsti aprāptasyānumānam / arthalakṣaṇāyāṃ hi pravṛttau dṛṣṭaṃ cārtham upalabhya vadhādi pretyaphalam api ca pratipadyamāno nāvasīdatīti / 

2.22 sarvo nyāyo 'yaṃ vartate nipātana itīdam āha---nipātanam apy evaṃ jātīyakam eva / 

2.23 nanu ca nipātanāc cāṇatvaṃ syāt / yathā prāpaṃ ca ṇatvam / evaṃ manyate / pratiṣedho nivṛttirūpa eva / tatra tannivṛttir yuktā / nipātanasya tu śabdātmakatvād ātmarūpaṃ pratipattavyam, evaṃ vākyāntareṇa vihitaṃ ṇatvam eva nivṛttam / yat tat śabdāntaram idaṃ sādhutvenākhyā[ya]te 'sya saṃjñāniyamo ghaṭate / 

2.24 kim anye 'py evaṃvidhayo bhavanti / ‘iko yaṇ aci’ (A 6.1.77) iti / atrāpi hi vikārivikārabhāvo nāsti, nityatvābhyupagamād ity asati bhasmakhadiranyāye śabdāntarapravṛttir iyam iti saṃhitāyām eva ‘dadhy’śabdaḥ sādhutvena vijñāyamāno na bādheta dadhiśabdam iti / 

2.25 ṣaṣṭhyātra nirdeśaḥ kriyata iti / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / yaṇaḥ [yadā] akṛtapūrve sthānaśabdasya pravṛttis tadātyantam aprasaṅgo, nivṛttāḥ saṃhitāyāmikaḥ iti / yaṇaṃ yadā buddhiḥ śabdatvenāviśati, tatrāpi buddhir buddhyantareṇa nivartyata iti vā utpadyate / tasmād ubhayasminn api darśane yuktā nivṛttiḥ / ihāpi yatra ṣaṣṭhī (na) bhavati ‘. . . saṃjñāyām . . . ’ (A 8.4.3) iti, tatrātyantaṃ nakārābhāvaḥ / 

2.26 iha tu ṣaṣṭhyabhāvād vikalpaḥ / kimiti ? tasyānabhidhānāt dyotitatvād vā vikalpaḥ prāpnoti / 

2.27 śabādeśāḥ śyannādayaḥ kariṣyante / yady evaṃ karotīti pittvātideśaḥ syāt / śyano nittvaṃ jñāpakaṃ, na bhavati / vidhyati / anyasmiṃs tu śittvam asti, tena ṅittvaṃ na [ca] bhavati / 

2.28 iha ca kurvīti num prāpnoti ‘śapśyanor nityam’ (A 7.1.81) iti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / śyano grahaṇāt / dhinuta iti katham ‘ato lopaḥ’ (A 6.4.48) iti / atrāpi liṅgam asārvadhātukatve, yad ayaṃ ‘tudādibhyaḥ śaḥ’ (A 3.1.77) iti śakāraṃ paṭhati / 

2.29 evam api śapaḥ sthāne prasaktaḥ sthānāpavādatvāt śnam tataḥ paraṃ prāpnoti / idaṃ tāvad ayaṃ praṣṭavyaḥ, iṭ kasmād ādir na bhavati / atha manyase---bhūyobhir ākṣiptatvāt iti / ihāpi śyanādibhir ākṣepaḥ / 

2.30 syād etat / ekā ṣaṣṭhī ‘lasya’ (A 3.4.77) iti / tac ca na / pratyādeśaṃ bhedād anekā ṣaṣṭhī / iha tu ‘phalipāṭinabhimanijanāṃ guk paṭinākidhataś ca’ (u 1.18) iti saṃbandhasāmānyopādānāt vibhāgavākyāni bhinnāny eva pratipādakāni iti bhinnārthā ṣaṣṭhī pratipādikākāryāṇām / 

2.31 yady evaṃ tadādeśenaiva supā (? numā) bhavitavyam / na / mittvād ayaṃ saṃnihitaḥ kasmāt paro bhavati ? yathā ‘bhrasjo ropadhayoḥ . . .’ (A 6.4.47) iti / payāṃsīty atra kasmāt naivam iti vicāritam etat ‘midaco 'ntyāt paraḥ’ (A 1.1.47) ity etasmin / 

2.32 tad vai prathamānirdiṣṭam iti / nanu ca sva(? anu)vṛtter vikalpaḥ syād ity etad ayuktam / pratijñā(? prasaktā)hānau antareṇāpy anuvṛttiṃ bādhyabādhakabhāvam ane[nā]bhyupagacchati, tasmin eva vikalpaḥ / tadanuvṛttāv api vikalpte sati pratijñā(? prasakta)virodho 'nanu(? dho 'nu)vādaprasaṅgaś ca saty evaṃ bhavati iti /

2.33 iha tarhi ‘avyayasarvanāmnām . . .’ (A 5.3.71) iti / deśavurodho nehāsti / kiṃ tarhi ? uktatvād vikalpam eva codayati / uccakaiḥ uccaiska iti prāpnoti / 

2.34 nāprāœte[hi] ke 'kajārabhyate ity etat pratipādayati / sutarāṃ pāṇinisūtre 'bhyupagamyamāne na vidhānaṃ na nivṛttiḥ kaṃ(? kā)cit / kiṃ tarhi ? atyantāpravṛttiḥ . . . eta idupapadyamānam iti / yady evam akac tarhi akacvṛttau viśeṣo na nirdiṣṭaḥ sāmānyena codita iti pratipadya . . . yatra ca vikalpo yatra samuccayaḥ saṃbhavati sarvatra sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvam upalabhya nātra codito viśeṣaḥ sāmānyaśabdenety etat pra[ti]pattavyam / 

2.35 nipātanam apy evaṃjātīyakam eva iti / yadi [tarhi] nipātanāni / etad eva dṛṣṭvā tad evaṃ paryanuyuktaṃ ‘. . . kriyāsātasya’ (A 6.1.144) iti / nipātane 'pi sati vikalpo dṛśyate---[satataṃ] saṃtatam iti / tasmān nivṛttir eva, na vikalpa iti [kathaṃ] pratipattavyam ? evaṃ ca sati dve ṣaṣṭhyau prasaktam (? -kte) iti / 

kartavyo 'tra yatno, bādhakāni [eva hi] nipātanāni bhavanti / pūrvoktena nyāyena / hitaśabdārtham idaṃ kartavyaṃ saṃhitaṃ sahitam iti prasiddhyartham / tatraivāyam upasaṃkhyeyaḥ / sāṃtatyaṃ ca na bhavati avyavikanyāyāt / 

3.1 saṃjñopasarjanapratiṣedha iti / śabdāntaratvād anyatvaṃ vākyakāro nirdiśati / bhāṣyakāras tu sa evāyam imām avasthām āpanna iti saṃjñopasarjanībhūtānām ity āha / 

3.2 sarvāya dehi iti / nanu ca gauṇamukhyanyāyāt na bhaviṣyati / yathāgnīṣomau māṇavakāv iti / naitad asti / padakāryeṣv ayaṃ nyāyaḥ, prāptipadikānāṃ ca saṃjñā pravartate / tatra ca nedam astīty etad uktaṃ ‘śabdāśraye [ca] vṛddhyāttve’ iti /

3.3 evam api ‘ekadravyopade’(-vyanive)śinī saṃjñā' iti (A 1.4.1 vā 44) saṃjñāsaṃjñā pūrvasyāḥ bādhikā iti, ‘samāsakṛttaddhitāvyayasarvanāmāsarvaliṅgā jātir iti’ (A 1.4.1 vā 41) yathaiva guṇavacanasaṃjñāṃ bādhate saṃjñāsaṃjñā, daivadattyam iti na bhavaty evam iyam api bādhitavyā, anāśritya tām idam ucyate / 

3.4 atisarvāya dehi iti / prādisamāso 'yam / bahuvrīhau tu vihita eva pratiṣedhaḥ / tadantavidhiḥ katham ? prayojanaṃ sarvanāmāvyayasaṃjñāyām iti / pradeśe vāṅgaṃ sarvādibhir viśeṣyate iti, tac cāvaśyam āśrayitavyaṃ paramasarvasmai dehīti prasiddhyartham / 

3.5 sa kathaṃ kartavyaḥ ? kiṃ saṃjñāyām atha pāṭhe, gaṇe 'tha sūtre 'thobhayatra ? iti / 

3.6 pāṭhāt paryudāsaḥ / saṃjñāyāṃ hi pratiṣedhe kriyamāṇe 'ntargaṇakāryam aparihṛtaṃ bhavati / gaṇe ca kriyamāṇe pratiṣedhe prathamādiṣv aprasaṅga iti ihaiva pāṭho viśeṣyate / paṭhitānām ihaiva saṃjñākaraṇam iti / sarvādīny asaṃjñopasarjanāni, [anyāni] tu naiva saṃniviṣṭāni / yāni caivaṃbhūtāni tāni sarvanāmasaṃjñāni iti sadṛśo 'py asaṃniviṣṭatvāt naiva pratipadyate saṃjñām / yathā kutsanāhīkṣṇyayoḥ gotrādīni (A 8.1.27), nānyatra gotrādīnīti /

3.7 kim aviśeṣeṇa ? kiṃ yattat sarveṣāṃ kāryaṃ tasmin pratiṣedhaḥ, ‘sarvanāmnaḥ’ iti ? viśeṣeṇa ca aviśeṣeṇa cety arthaḥ / gaṇapāṭhe yaḥ saṃniveśas tadupalakṣitaṃ kāryaṃ viśiṣṭānāṃ ‘. . . ṅatarādibhya . . .' (A 7.1.25), ‘tyadādīnām . . .’ (A 7.2.102) iti / yat tu ‘yuṣmadasmadbhyām . . .’ (A 7.1.27) iti, etad aviśeṣeṇa / yady apīyam ānupūrvī gaṇe etayos tathāpi seha nāśrīyate / saṃniveśāntaram evedam iti /

3.8 prayojanaṃ ḍatarādīnām adbhāve / atikatarād adbhāvo na bhavati, upasarjanatvāt / iha tu bhavitavyam ity u[tyabhyu]oagamyate, paramakatarad iti / 

3.9 upariṣṭādyogavibhāgaḥ kariṣyate / asaṃjñāyām ity asaṃyuktavidhānāt sarvādīny upekṣyante / tatra ca saṃniveśo 'nukrānto 'pekṣyate, na saṃjñeti / 

3.10 ‘anupasarjanāt’ (A 4.1.14) ity ayaṃ yogaḥ pratyākhyāyate / nanu ca pradhānena tadantavidhyartho 'yam ārabdhavyo yogaḥ, kaumbhakāreyaḥ, soka(? kauru)careya iti kathaṃ syāt ? na cedaṃ kṛdgrahaṇam, taddhito 'py aṇ asti /

3.11 naitad asti prayojanam / uktam etat---‘pratyayagrahaṇe astrīpratyayena’ iti / na vidhānam apekṣyate ‘strībhyo ḍhak’ (A 4.1.120) iti / yady evam upasarhane atiprasaṅgaḥ / atrāpi prativihitam ‘upasarjane na’ iti / 

3.12 pratyākhyātaḥ san paribhāṣā vijñāyate akārātkāraliṅgā / akārātkārau yatra brūyāt ‘anupasarjana’ ity etat tatropatiṣṭhate, anupasarjana artha iti / ṣaṣṭhyā vā ‘supāṃ suluk’ (A 7.1.39) iti luk / prātipadikanirdeśo vā / yathārthaṃ vibhaktiprakḷptir iti / 

3.13 yady evam atiyuṣmadatyasmad iti na sidhyati / atikrāntebhyo yuṣmānānaya atiyuṣmadānayeti ‘pañcamyā at’ (A 7.1.31) ity atkāro na prāpnoti / praśliṣṭanirdeśo 'yam / akārātkārau yatra akārād vidhīyete tatra anupasarjana a ad iti / teṣāṃ tyaṃtvā (? tyadādyatvam a)nupasaṃgṛhītam akārāntād avidhānāt / atha priyau dvau asyeti kathaṃ bhavitavyam ? na hy asāvakārāt parasyākāra iti / satvaṃ cāpratiṣeddham eva atitad iti / atisarvāyeti caitat cāparihṛtam eva / 

3.14 atha vā aṅgādhikāre tasya yad ucyate, gṛhyamāṇavibhaktes tad bhavati / pradeśe 'tulyavibhaktau tulyavibhaktau vā nirdeśe gṛhyamāṇarūpaiḥ śabdaiḥ viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāve kalpyamāne aṅgasyety etatprādhānyena yadi prakramyate, ḍatarādayo viśeṣaṇaṃ, siddhaṃ paramakatarat / iha prāpnoti atikataram iti /

3.15 atha ḍatarādayaḥ pradhānaṃ, tadantavidhyabhāvāt siddham idam atikataram iti / paramakatarad iti na sidhyati, tathātiyuṣmat atyasmad iti /

3.16 athaivaṃ pratipattir aṅgasyeti viśeṣaṇaṣaṣṭhī, tyadādīnām iti sthānaṣaṣṭhī, aṅgasya ye 'vayavās tyadādayas teṣāṃ vibhaktāv anantarāyām akāra iti paramasa iti sidhyati / ihāpi prāpnoti atitad iti /

3.17 yadi tv ihaiva prākaraṇikas tadantavidhiḥ śakyate kalpayituṃ sarvanāmāni sarveṣāṃ nāmāni iti, idaṃ prakaraṇaṃ sarvādī[nī]ti viśeṣaṇam ato 'trāyam artha āpadyate sarvādyantavat sarvanāmeti / saṃjñopasarjanāntaṃ ca sarvanāma na bhavati / nāsti tena tadantavidhir iti paramasarvasmā iti bhaviṣyati, nātisarvāyeti / ḍatarādikāryaṃ tu na śakyaṃ parihartum aprāmā(? kara)ṇi [ka]tvāt / aprā[kara]ṇike tu tadantavidhau ‘prayojanaṃ sarvanāmāvyayasaṃjñāyām’ (A 1.1.72 vā 8) ity ubhayatra pravṛttir apravṛttiś ca /

3.18 tasmāt prakārāntaram avalambate bhāṣyakāraḥ---gṛhyamāṇavibhaktes tad bhavati iti / gṛhyamāṇānām uccāryamāṇānāṃ śabdānāṃ yā vibhaktiḥ saṃbandhinī / saṃbandhaś ca na vidhānalakṣaṇa āśrīyate / kiṃ tarhi ? arthadyotananimittaḥ tyadādīnāṃ yo 'rthas tasya dyotikāyāṃ vibhaktāv aṅgasya, evaṃ ca ḍatarādīnāṃ yau svamāvarthasya dyotakau tayor iti, evaṃ sarvanāmno yau ḍasiṅi iti / iha tu iṣyate atiyuṣmad atyuasmad iti / 

3.19 yady evaṃ paramapañca iti / abhiprāyaḥ / vidhānalakṣaṇo vibhaktyāḥ saṃbandha iti / atha vā tyadādīnām iti svarūpasyāśritatvāt tyadādirūpasya yo 'rthas tatsaṃbandhinī yā vibhaktir iti / na ceyaṃ svarūpasya vibhaktir iti / 

3.20 naiṣa doṣaḥ / ṣaṭpradhāna eṣa samāsaḥ / arthadyotananimittaḥ saṃbandho bhavati, tyadādīnām iti [na] rūpam āśrīyate / lakṣaṇamātram arthasya śabdaḥ / tyadādīnāṃ yo 'rtho dṛṣṭas tatsaṃbandhinyāṃ vibhaktāv iti / pradhāne vā kāryasaṃpratyayāt tasyāsāv artha iti vyapadiśyate / yathā ha[ye]ṣu gacchatsu rājā gacchatīti / 

3.21 atra tv idaṃ codyaṃ---pañcakāḥ śakunaya iti svārthikattvātkanaḥ ‘ṣaḍbhyo luk’ (A 7.1.22) iti luk prāpnoti /

3.22 iha tarhi priyasakthnā / na hy atra sakthno 'rthasaṃbandhinī vibhaktir iti / asati tadantavidhau na sidhyaty etad iti / 

3.23 saptamīnirdiṣṭe yad ucyate prakṛtavibhaktau tad bhavati iti / ‘iko 'ci vibhaktau’ ity anaṅ ucyate / tat prakṛtavibhaktāv ity arthanirdeśo 'yam / 

3.24 trayas tūpāyāḥ / aṅgasya yā vibhaktis tasyām anantarāṇām asthyādīnām iti / atha vā prakṛtasyāsthyādibhis tadantavidhiḥ / aṅgasyeti vā viśeṣaṇam asthyādayo viśeṣyā iti / 

3.25 yady evam ati tat / idam api saptamyām ucyate / [te]nāsthyādivat prasaṅgaḥ / 

3.26 tac cāpi vaktavyam / na gṛhyamāṇavibhaktāv iti / kiṃ tarhi ? saptamīnirdiṣṭe yac ucyate iti / 

3.27 iha . . . ḍatarādibhya . . . iti pañcamī / nanu ca ‘ato bhisa ais’ (A 7.1.9) iti / [vibhakivi]pariṇāmāt tulyavibhaktitvam / atha vāṅgasya yo bhis tasyātaḥ prasyeti vidyate kalpanā / evaṃ vibhaktyantarayogo vādbhāve 'ṅgasya vā yā vibhaktiḥ katarādibhyaḥ pareti saṃbhavati / katham idam ucyate anyad ato vihitāt pratyayāt iti ? vihitaviśeṣaṇe ca paramakatarad iti na sidhyati / idaṃ ca na sidhyati paramaṣaṭ atiyuṣmad iti / 

3.28 tatrāyam abhiprāyaḥ / yadi yathāśruti kalpyamāne iṣṭaṃ na sidhyati, tatra pariṇāmo 'nyā vā kalpanā / iha cedam eva saṃgṛhyate / tasmād yathāśruti patigṛhyate / na cāsyāṃ śrutau tadantavidhir astīty etatpradarśyate pañcamyā / kathaṃ paramakatarad iti ? bhāṣye 'nudāhṛtatvāt saṃdigdham evedam / kathaṃ bhavitavyam iti ? anayā tu kalpanayā paramakataram iti bhavitavyam / 

kathaṃ paramaṣaḍ iti ? ṣaḍbhya iti naiṣā pañcamī / kā tarhi ? caturthī / yathā ‘āyudhajīvibhyaś chaḥ parvate’ (A 4.3.91) iti / ṣaḍarthau yau jaś śasau, tayor lug iti / atiyuṣmad iti katham ? yathā ‘ato bhisa ais’ (A 7.1.9) ity aṅgasya, yuṣmadasmadbhyāṃ parasyeti tadantavidhinā vāyavavāyavavibhāvena veti / 

3.29 e(? ye)ṣāṃ tu darśaṇaṃ paramakatarad iti bhavitavyam iti, tu evaṃ manyante---yathaiva ‘ṅyāpprātipadika . . . ’ (A 4.1.1) sūtre ekatvādiṣv artheṣu svādayo vidhīyante, tatra tadvṛtteḥ śabdasyaitā aṅgapadabhasaṃjñā bhaviṣyantīti asaty api prātipadikagrahaṇe vidhīyamānās tadvādi(? ci)no vihitā iti pratipāditam, evam ihāpi ḍatarādyartho(? -rthe) vidhīyamānas tebhyo vihita ity arthāśrayam eva vidhānaṃ manyante, na śabdāśrayam iti paramakatarad iti bhavitavyam / atrāpi hi ḍatarādyarthe eva vihitau svamāv iti / 

3.30 yady evam atisa iti atvaṃ na prāpnoti / yathaivātitad iti / tyadādipradhāna eṣa samāsaḥ / śobhanaḥ saḥ atisa iti / yadā ca nañsamāsa uttarapadārthapradhānas tadāpi asaḥ aṣaṭ akatarad iti bhavitavyam / 

3.31 atha vā nedaṃ saṃjñākaraṇam / sarvanāmānīti nedaṃ sarvādīnāṃ nāmadheyam / kiṃ tarhi ? pāṭhiviśeṣaṇam / saṃniveśasya viśeṣaṇam / sarveṣāṃ yāni nāmāni / te gaṇasaṃniviṣṭā, nānye / tenaitayor viśeṣe 'vasthitayor na bhaviṣyati saṃjñopasarjanayoḥ / svaviṣayasarvanāmatvaṃ caiṣāṃ, yathā jāteḥ svviṣayasarvagatatvāt sarvagatatvam / evaṃ sati saṃniveśāśrayaṃ kāryaṃ sarveṣāṃ yāni nāmāni teṣāṃ bhavati ‘adṅḍatarādibhyaḥ . . . ’ (A 7.1.25) ‘tyadādīnām . . .’ (A 7.2.102) iti / iha na bhavaty atikataram atitad iti / atra hi yo 'sāv atykrāmatso 'bhidhīyata iti / 

3.32 yady evaṃ saṃjñāśrayaṃ na prāpnoti, saṃjñāyāḥ akaraṇāt / sarvanāmaśabdo hi pāṭhaviśeṣaṇa upakṣīṇa iti / evaṃ ca satīdam uktaṃ bhavati ‘sarva[vi]śvibhayādīnām anarthakaḥ saṃniveśa’ iti / anvarthagrahaṇaṃ tatra vijñāsyate sarveṣāṃ yan nāma / tasmād aṅgātsmāyādayaḥ syur iti /

3.33 sarvaśabdaś ca kṛtsnavacanaḥ, sa tu saṃjhyāviṣayaḥ, prakāraviṣayaḥ, kalāvadviṣayaś ca / sarve manuṣyā āgatāḥ, yāvanto likhitāḥ ṣoḍaśa saptadaśa vā / evaṃ ca kṛtvā vaktāro bhavanti, savrāṇy annāni bhaktāni niravaśeṣāṇi ceti / prakārakārtsnye, sarvam annaṃ bhuṅkte sarvaprakāraṃ bhuṅkta iti / kalāvati ca, sarva odano bhukto, yaḥ pātryām āvarjita iti / 

3.34 yady evaṃ sa[kalaṃ] kṛtsnaṃ jagad ity atrāpi prāpnoti / yad akārāntaṃ tasmāt smāyādayaḥ, anyatrākajādayaḥ / etāny api svaviṣaye sarvanāmānīti eteṣāṃ cāpi śabdānāṃ yo viṣayas tasmin viṣaye yo yaḥ śabdo bhavati, ‘sarva odana’ ‘eka odana’ iti tasmin viṣaye sarvanāmāśrayaṃ kāryaṃ prāpnoti / sarvasminn odane ekasmin odana iti odanasminn iti prāpnoti / 

3.35 idaṃ nyāyavido bhāṣyakārasyāyuktatvam iva dṛśyate / katham ? sarvaśabdaḥ sāmānyavacanaḥ odanānodanaviṣayaḥ, evam odanaśabdo 'pi sarvāsarvaviṣayaḥ / yat tu viśeṣāvasthānaṃ sa vākyārtho na padayoḥ, evaṃ ca kṛtvoktam ‘yad atrādhikyaṃ vākyārthaḥ sa’ iti / katham odanaśabdasya (? -bdaḥ sarva)viṣaya iti avasīyate ?

3.36 athāpy ekaviṣayāv evam api kaścid chabdaḥ kaṃcid arthabhāgam āśrito yathotpalena [saha] nīlaśabdaḥ nīlabhāgam, [utpalaśabdaḥ] utpalabhāgam iti / ubhayaśūnyam ubhayarūpaṃ vā viṣaya etayor iti / evaṃ sarvaśabdaḥ kārtsnyabhāgam abhiniviṣṭa itaro 'py odanatvabhāgam iti / evam api tadviṣayatvam anupapannam / 

3.37 tatredaṃ darśanam---yad yadā sarvārthasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ nīlaṃ guṇaṃ dravyajātibhir upalabhate, tadā yathābuddhi śabdaprayoga iti svabuddhyanukāreṇa nīlaśabdaḥ sarvārthopagrāhī prayukta iti / yathaiva nīlaśabdena nīlaguṇaṃ dravyam abhihitam evam utpalaśabdo 'pi sarvārthasaṃsṛṣṭārtha iti / ubhau tadvannīsatto (? -ti saktau) / tayoḥ kim ity ekaḥ sarvanāmakāryaṃ pratipadyate, eko neti ? 

3.38 evaṃ tarhy ubhayam anena kriyate / sarvanāmatvena paricchinnāni sarvādīni, tāny eva sarvanāmasaṃjñāṃ labhanta iti / 

kathaṃ punar ekena yatnenobhayaṃ labhyam ? svārthanibandhanā hi śabdāḥ paratantrāḥ / ekayā pravṛtyāśritās tadupekṣayā pravṛtyantarābhāvād arthāntaro[pa]grahe asamarthāḥ / tatra nāvayavāv ubhayārthāv upapadyete na samudāya iti / yathā preraka eva prayatno na vidhāraṇe vibhavatīti yatnāntaram evārabhyate / 

3.39 ekaśeṣanirdeśo 'yam / kathaṃ punar ekaśeṣo ? yāvatā vastubhede saty anekaprasaṅge sa ārabhyata iti / vṛkṣaśvatvaplakṣaś ceti (? vṛkṣaś ca vṛkṣaś ca vṛkṣaś ca vṛkṣā iti) / iha tu na savra viśva ity eṣām anekatvaṃ, nāpi sarvādiśabdavācyasyārthasya / etāvat tu dvitīyaḥ sarvādi (? sarvanāma)śabdaḥ karoti---yāni sarvādīni sarveṣāṃ nāmāni, tāni sarvādīni (sarvanāmāni), anyāni na sarvanāmāni / kiṃca, ekaśeṣaśabdair ekakāla ekopakramaś cārthātmā vyapadiśyate / iha ca bhinnakālo bhinnopakramaś ca saṃbandhī arthaḥ / sarvanāmāni yāni tāni sarvādīni, yāni caivaṃbhūtāni, tāni sarvanāmasaṃjñānīti ayam arthaḥ śakte (? -rtho 'śakyaḥ) pratipattum ekaśeṣa uccarita iti / 

3.40 naivāyaṃ śāstraikaśeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ / ekaśeṣatulyatvāt tv ekaśeṣa iti upanyastaḥ / kiṃbhūtas tv asāv iti ? tantraṃ nāmopāyaḥ, yathaivaikaḥ śabdo nānāvaktṛvaśād eka evānekarūpo gṛhyate / yathā [vā] vāditropakaraṇe nālikayoc cāryamāṇa [eka] eva deśaviśeṣād anekaḥ pratiśrutkāśabda upalabhyate / na cāsyānekatvaṃ loke / ataś ca nānekatvaṃ, yad anukurvann ekam eva śabdaṃ prayuṅkte ghaṭa ity etad āhuḥ iti / yathaiva caiko 'nekarūpatvaṃ pratipadyate, upāyād evaṃ kiṃ na gṛhyate, aneka ekrūpa iti ? ata ācāryeṇa tantraṃ śabda upāyo labdha ity aneka [eka]rūpa uccaritaḥ / 

3.41 dvāv eva śabdānām upāyāu uccāraṇe, kramaś ca yaugapadyaṃ ca / krameṇa tu teṣāṃ bhidyate rūpaṃ, na samudāya iti / samudāya eva vā sarvādīni sarvanāmānīti tantreṇa nirdiṣṭaḥ / yadi cāvayavāv iti śatam api vākyānāṃ śakyaṃ pratipattum iti / 

3.42 atha vā vṛttir nāmātmadharmo vidyate śabdasya, yathā kriyāṇām / pañcakṛtvo bhuṅkta iti pañcasaṃkhyā na bhujeḥ / na hi pañca tā bhujayaḥ / kiṃ tarhi ? bhuṅkta iti bhujiśabda āvartinyāṃ kriyāyāṃ vartate / tasyās tu yā vṛttiḥ, sā yadā gaṇyate, katikṛtva āvṛtteti ? tadā kriyāsaṃbandhinyām āvṛttau gaṇyamānā¥āṃ pañcaśabdo vartamānaḥ pratyayam utpādayati / yathā samabhihāre vṛttasya dvirvacanaṃ, yadvety evaṃ savranāmaśabdo 'py āvartamāna ācaryeṇāśritaḥ iti / 

3.43 yady apy eka uccāryate, viniyoktā tu bhedenaiva viniyuṅkte, yathā saptadaśasu sāmidhenīṣu prathamottame ṛcāv āvṛttiśaktī sakṛd evāmnāye paṭhite, viniyoktā tu bhedena viniyuṅkte / triḥ prathamām anv āha trir uttarām av āheti /

3.44 atha nai(? vai)kasyāpi śakti[dvaya]m āśrayiṣyāmaḥ / yathā samandhakāre vraṇaṃ di[dha]kṣuḥ didṛkṣuś ca śaktitvayam āśritya pradīpaṃ preyayatīti / ācāryeṇāpi sarvanāmaśabdaḥ śaktidvayaṃ parigṛhya prayuktaḥ, yathā ‘idaṃ viṣṇur vicakrame’ ity atra eka eva viṣṇuśabdo 'nekaśktiḥ sannidhidaivatamadhyātmam adhiyajñaṃ cātmani nārāyaṇe caṣāle ca tayā [tayā] śaktyā pravartate / evaṃ ca kṛtvā ‘vṛko māsakṛd’ ity atra grahabhedo 'pi bhavati / candramasi pravṛttau māsaśabdo 'vagṛhyate / ‘vṛko māsakṛd’ iti āvṛttdarśa[nam] /

3.45 . . . [viśe]ṣe 'vatiṣṭhete / tathā sarvaśabdaś ca vākye dṛṣṭo viśeṣe bhavati / . . . eteṣāṃ tāvat sarvanāmatvaṃ naivam abhyupāgantavyam / ekaiko 'tra sarveṣāṃ vācaka ity evaṃ hi kalpyamāne kasyacid api na syāt sarvanāmatvam / idaṃśabdo hi pratyakṣe, na parokṣe / evaṃ tacchabdaḥ parokṣe, na pratyakṣa iti / tasmāt svaviṣaya eṣāṃ sarvanāmatvam abhyupagantavyam / sāmānyaviśeṣāṇām iva svaviṣayasarvagatatvam iti / 

3.46 anapakṣeya caudanādīn viśeṣān saṃkhyādravyaprakāraviṣayaṃ kārtsnyaṃ pravṛttinimittam upādāya pravartamānaḥ sāmānye 'vasthitaḥ / odanādiprayoge tu viśeṣagater vākyārtho 'yam iti gamyate / pravartamāno vaudane 'jahadeva svaṃ pravṛttinimittaṃ viśiṣṭabhāgopanipātitvāc chabdānām odanarūpeṇābruvan sākalyarūpeṇaivācaṣṭa iti sarveṣāṃ nāmatvaṃ na nivartate / 

3.47 yadā tu sarvaśabdaḥ kasyacin nāmadheyatvenopādīyate, tadā parityajya svaṃ pravṛttinimittaṃ svarūpamātropakārī saṃjñābhāvam anubhavatīti svapravṛttinimittaparityāge 'yam / etadviśeṣe 'vasthānam / evaṃ ca kṛtvoktaṃ kasyed iti saṃvijñapanapade ubhayathā kāyeti bhavitavyam / guṇanimittāyāṃ tu samākhyāyāṃ kasmā iti bhavitavyam iti / 

3.48 evam upasarjanapadam api tyaktasvārtham atyaktasvārthaṃ vātikrānte viśeṣaṇatāmāpannamatikrāntārthavṛtti bhavatīti / na cātikrāntaṃ svena pravṛttinimittena bhavatīti viśeṣeṇāvasthānam / vākye tu svaṃ nimittaṃ na jahātīti saṃjñā kriyate / 

3.49 [saṃjñā ca nāma yato na laghīyaḥ /] śabdavyavahāro laghīyān aṇīyāṃś ca / tato 'pi saṃjñā laghīyasī kartavyā / evaṃ loko vyavaharatīti / mahattvaṃ saṃjñāyām anumānam āśritanimittā, nimittena prayuktā, yathā vāsudeve kṛṣṇaśabdo nimittena prayuktaḥ, sa nāmadheyaṃ, tathā sahasrabāhur iti bāhusahasraprayuktaṃ nāmeti / evaṃ sarvanāmatvaprayuktaṃ nāmadheyaṃ sarvādīnām iti / 

3.50 atra codyate---evam api yat saṃjñāśrayaṃ kāryaṃ smāyādi, tatsaṃjñopasarjanībhūtānāṃ na syāt, sarvāyātisarvāyeti / kutaḥ ? sarvanāmnāṃ sarvanāmeti saṃjñā / yat tv antargaṇam ‘adḍḍatarādibhyaḥ . . . ’ (A 7.1.25) ‘tyadādīnāma’ (A 7.2.102) iti, tadaviśeṣeṇa prāpnoti iti / parihāraś codyate / bhavatv evam / akārātkārayos tu paurastyaḥ eva parihāraḥ / 

ihabhavantas tv āhuḥ---naivaṃ vijñāyate, sarveṣāṃ yāni nāmāni, tāni sarvanāmāni, nānyānīti / kiṃ tarhi ? evaṃ pratisaṃdhiḥ kriyate / kimartham eṣāṃ ca viśvobhobhayeti mahatī saṃjñā kriyate / kimartham ete mahatyāḥ saṃjñāyāñ prayojakā iti / etad anumīyate, sarvanāmatvam eṣāṃ vidyate yasmāt tasmād ete mahatīṃ saṃjñāṃ prayojayantīti / yathā sati kārṣṇye, sati ca sahasrabāhutve prayojakau saṃjñāyor evam amī apīti / evaṃ sati taddharmayuktā gṛhītā ataddharmayuktāś cāgṛhītā eveti / katarādayas tyadādayaś ca svadharmāṇa eva saṃniviṣṭā iti sarvaṃ kāryaṃ saṃjñāśrayaṃ saṃniveśāśrayaṃ cāsaṃjñopasarjanībhūtānāṃ pratipāditam iti / 

4.1 athobhasya sarvanāmatve ko 'rthaḥ ? akacprayojanam iti samāpto vākyārthaḥ / bhāṣyakāras tu naivaṃ vākyārthaṃ varṇayati / kevalaṃ pāṭhe prayojanāny upanyasyati / anyathābhāvo dvivacanaṭābviṣayatvāt / ubhaśabdo 'yaṃ dvivacanaṭābviṣayaḥ / ubhaśabdo hi pradhānaviṣayaṃ bhedam āśritya pravṛttaḥ, ubhau saṃmukhau hastau bhavata iti / yathā dviśabdo bhedamātravacanaḥ / ato 'yaṃ dvivacanaviṣayaḥ, ṭābviṣayaś ca / na yadā . . . ṭābviṣayaḥ iti / dvivacanād anyatra ṭābviṣayaḥ ṭāpprakāraviṣaya ity evaṃ manyate / ke punaṣṭāpprakārāḥ ? svārthikā iti / 

4.2 ihānye vaiyākaraṇāḥ paṭhanti ‘pratyayottarapadayor advivacanaṭāpor ubhasyobhayaḥ’ / anyeṣām ‘ubhayasya dvivacanaṃ ṭāp ca lopaś ca tapayaḥ’ / teṣāṃ ṭāp iti ṭābādayo nirdiśyante / yathā ‘ardharcāḥ puṃsi ca’ (A 2.4.31) iti / anyeṣāṃ evaṃ pāṭhaḥ ‘advivacanaṭāpau’ iti / kecit punar evaṃ paṭhanti ‘ubhasyobhayo 'dvivacae’ / ubhasyobhayo bhavati advivacana iti / 

4.3 etad iha śāstre nyāyasiddham / katham ? iha tāvat ‘ubhayo maṇir ubhaye devam anuṣyā’ iti bhedavacanasyobhaśabdasyāvayavabhedānugate 'vayavinyekasmin maṇau, param avayavabhedaṃ pratipanne samudāye devam anuṣyā ity ubhasyāpravṛttiḥ / vṛttāv api saṃkhyāviśeṣo vyaktābhidhānam iti parityaktabhedam upasarjanaṃ vartata iti / nivṛtte ca bhede nivartitavyam ubhaśabdena / ekādayaś ca śabdā naitasya paryāyā iti / ya eva śabdaḥ kayācic chrutyā tulyo 'rthena vā kenavit tenaiva pravartitavyam ity ubhayaśabda eva pravartate / bhinno hy etayor u[bho]bhayaśabdayoḥ śabdopakramo 'rthātmā, arthavas tu tu tulyam ubhayoḥ / ubhau ubhayamānayeti tāv evānīyete / 

4.4 yady evaṃ dviśabdo 'pi bhedasya vṛttau parityaktatvād asādhuḥ prāpnoti / yady api parityakto bhedaḥ, yat tu dviśabdasya svaṃ pravṛttinimittaṃ tasyāparityāgāt pravartate / tena vṛttiviṣaye 'vayavāvayavinoḥ so 'yam ity abhisaṃbando bhavati / yāv etau dvau so 'yaṃ saṃgha iti dvayaśabdo na pravartate /

4.5 ubhaśabde kasmād iti ced evamartham eva ācārya ‘ubhād udātto nityam’ (A 5.2.44) iti nityagrahaṇaṃ karoti / nityam ayam anena rūpeṇāvayavinam ācaṣṭe, na tūbhaśabdarūpeṇeti sthitam / iha śāstre upasarjanam ubhaśabdas tyaktabhedaḥ sannāvayavinaṃ prāpnoti (? śāknoti) vaktum ity ubhayaśabdaḥ pravartate / 

4.6 yadā punar ayam ubhaśabdo dvivacanaṭābviṣāyaḥ / śābdasvabhāvato vacanān nyāyād vā / ka idānīm asyānyatra ? ubhayo 'nyatra / ubhaśabdo 'yaṃ yathā dvivacanaṭābviṣayas tathā pratipāditam / saṃprati yad uktam akajarthaḥ pāṭha iti taṃ paryanuyuṅkte / kiṃ ca syād yady atrākaj na syāt ? kaḥ prasajyeta / aoavādasyākaco 'bhāvāt / rūpe svare ca viśeṣam apaśyann āha---kaś cedādnīṃ kākacor viśeṣaḥ ? iti / avagrahe naivādaraḥ / ‘na lakṣaṇena padakārā anuvartyāḥ’ iti / 

4.7 ubhaśabdo 'yaṃ dvivacanaṭābviṣaya ity uktam / anyābhāvo dvivanaṭābviṣayatvād iti bruvatā pratipāditam---ayam ubhaśabdo dvivacanaviṣayaḥ, ṭābviṣayaś ceti / tena dvivacane parataḥ śrūyamāṇa ubhaśabdena sādhunā bhavitavyaṃ na lupte, nāpi vyavahite iti / viṣayamayaṃ paratvopalakṣitaṃ pratipannaḥ / yadi vākac syāt, ayam ako liṅgāt tanmadhyapatitaḥ śrutibhede hetur na bhavatīti pratipādite dvivacanaviṣayatā na virudhyate / 

4.8 ke punaḥ sati nāyaṃ dvivacanaparaḥ syāt / pūrvasya luptatvād itarasya ca vyavadhānād iti asādhutvam ubhaśabdasya prasaktam ity aprayogo vā, atha vā ubhayaśabdasya prayogaḥ prāpnoti / tatra dvivacanaparatā vaktavyā / atideśaḥ kartavyaḥ / kaviṣaye dvivacanaviṣayavad bhavatīit, dvivacanapare vā ke ubhaśabdaḥ sādhur bhavatīti vaktavyam / 

4.9 yathauva tarhi ke sati na . . . dvivacanapara evamāpy api sati ity etan nopapadyate / āpa āśrtitatvāt / evam uktaṃ ‘dvivacanaṭābviṣayatvāt’ iti pratipādita ābviṣayasya sādhutve 'nupapannam etat ‘evam āpy api sati na dvivacanaparaḥ syāt’ iti /

4.10 tatra varṇayanti---‘ubhasyobhayo 'dvivacanam i(? -ne' i)ti etasmin lakṣaṇe ṭāpo nāsti grahaṇam, ata etasminn idaṃ codyate iti / ihabhavantas tv āhuḥ / idaṃ darśitaṃ bhavatā dvivacane 'nantare śrūyamāṇe sādhur ubhaśabda iti na (ca) ṭāpo grahaṇam / na caitāvatā kṛtī tvam / idam api vaktavyam ‘sa ca ṭāp dvivacanapara eva bhavati, na ekavacanadvi(bahu)vacanapara' iti āpy āpi dvivacanaparatā vaktavyety ayam artho bhāṣyakāreṇopanyasto yathaiva . . . ke sati evam āpy api sati iti / 

4.11 avacanād āpi tadvacanam (tatparavijñānam) / na dvivacanaparatā evam āśrīyate dvivacanaṃ paraṃ [yasmā]t sa dvivacanaviṣayaḥ (? -paraḥ) iti, kiṃ tarhi ? arthalakṣaṇaṃ dvivacanaparatvam / yatra parārthābhidhānaṃ tatra bhede nivṛtte asādhutvam / ṭābādīnāṃ svārthikatvāt, yadāpi viparītaguṇapradhānād aparityaktabhedaḥ kaparo 'pi sādhur iti /

4.12 yady evam iha katham ubhābhyām ubhayror iti ? ete api na bāhyārthe iti sādhutvam ubhaśabdasya prāpnoti / tataś ca ubhataḥ ubhatreti prāpnoti / atha manyase bāhyāyām eva śaktau tral, yathādhistrīti striyāṃ bāhya evādhikaraṇe samāsa iti, evaṃ sati ubhābhyām ubhayor ity atrāpi sādhutvaṃ na prāpnoti / tasmād eṣa parihāro 'vyayatvāt tasilādīnām ‘sadūśaṃ triṣu liṅgeṣu sarvāsu ca vibhaktiṣu’ iti / vibhaktyarthamātravacaneṣu tasilādiṣu sarvo bhedaḥ parityakta ity asādhutvam asmin viṣaya iti ubhayo 'nyatrety ubhayaśabdaḥ pravartate / 

4.13 yady evam ubhayo maṇir ity atrāpy avayavini parārthe vartamāno bhedaṃ jahāti ity asādhutvaṃ prāpnoti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / etad evāsya prathamam utpattirūpam / atra pratipadya rūpam ayam anyatrobhaśabde nivṛṭte 'pi pravartate parārthābhidhāne cet pravṛtto 'yam atyantam ubhaśabda iti / 

4.14 nipātanena dvivaṇḍyādiṣu vikalpaḥ pratipādyate ubhāñjaliḥ ubhayāñjaliḥ ubhāpāṇiḥ ubhayapāṇir iti / etac ca sarvaṃ kākacordvāreṇobhaśabdasya viṣaye sādhutvam asādhutvaṃ ca pratipāditam / akajarthas tu na kartavyaḥ pāṭho 'nyārthas tu kartavyaḥ / ‘sarvanāmnaḥ tṛtīyā ca’ (A 2.3.27) ubhābhyāṃ hetubhyā idaṃ labdham, ubhayor hetvor iti / 

4.15 atha bhavataḥ / ayam api kimarthaḥ saṃniveśaḥ ? halantān na saṃbhavanti smāyādaya iti praśnaḥ / bhavato 'kaccheṣātvāni / śeṣo na sarvanāmalakṣaṇaḥ / kiṃ tarhi ? saṃniveśāśrayaḥ ‘tyadādīni . . . ’ (A 1.2.62) iti / udāharaṇamātram . . . / tṛtīyāpīṣyate / bhāṣyakārasyāpi udāharaṇamātram / anyāny api saṃniveśāśrayīṇi kāryāṇi vidyante / bhāvatyam iti mā bhūt / 

vibhāṣā diksamāse [bahuvrīhau] // 1.1.28 //

1.1 diggrahaṇaṃ kimartham ? bahuvrīhigrahaṇe ya[d a]tra vṛttisūtra uktaṃ tadvaktukāmasyāyam abhiprāyaḥ / granthacchāyā tu na bhagnā bhavatīti sarvaṃ vṛttigranthasūtram upanyasyati / abhiprāyo 'pi cāsti paryanuyoga iti / evaṃ manyate / yathaiva vidhipratiṣedhāvasthā syāt, vyaktyantare ca vikalpaḥ / itarathā hi vikalpena kṛtatvād anarthakaṃ pratiṣedhavacanaṃ syāt / vaiparītyam api puruṣahitopadeśān neṣyata iti / ‘na bahuvrīhau’ (A 1.1.29) iti pratiṣedhaṃ vakṣyati / na hy etadviśiṣṭaviṣayāvadhāranam antareṇājñānaṃ nivartate / tatra nāsūyā kartavyā yacchāstram evopadeśakam ity etad anena granthenācaṣṭe / 

2.1 atha sa[māsa]grahaṇaṃ kimartham / evaṃ manyate / yathai samāsa (? bahuvrīhi)grahaṇe sati sublopapuṃvadbhāvau pratipadyate, evam imām api vibhāṣāṃ kimiti na labheteti / samāsa eva yo bahuvrīhiḥ / yathā kartaiva yaḥ kartā, evaṃ samāsa eva yaḥ samāsa(bahuvrīhi)s tatra yathā syāt / yas tv asamāse 'pi, tatra mā bhūd iti / athavā samāsa ity etat svaryate / samāsa iti prakṛtya [yo] bahuvrīhir iti / 

2.2 atha keyaṃ vācoyuktiḥ bahuvrīhivadbhāvena yo bahuvrīhir iti / yāvatātideśiko bahuvrīhir eva na bhavati, kevalaṃ kāryaṃ pratipadyate ? naiṣa doṣaḥ / yathā eṣa brahmadatta ity antareṇa vatimatideśa evam ayam api vyapadeśaḥ / atha vātideśe vyapadeśātideśapakṣo 'pi nimittam antareṇa vidyate / tato 'pi yuktam idam ucyate /

3.1 atha bahuvrīhigrahaṇaṃ kimartham ? vakṣyamāṇaṃ buddhau vartate / saṃprati tv evaṃ manyate / dvandve ye 'vayavās te sarvādiṣu paṭhyante / teṣāṃ ca vṛttimātre puṃvadbhāve kṛte samudāyāt sa evāvayavo 'bhidhatte / . . . nābhāvaḥ suṭa iti / 

3.2 dvandev mā bhūt / dakṣiṇottara[pūrvāṇām] / sarvanāmno vṛttimātre puṃvadbhāve prakriyābhedaḥ / kecit sarveṣām eva puṃbhadbhāvaṃ kurvanti / kecid uttarapade pūrvapadasya / tatra ya uttarapade pūrvapadasya kurvanti te na sarvatra, lokavijñābād iha na bhavitavyaṃ puṃvadbhāvena ‘aparādakṣiṇottarāṇām’ iti / 

3.3 nanu ca sarveṣāṃ puṃvadbhāve sati paravalloṅganirdeśādāv utpa(? -nupapa)ttiḥ syāt / naiṣa doṣaḥ / vākyāvasthāyāṃ parasya liṅgaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ, tatsamāsasyātidiśyate / atha vā puṃvadbhāvena śabdamātraṃ nivartyate / arthasyānivṛttatvāt paravalliṅgam atidiśyate / tatra samudāyāt pūrvasmin nivṛtte vā ṭāpi ṭāb utpadyate / ṭābantād vā dvitīyaṣṭāb iti / antādivadbhāvāc cāsti tābantatā, tadantavidhiś cāsti ‘prayojanaṃ sarvanāmavyayasaṃjñāyām’ iti (A 1.1.72 vā 8) / atha ṭāpottarasya puṃvadbhāvo nāsti, na ca dvitīyaṣṭāb utpadyate, tadā ‘pratyayagrahaṇe yasmāt sa vihita . . . ’ iti katham aṅgasyābantatā ? uktam atra ‘astrīpratyayeneti’ 

3.4 yena nāprāpta . . . iti / nanu ca viṣayabhedād ayuktam idam ucyate / na hi dvandve ‘na bahuvrīhau’ (A 1.1.29) ity asti pratiṣedho, bahuvrīhau vā ‘dvandev ca’ (A 1.1.31) iti / 

3.5 ucyate / anyena prakāreṇedam upanyastaṃ ‘yena nāprāpta . . . ’ iti / katham ? yena nāprāpta nāvyāpte svasmin viṣaye pratiṣedhena tasyeyaṃ vibhāṣā syāt / tatra sa pratiṣedho 'nayā vibhāṣayā bādhitavyaḥ / kuta etat ? tasminn iyaṃ paravartamānād aprasaṅgā bahuvrīhau prāktapravṛttā kṛtārthā satī vipratiṣedhe paratvāt pratiṣedhāntareṇa bādhyate / 

3.6 atha vā purastād apavādāḥ . . . śāstrāntareṇa bādhyante / śāstrāṇi ca bhedenāpekṣyante ity anantare kṛtārthaṃ śāstram itareṇa saha spardhate / 

lakṣaṇapratipadoktayoḥ / nanu ca ‘diṅnāmāny antarāle’ (A 2.2.26) ity evaṃ samāsaḥ / yatra ca paricchinnarūpaḥ śabda upādīyate tatredam ucyate / yathā ‘śreṇyādayaḥ kṛtādibhiḥ’ (A 2.1.59) iti / ucyate / vyapadeśapratipadoktatā ihāśrīyate / ‘diksamāsa’ ity evaṃ vyapadeśaṃ kaḥ pratipattum arhati ? kiṃ yatra dig iti saṃśabdanam asty uta neti / yuktaṃ yatra dig iti saṃśabdanam asti / 

3.8 nanu coktaṃ sarvo bahuvrīhiḥ pratyārambhāt pratipadokto mahābāhur iti yathā / samāsāntarāpekṣātaḥ tad uktam / svakakṣāyāṃ tv asti lakṣaṇapratipadoktatvam / yathā ‘sādhakatamaṃ karaṇam’ iti (A 1.4.42) /

3.9 ayaṃ parihāra ārabhyamāṇe ‘diṅnāmāny antarāla’ (A 2.2.26) ity etasminn upapadyate / yadā tu pratyākhyāyate ‘digsamāsasahayogayoś cāntarālapradhānābhidhānāt’ (A 2.2.28 vā. 1) iti tadā na saṃbhavaty ayaṃ parihāraḥ / 

3.10 idaṃ tarhi prayojanam / uttarārtham eva bahuvrīhigrahaṇam avasthitam / vacanaprāmāṇyaṃ ca / bahiraṅgāpi(?-hi) jasi vibhāṣāntaraṅgeṇa pratiṣedhena bādhyate / itarathā hi dvitīyasya bahuvrīhigrahaṇasyānuvṛttir anarthikaiva syāt / 

3.11 kathaṃ punar atra ‘vibhāṣā jasi’ iti prāptir asti / yāvatā yayor eva śabdayor dvandvas tāv eva na sarvādiṣu paṭhyete vastrāntaravasanāntaraśabdau ? tadantavidhinā syād iti / sa ca nāsty antaraśabdasyopasarjanatvād anvarthasaṃjñākaraṇāc ca / saṃjñopasarjanayoḥ sarvāditvam eva nāsit, yathātisarvāyeti, na dṛśyate bahuvrīhigrahaṇasya prayojanam / 

na bahurvīhau // 1.1.29 //

1.1 kim udāharaṇam ? abhiprāyaḥ / sarvanāmnaḥ pūrvanipātāt tadantatā nāsti / yatra cānyasya pūrvanipātaḥ, sa na prayokṣyate priyaviśvāyeti / kutaḥ ? kulyārthasya śabdāntarasya bhāvāt viśvapriyāyeti prayokṣyate / 

1.2 idaṃ tarhi dvyanyāyeti / saṃkhyāyā nityasaṃkhyeyaparatantrāyā viśeṣaṇabhāvāt pūrvanipāto 'vaśyaṃbhāvīty asti tadantavidhiḥ / nanu cātrāpi sarvanāmna eva / evamartham upasaṃkhyānaṃ sarvanāmasaṃkhyayoḥ saṃnipāte yasya na prāpnoti pūrvanipātas tasya kathaṃ syād iti / kasya ca na prāpnoti ? sarvanāmnaḥ / 

1.3 naiṣa doṣaḥ / vakṣyaty etat iti / anenaitad vyākhyāyate, ‘nāyaṃ vākyārtho < yas tvayā parikalpita’ iti / idaṃ cāpy udāharaṇam / etat pradarśayati / loke cāyaṃ śabdo dṛṣṭaprayogaḥ pratiyaviśvāyeti / darśanasya ca vāvacanam eva liṅgam ‘vā priyasya’ (A 2.2.35 vā. 2) iti / 

1.4 kiṃ prayojanam ? abhiprāyaḥ / ātvaṃ tṛtīyā syāmādaya iti etatsarvanāmakāryam / etac ca bahubrīhau na saṃbhavati / ādyaṃ tāvat dṛkdṛśyavatuṣu vidhīyate / tṛtīyasyā aśravaṇaṃ smāyādīnāṃ ceti kāryāntaram upanyasyati akac mā bhūd iti / ajanteṣu kākacor viśeṣam apaśyann āha---kaś cedānīṃ kākacor viśeṣa iti / vyañjanānteṣu viśeṣaḥ / viśeṣapradarśanaparam etat / avyañjanānteṣv apy asti viśeṣaḥ / dvikaputro dvakiputra iti / 

1.5 kathaṃ punar icchatāpi / anupajāta evānyapadārthe bahuvrīhinimitte / pratiṣedhasya nimittaṃ bahiraṅgam / sarvanāmasaṃjñā caikaśabdanibandhanā nimittam akaca iti bahuvrīhyadhikaraṇasyākacā bhavitavyam / 

1.6 gomatpriya iti yathā / śāstre yat liṅgadarśanaṃ ‘pratyayottarapadayoś ca’ (A 7.2.98) iti tad evaṃ samarthyate bahiraṅgo vā samāso 'ntaraṅgaṃ bādhate, samāsanimitto vā vidhir antaraṅgaṃ bādhata iti etatsāmānyam upādīyate jñāpakam / kiriyate tatra yatnaḥ / abhiprāyaḥ / lugviṣayam eva jñāpakam, na sarvaviṣayam / mā bhūd antaraṅgaparibhāṣāyā bādhanam iti / 

1.7 asti . . . etasya vacane / yatra vidhipratiṣedhau samānāśrayau / priyaviśvāyeti / upasarjanapratiṣedhenāpy etat siddham / yāvantaḥ prakārāḥ sarvādisūtra uktāḥ anvarthasaṃjñākaraṇādayaḥ ta iha draṣṭavyāḥ / 

1.8 saṃprati śabdārtha evāyam iti pratipādayati---asti tādarthyāt tācchabdyam / 

1.9 yady evam ahakaṃ pitety atrāpi tādarthyād akacaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ prāpnoti / naiṣaḥ doṣaḥ / bhedaviśiṣṭatvād asya śabdasyaitad upakṣepasya nāsti tadarthatā / aprayogārhā eva hy alaukikās tādarthyaṃ pratipadyante / 

1.10 gonardīyas tv āha / kim asyācāryasya darśanam ? eke tāvat varṇayanti---vastrāntaravasanāntarā ity evamarthaṃ bahuvrīhigrahaṇaṃ paṭhitavyam / tatra sati prayojane 'śakyam antaraṅgo vidhir bahuvrīhiṇā bādhitum / tādarthyaṃ vā pratipattum iti / etan na / saṃpraty evoktam ‘upasarjanapratiṣedhenāpy etat siddham’ iti / antaraśabdaś cāpy upasarjanam / tasmād etat bhāṣyakāro vyāṃcati (? vyācaṣṭe) sūtram iti / 

2.1 āḍhyapūrvāya dehi / abahuvrīhitvāt pratiṣedho na prāpnotīty upasaṃkhyāyate / vyavasthāyām ity ucyate / na tatra (? cātra) vyavasthā gamyate / prādhānyenety arthaḥ / pūrvaśabdo hi vṛttau āḍhyatvānugate bhūtapūrve guṇatām āpannaḥ / apradhānatvād upasarjanatve sati sarvāditvam eva nāsti yathātisarvāyeti / 

2.2 anye varṇayanti / na cātra vyavasthā gamyata iti / yathā dakṣiṇā gāthakā iti dākṣiṇyam apāyi gāthakeṣv evam āḍhyatvam apāyyāḍhyapūrva iti / etat tu na saṃbhavati / kutaḥ ? yady apy āḍhyatvam apāyi, pūrvatvaṃ tu vyavasthitam / vyavasthā ca pūrvādīnāṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ nāḍhyatvasyeti / 

2.3 anye jahatsvārthāyāṃ vṛttau nāsti vyavasthārtha iti varṇayanti / 

tṛtīyāsamāse // 1.1.30 //

1.1. samāsa iti vartamāne / evaṃ manyate---akṛtena samāsagrahaṇena tṛtiyety etāv iti sūtre vihite śrutānumitibhyāṃ vākyāntaram upaploṣyate tṛtīyāse sarvādīni [na] sarvanāmasaṃjñānīti / pūrvatra tādarthyāt tācchabdye vṛttāv akac na śrūyate / iha ca vākya eva sarvādikāryaṃ nivartyate / kasyacic ca sadṛasya dharmasyānugamāc chakyata evaṃbhūtāṃ vṛttiṃ niṣpādayitum iti tādarthyam adhyavasīyate / yat tv atyantaṃ viguaṇaṃ vākyaṃ tasmin nāsti pratiṣedho 'devadattena pūrvasmin kāla’ iti / 

1.2 atha vā samāsa iti varta[māne]punaḥ samāsagrahaṇe (? ṇasya) / nanu cāsamāsagrahaṇam etat ? ucyate / yady apy evaṃ tathāpi kṛtākāre savarṇanirdeśe samāsa ity etac chrutisaṃnipāti netarad iti samāsagrahaṇam iti bravīti / yogavibhāge ca nañivayuktanyāyena samāsasadṛśe pratiṣedho 'samāse, na tu ‘devadattena pūrvasmin kāla’ iti / 

vibhāṣā jasi // 1.1.32 //

1.1 jasaḥ kāryaṃ prati vibhāṣā / akaj jhi na bhavati iti / ubhayam atra sarvanāmāśrayaṃ jasaḥ kāryam akañ ca / tatrākci pratiṣiddhe [ke] ca sati vyavadhānān nāsti śībhāvaḥ, katarakatamakā iti / etac copasaṃkhyeyam iti kecid varṇayanti / kecij jasa i iti jasīti / anye tu pratiyogaṃ saṃjñāvidhānāt ‘jasaḥ śī’ (A 7.1.17) ity etasmin saṃjñā vidhīyate, tena jaskārya eva vibhāṣāny atra pratiṣedha iti //

pūrvaparāvaradakṣiṇottarāparādharāṇi vyavasthāyām asaṃjñāyām // 1.1.34 //

1.1 avarādīnāṃ ca punaḥ sūtrapāṭhe grahaṇānarthakyaṃ, gaṇe paṭhitatvāt / nirjñātā vṛttikārodāharaṇebhya eva jasi vibhāṣā / sarvādiṣu ca sarvatra nityā sarvanāmasaṃjñā kathaṃ syād iti pūrvādīni viśiṣṭārthopalakṣitāni paṭhitānīty etad api nirjñātam / tad iha vākyakāro na sūtrārabhbhaṃ paryanuyuṅkte / sarveṣāṃ tu svarūpeṇa pratipadam uccāraṇaṃ tribhiḥ sūtraiḥ kimarthaṃ kriyata ity etadvākyenopanyasyati / 

1.2 avarādīnām avaraprakārāṇām āśritasvarūpāṇām / gaṇapāṭhe ni[rdiṣṭā eva pratyabhijñātā] bhaviṣyantīti pratyākhyāta eva vākyakāreṇa trisūtryāṃ pratipadam avarādīnāṃ pāṭhaḥ / bhāṣyakāras tu sūtrakārābhiprāyaṃ vyācakṣāṇaḥ pratividhatte---kathaṃ punarjñāyase sa pūrvaḥ pāṭhaḥ, ayaṃ punaḥ pāṭha iti ? 

1.3 atra bhinnagranthasaṃniveśe kathaṃbhūtaṃ paurvāparyam ? paurvāparyeṇa pāṭhāśrayaṃ gauravaṃ parihṛtaṃ bhavati / tatraitasminn anye bhāṣyakārasyābhiprāyam evaṃ vyākhyātāraḥ samarthayante / ayam āśiśabdo niyatanirjñātasaṃniveśeṣu pūrvavyavasthiteṣu keṣucid gaṇeṣūpādīyamāno dṛṣṭaḥ / tadyathā śreṇyādiṣu / tatraiva vyavasthārtham ācakṣate / kvacit tu laukikāviṣṭaprayogasadṛśāḥ kenacid ekadeśasya sādṛśyena samanvitāḥ pratipadya prakārārtham āśiśabdaṃ sūtre 'pūrvasamudāyavṛttyudāharaṇasthānīyā gaṇāḥ paṭhyante / yathā kṛtādayaḥ / tathā ca vakṣyati ‘śreṇyādayaḥ paṭhyante, kṛtādir ākṛtigaṇaḥ’ iti /

1.4 yeṣu ca vyavasthārtha ādiśabdas teṣu pūrvaṃ vyavasthitasaṃniveśeṣu nirjñātā(? niyatā)nupūrvyeṣu sūtram ārabhyate / yeṣu tu prakārārtha ādiśabdas teṣu śiṣṭaprayogasādṛśyamātranibandhane pūrvaṃ sūtre paṭhite vṛttyudāharaṇavad uttarakālaṃ gaṇanidarśanārthenārthāḥ paṭhyante / na ca teṣu bhāṣyasūtreṣu gurulaghu prati yatnaḥ kriyate / tathā ca ‘na cedānīm ācāryāḥ kṛtvā sūtrāṇi nivartayanti’ iti / bhāṣyasūtrāṇi hi lakṣaṇaprapañcābhyāṃ nidarśanasamarthanaparāṇi / tathā ca vibhaktīnāṃ sūtrasaṃniveśaviśeṣās teṣu kiṃcid vikalpyate kiṃcid avikalpitaṃ tathaiva paṭhyate, tathā vyākaraṇāntareṣv avicchinnānupūrvyapāramparyā gaṇasūtrasaṃniveśaviśeṣās teṣu kiṃcid vikalpyate, kiṃcid avikalpitaṃ tathaiva paṭhyate / tathā hi---pūrvācāryasaṃjñābhiḥ pūrvācāryanirdeśaiś ca prātipadikapāṭhaprāyeṇa vyavahāro dṛśyate / kṛnmakārasaṃdhyakṣarānto 'vyayībhāvaścety evamādi / 

1.5 tatra vyavasthāpya(? khya) ādiśabde vijñāyamāne sa pūrvaḥ pāṭhaḥ, pūrvam ācāryeṇoccāritaḥ / sūtrapāṭhas tv avarakālaḥ / tathā ca laghu sūtraṃ kartavyam ity avarādīnāṃ punaḥ pāṭhaḥ pratipadaṃ trisūtryāṃ pratyākhyātaḥ / yadi tu prakārārtha ādiśabdaḥ pścād ācāryeṇa grahaṇakavat gaṇeṣu pāṭhāt kecit samuccitāḥ, kuśalair vā praṇetṛbhiḥ smṛtyartham uparacitās tatas teṣu gurulaghubhāvaṃ pratyanādaraḥ / paṭhitam api ca punaḥ paṭhyate / upasaṃkhyānāny api ca prakṣipyante / ‘upāddvyajajinamagaurādayaḥ’ (A 6.2.194) / ‘nirajinodajinopājināḥ’ / ‘śunaḥ prasāraṇaṃ dīrghatvam’ / ‘dvitīyānupasarge’ / ‘pravṛddhādiṣu khaṭvārūḍha’ iti / ‘nadyajādyudāttatve’ bṛhanmahator upasaṃkhyānam (vā. 6.1.173.1) / ‘gaurādiṣu bṛhatyañ mahatyaḥ saudharmya’ iti / bidādiṣu gopavanādis tu saṃhitāpāṭho dvitīyādhyāyavad eva / kaṇvādayo gargādiṣu paṭhitāḥ, ‘kaṇvādibhyaḥ . . . ’ (A 4.2.111) iti punaḥ paṭhyante / ‘haritādibhyo 'ñaḥ’ (A 4.1.100) ity atra punar aharitādayo na paṭhyante / ‘sarvatra lohitādikatantebhyaḥ’ (A 4.1.18) iti ca / ‘kaṇvāt tu śakalaḥ pūrvaḥ katād uttara iṣyate’ (ma. bhā. 4.1.18) / anyathā khalv api cchede 'nyathā saṃhitāyām / anyathā mindair anyathāhāryaiḥ paṭhyate / sthūlabāhu sthūla bāhu / triveṇī trivaṇaṃ ca / paśau lūnaviyāte, paśau lūnavihīne, paśau lūnavighāte’ / prādutpattau kapigaviyudhayaḥ paṭhyante / jārabharā śvapacā iti pratipadam eva / yathā codanapāce ubhayam iti vipratiṣedho vyākhyātaḥ / nyaṅkvādiṣu ca śvapāla iti kutvam ārabhyate / sarvaṃ cedaṃ smṛtaṃ yat kiṃcit kathaṃcidupanibaddhaṃ pramāṇam / evaṃ ca prakārārtha ādiśabdaḥ sūtre paṭhitaḥ / paścād gaṇapāṭhe niścityārthaṃ prapañcenopādānaṃ na doṣavat syād iti / tad etat paryāyeṇa / 

1.6 kathaṃ punar jñāyate vyavasthārtho 'yam ādiśabdo na punaḥ prakārārtha ity api (upa)viṣṭaṃ bhavati / kutaḥ punaḥ prakārāśaṅkā ? ubhayathā śāstrapravṛtteḥ prayogadarśanāt / ‘indrāgnī paramasyāṃ pṛthivyāṃ madhyamasyām avamasyām uta sthaḥ (Ṛgveda 1.108.10)’ / ‘samānatra tiṣṭhañ juhoti’ (Maitrāyaṇī 1.4.12) /

1.7 tāni [hi] pūrvādīni / imāny avarādīni / pūrvādibhyo navabhya iti liṅgāt kramaniyamavyavasthāṃ kṛtvā bravīti / itaras tv evaṃ manyate / pāṭhakramaviṣayam uccāraṇam anenopanyastam iti / kathaṃ [punar] jñāyate sa pūrvaḥ pāṭhaḥ ? iti / granthasaṃniveśābhidhānaṃ pāṭhapaurvāparyam abhidhīyate / tad eva(m ava)tiṣṭhate / tad yathā—kathaṃ jñāyate jyeṣṭho 'yaṃ kaniṣṭhaḥ ? iti praśne pūrvakālajātatvād avarakālajātatvād iti ca nirṇayo 'bhidhīyate / tad evam iha paurvāparyeṇoccāranaṃ nirṇayārtham anenāśritam ity abhiprāyaḥ / tāni pūrvādīni / pūrvagrahaṇena ca gaṇapāṭhasaṃniviṣṭhaḥ pūrvaśabdo nirdiśyate / imāny apy avarādīni / sūtreṣu saṃniviṣṭhaḥ pūrvaśabdaḥ eva nirdiśyate / 

1.8 kecit tu pūrvaśabdam avaragrahaṇena nirdiṣṭaṃ manyante / tatra yathā pūrvabhāva iti sattaiva ca sarvavyāpārāpeṣayā nāśaṃ pratyavarabhāva ity ākhyāyate, nāśo hi viśeṣasattāyās tirobhāvād anta iti vyapadiśyate, tathehāpi niyatakramayoḥ pāṭhayor bhedāt pūrvaśabdasyaiva pūrvāvaravyapadeśo vijñāyate / ādiśabdena vyavasthāprārambho lakṣyate / 

1.9 tāni hi pūrvādīni / pūrvaṃ prāptavyavasthāviśeṣaḥ pū(? sa)rvaśabdas teṣām ādiḥ tallakṣito 'nyeṣām ārambha iti /  imāny avarādīni / sarvanāmaprakaraṇasthasūtrāṇam avarakālatvena nirjñātaḥ prathamasaṃniviṣṭas tallakṣitaḥ śeṣaḥ, śeṣa ārambha iti / pūrvaśabdena vā sūtrāṇīty avarādīni pratinirdeiśyante / 

1.10 tad evaṃ liṅgāt kramaniyamavyavasthāmanāśritāṃ manyamāna āha---imāny api pūrvādīni / pratijñāmātreṇāśakyāny upavyākhyātum / ubhayathā śāstrasya pravṛtteḥ, prayogadarśanāc ca śakyo 'yam ādiśabdaḥ prakārābhidhāyī samarthayitum / 

1.11 tataḥ prāk upanyastaṃ liṅgadarśanaṃ kramaniyamavyavasthāhetutvenābhivyaktīkriyate---yad ayaṃ ‘pūrvādibhyo navabhya (-bhyo vā)’ iti navagrahaṇaṃ karoti / gaṇapāṭhe hi tyadādinivṛttyarthaṃ navagrahaṇam arthavat / yadi ca prasiddhavyavasthito niyatānupūrvīkaḥ paricchinnasvarūpaviśeṣo gaṇapāṭhas tato navagrahaṇaṃ śakyābhisaṃbandhaṃ kartum / tad yathā / ‘śamām aṣṭānāṃ . . . ’ (A 7.3.74), ‘phaṇāṃ ca saptānām’ (A 6.4.125), ‘kiraś ca pañcabhyaḥ’ (A 7.2.75) iti / tad evaṃ sūtreṣu navānām eva pūrvādīnāṃ saṃniveśāt laghunopāyena siddhāyāṃ pratipattau navasaṃkhyāgrahaṇaṃ jñāpakam---‘asti ca pāṭho bāhyaś ca sūtrād ācāryeṇa pūrvamāmnāto vyākaraṇāntarebhyaś ca kaścid avicchinnaprāraṃparyo naiva kalpita’ iti / sūtrasaṃniveśenāpi ca kvacit vyavaharati / tad yathā---‘cādiṣu . . . ’ (A 8.1.58) / tad evaṃliṅgo vyavasthārtho 'yam ādiśabdaḥ / chandoviṣayatvāc ca prayogadarśanam aliṅgaṃ prakārābhidhāyitvasya / 

1.12 apara āha / tāni pūrvādīni sarvādigaṇe paṭhitāni / teṣāṃ tatra nirjñātaṃ sāhacaryam arthaviśeṣeṣu nibaddhānam / ekadeśopādāne 'pi sūtreṣu saṃpratyayaḥ siddhaḥ / tad yathoktaṃ ‘ktena ca pūjāyām’ (A 2.2.12) iti matibuddhipūjārthalakṣaṇaṃ vijñāyate / tathā ca pūrvāvaraśabdau gaṇe paṭhitau dṛṣṭvā sūtreṣu sāhacaryāt śakyalakṣaṇatvaṃ manyamānair avarādīnāṃ sūtreṣv ekadeśaḥ paṭhyate / imāny api pūrvādīni / gaṇapāṭha ihāśrīyate / tena, lakṣaṇatvena ca yasya kasyacid ekasya śabdasyopādānaṃ, tataḥ samudāyaḥ kriyate, tasmād upalabhyamānena krameṇa paurvāparyam iti / 

1.13 evaṃ tarhi gaṇapāṭhe viśiṣṭārthopalakṣitānāṃ pūrvādīnāṃ sūtreṣv api vibhāṣāyām ārabhyamāṇāyāṃ tadarthānām evāvyabhicāreṇa grahaṇaṃ kathaṃ syād iti sūtratrayavibhāgena pratipadapāṭhaḥ punar āśrīyate / viṣayāntare hi yāny upalakṣaṇāni prathanam upādīyante teṣāṃ niyatas tadviṣayeṣu kāryeṣu saṃnidhānavyāpāraḥ / tad yathā---śuklavāsasamānaya, jaṭinam āpīḍinam iti / yasya tu kenavil lakṣaṇena lakṣitasyāny atra cārthe śiṣṭasya viṣayāntareṇa punar upādānaṃ [na] tatra lakṣaṇānāṃ śabdavyāpāraḥ kalpitaḥ saṃnidhīyate / ato vibhāṣāyām arthānāṃ saṃnidhānena śabdābhedaṃ prati vyāpārāthaṃ punarvacanam / etac cāyam ante kariṣyati / 

1.14 itaro yathaiva ‘pūrvādibhyo navabhyaḥ . . . ’ (A 7.1.16) iti vyavasthādivṛttānām eva pūrvādīnāṃ grahaṇaṃ tathehāpi bhaviṣyati / bhedakāś cārthāḥ / tathā hi---sadēśasyāpy asaṃniviṣtasya na bhavati grahaṇaṃ, tailamātrā ghṛtamātreti / tatra yathaivānubandhenārtha upalakṣitaḥ śrutiyoge 'pi śabdasya bhedahetur vijñāyate / tathā vyavasthādayo 'py arthaviśeṣavyavacchede hetur bhaviṣyatīti manyamāna āha---evaṃviśiṣṭāny evaitāni gaṇe paṭhyanta iti /

1.15 dvyādiparyudāsena paryudāso mā bhūd iti punarvacanam / iha tyadādīny āpiśalaiḥ kim ādīny asmatparyantāni, tataḥ pūrvaparāvareti yogatrayaṃ, tataḥ svarādīni / tayā ca pāṭhavyavasthayā pūrvādīnāṃ dvyādiparyudāsaḥ prasajyate / tatra yathaiva ‘anulakṣaṇevacanānarthakyaṃ sāmānyakṛtatvāt’ (vā 1.4.84.1) vidyud anu vanam iti karmapravacanīyasaṃjñā / tasya bādhikā tṛtīyā punarārambhāt sthānāntaraprāptakarmapravacanīyalakṣaṇadvitīyā bādhate / tathā paryudāsāt tasilādiṣv apavādabhūtāṃ nivṛttiṃ punaḥ pratipadam uccāraṇena vidhīyamānā sarvanāmasaṃjñā tasilādīnāṃ sthānāntarāpattiṃ kalpayantī bādhate / tatra pūrvādīnāṃ nityā saṃjñā tribhiḥ sūtraiḥ punar vidhīyate / vākyabhedena ca svaritacihnāsañjanāt pratisūtraṃ jasi vibhāṣānumīyate / ca cedam ekavākyabhāve vibhāṣāyāḥ saṃbhavati / pratipadoccāraṇaṃ vākyabhedena vibhāṣāyāḥ saṃnidhānārtham / ākāṅkṣāsaṃbhave 'sati tu yuktam ekavākyatvam / 

1.16 itaraḥ pūrvaṃ sūtrapāṭho yaiḥ parigṛhyate, sarveṣāṃ teṣāṃ tu kramavyavasthā na niyamakāri liṅgam upādatte / tad yathā / devabrahmaṇor anudāttavacanaṃ jñāpakaṃ saty anyathāpāṭhe sāmarthyāt vyavasthāntaram avatiṣṭhata iti / dvyādipratiṣedhāc ca kima upasaṃkhyānaṃ jñāpakaṃ neha prakaraṇe pūrvaṃ sūtrapāṭhaḥ parigṛhyata iti / 

1.17 ‘pūrvatrāsiddham’ (A 8.2.1) iti liṅgasya tadviṣayatānivṛttyarthaṃ vārttike śabdāntaraviṣayaṃ liṅgāntaram upādatte---‘jaśbhāvād iti ced uttaratra’ (vā. 8. 3. 13. 2) iti /

1.18 yeṣāṃ pūrvādīnāṃ siddhā sarvanāmasaṃjñā teṣām eva jasi vibhāṣāṃ vakṣyāmīti pratipadaṃ yogatrayeṇa punarvacanam / vibhaktāś caite yogeṣv arthā na śakyā ekasmin yoge saṃkaram antareṇopādātum / tasmāt pāṭhāntare 'pi parikalpyamāne pūrvādīni sapteti yogabhedo [na] nivartate / tathā ca yathā tāny eva grahaṇānīti na gurulāghavakṛtaṃ vaiṣamyaṃ, tathā ta eva trayo yogā iti na kaścil lāghavakṛto viśeṣaḥ / 

1.19 kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ pratipadamātreṣṇāgṛhyamāṇeṣv artheṣu prāptavibhāṣā na saṃbahvati / prāptavibhāṣāyām anuvādapūrvā pravṛttiḥ, pravṛttibhedāc coktānuvāde ca prasiddhā śabdasya pravṛttir uparudhyate / pravṛtibhede 'pi gauravam anuṣajyate / uktaṃ cālpena yatnena mahato mahato 'rthān iti / tatrāpūrvo vidhir astu niyamo 'stv atypūrvo vidhir bhaviṣyati, na niyama iti / 

1.20 kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ gaṇapāṭhe dṛṣṭānām arthānāṃ sūtreṣv antarbhavatām abhedakatvaṃ na vijñāyeta ? nyāyavirodhasaṃbhave lakṣaṇasyāny atra caritārthasya saṃnidhānena vyāpārāsaṃbhavāt tannyāyavirodhasaṃnidhiḥ / tad yathā---yo 'sau chāgo baddhaḥ sa śīghramānīyatām, ya udake prāṇāyāmenāvasthitaḥ so 'sau bhojyatām, vācaṃyamo niścalaḥ sthāyī sa sākṣitve niyujyatām iti / avirodhe tu vinā śabdavyāpāreṇa / yaḥ śuklavāsā dṛṣṭaḥ sa bhojyatām iti saṃnidhānāsaṃnidhānābhyāṃ lakṣaṇam aniyamenaiva upatiṣṭate / tena pravṛttibhedo na vijñāyate viṣayasyāsvaritatvāt / 

1.21 nanu cātrāpi pūrvādīni sarvādīnīti sarvanāmasaṃjñāviṣayo nirjñātaḥ svaryate / sarvanāmaśabdo vā saṃjñiviśeṣaṇārtho vijñāyate / yathā saṃkhyāśabdaḥ ṣaṭsaṃjñāyām / neha sarvādīnām anuvṛttiḥ śakyā kartum / tayagrahaṇe ihānuvṛttau satyāṃ prāptavibhāṣā vijñāyate / nāpi sarvanāmaśabdaḥ svarūpapadārthakaḥ uttareṣv api yogeṣu saṃjñāvikalpenārthiṣu saṃjñādharmeṇa pravṛttas tenaivābhinnena rūpeṇa saṃjñiviśeṣakaḥ śakyo vijñātum / ‘pūrvādibhyo navabhyaḥ’ ity atra ‘sarvanāmnaḥ smai’ (A 7.1.14) iti rūḍhasaṃbandhaḥ sarvanāmaśabdaḥ saṃjñipadāthakaḥ pūrvādīnām avacchede hetur vijñāsyate / yeṣu tv antargaṇeṣu caritārthāni lakṣaṇāni teṣv avirodhanaiṣāṃ lakṣaṇānāṃ saṃnidhānāsaṃnidhānaprayatnaḥ prakalpayitavya iti /

svam ajñātidhanākhyāyām // 1.1.35 //

1.1 svaśabdo 'yam ātmātmīyajñātidhanaparyāyavacanaḥ / āminijviṣaye ceśvaravācyaiś caryavācī vā / tad yathā---‘svakṛtā iriṇe parācīrnidadhāti’ (MS 3.2.4) / ‘na hi svaḥ svaṃ hinasti’ (TS 5.1.7.1) / tathātmīye---svaṃ gṛhaṃ, svaḥ puruṣa iti / dhane vartate- . . . / tad yathā---‘yāvanta evāsya svāstānasmā anukānavivādinaḥ karoti’ (MS 4.3.7) iti / 

1.2 ākhyāgrahaṇaṃ kimartham / abhiprāyaḥ / saṃsargo vākyārthaḥ / yathā---paśuṃ chāgam iti paśuśabdasya paśutvaṃ tadupalakṣitaṃ vā dravyam abhidheyam tathā chāgaśabdasya prayoge kevalaṃ medhyāvāpamātraṃ na tu paśutvaṃ nyūnam adhikaṃ vā, gardabhādīnām aśvatarāṇāṃ nivṛttiḥ / na hi saṃbhavati yac chāgaikārthasamavāyi ca paśutvaṃ, nyūnatvam ādhikyaṃ vā syād gardabhaikārthasamavāyi ceti / evaṃ svaśabdo 'py ātmīyavacana evādhikyaṃ tu vākyārtha iti saṃsargadarśī pratyācaṣṭe ākhyāgrahaṇam / 

1.3 jñātidhanaparyāyavācī yaḥ svaśabdas tasya grahaṇaṃ yathā syāt iti / pratiṣedha iti vākyaśeṣaḥ / anyeṣām ‘ajñātidhanaparyāyavācī’ iti granthaḥ / te vidhav ity evaṃ samarthayante / asya tu bhedo vākyārtha ity etaddarśanam / bahavaḥ svaśabdāḥ pratyarthavṛttayaḥ / tatra śruteḥ sāmānyāda anabhivyaktārthaviśeṣāḥ śabdāntarād viśiṣṭārthavṛttasya eta iti khyāpyante / tasmāt sva putrā iti svaśabdasyaiva jñātir artha ity etasmin darśane prāpnoti pratiṣedha iti ākhyāgrahāṇenaitat kriyate---śabdāntaraprayogam antareṇa yatra svarūpād eva jñātidhanasaṃpratyayas tatra kathaṃ syāt pratiṣedha iti / 

1.4 atha vārthaprakaraṇābhyāṃ yatra nirjñātaṃ jñātidhanavacanatvaṃ tatra mā bhūt pratiṣedha ity etadākhyāgrahaṇena kriyate //

antaraṃ bahiryogopasaṃvyānayoḥ // 1.1.36 //

1.1 atra bhinnā pratipattiḥ / kecin manyante---bahiṣā yoga iti / teṣām antare gṛhāḥ, antarā gṛhā iti cāṇḍālamṛtapagṛhā ucyante, ye prākārād bahiḥ / yeṣāṃ tv artho bāhyena yogo bahiryoga iti teṣām abhyantare gṛhāḥ te antare, antarā gṛhā iti cocyante / yathā cātra vipratipattir evam upasaṃvyāne 'pi, karmakaraṇaśabdatvāt / upasaṃvīyate 'nenety upasaṃvyānam / 

1.2 upasaṃvyānagrahaṇam anarthakam iti / śāṭakayugādyarthaṃ tarhīdaṃ vaktavyam / abhiprāyaḥ / bahiryoge samapramāmeṣv aparihiteṣu ca durjñānam ākāraviśeṣādi / aparihitam api puṣpaṭādi kaupīnādi ca nirjñāyate uttarīyatvena cāntarū¥īyatvena ca / śāṭakayuge tv etan nāsti / tasmāt na sidhyati bahiryogeṇeti /

1.3 atrāpi ya eṣa manuṣyaḥ / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / kriyamāṇepy upasaṃvyānagrahaṇe na sidhyati śāṭakayuga iti manyate / bhāvyupasaṃvyānaṃ buddhau kṛtvā pravartiṣyate vyapadeśa ity evaṃ kecid vyākhyātāro varṇayanti / anye tu manyante / ādigrahaṇaṃ vākye kṛtaṃ, tasyedaṃ prayojanaṃ---śāṭakatrayārthaṃ śāṭakacatuṣṭayārthaṃ vā / tatra triṣu śāṭakeṣu parihiteṣu bāhyena yoga ity etasmin sūtrārthe prathamasya śāṭakasya na prāpnoti / atha bahiṣā yoga ity evam api na prathamasy ana madhyamasya / iṣyate cobhayasyāntare śāṭakāḥ, antarāḥ śāṭakā iti / tasmād yugārthaṃ pratyākhyātam / evam arthaṃ tu vaktavyam / 

2.1 apurīti vaktavyam / ‘prātipadikagrahaṇe liṅgaviśiṣṭasya’ iti prāpnoti / tasmāt pratiṣedhavacanam / pūś ca kasmiṃś cid deśe prākārābhyantare eva kriyate, kasmiṃś cid bahiḥ prākārād iti / ubhayaprakāro 'py asti puro bahiryoga iti //











mahābhāṣyadīpikāyāṃ ṣaṣṭham āhnikam: uttarārtham svarādi[nipātam avyayam] // 1.1.37 //

[1] prakāracatuṣṭayam iha---cādiṣu svarādīnāṃ pāṭhaḥ, svarādiṣu vā cādīnām / tathā nipātasaṃjñaiva vā kartavyā, avyayasaṃjñaiva vā / ekapāṭhe caikaiva saṃjñā kartavyā / yadi vā nipātasaṃjñā, yadi vāvyayasaṃjñā / avyaye tv etanniścitam---‘iha kiṃcid avyayaṃ kriyāpradhānaṃ, kiṃcidvibhaktyarthapradhānam / na caitasyārthasya liṅgasaṃkhyābhyām asti yogaḥ’ iti / svarādayo 'pi vibhaktyarthapradhānatvād asattvavacanā eva / evṣa khalu viśeṣo vācakāḥ svarādayo dyotakāś cādaya iti / sarvathā cāsattvavacanatvam asti svarādīnām iti pṛthakpāṭhaṃ paryanuyuṅkte / prativacanaṃ tv asamarthaṃ dṛśyate---cādīnāṃ vā asattvavacanānām iti / 

[2.1] saṃpratyanyatarā saṃjñā kim iti na kṛteti pṛcchati---atha kimartham ubhe saṃjñe iti / ‘prāgrīśvarān nipātāḥ’ (A 1.4.56) iti paṭhitvā tata idaṃ paṭhitavyaṃ ‘svarādi’ (A 1.1.37), ‘taddhitaś cāsarvavibhaktiḥ’ (A 1.1.38), tataś ‘cādayo 'sattve’ (A 1.4.57) iti / puṣkalaṃ prayojanam ācaṣṭe---svarādīnām api pragṛhyasaṃjñā prasajyeteti / kvātra---‘kimo 't’ (A 5.3.12) ity ekāras taddhitas tasya pragṛhyāśrayaḥ [prakṛtibhāvaḥ] syāt / 

[2.2] cādiṣu yathā ‘cādilope vibhāṣā’ (A 8.1.63) iti / tac ca na / anāṅ iti pratiṣedhavacanaṃ liṅgaṃ, na catvāro gṛhyanta iti / 

[2.3] evaṃ tarhy avyayasaṃjñeti / atrāpy anuṣaktaḥ pūrvo doṣaḥ / avyayasaṃjñā cedaivaikā, tatsūtram evaṃ paṭhitavyam---avyayam ekājanāṅ iti / anuṣaktāḥ svarādayaḥ / 

[2.4] yas tu doṣa ucyate tadgarīsayā nyāseneti / ayaṃ ca śakyapratividhānaḥ / evaṃ paṭhiṣyate---avyaye nañkucādīnām iti / caturṣv eva saṃpratyayaḥ prāpnoti / ktvāyāṃ vā pratiṣedhāt nipātanāc ca snātvākālakādiṣv aprasaṅga iti nārthaḥ parigaṇaneneti / 

taddhitaś cāsarvavibhaktiḥ // 1.1.38 //

[1.1] kim ayam avyayasaṃjñā taddhitāntasyāhosvit taddhitamātrasyeti ? samudāyasyeti vijñāyate / kutaḥ ? pratyayagrahaṇe yasmāt sa vihitas tadāder iti / 

[1.2] nanu ca saṃjñāvidhau pratyayagrahaṇe tadantavidhiḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ / evaṃ tarhy aupasaṃkhyānikaḥ prākaraṇikas tadantavidhir iti---‘prayojanaṃ sarvanāmāvyayasaṃjñāyām’ iti / yathā paramoccaiḥ paramanīcair iti /

[1.3] kaḥ punar viśeṣaḥ samudāyasyāvayavasya vāvyayasaṃjñāyām ? avayavasyāvyayasaṃjñāyāṃ satyāṃ tatra yatreti luko 'bhāvaḥ / na hi pratyayāt subvihita iti / 

[1.4] ucyate / vacanaprāmāṇyād atra paratvamātramāśritya luk kariṣyate / akaharthaṃ syād vacanam / tasmāt samudāyasyaivāvyayasaṃjñāyāṃ satyām avyayavihitāyāś ca supo luganyaganyātva(?)vidhau nāsti doṣaḥ / 

[1.5] atha samudāya āśrīyamāṇe katham idaṃ saṃbadhyate, tena, asarvavibhaktir iti ? na hi samudāyasyotpattau nimittaṃ vibhaktiḥ, anumeyatvāt samudāyānām / pratyayā eva hi vidhīyante / 

[1.6] atrāpi samudāye 'saṃbhavād avayavāśrayo vyapadeśaḥ pravartate / taddhitāntaḥ śabdo 'vyayasaṃjñaḥ sa ced asarvavibhaktyavayavo bhavatīti / nāsti viśeṣaḥ pratyaye saṃjñani tadante vā / 

[2.1] asarvavibhaktir iti / vigrahabhedād bhedaṃ pratipannāḥ vṛttikārāḥ / keṣāṃ cid vigraho ‘nāsya sarvā vibhakaya utpattau nimittam’ iti / na tv īdṛśānām asamānādhikaraṇapadānām asti bahuvrāhir ity arthopapradarśanam etat, vibhaktayaḥ kāraṇaṃ pratyayaḥ kāryam ity asyārthasya / vartipade tu asarvā vibhaktayo 'syeti / anyeṣām ayaṃ vigrahaḥ---asarvā vibhaktir asmād iti / teṣām api matvarthe ‘vā pūrvasya vidhānāt’ pañcamyarthabahuvrīher abhāva iti taddhitasya kāraṇatvaṃ, kāryatvaṃ ca vibhakter iti / pañcamyā pradarśanam eva kevalam, iyam eva tu kalpanā---asarvā vibhaktir asyeti /
