[2.2] tatra ya evaṃ varṇayanti na sarvā vibhaktaya utpattau nimittam asyeti te pratyudāharanti---taddhita iti kimartham ? upasarajaḥ manduraja iti / yaś ca vibhakter ananataraḥ pratyayao yaś ca vyavahitaḥ sarvaḥ sarvasya vibhaktinimittatvena pravartate / ayaṃ ca ‘saptamyāṃ janer ḍaḥ’ iti saptamīm āśritya vidhīyate / taddhitas tu na bhavati / 

[2.3] asarvavibhaktigrahaṇasya pratyudāharaṇam aupagavaḥ kāpaṭavaḥ iti / katham idaṃ pratyudāharaṇam ? yāvatā ca kriyamāṇe 'py asarvavibhaktigrahaṇe 'vyayasaṃjñā prāpnoty eva / katham ? yadi tāvad evam āśrīyate yasya śabdarūpeṇami[? i]daṃ vibhaktir āśrīyate yathā ‘pratiyoge pañcamyās tasiḥ’ (A 5.4.44), ‘. . . akartari tṛtīyāyāḥ’ (A 5.4.46) iti, so 'yam asarvavibhaktir iti / aṇaś ca ‘tasyāpatyam’ (A 4.1.92) iti ṣaṣṭhī artharūpeṇāśritā na śabdarūpeṇeti / iha doṣaḥ ‘prakāravacane thāl’ (A 5.3.23) iti / na hy atra kācid vibhaktiḥ śabdavatī asti / 

[2.4] athāpy evaṃ kalpyate / aṇayaṃ sarvā vibhaktīr āśritya vihitaḥ / ‘tasyāpatyam’, ‘tatra bhavaḥ’, ‘tata āgataḥ’ iti / evam api na saṃbhavati caturthyanupādānād iti / sāpy asti / ‘bhaktād aṇanyatarasyām’ (A 4.4.68) iti bhaktam asmai dīyate bhākta iti / kiṃ tu dākṣir iti iñ apatya eva vihita ity asarvavibhaktitvam avyāvṛttam eva / 

[2.5] tatra darśanaṃ vṛttikārasya lakṣyate ‘prātipadikāt taddhita utpadyate’ iti / yac ca vigrahapadaṃ bhedaviṣayaṃ tadantyaṃ vṛttiviṣayaṃ na bhavatīty anvākhyānakāle pratyayotpattau cikīrṣitāyāṃ yac ca vibhaktyantaṃ yac ca prātipadikam ubhayam api tadaprayogārham iti vibhaktyantāpi prakṛtiḥ saṃbhavati prātipadikam apīti sarvavibhaktitvam avasīyate / asmiṃs tu yo doṣaḥ sa vārtika eva darśito vinā nāneti /

[2.6] asarvavibhaktau / asarvavibhaktiḥ nimittam iti asminn arthe vṛtto yady ayaṃ bahuvrīhir āśrīyate avibhaktir ity upasaṃkhyānaṃ kartavyam / tatrāvibhaktinimittam iti katham āśrīyate ? yadi tāvad avidyāmānavibhaktiḥ śabdaḥ prātipadikaṃ nimittam asyety evaṃ varṇyata aupagavādiṣu doṣaḥ / athāśrūyamāṇavibhaktir avibhaktir nimittam asyeti evam api nitya iti doṣaḥ / ayaṃ hi tyabaśrūyamāṇavibhakter niśabdād vihita iti / anāśritavibhaktiviśeṣaḥ avibhaktiḥ, evam api viśaṃkaṭādiṣu doṣaḥ / tasmān nāyaṃ pāṭhaḥ / kiṃ tarhi ? pradarśanam etat / kasya ? gaṇapāṭhasya / ye gaṇe upasaṃkhyātā, arhanti te 'smin sūtrārtha upasaṃkhyānam iti / sūtrārthāntare tu na ta upasaṃkhyātavyāḥ / 

[2.7] nanu vācā prakaraṇam upasaṃkhyānam ucyate / naiṣa doṣaḥ / pāṭham apy upasaṃkhyānaśabdenācaṣṭe / tad yathā / dvyādiṣu pratiṣedhāt kima upāsaṃkhyānam (tu. Pā. 5.3.2, vā. 2) iti / 

[2.8] tatraitat syāt / sati gaṇapāṭhe [kiṃ] sūtreṇeti / lakṣaṇaprapañcābhyāṃ kathaṃ nirdiṣṭāḥ syur iti / 

[2.9] atha vā pūrvakāraṇe saty evaṃ pravartate avibhaktinimittasyeti / yeṣām anena lakṣanenāvibhaktinimittasya na prāpnotiṣyate ca, teṣām upasaṃkhyānaṃ kartavyam ity anāśritya gaṇapāṭham ucyate vinā nāneti / vibhaktiviśeṣasya taddhite 'nupādānāt ‘aviśeṣeṇaivotpadyante utpannānāṃ niyamaḥ’ iti sarvavibhaktitvam avasitam / viśaṃkaṭādayo 'py evaṃ prasaktāḥ / teṣāṃ tu pūrveṇa krameṇāprasaṅga uktaḥ / 

[2.10] kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ na sidhyati ? sūtrārthāntare siddhiṃ yasya ṣṭayi(?) / 

[2.11] sarvavibhaktir hy eṣa bhavati / kiṃ kāraṇam ? aviśeṣeṇa . . . asarvavibhakter anyaḥ ko bhavitum arhati ? yasya vā sarvā vibhaktaya utpattau nimittaṃ, yasya vā naiva kācit so 'pi tasmād anya eva bhavati / atha vā aviśeṣeṇa vihitatvāt iti / te varṇayanti---vibhaktiviśeṣam anāśritya vihitatvāt, atha vā supām aviśeṣavihitatvāt avyayāt sarvavibhaktyantād utpattir iti / 

[2.12] tralādīnāṃ cipasaṃkhyānam / ādiśabdaḥ prakāre draṣṭavyaḥ / tasilo 'py upādānāt bhāṣye / 

[2.13] nanu ca viśeṣeṇaite iti / abhiprāyaḥ / abhavad ādiyoge pañcamīsaptamyāv eva nimittam iti / itaro bhavadādiyoge sādhutvābhyanujñānād etasmin viṣaye sarvavibhaktitvam asti / tataś cāprāptir itīdam āha---vakṣyaty etad iti /

[2.14] yadi punar avibhaktiḥ śabdaḥ iti / aśrūyamāṇavibhaktir iti / yady evaṃ rājapuruṣādiṣu rājaśabdo 'vibhaktitvād avyayaṃ bhavati, pūrvapadaprakṛtisvaratvaṃ kasmān na pratipadyate ? samāsāntodāttatvam aluksamāseṣu bhaviṣyati dāsyāḥ putrādiṣu / parugaṇanād apy etat siddham---‘avyaye nañkunipātānām’ iti / 

[2.15] idaṃ tarhi dadhi madhv iti / avyayalakṣaṇo 'kac syāt dadhakīti / dadhikam iti ceṣyate / evaṃ tarhi śabdākṛtir āśrīyate / na ceyam ākṛtir avibhaktiḥ / vacanāntare savibhaktir api dadhinī dadhīnīti / śabdākṛtigrahaṇe cārthākṛtir apy āśrīyate / vacanāntare savibhaktir api dadhinī dadhīnīti / śabdākṛtigrahaṇe cārthākṛtir apy āśrīyate / tena tulyāyām itiśabdākṛtāv arthākṛter bhedād avyayatvaṃ na bhaviṣyati yathā itiḥ śakaṭam iti / 

[2.16] kathaṃ punar ayam itaretarāśrayadoṣo na bhaviṣyati ? evaṃ manyate / laukikīṃ pratipattim āśrityāvyayasaṃjñāyām avibhaktitvam upādīyate / prayoge cātyantam ete 'vebhaktikāḥ / tasmāc chāstrapratipattim anāśritya pravartiṣyāmaha iti / 

[2.17] avibhaktāv itaretarāśrayatvād aprasiddhiḥ / śāstrapratipattim āśritya doṣa upādīyate / śāstreṇa hi prātipadikāt svādayaḥ pratipāditā aviśeṣeṇa / tadyathā babhrumaṇḍulamakānām apratyāyakānām apatye 'sādhutvam evam eṣām api nānāvinādīnām / tatra nivartakaṃ śāstram avibhakteḥ sādhutvasya pratipādakam ārabdhavyam ‘avyayād āpsupaḥ’ iti / avyayebhyaḥ svādayo notpadyante noccāryanta ity etam arthaṃ lukā vyācaṣṭe pratyayalakṣaṇam prārthatamānaḥ / yāni padasaṃjñādīni sati pratyaye dṛṣṭāni tānīhātyantaṃ pratyayasyāsaṃbhave bhavantīty evam artham / evaṃ ca satyavibhaktitvam ayaṃ pratipattā śāstrāt pratipadyate / avibhaktitvaṃ ca luki sati, luk cāyaṃ yatnapratibaddha ity evaṃbhūtalakṣaṇaṃ nārthakriyāyāḥ karaṇe samarthaṃ bhavatīti / 

[2.18] aliṅgam asaṃkhyam iti vā / pūrvasyānavasthitatvād vikalpānupapattiḥ / vākyasya vākyena saṃbandhād adoṣaḥ / idam evaṃ saṃbandham upanīyate---‘avibhaktāv itaretarāśrayatvād aprasiddhaḥ, aliṅgam asaṃkhyam iti vā’ itaretarāśrayatvād aprasiddhir ity eva / evaṃ cāśrīyamāṇe 'nivṛttam itaretarāśrayatvam iti / 

[2.19] atha vaivaṃ saṃbandhaḥ kriyate / yad uktam avibhaktiḥ śabda ity atra vibhaktiśabdaḥ svādiṣu vā vṛtto 'tha vā vibhāgo vibhaktir iti bhedavacana āśrīyate / etāv antaś ca nāmno vibhāgā yad uta liṅgasaṃkhyākarmādayaḥ / tatrāliṅgasaṃkhyopādānāt karmādayo nirastāḥ pratipattavyāḥ / tasmād etatpratipādayati---luptavibhaktir vā avibhaktiḥ / atha vā bhedānaprāpto 'yaṃ so 'vibhaktir iti / 

[2.20] arthasya ca vyākaraṇe 'tyantam anupadeśād itaretarāśrayadoṣam apaśyann idam upanyasyati---atha vā aliṅgam asaṃkhyam avyayam iti /

[2.21] evam apītaretarāśrayam eva bhavati / vyutpatsuḥ arthamayaṃ kvacic chāstrād eva pratipadyate / yathā kartrabhiprāyaṃ kriyāphalasya dhātoḥ saṃbhavati, asya na saṃbhavatīti pratipadyate svaritettatvāt ñittvāc ca / yas tu vidvān asāvasaty api liṅge pratipadyate yathā pratyākhyātaṃ svaritañitgrahaṇam / evaṃ ca kṛtvā ta evācāryeṇa paṭhitā ubhayavanta iti / ato 'yam api śāstrāt pratipadyamāno liṅgasaṃkhyāvattvam api śāstrāt pratpadyata iti itaretarāśrayadoṣam āha /

[2.22] nedaṃ vācanikam asaṃkhyatāliṅgatā vā / kiṃ tarhi ? svābhāvikam etat / sarvam eva svābhāvikam, [kiṃ]cit tu dṛṣṭavyabhicāraṃ yathākṣīṇi me darśanīyāni, padā me sukumāratarā iti (MBh 1.322.5, 6) / vyabhicāranivṛttyarthaṃ yatno yatrā śrīyate tadvācanikam ity ucyate / yat tv adṛṣṭavyabhicāraṃ na tatrāniṣṭaṃ dṛśyata iti yatno na kriyate / tathā coktaṃ ‘na vāniṣṭādarśanād iti’ (vārttika 2.2.30,2) / na hi rājapuruṣe puruṣarāja iti asti prayogaḥ / na ca prapacatīti prayoktavye pacatipreti / tatra śabdeṣv eva tāvad dṛṣṭavyabhicāreṣv ayaṃ vṛttāntaḥ, kiṃ punar artheṣu ? tasmāt pratijñānād eṣā aliṅgatā asaṃkhyatā vā dṛṣṭādṛṣṭavyabhicārā / evaṃ sati nāsti itaretarāśrayadoṣaḥ / 

[2.23] siddhaṃ tu pāṭhāt / yāvatā vākyakārasya sthita evetaretarāśrayadoṣa iti pāṭhopanyāsaḥ / anye tu vārthe tuśabtaṃ varṇayanti---siddhaṃ vā pāṭhād iti / teṣāṃ api na saṃbhavati sarvasya pratyākhyātatvāt / 

[2.24] tasilādayaḥ prāk pāśapaḥ / yāvad edhāc ca / gaṇe 'py evaṃ paṭhyate / tasilādis taddhita edhācpratyanta iti / śasprabhṛtayaḥ prāk samāsāntebhya iti / ‘bahvalpārthāc chas’ ity ataḥ prabhṛti yāvan ‘madrāt parivāpaṇe’ ḍāc iti / thāl ‘viśvemāt thāl’ iti / ayaṃ vārttike nopasaṃgṛhītaḥ / yatnas tu kriyate / ya evaṃ ‘prakāravacane thāl’ (A 5.3.23) chandasi sa eva pratnādibhayḥ (A 5.3.111) svārthe bhavatīti / māntaḥ am ām / vārttike tu taddhitāḥ prakṛtā iti ābmir na paṭhitaḥ / kṛtvorthaḥ kṛtvasuc suc dhā / tasivatī / nānāñau tathā tenaikadik tasiś ca / vatiḥ ‘tena tulyaṃ kriyā ced vatiḥ’ (A 5.1.115) iti / gaṇe pāṭha etāvatām iti paṭhitam / 

[2.25] atha vā punar astv iti / arthāśraye parihṛtyetaretarāśrayaṃ saṃprati śābdāśraye pariharati / yo hy arthaḥ śāstrāt pratipadyate [sa laukikād api pratipadyate] / yadi avibhaktikānāṃ śāstrāt prayogo vyavatiṣṭhate na te 'bhijñavadaśāstrajñā avibhaktikān prayuñjīran / prayuñcate ca / tasmāt laukikīm eva pratipattim āśrityāvyayasaṃjñā pravartiṣyate / na śāstrapratipattim iti / saiva pratipattir idānīṃ vispaṣṭam ājñāyate / abhijñāś ca punar laukikā iti / anenārthāvadhāraṇāśrayaṃ samādhim āha / laukikā hy ekatvadvitvabahutvavato 'rthān niḥsaṃdigdhān avadhārayanti / pradarśanārtham idam upādīyate---anyena hi vasnenaikaṃ gām iti / na tv ayaṃ śabdapra[vṛ]ttāb upayujyate / mūḍho 'pi hi krayavikraye pravartamāna evam eva pravartate / tasmād arthāvadhāraṇapratipādanaparam etat / yathaiva cārthavyavahāre 'vadhāritā arthāḥ, evam eva śabdaprayoge 'pīti tadupādāneṣv artheṣv ekāntāvadhāritā arthā atyantam eva ekatvādiyogino 'rthān prati / kuta ete ? yathāprabhraṃśoccāraṇe 'pi vṛkṣādiṣv ekavacanabahuvacane na vyatikareṇoccārayanti / saṃkhyāśabdāṃś ca tatsamānādhikaraṇān prayuñjate / na ca nānādiśabdān prāyaḥ prayuñjate / idaṃ ca nākhyātam asyaikādiṣv artheṣu vacanānīti / tatrāvibhaktiḥ śabdo 'vyayam ity etāv atyukte yeṣv ekatvādayo na santi te nāmāvibhaktikāś ceti paṭhyate iti yuktam / 

[2.26] tac cāpy evam anumānagamyaṃ dṛśyatām iti / saṃpratyupapattim āha / upapattyāpy ayam artho jñāyate / yady api vibhāga upapadyeta prāyoktir eveyam iti grahītavyam / kiṃcid avyayaṃ vibhaktyarthapradhānam / avyayānāṃ hi dvividho dharma ākhyāyayate / tatrāpi tv idaṃ grahītavyam / kiṃcid avyayaṃ naiva vibhaktyarthapradhānaṃ nāpi kriyāpradhānam iti kasyacic ca dharmasya prāptir āc chādyate etena dharma ānāyyonutyaktavyo yas tatra vinyāsaḥ etad akathitenācaṣṭe (?) / 

[2.27] uccairnīcair iti vibhaktyarthapradhānam iti / saptamyarthapradhānaṃ dvayam ity eṣa nirṇayo bhāṣye / evaṃ hy uktaṃ---‘uccaiḥ kaṭānāṃ sraṣṭā’ (MBh 1.467.9) iti / adhikaraṇamātrāvyayam iti śaktiś ca vibhaktyarthaḥ / sa cāsattvaḥ / tatkṛto hy asti liṅgasaṃkhyāsaṃyogābhāvaḥ / 

[2.28] hiruk pṛthag iti kriyāpradhānam iti / yathaiva prādayaḥ kriyāgataṃ viśeṣam eva dyotayantaḥ kriyāpradhānāḥ, evaṃ hirugādayo 'pi kriyāpradhānā ity ucyante / yady evaṃ kriyāśabdam antareṇa prayogānupapattiḥ---pṛthag devadatta iti / na hi bhavati pradevadatta iti / sādhanasyākṣepāt prayogo bhaviṣyati, yathā niṣkauśāmbir iti / samāse yuktam iti ced asamāse 'pi dṛṣṭa ākṣepaḥ / ‘prādeśaṃ prādeśaṃ viparilikhati’ (MBH 1.346.24) iti kriyāntaraprayogaṃ cāntareṇa prayogo bhāṣye / prādeśenāsau vimāya likhatīti / yady evaṃ pṛthagbhūta iti sādhanaprayogo 'nupapannaḥ / naiṣa doṣaḥ / prapatitaparṇaḥ praparṇaḥ iti uathā prayujyate, evam ihāpi prayokṣyate / nimittāntaravidhānāc ca sarvadā kriyādharmamajahat pravartate hi nāstiliṅgasaṃkhyābhyāṃ yoga iti sūtraṃ pratyākhyātaṃ taddhitaścāsarvavibhaktir iti / 

[2.29] atra codyate / svarādiṣu katham ? yadi te sattvam arthaṃ bruvanti viruddham idam iha kiṃcid avyayam iti / liṅgasaṃkhyāyogaś ca sattvavacane na vyāvartata iti avibhaktir avyayam ity anupasaṃgrahaḥ svarādīnām / evaṃ pīlumūlīta iti yadi niyatam antapadārthaḥ sattvavacanatā / atha tṛtīyārthe 'rtho 'sattve tad vartate iti / 

[2.30] atra tu svarādīnāṃ cāditaḥ pṛthakpāṭho yaḥ samarthito bhāṣye sa nirūpyate / ihāpi pīlumūlataḥ iti anyapadārthopalaṣite tṛtīyārthe tasiḥ, pīlamūlī vibhaktyarthopalakṣite anyapadārthe ity evaṃ varṇayanti / yathā kartarīti codanā pradhāne guṇabhūte ca kartari pravartate pacati pācayatīti / 

[2.31] idaṃ cāpy adyatve 'tibahu kriyate / adyatva iti sūtrārambhaviśiṣṭo vartamānakāla āśrīyate / anārabhyamāṇe asmin yoge 'tibahu kriyate / kartarīti (?) vacanavidhāna ekagrahaṇa(?vacana)m ity etāvad ucyeta, dvayor dvivacanaṃ bahuṣu bahuvacanam ity ekabādhiṣyate / tenāsaṃkhyeṣu cāvasthāsyate ekasaṃkhyeṣu ceti / 

[2.32] nanu cāviśeṣeṇa prātipadikād utpannānāṃ niyamo 'yam / tatra pañcake prātipadikārthe vacananiyame arthasyāniyamo 'rthaniyame vacanasyāniyama iti kathaṃ bādhopapadyate ? atha trike prātipadikārtha ekavacanasya bāhye 'rthe 'nupādīyamāne kathaṃ tadarthābhidhāyitvam ? athānupātto 'py arthaḥ parigṛhyate dvayor api prāpnoti / tasmāt paribhāṣāpakṣam āśrity edam uktam---ekavacanam utsargaḥ kariṣyate /

[2.33] yatra dvivacanam iti brūyāt tatra dvayor ity etad upasthitaṃ draṣṭavyam iti / dvivacanasaṃjñino 'pi vidhāṇakāle dvayor ity asyopasthānād ity evaṃbhūtaṃ vidhānaṃ vṛttam---ṅyāpprātipadikādaubhyāmos dvayoḥ, evaṃ jasādayo bahuṣv iti / asyāṃ ca kalpanāyāṃ nāprāpta ekavacana idam ārabhyata iti bādhake kalpyete / tayoś ca viśeṣavidhānād ekasminn aprasaṅga iti / 

[2.34] na cāpy evaṃ vigrahaḥ kariṣyate / na sarvā asarvā iti / kiṃ ca bhoḥ, asmin vigrahe doṣopanyāsaḥ na sarvā vibhaktayo 'smād iti ? nety āha / kiṃ tarhi ? na sarvā vibhaktayaḥ utpattau nimittam asyeti / ucyate / asmin vigrahe kriyamāṇe sūtre yo doṣaḥ sa uktaḥ / idānīṃ vṛttikārāntara[mata]m upanyasyati yasmin pakṣe kriyāmāṇe sūtre tu na kaścit doṣo bhavati / sa tu kena darśanena ? asarvā vibhaktaya iti / etad utsṛjya evam āśrayati ayam asarvā vibhaktir asmād iti / 

[2.35] [atra] ucyate / avyayeṣu vibhaktyutpattau catvāraḥ prakārāḥ / aviśeṣeṇotpadyanta utpannānāṃ niyama ity etasmin darśane 'nāśritārthopanibandhanāḥ kevalaṃ saṃskārāyaivotpadyante / tatra yady api asarvā vibhaktir ity ayaṃ vigrahas tathāpi . . . kṣatiḥ, savrasyāvibhakter utpannatvāt / sarvasyāś cotpattau liṅgam---‘nāvyayībhāvād ato 'mtvapañcamyāḥ’ (A 2.4.83), ‘tṛtīyāsaptamyor bahulam’ (A 2.4.84) iti / ekārthāyāṃ pravṛttāv arthāt tathāvyayāt yathāvyayībhāvāt / 

[2.36] athāpy evaṃ kalpyate, kenacid eva vacanena saṃskartuṃ śakyate / kutaḥ svādīni tadāśritāni ca pravartiṣyante ? amavyatikrame ca prayojanaṃ nāsti / tasmāt prathamāyā eva kevalam ekavacanam iti / evam api tathāvigrahaḥ kriyate yathā sidhyati / 

[2.37] atha tu prātipadikagrahaṇasya prayojanam uktam / avyayebhyaḥ kathaṃ prathamā sapūrvāyāḥ prathamāyāvibhāṣety evam artham ity etasmin vigrahe sidhati na sarvā vibhaktayo 'smād iti / asmiṃs tu na sidhyati na sarvā vibhaktir asmād iti / sarvā hi vibhaktis tūtpannā vyavasīyate / 

[2.38] tatra yadanantaram upanyastaṃ sa pakṣa āśriyate / idaṃ cānantaram ekavacanam utsargataḥ, sarvāny ekavacanāni avyayebhyaḥ pravartanta iti etāsmin darśane / sarvaśabdaś ca prakārakārtsnye 'pi vartate / sarvāṇy annāni bhuktāni, sarvāṇi caraṇāni dṛṣṭānīti / ekadeśe 'pi pravartamāne suśabde vāmśabde vā utpannāḥ sarvā vibhaktaya iti vyapadeśaḥ syāt / tasmāt sākalyapratipattyarthaṃ vigraha āśrīyate asarvo vibhaktir iti /

[2.39] yac ca liṅga sarvavibhaktyutpattau tadavyayībhāvasyaiva bhedavattvam ity asya pratipādakaṃ ca / yathā samāsakāṇḍe ekavadbhāvakāṇḍe caikārthībhāvasya samāhāravyavasthāviṣayaṃ vacanam / ‘sapūrvāyā . . . vibhāṣā’ iti vyavasthitavibhāṣety evaṃ samarthayitavyam / kramavyatikrame 'pi śāstre dvitīyaiva pūrvā na prathameti prayoge 'py anukramabhāva iti /

[2.41] evaṃ gate kṛty api tulyam etat _ evam avasthite vigrahe na sarvā asarvety asarvavibhaktir ity etāvat tu paṭhitavyam / tataś ca kṛty api tulyam etat asarvavibhaktitvam iti / ‘kṛnmejantaḥ’ (A 1.1.39) ity etan na paṭhitavyam / māntasya kāryaṃ grahaṇaṃ na tatreti athaḥ / trayaḥ kṛdarthā iti ktvātosunkasunāṃ nirdeśaḥ / ete kṛdarthā grahaṇena pāṭhena na kāryāḥ / tato yogāḥ / yogāś ca kāryāḥ, ‘svarādinipātanam’ (A 1.1.37) ity evamādayaḥ / 

[2.42] athānye ca varṇayanti / tenānye ca na kartavyā iti / atra tv avyavasthāvācitvāt śībhāvo durlabhaḥ / iyāḍiyājīkārāś ceti evam eke varṇayanti / ‘trayaḥ kṛdarthāgrahaṇena yogāḥ’---kṛdartham agrahaṇaṃ yasmin hetau sa kṛdarthāgrahaṇaḥ / yasmin hetāv āśrīyamāṇe kṛdarthāgrahaṇam ārabhyate / kaś cāsau hetuḥ ? na sarvā vibhaktir iti vigrahaḥ / taddhita iti cāgrahaṇam / tatra yathaiva ca ‘kṛnmejantaḥ’ ity etan na paṭhyate evam asmād api ye trayo yogāḥ, te 'pi na kartavyāḥ ‘svarādinipātamavyam’ (A 1.1.37), ‘ktvātosunkasunaḥ’ (A 1.1.40), ‘avyayībhāvaś ca’ (A 1.1.41) iti / 

[2.43] idānīm evam avasthite saṃkhyāviśeṣaṃ ye cāśritāḥ, teṣāṃ prāpnoty avyayasaṃjñā asarvavibhaktitvāt eko dvau bahava iti / ata eṣāṃ vyāvṛttyarthaṃ kuṇināpi taddhitagrahaṇaṃ kartavyam / kriyamāṇe 'pi taddhitagrahaṇe ṇamulādīnām aprāptir iti ‘kṛnmejantaḥ’ ity etad vaktavyam, svarādayaś ca na kṛto na taddhitāḥ / idānīm āha---tasmāt svarādigrahaṇaṃ ca kāryam iti / evam api viśatyādiṣu doṣaḥ / viṃśatiśabda ekavacanāntas taddhitaś ca / apare varṇayanti / evam api varaṇādiṣu doṣo bhavati / varaṇaśabdo 'pi bahuvacanāntas taddhitaś ca / evam ubhayaśabdo 'pi kadācin na dvivacanaviṣaya ity avyayasaṃjñā prāpnoti / ato gaṇapāṭha eva jyāyānasyāpi vṛttikārasyety etad anena pratipādayati / kṛttaddhitānāṃ grahaṇam / gaṇapāṭha eva / kṛtaś ca taddhitāś ca gaṇa eva paṭhitavyā iti / tasmiṃś ca satīdaṃ sūcakam uktaṃ sūtrakāravṛttikārayor lakṣaṇabhāvena pravṛttam ity etad avasthitam / 

[3.1] pāṭhena ca samastam avyayasaṃjñam idaṃ paṭhantv ity uktam---pāṭheneyam avyayasaṃjñā kriyata iti / seha na prāpnoti paramoccaiḥ paramanīcair iti / saṃjñāvidhau pradeśe śrutiprakaraṇasāmarthyānām abhāvāt nāsti tadantasya prāptir iity aktaparimāṇānām eva saṃjñayā bhavitavyam iti tadantavidhinā bhaviṣyati / aupasaṃkhyānikena ca tadantavidhinā prayojanaṃ ca sarvanāmāvyayasaṃjñāyām iti / 

[3.2] ihāpi tarhi prāpnoti [atyuccaiḥ] atyuccaisau atyccaisa iti / katham atra samāso yāvatā uccaiḥśabdo 'dhikaraṇa[śakti]pradhānaḥ, na ca tasyātikramaṇena saṃbandhaḥ / yathā ‘tatra paśyeti’ na dṛśinādhikaraṇasya saṃbandhaḥ / kasya tarhi ? tatrasthasya / athaivaṃ parikalpyate uccādhikaraṇasahacaritatvāt uccairvyapadeśa iti / evam api avyayatvaṃ durlabham / arthāpetaṃ vidhānaṃ kalpyate vinā nānā iti yathā / evam api dvitīyasyā abhāvaḥ / idaṃ coktam---atyādayaḥ krāntādyarthe dvitīyayā saha samasyanta iti / tasmād evaṃ kalpyate ‘bhavati vai kasyacit arthāt prakaraṇād vāpekṣyaṃ nirjñātam’ / yathā ‘brāhmaṇasya śuklāḥ’ iti / na ca saunāgaṃ darśanam āśrīyate / kiṃ tarhi ? siddhaṃ tu ‘kvāṅsvatidurgativacanāt’, ‘prādayaḥ ktārthe’ iti / 

[3.3] upasarjanasya neti pratiṣedho bhaviṣyati / samudāyasyānupasarjanatvāt kathaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ syād iti / ucyate / upasarjanāvayavatvād upasarjanavyapadeśo bhaviṣyati / upasarjanāvayavasya na bhavaty avyayasaṃjñeti / sarvanāmasaṃjñāyāṃ prakṛtaḥ paṭhyate tatra pratiṣedhaḥ / tasmiṃś ca svaritaichnam āsaṅktavyam iti / sa vai tatra pratyākhyāyate ca / anuccāryamāṇe aśakyaḥ svaritāsaṅgo 'pekṣyo vā / 

[3.4] anvarthasaṃjñā yathā vijñāyeta / nanu cānvarthasaṃjñāyāṃ kalpyamānāyām arthe pāratantryam āpadyamānā svarūpādhiṣṭhānā na bhavatīti saṃjñātvaṃ durlabham / athārthanirapekṣā svarūpamātranibandhanā, arthopādānam alabhyam / ucyate / loke dviprakārā saṃjñā / sati ca nimitte nimittaprasaktā na tu nimittam abhidadhatī pravartate / yathā vāsydevasya śrīkṛṣṇa iti nāmadheyaṃ sati kṛṣṇaguṇe kṛtam, na tu kṛṣṇaśabdaḥ kṛṣṇaygaṃ pratyāyayan pravartate / kadācit gaurasyaiva kṛṣṇa iti saṃjñā kriyate / evam iyam avyayasaṃjñā nimittena anānāgamanena prayuktā anabhidadhatī anānāgamaṇaṃ pravṛtteti / asyānumānaṃ mahattvakaraṇaṃ yasyānānāgamanam asti tasyeyaṃ saṃjñeti / yasya tu nānāgamanam asti na tasya / atyuccaiḥśabdaś ca nānā gacchatīti anavyayatvam / 

[3.5] atha vā yathaiva prasāraṇasaṃjñāyāṃ vibhaktiviśeṣo liṅgam āvṛter evam ihāpi nibandhanavākyam etad upanyastam / etad upagrahaṃ tu viniyogavākyam āvṛtta, ity asya vā liṅgaṃ mahattvaṃ tenāvyayam ity āvarti śabdarūpam avasīyate / tantreṇa vaikakriyayā dvau śabdau uccāritāv ity asya vā mahattvaṃ liṅgam iti / 

[3.6] na vyeti ity avyayam iti / kva punar na vyeti ? śloke saptamyā nirdeśa iti ihāpi tathaiva saptamīnirdiṣṭam / strīpuṃnapuṃsakāni sattvaguṇāḥ ekatvadvitvabahutvāni / guṇānāṃ rūpādīnāṃ pravṛtter arthe ye sthityāvirbhāvatirobhāvās te luṅgatvena parigṛhyante / avigamanaṃ ca viśeṣābhidhānaśaktyabhāvāt sāmānyaśaktyabhidhānam evam yathā pañcakeṣv iti / caritārthatvād vā rakṛtyābhihitam ekatvam iti tadvacano na pravartate yathāgnicid iti / 

[3.7] atha vā, na sāmānyābhidhānaśaktir na viśeṣābhidhānaśaktir yatra tatreti / etam evārthaṃ ślokenācaṣṭe / sadṛśaṃ triṣu liṅgeṣu / sādṛśyaṃ hi sāmānyopādānāt atyantam abhāvāt bhedatyāgād vā rūpasādṛśyāt bhavati / sarvāsu ca vibhaktiṣu iti karmādīnāṃ grahaṇam / vacaneṣu ca sarveṣu iti saṃkhyāyā grahaṇam / sarveṣv eteṣu nāmabhāgeṣu yan na vyeti tadavyayaṃ pratipattavyam / svarādiṣu sattvavacaneṣu katham ? tatrāpi dravyavacanatvam / tat tu kiṃ liṅgam kiṃ saṃkhyam ity etan nāsti, sāmānyasaṃkhyāprāptam ucyate / abhedaikatvasaṃkhyāyāḥ saṃkhyāvānayam iti etāvanmātram / kim eko dvau bahava ity etan nāsti / 

kṛnmejantaḥ // 1.1.39 //

[1.1] ubhayanimittasaṃbhave anyataropādāne 'nyatarasyābhāve ubhayatra ca doṣadarśanaṃ vacanaṃ yatrāsti tatra praśnaḥ katham idaṃ vijñāyata iti /

[1.2] kṛdyo mānta ity ayaṃ pakṣa evam upapadyate / yadi tv eṣā saṃjñā teṣu saṃjñiv aprāptasaṃjñābhāveṣu pravartate tatas te saṃjñinaḥ pārāthyam aprāptā viśeṣaṇasaṃbandhārhā kṛnmej iti saṃbadhyante ity asya pakṣasya pravṛttiḥ / 

[1.3] atha tu prathaṃ saṃjñayā gṛhītāḥ kṛtaḥ, saṃjñāntarasya pratyāyanāya pravṛttās teṣāṃ tadantabhāve saṃjñābhāvaṃ pratipannānām apradhānānāṃ saṃjñādvāraviśeṣāṇāṃ [tadantavidhiḥ] kṛdantasya meco viśeṣaṇam ity ayaṃ pakṣaḥ [a]kṛdantaṃ yanmāntam iti / 

[1.4] na cātra tadantavidhir aśakyaḥ saṃjñāvidhay pratyayagrahaṇa iti / kutaḥ ? prayojanaṃ sarvanāmāvyayasaṃjñāyām ity ārambhāt / anvarthanirdeśād vā na vyetīty avyayam iti / na hi nānāgamanam agamanaṃ vā pratyayamātrasya vidyata ity asatyevāntagrahaṇa idaṃ dvaitaṃ dṛśyate / 

[1.5] ye tv anādṛtyaupasaṃkhyānikaṃ tadantavidhim anvarthasaṃjñāṃ cāntagrahaṇādasya pakṣasya saṃbhavaṃ kalpayanti ta evaṃ paryanuyoktavyāḥ---kiṃ prāptam ? tadantavidhisaṃyogaḥ, mejantaṃ kṛt tasya saṃjñā kalpyate, āhosvit meco 'tra pradhānaṃ tadviśeṣaṇaṃ kṛd iti / yadi prāptaḥ tadantasaṃyogo yadartham asya pakṣasyopādānaṃ tad eva na sidhyati kārayāṃcakāre iti / na hy atra kṛnmānta iti / 

[1.6] athaivaṃ kalpyate meco 'vyayasaṃjñā sa cet kṛd iti vacanāt kevalo makāro nāsti iti syāt māntasyāvyayatvam / ec tu śuddho 'sti ‘dṛśa’ iti / tadantasya na syāt ‘kartave’ iti / tasmād anarthakam antagrahaṇaṃ dṛśyate / 

[1.7] nyāse tu prayojanam antagrahaṇasyoktaṃ svabhāvaijantapratipattyartham / iha mā bhūt kumbhakārebhya iti / 

[1.8] atha vā, yatheṣṭaṃ viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyasaṃbandhāśrayeṇa nyāyaprāpta iha vacanena nirdiśyate kṛd atra pradhānaṃ meco viśeṣaṇam iti / 

[1.9] atha vā, idaṃ grahaṇakavākyam / etannibandhanaṃ tu yatprayogasaṃskārasamavāyi tad evaṃbhūta pratipattavyam ‘kṛdanto mejavyayam’ iti / 

[2.1] yadi vijñāyate kṛdyo mānta iti / kārayāṃcakāreti / na hy atra kṛnmānto lakāraḥ, iti atrāprāptir avyayasaṃjñāyāḥ / kṛdantaṃ yanmāntam ity āśrīyamāṇe bhavaty etat pratyayalakṣaṇena kṛdantaṃ, śrutyā ca māntam iti / evaṃ tu vijñāyamāne pratāmau pratāma ity atrāpi prāpnoti / etad api kvipā kṛdantaṃ māntaṃ ca śrutyeti ‘avyayād’ (A 2.4.82) iti luk / 

[2.2] nanu caikavacanasyāma uttarasya halṅyādilopenaiva nivṛttiḥ siddhā / kiṃ lukā prayojanam ? ayam abhiprāyaḥ / yathaivānuprayoge sādhanabhedāt dvivacanabahuvacane, evam āmprakṛtāv iti / liṭ dvayor bahuṣu ca kartṛṣu karmasu cotpadyate / tenāmantād api dvivacanabahuvacane pravartate kārayāṃcakratuḥ, kārayāṃcakrur iti / akac tu nodāhriyate, aparisamāptārthatvāt ajñātādibhir yogābhāva iti / 

[2.3] iṣyata eva pratyayamātrasya iti / etat pratipādayati---na hi taddhitaḥ kaścid asti ya āmo lupyate / katham ? yadi tāvad anabhivyaktā āmantena kutsāpraśaṃsāprakarṣā iti pratyayā vartante teṣāṃ lukā bhavitavyaṃ lukyanabhivyakter anavasthitatvāt / athānuprayogād utpannāḥ kutsādīn abhivyañjanti . . . āmantasya, evam api notpattavyamāmantād iti / 

[2.4] upapattyā pratipādyānupattiṃ śāstreṇāpi pratipādayati---ātaś ceṣyate / evaṃ hy āha---kṛñ cānuprayujyate liṭi iti / atra ām ity anuvartate, āmo 'nuprayujyate, āmo 'nantaram ity arthaḥ / yadi ca tarabādiḥ syāt, kṛñādir nānantaraṃ prayujyeta iti / evaṃ ca kṛtvā ‘vyavahitanivṛttyartham’ iti / kiṃ ca / svādayo yadi lupyeran, vacanam apy abhivyaktam iti tatra yathaiva kṛtsnapadāntarāt puruṣopagrahādir evam āmanteṣv api prayujyata iti anuprayogo yaḥ sarvārthābhivyaktaya āśritaḥ sa na syād iti / 

[2.5] yad ayaṃ praśānśabdaṃ [svarādiṣu] paṭhati / atra hi ‘mo no dhātoḥ’ (A 8.2.64) na tv asyāsiddhatvāt siddham avyayatvam / na ca ‘śāna tejane’ ity asyaitat rūpaṃ saṃbhavati / tasya nityaṃ sannantatvāt / yadi pratyayalakṣaṇam atra na bhavati kathaṃ kārayāṃcakāreti / tulyajātīyatvasya jñāpakaṃ dhātor makārāntasya na bhavatīty etad āśrīyate / sopasargatvaṃ tu nāśrīyate / iha mā bhūt pratāma iti / asmiṃs tu pakṣe lāvayater laur ity atra prāpnoti / bhavaty ayaṃ kṛdanta ejantaś ceti / ananyaprakṛtiparihāraḥ / atha vā, paribhāṣayā paryudāsa iti doṣa evāyaṃ laur iti / 

[2.6] anikārokāraprakṛtiparihāre ecaḥ saṃbhavatīkārokāraprakṛtitvaṃ na makāyasya / ādhaye / idam atra saṃpradhāryaṃ---guṇe kṛte kim avyayasaṃjñayā bhavitavyam ayādibhir iti / paratvād ayādayaḥ / antaraṅgatvād avyayasaṃjñā / kṛtāyām avyayasaṃjñāyāṃ lukā bhavitavyam / anityatvaṃ cobhayor api / luki sati ayādīnām abhāvaḥ / ayādiṣu satsu vihitanimittāvyayasaṃjṣeti luko 'bhāvaḥ / antaraṅgān api vidhīn bahiraṅgo luk bādhate iti luk syāt / tasmād vaktavyam etad atra / 

[2.7] kumbhakārebhya iti / śakyaṃ tatra pratiṣedho vidhātuṃ lakṣaṇapratipadoktayoḥ pratipadoktasyaiveti / yo hi nityaṃ tadrūpaḥ pratyakṣaḥ antaraṅgaś ca tasya grahaṇam uktam / ayaṃ tu anumātavyaḥ anityo bahiraṅgaś ceti / etat tu noktam, paribhāṣāntaraprayojanāni vicārayiṣyāmīti / 

[3.1] saṃnipātaḥ saṃśleṣaḥ samparkaḥ ānantaryam ity anarthāntaram / etallakṣaṇo yo vidhir iti tadarthaḥ / so 'nimittam / kasya ? tadvighātasya / taṃ vihantīti tadvighātaḥ, vighātakasya vidhyantarasyānimittam / vighātaṃ ca prayojaneṣv eva pradarśayiṣyāmi yasya yasya vighāto bhavati / 

[3.2] hrasvatvaṃ tugvidheḥ grāmaṇikulam / ‘iko hrasvo 'ṅyo gālavasya’ (A 6.3.61) iti pūrvapadottarapadāśrayaṃ hrasvatvaṃ saṃnipātavidhiḥ, sa tadvighātasyānimittam / kasya punar atra vighātaḥ ? īkāras tāvat saṃnipātī hrasvenaiva vihitaḥ / nātra tuk saṃnipātino vighātakaḥ / kiṃ tarhi ? saṃnipātasya / yat pūrvottarapadayor ānantaryaṃ tasya tuk vighātako vartate / ayaṃ ca tuk yadi avayavabhakto na cāvayavāvayavo 'vayavagrahaṇena gṛhyate, tata idaṃ prayojanam upapadyate / atha tu samudāyabhakto 'vayavāvayavo 'vayavo vā bhavati, naivānantaryaṃ vihanyata iti aprayojanam idaṃ paribhāṣāyāḥ / 

[3.3] bahiraṅgaṃ hrasvatvam antaraṅgas tuk / dvipadāśrayatvāt hrasvo bahiraṅgaḥ, ekapadāśrayatvāt antaraṅgas tug iti / atha grāmaṃ nayati kulaṃ grāmaṇi kulam iti katham, yatraittarapadikaṃ hrasvatvaṃ na bhavati ? atrāpi ‘hrasvo napuṃsake prātipadikasya’ (A 1.2.47) samudāyāśrayaṃ hrasvatvaṃ varṇāśrayas tuk ity upapadyate bahiraṅgatvam / saṃnipātaparibhāṣāyāṃ tu satyāṃ grāmaṇicchatram iti doṣaḥ / atra hi gramaṇyaḥ chatram iti hrasvatve kṛte kviblakṣaṇas tuk antaraṅgatvān nāsti, chalakṣaṇo 'pi saṃnipātalakṣaṇatvād ity atukkasya śravaṇaṃ prāpnoti grāmaṇichatram iti / bahiraṅgaparibhāṣāyāṃ tu satyāṃ chalakṣaṇaḥ samānāśrayatvāt pravartate / 

[3.4] nalopo vṛtrahabhiḥ / vibhaktiprātipadikāśrayo nalopaḥ saṃnipātalakṣaṇaḥ ity animittaṃ tuko bhavati / atha ‘ato lopaḥ’ kvipi kasmān na bhavati ? uktam etad ‘ārdhadhātukopadeśe yadakārāntam’ iti / asiddho nalopa iti / dvividham apy asiddhatvam atropapadyate pūrvatrāsiddhatvaṃ bahiraṅgāsiddhatvaṃ ca / vibhaktyāśrayatvān nalopo bahiraṅgo bhavati / 

[3.5] udupadhatvam akittvasya nikucite / nalopo yaḥ ‘aniditām . . . ’ (A 6.4.24) iti sa kitpratyayāśrayaḥ / tadantaraṃ cidupadhatvam upajāyamānaṃ saṃnipātinaḥ pratyayasya kittvavyapadeśaṃ nihantīty anayā paribhāṣayākittvavyapadeśasya nimittaṃ na bhavatītikalpyate / nātra saṃnipātino 'saty api kittve kaścid ānantaryasya vighātaḥ / 

[3.6] nābhāvo yañi dīrghatvasyāmunā / ne kartavye mubhāvaḥ siddhaḥ / ne tu yat prāpnoti tatpratyasiddhatvam ity arthaṃ parikalpya prayojanopanyāsaḥ / sūtrīyam ukāramātram āśritya nābhāvaḥ pravartamānaḥ saṃnipātalakṣaṇo vighātasyānimittam / kasyātra vighātaḥ yāvatā paraḥ saṃnipātī nābhāvenaiva vihataḥ ? ānantaryam asty eva / tatredaṃ kalpyate / sa ca saṃnipātī dīrghatve kriyamāṇe ākāreṇa vihanyate / tasmāt pūrvasya saṃnipātino rūpavighātasyānimittaṃ bhavati / 

[3.7] na mu ṭādeśa iti / sarvavibhaktyantaḥ samāsa ity evaṃ vakṣyati / sarvavibhaktyā ca saṃbandhasāmānyāśrayād eva sthāsyate / 

[3.8] ātvaṃ kittvasyopādāsta / luṅi sici pare bhūte guṇe kṛte uttarakālam ātma itīmāṃ prakriyām āśritya saṃnipātalakṣaṇatvam ātvasya / tadātvaṃ ghusaṃjñāpratibaddhasya kittvasyānimittam / atrāpi vyapadeśanumittaṃ kittvam iti / evaṃ ca kṛte hrsvād aṅgād iti parasya saṃnipātino rūpam apy atra vihanyate / 

[3.9] tisṛcatasṛtvaṃ ṅībvidheḥ / tricaturau ca vibhaktiṃ cāśrityādeśaḥ saṃnipātalakṣaṇaḥ / sa īkārasyānimittam / īkāro hi pravartamānaḥ saṃnipātinor ānantaryaṃ vihanti pūrvasya ca yaṇādeśena rūpam iti /

[3.10] yad ayaṃ ‘na tisṛcatasṛ’ iti / idam aliṅgam / kutaḥ / upasamastārtham etat syāt priyatisṛṣṇāṃ brāhmaṇānām iti / atha tu chandasy uktaṃ, tatropanyastam / etau hi tisṛcatasṛśabdau svasrādiṣu paṭhyete iti / 

[3.11] śatāni sahasrāṇi / aṅgaṃ sarvanāmasthānaṃ cāśritya num saṃnipātalakṣaṇaḥ / parasya saṃnipātino lakṣaṇasya luki kṛte rūpavighātaḥ / tataś cānantaryavighāto 'pi / atrāpy upadeśavacanam astīti, etad apy aprayojanaṃ dṛśyate ṣṇāntety antavacanāc ca / 

[3.12] śakaṭau paddhatau / acca gheḥ (A 7.3.111) / audārabhyate / tenaikāreṇa parasya vikṛtaṃ rūpamatvena pūrvasya, tannimittas tu ṭāp pravartamānaḥ aṅgapratyayayyor ānantaryaṃ vihanyāt / taccāśakyaṃ vihantum / ekādeśasyāntavadbhāvāt / atha tv evaṃ vihanyetakārye hy antavadbhāvaḥ, na cānantaryaṃ nāma śāstrakāryam / laukikam etad iti syād vyavadhānam / ṭāpi sati yāḍāmbhāvayoḥ kṛtayoḥ śakaṭāyām ity aniṣṭaṃ rūpaṃ syāt / athāpi audac ca gher iti taparatvasya idam eva prayojanaṃ varṇayanti tan mā bhūd iti /

[3.13] iyeṣa uvoṣa / guṇe kṛte . . . ām prāpnoti / atra iyaṅi vihite prāglādeśāt dhātvadhikāra ity antaraṅgatvāt lādeśa kṛte dvirvacanāt paratvāt ‘puganta . . .’ (A 7.3.86) iti ca guṇe kṛta ekāraḥ saṃnipātalakṣaṇaḥ sa nimittamāmo na bhavati / āmi sati saṃnipātinaḥ pratyayasya rūpavirodho vighāta iti / uvoṣeti katham upanyastaṃ yāvatā ‘uñavidajāgṛbhya . . . ’ (A 3.1.38) iti vibhāṣita evāmpratyayaḥ / evaṃ manyate vibhāṣā kiti kṛtārthā ūṣāṃcakratuḥ ūṣāṃcakrur iti / iha tu piti guṇe kṛte lakṣaṇāntareṇām pratyayaḥ pravarteteti / atrāpi matupo 'yam artho varṇyate nityayoge matub iti / tena nityaṃ yo gurumān tasyāyamām / ayaṃ hy anitya iti idam apy aprayojanam / 

[3.14] tasya doṣo varṇāśrayaḥ . . . / katham a[tra] vyavasthitaṃ saṃnipātalakṣaṇatvaṃ yāvatā prakṛtirevāvasthitasya pratyayasya nimittam / evaṃ manyate---svabhāvato 'vasthitasya ‘at iñ’ (A 4.1.95) ity anena pratyayatvasaṃjñā upādīyate / samudāyasya vā sādhutvaṃ tasyaiva vā parasya sataḥ sādhutvam iti / saṃjñāyā vā saṃnipātalakṣaṇāyāḥ sādhutvam /

[3.15] na pratyayaḥ saṃnipātalakṣaṇaḥ / asyābhiprāyaḥ / bhavati vai kiṃcid ācāryāṇāṃ kāryavat / ‘buddhau kṛtve’ti prakṛtito 'jāta eva pratyaya upalabhyate / ayaṃ ca pakṣaḥ ‘paraś ca’ (A 3.1.2) iti sūtre varṇitaḥ / ‘etad eva tat parasya kāryaṃ yad asau paraḥ syād’ iti / kiṃ ca na sādhutvaṃ saṃjñā vā varṇavicālasya nimittaṃ syāt / saṃjñā pāraṃparyeṇaiva nimittam / na caivaṃ bhūtaṃ nimittaṃ bhavatīty etad ācaṣṭe na pratyayaḥ saṃnipātalakṣaṇa iti / 

[3.16] aṅgasaṃjñā tarhi [a]nimittaṃ syāt / aṅgasaṃjñā hi prakṛtipratyayasaṃnipātalakṣaṇā / sā varṇavicālasyānimittaṃ syāt / sāṃnipātī hi rūpeṇa kriyate iti / etasmin idam asamarthitam avarṇāśrayaḥ pratyayo 'varṇavicālasyeti / aviśeṣeṇāṅgasaṃjñāyāḥ saṃnipātalakṣaṇatvāt sarvāṇy evāṅgāni kāryāṇi paryavasannāni bhavanti / 

[3.17] kecid idam udāharaṇam upanyasyanti / adhukṣat, alikṣat iti / ayaṃ hi ksaḥ śalantāṃ prakṛtim aniṭaṃ ccliṃ cāśritya pravartamānaḥ saṃnipātalakṣaṇo bhavati / ayam animittaṃ ghatvaḍhatvakutvānām / na cātrāsiddhatvam asti / adeśatvād asyāḥ paribhāṣāyāḥ / vidhideśā ceyam iti pūrveti aśakyā vyapadeṣṭum / bhāṣyakāreṇa tv avarṇāśrayaḥ pratyaya itīmam eva vākyapāṭham āśritya dākṣir ity upanyastam / 

[3.18] āttvaṃ pugvidheḥ / krāpayati / ṇau parabhūte vṛddhau kṛtāyām āttvam ity asyāṃ prakriyāyām āttvaṃ saṃnipātalakṣaṇaṃ, tat puko nimittam / ānantaryaṃ hi puk saṃnipātinor nihanti / aṅgasya ya ākāras tasya pug iti avayavabhaktaṃ pukaṃ matvā doṣa uktaḥ / 

[3.19] adīdapad iti / pukā cānantaryaṃ vihatam / pūrvasya tu saṃnipātino vighāta iti animittaṃ syāt /

[3.20] tyadādyakāraṣṭābvidheḥ / atvam aṅgavibhaktyoḥ saṃnipātakṛtaṃ ṭāpo 'nittaṃ syāt / ṭāpi hi sati parasya saṃnipātino 'bhāva iti /

[3.21] papivān ity atra pūrvasya saṃnipātino nivṛtter animittam iṭ syāt /

[3.22] agnimān ity atra udāttapūrvaṃ matupaṃ cāśritya matupa udāttatvaṃ saṃnipātalakṣaṇam / tat pūrvasya ‘anudāttaṃ padam eka[varjam]’ (A 6.1.158) iti nighāte kartavye na nimittaṃ syāt / anudāttatve sati pūrvasya saṃnipātinaḥ śrutir nihanyata iti / 

[3.23] brāhmaṇi brahmabandhu / atra hi hrasvatvam ‘ambārthanadyor hrasvaḥ’ (A 7.3.107) iti saṃbuddhau vidhīyamānaṃ saṃnipātalakṣaṇaṃ parasya saṃnipātino vināśe na nimittaṃ syād iti / mā bhūd evaṃ ṅyantād ity evaṃ bhaviṣyati---‘halṅyāp’ (A 6.1.68) iti dīrghagrahaṇe asati sthānivadbhāvo nāsti pratiṣedhāt / evaṃ hy uktaṃ ‘ṅyābgrahaṇe dirgha’ iti / iyaṃ ca vicāraṇā brāhmaṇiśabda upapadyate, na brahmabandhuśabde, aṅyantatvād iti / nityaḥ saṃbuddhilopa iti / na saṃbuddhisaṃśabdanena yo lopa ity evaṃ, kiṃ tarhi ? yena kena lakṣaṇena saṃbuddher yo lopa iti ubhayiś cānityayoḥ paratvāt hrasvatve kṛte lopo na prāpnoti / ko 'vakāśa iti cet, devadatta yajñādatteti / 

[3.24] ete doṣāḥ samā bhūyāṃso vā / laukikīyaṃ vācoyuktiḥ / loke bahuṣu copakāreṣu kṛteṣu kasmiṃś cid eva pratibadhyamāno bravīti---bahutato 'narthaḥ kṛta iti / evam ihāpi sadoṣatvāt paribhāṣāyā evam ucyate //

[3.25] nanu ca sarvādiṣu samaśabdasya paṭhitatvāt sama iti bhavitavyam / nipātanāt siddham iti cet ‘yathāsaṃkhyam anudeśaḥ’ samānām’ (A 1.3.10) ityādiṣu pāṭhānarthakyam / tatraivaṃ vyācakṣate / sarvaśabdaparyāyavācī yaḥ samaśabdaḥ sa sarvādiṣu paṭhito na tulyārtha iti / asti punaḥ samaśabdaḥ sarvādiṣu paṭhitaḥ sarvaśabdasyārthe prayuktaḥ ? astīty āha / kva ? loke vede ca / loke tāvat rājanyavidyamāne prayoktāro bhavanti ‘samarājyam idam idānīm’ / sarveṣāṃ rājyam ity arthaḥ / vede---‘mā naḥ samasya dūḍhyaḥ (ṚV 8.75.9)’ iti prayogaḥ / etasya niruktakāro (5.23) vyākhyānaṃ karoti---‘mā naḥ sarvasya durdhiyaḥ pāpadhiyaḥ’ iti / anyeṣāṃ punarlakṣaṇam---‘samo yukte’ / samaśabdo yukte 'rthe nyāyye 'rthe vartate, sa sarvanāmaśāṃjño bhavati / iha tu na samaśabdo yuktārthe prayukta iti doṣābhāvaḥ / 

[3.26] tasmān nārtho 'nayā paribhāṣayā / ko 'bhiprāyaḥ ? yadi doṣāḥ bhūyāṃsaḥ alpāni prayojanāni / tāny evopasaṃkhyātavyāni / na kartavyā paribhāṣā yasya doṣā anujñāyante iti / na hi doṣāḥ santīti paribhāṣā na kartavyā / etat pratipādayati---sarvā vāk sāparādhā doṣānuṣaṅgiṇī yathā sthānivadādeśa ity asminn alvidhir api prasakta iti kiṃ na kartavyā ? doṣeṣu tu pratividadhāti / yac ca manyase doṣā bhūyāṃsa iti tac ca na / kutaḥ ? yasmāt doṣāḥ khalv api sākalyena parigaṇitāḥ / prayojanānām udāharaṇamātram / katham ? iha sārvadhātukārthdhadhātukayor guṇe bhavatīti, akṅitīti viśeṣe 'syābhāvāt etad varjam anyasmin viśeṣe nāsau pravartata ity adhyavasīyate / evam iha ye doṣā upāttās tān varjayitvāny atredaṃ pravṛttaṃ prayojanopasaṃgrāhi sāmānyaviṣayaṃ śāstram itīdam ucyate---tasmād yāny etasyā iti / 

[3.27] sarvāṇy eva tu bhāṣye uktāni prayojanāni / na doṣaḥ sākalyena parigaṇitāḥ / doṣāntarasya bhāvāt / iha ‘ṅer yaḥ’ iti yatvaṃ saṃnipātalakṣaṇaṃ tat dīrghasyānimittaṃ prāpnoti / saṃnipātino rūpāntarapratipatteḥ / yathā amunā iti / 

[3.28] pratividheyaṃ doṣeṣv iti / na ca pratividhānam uktam / tatraivaṃ pratividhīyate / aṅgasaṃjñā ced animittaṃ syāt, anavakāśā eva aṅgavidhayaḥ saṃpadyante / tataś cāṅgasaṃjñāyā apyānarthakyam / krāpayatīty ākārāntasya vidhānāt arthavato hy āgamas tadguṇībhūto gṛhyata iti śāstre yad ānantaryaṃ na hi tad vihanyate / adīdapad iti liṅgāt hrasvatvaṃ yad ayaṃ ‘māraṇatoṣaṇaniśāmaneṣu jñā’ jānāter mitvaṃ śāsti / atra upadhādhikārāt nākṛte puki hrasvatvam astīti nirdeśād avasīyate ‘āpjñapyṛdhāmīt’ (A 7.4.55) iti / yā sā ‘na yāsayoḥ’ (A 7.3.45) iti nirdeśāt / anyeṣu tv ākāralopādiṣu cintyaṃ pratividhānam / 

avyayībhāvaś ca // 1.1.41 //

[1.1] yadi sūtram atha vā pāṭhaḥ ‘kṛnmakārasaṃdhyakṣarānto 'vyayībhāvaś ca’ iti athāpy anavyayam avyayaṃ bhavatīti avyayībhāva iti tadvat atideśo 'thāsarvavibhaktir athāliṅgasaṃkhya iti vā / sarvathaiva tu lugmukhasvaropacārāḥ prayojanam (vā. 1) iti / 

[1.2] upāgni pratyagni iti kriyāviśeṣane vibhaktyarthe 'sattvavacanatve ca sāmānyasyopādānāt aliṅgasaṃkhyatvam asty eveti / ‘pañcan’śabdasya sattvavacanatve 'pi liṅgaviśeṣopādānasāmarthyaṃ nāsti / dāsyāḥ putrādiṣu tu naiva saṃkhyārthaṃ vibhakter upādānam, kiṃ tūpādhir ākrośasya / varṣāsujaḥ foṣucara iti saṃjñāyāḥ abhivyaktyartham / iha tu sulug iti saṃskāramātraṃ vibhaktyā kriyate, na aṃkhyāviśeṣagatir iti / 

[1.3] upāgnimukha iti mukhasvaraḥ syāt / upapayaskāra iti ca satvaṃ syāt / evaṃ vaktavyam---svaropacāranivṛttiḥ prayojanam iti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / yathā mukhasvara upacāraś ca / yathotsargaḥ sāpavādo vyavasthito bhavati tathaiva tatprayojanam ity etad ācaṣṭe / 

[1.4] kiṃ punar idaṃ parigaṇanam āhosvid udāharaṇamātram / nanu codāharaṇamātre vākyopanyāsa eva na prayujyate, sarvaprayojanānām iṣṭatvāt / eṣo 'bhiprāyaḥ praśne / yady udāharaṇamātram, vākyam anarthakam eva / pariṅaṇane sūtrānarthakyam / śakyante hi liṅgād apy etāni pratipattam iti / 

[1.5] parigaṇanam ity āha / apy evācāryaṃ nirdiśann itiśabdo lolaṃ vā / api khalv apy āhur iti / evaṃ khalv apy āhur ity arthaḥ / parigaṇanam evānena samarthayante / yasmād anyo 'pi pratiṣidhyate, tasmād etāny eva prayojanāni / atha vā svarūpeṇa kenacid eva prayojanānyanvākhyātāni / anye punar aniṣṭāni vyāvartayanti / teṣāṃ yāny avyāvartitāni prayojanāni ity etat bhāṣyakāro vyācaṣṭe / 

[1.6] uccair adhīyāneti / āmantritakārakatvāt parāṅgavadbhāvaḥ prasaktaḥ / so 'vyayānāṃ neti pratiṣidhyate / tena nānāpadasvara eva bhavati / sa ihāpi syāt---upāgnyadhīyāna iti / agneḥ samīpe 'dhyayanādhikaraṇe upāgniśabdo vartate ity āmantritakārakatvād avyayapratiṣedho neṣyate / parāṅgavadbhāvād aikasvaryam eva bhavati / akacyavyayagrahaṇāt / yathaivāvyayasya prakarṣāḍayaḥ kriyādvārakāḥ, evam ihāpīty asty akacā saṃbandha ity upāgnakīty aniṣṭaṃ rūpaṃ prasajyeta / upakumbhībhūtaṃ kumbhasamīpasthībhūtam ity arthaḥ / 

[1.7] ācāryapravṛttir jñāpayatīti / atra codyate---yady asmāl liṅgāt nivṛttir yogasya ca kriyate, tarhi idam api nārabdhavyaṃ ‘sārvadhātukārthadhātukayoḥ’ (A 7.3.84) iti / kṅiti pratiṣedhād asti vidhir iti śakyaṃ pratipattum / tatraiṣo 'bhiprāyaḥ / kriyamāṇe 'py asmin yoge viśiṣṭāny eva prayojanāni smartavyāni lugādīni, na tu parāṅgavadbhāvapratiṣedhādīti / evaṃ cet liṅgād eva tāni sādhayituṃ śakyāni / kiṃ sūtreṇeti / 

[1.8] anuttarapadasthasyeti vartate / avaśyaṃ cātrānuvṛttir abhyupagantavyā / paramapayaḥkumbho gopayaḥkumbha ity evam artham / tasmād uttarapadasthatvād iha na bhaviṣyati upapayaḥkāra iti / 

[1.9] naikaṃ prayojanaṃ yogārambhaṃ prayojayati---saṃhñāyogaṃ paribhāṣāyogaṃ vā / anyat tu prayojayati ‘jāgurūkaḥ’ (A 3.2.165), ‘aṇ kuṭilikāyāḥ’ (A 4.4.18) iti / 

[1.10] yady etāvat prayojanaṃ syāt / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / sūtre sāmānyenoccāryamāṇe viśeṣakāryasyānumānāt pratipattiḥ / kārye evaṃ tūccāryamāṇe sākṣāt pratipattiḥ / tatra sākṣāt pratipatter anumānapratipattir garīyasīti svaraprakaraṇa eva paṭhitavyaṃ ‘nāvyayībhāvāvyayadikśabdagomahat’ iti / na caivaṃ paṭhitam / tasmād idam api na prayojanam iti sarvavyākhyānakārair idam avasitaṃ mukhasvareṇaiva bhavitavyam upāgnimukha iti / 

[1.11] anye varṇayanti / idam ekaṃ prayojanaṃ mukhasvarapratiṣedho, na cāsya grahaṇaṃ tatrāsti / ‘naikaṃ prayojanaṃ yogārambhaṃ prayojayati’ iti yad uktam anavyayam avyayam adhyavasitaṃ saṃbhavatīti tenedaṃ sādhyate / tasmin pratiṣedhe tenāvyayadikśabdenāvyayībhāvasyāvyayakāryātideśa iti / 

[1.12] atha varṇyate bhāṣye---tatraivāyaṃ brūyāt / na coktam / idaṃ ca sūtram asti / tasmād anyad api prayojanam ihāgamayitavyam / kiṃ punas tat ? ṣaṣṭhīsamāsasya pratiṣedho devadattasyopakumbham iti ‘. . . suhitārthasadavyayam . . . ’ (A 2.2.11) iti / 

śi sarvanāmasthānam // 1.1.42 //

[1.1] śi sarvanāmasthānaṃ suḍanapuṃsakasyeti cet jasi śipratiṣedhaḥ / yady evaṃ paṭhyeta / atha tv evaṃ paṭhyate---śtrīpuṃsayoḥ suḍ iti / naiṣa doṣaḥ syāt / asmin hi nirdeśe 'napuṃsakasyeti śakyam arthaḥ kalpayituṃ napuṃsakasyeti na bhavatīti / 

[1.2] atha ‘śi sarvanāmasthānam’ iti kimartham asyopādānam ? ucyate / viṣaya pradarśanārtham / evaṃ hi vijñāyamāne śisarvanāmasthānam iti yā prāptiḥ sā na prāpnoti / apara evaṃ varṇayanti / yadi yathā ‘śisarvanāmasthānam’ iti svatantraṃ lakṣaṇaṃ saṃjñāyām evaṃ vākyabhedena suṭ sarvanāmasthānaṃ tato 'napuṃsakasyeti pratiṣedhavākye ayaṃ doṣaḥ /

[1.3] jasi śipratiṣedha ity adhikaraṇe viṣayabhāvenopātte jasviṣayo yaḥ śiḥ, atha vā jaseḥ śiḥ jasiśir iti / śilakṣaṇasyāvakāśaḥ śasviṣayaḥ śiḥ kuṇḍāni paśyeti / 

[1.4] asamarthasamāsaś cāyaṃ draṣṭavyaḥ / yatra vartipadasyā kriyābhidhīyamānā nityatvāc cāvagatārthāśrūyamāṇānumīyamānā svabhāvanivṛttā nañā dyotyate, tatra sākṣāt saṃbandhāt samarthasamāsaḥ / iha tu napuṃsakaśabdasya naño 'saṃbandhāt sarvanāmasthānasaṃjñākartṛkena bhavati nāsaṃbandhād idam ucyate ‘asamarthasamāsaś cāyaṃ draṣṭavyaḥ’ iti / 

[1.5] asamarthasamāse ca saty ayaṃ doṣaḥ / kutaḥ ? pratiṣedhāt / yady api prasajyapratiṣedhe paryudāse cobhayatrāprāpty anumānam, ayaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ---prasajyapratiṣedhe vicchedoṣakramā pravṛttiḥ, paryudāse tu viśeṣṭam eva pratipadyamāno vicchedopakramaṃ pratipadyate / 

[1.6] asmiṃś ca nirdeśe śakyam upakalpayitum / tatreṣṭārthatvād evaṃ kalpayiṣyāmaḥ---paryudāso 'yam iti / sati paryudāse 'napuṃsakam anyopalakṣaṇārtham upādīyate / dviprakārā hi pravṛttiḥ svalakṣaṇena paralakṣaṇābhāvena [ca] / yathā kṣatriyaviṭsūdrān bhojyeti vyapadeśaḥ, atha vābrāhmāṇān bhojayeti / yadi cādyam eva vidhānam idaṃ nānyatpratipādakaṃ kiṃcid asti brāhmaṇasyaikānteneti, na brāhmaṇo bhojyate / yatra ca niyamas tatrāpy apravṛttiḥ / iha ca napuṃsakābhāvena strīpuṃsāv upalakṣyamāṇāv iti upalakṣaṇasya prādhānyāt pratiṣedho vidhir vā tatsamavāyīti napuṃsakaṃ na spṛśati, kiṃ tu tad upalakṣitau strīpuṃsau ity etad ācaṣṭe napuṃsake na vyāpāra iti /

[1.7] aprāpter vā / saty asamarthasamāsadarśane pratiṣedhasyāprāptiḥ / kutaḥ ? anantarasya vidhir vā . . . pratiṣedho veti / ānantaryaṃ cehārthakṛta śrutikṛtaṃ ca / katham ? napuṃsakasya neti pṛthakpratiṣedhe āśrīyamāṇe ‘śi sarvanāmasthānam’ aśakyam idaṃ pratiṣeddhum / kutaḥ ? ārambhasāmarthyāt / napuṃsakaviṣaya eva hi śiḥ / ato 'rthādiyamanantarā prāptiḥ suḍ iti iyaṃ pravṛttirūpeṇāśritā / aśakyāsty eva pratibaddhum, suṭo yā kācit prāptiḥ suḍ iti vā śir iti veti / kiṃ tarhi ? suḍ ity evaṃ prāptiḥ sā napuṃsakasya neti idam ucyate pūrvā prāptir apratiṣiddhā tayā bhaviṣyati / 

[1.8] nanu ceyaṃ prāptiḥ pūrvāṃ prāptiṃ bādhata iti / vipratiṣedhenety abhiprāyaḥ / kayoḥ punar atra vipratiṣedhaḥ ? samāveśaḥ saṃjñānām iṣyate 'taḥ sa tayor nāsti vipratiṣedhaḥ / lakṣaṇayor api na saṃbhavaty ekakāryatvāt / ekaṃ kāryaṃ saṃjñā / ucyate / pratiṣedhāviśiṣṭatadviśiṣṭayor eva vipratiṣedho lakṣaṇayor vā / pratiṣedhāc cāsti virodhaḥ / yāś ca svabhinnaphalā viruddhas tāsāṃ samāveśa iti / tatra śir ity asyāvakāśaḥ kuṇḍāni paśyeti / suḍ ity asyāvakāśaḥ rājānau rājāna iti / ihobhayaṃ prāpnoti kuṇḍāni tiṣṭhanti iti / paratvāt suḍ ity anayā bhavitavyam / paratvaṃ pūrvasyā anuvṛtter anumānam / parasyā api pratiṣedho 'numānam aprāpteḥ / na ca devadattasya hantari hate devadattasyāsti prādurbhāvaḥ / notsahate pratiṣiddhā satī bādhitum / 

na veti vibhāṣā // 1.1.44 //

[1] navetibibhāṣāyām arthasaṃjñākaraṇam / ubhayathāpy anupapannam etat / arthābhāvād asattvabhūtatvāc cārthasya saṃjñāsaṃbandhānupapattiḥ / naśabdavāśabdau bhinnau upādīyete, athāpy ekau navāśabdau yathā nakir mākir iti, sarvathā padāntaropādānasya pratiṣedhyasya vikalpyasya vā nivṛttiḥ vikalpo vāvadyotyate / iha ca ‘na veti vibhāṣe’ti pratiṣedhyavikalpyavācinoḥ śabdayor aprayogād artha eva nāsti / kasya saṃjñā kriyatām ? athāpy asty arthaḥ saṃnihitaḥ syāt, evam api na bhavati, vā bhavati, na vā bhavaty evaṃbhūtaḥ syāt / evaṃbhūtasya cārthasyāsattvabhāvāt saṃjñāyā anabhisaṃbandhaḥ / saṃjñāṃ prati hi saṃjñini ṣaṣṭhī prathamā vā vyāpriyate / asattvabhūtasya ca na śeṣabhāvāpattiḥ / nāpi idaṃ tad iti vyapadiśyata iti / 

[2] yat tāvad ucyate arthābhāvād ity etad ayuktam / kutaḥ ? upalakṣaṇabhāvena sūtre pravartamāno navāśabdo dravyaśabdasāṃnidhye pravartate kriyāśabdasāṃnidhye vā / kriyāśabdaś ca guṇībhūtakriyaḥ pradhānībhūtakriyaś ca / tatra dravyaśabdayoge na staḥ pratiṣedhavikalpau / kutaḥ ? dravyaśabdāḥ pravartamānā dravyeṣv avasthitākārām eva buddhiṃ pravartayanti, yady asti yadi nāsti so 'rthaḥ / tasmād evaṃbhūto yo 'rthaḥ tasya rūpam evāśakyaṃ pratiṣeddhuṃ vikalpayituṃ veti / 

[3] yo 'rtho vidheyarūpaḥ sa eva nivāryate vikalpyate vā / kaḥ punar asau ? kriyā, sattā, prātipadikaṃ vā ālambhanaṃ kriyāśabdaprayoge /

[4] katham apācaka iti ? atra kecit pacāv eva nañaḥ saṃnipāta iti pratipannāḥ / kecit punar evaṃ manyante---oacikriyākartur yaḥ sattāviśeṣas taṃ svabhāvanivṛṭtam avadyotayatīti / 

[5] iha kathaṃ ‘na pacatī’ti ? atrāpi kecit pacim eva sattāviśeṣe pratipannāḥ / kecit punar bhavati pacatīti sādhyasādhanabhāvasyādṛṣṭatvāt paceḥ sattāsaṃbandham eva nivartayati nañ iti / 

[6] yathā mātuḥ smaratīty atra bhinne darśane / kecin manyante---yo 'yaṃ śeṣabhāvo mātuḥ smaraṇe, ayaṃ yathā rājñaḥ puruṣo, vṛkṣasya śākheti kriyāvyaveto dadātināvasthānena ca, iyaṃ smaraṇakriyā api / smaraṇaṃ mātṛviṣayatvāt tatra pravartate tatrāvatiṣṭhata iti pravṛttir avasthānaṃ vā śeṣasya nimittam iti / tatra sādhyāpi smṛtiḥ sādhyarūpeṇāvivakṣitā saṃbandhitvenopakrāntā ṣaṣṭhyā nimittaṃ bhavati / yadā sādhyarūpā vivakṣyate, tadā dvitīyaiva bhavati mātaraṃ smaratīti / yathā vṛkṣasya phalam iti, vṛkṣāt phalaṃ patatīit / 

[7] keṣāṃcid darśanam---asaty eva kriyāntare smaraṇam eva kriyā śeṣasya nimittaṃ bhavati / kriyā hi sādhyatvena saṃbandhitvena vā śakyā dravyeṇāpekṣitum / 

[8] dravyāṇāṃ parasparam upakāraābhāvaḥ / yuktaḥ kriyāvyayetaḥ saṃbandha iti / ato 'rthavān navāśabdaḥ / 

[9] yad apy ucyate asattvabhāvād asaṃbandhaḥ iti / atra brūmaḥ / saṃjñākaraṇakāle sattvabhūtasya pratyavamarśāt yuktaḥ saṃjñāyāḥ kriyāsaṃbandhaḥ / iha yodopakāritvenāśrito 'rthātmā vasturūpatayā upasaṃhriyate, tadā pratyavamṛṣṭaḥ sarvanāmnā kāryayogī saṃpadyate / yathā pacati yas tasyāpatyam iti / tathā sattvabhūto 'yam artha iti / evam iha saṃjñāsaṃbandhakāle sarvanāmnā pratyavamṛṣṭaḥ naño 'rtho vārthaś ca vastubhūtaḥ / āśritaśabdavyāpāre tu pradeśeṣv asattvabhūta eveti yuktaḥ saṃbandhaḥ, na bhavatīti yo 'rtho vā bhavatīti yo 'rthas tasyeyaṃ vibhāṣeti saṃjñā pratipattavyeti /

[10] kathaṃ punar ekam eva vastu sattvabhūtam asattvabhūtaṃ syāt ity atra kaiścit vāstavaṃ sarvaśakti iti prapañcitam / tasya tu viśiṣṭārthabhāgopanipātinaḥ śabdās tāṃ tāṃ śaktim avacchindantīti pratipannāḥ / apare punaḥ---naiva vastu kiṃcid asti, śabdā eva tu pravartamānās taṃ tam arthaṃ prakalpayanti / sā ca prakalpanaśaktiḥ niyatā ‘gaccha hanyate kaṃsaḥ’ iti / ekasminn eva vastuni kaścit pacatīti prayuṅkte / kaścit śeṣabhāvam āpādya prayuṅkte pacateḥ karteti / kaścit pācakaḥ / na tu karaṇādirūpaṃ, dātreṇa lunāti, grāmād āgacchati, brāhmaṇāya dadāti iti / 

[11] atha ko 'yaṃ nirdeśaḥ navetivibhāṣāyām iti ? yāvatārthavat samudāyānāṃ prātipadikasaṃjñā pratiṣiddhaiva / ucyate / anukaraṇaśabdatvād atra prātipadikatvam / tataś cārthavatāṃ nāyaṃ samudāyaḥ, eka evāyaṃ śabdaḥ svenārthenārthavān iti / nanu ca prakṛtivad anuparaṇam ity anayā paribhāṣayā prātipadikasaṃjñāpratiṣedhaḥ prāpnoti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / prakṛtidvārikā yā saṃjñātideśād upalabhyate sā śaktā pratiṣeddhum / yā tu svāśrayā tasyā apratiṣedhaḥ / evam apy aṭābantatvād yāḍāmbhāvau na prāpnutaḥ / āpaḥ samudāyād avihitatvāt / ayam apy adoṣaḥ / āb ante yasya śrūyate sa ābanta ity ucyate / pratyayagrahaṇaparibhāṣā hi pratiṣiddhā astrīpratyayeneti / evaṃ caitat / atha vā evaṃ varṇyate / na veti uccārya āśritya yā vibhāṣā, tasyāṃ vibhāṣāyām idam ucyate---arthasaṃjñākaraṇam iti /

[12] kiṃ prayojanam ? kim evaṃ sati bhavataḥ prayojanam abhipretam ity āha / śabdasaṃpratyaya iti / atha vā vaiparītyenāpi nivartakabhāvena pravartamānaṃ prayojanatvenopadiśyate / yathānyatreti / hetudvayam atrāntarbhūtaṃ dṛṣṭānte / anyatra hi saṃjñākaraṇe 'rthaśruter abhāvāt / yathā dādhāśrutiḥ svārtham anupahitārthapratyāyane vyāpṛtā saṃjñitvenāśrīyate iti dāśatiprabhṛtīnāṃ nivṛttyartham / pradeśe ca dādhārūpaṃ pratīyamānam / na ca pratiyamāne śabde tadarthasya saṃnidhānaṃ yuktam anumeyatvād iti saṃjñākāle nāpi pradeśe 'rthasyāsti saṃsparśaḥ / evam ihāpy arthasyāsāṃnidhyāt pradeśe cānumīyamānatvāc ca arthasaṃpratyayo na yukta ity etad ācaṣṭe---yathānyatreti / tena vibhāṣāpradeśeṣu navāśabda ādeśaḥ syāt prathamānirdeśe 'pi, yathāvirāga viraṅgaṃ ceti /

[13] tat tarhi kartavyam arthasaṃjñākaraṇam / sāmānyakaroter viśeṣakarotiḥ karma / atha vā śāstram arthasaṃjñākaraṇaṃ kartavyam iti / itikaraṇo 'rthanitdeśārthaḥ / itikaraṇaḥ kriyate / kriyata iti karaṇaḥ, itiś cāsau karaṇaś ca itikaraṇaḥ / api sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvāt sādhyasādhanasaṃbandhaḥ / 

[14] kiṃ gatam etat itinā āhosvit śabdādhikyādarthādhikyam ? etad uktaṃ bhavati / kim anupapattikam asmābhir grahītavyam, yasmāt bhavatetir uccārita iti yasyeyaṃ kākavāśitasyāvasthā iti / aparaḥ prakāraḥ / smṛtiśāstram idam / tatra yathaiva lopo(? loke) loṣṭādibhiḥ parimāṇaṃ laukikāt smaranti, evam avyayam itiḥ smṛtihetur atra, yathā viparītaliṅgapravṛttes tu śabdo liṅgatvena prayukta ity uktaṃ bahuguḍo drākṣeti / 

[15] gatam ity āha / gataṃ pratītam upasaṃgṛhītam ity arthaḥ / kutaḥ / kiṃ lokād atha vyākaraṇāt iti / kecid arthāḥ śāstrād gamyante---yathākāraikāraukārā vṛddhir iti śāstrād eva samadhigamaḥ / paśur apatyaṃ devateti lokāt / atha vā kuta iti kuto hetoḥ, kuto nimittād iti / vyākaraṇe aparisādhitatvāt lokam evopādatte---lokata iti /

[16] tad yathā loke gaur ity ayam āheti gośabdād itikaraṇaḥ paraḥ prayujyamānaḥ / gośabdaprakṛtiko gośabda ity ucyate prātipadikād itiśabdasya prayogāt / 

[17] kathaṃ punar nitye śabdārthasaṃbandhe ‘śabde cārthe cā’py atyantam asaṃbaddha evārthena / atha kecit śabdāḥ svarūpādhiṣṭhānā eva / kecit arthādhiṣṭhānā eva / sarvathā ca svasmāt arthāt pracyutir nopapadyate /

[18] tatra yathtiva ‘svārtham abhidhāya śabdo nirapekṣaḥ’ ity ayaṃ karamaḥ upanyastaḥ, pratipattyupāyārtham anuṣṭhānam / na hi tatra sakṛduccāritasya śabdasya pravṛttir asakṛd itīdam abhidhāyedaṃ bravītīti na yujyate / samuccayenābhidhānaṃ nāsti, anekaśabdādhiṣṭhānatvāt samuccyasya / samuccyaś ca---plakṣaś ca nyagrodhaś ca, vṛkṣaś ca vṛkṣaś ceti / vikalpo 'pi na saṃbhavati / paryāyeṇa śruter arthānām anavagateḥ / tasmāt saṃsargavādinaḥ sarvaviśeṣayuktārthān saṃniviṣṭā buddhir utpadyate / tasmin viśeṣaśabda uccārite sa tu yathā pānakadravya āsvādite guḍādīnapoddharati idam asti idam asti iti, evaṃ viśiṣṭākārāyāṃ buddhau pravṛttāyāṃ saṃbhavinas tadviṣayān arthān apoddharati / apoddharaṃś ca kaścit kāryapratipattyartham etadrūpāṃ pratipadyamāno nāgṛhītaviśeṣaṇā viśeṣye buddhir ity evam pūrvaṃ jātīḥ pratipadyate / kaścit tu niradhiṣṭhānasthāyino 'pravṛttir iti pūrvaṃ dravyam eva pratipadyate /

[19] evam ihāpi naiva pūrvaṃ śabde paścād arthe kiṃ tu yathā vṛddhirūpatām āpāditeṣu ādaikṣu tādrūpyeṇa vipariṇateṣu so 'yam ity abhisaṃbandhāt ekayogakṣemībhūteṣu mṛjer vṛddhir iti ta eva vṛddhirūpeṇābhidhīyante, evaṃ loke 'pi yo gośabdaḥ so 'yaṃ piṇḍa iti gośabdo gorūpatām āpāditaṃ piṇḍam abhidadhatīti kevalaṃ gośabdaprayoge kim ayam arthe pratyasta uta nety aviśeṣaḥ / itiḥ prayujyamāno 'rthe yāsyāpratyastarūpatā tasyām avasthitasya yaḥ prayogaḥ tam abhivyanakti itiśabdaḥ / tatrāsya yo 'rthas tasya śabdabhāvenāvipariṇāmaḥ / iyam atra pracyutiḥ / 

[20] yady evam iha kathaṃ navāśabdād itikaraṇa iti / na hy atrārthāsaṅgāt śabdaḥ svarūpe pravartata iti / satyam, evam etat / nirjñāte tu śabdārthayoḥ saṃbandhe kadācit yo 'yam arthaḥ sa navāśabda iti vipariṇāmaḥ / itiśabdaḥ pravartamāno navāśabdārthasya śabdena yo vibhāgābhāvas taṃ vyavacchinatti ity etāv etaiva pratipādayanti / anye varṇayanti / śabdārthayoḥ kadācit śabdaḥ pradhānam artha upalakṣaṇam, artho vā pradhānaṃ śabda upalakṣaṇam / guṇapradhānabhāvasya viparyayakṛd itiśabda ity etat pracyavanam / atha vā eka eva śabdaḥ sarvaśaktipracitaḥ / tasya itinā śaktir apacchidyate ity etat pracyuvanam / atha vā bhinnā eva śabdāḥ, sārūpyāt sa evāyam iti tattvabuddhyutpattau nimittaṃ bhavanti ity anya eva gośabdo yo 'rthe prayujyate 'nya evāyam iti pare / asmin darśane na pracyutir upapadyate / atha vā śakyam evā[n a]rthavad varṇavat padāny anarthakāny eva / evam eva ca vākyāt evārthapratipattiḥ gaur ity ayam āheti / samudāye 'rthavati kuto gośabdasya śabdapadārthakatvam iti pracyuter abhāvaḥ / vyākāraṇe tu śabdavyavahāre prasiddhā loke 'rthā iti prasiddheḥ viparyāsamupadyotayanta itiśabdā iti sthitam etat---itikaraṇo 'rthanirdeśārtha iti / 

[21] atha keyaṃ vācoyuktiḥ yāsāvarthapadārthakatā iti / athapadārthakatā hi saṃbandha ucyate / saṃbandhād ayaṃ pracyavamāno 'rthād api pracyuto bhavati iti / kiṃ ca saṃbandhāc cāyam artha upātta iti yad eva nimittam arthābhidhāne tasyaivopādānaṃ yuktam iti /

[23] samānaśabdapratiṣedhaḥ / samānaḥ śabdo vācako yeṣām arthānāṃ te samānaśabdāḥ / teṣāṃ samānaśabdānām arthānāṃ saṃjñitvasya pratiṣedho vaktavyaḥ / navāśabdo hy anekārthaḥ / pratiṣedhavikalpatvādiviśiṣṭe pravṛttinimitte cānāśrīyamāṇe sarvasya navāśabdavācyasyārthasya saṃjñayā bhavitavyam / āśrīyamāṇe vā viśeṣe kuta etat ayaṃ viśeṣo netara iti saṃdehaḥ / ataḥ sarvaprasaṅgaḥ saṃdeho vā iti samānaśabdapratiṣedhaś codyate / navā kuṇḍikā, navā ghaṭikā iti pratyagravacanatā nirdiśyate / yuktaṃ ca yat pratyagravacanasyaiva syāt / pratiṣedhavikalayor hi vākyaikadeśabhūyayor navāśabdayoḥ śravaṇād anumīyamānaṃ vākyāntaram / iha tu sākṣāt pratyagravācitvam iti / 

[23] kiṃ ca syāt / saṃjñā hi na saṃskāraḥ / kiṃ tarhi / tatprayuktaṃ kāryam iti / vibḥāṣā diksamāse bahuvrīhāv iti / kathaṃ kṛtvedam ucyate yāvatā sarvādayaḥ śabdāḥ, na ca navapurāṇatvaṃ śabdadharmaḥ / na ca sarvanāmasaṃjñāyā navapurāṇatvena saṃbhavo 'sti / kiṃ ca dakṣiṇapurveti diśa eva tad abhidhānam / tasyāś ca navapurāṇatvenātyantam asaṃbhavaḥ / sūtre ‘vibhāṣā diksamāsa’ ity anena pratyagrārtha ity etad upasthāpyate / tatra pratyagrārthavṛttau yaḥ tasya sarvanāmasaṃjñā syāt ity ayam arthaḥ prasajyate / yadā ca ‘itaḥ idam’ ity evaṃrūpā dik bhāgeṣu nyastā bhavati tadā eṣa evārtho 'vadhitvena pravartate---pūrvo vṛkṣo grāmād iti / evam antarālam api yadā bhāge nyastarūpaṃ bhavati tadā śālānyā vāntarālatvenāśrīyamāṇā navapurāṇatayā saṃbhavatīti smāyādivyaṅgyaṃ pratyagratvam etadanvākhyānāt pratipadyate, yathā vṛkṣaparṇaṃ svarādivyaṅgyaṃ pratipadyate susthaṇḍileṣv iti /

[24] na vā vidhir pūrvakatvāt pratiṣedhasaṃpratyayo yathā loke / arthaprakaraṇādibhiḥ śabdārthasyāvacchede viśeṣe 'vasthānaṃ dṛṣṭam / saindhavaśabdasya saindhavam ānayeti bhojanaprakaraṇe lavaṇe 'vasthānaṃ dṛṣṭam / mṛgayāprakaraṇe 'śve 'vasthānam / evam iha pradeśānābhūta saṃjñāvidhānāt yathābhūtaḥ pradeśe 'rtho, navāśabdasya tathābhūtasyopādānaṃ yuktam / vidhipūrvakā vibhāṣāśabdāḥ pradeśeṣu / tasmāt vidheyasya codyamānatvāt prakñātatvād vā pratiṣedhārthīya evam bhaviṣyati, na pratyagrārthīyaḥ / loke 'pi yatra viṣaye prastute navāśabdaḥ prayujyate, pratiṣedhārtha eva tatra gamyate na pratyagrārtha iti etad upādāya dṛṣṭāntopanyāsaḥ /

[24] itaro dṛṣṭāntaṃ vyabhicārayati---asti kāraṇaṃ yena loka iti / viliṅgaṃ hi bhavān nirdeśaṃ karoti / viliṅgam iti vyadhikaranam ity arthaḥ / viliṅganirdeśād eva pratiṣedhasaṃpratyayaḥ, na tu vidhipūrvakatvād iti / etam evārthaṃ samarthayate---aṅga hi samānaliṅgo nirdeśaḥ kriyatām iti / etac caiva na jānīmaḥ kvacid vyākaraṇa iti / naiva vyākaraṇe kvacit samādhikaranena nirdeśaḥ / ihāpi ‘vibhāṣā senāsurācchāyā’ śālāniśānām (A 2.4.25) iti / bhavet samānaliṅgo nirdeśo, na tu samānavibhaktikaḥ / tasmān naiva vyākaraṇe vibhāṣāpradeśeṣu śakyaṃ jñātuṃ śrutisaṃnipātāt samānādhikaraṇatvaṃ vyadhikaraṇatvaṃ vā / yadā tu pauruṣeyeṇa vyāpāreṇa dakṣiṇapūrvādiṣu kalpyate tadā syād vā na vā pratiṣedhavācinaḥ śurisaṃnipātāt saṃpratyayo, na tu pratyagravācina ity etad anena prakāreṇa vyācaṣṭe---api ca kāmacāra iti /

[26] anena yat prāg uktaṃ ‘asti kāraṇam’ iti tad vyabhicārayati / samānaliṅge 'pi nirdeśe pratiṣedhavācinaḥ saṃpratyayād ayuktam idam ucyate viliṅgaṃ hi bhavān nirdeśaṃ karoti iti / prayujyamāno 'pi navāśabdo yadā vidheye saṃnipatati tadātyantaṃ dravyeṇāsaṃbandhāt pratiṣedhavācī / yadā tu dravyavācinā samānādhikaraṇas tadātyantaṃ pratyagravācīti / na śakyaṃ vaktuṃ sa eva liṅgavān aliṅgaś ceti / yadā ca prasiddham idaṃ bhavati yathā ca yavāgūṃ navām atyuṣāṇṃ nityaṃ bhuṅkte iti taittirīyakāḥ / tasmān navāśabdo na pratyagravācī sṃbhavati iti pratiṣedhavācy eva pratīyate / yasya tu naivaṃ yavāgūbhojanaṃ prasaktam, sa pūrvam evipadiśyate tatra pratiṣedhavācino 'narthakam upādānam iti pratyagrārthaḥ pratīyate iti yuktam / prayoktuḥ kāmacāra iti / iha tu śāstre yady api kāmacāraḥ syāt, evam api na doṣaḥ syāt / yats tu prayoktur ekarasaiva pravṛttiḥ, tata idam api nāsti kadācit pratyagravācina iti / 

[27] etat samarthayate---na ceha . . . vibhāṣāgrahaṇeneti / kutaḥ ? saṃjñāgrahaṇasya prādhānyāt / tatra bhavatir vidheyatvenopāttaḥ pradhānam, tadaṅgānītarāṇi iti / pradhānam utsṛjya vidheyaṃ na tadaṅgeṣu sarvādiṣu saṃnipatiṣyati vibhāṣāśrutir iti pūrva eva hetuḥ pratyāgrārthāgrahaṇasya ‘na vā vidhipūrvakatvāt pratiṣedhasaṃpratyaya’ iti / 

[28] vidhyanityatvam anupapannaṃ pratiṣedhasaṃjñākaraṇāt / sūtre navāśabdau ekanipāto yathā nakir mākir iti / pratiṣedhārthīyaś cāyaṃ yathānantaram eva pratiṣedhe prayukte vākye ‘na vā vidhipūrvakatvād . . . ’ iti / tena vibhāṣeti navāśabdasya yo 'rthaḥ pratiṣedhas tasya saṃjñā ‘vibhāṣā śveḥ’ iti / evaṃ ca sati śuśāva śuśuvatur iti na sidhyati / 

[29] siddhaṃ tu prasajyapratiṣedhavacanāt / loke 'pi prasajyapratiṣedho dṛṣṭaḥ / grāmo bhavatā gantavyo na veti / na hy antareṇa pratiṣedhyaṃ pratiṣedhavākyasya pravṛttir yuktā / ataḥ pratiṣedhavākyād eva prasaṅgavākyam anumīyate / vipratiṣiddhaṃ tu / ekaṃ lakṣyaṃ buddhau kṛtvāha ‘vibhāṣā śveḥ’ iti / ekam idaṃ śvayatirūpam / tatraikasyaiva rūpasya bhavati na bhavatīti ca vipratiṣiddham āpadyate ity etad ācaṣṭe---na jñāyate kenābhiprāyeṇeti / 

[30] itaraḥ samadhigataprāmāṇyatvād ācāryasya ‘vibhāṣā śveḥ’ iry etad evaṃ pratipattavyam / bahava ete śvayatayaḥ lakṣyasamavāyino 'nena rūpeṇa gṛhītāḥ / teṣu kiṃcid eva rūpaṃ nivṛttiṃ parigṛhyāvasthitaṃ saṃprasāraṇasya, kiṃcit prasaṅgam iti / yathaiva bhinnān dhātūn samāśritya keṣuṣit pratiṣedhaḥ syād evam api draṣṭavyam / kutaḥ ? ekasminn atyantaṃ vipratiṣedhāt vidhipratiṣedhayor iti / evam ‘ūrṇor vibhāṣā’ iti / bahava ūrṇotayaḥ, bahava iḍādayaḥ pratyayā grahītavyāḥ samudāyo vety etad anena pratipādayati / na vā prasaṅgasāmarthyād anyatra pratiṣedhaviṣayāt iti / 

[31] tad etat kva siddhaṃ bhavati ? yā aprāpte vibhāṣās tāsu hi dvāv api prasaṅgapratiṣedhau akṛtārthāv iti yuktaṃ yat pratiṣedho vikalpārthaḥ syāt / prāptikaraṇasya tulyatvāt / yā hi prāpte vibhāṣā tasyāṃ pūrvo vidhiḥ sāmānye utsṛṣṭaḥ kṛtārthaḥ / tasya viśeṣe aprāptyanumānaṃ vādhaḥ prāpnoty eva / tena na vikalpaḥ / atra codyate / yadi prāptavibhāṣāsv ekāntena nivṛttiḥ prāpnoti, ‘vibhāṣā dikyasamāse’ ity atra vibhāṣāśrutiḥ navāśabdārthasya pratiṣedhasya vāciketi bahuvrīhāv ity eva vaktavyam / ‘na bahuvrīhau’ iti pratiṣedhopādānam anarthakam / vibhāṣāgrahaṇam eva tatrānuvartiṣyate / tathā ‘vibhāṣā vipralāpe’ iti ‘vyaktavācāṃ samuccāraṇe’ kṛtārtho vidhir vipralāpe bādhyeta / 

[33] vibhāṣeti mahagīyaṃ saṃjñā kriyate / anyatra mahatīnāṃ sāṃjñānām anvarthatvam / iha ca na mahattvam anvarthānumānam / kiṃ tarhi ? saṃjñibhedasyaivānumānam / nāyam ekanipātaḥ / nipātadvayam etat / dvayoś cārthayoḥ pratiṣedhavikalpayor eṣā saṃjñā / na ca vibhāṣā śabdasya vācyaṃ saṃjñidvitvam / kiṃ tarhi ? pravṛttinimittamātram / atra tv etad eva vicārayitavyam---kim iyaṃ saṃjñā pratiṣedhavikalpayoḥ pratyekaṃ yathā vṛddhisaṃjñā, atha samudāyasya yathā saṃyogasaṃjñeti / tatra yadi pratyekam, idaṃ nāsti pratiṣedhavikalpābhyām ekasmin pravartitavyam iti / tena yathaiva kvacid ākāro vṛddhiḥ kvacid aukāra evaṃ kvacit pratiṣedhaḥ kvacid vikalpaḥ syāt / kramaś ca durlabhaḥ, pūrvaṃ nivṛttiḥ paścād vikalpa iti / atha samudāyaḥ saṃjñī, evam api yathā saṃyogasaṃjñā samudāyasyaiva nāvayavayoḥ evam iyam apīti idaṃ nopapadyate, aprāptavibhāṣāsu pratiṣedhyaṃ nāstīti kṛtvā vety anena vikalpo bhaviṣyati iti, samudāyasya cet kṛtārthā saṃjñā sā katham avayavapradeśaṃ pratipādayiṣyati ? kramaś cātyantam eva durlabhaḥ samudāyasyākramatvāt / ata idam api nopapadyate nety anena pratiṣiddhe vety anena vikalpo bhaviṣyatīti / ucyate / yathā akṣā bhajyantām, bhakṣyantām, dīvyantām iti samudāyaḥ kriyābhir abhisaṃbadhyamāno 'vayavasaṃsparśāt kriyāḥ pratipadyate kenacit tatrāvayavena kiṃcid eva kartavyaṃ kaścid bhakṣayitavyaḥ, kaścid bhaṅktavya iti bhagne 'vayave bhagnaḥ samudāyaḥ, bhakṣite bhakṣitaḥ samudāyaḥ / evam ihāpy aprāptavibhāṣāsv avayavavyāpāre kasmiṃś cid ānarthakyam iti naño 'rtho na vyāpriyate / vety etasmiṃs tu vyāpriyamāṇe samudāya eva vyāpṛto bhavati iti / 

[33] evam api vidhipratiṣedhayoḥ saṃjñā kṛtā / sāvastubhūtayor avyāpṛtayor na hi sidhyati / śakyate hi viruddhāv apy arthāv upādāya saṃjñā kartum / janmamaraṇayor iyaṃ saṃsāreti saṃjñeti / pradeśeṣu tu saṃprasāranaṃ vibhāṣeti vibhāṣāśabdaḥ sakṛnnivṛttipravṛttirūpeṇa tam arthaṃ pratyāyayati / sa cārthaḥ kriyā iti / sa eka evāviśiṣṭo nañā vāśabdena vābhivyajyamānadharmā syāt / yathābhyuddharati, upasamāharati ity eka evoddhartābhimukhyaviśiṣṭo haratīty upasargaprayoge 'bhivyajyate / evaṃbhūtas tāvad ayam arthātmā na saṃbhavati, virodhāt / yathā hy uccatābhimukhyaṃ caikārthaviṣayaṃ saṃbhavati, evaṃ tadbhavati na bhavatīti bhāvābhāvayor atyantavirodhād ekā kriyā evaṃviśiṣṭā na saṃbhavati / atha manyase, dve ete kriye iti / dvayoḥ kriyayor asaṃbandhād ekavākyatāyām asatyāṃ vibhāṣety etatpadaṃ na śaknoti saṃprasāraṇāsaṃprasārane upasaṃhartum / tatra saṃprasāraṇaṃ na bhavatīty etasyopasaṃhāre dvitīyā pravṛttir nāsti vibhāṣāśabdasyeti idaṃ nāsti ‘na bhavatī’ti / atha kalpito bhavatir adhyāhriyate vivṛttimato bhavater apravṛttir iti pradeśeṣu navato 'rthasyānirūpaṇāśaṅkārthakarane cātyantam asāmarthyād idam ucyate bhavatīti cet na pratiṣedhaḥ / yadi saṃprasāraṇaṃ bhavatīty etan nirūpyate pratiṣedho nāsti / atha na bhavatīty abhāvarūpeṇāśrīyate, vidhir nāsti / kuto vidhir nāsti ? vidhipratiṣedhayor atyantavirodhād iti / 

[34] siddhaṃ tu pūrvasyottareṇa bādhitatvāt / pūrvaṃ vidhim uttaro vidhir bādhate / etad uktaṃ bhavati---prathamaṃ pratiṣedhena samīkṛte paścād vikalpaḥ pravartate iti / yadi hi pūrvaṃ vikalpaḥ pravarteta paścāt pratiṣedhaḥ, vikalpo 'narthakaḥ syāt, atyantabādhitatvāt iti / kathaṃ punar ayaṃ kramo labhyaḥ / ucyate / avicchedenāśritāvayavakramavṛtteḥ samudāyasyācāryeṇeyaṃ saṃjñā kṛtā neti veti ceti / yadi ceyam anena vyāpāreṇa pradeśeṣu kāryaṃ na vidadhyāt, saṃjākaraṇam evānarthakaṃ syāt / bhavati ca loke 'vayaveṣu dṛṣṭeṣu samudāye smṛtiḥ / yo hi draṣṭāvayavaṃ pṛthak śuklam upalabhate pṛthak kṛṣṇaṃ, smaraty asau samudāyasya---śabalo 'yaṃ samudāyaḥ / nanu coktaṃ śabda evaṃbhūtam athaṃ na śaknoti vaktum ity aśabdo 'yaṃ vyāpāra āśrito bhavati iti / ucyate / aśabda evāyaṃ vyāpāraḥ / saṃjñākaraṇaṃ tūpāyabhūtam / evaṃprakārasya vikalpasyopasaṃgrahaḥ / triprakāro hi śāstre vyāpāraḥ / vidhirvānityatāvibhaktaḥ, yathā ‘vibhāṣor ṇoḥ’ iti / nivṛttirvānityatāyuktā, yathā ‘vibhāṣā vipralāpe’ iti / na caivam arthaṃ saṃjñākaraṇam arthavat / laukikyā pravṛttyā siddhatvāt / yāś caitā ubhayatravibhāṣāḥ ‘vibhāṣā śveḥ’ ity evamādayaḥ, tāsu laukiko vibhāṣāśabdaḥ sakṛta saṃnipatan vidhau vā saṃnipatet pratiṣedhe vā / pratiṣedhe saṃnipatati pravṛttyuttaram asyābhāvāt vidhir na prakalpayet, vidhau vā saṃnipatitaḥ tadupakṣayavattvāt pratiṣedhe na saṃnipatet / evam eva ca kṛtvoktam idaṃ---‘kim idaṃ ralaḥ ktavāsanoḥ kittvaṃ vidhīyate, āhosvit pratiṣidhyate’ iti / tasyaivaṃ pratipadyamānasya saṃjñākaraṇenācārto vicchedenāvayavayoḥ krameṇa vyāpāraṃ pradeśeṣu pratipādayati---anena krameṇa bhavatā pradeśeṣu ayam arthaḥ pratipattavyo vibhāṣāśabdasyeti / yas tu vidvān evaṃ pratipadyate lakṣaṇam asakṛt pravartamānaṃ pratilakṣyaṃ bhidyate, śver iti ca sarvaḥ śvayatir bhedenopātto liḍviṣayo vibhāṣāśabdaś ca tadbhedād bhidyamāno 'sakṛt pravartate iti taṃ prati saṃjñākaraṇam evānarthakam ity ante vakṣyati ‘aśiṣyo vā viditatvāt’ iti /

[35] sādhvanuśāsane . . . yasya vibhāṣā tasya sādhutvam / idam uktaṃ purastāt ‘vidhipūrvakatvāt pratiṣedhasaṃpratyayaḥ’ iti / śāstraṃ ca sādhutvapratipattyartham iti saṃjñādayaḥ sādhutvāṅgabhūtāḥ / ataḥ sādhutvasya prakaraṇatve prādhānye samadhigate sādhutvabhavanaṃ vikalpyate ity etad āpannam / na hi pradhāne saṃbhavati, kāryaṃ tadaṅge yuktam iti ‘vibhāṣā śveḥ’ ity etad uktaṃ bhavati---śvayatiḥ kṛtasaṃprasāraṇarūpo vibhāṣā sādhur bhavati / kṛtasamāsarūpaḥ sādhur vibhāṣā bhavati iti / evaṃ ca kṛtvoktam iha yat pradhānabhūtaṃ tat prakṛtam / prakaraṇāt guṇaviniyoga iti / sati caivaṃ sādhutvaṃ vibhāṣitaṃ syāt / na tu dvaitam iti / aparaḥ prakāraḥ / śāstropasthitam etat / bhinnārthayor vṛttivākyayor itaretararūpopādānam aśakyam / tataś ca vāvacanam anarthakam / saṃjñāyāś ca svasmin viṣaye bhāvābhāvayor aniṣṭatvāt sādhutvam eva vibhāṣayā saṃbadhyate / evam asit doṣa iti / 

[36] asādhuḥ na prayokṣyata iti / yathā bhakṣyābhakṣyasaṃnidhau bhaṣyam evopādīyate, evam ihobhayasaṃnidhau sādhv eva prayokṣyata iti / na caiva hi kadācit rājapuruṣa ity asya śabdasya asādhutvam iṣyate / na hi śiṣṭā etaṃ śabdaṃ kadācid api na prayuñjate / na cātra sādhau viśeṣasamadhigamahetur asti yenedaṃ vijñāyeta ayaṃ sādhur ayam asādhur iti / yāny api samāsāntodāttādīni teṣām api sādhutva evāṅgatvam iti kṛtasamāsāntodātto vibhāṣā sādhur bhavati iti / 

[37] api ca dvedhāpratipattiḥ / yad arthaṃ ca śāstraṃ vikalpitasya rūpasya pratipattyarthaṃ tathā cāpratipattiḥ / sādhutve vikalpyamāne śvayatiḥ kṛtasaṃprasāraṇo vibhāṣā sādhur ity etad āpadyate / tena śiśvāya śiśvayatur iti rūpasyāsiddhiḥ / 

[38] yasya punaḥ kāryāḥ śabdāḥ vibhāṣāsau samāsaṃ nirvartayati / kiṃbhūtā punar anityatā śabdānām, kiṃbhūtā vā nityatā ? yadyākṛter nityatvāt, athāpi vyaktīnāṃ nādābhivyaṅgyānāṃ nityatāśrīyate, kaḥ saṃbandho vibhāṣayā ? na hy ācāryapravṛttibhedāt saṃbhavati, na vaiṣam asty aśāstrasyaivaṃ pravṛttatvāt / tasmād yadi pāṇinīyam atha vyākaraṇāntaraṃ sarvatra pratipattṛgata eṣa vicāraḥ / kaścit pratipattā idam anvākhyānaṃ vikāravikāribhāvena pratipadyate / ‘iko yaṇacī’ti dadyatreti dadhiśabdasyaiveyam avastheti pratipadyate / anyas tadanyathā pratipadyate, śabdāntaram evedaṃ saṃhitāviṣayam avasthitaṃ yaṇrūpeṇa / śāstraṃ tu kevalam anvākhyāyakam iti / ato ya evaṃ pratipannaḥ śāstrapravṛtte rājan as puruṣa s ity asyāḥ prakṛteḥ dvau śabdau pravartete rājapuruṣo rājñaḥ puruṣa iti ceti tasya samāso nirvartyatvāt pradhānam / pradhāne ca vikalpyatvam iti doṣābhāvaḥ / 

[39] yasyāpi nityas tasyāpy eṣa na doṣa bhavati / saty api sādhutvasya prādhānye vikalpo na śakyate kalpayituṃ sādhutvasyāvikalpyatvāt / kathaṃ ca tad avikalpyam ?  apauruṣeyatvāt / tatra yathaiva brāhmaṇaśatena bhuktam iti saṃkhyā prādhānyenāpi codyamānāsaṃbavāt bhujinā na sāṃbadhyate iti lakṣaṇatvena saṃkhyeyasya pravartate śatasaṃkhyair bhuktam iti, evam atrāpi yat pauruṣeyaṃ tat śakyaṃ vikalpayitum iti saṃjñaiva pauruṣeyī, sā vikalpyate, na sādhutvam iti / tad yathā / medhyaḥ paśur vibhāṣita iti / paśutvānaḍuttvayor apauruṣeyatvāt prākaraṇikam ālambhanaṃ vikalpyate / na tv etad vicāryate anaḍvān nānaḍvān iti / vicārayitur vā buddhir naivaṃ pravartate, avicārto 'rtho na vicarati bhāvābhāvābhyām / 

[40] nanu cedam uktaṃ ‘na ca sṃjñāyā bhāvābhāvāv iṣyete’ iti / satyam, evam etat / kiṃ tu pratipattivaśād etad ucyate / yas tāvad evaṃ pratipadyate ubhayatra vṛttau vākye ca tasyaivānayanam / śrutiśca tulyarūpā yathā dāsyāḥ putro dāsyāḥ putra iti, ekāntena vṛttiḥ vākyasya bādhikā prāpnoti / yathārthadhātuke bhavati / sūtraṃ tv ekāntena bādhitveti vibhāṣārabhyate yathā ‘vā liṭi’ (A 2.4.55) iti / taṃ prati idam ucyate---‘kiṃ tarhi ? saṃjñā saṃbadhyate’ iti / yas tu bhinnārthatām eva pratipannas taṃ prati tad uktaṃ ‘na ca saṃjñāyā bhāvābhāvāviṣyete’ iti / ‘na caitan mantavyaṃ svamanīṣikayocyate’ iti / evaṃ hi ‘samarthānāṃ prathamād vā’ (A 4.1.82) ity atroktam---‘na tarhi vāvacanaṃ kartavyam ? kartavyam ca / kiṃ prayojanam ? nityāḥ śabdāḥ / tatrānena vākyasya sādhutvaṃ kriyate’ iti / 

[41] kāryeṣu yugapadanvācayayor yaugapadyam / ko 'sya vākyasya saṃbandhaḥ ? idam upanyastaṃ bhavatā nityatādarśino vibhāṣā sādhutvaṃ prāpnoti iti / tat parihṛtam / kāryatve tv abhyupagamyamāne sādhutvasya vibhāṣā doṣo nāsti / kiṃ tv ayaṃ doṣo ‘yugapadanvācayayor yaugapadyam’ / yugapatkṛteṣu yugapacchabdaḥ pravartayatvāt pravṛtto yathā yaugapadyam iti bhāvapratyayaḥ / anvācayaś cātra samudāya evābhipretaḥ / sa tv anumīyamānaḥ śabdādhikārād anumito ‘ḍhak ca maṇḍūkāt’ (A 4.1.111) iti anvācayaśabdenoktaḥ / ‘tavyattavyānīyaraḥ’ (A 3.1.93) ‘ḍhak ca maṇḍūkād’ iti / kaḥ pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ ? evaṃ manyate / yasya kāryāḥ śabdāḥ prayoganirapekṣaḥ sa śabdān anvācaṣṭe / śāstrāt samadhigamya śabdāḥ prayujyante iry ekaśabdāś ca tavyattavyānīyara ekasādhaneṣu cādiṣṭāḥ / nāntarīyakam anuṣaktam eva yathā nirmathane 'gnyartham upātte agniṣṭomo 'pi prathamaḥ pravartate, evaṃ tavyati pravṛtte itarayor api tavyānīrayoḥ pravṛttyā bhavitavyam iti / pravṛttivat prayoge 'pi yaugapadyena bhavitavyam / atha vā śabdavat artho 'pi teṣāṃ kārya iti pratipannayaugapadyānāṃ samuccitānāṃ cārtho vidhīyate / ati yathābhūtānām artha upāttas tathābhūtānām eva prayogeṇa bhavitavyam /

[42] yasya punar nityā iti / sa hi loke prayuktānāṃ sādhutvamātram anvācaṣṭe / na caivaṃbhūtasyāsti prayoga iti na yuktā yugapadvacanatā /

[43] pratyayaḥ paro bhavatīti / nanu caikasādhanatvāt yathaiva tavye nāsti dvitvam, tavyattavyānīyarām ekasādhanatvāt ekatvam eveti syāt paratve saṃbhavaḥ / tathā vyavahite 'pi paraśabdaḥ prayuktaḥ---oaraṃ mathurāyāḥ pāṭaliputram iti / anekasya ca paratvam uktam---‘ekā prakṛtir bahavaś ca yañaḥ’ iti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / anekatvaṃ tāvad anenādāv evābhyupagatam / anekatve hi sati idam upapadyate yaugapadyam anvācayo vā / liṅgaṃ cāsti śāstre ‘sadavyayatvaya-’ (A 2.2.11) iti / tasmāl lāghavārthaṃ ‘pṛthak vibhaktīr moccīcaram’ ity ekasādhanānām upādānam / vyavahite ca paraśabdo nivartitaḥ pañcamīnirdeśeṣu nirdiṣṭagrahaṇena / anekāś ca [yañbvyakta]yaḥ paratvena yuktāḥ, yañaḥ ākṛteḥ paratvenābhyupagamād ākṛtiḥ paraiva, vyaktayaḥ kāmaṃ vyavahitāḥ / tavyādayaś caikākṛtiyuktā na saṃbhavantīti na saṃbhavaty ākṛtiparatvam / 

[44] nāpi brūmaḥ pratyayamālā prāpnoti / naivam ucyate ekasyāḥ prakṛteḥ pare bhavantv iti evaṃ yugapad uccarateti / kiṃ tarhi ? anyābhyaḥ prakṛtibhyaḥ pare prayujyanta ekasmin kāle ity evaṃbhūtā yugapadvacanatā codyate / codanāyām evaṃbhūtasyārthe viniyogāt / 

[45] arthagatyarthaḥ śabdaprayoga iti / pratyayānām abhyupagato 'rthaś ca vidhīyate iry etac ceha nopāttam / satyāṃ vārthasya vidhīyamānatāyāṃ bhāvakarmaṇos tavyattavyānīyara ity etat kevalam upanidhānavākyaṃ viniyogavākyānāṃ pratipādakam / tebhyaś ca śabdān arthāś ca pratipadyate / tasmād etāni vākyāny upaplavante---dhātos tavyat bhavati bhāvakarmaṇoḥ / tavyo bhavati bhāvakarmaṇoḥ / anīyar bhavati bhāvakarmaṇoḥ, ity aniṣṭānuṣaṅgaś ca sādhanapratibaddhaḥ śakyaḥ pratihartum / codanāśabdeṣu prayoktradhīnā pratipattir iti śakyate yatheṣṭam abhisaṃbandhaḥ kalpayitum iti / 

[46] ācāryadeśaśīlane ca tadviṣayatā / yacchāstre punaḥ punaḥ pradeśeṣu ācāryagrahaṇam etat śīlanam ity ucyate / tatra yad deśaśīlanaṃ tat tadviṣayam iṣuaye ‘eṅ prācāṃ deśe’ (A 1.1.95) ity evaṃprakāram / yacvācāryāṇāṃ nāmagotreṇa diksaṃbandhena vā śīlanaṃ tattadviṣayaṃ prāpnoti iti codyate / kutaḥ ? evaṃ manyate / śāstraṃ kāranatvena yaḥ pratipadyate, śāstratantraḥ prayogo na prayogatantraṃ śāstram iti, tasyaitasmin darśane aprasiddho loke prayogaḥ śāstreṇa pratipadyata iti ‘iko hrasvo 'ṅyaḥ’ (A 6.3.61) iti etat pratipādakam---hrasvāḥ prayoktavyāḥ / sarvasmiṃś ca prayoktari prasakte prayokṛniyamo ‘gālavasya’ iti / etam evārthaṃ parigṛhya dṛṣṭānta uktaḥ---pañcakṛtvo jamadagnir vā etad iti /

[47] nanu cārthavādo 'yam / tatra vidhivākyaśeṣabhūtatvād arthavādasya, vidhir gamyate / sa ca vidhiḥ ‘catur avadyanti’ / ‘pañcakṛtvo jāmadagnyasya’ iti (mānavaśrautasūtra 1.3.2.5) / alaukikatvād asyārthasya śāstrād idam avasīyate / tatra yathaivāsyāvṛttiḥ sarvakartṛviṣayā ‘catur avadyanti’ iti, evaṃ pañcakṛtva iti prasaktā kartṛviśeṣe 'vadātari jāmadahnye niyamyata iti / nājāmadagnya . . . juhoti / jamadagnir vā etad iti / kecit so 'yam ity abhisaṃbandhenāpatyam eva pitṛśabdena vyapadiśanti / avādyad iti ca chāndaso leṭ vartamāne kāle pañcamo lakāro bāhulakāt śyan iti / anye punaḥ pitur ācaraṇāt putro 'py avadyatīti evaṃ manyante / 

[48] yasya punar nityāḥ śabdāḥ, tasya prayogatantratvāt śāstrasya, lokaprasiddhaprayogāṇāṃ smartāraḥ kevalaṃ bhavanti nābhisaṃbandhāra ity uttarapadahrasvatvaṃ vikalpitam, kevalaṃ gālavaḥ smartṛtvenopādīyate / gālavo hrasvatvaṃ smarati / kiṃbhūtam ? yathābhūtaṃ loke iti gālavasya yat pramāṇīkaraṇaṃ medhāpūjātha vā gālavao hrasvatvaṃ smaraty evam asyāgamo 'vicchinnaḥ / anye tu dīrghaṃ smaranti / na ca gālavena smṛtam anyo na prayuṅkte / gālavo vānyena smṛtaṃ dīrgham iti nāsti tatra tadviṣayatvam / deśagrahaṇaṃ kīrtyartham iti / kīrtiḥ khyātiḥ saṃśabdanam, khyātiliṅgatvāc ca prāsādādayo 'pi kīrtir eveti vyapadiśyante / saṃśabdadyamānāś ca kecit saṃskāraṃ pratipadyante yathā indradyumnādaya iti / nanu ca yasyāpi kāryā iti / śakyate tatrāpi pūjā kalpayitum---dṛśyate hi loke āhopuruṣikayāpi pūjā, gālavasya yamāt hrasve pravṛttis tasmāt tvam api prayuṅkṣveti saṃbhavati pramāṇīkaraṇāt pūjā, dvidhā pratipattis tu na bhavatīty āha---tatkīrtane ca dvedhāpratipattiḥ / śāstrāt hrasvam eva pratipanno na dīrgham iti / 

[49] aśiṣyo vā viditatvāt / vāśabdo vikalpe / śiṣyo vā ity arthaḥ / śabdasaṃjñā svarūpādhiṣṭhānā / yā tasyās tadrūpāyā eva pradeśeṣu pravṛttiḥ śāstre ca tayā saṃvyavahāro yathā vṛddhyādiṣu / iyaṃ caivaṃbhūtā na bhavatīti etad ācaṣṭe / ye 'pi hy etāṃ sāṃjñāṃ nārabhanta iti / sāmānyena dṛṣṭāntam upādāyaikadeśam udāharaṇatvenopanyasyati---yājñikāḥ khalv apīti / atha vā laukikāḥ śāstrād āgamayya pratipadyeran / yājñikās tu bhinnatantratvāt tasmin śāstre śāstrāntarasaṃjñayā na vyavahareyur iti / 

[50] tatra yadaivaṃ pratipattiḥ śāstrākṛtir lakṣyākṛtau pravartata iti tado 'bhayatravibhāṣārtha kartavyo yogaḥ / itarathā hi ‘vibhāṣā śveḥ’ (A 6.1.30) iti vibhāṣā śabda ekasmin vākyaśeṣe upakṣīṇo na bhavati, bhavati vā, pravṛttyantarasyābhāvāt ubhayam asamarthaḥ kartum iti / yadā tv evaṃ manyase lakṣyavyaktiṣu śāstravyaktaya iti tadaitan nibandhanād ‘vibhāṣā śveḥ’ iti yāval lakṣyaṃ tatsamavāyino bahūn yogān pratipadyata iti nārabdhavyo yogaḥ / etam eva cārthaṃ pradarśayatā ācāryeṇa pradeśeṣu bhinnalakṣaṇaḥ śabdavyavahāraḥ kṛtaḥ / etat pradarśayati---ācāryaḥ khalv api saṃjñāmārabhamāṇa iti / kathaṃ tad uktaṃ ‘bhāradvājaḥ svasmāt mātāt pracyāvyate’ iti ? ucyate / yathānena smṛtvopanibaddhaṃ tataḥ pracyāvyata iti / 

[51.1] aprāpte trisaṃśayāḥ / aprāpta ity etat svaryate / trisaṃśayā iti na ‘trayah saṃśayāḥ yāsu vibhāṣāsu tāstrisaṃśayāḥ’ / kuta etat ? ekatvāt saṃśayasya / yathā sthāṇuḥ puruṣa ity eka eva saṃśayo na dvau saṃśayau / atas tryadhiṣṭhānatvāt trisaṃśayā iti ucyate / 

[51.2] ‘dvandve ca / vibhāṣā jasi’ iti vibhāṣāprakaraṇopalakṣaṇārtham upāttam / iha tu saṃśayaḥ ‘prathamacaramataye’ti [yadi ‘sarvādīni’ ity anuvartate tataḥ] prāpte 'tha nivṛttaṃ pūrvavipratiṣedhaś caivam aprāpte, tathā paravipratiṣedha evam ubhayatra / ‘sarvādīni . . .’ ity asyāvakāśo [jasbhinne, dvādiṣu ca vikalpo] yasmāt sarvādi na bhavati dvaye dvayā iti / ihobhayaṃ prāpnoti ubhaye iti / dhyānagrahakāreṇoktam---aprāpte pūrvavipratiṣedhāt antaraṅgatvād vā / antaraṅgam ity eva kasmāt ? prakṛtimātrāśrayaṇāt / vibhāṣā tūbhayajasimāśritya bahiraṅgā / ayaṃ tūbhayam etad anāśrityāprāpte vibhāṣāṃ varṇayati---aprāpte 'yac pratyayāntaram iti / yas tvayajādeśaḥ parigṛhyate, sa hy ubhayaśabdāt pratyayāntaraṃ vijñāsyate / tatraivaṃ pāṭhaḥ kriyate---‘ubhād avayave 'yac’ / tataḥ ‘saṃkhyāyās tayap’ / ‘dvitribhyāṃ tayasyāyajve’ti / atha vā ‘saṃkhyāyā avayave tayap’ / ‘ubhād ayac’ / ‘dvitribhyāṃ vā’ / tailamātrā . . . atrāpi prāpnoti / nanu cākārānta evāyam / naiṣa doṣaḥ / naivam ucyate tailamātrāśabdāt prāpnotīti / kiṃ tarhi ? tailamātrīti bhavitavyam iti / sadṛśasyāpy asaṃniviṣṭasyeti / kathaṃ cāyaṃ na saṃniviṣṭa ity etat śakyaṃ pratipattum ? ucyate / anubandhe 'saty api artha evopalakṣito nimittaṃ pratyabhijñāne / evaṃ cehārthenaiva saṃniviṣṭo vijñāsyate, pramāṇārthavṛttiḥ yaḥ sa saṃniviṣṭa iti / yas tu parimāṇārthavṛttiḥ syāt iyam asya mātrā idam asya parimāṇam iti tasyāsaṃniveśaḥ / prātipadikāt parasya pramāṇavācinaḥ saṃniveśāt yathoktaṃ ‘luṅi sijādidarśanāt’ iti /

[51.3] ūrṇor vibhāṣā / yady aparisamāptakriyam idam avidhāyakaṃ kasyacid āsaktasvaritaliṅgam upādīyate, [yathā] ‘samarthānāṃ prathamād vā’ iti, ataḥ ‘asaṃyogāl liṭ kit’ ity atra pratilabdhakriyaṃ tasmin nānāvākyatvenaiva pravartate / asaṃyogāl liṭ kit bhavati / ūrṇoter vibhāṣā bhavatīti / nānāvākyatvaṃ cāsaṃyogagrahaṇād evānumīyate / asaṃyogānta eva hi ūrṇotir iti / iha tu vacanam iḍupajīvanārtham / atha vidhāyakam idam / evaṃ sati liḍviṣaye 'nārambhāt sāvakāśāv etāv iti vipratiṣedhe sati iha yadi paravipratiṣedha evaṃ sati aprāpteyam / pūrvavipratiṣedha evam ubhayatra / katham ? iha pūrvā vibhāṣā paro nityo vidhiḥ / pūrvatra parā vibhāṣā pūrvo vidhir iti 

aprāpte / anyad dhi kittvam anyat ṅittvam / vibhāṣayā ṅittvaṃ, nitye vidhau kittvam / etat śrutibhedād anyatvaṃ saṃprasāraṇāsaṃprasāraṇādikāryam etayor bhinnam iti / 

ekaṃ cet ṅitkitau / katham ekatvam ? evaṃ manyate / nāyaṃ tadvad atideśaḥ / kiṃ tarhi ? kāryātideśaḥ / yad atideśāt pravartate tatkāryam / taccorṇoter ṅittve kittve cāviśiṣṭam / guṇapratiṣedho 'tra kāryam / sa tu kiṃ nityaḥ pratiṣedha āhosvit vibhāṣeti / etad āha---‘ekaṃ cet’ iti / yady api nānā evam api saṃdehaḥ / kathaṃ punar ‘vibhāṣorṇoḥ’ ity etat ‘sārvadhātukam apit’ (A 1.2.4) ity atra vartate ? iṭaś cāpittvāt vākyabhedena pravṛttir ity anumīyate / tatra yadi sārvadhātukaṃ ṅit bhavati ūrṇoter iha sārvadhātukaṃ vibhāṣeti prāpte / atha na svaryate viśeṣeṇa / tatra ‘sārvadhātukam apit’ ity asyāvakāśaḥ---cinutaḥ / ‘vibhāṣorṇoḥ’ ity asyāpi prorṇavitā prorṇuvitā / ihobhayaṃ prāpnoti---praurṇuvīti / ata āha---aprāpte / paravipratiṣedho 'yam iti / 

[51.4] vibhāṣopayamane / nanu ca gandhanopayam anayor atyantabhedāt saṃdehena na bhavitavyam / evaṃ manyate / gandhanāṅgam upayamanaṃ gandhanam iti vyapadiśyate / yathā yā yonau vidyotate, tasyā yā balāhakāt vidyotata iti / atra pūrvavipratiṣedhād vāprāpte vibhāṣā / atha vopayamānāśrayaṃ bhavati / na ca gandhanāśrayaṃ bhavati / kutaḥ ? tasyāprādhānyāt / ity evaṃ seyam aprāpte vibhāṣā / 

[51.5] anupasargād vā / upaparābhyām ity anenārambhād vā vṛttyādayo na saṃbadhyante eva / upaparāpūrve na saṃbhavanti vā vṛttyādayaḥ / ‘āṅaḥ udgamane’ (A 1.3.40) ity arthāntaram evopādīyate vṛttyādivirodhi / tathā ‘veḥ pādaviharaṇe’ (41) / ‘propābhyāṃ samarthābhyām’ (42) iti prārambhe samānārthatvam etayor na vṛttyādiṣu iti / 

[51.6] vibhāṣā vṛkṣamṛgādīnām / ye 'tra mṛgādayaḥ prāṇinas te na saṃbadhyante ‘jātir aprāṇinām’ (A 2.4.6) ity anena / vṛkṣādayas tv aprāṇina eva, tatra ye carmamayā mṛgādayas tatreyaṃ vibhāṣā / na tu prāṇiṣv ity evaṃ bhavitavyam / prāpte vibhāṣā / ‘jātir aprāṇinām’ iti nivṛttam, pūrvavipratiṣedhaś ca / antareṇāpi pūrvavipratiṣedham iyam aprāpte vibhāṣā / gauṇamukhyayor mukhye saṃpratyaya iti mukhyeṣu paśuṣu vibhāṣā na mṛdgavādiṣu / 

[51.7] uṣavid . . . / pratyayāntād iti vāmi prāpte / ijāder ity atra pratyayāntād ity etan na saṃbadhyate / asyāpi vidher nityatvāt vacanānarthakyam āpadyate iti / atha kathaṃ pratyayāntā uṣādaya uṣādivyapadeśaṃ labhante, yāvatāaktaparimāṇānuṣādīn parigṛhya vibhāṣā vidhīyate ?
