ucyate / ‘sanādyantā dhātavaḥ’ (A 3.1.32) ity atra prakāracatuṣṭayam---tad ekadeśavijñānam, tadvad atideśaḥ, saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhaḥ, pratyākhyānam iti / tad ekadeśavijñānaṃ tāvat---yathā samāsānta ity antagrahaṇena samāsaikadeśatvaṃ ḍajādīnām iti bahvṛcādigrahaṇena gṛhyante, evam ihāpi snādayo 'natā eṣām = ekadeśā eṣāṃ te ime sanādyantā ity evaṃvijñāyamāne uṣeḥ sanekadeśo gṛhyate uṣigrajaṇeneti / nanu caivaṃ kalpyamāne saṃjñāvidhir ayaṃ na bhavatīti sanā tadantavidhir asti / evaṃ ca sati sanantopyanyasyaikadeśo vijñāyeta /  naiṣaḥ doṣaḥ / samānārthasya vā sananta ekadeśaḥ syāt, anyārthasya vā / tatra samānārthasyoktatvāt prayogo nāsti pac pipakṣatīti / anyārthasya virodhāt asaṃbandhe sati prayogābhāvāt pipakṣatīti / atha vā sanādineva prādhānyenāśrayiṣyāṃaḥ / na ca pradhānena tadantavidhir astīti / ekadeśeṣu pratyayād ity anuvartamāne uṣigrahṇena grahaṇāt vibhāṣety evaṃ prāpte iyaṃ vibhāṣā / atha nivṛttam, kevalo 'pi uṣir uṣiḥ sanatopi / tatra yadi pūrvavipratiṣedha evam aprāpte paravipratiṣedha evam ubhayatrety asti saṃdehaḥ / atha vā tadvad atideśo 'yam, yathā ‘bahugaṇavatuḍ ati saṃkhyā’ (A 1.1.23)---saṃkhyāśabde ekādipadārthake āśrīyamāṇe tadvad atideśo vijñāyate---saṃkhyāvad bhavatīti / evam iha śāstre bhūvādiṣu dhātuśabdaḥ prasiddhaḥ, kāryāṇi ca yāni tāni upādāya padārthaikadeśabhūtāni ihopāttāḥ sanādyantā dhātava iti sanādyantā dhātuvad bhavantīti dhātukāryaṃ labhante / etat tu parīkṣyam, saty api ca tadvad atideśe kim uṣādibhiḥ sanantaiḥ sāmānyātidiṣṭaṃ kāryaṃ labdhavyaṃ pratyayotpattiḥ, antodāttatvam, athoṣādīnām api yat kāryaṃ tad apīti / eṣa sāmānyakāryaṃ prati prakṛtaḥ / kuta etat ? sāmānye viśeṣāṇāṃ vyabhicāritvāt / yathā brāhmaṇavad asmin iti na māṭhare yat tad atidiśyate / evaṃ ca kṛtvoktaṃ ‘sāmānyātideśe hi viśeṣān atideśaḥ’ / satyam, evam etat / iha tu sāmānye 'tidiśyamāne 'tideśa evānarthakaḥ / saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhenāpy asyārthasya tulyatvāt / tasmāt pade tattvāt tu yad bhavatīti / etad evaṃ pratipattavyam / lāghavārtham etat sāmānyaśabdopādānam / etat tu prativiśeṣam upasaṃhṛtaṃ bhūvādirūpeṇopāttam / sananto bhavativat edhativat bhavatīti, asti uṣeḥ sanantasya uṣivad atideśāt vibhāṣā kāryapratipattir iti / ayaṃ tv atra doṣaḥ---sanādyantānāṃ sarvadhātūnāṃ yat kāryaṃ tat prāpnoti iti / yadā saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhas tatkatham ? yady evaṃ kalpyate dhātava ity etat bhūvādipadārthakam, te ca bhūvādayaḥ sanādyantānāṃ saṃjñā / evaṃ tu kalpyamāne ‘dhātoḥ karmaṇaḥ . . . ’ (A 3.1.7), ‘dhātor ekācaḥ . . . ’ (A 3.1.22) ity atrāpi prasaṅga iti / atha manyase pratīyamānā atra bhūvādaya iti, ihāpi pratīyamānā ‘sanādyantā dhāvatavaḥ’ iti / tasmād ekakālaiveyaṃ svarūpapadārthikā bhūvādīnāṃ sanādyantānāṃ ca dhātusaṃjñā / yatra tu viśeṣa uccarati, sā saṃjñā syāt yathā ‘vibhāṣā kṛvṛṣoḥ’ (A 3.1.120) iti cikīrṣateḥ saṃjñā syāt / etac ca pratyākhyātam---dhātvarthe vidhīyante sanādayaḥ, tatra sthānivadbhāvāt siddhā saṃjñeti / aprāpte / pratyayāntā dhātvantarāṇi / etad ākhyātam anena prakāreṇa naikadeśasaṃvijñānaṃ sanādīnāṃ, tadvad atideśo [vā], kiṃ tu saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhaḥ, pratyākhyānaṃ vā / tataś cādhhikyāt uṣādigrahaṇenāgrahaṇāt saṃdeho nopapadyate / apare saṃśayam evaṃ varṇayanti / uṣiḥ kvibanta ācāre kvipā pratyayāntaś ca bhavati tacchrutiś ceti / idam apy evaṃ samarthayante ‘pratyayāntā dhātvantarāṇi’ iti / naitasmād ācārakvib astīti / 

[51.8] dipādīnāṃ vibhāṣā / ‘dīpajana . . . ’ (A 3.1.61) ity etat svaryate itīhānumitam / samudāyenānumīyate---bhāvakarmaṇoś ciṇ bhavati, dīpādīnāṃ vibhāṣeti / na tv aṅgāṅgibhāvena / kuta etat ? pṛthakpāṭhāt / itarathā hy evam eva paṭhet---‘bhāvakarmaṇor dīpādibhyo vibhāṣā’ iti /

atha vidhāyakam idam, kartarīti ca nivṛttam, paravipratiṣedhāt aprāpte, pūrvavipratiṣedhād ubhayatra prāpte iyaṃ vibhāṣā / 

aprāpte / kartarīti hi vartate / vidhāyakaṃ cedam ity abhiprāyaḥ / evam api saṃdeho nyāyye vā kartari karmakartari veti / yadi kartarīty etad api svaryate, tatra kartṛparigṛhītā dīpādayo vākyabhedeneha kriyāśabdaṃ labhante / bhāvakarmaṇoś ciṇ bhavati / dīpādibhyaḥ kartari karmaṇi vibhāṣeti prāpte yaṃ vibhāṣā / atha kartarīti na svaryate, karmakartā kartā bhavatīti dīpādīnāṃ karmakartari ubhayaprāptau pūrvaparavipratiṣedhāhyāṃ saṃdeha upapadyate / sakarmakasya kartā karmavad bhavatīti / yady api budhiḥ sakarmakas tathāpy ekarasaṃ parihāraṃ vakṣyāmīti aviśeṣeṇoktam---akarkāś ca dīpādaya iti / sopasargāḥ sakarmakā bhavantīti / sopasargo hy arthāntaravṛttitvaṃ pratilabhate, bhavati anubhavatīti / karmasthabhāvakānāṃ karmasthakriyāṇām iti / parispandāparispandabhedena vibhakto 'rthātmā bhedenopātto---bhāvaḥ kriyeti / kathaṃ punar etal labhyam ? evaṃ tatroktam---‘karmasthakriyā karmaśabdenocyate’ iti / yathā pacatiḥ karmasthakriyaḥ / karmaṇi hi kriyākṛto viśeṣa upalabhyata iti / 

idaṃ tu ‘acaḥ kartṛyaki’ (A 6.1.15) ity anena virudhyate / evaṃ hi tatrāvasthitaṃ ‘jāyate svayam eva’ iti / janer upadeśe evātvam / tataś copadeśe 'janto 'yam iti / yadi ca kartṛsthabhāvako 'yam, kuto 'sya kartṛyak ? iti / 

[51.9] vibhāṣāgre[prathamapūrveṣu] / ābhīkṣṇye iti nivṛttam, pūrvavipratiṣedhaś ca / tatra tv idam uktam---‘agrādiṣv aprāptavidheḥ samāsapratiṣedhaḥ’ iti / aprāptavidhāne hy agrādayo nimittaṃ pratyayotpattau / atha tu prāpta iyaṃ vibhāṣā, evaṃ sati nivṛtter nimittam agrādaya ity upapadatvabhāva iti / atha tu prāpta iyaṃ vibhāṣā, evaṃ sati nivṛtter nimittagrādaya ity upapadatvābhāva iti / parihāraścokto 'maiva yat saha nirdiṣṭam upapadaṃ tat samasyata iti /

[51.10] tṛnnādīnāṃ vibhāṣā / ‘tṛnnannatīkṣṇaśuciṣu’ (A 6.2.161) iti, ‘ākrośe ca’, ‘saṃjñāyām’ iti---yadi ‘ackau’ iti nivṛttam, ataḥ saṃdehena bhavitavyam / atha ‘ākrośe’ ity atra ackāv anuvartete, niḥsaṃdigdham aprāptavibhāṣeyam / 

[51.11] ekahalādau (A 6.3.59) ‘saṃjñāyām’ ity anuvṛttam / ‘aiṣaṃ vāsa . . .’ (58) ity atra nānumīyeta, ārambhasāmarthyāt / asti punaḥ kācit saṃjñā saṃbhavati ‘ekahalādau pūrayitavye’ ? astīty āha---upadānam iti / 

[51.12] cādibhir yoga iti / kasya yoge ? yuṣmadasmadoḥ / grāmas tava ca svaṃ mama ca svam iti / yadā tu svasya yogas tadā bhavamty evādeśāḥ / grāmaś ca te svam / nagaraṃ ca me svam iti / 

[51.13] yaṅi iti vā nitye prāpte / ‘aci vibhāṣā’ (A 8.2.21) ity atra ‘yaṅi’ iti vartate / yaṅi acīti / tatraivaṃ bhavitavyam, yatra yaṅparaḥ ajanantato bhavatīti / kva ca bhavatīti ? yaṅluki nijegila iti / apare varṇayanti / avayavalope kṛte yaṅi ya ekadeśas tasminn acīti / ye tu nijegilyate iti varṇayanti teṣām ubhayata āśrayāt antādivadbhāve 'sati durlabhaṃ yaṅo 'cparatvam /

[51.14] prāpte ca / na pūrvavipratiṣedho na paravipratiṣedhaḥ / kiṃ tarhi ? anuvṛttir āsu / 

[51.15] vibhāṣā vipralāpe / avyaktavācām api ca vipralāpo 'sti / vipravadanti śakunaya iti iṣṭāniṣṭasūcakatvāt / vyaktavācām api saṃbhavati / vipravadante mauhūrtā iti / prāpte / vyaktavācām iti vartate / tena vipravadanti śakunaya iti bhavitavyam /

[51.16] vibhāṣopapadena pratīyamāne (A 1.3.77) / saṃnidhānam upapadātmanepadābhyām avadyotyate, yathā tvaṃ (svaṃ?) pacasīti / evaṃ ca kṛtvā saṃdehe tūpapadam avadyotakam / ekāntena aprāptavibhāṣeyam / ubhayatra / nivṛttam, pūrvavipratiṣedhaś ceti / 

[51.17] hṛkrot anyatarasyām / harateḥ pratyavasānārthatā nimittaṃ gatyarthatā ca karoter akarmakatvam / 

prathamasya prathame pāde ṣaṣṭhamāhnikam //






mahābhāṣyadīpikāyāṃ saptamam āhnikam 

ig yaṇaḥ saṃprasāraṇam // 1.1.45 //

[1] kim iyaṃ vākyasya . . . saṃjñā . . . āhosvid varṇasya ? ubhatanimittasaṃbhavāt, yaugapadyābhāvād ubhayoś ca doṣadarśanāt, śāstre ca dvayor liṅgadarśanāt praśnaḥ / nanu cākhyātaṃ saviśeṣaṇaṃ vākyam ity ktam / nākhyātapadaṇ śrūyate / athaivaṃ kalpyate ‘astir bhavantīpara . . . ’ (vā 2.3.2.11), evam api vipratiṣiddhatvam āpadyate---‘saṃprasāraṇaṃ bhavatī’ti saṃprasāraṇapradeśeṣu idam upatiṣṭhate ‘igyaṇo 'stī’ti, yady asti na tarhi bhavati, atha bhavati na tarhi astīti / 

[2] ucyate / sopaskāratvāt sūtrasya ‘bhavatiṃ’ vākyaśeṣatvena samarthayiṣyāmahe---‘igyaṇo bhavatī’ti /

evam api yaugapadyā(? padatvā)bhāvād asya vākyasya saṃjñitvam anupapannam / prathamānto hi saṃjñī ṣaṣṭhyanto vā, na ca vākyaṃ prathamāntaṃ ṣaṣṭhyantaṃ vā / ucyate / vākyārtho vākyam ity abhidhīyate, yathā matvartho matupśabdenocyate ‘matau chaḥ sūktasāmnoḥ’ (A 5.2.59) iti / 

nanu cārtho 'py asattvabhūto 'yam iti [kimiti] vākyasyeti vākyārthopalakṣitam ? ucyate / saṃhñāsaṃbandhakāle pratyavamṛṣṭaḥ, sarvanāmnā vasturūpeṇopasaṃhṛtaḥ, ‘idaṃ tad’ iti vyapadeśārhaḥ, saṃjñitvam anubhūya pradeśeṣu svena vyāpāreṇa pravṛtto 'sattvabhūta evopatiṣṭhate iti / ‘igyaṇo bhavatī’ty asya yo 'rthas tasya saṃprasāraṇasaṃjñāyāṃ pradeśev idam upatiṣṭhate, igyaṇo bhavatīgyaṇo jāyate igyaṇo niṣpadyate iti / 

[3] āhosvit varṇasya ? yaṇaḥ sthāne ya ik tasya saṃprasāranam ity eṣā saṃjñeti / atha varṇasaṃjñitve sati kiṃ yaṇaḥ sthāne ya iktasya saṃjñā, āhosvit yaṇa evety anirṇītam e[va] / saṃprasāraṇaśabde hi svarūpapadārthake yaṇā saṃjñitvena bhavitavyam / manaṇarina(?) saṃjñinirdeśe tv ikā bhavitavyam / ucyate / yaṇa iti caiṣā ṣaṣṭhī, naiṣā saṃprasāraṇena kṛte ṣati (?) / ke (?) kiṃ tarhi ? ikā / kuta etat ? anyathā virodhāt / evaṃ hi vijñāyamāne yaṇaḥ saṃprasāraṇasaṃjñeti ig ity asyopādā[naṃ vyarthaṃ syāt] /

[4] [vākya]saṃjñā ced varṇavidhiḥ / varṇasya yo vidhiḥ, pūrvatvaṃ dīrghatvaṃ ca na sidhyati / tasmād varṇasya vidheyā saṃjñeti / tathā hi, vākyasya saṃprasāraṇasaṃjñāyāṃ satyāṃ iko yajjanma tasyaiṣā saṃjñā / janma cārtho, na cārthasya pūrvaparavyapadeśo na ca sthānitvam iti / kimarthaṃ punar idaṃ dvayam ucyate, nirdeśasya kāryasya cāsāṃbhava iti ? etatpradarśayati---yady api apaśabdena nirdeśaḥ kriyate, yathā kair āgacchāmīti, evam api nirdeśamātraṃ syāt, kāryaṃ tu vākye 'saṃbhavān naiva pravartate / 

[5] varṇasaṃjñā cen nirvṛttiḥ . . . ‘ṣyaṅaḥ saṃprasāraṇam’ (A 6.1.13) iti / sa eva hi tāvd ika durlabhaḥ / alabhyo 'pi loke durlabhaḥ ity ucyate / athāpi kathaṃcil labhyetety a(pi) vyā(khyā)naparikalpanayocyate / kenāsau yaṇaḥ sthāne syād iti / evaṃ varṇayanti---atha vā sa eva [hi] tāvad i[g]a durlabhaḥ iti śāstrāntarasyāpravṛttimātram atrocyate, yathā vṛddhisaṃjñāyām itaretarāśraya[pra]saṅgād doṣa uktaḥ / athāpi kathaṃcil labhyeteti ‘siddhaṃ tu nityaśabdatvāt’ (A 1.1.1, vā 9) ity etad āśrityocyate / yathoktam avasthitā evākāraikāraukārāḥ / kenāsau yaṇaḥ sthāne syāt ? anenaivāsau vayavasthāpyaḥ / etatpradarśayati---netaretarāśrayadoṣa igrūpe / kiṃ tarhi ? sthānipratipattau / yo hi yaṇaḥ sthāne ik tasya saṃprasāraṇasaṃjñā, satyāṃ ca saṃprasāraṇasaṃjñāyāṃ yaṇsthānikatvam iti / vikāravikāriprakalpanā śāstrān, na lokād, itīdam ucyate ta[d eta]d itaretarāśrayaṃ bhavati iti / 

[6] vibhaktiviśeṣanirdeśas tu jñāpakaḥ / bhūtavibhaktyā nirdeśo vyākaraṇe, ‘saṃprasāraṇasya’ (A 6.3.139), ‘saṃprasāraṇāt’ (A 6.1.108) iti / vākyārthe ca saṃjñiny etan na saṃbhavati / ‘ṣyaṅaḥ saṃprasāraṇam’ (A 6.1.13) ity etac ca varṇe na saṃbhavati iti idam anumīyate---āvartata etat sūtram ālasyāt tu sakṛd uccāritam / tantreṇa vā dvau yogāv etau / yathaikaḥ śabdo (dhikaraṇabhedenoccāryamāṇo dvirūpo lakṣyate, evam aneka ekarūpa, iti / ubhayathā ācāryeṇa śiṣyāḥ pratipāditāḥ---kecit vākyasya, kecit varṇasyeti / 

[7] atha vā punar astu vākyasya [eva] / antareṇāvṛttiṃ tantraṃ ca pratipādayati / yathā kākāñjātaḥ kākaḥ / atra codyate---svajātiyogād eva kākaḥ kāko bhavati / evaṃ ca kṛtvoktam ‘yenaiva hetunā ekaḥ kākas tenaivānyo 'pi’ iti / evam evaitat / idaṃ tu pratipādayati---santi loke pitṛsamākhyāḥ pytreṣu, babhrur maṇḍur lamaka iti / bābhravyamāṇḍavyalāmakāyaneṣu tā adhyastā iti / kiṃ ca, yadā kākatvopavyañjanāni na vidyante, tasyām apy avasthā[yāṃ] kākaḥ / tatra yathaiva vāsitaṃ jāter asti vyañjakam evaṃ janmāpīdam upanyastam / śabdo 'pi so 'yam ity abhisaṃbandhāt kriyāsāggāc ca pravartate / oraṣṭhasyeyaṃ strīti bhedapūrvake so 'yam ity abhisaṃbandhe praṣṭhaśabdaḥ strīvṛttiḥ / atha vā, orasthānāsaṅgaḥ striyām/ āsakte prasthāne puruṣa iva nirapekṣa eva praṣṭhaśabdaḥ striyāṃ vartate / evam ihāpi kākāj jāta iti bhedaḥ so 'yam ity abhisaṃbandhe vā nimittam / atha vā, kākavāsitānuṣaṅgaḥ kriyate / jāter atrārthasyābhivyañjakaṃ janmety etat pratipādayati, yasmāt kākāj jātas tasmāt kāko 'yam iti / evaṃ saṃprasāraṇāj jātam iti kriyāyās tadvataś cābhedena vyapadeśaḥ / atha vā, kriyār¥oānuṣakto varṇas tāṃ pratipadyate, kāryaṃ tu dravyabhūtaḥ pratipadyate, yathā saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhe vākyārthaḥ pratyavamṛṣṭaḥ saṃjñitvam anubhavati, pradeśeṣv asattvabhūta eveti / 

[8] atha vā, dṛśyante vākyeṣu vākyaikadeśān prayuñjānāḥ / praviśeti kriyā, na ca niradhiṣṭhānā kriyā pravartata iti yogyasādhanam upādatta iti karmasādhanam / praviśa gṛham ity asya yo 'rthaḥ padadvayavācyaḥ, sa praviśaśabdenocyate / tac ca sādhanaṃ kecid abhidheyatvena pratipannāḥ, kecid gamyamānatvena / piṇḍīpraveśasyāsaṃbhavāt yogyaṃ sādhanam abhidhīyate, gamyate vā / evaṃ piṇḍīm ity api piṇḍīṃ bhakṣayet ity asyārthasya vācakaḥ / (sādhanānām ity api?) sādhanaṃ nāntareṇa kriyām iti yogyāṃ kriyām upādatte / yathā dadhighaṭādiṣu pūrṇādayaḥ kriyā upādīyante, evaṃ ‘saṃprasāraṇāt’ ity eṣa śabdo yogyāṃ kriyām upādatte / nirvṛttiś ca yogyeti saṃprasāraṇanirvṛttād iti etam artham ayam abhidadhāti vā saṃprasāraṇād iti śabdo 'tha vā gamyamāno 'arthaḥ / kutaḥ ? vākyārthe saṃjñini pūrvatvadīrghatvayor asaṃbhavāt / 

[9] padeṣu ca padaikadeśān / nanu ca svarūpādhiṣṭhānāḥ saṃjñā rūpāntare[ṇa] yuktāasamarthāḥ pratyāyana iti noccaritasya lopo 'sti / kiṃ ca, yadā mātāpitṛbhyāṃ tatraiva kṛtaṃ nāmadheyam / tatra kecid evaṃ pratipannā---ekadeśa uccarite samudāyānumānam, tasmād arthaptatipattir iti / anye manyante---samudāye 'vayavāv api staḥ---deva iti, datta iti / tau ca samudāyavat saṃjñātvena saṃbaddhāv iti / anye manyante---anuniṣpādīni saṃjñāntarāṇi evaitāni, teṣāṃ tu lopadvāreṇānuniṣpādinaḥ evaṃbhūtāḥ sādhavo na tu drākhā evaṃbhūtā iti lopam ārabhamāṇasya pāṇiner matiḥ / yathā ca saṃjñā anuniṣpādinyaḥ, evaṃ sati nārthā anuniṣpadyante / vākyārthe saṃjñini yaṇaḥ sthāne [ik] iti [nirvṛttā] ikaḥ saṃjñitve nānuniṣpadyante / saṃprasārananirvṛtteḥ saṃprasāraṇanirdeśāt pratipattir bhavati iti / 

[10] atha vā, punar āhāyām iti / atraitat pradarśayati---yathā ‘brāhmaṇaśataṃ bhojyatām’ iti bhujiviśeṣa [e]vāyam, evaṃ saṃkhya bhohitā bhavati yadi saṃkheyā bhuñjate / yathā ‘vyāghno bhojyatāṃ, hastī bhojyatām’ iti bhujiviśeṣā evāmī / [evaṃ] ‘saṃprasāraṇasya dīrghatvaṃ bhavati’, ‘pūrvarūpatvaṃ bhavatī’ty evaṃbhūtam eva dīrghakaraṇaṃ vā pūrvarūpakaranaṃ vā yatkriyāphale varṇe kriyata iti / 

[11] atha vā, punar astu varṇasya . . . bhāvinī[yaṃ] saṃjñā vijñāsyate / kiṃ saṃjñāsāṃjñisaṃbandhe saṃjñāyā bhāvitvam atha pradeśeṣu ? yadi tāvat saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhe, bhūtānāṃ saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhena bhavitavyam / atha pradeśeṣu, asyaiva nirvṛttau varṇo dīrghavidhau pūrvavidhau ceti kuto bhāvitvaṃ kalpyate ? ucyate / yathaivetaretarāśrayadoṣo vṛddhisaṃjñāyām uktaḥ ‘sato vṛddhyādiṣu saṃjñābhāvāt’ (A 1.1.1, vā 8) iti, yadi saṃjñākāle saṃjñino bhūtatvena bhāvitvena vāpekṣyante, yatra tu tadāśrayā ākārādayaḥ ‘mṛjer vṛddhiḥ’ (A 7.2.114) iti tatretaretarāśrayam / evam iha saṃjñākāle igyaṇa iti saṃjñinām anapekṣya(? -kṣayā) bhūte[ṣv i]va pravṛttā saṃjñā / iha tu ‘ṣyaṅaḥ saṃprasāraṇam . . .’ (A 6.1.13) iti saṃprasāranaśabdaḥ kathaṃ pravartate ? na tāvat ya iko nirvṛttās ta iha [ādeśāḥ] śakyāḥ kartum, tad upakṣayatvāt teṣām; nāpi tadākṛtayo viśeṣābhāvāt / na hīndrādiṣu ya ikāro yaś ca yanaḥ sthāne ya[ig] iti tasya bhavati (nāsti?) viśeṣaḥ iti kiṃ pratipadyāmahe ?

saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhe yo bhāvī sa bhūta evam upātta iti / tatra ‘ṣyaṅaḥ saṃprasāranam’ ity etad uktaṃ bhavati---ṣyaṅaḥ tathābhūtam ādeśaṃ kuru yaḥ saṃprasāraṇam ity etāṃ saṃjñāṃ pratipadyate / etatsaṃjñāyogyasyādeśasya karaṇopāyo 'yam / yathā ‘asya sūtrasya śāṭakaṃ vay’ iti saṃtānavacane vayatau śāṭa[ka]śabdo 'sati śāṭake pravartamāno bhāvinaṃ saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandhaṃ buddhau kṛtvā bhūtatvenopātta iti pratīyate / asati hy arthe śabdaviśeṣaprayogo viśiṣṭārthapratipattyartha ity etatpratipadyate---ya utā etām ākhyāṃ pratipatsyante, tathābhūtaṃ saṃtānam ārabhasveti /

[12] ijādiyajādipravṛttir iti / buddhivipariṇām amātraṃ sthānyādeśabhāvo, yathā ‘prācīnaṃ grāmādāmrāḥ’ iti / loke hīṣṭaṃ, yaṣṭavyam iti ijādiyajādiyajādipravṛttir asti / śāstāt tu yaja, ḍuvapa, vaheti ijādibuddhir nivarti(? -rta)te, praṣavigṛṇa(?) ‘vacisv api . . . ’ (A 6.1.15) ity anena viśiṣṭe viṣaye janyate kiti ṅitīk sādhur bhavati iti / ya eva loke dvedhā prayogo 'vasthitaḥ, sa sādhur bhavatīti evtāvat śāstreṇānvākhyāyate iti / 

tatra sādhvabhimatasyeti granthaḥ / ta evaṃ varṇayanti---sādhūnām abhimatasyeti / ijādirūpasya śāstreṇa kiti ṅiti ca pratipattiḥ kriyate iti / 

etasmiṃs tu pakṣe aduhitarām ity avyāvṛttam / tathā vibhaktiviśeṣanirdeśāt yadā dve sūtre tadāpy avyāvṛttam iti / 

ādyantau ṭakitau // 1.1.46 //

[1] samāsanirdeśo 'yam / tatra na jñāyate iti / kim anenādhigatam ? yathāsaṃkhyakatvaṃ samāsanirdeśe na jñāyate, yāvatā asamāse 'pi devadattasya yajñadattasya cājā dhanam avayaś ceti na jñāyate kasyāvayaḥ kasyājāḥ iti / 

[2] ucyate / etad anena prakāreṇa pratipādayati---loke śāstrāntareṣu ca nāsti yathāsaṃkhyam, vyākaraṇa eva kevalam iti / kramaś ca niyamyate---

      śruter arthāc ca pāṭhāc ca pravṛtteś ca manīṣiṇaḥ /
      sthānān mukhyāc ca dharmāṇām āhuḥ śrutavidaḥ kramāt //

śruteḥ kramāhuḥ ‘hṛdayasyāgre 'vadyati, atha jihvāyā, atha vakṣasaḥ’ (Taittirīya Saṃhitā 6.3.10.4, Śatapatha-Brāhmaṇa 3.8.3.16, 17) / athaśabdo 'nantarārthasya dyo[ta]kaḥ śrūyate, tatredaṃ kṛtvā idaṃ kartavyam iti kramapravṛttiḥ / 

arthakramo---yadāpy evam ucyate---devadattaṃ bhojaya, snāpayānulimpor dvatayābhyañjayeti, arthāt kramo niyamyate 'bhyañjanam udvartanaṃ snāpanam anulepanaṃ bhojanam iti / 

pāṭhakramo niyatānupūrvīkeṣu vedavākyeṣv anekārthopādāne uddeśinām anudeśināṃ ca sakṛd arthitvena vyavatiṣṭhate / yathā smṛtau parimārjanapradāhanatakṣaṇanirṇejanāni taijasamārttikadāravatāntavānām ity evam / anye manyante---anekaś ca yadaikārthe upādīyate devadattaṃ [ca] yajñadattaṃ ca viṣṇumitraṃ ca bhojayeti vivṛtenaiva(?) krameṇa bhujiṃ pratipadyante / tatra yadā darśanaṃ bhinne saṃbandhiny upādīyamāne 'py asyedam asyedam ity anena prakāreṇa śakyā yathāsvaṃ pratipattiḥ kartum, yathā ghno jighnaḥ, dhmo dhama iti / saṃjñayā samāsanirdeśo tu saṃjñāyā akramāḥ saṃjñino yugapatpratipadyamānāḥ sarvair anudeśibhiḥ saṃ[ba]dheran / evaṃ samāsanirdeśe 'pi samudā[yā]tmaka eko 'rtho 'bhinno dvaṃdvenocyate iti idam uktaṃ vākyakāreṇa ‘saṃjñāsamāsanirdeśāt sarvaprasaṅgo 'nudeśasya’ / tatra yathāsaṃkhyād evaṃ pratipadyeran iti tipo ṇal bhavati, tasaḥ atus bhavati, ity ataḥ samāsanirdeśo na kalpaśakyaḥ (?) saṃbandhayitum ity etad ācaṣṭe samāsanirdeśo 'yam iti / 

[3] yady api [tāvat] loka eṣa dṛṣṭāntaḥ yady api loke darśanam / dṛṣṭāntasyāpi tu darśanasyāpi tu / puruṣārambha iti / apūrvam idam aprasiddhaṃ loke ārambhasādhyam iti kathyate / 

[4] kau punaḥ ṭakitau bhavataḥ iti / etat pṛcchati---yasya ṭakitau [a]bhidhīyete kau tau tasya bhavataḥ ? kiṃbhūtaḥ ādir bhavati, anto vā ? āgamāv ity āha / avasthita ārdhadhātuke paścād vidhīyamāno 'vayavāntaram anivartayann avayavatvaṃ pratipadyate ity etad ācaṣṭe / 

[5] yuktaṃ punaḥ [yan] nityeṣv iti / śāstrasiddhānto 'yaṃ na bhavatīty etat bravīti / atha kenacit prakāreṇa itavya evāyaṃ svabhāvataḥ, kim anena iṭ bhavatīti ? atha yuktaṃ yann ity eṣu śabdeṣv iti / yathaivāsti[mapamṛdya bhavatiṃ] pratipadyase nitye 'pi śabda, evam āgamam api pratipadyasv eveti / śabdāntarair iha bhavitavyam iti / naivāstim apamṛdya bhavatiḥ kriyate / kiṃ tarhi ? asteḥ prasaṅge ity arthaḥ /

[6] ādeśās tarhīme bhaviṣyantīti / naiveyaṃ paribhāṣā / saṃjñāsaṃjñisaṃbandho 'yam---ādyantayoṣṭakārakakārāvitau saṃjñe bhaviṣyata iti / ādyarthasyāntārthasya ca saṃjñā, arthapadārthayor evopādānāt / ‘iti’śabdo vā svartate / yady evaṃ, ṭakārakakārayoḥ [it]saṃjñā na prāpnoti / na hīdānīṃ ṭakārakaārāvādyantau bhavataḥ / itsaṃjñau ca saṃjñeti pratijñātam / tatretsaṃjñakānāṃ yo deśas taddeśatvād iddharmeṇa vyapadiṣṭau nivṛttisāmānyāc ca yathetsaṃjño nivartate evam etāv api iti / tena iḍ iti yaṣṭakāraḥ tenādyartha upasthāpyaḥ ity etad uktaṃ bhavati iṭ, bhavatīti / sarvasmiṃś cādeśe ikārādau prasakte sthāne 'ntaratamatvāt tavyasya itavyo bhavatīti / 

[7] iha tu kathaṃ— . . . aḍudāttaḥ (A 6.4.71) iti ? evaṃ manyate — aḍ iti bahuvrīhau sati akārādir ādeśa iti / atrāṅgasya pratyāyanāt udāttaśrutor aṅge saṁpati / evaṃ ca satyaniyatodāttam aṅgaṃ prāpnoti iti / tripado 'yaṃ bahuvrīhir iti vākya evākāra udāttaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ pratipadyate—akāraḥ udātta ādir asyeti / tena vṛttāv api tat śakyānugamaṃ bhavatīti yathā citragur iti / yatra tarhi anuvṛttyā / na hi tatra śabda uccāryate / kiṃ tarhi ? anumīyate, ity abhiprāyaḥ / yadā tv idaṃ darśanaṃ tatsadṛśasya śabdāntarasyānubhānāt vākyāntaram upajāyate iti tadā na doṣaḥ, atra ca hetuvākyam idam ‘āḍajādīnām’ (A 6.4.72) ity evaṃbhūtam idaṃ vākyam āḍudātto 'jādīnām iti / 

[8] atha vā, yattāvadayaṃ sāmānyena śaknoty upadeṣṭum iti paribhāṣāpakṣe 'pi samādhim āha / ihāṃsti kecit sakāramātram upadiśya pitsu aḍāgamaṃ vidadhatim kecit akārasya lopam apitsu vacaneṣu / evam ayam ācāryaḥ tavyādivalādyārdhadhātuka[m] udā[ttā]bhyaś cānudāttābhyaś ca prakṛtibhya upadiśya udāttābhya iṭaṃ vidadhadetat pratipādayati — udāttebhyo dhātubhya itavyaḥ pratyaya iti / evaṃ ca kṛtvāha (tena) tena viśeṣeṇa śabdāntaraṃ samudāyaṃ pratipadyate iti / samudāyagrahaṇam evam artham---na tavyādāv avasthite paścād ikāra āgacchatīti / kiṃ tarhi ? samudāya evekāraviśiṣṭa iti / tad yathā khadirabarburayor iti / yat sāmānyaṃ manyase [i]ty etad apekṣā ṣaṣṭhī / kiṃ punas tayoḥ sāmānyam ? khadirabarburau gaurakāṇḍau sūkṣmaparṇāv iti / samudāyam eva viśiṣṭaṃ pratipadyanta ity etad ācaṣṭe / evam ihāpīkāro nādhīyate nāpy apanīyate ārdhadhātukāntarāt, kiṃ tu kasyāś cit prakṛter ita[vyaḥ], kasyāś cit prakṛtes ta[vya] iti / 

[9] atha vaitayānupūrvyā . . . upaśiśati iti / sarvāpalavam (?) evedānīm upanyasyati / yo 'yaṃ vibhāgo bhavatāśritaḥ pūrvaṃ prakṛtiḥ paścāt pratyayas tata i[ḍ] iti, sarvam etat pratipādanopāyamātra, na hi bhūta (? bhūtārtha) eva iti / bhūvādayaḥ kecid api [na] bhavanti prayogaśabdasya bhavatyādeḥ, śapi vartamāne laḍ ity evamādiḥ / kiṃ tarhi ? upāyā ete prayogasya / na ca śāstra upāyanityatā pratijñātā / kiṃ tarhi ? prayoganityatā pratijñātā / na ca prayogopanipātini samudāye eṣa vibhāgo 'sti / pūrvaṃ prakṛtiḥ paścāt pratyayas tata iḍāgama ity etat pūrvakaṃ tu samudāyaṃ pratipadayate / saṃjñepata etat pradarśayati---anṛtena satyaṃ pratipadyasv iti /

[10] ṭakitorādyantavidhāne pratyayapratiṣedhaḥ / ‘ādyantavidhāna’ iti śāstraṃ saṃbadhyate ‘ādyantau ṭakitau’ iti / ādyanto vā āditvasya vidhāne ṭitvakitvayoḥ = pratyayasya ṭitaḥ ṭitvasya vā pratiṣedho vaktavyaḥ ‘ādyantau ṭakitāv apratyayāv’ iti / paravacanāt siddham / paravacanād iti ‘vipratiṣedhe param’ ity asmāt paravacanāt pratyayasya paratvaṃ bhaviṣyati / ādyantāv ity asyāvakāśa ‘iṭ valādeḥ’ (A 7.2.35), ‘bhiyo hetubhaye ṣuk’ (A 7.3.40) iti / pratyayaparatvasyāpi ‘tavyattavyārnīrā[ra]ḥ’ (A 3.1.93) iti / ihobhayaṃ prāpnoti ‘careṣ ṭaḥ’ (A 3.2.16), ‘āto 'nupasarge kaḥ’ (A 3.2.3) iti / atha vā, paravacanād iti ‘pratyayaḥ’, ‘paraḥ’ (A 3.1.1,2) ity asmād eva paravacanāt vipratiṣedhopasaṃhitāt ity evaṃ varṇyate / atha vā, paravacanād iti yaḥ paraḥ sa pratyayasaṃjñaḥ, sati ca pratyayaparatve ṭakārakakārayor itsaṃjñā, satyāṃ cetsaṃ[jñā]yāṃ ṭitkidvyapadeśaḥ, ṭitkidvyapadeśe satyāditvamantavyaṃ ceti nāsti pra[tya]yasya āditvamantavyaṃ vā / 

evaṃ tu bahujādīnāṃ pratyayatvaṃ vyāhanyeta / tasmān  na paratvapratibaddhaḥ pratyayaḥ / tena pareṇa bhavitavyam; asyāpavādaḥ purastāt, prāk ṭeḥ, acaḥ para iti / anye varṇayanti—paścamyā paratve siddhe punaḥ paragrahaṇam evam artham, bādhanārtham / tena paratvāpavādaḥ ādyantau ṭakitāv iti punaḥ paragrahaṇāt bādhyate / tasmāt pratyayaḥ paro bhaviṣyatīti / vipratiṣedhas tu yathoddeśaṃ saṃjñāparibhāṣam ity etasmin pakṣe uktaḥ, kāryakāle tu ‘careṣ ṭaḥ’ ity ekavākyabhāvāt pūrvaparatvaṃ durlabham eva / 

[11] paravacanāt siddham iti cet nāpavādatvāt / evaṃ manyate---ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśe vā ṭikkitau, pratyayādhikāre vā / tatra ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśe ‘ṣaṣṭhī sthāneyogā’ (A 1.1.49) ity etad asti / itaratrāpi pratyayaparatvam / evaṃ ca sati nāprāpte '[nya]sminn idam ārabhyate iti yathaiva sthāneyogatvaṃ bādhata evaṃ pratyayaparatvam api / dṛṣṭāntaṃ copādatte---tad yathā ‘mid aco 'ntyāt para’ ity ayaṃ yogo ‘napuṃsakasya jhalacaḥ’ (A 7.1.72) iti, ‘rudhādibhyaḥ śnam’ (A 3.1.78) iti ca pravartamānaṃ sthāneyogaṃ pratyayaparatvaṃ ca bādhate / 

[12] viṣama upanyāsaḥ / iha yady api lakṣaṇam asaṃdigdhaṃ, yat tu nimittaṃ tasya saṃdehād apavādo 'pi saṃdigdhaprāptitvād durbalo 'numīyate / tathā coktaṃ ‘tātaṅi ṅitkaraṇasya sāvakāśatvam’ (tu. pā. 1.1.53, vā 2) iti / ṅittvaṃ hi sarvādeśāpavādaḥ / yat [tu] tātaṅo ṅittvaṃ tat saṃdihya[te], prayojanāntarasya saṃbhavāt / guṇapratiṣedhārtham api ṅittvaṃ saṃbhavati iti saṃdihyate---kim asya ṅittvaṃ guṇanivṛttyartham athāntyapratipattyartham ? nimitte saṃdihyamāne saṃdigdhanimittaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ yathānaṅādiṣu evaṃ tātaṅi na pravarteta, iti dvayoḥ saṃdihyamānayoḥ vipratiṣedhaḥ uktaḥ / evam ihāpi ‘careṣ ṭaḥ’ iti yadi tannimittam ādyantaparibhāṣāyās tatsaṃdihyate, kimīkārārtham athādyartham, iti yathā iḍāgamādiṣu pravartata evam ihāpi pravartate, śramādīnāṃ tu vyavasthitaṃ prayojanāntarābhāvād asaṃdigdham iti nimittaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ cāsaṃdigdhe tataś cāpavādatvaṃ yuktam itīdam āha---viṣama upanyāsa iti /

[13] prayojanaṃ nāma [tad] vaktavyaṃ yan niyogataḥ syāt tatorayojanam / sa (?) ceha nimittaṃ yad anapekṣya kiṃcit pravartate, yathā ‘kamer ṇiṅ’ (A 3.1.30) iti ṅitkaraṇam ātmanepadārthaṃ, vṛddhipratiṣedhārthaṃ, sāmānyagrahaṇāvighātārthaṃ vānyanirapekṣaṃ pravartate / ṇakāraḥ sāmānyagrahaṇe vṛddhau ca / evam iha tātaṅo ṅittvaṃ na ṅittvamātrād eva guṇapratiṣedhe pravartate / kīṃ tarhi ? sati sarvādeśe / evaṃ ṭittvasyāpi prayojanaṃ śakyaṃ vaktuṃ tadā yadi paraḥ, saṃbhavati tataṣṭittvam īkārasya nimittam iti / yasmāt prayojanāt saṃdigdhatvaṃ ṭittvasya parigṛhyate, tad eva prayojanaṃ paratvapratibaddham ity ataḥ saṃdehanimittam ity asaṃdehāt paratvam anena bādhitavyam iti idam ucyate---nāpavādatvād iti / 

[14] kathaṃ kṛtvedam ucyate---‘paravacanāt siddham’ iti ? ṭita īkāraprayojanasaṃbhavaṃ dṛṣṭvā / saṃbhavatīdaṃ prayojanaṃ parabhūte 'vatiṣṭhamāne ṭitkaranasya, yadi labhyeta kathaṃcit paratvam iti saṃbhavamātram āśrityedaṃ prastutam / itaratra tvatyantaparatve mittvam aparudhyate / yadā tu tāvatkālā pravṛtir āśrīyate, na prayojanasaṃbhavāt, tadānupajātam eva nimittam īkārasya / sa hi paratvapratibaddhaḥ / tasmān na paratvapratipattir itaretarāśrayatvāt / idam evāpratibaddhaṃ sati nimitte ṭittve kim iti na pravartiṣyata iti [idam] uktaṃ ‘prayojanaṃ nāma [tad] vaktavyam’ iti / 

atha vā, paracanāt siddham iti---ṭita īkāro bhavatīty evaṃ tatrāvasthitaṃ ṭidantād iti / yadi ca pratyaya ādiḥ syāt, ṭidantābhāvād īkāra eva na syāt / tasmād vaiparītyenedam eva pratayaparatvaṃ bādhakaṃ syād iti / ayaṃ tūbhayavantam āśritya bravīti---‘prayojanaṃ nāma [tad] vaktavyam’ iti / etam evārtham anena bhāṣyeṇa kathayati---kutas tv etat ṭitkaraṇādayaṃ paro bhaviṣyati, na punar ādiḥ iti / kutaḥ prayojanasaṃbhava āśrīyate, na punas tāvat kālaḥ pravṛttisaṃbhavaḥ ? ekāntenāśrīyamāṇe ṭita īkāravacanasāmarthye ṭitaḥ khalv api eṣa parihāraḥ ṭita evety arthaḥ / tatrāditvaṃ paratvapravirodhīti vipratiṣedha upapadyate / 

[15] kitastv aparihāraḥ [avayavarūpe]ṇotpattau liṅgaṃ cāsti ‘neṭi’ (A 7.2.4) iti pratiṣedhaḥ / samīpavacane hi vyavadhānād evāprāptiḥ syāt / na vāvayavaḥ samudāyāt paraḥ iti śakyaḥ pratipattum / atha samāsāntāḥ kathaṃ samāsaikadeśāḥ santaḥ pratyayaparatvaṃ pratipadyante ? evam ihāpi dhātum avasthāpya tataḥ paro 'yam iti pratipadyasva, asyāyam antaḥ bhavatv iti / tatrāyaṃ doṣaḥ---ādhyoḥ pradhyor iti kiṃ dhātv ekadeśa iti dhātuyaṇam iti vibhakty udāttatvaṃ pratiṣidhyeta, yathā khalapva iti / evam ādhī iti / ‘eranekāca . . . ’ (A 6.4.82) iti yaṇādeśaḥ syāt / 

[16] ṭi[ta]ś cāpy aparihāraḥ / prayojanasaṃbhave 'pi parigṛhīte yaś ca syād ita prati (?) / kvāyaṃ syād iti / 

ya ubahyavān ‘gāpoṣ ṭak’ iti / atra hi ādyantayor nimitte ṭi[ttva]kittve staḥ / tatra ṭidādir ity asyāvakāśaḥ ‘iṅ valādeḥ’ ( 7.2.35) iti / kidanta ity asyāpi ‘bhiyo hetubhaye ṣuk’ (A 7.3.40) / ihobhayaṃ prāpnoti ‘gāpoṣ ṭak’ iti śabdaparavipratiṣedhād anta ity etat bhavati / tataś ca ṭidantād īkāraḥ siddhaḥ / careṣ ṭādiṣu paratvaṃ nāstīty aparihāro 'yaṃ ‘paravacanāt siddham’ iti / 

[17] siddhaṃ tu ṣaṣṭhyadhikāre vacanāt (vā. 3) / sthāneṣaṣṭhyadhikāre cāyaṃ yogo vaktavya iti / yatra sthānaṣaṣṭhī tatra ṭitkitau ādyantau bhavataḥ / pratyayādhikāre yā ṣaṣṭḥī paratvena virodhāt iti gāpoṣ tag iti atrāpi nāsty antasya pravṛttiḥ / 

[18] ādyantayor vā ṣaṣṭhyarthatvāt . . . (vā. 4) iti / arthaśabdo nābhidheyavacanaḥ, na hy ādyarthaḥ ṣaṣṭhyabhidheyaḥ / tasmāt prayojanavacano 'rthaśabdaḥ / ādyantayoḥ ṣaṣṭhīprayojanatvāt tadabhāve ṣaṣṭhyabhāve pratipadyāmahe---ādyantau nūnaṃ prayojakaṃ nāsti, yena prayojyā ṣaṣṭhī prakḷpyeteti / 

itaraḥ ṣaṣṭhī yasmān nāsti tasmād ādyartho 'pi nāstīti bruvatā śabdapūrvo 'rtho 'bhyupagato bhavatīty āha---yuktaṃ punar yat śabdanimittako nāmeti / itaraḥ śabdābhāvo 'rthābhāvasyānumānam arthapūrvaka eva tu śabda ity etad ācaṣṭe---arthanimittaka eva śabda iti / yadi cādyantārhaḥ prakrānto 'bhaviṣyat ṣaṣṭhīm evodacārayiṣyat / tasmād eva pratipattavyam---ādyantau ṭakitāv etadvarjam iti / kiṃ ca ṣaṣṭhī pratyakṣapakṣaḥ ādyarthapratipattau, anumānapakṣo 'yaṃ ṭittvāt kittvāc ceti / tasmād yuktam ucyate ‘tadabhāve 'saṃpratyayaḥ’ (vā. 4) iti / yatra tu pratyayavidhau ṣaṣṭhy asti, sāpi pañcamyarthe / atha vā, supāṃ supo bhavantīti / atha vā, gāpor yo dhātus tasmāt, kapijñātyor yat prātipadikaṃ tasmāt iti pratyayotpādikā pañcamy eva, na ṣaṣṭhī / 

mid aco 'ntyāt paraḥ // 1.1.47 //

[1] kim artham idam ucyate . . . sthānaparapratyayāpavādaḥ / parasya pratya[ya]syāpavāda iti dharmapratiṣedho, na dharmipratiṣedhaḥ / para ity evaṃ vā yaḥ pratyayo = yat saṃvijñānam, tasya pratiṣedho 'nena vijñānenāntyād acaḥ para iti / guṇavacano vā paraśabdaḥ antareṇa bhāvapratyayam / pratyayasya paratvaṃ pratyayapara iti / rājadantāditvāt pūrvanipātaḥ / bhāṣyakāras tu akṣarārtham anādṛtyārthaṃ vyācaṣṭe pratyayaparatvasya cāpavāda iti / 

[2] kimarthaṃ punar idaṃ noktaṃ purastād avapāvā iti ? idaṃ ca siddham evaṃ syāt pratyayau ṭitkitau parau bhavati, na punar ādyantau iti / sthāneyogatvasya bādhitatvān na pratyayaparatvasya / evaṃ manyate---nyāyo 'yaṃ katam ? śāstrāpekṣāyāṃ pravṛttau bhedena ca vyapekṣāyāṃ yaḥ prathamas tasminn eva pravartitavyam, uttarasya ca vṛttānto na tāvat jñāyata iti / prayogāpekṣāyāṃ samudāyāpekṣāyāṃ vā prayoge paurvāparyābhāvāt, samudāyasya cānantaryād idaṃ nāsti purastād iti vānantara iti vā / evaṃ madhya iti pūrvasminn apūrvasmin upalabdhe kakṣāpravṛttiḥ, na tu parasminn anupalabdha iti / 

[3] ayaṃ ced a[tra] nyāyo nāsti, kutaḥ ‘śnamo mitkaraṇāt’ iti ? [śnamo] mittvaṃ kuto nirjñātam ? prayogāt---ruṇaddhi, bhinattīti / evaṃ hy uktaṃ ‘vttajño jy ācāryo 'nubandhānāsajati’ (MBh. 1.266.20--21) iti / evaṃ ‘ḍityabhasyāpy anubandhakaranasāmarthyāt’ (MBh 3.225.15) iti / atha vā, śāstram apratibaddhaṃ saṃjñāṃ pratipādayati / tatpūrvakaś ca lopaḥ pravṛṭtaḥ / kiṃ punar asya prayojanam iti vicāraṇāyām idaṃ pratipadyate---viśiṣṭadeśatvam adhikāraprāptaṃ paratvam apakṛṣya ‘mid aco 'ntyāt paraḥ’ ity etatsaṃbadhyate ity anena darśanena pūrvatrāyaṃ yogo dṛṣṭāntatvenopanyasto ‘yathā mid aco antyāt para ity eṣa yogaḥ’ iti /

[4] kuṇḍāni vanāni / ‘napuṃsakasya jhalacaḥ’ (A 7.1.72) iti / tatra yadi napuṃsakasyety etad uktaṃ bhavati---yathā ‘ruhaḥ paḥ’ (A 7.3.43) iti ruher hasya ity evaṃ prāpte sthāneyogatve 'yam apavāda ārabhyate / atra vicaryāte---ekaiveyaṃ ṣaṣṭhī napuṃsakasya iti / tatra yadi napuṃsakasyety ajviśeṣaṇārthā ṣaṣṭhī, nāsti sthāneyogatvam / atha sthāneyogatvam, aco viśeṣaṇatvaṃ naiva saṃbhavati / yathā mida igviśeṣaṇe nāsti sthāneyogatvam / satyam, evam etat / yadā tv etāv adupādīyate, napuṃsakasyājantasyācaḥ paro bhavatīti, na punar aṅgasyeti etatsaṃbandhinaḥ anena kiṃcid apekṣitaṃ, na tā sthāneyogatvam asti śabdopādānam, saṃnidhānāt tv aṅgasyety etatsaṃbandhinam acamantyaṃ pratipadyate / 

[5] bhaved idaṃ yuktam udāharaṇaṃ kuṇḍānīti / atra hy aci nivartite saṃbhavo nāsti yad aco 'ntyāt paraḥ syāt / iha tv asti saṃbhavaḥ payāṃsīti / nivartya sakāram antyād acaḥ paraḥ syād iti / 

[6] naiveśvaraś cājñāpayatīti / śāstre kācid ārabdhā bādhā ‘vipratiṣedhe param’ (A 1.4.2) iti / kācin nevārabdhā ‘nityam anityaṃ bādhata’ iti / iyaṃ ca ‘pakṣe 'ntaraṅgaṃ balīyo bhavatīti’/ iyam api nārabdhā śāstre, lo[ke] hy ārabdhā syāt iti / loke 'pi tv anārabdhā / naiveśvara ājñāpayatī[ti] dṛṣṭārthatvam ācaṣṭe / īśvaravacanaṃ dṛṣṭam evārthaṃ vadhādim uddiśyānutiṣṭhanti / dharmasūtrakārāṇāṃ tv adṛṣṭam artham idaṃ bhakṣyam idam abhakṣyam idam anena vyācaṣṭe / 

[7] kiṃ tarhi ? laukiko 'yaṃ dṛṣṭānta iti / laukikam etat pradarśanam iti / darśanaṃ dṛṣṭānta ity āha / atha vā, yad vakṣyamāṇaṃ tasya pratinirdeśaḥ---tad yathā dadhi brāhmaṇebhyo dīyatām iti / loke hy arthaprakaraṇāntareṇāpi yaḥ kaścid ukto ‘dadhi brāhma[ṇe]bhyo dīyatāṃ takraṃ kauṇḍinyāya’ iti, na jātu jaścit kauṇḍinyāya dadhi dadāti / tasmā[d] darśanam eva hetuḥ / nātropapattir vaktavyā, yathāgner auṣṇyaṃ nopapattita ucyate / 

[8] upapattimad [a]pi tu kriyate / (1) iha tu ‘dadhi brāhmaṇebhya’ iti yo brāhmaṇaśabdaḥ sa kiṃ kauṇḍinye tātparyeṇa pravṛtto 'tha nāntarīyakatvāt, yathā ‘grāmaṃ gacchan kuḍyamūlam upamarda’ ity evaṃ pravṛtta iti / takradāne tu tātparyeṇopātta iti / yasmiṃś ca nāntarīyakatvaṃ yasmiṃś ca tātparyam, na [ta]yoḥ samudāyo vā vikalpo [vā] yuktaḥ iti / (2) atha vā, brāhmaṇaśabdaḥ kauṇḍinye saṃdihyate / asti hi brāhmaṇaśabdaḥ kauṇḍinyopagrāhyanyasyāpy upagrāhī / tatra ko 'tra prayukta iti saṃdihyate / saṃdigdhāsāṃdigdhayoś ca tulyavat prasaṃkhyānānupapattiḥ / (3) atha vā, brāhmaṇaśabdasya na padārthaḥ kauṇḍinyaḥ, rūpaṃ tu saṃbhavati, kauṇḍinyaśabdasya tu padārtha eva / na ca pratyakṣānumānikayoḥ samakakṣatvaṃ yuktam iti /

[8.1] atraivocyate---na kauṇḍinyaprayuktaṃ dadhidānam iti brūmaḥ / kiṃ tarhi ? brāhmaṇaprayuktam / asti kauṇḍinye pravṛttiḥ brāhmaṇyasaṅgāt tātparyeṇāṃsaṃhatadenaśabdārthayo ca(?) / tatra yathaiva kauṇḍinyatvāt takraṃ pratipadyata evaṃ dadhya api brāhmaṇyāt kathaṃ na pratipadyate ? iti / 

[8.2] (4) ato 'prāptyanumānaṃ bādhety eva bādhālakṣaṇaṃ parigṛhyate naiyāyikaiḥ / aprāptiś ca nopajanyate svabhāvāt / aprāptau vidhir anumīyate / tad yathā---dadhi brāhmaṇebhyo dīyatām, kauṇḍinyāya na dātavyam iti / sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvena dadātir nivṛttiś ca śrūyate / yadi brāhmaṇyāt dadātiḥ pravartate ekāntena, pratiṣedhaśrutir bādhitā / atha vikalpena, kadāci[d] dīyate kadācin na dīyate, pakṣe nivṛttir bādhyate / samudāyenāśakyā pratipattiḥ dadātinivṛttyor virodhād iti pratiṣedhaprāpter anumānam / yady api dvāv a[pi] vidhī-sāmānye viśeṣe [ca]---dadhi brā[hma]ṇebhyas takraṃ kauṇḍinyāyeti, tadāpi yathā dhūmavato dhūmo 'num ito(-tau) [a]numānatvenāvatiṣṭhate, evaṃ nivṛttivākye sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvo 'sti / tasmāt atrāpy aprāptyanumānam iti aprāptiḥ pratipadyate /

[8.3] (5) atha vā, dadhi brāhmaṇebhyo dīyatām ity ākṛtāvanyūnāyām aparipūrṇāyāṃ [ca] śabdaḥ pravartamānaḥ kauṇḍinye vikale 'pi pravartata iti dadhy api prāpnotīty etac cintya[m] / tatra yathaiva ‘brāhmaṇā bhojyantām’ ity atra māṭharakauṇḍinyau pariveviṣṭām iti pariveṣaṇarūpeṇocyamānau nānumānaśabdāt bhojane 'ntarbhāvataḥ, evaṃ takrakarmakaḥ (? -ke dāne) kauṇḍinyaḥ saṃpradānatvenocyamāno nāntarbhavati dadhidāna iti / 

[8.4] (6) evaṃ tāva[d] dvau dadātī iti kṛtvoktam / anye manyante---eka evāyaṃ dadātir bhinnakarmako bhinnasaṃpradānakaś ca / yasyāṃ cāvasthāyāṃ dadāter ekā bhāgo dadhnā viṣyate (?), tadaivaiko bhāgas takreṇeti / śabdaḥ pra(? -bdapra)vṛttikāle nāsti viccheda itīdam avasīyate tyaktaḥ kauṇḍinyo dadhidāna iti / ayaṃ tu paramārthaḥ---eka evāyam artho (?) loke vidyamāno 'deśavibhāgaḥ padārthavad upacaryate / ayaṃ ca lokasya vākyārthaḥ---kauṇḍinyaṃ varjayitvānyebhyo brāhmaṇebhyo dadhi dīyatām, kauṇḍinyāya tu takram iti / 

[9] antyāt pūrvo masjeḥ (vā. 2) / aco 'ntyāt mid ukta ity apurvam idam ārabhyate, bahūnāṃ ca saṃyogasaṃjñā ity asmin pakṣe idam ucyate / kim artham udāgaraṇadvayam upāttaṃ bhaṅktā iti ca magna iti vā ? yady api anuṣaṅgalope yatnaḥ kriyeta---anupadhāsthasya ca masher iti, tathāpi magna iti siddhaṃ syāt, idaṃ tu na sidhyati maṅkteti / 

[10] bharjim arcyoś ca (vā. 3) iti / ūmīm iti / marīciśabdo bāhvādiṣu iti / avaśyamayaṃ paṭhitavyaḥ, marīcer apatyaṃ mārīcir iti / bharujaśabdo 'py aṅgulyādiṣu bā[ba]hvādiṣv api, ‘bharuja candrabhāgā nādyām’ iti / tena bhavati bhārujiko bhāruhir iti (? bharujīti) / 

[11] svayam eva pakṣānupapādayati---yady anta iti vartate / tenācām antyāt paro 'nta ity etad vākyam upaskriyate / [paraḥ] sannantaḥ kim avayavasyāhosvit samudāyasyeti, yathāntime nigamayiṣyati---‘saṃghātabhakto 'sau notsahata . . . ’ iti, ato 'vasīyate saṃghātabhakto 'yam iti / 

athādir iti vartate, tataḥ parādiḥ / na hi saṃbhavatis ti(? -vo 'sti) yad aco 'ntyāt paraḥ syāt pūrvasya cādir iti ādyanuvṛttau parāditvam avasīyate iti / 

[11.1] (1) abhakte . . . kuṇḍāni vanāni, nāntam aṅgaṃ na saṃbhavatīti / nanu ca ‘tadādigrahaṇaṃ syādinumartham’ (A 1.4.13, vā. 1) ity uktam / asmiṃś ca pakṣe numarthaṃ [tat] smartavyam, pūrvānte tu siddham iti / parādāv api dīrghaḥ siddhaḥ, ‘ato dīrgho yañi’, ‘supi ca’ (A 7.3.101--102) iti vakṣyati / asthīnīti na sidhyati / 

(2) agne trīti / prātipadikānto nakāro na bhavati / chāndasaḥ puṃvadbhāvo bhaviṣyatiti, śibhāvo durlabhaḥ / atha varṇalopaḥ, tasya siddhatvān nopadhāyā iti dīrghatvaṃ na prāpnoti / śakyaṃ tu vaktuṃ madhye nāmāyaṃ dṛśyata ity atibahu smartavyam / 

(3) sarvāṇi jyotīṃṣi / numā supo vyavadhānā[d] ānantaryaṃ nāsti / pūrvāntatve sarvaśabdagrahaṇena grahaṇam, parādau subgrahaṇe[nā]ty ānantaryam / 

(4) kriyate nyāsa eva iti / etad uktaṃ bhavati---avaśyaṃ kartavyaṃ numgrahaṇam, yaḥ prātipadikanakāro na bhavati, nāpi vaibhaktas tadartham, prāhiṇvad ity evamartham / 

(5) dviṣaṃtapaḥ / prasaṅgenodāhṛtam ity evaṃ varṇayanti / ayaṃ hi tanmadhyapatitatvāt tadgrahaṇena grṛhyate / saṃyogāntalopaś cānusvāre 'pi siddhaḥ, idānīm ayaṃ [pa]dānta iti siddho 'nusvāraḥ / idaṃ tūdāharanaṃ paraṃtapa iti / 

(6) śībhāvaś ca na sidhyati / jatunī---nityatvāc ca paratvāc ca numi kṛte vyavadhānān napuṃsakasyāprāptiḥ śībhāvasyeti / 

[11.2] (1) parādau guṇavṛddhyautva . . . / . . . trapuṇe / ṅa idyasya so 'yaṃ ṅid ity asmin pa[kṣe] doṣo, yadā ṅ eva it ṅit iti tadā ‘varṇe yat syāt tac ca vidyāt tadādau’ iti ṅidādau bhavitavyam / 

(2) anusvāraś ca na sidhyati / atrāpi dvaṣaṃtapa ity anudāharaṇam / parāditve hi takāramayaṃ bhaktaḥ, sa ca padānta iti tadbhakto 'pi mum padāntavyapadeśaṃ lapsyate iti / 

(3) śībhāve nakārapratiṣedhaḥ / nakārasya śībhāve yā nivṛttis tasyāḥ pratiṣedho vaktavyaḥ / nirdiśyamānasyādeśā bhavantīti yathā ‘pādaḥ pad’ (A 6.4.130) ity atra pādantasya na bhavati / kiṃ tarhi ? karaṇasaṃnipātino rūpasya śrūyamānasya / evam ihāpi nauśabdyaukārāntasya na bhavati / kiṃ tarhi ? yad eva rūpamau iti  śrutisaṃnipāti tasyaiveti / yas tarhi nirdiśyate tasya . . . kasmān na bhavati ? numā vyavahitatvāt / śrutisaṃnipātino rūpa āśrīyamāṇe vyavahitatvād iti / tatrāyaṃ vākyārtho---nakārapratiṣedha iti nakāranimittaḥ pratiṣedho 'syeti / 

[11.3] pūrvānte napuṃsakopasarjanahrasvatvaṃ dvigusvaraś ca / napuṃsake numā saṃbhavād etāvad vaktavyaṃ napuṃsakahrasvatvam iti / ayaṃ tv abhiprāyaḥ---niṣkauśāmbinī ity atra paratvād upasarjanahrasvatvena bhavitavyam iti / nityatvān numi kṛte paratvād vopasarjanasyājantatā vihatā igantatā ceti hrasvasvarau na prāpnutaḥ / 

[11.3.1] na vā bahir aṅgalakṣaṇatvāt / hrasvatvaṃ vibhaktinirape[kṣam] / svare coktam---‘padagrahaṇaṃ parimāṇārtham’ (A 4.3.140, vā. 4) iti / num tu bāhyārthopādānāṃ vibhaktim apekṣata iti bahiraṅgaḥ / 

saṃghātabhakto 'sāv iti / samudāye rūpāntarayukte nāvayavāḥ svarūpaṃ jahati / yathā śabale 'vayavini nāvayavaḥ śuklavyapadeśaṃ kṛṣṇavyapadeśaṃ vā na labhate, evam aṅge nakārāntarūpe aratniśabdaḥ kim itīgantarūpatāṃ hāsyati ? svaraś cottarapade pūrvapadasyārabhyata, iti igantatvam avyāvṛttam iti siddhaḥ svaraḥ /

eca igghrasvādeśe // 1.1.48 //

[1.1] kimartham idam ucyate ? yasya yasya vacanam antareṇa so 'rthaḥ sidhayti, yasminn ārabhyamāṇe na sidhyati, prasaṅgadoṣo vā bhavati tatra [ta]tra praśnaḥ / iha tv ānarthakyāt praśnāḥ / yad vakṣyamāṇaṃ ‘siddham eṅaḥ sasthānatvāt’ (vā. 3), ‘aicoś cottarabhūyastvāt’ (vā. 4) iti /

[1.2] trayaś cātra prakriyābhedāḥ / nānāvākyānām ekavākyatā, bhūtaukadeśanivṛttiḥ, ekaśeṣavaca iti / etāṃś cottaratra bhāṣya eva darśayiṣyāmaḥ / 

[1.3] savarṇākāranivṛttyartham (vā. 1) eṅo hrasvaśāsaneṣv iti / praśliṣṭāva[rṇa]tvāt [eṅaḥ] savarṇāv ardhaikārārdhākārau sthātām, na tu viśli[ṣṭā]varṇasyaica ity ucyate / niyataviṣayāś ca varṇāḥ, yathā oṃvyāpāraḥ kasyāṃcit śākhāyāṃ naiva prayujyate, evam etāv ardhaikārārdhaukārāv anyatrāprayuktāv iha tu prayujyeyātām iti /

[1.4] imāv ecau samāhāravarṇāv iti / samāhārakriyamāṇavarṇāvayavatvāt samāhāravarṇāv iti vyapadeśāt / dvimātrāv aicau / mātrā avarṇasyeti / yo 'sāv aṣṭādaśadhā bhinnaḥ, tasya yā mātrā = ekadeśaḥ, so 'vayavo 'syeti / evam ivarṇovarṇayor api yojyam / tayor hrasvaśāsaneṣv iti / etad ācaṣṭe --- nivartakam idaṃ śāstraṃ, anyasya tu vidhāyakam iti / nanu vivṛta[ta]ratvād aicaḥ vivṛtā[vivṛtatarā]v idutau bhaviṣyataḥ, na tu saṃvṛto 'kāraḥ / tatra kim ucyate ‘kadācid avarṇaṃ syāt’ iti ? kathaṃ tāvad vivṛtatarasya vivṛtau bhavataḥ ? api ca sthānakṛtam āntaryaṃ balīyo bhavatīti etac chāstre 'vasthitam / tatredutoḥ karaṇe tālusthānataiva parigṛhītā kaṇṭhasthānatā parityaktaiveti / tatra sthāgrahaṇāt kadācid avarṇaṃ syād ity ucyate / atha vākāro 'pi śakyate vivṛtataraḥ pravartayitum ity asti prasaṅgaḥ, kṛtapratyāpattis tu prayokṣyata iti / 

[2.1] dīrgās tv akaḥ prāpnuvanti / hrsvaśrutyaikavākyatām avagacchad ikaḥ (?) kevalam apūrvān vidadhāti ity āntaratamyāt īkāra ūkāraś ca prāpniti, atiri atinu iti / kathaṃ punarbhāvyamānena savarṇānāṃ grahaṇam ? yaṇa (?yena) grahaṇaṃ saṃbhavati, tasmiṃś codyate / kena ca saṃbhavati ? ukāreṇeti / 

nanu ca hrasvādeśa ity ucyate [te]na dīrghā na bhaviṣyanti / dīrghe hi pravartamāne hrasvādeśaśrutir anarthikā syāt / tasmād etad uktaṃ ‘eca ik grasva ādeśo bhavati’ iti / itaro yady asya prayojanāntaraṃ na syāt, pratipadyemahi / asti tatprayojanam / kim ? viṣayārtham etat syāt / eco [hrasva]prasaṅga iti / hrasvādeśe=hrasvādeśavidhau, idam upatiṣṭhate ‘eca ig bhavati’ iti / itarathā hi hrasvādeśa ity asyāvacana ihāpi prāpnoti ---de3vadatteti / tasmāt hrasvaśāsanaviṣaye ‘hrasvo bhavati’ iti vākyabhedenopatiṣṭhate, prātipadikasya hrasvo bhavati, ecas tv ig bhavatīty eco 'nyatra hrasvo bhavati / tato 'nabhisaṃbandhāt sarvekāṃ prasaṅge āntaratamyāt dvimātraḥ prāpnoti iti / etat tu yathā digyādeśo dvirvacanāpavāda evaṃ hrasvāpavādo 'yaṃ vidhir ity evam upāttam / anyathā tu punar api [hrasvaḥ syāt] / 

[2.2] pa[rama]nayor na nānāvākyatvam / nanu caikavākyabhāve 'nuvādaḥ, nā[nā]vākya[bhāve] vidhiḥ ? satyam, evam etat / kiṃ tv ākāṅkṣāvato nirākāṅkṣīkaraṇam ayuktam, anekakriyopādāne ca gauravam ity ekaiva kriyāpekṣyate / kiṃ ca, nānāvākyatve kalpyamāne ‘eca ig’ ity atra ‘hrasvo napuṃsaka’ ity abādhitaṃ bhavaty asaṃsparśād asyeti /

nanu [cai]kavākyatve kalpyamāne ‘eca ik hrasvo napuṃsake prāptipadikasya’ iti hrasvaśrutyāci sthānitvenopātte ejapyantarbhūtaḥ, sthānitvena punaradhyāropyate, adhyāropyamāṇo hrasvo bhavaty eca itīdam uktaṃ bhavati---hrasvo napuṃsake bhavaty ecas tv ik hravo bhavatīti, evaṃ hi kalpyamāne pṛthak pravṛttir hrasvaśāsanasyānugṛhītā bhavati / 

[2.3] anye a¥punar apy evaṃ varṇayanti nivarta[ka]tvād iti / avasthitasyaiva eca ig iti anenaitāvat kriyate---ig avaśiṣyate anik nivartyate iti, yathaikaśeṣe eko 'vatiṣṭhate anye nivartanta iti / naivaicām ikāṃ ca sthānyādeśabhāvaṃ varṇayanti / yathābhūtaś cegavasthitas tathābhūta evāvaśiṣyate ity akārabhāgo nivartate 'nik, ig avasthāpyate / yaś cāvatiṣṭhate hrasva eva na dīrgha iti / tasmāt suṣṭhūcyate---dīrghāprasaṅgas tu nivartakatvād iti / 

atra tv idaṃ codyate---vivṛtataratvāddirnavapāyo(?)r vivṛtatarasya śravanena bhavitavyam, atiri atinu iti / iha ca bhinnaprayatnatvād asati sāvarṇye atirīn iti savarṇadīrghatvaṃ na prāpnoti / uttarabhūyas tv apakṣe cādhyarthamātrasya śravanaṃ prāpnoti / praśliṣṭāvarṇayoś cāśakyā vibhāgapratipattir (?) iti /

[2.5] naiṣa doṣaḥ / vivṛtataratvaṃ hy avarnam upāśritya pravartate, yathā sphaṭikasyopāśrayavaśāt tadrūpam / vyapagatopāśrayasya ca svarūpe 'vasthānam / evam avarnam upāśritya ca vivṛta[tara]tvam, tadapagame cāsya katham iva syād iti vivṛta evāvatiṣṭhate / uttarabhūyas tv apakṣe ca pratyā[khyā]tam eva sūtram / praśliṣṭāvarṇayor apy ācāryo vibhāgaṃ pratipādayati, yathā haṃsaḥ kṣīrodakaṃ vibhajatīti / 

[2.6] aicoścottarabhūyastvād iti / iha aicaḥ samudāyaḥ kaṇṭhyatālavyaḥ / samudāyasya ca nāsti hrasva ubhayasthāna ity āntaratamyaṃ samudāyagataṃ na pratipadyate / kiṃ tarhi ? yau tāv avayavau tayor ekaḥ kaṇṭhya ekas tālavyas tatra ko vāvayavo vyapadeśe nimittam, sa ca sthāni[tvena] prakṛ[taḥ] syār ity asyāṃ vicāraṇāyām idam ucyate---bhūyasaś ca grahaṇāni bhavantīti / yo 'vayava ādhikye nāvasthitaḥ sa vyapadeśanimittam / tasyārdhādhikyāt tadātmakatvaṃ tasya varṇasya lakṣyate, yas tu nyūnaḥ [sa] āgantum i(? -ri)va vijñāyate / ivarṇovarṇamātrā ca bhūyasī iti tālavyapa(? -vyauṣṭhyavyapa)deśaṃ labhate sthānī, na [kaṇṭhya]vyapadeśaṃ labhate yathā āmravanaṃ(? -ṇaṃ) śālavanam iti / satsvanyeṣv avayavabhūteṣu bhūyastvād āmrāḥ śālāś ca vyapadeśahetavo, nānye 'vayavāḥ / evaṃ brāhmṇānāṃ bhūyastvāt te vyapadeśahetavo, nānye 'vayavāḥ / manuṣyeṣu ca grāmaśabdo vartata iti dṛṣṭāntaḥ / sāraṇyake hi grāmaśabdavācye svāmitvena vyapadiśyeran---brāhmaṇā[nā]m ayaṃ dharma[grāma] iti / 

ṣaṣṭhī sthāne[yogā] // 1.1.49 //

[1] kim idaṃ sthāneyogeti / samāsāsamāsayor yugapad asaṃbhavāt [praśnaḥ] / saptamīśravaṇād asamāso 'yaṃ syād, yogeti ca ṭāpa upādānāt samāsaḥ syāt / itarathā hi yuktir i[ti] prāpnotīty āha---kim idaṃ sthāneyogeti / sthāne yogo 'syāḥ seyaṃ sthāneyogā / samāse 'rthapratipattir asti, asamāse tv anumānād apy arthapratipattir nāstīti samāsanirdeśo 'yam iti / nipātanāt tu saptamyalopaḥ / etad uktaṃ bhavati / prayoktavyo 'yaṃ śabda iti / tatra sthāneyogaviṣayaḥ saṃbandho, nānyaviṣaya ity etad ācaṣṭe / tṛtīyāyā vā etvam / sthānena yogo 'syā iti / ‘. . . ācche . . .’ (A 7.1.39) iti yaḥ śebhāvas tad etvam iti manyate / asya tu chāndasatvād bhāṣāyāṃ prayogo duḥsaṃpādaḥ /

[1.1] darśanadvayaṃ ca ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśeṣu / ‘aster bhūḥ’ (A 2.4.52) iti / eke manyante---‘asteḥ’ iti vā ṣaṣṭhī sā naiva bhuvaḥ sā naiva bhuvaḥ saṃbandhinī / kiṃ tarhi ? sthānaprayuktā / ādheyatvena tu bhavatir upādīyate iti / kecid dhi śabdā na svarūpād vyatirekam upajanayituṃ śaknuvanti / tad yathā---devadattasya yajñadatta iti / na yajñādatto yajñadattarūpāt devadatte vyatirekaṃ janayati / evaṃ hi kalpyamāne samuccayapratipattiḥ syāt---devadatto yajñadatto tajñadattaś ceti / tasmād ye 'nye 'rthā---devadattasya yajñadattaḥ pitā, putraḥ, svāmī, bhṛtyaḥ, samīpa ity etebhyo vyatire[kaḥ /] kecit tu svarūpād eva vyatirekam āhur iti / yathā dāsaḥ, putraḥ, kara iti / 

anye manyante  bhuśabda eva parigṛhya svasamavāyino dhrmān sāmīpyādīn vyatirekahetur iti / 

[1.2] sthānaṃ vārthaḥ, tiṣṭhaty asmin śabda iti / asmin [pakṣe] aster arthe bhavatir bhavati / nivṛttiś cetarasya yasmāt bhavatis tadarthamāheti /

[1.2.1] atha varṇād eśeṣu kaḥ sthānārtho, yāvatā artha eva varṇānāṃ nāsti ? tatrāpi ‘sarve sarvapadādeśāḥ’ ity asmin pakṣe dadhiśabdasya dadhy ādeśo, ruher api rupir iti yuktam / 

[1.2.2] atha vā, kālaḥ sthānaśabdenocyate---ikaḥ kālaḥ yaṇo 'dhitiṣṭhantīti / 

[1.2.3] yathā vā ‘aster’ ity etad anukaraṇam arthaprayuktānāṃ asirūpāṇām, te ca sthāninas teṣām artho 'bhidhīyate bhavatinā, evam ‘iko yaṇaci’ ity atrāpi iko ye 'nukṛtā dadhyādisthā ikas teṣāṃ yat svarūpaṃ so 'rthaḥ sthānam iti /

[2] kimarthaṃ punar idam ucyate iti / ārabhyamāṇe sūtre sarvatra sthāneyogatvaṃ prasajyate / tataś cāvayavaṣaṣṭhyādī[nāṃ] na prakḷptiḥ / ekasmiṃś ca viśeṣye saṃnipatitaviśeṣaṇānām upakārārthaṃ ca vyākhyānād yā . . . yam arthaḥ śakyo labdhum iti / 

[2.1] ṣaṣṭhyāḥ sthāneyogavacanaṃ niyamārtham (vā. 1) / ekaśataṃ ṣaṣṭhyarthā iti pāṭham āśrityocyate, svasvāmisaṃbandhasamīpasamūha iti / yāvanto veti / loke yāvanto 'rthā iti / atha vā, ‘yāvanto vā’ iti saty apy anekārthasaṃbhave pāṭhād ye saṃbhavanti / tāvatāṃ prasaṅge niyamārtham idam ucyate / 

tatra kecid evaṃ pratipannāḥ ---sāmānyāpahṛtabuddher viśeṣam apratipadyamānasya pratipattur viśeṣepra[ti]pattyupāyo 'yam / ‘viśeṣo 'trācāryasya vi[va]kṣitaḥ, sthāneyogatvaṃ nāma / evaṃ pratipadyasva ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśeṣu’ iti / apare manyante---‘aster’ ity etat sāmānyarūpaṃ notsahate viśeṣam upādātum iti sthāna ity etad upatiṣṭhate / tad apasthānād idaṃ vākyam anumīyate ---asteḥ sthāne bhūr bhavati iti / 

[2.1.1] avayavaṣaṣṭhyādaas tu na sidhyanti / aviśeṣasya vivakṣitatvāt na sidhyanti / aviśeṣasya vivakṣitatvā[d a]tra vākyāntaropalpavaḥ / goha iti copadhāyā iti [ca] sthānaṣaṣṭhyāv ete iti gohaś cāntyasya syāt, yatra vikṛtaḥ, kṅitsu na syāt / upadhāmātrasya ca syād dhātvantarasya pacāder iti / 

[2.1.1.1] avayavaṣaṣṭhyādīnāṃ cāprāptir yo[ga]syāsaṃdigdhatvāt (vā 3.) / aprāptir iti [na] ṣaṣṭhyā evāprāptiḥ / kiṃ tarhi ? niyamasyāprāptir iti / kathaṃ yad etat bhavatā pratipādyate ‘ṣaṣṭhyā vacanaṃ niyamārtham’ iti ? yatrānekārthaḥ saṃbhavati tatra niyamo 'nena kriyate / yatra cānyo 'rtho nirdiṣṭas tatra ca tannivṛttiṃ sthāneyogatvaṃ kalpayati ity etan na yuktam / yadi hi mayā yathā pratijñātaṃ tathā pratipadyase, niyamasyānekārthasaṃbhave pratipattir yuktā / na tv arthāntaraṃ nivartya tatra vidheyaṃ sthāneyogatvam iti / atha tv evaṃ manyase, vyākaraṇenaivārthā vidhīyante iti, evam api goher upadhāpekṣayā pravṛttasya kathaṃ tyāgo yujyate, apūrvasya ca sthāneyogatvasyopādānam iti yuktaṃ yad anekārthasaṃbhave sthāneyogatvam anyanivṛttyarthaṃ syāt / loke 'pi ca naivaṃbhūteṣūpadeśaḥ pravartata ity etat pradarśayati---laukiko 'yaṃ dṛṣṭānta iti / yau hi panthānāv ekadikkau bhavatas ta[tra] ca na jñāyate kasmin pathibhāge grāmaḥ saṃniviṣṭa iti, tam evopadeṣṭāra upadiśanti---hastadakṣiṇo hastavāma iti / guṇato 'samākhyāne bahuvrīhāv api paranipātaḥ sādhuḥ / dāśater dakṣater vā dakṣiṇaḥ, na tu vyavastheti / atha [vā], hastasaṃbandhena dakṣiṇo yas tasya hastadakṣiṇa iti [vācakaḥ], ‘tṛtīyā tatkṛtārthena’ (A 2.1.30) iti tatpuruṣa eveti / tiryakpathaṃ tu naivopadiśanti, tasyāvyāpārāt / na hy asay saṃdihyate iti / evam ayathā(? -mā)cāryo yaiva ṣaṣṭhī saṃdihyamānā tām eva viśiṣṭārthasaṃbandhinīm ācaṣṭe, yā tu viśiṣṭārthopanipātinī eva, na tām upadiśatīti / 

[2.1.1.2] atha vā, sthāne ayogeti / upapattinirapekṣo yaḥ śāstram eva pramāṇīkṛtya vyavaharati, taṃ pratīdam ucyate / avyaktayogeti / yady api ‘naño 'sty arthānām’ (A 2.2.24, vā. 15) iti uktam, evam api yogam antareṇa ṣaṣṭhy eva nāsti iti viśiṣṭo yogo 'vidyamāno 'syā iti parikalpyate / 

[2.1.1.3] atha vā, yogavatī yogeti / sarvasyāḥ ṣaṣṭhyā yogo 'stīti bhūbhnīti vya[pa]diśyate / yaivānekārthasaṃbandhinī saiva yogā=yogavatī / yā tu nirjñātayogā sā tu nāyogā nāpi yogavatīti sthāne bhaviṣyati / 

[2.2] viśiṣṭā vā ṣaṣṭhī sthāneyogā (vā. 4) / kalādhmātaṃ svaritaṃ cāśritya sthāneyogatvaṃ vakṣyāmi, liṅgāsaggāc ca (? -saṅgasya ca?) pauruṣeyatvaṃ pra[va]kṣya itīdam āha---na ca talliṅgam avayavaṣaṣṭhyādiṣu kariṣyata iti / 

itaro 'saṃpratyayādayaṃ liṅgāsaṅgaṃ na pratipadyate ity āha---‘śā hau’ (A 6.4.35) / śāsigrahaṇaṃ kartavyam iti / [e]vaṃ manyante---śsir atrānumīyate / sa copadhāviśeṣaṇatvena pravṛtto 'nāsaktaliṅga ihāpi tadrū[po]papanna upatiṣṭhata iti / 

yadevādaḥ purastād avayavaṣaṣṭhyartham iti / katham ? adhikāro nāma triprakāra iti / etad ācaṣṭe / yady evaṃ manyathāḥ---eko 'sau śabdo, na caikasya liṅga[va]ttvam aliṅgatā ca yukteti, tac ca na, triprakāratvād adhikārasya / yo hi cenānukṛṣyate ‘granthāntādhike ca’ (A 6.3.79), ‘dvitīye cānupākhye’ (A 6.3.10), ‘avyayībhāve cākāle’ (A 6.3.81) iti, asyānuvṛttir abhedena---yo 'sau ‘sahasya saḥ’ (A 6.3.78) iti / sa iha ca iha ca bhavatīti / evaṃ paribhāṣāyām api śakyam avasātum---pradeśān liṅgavataḥ samuccityaiva tantreṇāvasthitam ‘ikaḥ’ iti / viniyoge tu yo bhedaḥ, sa puruṣaviniyogabhe[dā]d iti / 

yas tv evam āsaktasvaritaliṅgo yoge yoge [sa]mupatiṣṭhate, tatredam avasīyate---tadvanta ete yogā iti / bhinner eva śabdaisthā(? -stāṃ)s tadvato yogān pratipadyamāno yathaiva rū[paṃ] pratipadyate buddhyā ta[thai]va tatra . . . evam anya eva śabdo liṅgam (? -śabdo 'liṅgaḥ), śabdāntaraṃ tu liṅgavad evaṃ buddhyā vyavasyatīti / 

saṃpratyayamātram etat syāt / etat pratipādayati---‘śāsaḥ’ ity uccāraṇā[d] atra liṅgāsaṅgāc ca ‘śa hau’ ity atra ‘śāsaḥ’ ity etad astīti saṃpratīyate samatu(? samaṃ tu) liṅgavān ity etad aśakyaṃ pratipattuṃ śrutim antareṇeti [āha]---na hy anuccārya śabdam iti /

[2.2.1] evaṃ tarhy ādeśe talliṅgaṃ kariṣyate / ‘śa hau’ iti ‘śā’śabdaḥ kalo dhmāto vā kariṣyate / na cedaṃ liṅgam ādeśasya / kiṃ tarhi ? prakṛter eva / sā tu noccāryate / niradhiṣṭhānasya ca liṅgasyāśakyaṃ karaṇam iti svaritatvaṃ saṃpratyayātrāmānaṃ(? -yamātrārthaṃ) liṅgakaraṇaṃ ca viśiṣṭarūpasyeti / 

[3] yatraikā ṣaṣṭhī anekaṃ ca viśeṣyam / kecit varṇayanti---yad anantaraṃ lakṣaṇaṃ tat saṃbadhyate / yadi hi liṅgena sthāneyogatvaṃ pratipadya sa iti (?) / vayaṃ tu brūmaḥ---yathā rasāmādyau na lidhyati (?) aṅgasyeti / yadi saṃdehe niyama iti pakṣo, sti saṃdehaḥ / athāvyaktayogā ayogā, evaṃ yogā yogavatīti, atra tv idaṃ codyate---yadi tāvat aṅgasyety anāśritā arthaṃ ṣaṣṭhyatyarthaṃ śabdamātram etac ca svaryate, ānarthakyā[d] yoga eva nāsti, kutaḥ sthāneyogatvam ? arthapratikambhastūttarityogai(? -rair yogaiḥ) saṃbandhe satīti / athāpy arthamātram anāśritaśabdavyāpāram āśrīyate, uttaratra dupāṇa(? -tūpātta)rūpatayā pravartate yathā vibhāṣāpradeśeṣu pratiṣedhavikalpau vyātṛto (? vyāpṛtau) na tu saṃjñāsaṃbandhakāla ity anayoḥ pratyakṣa(? pakṣa)yor yady asti yogo, yadi nāsti, ubhayathāpi na doṣaḥ / yadi tv āśritārthaḥ śabda āśritaśabdo vā arthātmāśrīyate, tadā viśeṣāpanayanād u[bha]yatrāpi yogenotsahate tadantavidhau sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ pratipattuṃ saṃprasāraṇena, avayavaviśeṣaṇena(? -ṣaṇatvena) ca vaiyadhikaraṇyam iti / 

evaṃ tu manyate---āśrīyamāṇe sūtre iyam api kalpanāsaṃbhavinī---aṅgasyety atrāpi viśeṣeś cāpavya(? -ṣārthasya vya)ñjanāni iti / . . . evam ihāpy a(? -hāpi / a)ṅgasyeti viśeṣābhidhānapakṣe [tu na] vākyaviśeṣāntarāṇām upajanaḥ, saṃbandhasāmānyopādāne vākyagamyā viśeṣā iti / 

nanu coktam, ekaśataṃ ṣaṣṭhyarthā iti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / kecid arthayogyā eva saṃbandhāḥ śabdenopādīyante / tad yathā---‘devadattasya dāsabhāryā’ iti / nāyaṃ saṃpratyayo bhavati---yā dāsabhāryā sā tasya bhagitīti / kiṃ tarhi ? upasarjanaviśeṣaṇa(dvaya ?)m eva rūḍham / tathā ‘etāni’ [devadattasya] dāsīgṛhāṇi iti rūḍhny eva / evam i[hā]pi ‘aster bhūḥ’ iti ya eva yogyo 'rthaḥ sa eva śabdeṣu bhavitum arhati iti / na cānyo 'rtho 'sti [ta]sya [yogyaḥ] ṣaṣṭhyartha iti sthāneyogatvam eva pratipatsyāmahe iti / 

itaro yogyaṃ śabdasamavāyinaṃ ṣaṣṭhyartham upādatta ity āha---śabdasyāpi śabde[na] nāntaryādayaḥ saṃbandhā iti / 

saṃbandha(? saṃdeha)mātram etat bhavati / etad uktaṃ bhavati---yathaiva kriyamāṇaṃ sūtraṃ vyākhyeyam evaṃ vyākhyānād eva pratipa[dya]sveti / atha vā, na saṃdehād asyābhāvaḥ / yathā samandhakāre na ca tāvat varnaviśeṣopalabdhiḥ, atha cāsti rūpam, evaṃ sarvadā viśe[ṣo ']ti, asaṃsṛṣṭaḥ [saṃ]sṛṣṭo vā / viśeṣapratipattyupāyas tu prārthayitavyaḥ / evam ihāpi ‘aster bhūḥ’ iti viśeṣapratipatter ayam upāyaḥ---ase(? -ste)r iti svarūpam ātmabhūtaṃ nityaṃ mithaḥ svarūpavad aviprayogenāvasthita[m] iti śabdārthānupātī sthānasaṃbandhaḥ, samīpādayas tu svarūpārthāc ca bāhyā iti / na vā samīpe bhavateḥ prayoga upapadyate, astinoktatvāt / atha vā, bhavatinoktatvād aste[r] eva prayoga iti / 

[4] ṣaṣṭhyantaṃ sthānena yathā yujyeta, yat tat ṣaṣṭhyantaṃ śrutyanupāti / yat pratīyamānam, tan na sthānena yujyeta iti / yataḥ ṣaṣṭhyuccāritā tasya kāryayogitvam, ity eṣo 'rthaḥ sūtrārambhāt labhyata iti / iyaṃ ca sā paribhāṣā ‘nirdiśyamānasyādeśā bhavanti’ (vā. 12) iti / tena ‘pādaḥ pat’ (A 6.4.130) iti pācchadaḥ ṣaṣṭhyantaḥ śrutyanupātī, tadantaḥ pratīyamāna, iti na tadantaḥ sthānena yujyate / nanu ca ‘pādaḥ’ ity etat saṃbhū[-śru]taṃ tadantaṃ pratyāyayatīti tadantasyādeśaprasaṅgaḥ / tatra kecid varṇayanti---tadantaparibhāṣā bādhyate 'neneti / etac cāsat / iha doṣaḥ syāt---pādayater apratyayāntasya padbhāvaḥ syā[t] / iha hi na syāt---vyāghrapād iti / tasmāt prātastha (? prāptasya) tadantavidher anena vyavacchedaḥ kriyate / yathaiva śāstrāt ‘pādaḥ’ iti pādantaṃ pratipadyase, evam ayaṃ(? -m eva ?) vā[smā]c chāstrād idaṃ vākyam evaṃ bhūtaṃ pratipadyasva---‘pādaḥ’ iti pādantasya yaḥ pācchabdas tasya padbhāvo bhavatīti / atha vā, yamuyā(? samudāyā) eva kāryasatve pā(? 'smā)c chāstrād avayavadvārakaṃ kāryabhāktvaṃ pratipadyante, na sākṣād iti /

kathaṃ ‘yīvarṇayor dīdhīvevyoḥ’ (A 7.4.53) iti ? tatrāpi dīdhīvevyor nirdiśyamānatvāt sarvādeśo lopaḥ prāpnoti / satyam, evam etat / upasaṃhāreṇa tu ‘alo 'ntyasya’ ity aṃ[tya]sya bhaviṣyati / 

sthāne 'ntaratamaḥ // 1.1.50 //

[1.1] kim uāharanam ? ‘iko yaṇ aci’ (A 6.1.77)---dadhy atra, madhv atra / ubhayopādānam udāhriyate / yasminn a[sya] saṃyogas taccodāharaṇam, yad arthaṃ codāhriyate taccodāharaṇam / praśnas tu lakṣaṇāntareṇāpi siddhatvād anarthakam etad iti / ayaṃ tu vaiṣamyamātraṃ paśyann upa[nya]syati---‘iko yaṇ aci’ iti / evaṃ manyate---ika iti bahavaḥ / sthānino bhavanti / ikārokārau pranyekam aṣṭādaśadhābhedāt ṣaṭtriṃśat / ṛkāra[ḷkā]reṇa saha triṃśad ity evaṃ ṣaṭṣaṣṭir bhedāḥ / yaṇo 'pi sapteti / atha vā, lṛkārasya sthānitvaṃ nāsti asaṃbhavād, iti sthānyādeśavaiṣamyam / 

saṃkhyātānudeśenāpy etat [siddham] / [a]pūrvo 'yaṃ vaiṣamyaprakāraḥ / atra na vyaktibheda āśrīyate / kiṃ tarhi ? jātiḥ / igjātir yaṇjātiś ca / tāsāṃ cobhayataś catuṣṭayam / yad apy uktaṃ ḷkāro nāstīti / na prayogaviṣayaṃ yathāsaṃkhyam / kiṃ tarhi ? anudeśaviṣayam / 

[tasthasthamipāṃ tāṃ]taṃtāmaḥ (A 3.4.101) / ayam abhiprāyo---yadā svaritena cihnena yathāsaṃkhyaṃ pratipadyate, tadā ‘iko yaṇ aci’ iti ikārasya prayoge 'samavāyino 'narthakaṃ svaritakaraṇam / yaṇo 'py evam eva / yakārasyājguṇo nāstīti svaritakaraṇam anarthakaṃ manyamāno yathāsaṃkhyaṃ pratipadyate / tāmādīnāṃ tu svaritakaraṇaṃ prayogasamavāyināṃ śrutyartham api syāt iti na yathāsaṃkhyaṃ pratipadyate / ekārthasyaikārtha iti / pradarśanamātram etat / evaṃ pratyantā(? -ktvā)parabhāvaḥ caitanyopādhir ity e[te] hy arthāḥ saṃbhavino, yeṣu ya[? sa]tsu vacanānāṃ vyatikaro na bhavati / na hi tāmādeśasthasaḥ sthāne pravartate / 

nanu caitad api saṃkhyātānudeśenaiva siddham / śakyam etat vyākhyānāt pratipattuṃ hita(? svarita)karaṇam eṣāṃ yathāsaṃkhyārtham, na śravaṇārtham iti hy anayoḥ svaritakaraṇam adhikārārtham eva, na śravaṇārtham / 

[1.2] yatrānekam āntaryaṃ tatreti / a[tra] svaritakaraṇāc chabāntaraṃ sūtrāntare anumīyate / anumīyamānaṃ cāntaratama ity etat apekṣamāṇaṃ vyāpriyate bhedena sthāne sthāna iti / tenāyam artho labhyate---sthāne 'ntaratamo bhavati, sthāne ca yo 'ntaratamaḥ sa bhavatīti / yatrāneko 'ntaratamaḥ saṃbhavati, sa cānekārtho bhinnena nimittena pravartate, tatra sthānāntaratamyaṃ balīya iti / itarathā hy ekakriyāsaṃnipāte ‘[sthāne] sthāne 'ntaratamo bhavatī’ti arthaguṇapramāṇāntaratamyaṃ nivartitam eva syāt / athāpy etāv iti vyāpriyate sthāne bhavatīty akiṃcitkaraṃ bhavatīti / kaścid ekena nimittenāntaraḥ, kaścit bahubhiḥ / yo bahubhiḥ sa kathaṃ syād iti / yathā loke kaścid ekapitṛkaḥ nānāmātṛkaḥ, kaścid ekamātṛkaḥ nānāpitṛkaś ceti, kaścid ekapitṛkaḥ ekamātṛkaś ceti / yadi ca laukikīmayam avasthāṃ pratipadyate, tam agrahaṇam anarthakam / laukikyāṃ pratipattau sūtram eva pratyākhyāyate / 

[2] kimarthaṃ punar idam ucyate / nanu ca prayojanam uktam, ‘akaḥ savarṇe dīrghaḥ’ (A 6.1.101) iti / ucyate / etad ayaṃ pṛcchati---yadīdaṃ nocyeta, kim aniṣṭaṃ syād iti / sthānina eka[tva]nirdeśāt / kathaṃ sthānī ekatvena nirdiśyate ? yadi tāvat saṃjñayā, ādeśo 'py eka eva dīrgha iti / atha saṃjñibhedād anekatvam ādeśa[sya], sthānino 'py anekatvam / tatra bhūtatvā[t] sthānī ekatvena nirdiśyata ity ucyate---‘akaḥ’ iti / bhūtavibhaktyā sthānī aci savarṇe nirdiśyamāno na saṃbhavati aneka iti bhedarūpatayā nocyate ‘akaḥ’ ity anena / ‘dīrghaḥ’ iti tu bhāvyamānatvāt bahurūpatayā saṃbhavatīti aneka iti [ni]rdiśyate / yathā loke ‘mṛtpiṇḍasya vikāraṃ kuru’ iti mṛtpiṇḍam ekatvena pratipadyate, vikāram eka(? -ramaneka)tvena / ātaś cānekatvena, yatra pratipadyate vikāraṃ karomīti / evam iha ‘dīrghaḥ’ iti bhāvyamānatvād ekaikasyākaḥ savarṇe paryāyeṇa savarṇadīrghāḥ prasaktā iti / 

apare varṇayanti / yathā saṃjñāsamāsanirdeśeṣu kramasyābhāvāt mithonirdeśe 'sati, sarvaprasaṅge yathāsaṃkhyam ārabhyeta, evam ihāpi ‘akaḥ’ iti saṃjñānirdeśe 'bhedarūpe, mithonirdeśe 'sati, ‘dīrghaḥ’ iti ca saṃjñānirdeśe sarvasyokālasyācaḥ pravṛttāv ayam artho 'śakyo 'syākaḥ ayaṃ dīrgho bhavatīti / kim arthaṃ punar mithonirdeśo na kṛta iti / laghvartham idam ata ārabhyate ‘sthāne 'ntaratamaḥ’ iti / 

[3] yathā punar iyam antaratamanirvṛttiḥ / [yathā punar iti /] yena prakāreṇa / yaś ca saṃhitāpāṭhaḥ / saṃhitayā [pā]ṭhe ca viśevatā saṃhitāpyasvi (?) tatrānena saṃhitayā ye 'dhyetāras te praṣṭavyāḥ / tatredaṃ darśanam---padaprakṛtiḥ saṃhitā (ṚV 2.1) iti / saṃhitaiva hi satyā asatyānāṃ padānāṃ prakṛtiḥ / tataḥ padānyapodghriyante kuryārthā (? kutaḥ ? artha)syācicchedā[t] śabdasyāpy avicchedaḥ / evaṃ ca kṛtvā ekapadenāsaṃhitā (?) / tatra ‘sthāne 'ntaratama uraṇ raparaḥ’ ity asya kiṃbhūtaṃ padam apoddhartavyam ity eta[d] atra pṛcchati / ‘sthāne ntara[ta]ma’ ity atra ‘ṣaṣṭhī’ [iti] vartate / tenātrādeśasya yā prakṛtir antaratamā saiva ṣaṣṭhyā nirdiṣṭā, nā(? a)nyā naiva nirdiṣṭeti prakṛtiniyamo 'yaṃ vijñāyate / ādeśāntaratamye tu prakṛter aniyamāt sarvatra ṣaṣṭhī, ādeśas tu niyamayate---ayaṃ na [bha]vaty ayaṃ bhavatīti / 

iko yaṇ acīti / kecid iko dūraviprakṛṣṭāḥ, arthamātrikebhyo dvimātrāḥ / mātrikās tv īṣatsaṃnikṛṣṭā iti hrasvair eva ṣaṣṭhī labdhā, [na] dīrghair iti / 

nanu cānudāharaṇam etad ityādāv uktam / satyam, evam etat / yadādeśāntaryapakṣas tadānyena siddhatvāt anudāharaṇam / prakṛtyāntaryapalṣe sarvaprathamam idam eva prayojanam / katham ? dīrghā ikaḥ ṣaṣṭhī na pratipadyante iti / yac ca tad uktam---sthānāntaryaṃ balīya iti, tatrānekena nimittena dvau kaścit sthānena kaścit pramāṇenety evamuktam / ayaṃ ca yaṇaḥ sthānena pramāṇena cāntara iti /

evam ‘iko guṇavṛddhī’ (A 1.1.3) iti / ekāra aikāraś ca dīrghaḥ / tayoś ca dīrghā evāntaratamā iti dīrghe ṣaṣṭhī / 

nanu nāyako lāvakaḥ---‘acaḥ . . . (A 7.2.115)’ ity evaṃ vṛddhi[ḥ], na tv iglakṣaṇā / atrāpy aca iti cāntaratamā prakṛtir iti yohayitavyam / 

tathā ṛvarṇasya guṇavṛddhiprasaṅge iti / kimarthaṃ punar idam ucyate ? ‘iko guṇavṛddhī’ ity anenaiva coditaṃ syāt / eṣa viśeṣaḥ---guṇo mātrike syāt, vṛddhimātre (? dvimātre) na syāt / vṛddhir astu dvimātre syāt, na mātrikasyeti / atra tv iko dīrghasyobhe api syātām iti / 

‘vānto yi pratyaye’ (A 6.1.79) / sthāninirdeśaḥ kartavyaḥ / sarvasyaico nirdiṣṭatvāt sarvasya vānta ādeśaḥ syāt / prakṛtipakṣe tu saṃśli(? -śri)te vāntasyāntaratamā prakṛtirokāraukārāv iti nārthaḥ sthāni[ni]rdeśena / atra vṛttisūtre katham avasthitam ? bhūtāpekṣāyāṃ pravartitavyam / tarhi ‘ecaḥ’ ity etad anuvartate ‘vānto yi’ ity atra / kiṃ tu yo vānta ādeśo 'cy atra dṛṣṭaḥ, sa bhavati yi pratyaya iti / etāvad apekṣyate --- kasya vāsau dṛṣṭo 'ci parabhūte ? okāraukārayor iti / 

tayos tarhi śakyārthād anyatreti / evaṃ manyate---śakyārtha ity asya parityāge kṣeyādayaḥ [na] śakyante nirvartayitum iti / ubhayato niyama iti / yogavibhāgo ['ya]m iti vyācaṣṭe / kṣayyajayyāv iti anena ceyādayo nirvartitāḥ / śakyārtha ity anenāpy etayor anyatrāprasaṅgaḥ / 

ihāpi tarhi niyamān na prāpnoti / evaṃ manyate---yadi sāmānyaviṣayam anumānam, sarvatra nivṛttiḥ / atha viśeṣo rāvipādāsyateti (?) / tulyajātīyasya niyamaḥ / kaṇṭhatāla[vya]tā viśeṣa upādīyate ity abhiprāyaḥ / itaraḥ praśliṣṭāvarṇaviṣayam etad iti pratipadyāmahe (? -dyāha)---evam api . . . raiyatīti / . . . rāyiś chāndasa iti / yakāraparasya bhā[ṣā]yāṃ prayogo nāstīti pradarśyate / evaṃ hy uktam---‘gosamānākṣaranāntāt’ iti / evam adhīyate yathā bhāṣāyām aprayoga iti / 

[3.1] ‘ūd upadhāyā gohaḥ’ (A 6.4.89) . . . upadhāgrahaṇaṃ kartavyam / atra codyate / samudāya evātra ṣaṣṭhīnirdiṣṭaḥ / anyasya saṃbhavo nāsti / tatra kathaṃ nv idam ucyate ? ayam abhiprāyaḥ / yadi liṅgābhāvādenāṃ viśeṣaṇaṣaṣṭḥīṃpratipadyase, 'smāc chāstrād anusaṃhāraṃ pratipadyasva / upasaṃhāraś cāyam evam anusaṃhara(? -re)ti---yo 'ntaratamo na saṃbhavati ādeśasya, asau naiva ṣaṣṭhyāṃ saṃbadhyate iti / yathālonty aparibhāṣayā ‘ruhaḥ paḥ’ (A 7.3.43) iti ruho haḥ iti saṃpratyayaḥ, evaṃ goha iti gohyupadhāyāḥ saṃpratyayena bhavitavyam / 

kriyate nyāsa eva / yady apūrvaṃ kriyate tato doṣaḥ syāt / yadi ca prakṛtipakṣo nirdoṣo 'lpadoṣo vā syāt yujyate vaktu[m] iti /

takāragrahaṇaṃ kartavyam / atha katham asati takāragrahaṇe caritam iti ? na ca nivarteta, anantarā hi niṣṭhā bhavati / kiṃ ca, ‘ādeḥ parasya’ (A 1.1.54) itīkārasya prāpnoti / asyāyam abhiprāyaḥ / ‘niṣṭhāyāḥ’ ity asyānenāntaratamāyāṃ prakṛtau upasaṃhārān niṣṭātakārasyaiva nakāro bhaviṣyatīti / kriyate nyāsa eva / etad uktaṃ bhavati / kim idam anusaṃhārāntaraṃ bādhata, āhosvid bādhyate 'nusaṃhārāntareṇeti / yadi tāvat bādhate, ‘ruhaḥ paḥ’ (A 7.3.43) iti bādhitatvād alontyānusaṃhārasya, oṣṭhyatvād ukāre ṣaṣṭhīty ukārasya prāpnoti / tathā trayam iti---atra yakārasya syāt / atha bādhyata iti manyase, goha ūkāra iti hakārasya prāpnoti / 

asati takāre bhinnavadbhyām ity atra jaśtvasya siddha[tvāt], dakāraś cāntaratamā prakṛtir nakārasya / evaṃ hy uktam---‘yathā tṛtīyās tathā pañcmā . . . ānunāsikyam eṣām adhiko dharmaḥ’ (MBh 1.61.18, 19) iti / atha manyase---‘. . . pūrvasya ca daḥ’ ity anena pa[ra]sya na bhavatīti, aprāptaprāpaṇārtham etat syāt / tasmād yathānyāsam eva jyāyaḥ / 

[4] kiṃ punar idaṃ nirvartakam iti / etad bhāṣyakāro granthacchāyārthaṃ praśnenopākramate / vākyakārasya tu yasmān nirvartake pratipādake vāyogasyānarthakyaṃ tasmān niyamārthatāyām arthavattā bhavatīti / yathā tu ṣaṣṭhīmanayena (?) svatantram eva yathā ‘iko yaṇ aci’ (A 6.1.77) iti, evaṃ sthāne 'ntaratamaṃ nirvartayati / athaś ca saṃnihitaḥ, sarvasyāntaratamaś ca vidyate śabda iti pravartayitavyam / sa tu kiṃ samudāyasya na (? -yasyaiva) bhavati, nā(? utā)vayavānām ? samudāyasya vā (? -sya avayavasya ca) yugapadasaṃbhavāt kadācit samudāyasyādeśaḥ syāt, kadācid avayavasya / ye 'py eta ādeśā ‘aster bhūḥ’ (A 2.4.52), ‘ruhaḥ paḥ’ (A 7.3.43) iti ca, etaiś ca sa [śa]bdaḥ kiṃ vikalpyate antaratama ādeśaḥ, āhosvit bādhayte ity etac ca parīkṣyam / 

[4.1] sthāne 'ntaratamanirvartake sthāninivṛttiḥ (vā. 2) / keyaṃ vācoyuktiḥ --- ‘sthānināṃ nivṛttiḥ prāpnoti’ iti, yāvatātrāvaśyam eva dadhiśabdo nirvarteta ? ayam abhiprāyaḥ / dadhiśabdasya hi dadhya (? dadhiśabda) ādiśyamāne tulyarūpebhyaḥ sva(? -pesasva-)re ca kiṃbhūtā nivṛttiḥ ? śūnyatā na saṃbhavati / śabdasya vyavahāra evocchinnaḥ syād iti / bhinnarūpa ādeśo yadi pravartate, evaṃ ca sati arthavattā na saṃnjavatūty ānarthakyam asyāṃ kalpanāyām ity evaṃ prakramyate /

yadi caivaṃ, kvacid dvairūpyam / keṣāṃcit granthaḥ ‘vairūpyam’ iti / tatra ye ‘vairūpyam’ ity udāharanti, visam ity ādeśasakāratvāt ṣatvaṃ prāpnotīti, ta evaṃ paryanuyoktavyāḥ---yady evaṃ, na bhaviṣyati / yadā tv idaṃ darśanam, avayavā api paryāyeṇādeśaṃ pratipadyanta iti, na hy atra samudāyaḥ kāryabhāktvenocyate, yathā ‘ekāco dve’ (A 6.1.1) iti, tadā vā (? tadaiva) prāpnoti ṣatvam iti vairūpayaṃ samarthyate / yadā tu samudāyaḥ kāryabhāk tadā mā bhūt ity ādeśagrahaṇam api samarthyate ity ayam api granthaḥ samarthito bhavati dvairūpyam iti / evam arthaṃ ca ‘kvacid’ ity āha / kasmimścit pakṣe vairūpyaṃ dvairūpyaṃ vā / evam ‘aster bhūḥ’ (A 2.4.52) ity evamādiṣv api yadāvayavayor ādeśas tadā pravartate [dvairūpyam], kvacit pakṣe 'sti trairūptam / 

api ceṣṭā vyavasthā nā prakalpeta / syād ayam abhiprāyaḥ / samudāye kāryasamāptir iti ya evārthaprayuktāḥ samudāyās teṣām eva kāryabhāktvam iti na syād vairūpyam iti / kiṃ tv iṣṭā vyavasthā na prakalpate / punaḥ punar anyenāntaratamena nivartakena pravartitavyam / nanu ca sakṛt pravṛtāv evāsya nivṛttiḥ syāt / etad eva vayaṃ pṛcchāmaḥ---sakṛt pravṛttiḥ kimarthā, yadi tathābhūta evādeśaḥ ? athāsati prayojane pravṛttaḥ, punaḥ [pravṛttaḥ] kena nivartyate ? tatra yo yaḥ śabda upajāyate, [sa] sarvo nivṛttyabhimukha iti yathaivādeśābhimukhasyāsādhor lāva[stha]sya [evam asya] prayogo na saṃbhavati / evaṃ sarvaḥ śabdo 'navasthitaḥ / evaṃ buddhāv ayaṃ sādhur ity anavasthāne prayoga evoparudhyeta / tathā dadhi atreti / nimittaṃ ca nimittī cādeśābhimukhāv iti na nimittaṃ nimittatvenāvatiṣṭhate na nimittī nimittitvenety e[ta]syāsminn anena kāryeṇa bhavitavyam ity apratipattiḥ / yathaiva tapte bhrāṣṭre tilāḥ prakṣiptā anavasthitā ity etāṃ buddhiṃ janayanty utpātanipātayor avicchedād, evam amī api sādhutvenānavasthitā nimittanimittibhāve[ne]ty anavasthā[nā]n na pratīyeran, iti / tasmād ayaṃ bhāvaḥ---pakṣo ['yaṃ] na saṃbhavati /

[4.2] astu tarhi pratipādakam / ‘akaḥ sarvaṇe dīrghaḥ’ (A 6.1.101) ity akārasya sarve dīrghāḥ kṛtāḥ pravṛttāḥ nivṛttāḥ /

anyena nirvṛttānām anena [pratipattiḥ] kriyate / ‘sthāne 'ntaratamaḥ’ iti sthāne yo 'ntaratamaḥ kṛtaḥ [sa] sādhur bhavati, sa pryoktavyaḥ / itare kṛtā api na prayoktavyā iti / yathā kaścit brūyāt---‘chāgā ānīyantām’ iti / ānīteṣu ca brūyāt---‘kṛṣṇam avasthāpaya / anyānapanaya’ iti / evam artham idaṃ sūtram, na cātrānarthakya iti / na ca parāṅgavat śabdabādhanam iti /

[4.2.1] nirvṛttapratipattau nirvṛttiḥ (vā. 3) / iṣṭaśabdānāṃ nirvṛttir na sidhyatīty abhiprāyaḥ / itaras tu nirvṛttisāmānyam upādāya paryanuyuṅkte---kiṃ tarhy ucyate, nirvṛttir na sidhyatīti / 

idam idānīṃ kimarthaṃ syāt / evaṃ manyate / [yadi] sarve sarvatra syur idaṃ sūtram anarthakam āpadyate / tasmāt sarvaprasaṅgo na bhaviṣyatīti /

[4.2.2] anarthakaṃ ca (vā. 4) / etad uktaṃ bhavati / śabdavyāpāro 'yaṃ na bhavati / ‘iko yaṇ aci’ (A 6.1.77) iti etat sādhutve yaṇo hi saṃhitāyāṃ prayogapravṛttyaṅgam, yathaiva ‘sthāne 'ntaratamaḥ’ itīdaṃ pravṛttyaṅgam / evam ‘akaḥ savarṇe . . . ’ (A 6.1.101) ity etad api / saṃprati tu nirvṛtteṣu teṣu tad idaṃ pravartamānaṃ na śaknoti pratipādayitum---‘ya ete sādhavas te saṃpratyayasādhavaḥ’ iti / anyo hi vakṣyati---‘ta eva sādhavaḥ’ iti / ato yathā bhuktasya bhujipratiṣedho 'śa[kya]vacana evam eṣāṃ nirvṛttānām aśakyā nivṛttir iti / yathaiva kaścit brūyāt ānīteṣu chāgeṣu ‘nānītāś chāgāḥ’ iti, tathābhūtam idam / tasmāc chabdaprasaṅgam aprakramya buddhau prasaktasya nivṛttiṃ manyate / yadaivaikaśbadaprakramo 'pravṛttir iko yaṇ bhavatīti / prakṛte kasmkiṃś cidādeśe, vakāre 'nyasimn vā, nedam asty antaratamaḥ prāk bhavatīti, anantaram asyābhāvād iti (?) / atha pravṛttirūpaṃ nāśrīyate prasaṅgarūpe ca bhedavatāṃ prasaṅga itīko yaṇāṃ prasaktānām iti, pakṣa evāyaṃ nāsti---pratipādakam idam iti / ata upa(? ukta)kalpanāyām ānarthakyam /

itara[s] tv āha---‘uktaṃ vā’ iti . . . /

uraṇ raparaḥ // 1.1.51 //

[1] kim idam u[ra]ṇraparavacanam anyanivṛttyartham ? ikoguṇavṛddhi(pā. 1.1.3)sūtre 'sya sūtrasya vijñātaṃ pāratantryam / saty api tu pāratantrye ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśaviṣayatvamātram asyeti yatra ṣaṣṭhī tatrāyam apūrvam ādeśāntarāṇāṃ bādhakaṃ nirvartayati / nirvartya [cā]syaiva raparatvaṃ vidhatte vākyabhedāt---uḥ sthāne 'ṇ bhavati, sa ca rapara iti / tatrodāttānudāttasvaritavidhāne ‘acaḥ’ iti ṣaṣṭhī[sad]bhāvād idam upasthitam---uraṇ bhavatīti / ṛvarṇasyāpūrvam acidyogo(?) 'nantaratamānādeśān nivartayati iti kṛtārtho (nyatra sthānentaratamaparibhāṣā bādhyate / āhosvid raparatvamātram anena kriyata ? iti / guṇāsaṅgamātra[m a]sya yogasya vyāpāraḥ / uraṇ iti tu ya uḥ sthāne aṇ bhūto 'nyena lakṣaṇena tasya [ra]paratvam ity etāva[ty e]vāpekṣā---uraṇ iti ya uḥ sthāne 'sti sa raparo bhavatīti aṇo bhūtāḥ prāpnuvanti /

[. . . udāttādiṣu doṣaḥ / (vā. 1)] te tu kim udāttādiguṇayuktā atha na ? ubhayathā doṣo 'dhikṛtadoṣo rūpasya śravaṇādevaṃrūpaḥ syāt ? evam api ṛkārasyāṇ raparaḥ prāpnoti / tṛtya ity etanunukṣārthaṃ (?) vaktavyam eva / pratipadādeśeṣu tu ‘ṛta iddhātoḥ’ (A 7.1.100), ‘udoṣṭhyapūrvasya’ (A 7.1.102) iti bādhyate 'yam, ‘uraṇ raparaḥ’ iti / tatra hi sarveṣāmaṇāṃ vidhāne uditi śrutir uparudhyate / kiṃ tv anena vidhīyamānatvāt / etad aṅgaṃ raparatvaṃ durlabham /

guṇavṛddhyor avarnāpratipattiḥ (vā. 2) / sūtavedanena (? bhūte cedanena) raparatvamāsajyate, guṇavṛddhicodanāyām aṇa ṛvarṇasya sthāne durlabho yasya raparatvaṃ kriyeta / kiṃ hi sādhīyaḥ ṛvarṇasyā[sa]varṇe / kim asti kāraṇam ṛvarṇasyā[sa]varṇe, atha vā, kiṃ sādhutaraṃ kāraṇam iti / pūrvasminn api pakṣe eṣa doṣaḥ / yo 'pavādapakṣaḥ prāk upanyastaḥ, tatrāpi ayaṃ doṣaḥ / kaḥ ? avarṇāpratipattiḥ / 

na punar eṅaicau / kiṃ tarhi ? idutāv api prāpnutaḥ ity avarṇapratipattir ekāntena yā sā na saṃbhavatīti / atha matam etat---uḥ sthāne 'ṇaś cānaṇaś ceti / yat pūrvam upanyastamapūrvo bādhako 'ṇ nirvartyate iti, tan na kiṃ tarhi ? niyamyata iti /

tatra kecid varṇayanti / sthāne iti kṛtaśebhāvo 'nuvartate ṣaṣṭhībahuvacanāntaḥ saptamībahuvacanāntao vā / tatraivam abhisaṃbandhaḥ kriyate---guṇo bhavati, vṛddhir bhavatīti, ṛvarṇasya sthāne guṇaḥ aṇ raparo bhavatīti, vṛddhir aṇ raparo bhavatīti / na cekārokārau guṇavṛddhī iti [i]dutau na bhaviṣyataḥ / atha vā, ‘uraṇ raparaḥ’ iti yā ur iti ṣaṣṭhī, neyam anapekṣā / kiṃ tarhi ? prasajyamānāpekṣā / uḥ sthāne ye prasaktās tatrāṇeva bhavati / anyo na bhavati / sa ca bhavan raparo bhavati iti / prasaṅgaḥ ādeśānāṃ śāstrāntarapratibaddha iti śuyaṃ (?) niyamo na kriyate iti kuta idutoḥ prasaṅgaḥ ? siddhā pūrvasmin pakṣe avarṇasya pratipattiḥ / ya eva hy asau paurastyo 'pavādapakṣaḥ, sa evāyam anyasya nivṛttirūpeṇāśrīyate, nāṇo 'pūrvasya nirvṛttirūpeṇeti / evaṃ ca kṛtvā keṣāṃcit pūrvatra granthe evakāro nāsti / etāvān eva granthaḥ---‘uḥ sthāne aṇ’ / na purvasmin vidhīyamāno 'nyaṃ nivartayatīti yathaiva niyame 'nyanivṛttir evam ihāpi iti / 

yat tu tad uktam udāttādiṣu doṣa iti sa iha doṣo jāyate ? praśnenopakramate, kathaṃ manyate bhavān ? iti / abhiprāyaḥ / ayaṃ sthānentaratamaparibhāṣā(? -ṣayā) vyavasthānaṃ prakurute / aca udāttāda[yo] 'ntaratamā bhavanti / vyavasthāpiteṣu antaratameṣu naivāsti aṇaḥ prasaṅgaḥ / atha tv iyam eva paribhā[ṣā] aca udātta ity ādyam eva prasaṅgam adhitiṣṭhati, bhavati doṣa iti / itara uraṇraparaparibhāṣā(? -ṣayā) pūrvam avasthāpiteṣv anantaratameṣv apravartamānomuve (?) antaratamaparibhāṣām i(?)ty āha---jāyate [sa] doṣaḥ / katham ? udātta ity anenāṇo 'pi pratinirdiśyante [anaṇo 'pi] iti / yady api pratinirdiśyante, na tu prāpnuvanti, sthāne 'ntara[ta]mo mani(?) bhavatīti / antaratam aparibhāṣayā prakṛte niyame tata idaṃ niyamāntaraṃ na pravartiṣyata ity abhiprāyaḥ / kuto nu khalv etat ? dvayoḥ paribhāṣayoḥ . . . antaratamaparibhāṣāyā avakāśaḥ ‘akaḥ savarṇe dīrghaḥ’ (A 6.1.101), ‘jhayo ho 'nyatarasyām’ (A 8.4.62) iti / ‘uraṇ raparaḥ’ ity a[syā]vakāśaḥ---kartā, hartā / ihobhayaṃ prāpnoti---kṛtiḥ, hṛtir iti / sthānentaratamaparibhāṣayā [vyavasthā] bhavati, na punar ‘uraṇ raparaḥ’ ity anayeti / evaṃ varṇayanti / kuta etat---pūrvavipratiṣedho bhaviṣyati, na punaḥ paravipratiṣedha iti ? atha vā, ‘aca udāttaḥ’ ity etasmin prasaṅge 'ntara[ta]me pravṛtte niyamāntaraṃ [na] pravartiṣyate, na punaḥ paurastyam eva prasaṅgam ‘uraṇ raparaḥ’ iti etad eva niyamayatīti ?

ye cāpy ete ṛvarṇasya iti / yatra hi ni(? pra)vṛttir anyasyāsti tatrāyaṃ niyamo (? yogo) niyamarūpatāṃ pratipadyate / iha cekārokārayor eva pravṛtir iti niya[ma]syābhāvāt pradhānavākyam eva na pravṛttam---‘uḥ sthāne 'ṇ bhavati’ iti / tad apravṛttau ca guṇavākyasyāpravṛttiḥ ‘raparo bhavati’ [iti] /

siddhaṃ tu prasaṅge raparatvāt / dve sthānagrahaṇe atrānuvartete ity etac cetasi vartate / yady api dvitīyaḥ pakṣo raparatvamātram anena kriyata iti sa evāyaṃ pakṣa, ayaṃ tu viśeṣa upādīyate---pūrvatra kālo 'navaghṛtaḥ / saṃprati kālo 'vadhriyate / iha tu prāptir na niyamo nirvṛttir iti / tatra niyamaṃ nirvṛttiṃ ca parityajya prāptau=prasaṅga eva rapratvaṃ kriyate / etad uktaṃ bhavati---ya eva [prā]ptyā niyatakālaḥ sthāne bhavatīti, sa eva [ra]paratvakālaḥ / tatra kena bhavitavyam iti buddhipravṛttikāla evāṇā pratilabdhaṃ raparatvam iti guṇavṛddhikāle rephasyāsaṅgād antaratamo bhaviṣyatīti pratipāditam etat sthānentaratamaparibhāṣāyāḥ pravṛttinimittasyāṅgatveneyam upaplavate uraṇraparaparibhāṣeti / 

yady evam ādeśo 'viśeṣito bhavati iti / evaṃ manyate / ekaṃ sthānam anuvartate / tena ca kālo 'vadhṛtaḥ / sa tv avadhṛtakālo raparāsaṅga uḥ samīpe anantaro vā prāpnoti / katham ? yatra hi ṣaṣṭhī bhūta upādīyate, tatra ‘ṣaṣṭhī sthāne . . . ’ (A 1.1.49) iti neyam upatiṣṭhate / ātaś ca nopatiṣṭhate yat paṭhati---‘udīcāmātaḥ sthāne . . . ’ (A 7.3.46) iti / 

dvitīyaṃ sthānagrahaṇam anuvartate / kecid varṇayanti / saptamyantam iha sāmarthyāt prathamāntaṃ bhavati, aṇ sthāna iti / vayaṃ tu brūmaḥ---vyākhyānam etad evaṃ tu kriyate---uḥ sthāna iti yasmin kāle raparaś ciave(? -ścaivaṃ ce)daṃ sthānaṃ nāvaṣṭabdham ādeśeneti tasminn eva kāle [ra]paratvam iti / 

aparaḥ kalpaḥ, yenāpavād apakṣo niyamapakṣaś ca saṃbhavatīti / aṅgasya guṇo bhavatīti / evam api hi ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśeṣu ‘uraṇ raparaḥ’ ity upasthitam evaṃ vyāpriyate---aṅgasya guṇo bhavati, ustvaṇ bhavati sa ca rapara iti / evaṃ tāva[d] apūrvo 'ṇ anena vidhīyate / [a]to 'pavādapakṣaḥ / atha guṇavṛddhiśabdābhyām abhisaṃbadhya ekavākyabhāvam āpadyate---aṅgasya guṇo bhavati, us tu guṇo 'ṇ bhavati, nānyo guṇaḥ / vṛddhir aṇ bhavati, nānyā vṛddhi[r i]ti, evaṃ niyamo jāyate / asyāṃ tu kalpanāyām aviśeṣo dṛśyate yat pūrvasyāṃ kalpa[nā]yāṃ dvitīyaṃ sthānagrahanam āśrīyate 'taḥ sthānānām aprasaktānām aṇeva bhavati / asaty anyasmin prakāre guṇavṛddhiti(?)sthānaprakaraṇe sāmarthyāprāptānām ādeśānām apekṣāprasaṅgene(?)ṣāmaṇeva bhavati / asaty asmin prakāre guṇavṛddhicodanayor ekavākyatve ur guṇo bhavati, aṇ vṛddhir bhavatīti / anyat tu sarvaṃ gaṇanaṃ doṣāpratividhānaṃ ceti / 

[2] aṇgrahaṇaṃ kimartham ? uttaraprabandhopanyāso 'yam / paitṛṣvaseyaḥ / lopavacanaṃ kimartham iti cet, sarvādeśārtham, parādipakṣe vā rutvārtham iti / hotāpotārau / rephasya saṃyogāntalope kṛte nalopo na syāt / yathā pacann iti / 

[3] ekādeśasyopasaṃkhyānam (vā 3) / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / yathā kanyāpratigraha evaṃ paṭhyate ‘prajāpataye’ iti, na tv alaṃkārasya pṛthagagnaya iti, vāsasaś ca bṛhaspataya iti, evam iha ‘. . . pūrvaparayoḥ’ (A 6.1.84) iti samudāyaḥ sthānitvenocyate / sa tu pūrvaparāvaya[va]tvāt pūrvaparayor ity ucyate yathā pravaṇam iti samudāyaś ca ṇakāro bhavati, iti raparatvābhāvaḥ / 

avayavagrahaṇāt siddham / asty asmin samudāye ṛkāro 'vayavas tadāśraya ur iti vyapadeśa iti [ra]paratvaṃ bhaviṣyati / tad yathā, māṣā na bhoktavyā iti [ukte] miśrā api na bhujyante / ayaṃ [tu] nyāyo vi[ru]dhyate / evaṃ hy uktam---‘nāvyapavṛktasyāvayave tadvidhir yathā dravyeṣu’ (MBh 1.25.3) iti / ghṛtaṃ na vikreyam, tailaṃ na [vi]kreyam iti gāvaḥ sarṣapāṇi ca vikrīyante / evaṃ ca sati surādīnām aṣṭa(? -nāṃ vyapavṛkta)vyapadeśo na dṛṣṭaḥ / evaṃ māṣādīnāṃ bhojanaṃ nāmnātam / kevalam arthitvaprasaktam / na ca śabdo 'sti loka evaṃbhūtā mā[m] māṣā, naivaṃbhūtā, iti / yāvati māṣagandho 'sti sarvo 'sau samudāyo māṣā eva, tadavayavatvāt / samudāye ca kāryaṃ na kiṃcid upadiṣṭam iti avayavāśrayo bhavati vyāpāraḥ /

evaṃ tu kalpyamāna---ādeśe rāntapratiṣedhaḥ / ādeśe 'pi vidyate aṇrūpaṃ samudāyaś cāvyāpṛta iti / yathaiva [ṛ]varṇarūpaṃ vidyate iti ur iti vyapadeśa evam ādeśo 'pi aṇavayavatvāt aṇ iti vyapadiśyata iti raparatvaṃ prāpnoti / kathaṃ punaḥ nyāyaḥ parityajyate ? ayam abhiprāyaḥ / sthānyādeśabhāva ‘ur aṇ raparaḥ’ iti śabdavān / asti mukhyo 'ṇ, ṛvarna ca sthānitveneti / samudāye sthānyādeśe ca samudāyasya nivartyatvān nivartakatvāc cāvayavarūpatātyayogāśritaṃ (?) na nivartyaṃ na nivartakam / atha tu śabdavyāpāram āśritya tuvyārūpetigraha (?) āśrīyate, siddhaṃ sthānitvam, ādeśe tu raparatvaprasaṅgaḥ / yadi punaḥ ṛvarṇāntasya sthāninaḥ / kecit tu bruvanti, antagrahaṇaṃ kartavyam iti / anye varṇayanti, yatra ṣaṣṭhī tatredam upatiṣṭhate iti ṣaṣṭhyantasya tadantavidhiḥ / kva ca vidhānam ? aṅgasya guṇa iti / atra cegantasya ṛvarṇāntasya ceti yady aprāptānusaṃhāro 'bhisaṃbadhyate, karteti vyapadeśivadbhāvāt sety astīty ata āha---etad api vyapadeśivadbhāve[na] ṛvarṇāntam iti / 

athavatā vyapadeśivadbhāvaḥ / tarhy arthopādānatyāgābhyāṃ bhedaḥ śakyate kalpayitum, na hy ekarūpapratibaddho vyapadeśivadbhāvaḥ / na hi bhavati---ghaṭasya ghaṭa iti / iha ca [raparatvasya] pratiṣedho vaktavyo---mātuḥ pitur iti / evaṃ manyate / ‘ṛta ut’ (A 6.1.111) iti pūrvaparayor uttve, raparatve ca kṛte, vibhaktisakārasya rutvaṃ, ‘ro ri’ (A 8.3.14) iti lopaḥ, dīrghatvam iti mātūḥ karotīti prasajyeta / 

ubhayaṃ na kartavyam, upasaṃkhyānaṃ niṣedhaś ca / iha dvayoḥ ṣaṣṭhīnirdiṣṭayoḥ iti / etat pratipādayati / pūrvaparayor ity avayavāv eva sthāninau, pratyekaṃ vākyaparisamāpteḥ / tadupalakṣitaḥ samudāyo, liṅgaṃ caikagrahaṇaṃ sthānibhedāt bhedo mā bhud iti / yathaivaikaḥ putraḥ ubhayoḥ saṃbandhitvena vyapadiśyate, evam ādeśo 'pīti / 

‘rāt sasya’ (A 8.2.24) iti [sakārasya] lopo yathā atsā iti / ‘apratyayavisarjanīyasya’ (tu.
