A 8.3.31) iti ṣatvaṃ prāpnoti / evaṃ manyate / samudāyābhiniveśinī saṃjñā nākāram āskandati / tena rephaḥ apratyayaḥ iti / pratyayavisarjanīyaś cāyam / ayam abhiprāyaḥ / yo 'sāv akāras tasya pratyayāvayavatvāt pratyayavyapadeśaḥ / tato rephaḥ pratyayasya [vya]padiśyate iti / 

lupyate 'tra pratyayavisarjanīya iti / yasyām avasthāyām uttvaṃ vidhīyate tasyāṃ naivākāreṇa labdhā pratya[ya]saṃjñā / uttarakālaṃ sakāreṇa labdhā / tadvinivṛttau pūrvamakāro la(? -kāreṇāla-)badhā idānīṃ labhyata ity ayuktam idam ucyate / tasmāt ṣatvaṃ prāpnoti / idānīṃ liṅgān nivartayati---

bhrātuṣputragrahaṇaṃ jñāpakam ekādeśāt ṣatvaṃ na bhavatīti / ekādeśo nimittaṃ yasyeṇas tasmān na bhavati parasya visarjanīyasyeti / 

[4] kiṃ punar ayaṃ pūrvāntaḥ ? kathaṃ punā rapara iti nirdeśe guṇībhūto repho 'ntatvenāditvena vā saṃbadhyate ? syān matam, rephāsaṅgamātram anena vidhīyate iti prādhānyaṃ rephasyeti / yady arthakriyāyāṃ prādhānyam, ta[rhi] śabdas tu na tathābhūtaṃ rephaṃ codayatīti viśeṣaṇān abhisaṃbandhaḥ / tatraivaṃ bruvate---[e]tad uktaṃ bhavati---pūrvānte pakṣe paragrahaṇaṃ na kartavyam / etā[va]d astu---‘ur aṇ ra’ iti / ‘anta’ iti vartate / evaṃ parādipakṣe 'pi / ur aṇ iti pañcamīpakṣe 'syāḥ paratve kalpite paragrahanaṃ [na] kartavyam / atha kriyate, vyaktyarthaṃ saṃpadyata iti ayaṃ sūtrārthaḥ saṃpannaḥ---uḥ sthāne yo[' ṇu]kto 'smāt paro ro bhavati, sa tv ādir bhavati / athobhayaṃ nānuvartate, tato 'bhakta iti / 

iyaṃ tu vicāraṇā niyamapakṣam āśritya yuktā / tasmiṃś ca pakṣe pūrvavipratiṣedhāśrayaṇaṃ kṛtam / niyamasya vā niyamāntareṇa saṃbandhaḥ / pratipadādeśeṣu sarvān ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśā[n] samuccitya samudāye, nirvartyam astīti niyamaḥ siddha iti parigṛhyate / doṣe saṃbhavanti trayaḥ pakṣāḥ / 

yadā tu ‘prasaṅge raparatvam’ ity eva pakṣas tadā yathaivānaṅādiṣu nakāraviṣayā vi[cā]raṇā nāsti---kim anaṅo nakāro 'bhaktaḥ parādir iti, evam eva vicāraṇayānayā [na] bhavitavyam---kim ayaṃ parādir abhakata iti / śakyate tv asmin pakṣe 'pi ādiśabdānuvṛttisāmarthyād evaṃ kalpayitum---sthāninivṛttikāle tadavayavaḥ, ūrdhvaṃ tu yāni kāryāṇi teṣv ayaṃ parādiḥ syādivat bhavati iti / 

tatra pūrvānta iti samudāyasyaiva vāntaḥ, atha vāṇaḥ / avayavāvayavatvāt tu samudāyasyāpi bhavati / evaṃ yaḥ paro niyatasaṃnidhāno vibhaktipratyayo 'nyo vā pratyayasamudāyas tasyādi[r i]ti /

[4.1] gīḥ / pūḥ / abhakte hy anavayavo 'ntavyapadeśaṃ na labhate iti / nijegilyate / abhakte rephe gira iti eṣāvayavāsaṃbandhinī [ṣa]ṣṭhī aśakyā kalpayitum / gīryate svayam eva / repheṇa yako vyavadhāne, saty api copadeśavacane na sidhyati, vyavadhānād eva / svaravidhau vyañjanam avidyamānavat iti / asmin pakṣe iyam āśrayitavyā paribhāṣā / pratividheyaṃ doṣeṣu / abhyastasvare 'py eṣa eva parihāraḥ / uktatvāt tu na prayuktaḥ / [halādiśeṣa /] yathābhūtaḥ śiṣyate, tathābhūtena nivartitavyam / evam abhyāsaḥ ahal bhavatīti asyāṃ kalpanāyāṃ na prāpnoti, anekadeśatvād rephasyeti / visarjanīyapratiṣedha iti / abhakto hi tadādigrahaṇāt padānto bhavati / ‘yasmāt suptiṅvidhis tadādi subantaṃ [tiṅantaṃ] ca’ (tu. pari. 23) iti / pratyayāvyavasthā ca / kirataḥ, girataḥ iti / ko dvivacanenānurodhābhiprāyaḥ ? kira[tī]ty atrāṅgaṃ ca prāpnoti vikaraṇaś ca / nityatvāt vikaraṇena bhavitavyam / āṅgo hi guṇaḥ anityaḥ / kṛte vilaraṇe pratiṣedhāt / tasmāt vikaraṇe kṛte tannimitte cettve sādhutvasyābhyanujñānāt nāsti avyavasthā / iha tu dvivacane ubhayaṃ nityam, āṅgaṃ vikaraṇaś ca / paratvāt āṅge kṛte paścāt vikaraṇa iti repho 'bhaktatvāt tatra [dhātvavayavo na] bhavatīti rephāt parasya vikaraṇasyāsādhutvaṃ prasaktam / athāpi vikaraṇāt paro rephaḥ sa kalpate, aṇaḥ paro na bhavatīti rūpāprasiddhiś ceti dvivacane eva pratyayā[vya]vasthodāhriyate /

[4.2] pūrvānte rvavadhāraṇam / ‘roḥ supi’ (A 8.3.16) iti niyamārthaṃ kartavyam / gīrṣu, pūrṣu, ayaṃ hi supi padānta iti / parāditve sati tu subgrahaṇenaiva gṛhyante iti / abhakto 'pi tadādivacanāt prāpnoti iti kartavyaṃ rvavadhāraṇam / parādāv api yaḥ svābhāvikas tasya nivṛttyarthaṃ [karta]vyam, iha mā bhūt---caturṣv iti / 

nārkalpir ityevamartham / kalpipadasaṃghā[ta]bhakto 'yaṃ notsahate 'vayavasya padāntatāṃ vihantum / nṛkalpaśabdād iñi vihite vṛddhau kriyamāṇāyāṃ rephaḥ parādiḥ kasya bhavat ? pratyayasamudāyasya kalpiśabdasya / samudāyaṃ prati tadavayavo repho na śakyate tasmin pūrva iti vyavacchettum / yas tu kalpaśabdas tasyāvayavo na bhavatīti avayavo 'vayavāntarā[t] pūrvaḥ paraś ca śakyate vyapadeṣṭum / tasmāt kalpapratyaye tadādigrahaṇād asti rephasya padāntatvam / keyaṃ vācoyuktiḥ---‘notsahate 'vayavasya’ iti ? ayam arthaḥ / asya saṃghātabhaktatvāt [ayaṃ] saṃghātasyāvayavaḥ / tasya saṃghātāvayavasya yā kalpe 'vayave padāntatā [tāṃ] notsahate vihantum iti / anyeṣāṃ granthaḥ---‘kalpi(? -lpa)padasaṃghātabhakto 'sau’ iti / ta evaṃ varṇayanti / kalpāvayavaṃ padaṃ kalpi(? -lpa)padam iti vyapadiśyate / tena nārkalpiśabdaḥ kalpi(? -lpa)padam / tasya yaḥ saṃghāto 'vayavaḥ  kalpir iti / 

[4.3] [dhunutaḥ /] atrāpy ekavacane dhinotīti lope saty asati vā nāsti viśeṣaḥ / [ād gunena vā, vikaraṇasya] gune kṛte vā ‘ato guṇe’ (A 6.1.97) iti [pararūpeṇa] yathā paca iti / ato dvivacanam udāhṛtam / ārdhadhātukopadeśe yad akārāntam iti / ārdhadhātukapratipattau yad akārāntam iti / yasmin hi kāle ārdhadhātukaṃ svarūpaṃ pratipadyate, tasmin kāle na kadācid anena saṃniyogaśiṣṭayor anayor akārāntatā apratipanneti siddho lopaḥ / 

nirdiśyamānasyādeśā bhavanti iti / nanu cānusaṃhāro bādhyate yathā iti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / ṇala iti yat ṣaṣṭhyantaṃ tena yaḥ pratyāyitaḥ prayogaviṣayaḥ sa ādeśaṃ pratipadyate / [tad eva] rūpam asyety abhedād ekatvādhyavasāya iti idam ucyate---ṣaṣṭhīnirdiṣtasyeti / ataś cākārāntād aṅgād akārasya aukāro bhavati iti / akārarūpaṃ śrutisaṃnipāti, yas tu tadbhaktaḥ so 'numīyamāna iti [na] bhaviṣyati ‘ādeḥ parasya’ (A 1.1.54) iti / repheṇa vyavahitatvāt / akāraś ca svarūpeṇāśrīyate / tadrūpaṃ ca vyavadhīyate / yadi tu liṭa iti vā pratyayasyeti vocyetāvyavadhānā[t] syād ādeśa iti / 

caṅy upadhāhrasvatvam / na[nu] ca jñāpakād anupadhāsthasyāpi syā[t, ya]d ayam ‘at smṛdṝtvara . . . ’ (A 7.4.95) ity etvaṃ śāsti / satyam, evam etad, atiprasaṅgas tu / ihāpi syāt---acīkṛtat, acikīrtat iti / 

iṭaś ca vyavasthā na prakalpate, āstaritā iti / evaṃ manyate / guṇaś ca prāpno[ti], iṭa ca / paratvān nityatvāc ca guṇaḥ / kṛte ca guṇe iṭpratiṣedhaḥ prāpnoti, ‘neḍ varamanādau’ ity a[pra]vṛttirūpāvyavasthā / pravṛttau satyāṃ rephāt pūrvaṃ ceṭaḥ śravaṇam ity aniṣṭā vyavasthā / yadi tu pūrvam iṭ syāt paścād gunaḥ, na kaści[d] doṣaḥ syāt / yathābhūtaḥ paras tathāvirbhūtasyādir bhavatīti / 

dīrghatvaṃ na prāpnoti / gīḥ, pūr iti / yadi tāvat akṛte vilope utta(? iduttvaṃ) raparatvaṃ ca, vilopa eva na prāpnoti, apṛktasaṃjñāyā abhāvāt / [ta]smād antaraṅgatvāt kṛte vilope utvaṃ (? idutvaṃ) raparatvaṃ ca / supy utpanne yaḥ paraḥ, tasyādir ayam ity asyāṃ kalpanāyāṃ ‘rāt [sa]sya’ (A 8.2.24) iti lope kṛte na prāpnoti dīrgha iti /

alo 'ntyasya // 1.1.52 //

[1] kim idam algrahaṇam / tulyatvān nirdeśasya na jñāyate---kiṃ prathamābahuvacanāntam, uta ṣaṣṭhyekavacanam iti / dvaitaṃ samarthayante, uttaraṃ ca granthaṃ varṇayanti---yadi punar ‘alantyasya’ ity cuyata iti / yady evaṃ vyākhyāyeta / prathamābahuvacanāntam etad ity asaṃdigdhena paryāyeṇa vyācaṣṭe / anyeṣāṃ tv evam eva granthaḥ / kim idam algrahaṇam antyaviśeṣaṇam evaṃ bhavitum arhati, iti antyasamānādhikaraṇam evāli(?)m adhyavayanti / yady apy antyaviśeṣaṇam ādeśo 'viśeṣito bhavati, iti / anekālapyādeśasya rūpam avadhṛtam / evaṃrūpo 'ntyasyālo bhavatīti, anekā[lapyā]deśo 'ntyasya prasajyeta / tasmād asmin pakṣe sūtram ārabdha[vya]m ‘anekālśit sarvasya’ (A 1.1.44) iit / yadi punar ‘alantyasya’ iti ucyeta / yady etad vyākhyāyeta vā / evaṃ hy āśrīyamāṇe ādeśarūpam avaṣṭānta(? -)malātmakam antyasya pratipāditaṃ bhavati iti lopaḥ sarvasya prāpnoti, ‘. . . rahorajasāṃ lopaś’ (A 5.4.51) iti, virajīkarotīti / kiṃ cālsamudāyaś cāntyasya prāpnoti iti, anyatra varṇagrahaṇe jāteḥ pratinirdeśāt / algrahaṇam idānīṃ kimartham iti cet, lopārtham / lopaś cāntyasya mā bhud iti / liṅgāt tu ayaṃ na jātinirdeśa iti gamyate / kiṃ liṅgam ? ‘ṅic ca’ (A 1.1.53) iti / jātinirdeśe hi ṅid apy antyasya siddha eva i[tī]daṃ niyatasāmarthyaṃ bhaviṣyati / niyamaphalatvā[t] niyama iti ucyate / antyasyādeśa bhavantīti anenānalo 'py ākṣepād algrahaṇam analovyāvṛttyartham /

evam apy antyo 'viśeṣito bhavati, evaṃrūpasyeti / evaṃ sati yāvānantyo vyākaraṇe saṃbhavati ṣaṣṭhīnirdiṣṭaḥ, sarvasya prāpnoti / paramānaḍudbhyām ity anaḍuhaḥ ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśāt sarvasyānaḍucchabdasyāntyatvād dakāraḥ prāpnoti / vākyasyāpīti / doṣaparatvād vākasyopādānam / na tu saṃbhavati vākyam antyaṃ ṣaṣṭhīnirdiṣtam iti / tasmā[t] doṣavān evādeśaviśeṣaṇapakṣa ity antyaviśeṣaṇapakṣa eva nirdoṣaḥ / yadi khalv apy evṣo 'bhiprāyaḥ / etat pratipādayati / avaśyakartavyo 'yaṃ yogaḥ patiṣyati / vipratiṣedhe 6¥'pavādavipratiṣedhāt sarvasya ādeśa iti / asati hi pāṭhe kramābhāvān nāpi pūrvo nāpi paraḥ saṃbhavatīti / atha tu lāghavamātraṃ dṛśyate, antyaviśeṣaṇapakṣe 'pi tan na kariṣyate /

[2] alo 'ntyasyeti sthāne vijñātasyānusaṃhāraḥ / ‘ṣaṣṭhī sthāneyogā’ (A 1.1.49) ity eṣa yogaḥ kṛtsnaḥ svaryate / tena yā ṣaṣṭhī sthāneyogatvena nirjhātā, paricchinnā, viṣayāntarādavacchinnā, nākrāntāpavādaiḥ, sāntyasyālo bhavati / ādeśo vā yaḥ . . . / [ayaṃ] sthāne[nu]saṃhāro '[nya]tra na pravartate / yathāśrutasyaivādir anto vā ṣaṣṭhyantasya, paro vā kriyate, iti / 

itarathā hy aniṣṭaprasaṅgaḥ / yadi ṣaṣṭhīty etāvan mātraṃ svaryate, yāvatī ṣaṣṭhī vyākaraṇe sā na śrūyamāṇasya / kiṃ tarhi ? antyasyāla, iti yathaiva ṣaṣṭhyapavādasyāṅgam evam ‘alo 'ntyasya’ ity etad apy aṅgam, iti ārdhadhātukasye[tī]yaṃ ṣaṣṭhī [na] samudāyasya / kiṃ tarhi ? antyasyāla iti / sā tu yathārthā(? -rthaṃ) pravartate, [kvacit sthānyādeśabhāvena,] kvacit sthānyādeśocchedeneti / ṭitkinmito 'py antyasya syur iti / ṛkārāt pūrva iḍāgamaḥ prāpnoti---bhaviteti / evaṃ punar apy ākārasyāgamo na samudārasyeti, yad uktam ‘arthavato hy āgamas tadguṇībhūto gṛhyate’ (MBh 1.75.5) iti, tan na saṃbhavati / tathā ca praṇidāpayatīti ṇatvaṃ na sidhyati / tathā num kasya ? antyasyeti pūrvāntapakṣe 'py aṅgasya neti tasya nalopo gīrghatvaṃ ca na prāpnoti / 

[2.1] yadi punar ayaṃ yogaśeṣo vijñāyeta / ṣaṣṭhīmātraṃ nānusaṃhriyate ity etat pratipādayati / katham ? idaṃ hi sūtram ‘alo 'ntyasya’ iti . . . / yogaśeṣe ca / kim ? aniṣtaṃ prasajyeta / ārdhadhātukasyeti ṣaṣṭhīrūpam antye 'pi vyavasthitam autpattikena (? autsarginekeṇa) vākyena / tatrādiṣṭid bha[va]tīti pravartamāno 'pavādaḥ kiṃ ṣaṣṭhīrūpaṃ bādhatām---nedam atra saty antyasyāla iti; atha viṣayaniyamaṃ bādhatām---sthāneyogā na bhavatīti ? yuktaṃ ya[d a]tra ṣaṣṭhīrūpam abādhamāno viṣayaṃ bādheta / upasaṃhārāntaram anurūpam eva bādhyate, na tu viṣayaḥ / (‘ādeḥ parasya’, ‘anekālśit sarvasya’ iti /) asmin pakṣe tulyo doṣa ity āha---tasmāt suṣṭhūcyate . . .‘itarathā hy aniṣṭaprasaṅgaḥ’ iti / ayaṃ cānusaṃhāro 'nusaṃhārāntareṇāsaṃbhavād bādhyate [‘ādeḥ parasya’ ity anena] / ‘anekālśit sarvasya’ tu saty api saṃbhave bādha[ka]m iti /

ḍic ca // 1.1.53 //

[1] tātaṅi ṅitkaraṇasya sāvakāśatvāt iti / apavādo 'pi sthānāntaraprāptyā bādhyate / devadattaputra iti samāsāntodāttatvena ‘kārakād dattaśrutayoḥ . . .’ (A 6.2.148) ety etad bādhyate, evaṃ ‘ṅic ca’ iti / yady api sarvānusaṃhārasya bādhakam, evam api tātaṅo ya[t] ṅitkaraṇaṃ tad anyārtham api saṃbhavatīti ṅittvanimittaṃ saṃdihyate / [te]na saṃdehāt saṃdigdhopasthāno yogaḥ, yathaiva utsargaḥ / saṃdigdhopasthāna ity apakṛṣyamāṇabalo nonsahate bādhitum iti vipratiṣedha ucyate /

[2] prayojanaṃ nāma . . . yan niyogata iti / na hi yathānyanirapekṣaṃ ṇiṅo ṅittvam ātamanepadārthaṃ sāmānyagrahaṇāvighātārthaṃ [ca] saṃbhavaty evaṃ ṇitkaraṇaṃ sāmānyagrahaṇārthaṃ saṃbhavatīti yuktaṃ ṇiṅi ṇi[t]karaṇasya sāvakāśatvād iti (? -tvam iti) / iha tu yadā sarvādeśatvaṃ labdhaṃ syāt syāt tadā guṇapratiṣedhārthaṃ bhavet ṅitkaraṇam / tad eva tu duḥsaṃpādam iti yad eva nimittaṃ saṃdigdhopasthānatāyās tad eva nāstīti svanimittaprayuktaṃ ṅidyo 'ntyasyety etat kim iti na pravartiṣyate ? 

[3] evaṃ tarhi . . . yad etaṃ ṅitaṃ karotīti / ṅitkaraṇād evāyaṃ sarvādeśaḥ syād ity evaṃ pratipādayati / katham ? etat ṅitkaraṇaṃ guṇavṛddhiniṣedhārtham evācāryasya / tac ca nāsati sarvādeśatve saṃbhavatīti sarvādeśābhyupāya eva ṅitkaraṇam / yadi tv antyārthaṃ ṅitkaraṇaṃ saṃpadyate, śakyate hi prakārāntareṇāntādeśatvaṃ pratipattum iti idam āha---itarathā hi loṭa eruprakaraṇa eva brūyād iti / 

anekāl [śit sarvasya] // 1.1.55 //

naitad asti / śitkaraṇād eva sarvādeśo bhaviṣya[tī]ti / anekāltvād ity arthaḥ / nanu ca śakārasya śravaṇaṃ prāpnoti / naiṣa doṣaḥ / e × × × × vā × kā × × nanyārtho lopa iti / tatra śakārasyedaṃ prayojanaṃ samudāyo nivartayitavya iti / samudāye ca nivartita kāryārtham upātto nivartiṣyate /

idaṃ tarhi ‘aṣṭābhya auś’ (A 7.1.21) iti / pūrvatra ‘alo 'ntyasya’ iti darśitam / idānīm ‘ādeḥ parasya’ (A 1.1.54) iti prāptiḥ pradarśyate iti / evamarthe vopādānam / atha vodāharaṇasaṃbhavaḥ pratipādyate / viśeṣaṇārthaḥ śakāraḥ / anyacchakārasya prayojanam iti sarvādeśārthaṃ na bhaviṣyati / [aṃ]tyasyāyaṃ sthāne bhavann iti / katham antyasya śibhāvaḥ śībhāvo vā, yāvatā ‘sarvanāmno’, ‘napuṃsakāt’ itīyaṃ pañcamī ? tatra ‘ādeḥ parasya’ iti prabhavitavyam / evaṃ manyate / ‘sarvanāmnaḥ’ iti iyaṃ saṃbandhaṣaṣṭhī / ‘napuṃsakāt’ iti ca vihitaviśeṣaṇa[m] /
